Text:

|| śrīmaddhanumadbhīmamadhvāntargatarāmakṛṣṇavedavyāsātmakalakṣmīhayagrīvāya namaḥ ||
śrīvyāsatīrthaviracitaṃ bhedojjīvanam
svabhāvataḥ svatantratvapramukhairnikhilairguṇaiḥ |
vibhinno viśvato viṣṇurjayatājjagadīśvaraḥ ||
bhagavantarāyakṛtabhedojjīvanabhāvapradīpaḥ --
svataḥ svātantryeṇāsamasukhamukhaiḥ sarvasuguṇaiḥ supūrṇaṃ nirdoṣaṃ nikhilacidacidbhinnamaniśam |
bhaje śrīmatpūrṇapramatigururāṭsvāntakamalasthirāvāsaṃ vyāsaṃ cidabhayakarāmbhojayugalam ||1 ||
praṇamya śrīmadānandatīrthasaccaraṇāmbujam |
jayarājamunīnvyāsamunīnasmadgurūnapi ||2 ||
tadanugrahato bhedojjīvanaṃ vyākaromyaham |
upajīvya mahadvācaṃ yathāmati harermude ||3 ||
śrīmadvyāsatīrthapūjyacaraṇo māyimatanikṛntanaṃ bhedojjīvanaṃ vitanvan vighnavighātādyarthaṃ vartiṣyamāṇagranthopakṣepāya tadupayogiviśeṣaṇavattayā viṣṇuṃ stuvaṃstadutkarṣajñānaṃ prārthayate || svabhāvata iti || jagadīśvaro viṣṇurjayatāt |
utkṛṣṭo vartatāmiti yojanā |
jidhātoḥ sakarmakatve'pyatrotkarṣaprāptyarthakatvādakarmakaḥ prayogaḥ |
yathoktam -- `jayerjayābhibhavayorādye'rthe'sāvakarmakaḥ |
utkarṣaprāptirādyo'rtho dvitīyārthe sakarmaka' iti |
atra nityotkarṣavato'pi bhagavata utkarṣaprāptiprārthanā `jaya jaya jahyajāmajite'tyādāviva tajjñāpakābhiprāyatvādupapannā |
yathoktam -- `sarvadā jayato viṣṇorjayastasya prakāśanam |
śravaṇaṃ dṛṣṭirityādiprārthyaṃ tajjñāpanaṃ smṛtam |
śṛṇoti paśyati hyeṣa jayatītyādikaṃ svataḥ |
jaya paśya śṛṇuṣveti tattajjñāpanameva tvi'ti |
eke tu bhagavadutkarṣasya nityasiddhatve'pi bhāvikālasambandhitvena tadicchāyā bhagavatprītihetutvena jayatāditi bhāvikālasambandhitvenotkarṣecchārthakaloṭ upapannaḥ |
rājña evamevotkarṣa uttaratrāpi syāditīcchāvatsu rājñaḥ prītidarśanādityāhuḥ |
taccintyam |
bhagavadutkarṣasya nityasiddhatvavadrājādīnāṃ bhāvikālasambandhyutkarṣasyāniścitatvāttadicchāvatsu rājaprīteryuktatve'pi nityasiddhabhagavadutkarṣaviṣaye tādṛśecchāyāḥ prītihetutvāyogena tasyā akartavyatvāt |
ata eva mahābhāratatātparyanirṇaye śrīkṛṣṇasatyabhāmābhyāmaditidattāśīrvacanasyāsurāveśātdajñānamūlakatvamuktam -- `tamāsurāveśavaśādajānatī satyāṃ ca sarvaprabhavau jagatprabhū |
nirdoṣasaukhyaikatanū śubhāśiṣastābhyāṃ dadau so'ditirātmaputravadi'ti |
anyathā'dityāśīrvacanasyāpyuktarītyā bhagavatprītihetutvāpātāttena tasyāsurāveśādimūlakatvoktyayogāditi || kecittu viṣṇurjagat duṣṭakṛtavighnādikaṃ viśvaṃ jayatādabhibhavatvityartha iti vyācakṣate |
apare tu jagat śrutitātparyānabhijñamadvaitivādijātaṃ jayatānmatkṛtabhedojjīvanena parābhāvayet |
antarṇītaṇijartho'yamiti vyācakruḥ |
nanu kathaṃ viṣṇorjagadīśvaratvaṃ niyamyaniyāmakabhāvasya bhedavyāptatvāt |
tasya ca vilakṣaṇadharmayoganibandhanatvādityato jagadvilakṣaṇadharmavattvena viṣṇostato bhedaṃ sādhayati |
svatantratvapramukhairityādinā |
nikhilā niśśeṣāḥ khilā apūrṇā na bhavantīti nikhilāḥ pratyekaṃ niravadhikāśca |
tairguṇairjñānānandabalādibhirnimittairvinśvataścetanācetanātmakājjagato vibhinna iti |
bhedābhedamatanirāsāya vītyupasargaḥ |
atyantabhinna ityarthaḥ |
bhedasyāvidyādyupādhikṛtatvamatanirāsāyoktaṃ svabhāvata iti |
vibhinna iti sambandhaḥ |
vītyupasargalabdhārthakathanamevaitat |
atyantabhedasyānaupādhikatvaniyamāt |
nanu na viṣṇurnikhilapūrṇaguṇaścetanatvāddevadattavaditi cenna |
asyānumānasyāsvatantratvarūpopādhiduṣṭatvāditi bhāvenoktaṃ svatantratvapramukhairiti |
svatantratvasya nikhilapūrṇaguṇatvopapādakatvātpramukhatvoktiḥ |
yathoktaṃ nyāyavivaraṇe -- `svatantrasya pūrṇaguṇatvaniyamādi'ti |
`yadadhīnā guṇāścaiva doṣā api hi sarvaśaḥ |
kathaṃ tasya guṇā na syuḥ syurdoṣāśca kathaṃ punaḥ' ityanuvyākhyāne ca |
svatantratvaṃ ca svasattādau parānapekṣatvaṃ vivakṣitam |
svecchāmātrādhīnapravṛttimattvaṃ vā |
kecittvanadhīnatvameva svatantratvaṃ vivakṣitamityāhuḥ |
taccintyam |
tasya svatantraśabdenālābhāt |
svasya tantro'dhīno hi svatantraḥ |
bhagavati svādhīnatvasya sattvenānadhīnatvābhāvādasambhavāpattyā parādhīnatvasyāvaśyaṃ vivakṣaṇīyatvācca |
ata evoktamanuvyākhyāne -- `svecchānusāritāmeva svātantryaṃ hi vido viduḥ' iti |
`svatantraḥ svecchādhīnapravṛttiḥ' iti tattvodyotaṭīkāyāṃ ca |
etena niyamyaniyāmakabhāvasya bhedavyāptatvenaikasminbhagavati kathaṃ svādhīnatvarūpasvatantratvopapattiriti nirastam |
svecchānusāripravṛttereva sktantraśabdārthatvāt |
icchāpravṛtyośca bhedābhāve'pi tatpratinidherviśeṣasya sadbhāvāttadbalena niyantṛniramyatopapatteriti || atra kecit |
īśitavyena jagatā sambandhābhāve tadīśvaratvaṃ na sambhavatītyata uktaṃ viśvato viṣṇuriti |
viśvasminvyāpta ityartha iti vyācakṣate |
tadapi cintyam |
jagatyasthitasyāpīśvarasya tanniyamanopapatteḥ |
paraṃ kevalalīlayaiva jagati sthitirīśvarasya |
yathoktamanuvyākhyāne -- `tatra tatra sthito viṣṇustattacchaktiprabodhakaḥ |
dūrato'pyatiśaktaḥ sa līlayā kevalaṃ prabhuḥ' iti |
mudgalāryatanūjānandatīrthācāryaviracitabhedacintāratnamnisargasvātantryapramitisukhasaundaryakaruṇāmukhairlokavrātātsvaguṇanicayairgāḍhamitaraḥ |
hariḥ śrīmānasmātsthiratarabhidā bhinnajagatassadā sākṣādīśo jayati jagati śrīparivṛḍhaḥ ||1 ||
pūrṇabodhāñjayāryāṃśca vyāsarājagurūnapi |
natvā tatkṛpayā kurve bhedojjīvanavistṛtim ||2 ||
prācīnaprapañcitamapi pañcavidhaṃ bhedaprapañcaṃ vikṣiptaṃ saṅkṣepādinā sphuṭīkariṣyan jijñāsājananāya tamevopakṣipan prāripsitaparisamāptaye sveṣṭadevatāśiṣamāśāste -- svabhāvata iti || atra svatantratvasya pramukhatvaṃ nāma svetarasakalaguṇasādhakatvam |
yathā''hurācāryāḥ -- `svatantratvātsukhasajjñānaśaktipūrvairguṇaiḥ pūrṇa' iti |
viśvato vibhinna iti sambandhaḥ |
vītyupasargo bhedābhedaniṣedhāya |
viśvata ityuktyā tatrāpyavāntarabheda upakṣiptaḥ |
ekasya viśvatvāyogāt |
mithyāguṇaiḥ satyatvālābhātsvabhāvata iti |
prāpteriti śeṣaḥ |
svabhāvato vibhinna iti vā sambandhaḥ |
tadapi bhedasyopādhyāyattattvaniṣedhāya |
prayogaśca viṣṇurviśvapratiyogikapāramārthikabhedādhikaraṇaṃ viśvāvṛttipāramārthikaguṇādhikaraṇatvāt |
yo yadavṛttiyatsattākaguṇādhikaraṇaṃ sa tatpratiyogikatatsattāsamānasattākabhedādhikaraṇam |
yathā ghaṭaḥ paṭabhidādhikaraṇamiti |
guṇapāramārthyaṃ ca `satyasso asya mahimā' ityādi pramāṇasiddhamiti bhāvaḥ |
aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā ca na doṣa iti vakṣyati |
jagadīśvara iti bhede yuktyantaroktiḥ |
śatrūnityadhyāhareṇābhibhāvayatāditi vā'rthaḥ |
tvamityadhyāhāreṇa madhyamapuruṣo vā |
` āśiṣi liṅloṭāviti ' smaraṇādāśiṣi loṭ |
viṣṇorjaya eva svasyāśāsiteti svasyāsīstvaṃ svāpekṣayā na tu viṣṇvapekṣayeti jñātavyam |
tena pūrṇakāme kathamāśīrvacanamiti nirastam |
kumbhārivāsudevācāryakṛtabhedacandrikā -- 
pramitāsahavāsidharmavāsaṃ mayi sambhāvayateti bhāvanāya |
narasiṃhaśarīramādadhāno mayi mūḍhe'stu sarasvatīṃ dadhānaḥ ||1 ||
brahmaṇyatīrthacaraṇaśrīpāṇyaraṇisambhavaḥ |
madhvoktimāruteto'stu vyāsāgnirmattamaśchide ||2 ||
māyimattebhakumbhārivāsudevo hadermude |
haryālambo vitanute bhedojjīvanaṭippaṇīm ||3 ||
śrīmadvyāsatīrthaparamahaṃso viṣṇuviśvakṣīranīrabhedojjīvanaṃ kariṣyanvighnavighātādiprayojanāya vartiṣyamāṇagranthasaṅgatyupayogiviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭatvena viṣṇuṃ stuvannāśāste || svabhāvata ityādinā || jagadīśvaro jayatāt utkṛṣṭo vartatām |
utkarṣaprāptāvayamakarmakaḥ |
karmaṇo dhātvantargateḥ |
`dhātorarthāntare vṛtterdhātvarthenopasaṅgrahāt |
prasiddheravivakṣātaḥ karmaṇo'karmikā kriyā |
jayerjayābhibhavayorādye'rthe'sāvakarmakaḥ |
utkarṣaprāptirādyo'rtho dvitīye'rthe sakarmaka' ityukteḥ |
yadyapi bhagavata utkarṣeṇa vartanasya nityasiddhatvāt asiddheṣṭaprārthanārūpāśīrarthako loṭ na yujyate |
tathā'pi durvādibhirbhagavato viśvābhedasya sādhitatvena sādhūnāṃ tasya viśvotkarṣaprāptibuddherasiddhatvena lokadarśanabhāṣayā `jaya jaya jahyajā'mityādaviva loṭ yujyate |
abhīṣṭārthako'yaṃ vā loṭ |
yadveśvaro jagadgacchati śrutimārgāditi śrutitātparyānabhijñamadvaitavādijātaṃ jayatāt |
antarbhūtaṇijartho'yaṃ jidhāturabhibhāvayet matkṛtabhedojjīvanena tatparābhāvayediti bhāvaḥ |
evañca loḍapi yathāśruta eva sambadhyate |
īśitavyena jagatā sambandhābhāve tadīśvaratvaṃ na sambhavatītyata uktaṃ viśvato viṣṇuriti |
saptamyantāttasiḥ |
viśvasminvyāpta ityarthaḥ |
yadvā viṣṇurjayatāditi sambandhaḥ |
jagadīśvarapadaṃ tatsadbhāve pramāṇasūcanaparam |
viṣṇuviśvayoḥ sati bhede tatra vyāptistadīśvaratvaṃ vā syādityata uktaṃ svabhāvato na tūpādhito vibhinna iti |
pañcamyāstasiḥ |
viśvata iti dehalīpradīpavadubhayatrāpyanveti |
tatra kiṃ mānamata uktaṃ svabhāvataḥ svatantratvapramukhairnikhilairguṇairiti |
jñaptisādhanatvena tṛtīyāprātipadikānāṃ jñāneṣu lakṣaṇā vā |
tṛtīyārthe tasiḥ |
svabhāvataḥ svarūpabhūtairna tvavidyādyupādhiprayuktaiḥ svatantratvaṃ pramukhaṃ mukhyameṣāṃ sarvajñatvasarvaśaktitvapūrṇānandatvādīnāṃ taistadguṇasaṃ jñānaścāyam |
tena svatantratvasyāpi samāsārthe'ntarbhāvaḥ |
vyatirekiṇaścaite viśvabhede hetavaḥ |
cetanabhedācetanabhedayoḥ pratyekaṃ prasiddhyorviṣṇau sādhanānnāsiddhiḥ |
na cāsādhāraṇyam |
dvitvarūpasādhyatāvacchedakāvacchinnavattvena niścitāddhetoravyāvṛtteḥ |
ekādhikaraṇāntarbhāvena sādhyatāvacchedakāvacchinnasādhyāprasiddhāvapyadhikaraṇadvayāntarbhāvena tatprasiddheśca |
anyathā pṛthivyāṃ trayodaśabhedānāṃ sādhyatvapakṣaḥ pareṣāmapyanupapannaḥ syāt |
athavā vakṣyamāṇarītyā viśvatādātmyābhāvasya sādhyatvādabhāvasādhyake vyatirekiṇi sādhyāprasiddheradūṣaṇatvāt |
sādhyaviśeṣyakāyā evānumiteḥ prathamaṃ sambhavānnānupapattiḥ |
avaśiṣṭamagre vakṣyate |
nanu kimidaṃ svatantratvam |
svādhīnatvamiti cenna |
svasyānugatasyaikasyābhāvena bhagavadadhīnatvasya paryavasāne niyamyaniyāmakabhāvasya bhedavyāpyasya bhedābhāve ekasminnayogenāsiddhervirodhācca |
viśeṣaṇānupādāne ca viśvasminnapi sattvena vyabhicāraḥ |
na ca svatantratvaṃ `svayaṃ dāsāstapasvina' ityatra svayaṃ dāsatvaṃ svātiriktadāsaśūnyatvamiva svātiriktaniyāmakaśūnyatvaṃ tacca bhagavatyastīti nāsiddhirna vā virodhaḥ |
nāsti ca viśvasminniti na vyabhicāra iti vācyam |
abbhakṣo vāyubhakṣa ityādāvivāsādhāraṇyena vyapadeśamāśritya svamātratantratvaṃ svamātrādhīnatvam |
svādhīnatve sati svānyānadhīnatvamiti svaśabdādupasthitisambhavena tatra bhāvāṃśasya vaiyyarthyādavivakṣaṇena svānyānadhīnatvasya śabdādupasthitisambhave'pi svātiriktaniyāmakaśūnyatvasya svatantraśabdādupasthiteḥ |
na ca svānyānadhīnatvamapi tattulyasattākamitīdameva svatantratvamiti vācyam |
bhagavadanyānadhīnatvasyāpi bhagavatīva viśvāsminnapi sattvena vyabhicārānirāsāt |
na ca bhagavadanyasvottamādhīnatvasya viśvāsminnasattvena vyabhicāra iti vācyam |
tathā'pi lakṣmyāṃ tadaparihāraditi cet |
ucyate |
svādhīnatvāṃśasyeva bhagavadanyānadhīnatvāṃśasyāpi vaiyyarthyenāvivakṣitatvāt |
anadhīnatvameva svatantratvam |
taccāsti bhagavati |
nāsti ca viśvasminniti na kaścitkṣudropadravaḥ |
anenaiva padyena viṣayādikaṃ sūcitaṃ bhavati |
viśvabhinno viṣṇurviṣayaḥ |
tajjñānamavāntaraprayojanam |
mokṣaḥ paramaprayojanam |
tatkāmo'dhikārī |
yathā yogyaṃ sambandhaḥ |
hulagīśrīnāthācāryakṛtā bhedasañjīvinī --
hariḥ om
numaḥ satyavatīsūnuṃ yatprasādācchriyaṃ dhiyam |
kīṭo'pi niścalāṃ prāpa lokakautukakāriṇīm ||1 ||
natvā nandajayavyāsamuniprabhṛtisadgurūn |
śrīnāthanāmā tanute bhedasañjīvanīṃ śubhām ||2 ||
bahvyaḥ santyatra ṭippaṇyastathā'pyeṣā kṛtirmama |
nopekṣyā tadanuktārthaleśasyāpyatra śaṃsanāt || 3 ||
granthādau maṅgalaṃ kṛtvā nibadhnāti -- svabhāvata iti || jayatādityāśīrloṭo bhāvikālasambandhitvenecchārthaḥ |
bhāvikālasyedānīmasatvāt tatsambandhitvenecchāyuktaiva |
uktecchāyāstadakāraṇatve'pi tādṛśecchāvatsu bhagavatprītirbhavatyeveti tādṛśecchā kartavyaiva |
`rājña evamevotkarṣo'stūttaratrāpī'tīcchāvatsu rājñaḥ (rājñāṃ) prītidarśanāt |
ato bhagavadutkarṣasya sarvadā siddhatvātkathamatreccheti śaṅkā'navakāśaḥ |
vibhinna ityatra vītyupasargeṇa bhede satyatvamanaupādhikatvaṃ tata eva muktāvapyanivartyatvamabhedāsāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ ca labhyate |
tadupapādanāya svabhāvata ityādikamupāttam |
svabhāvata eva viruddhadharmavattve kadācidapyabhedasattve tatsvarūpasyaiva nivṛttyāpattyā kadā'pi kathamapyabhedo nāstīti labhyate |
svatantrasya sarvaniyāmakasya sarvajñasyopādhyadhīnabhedādiprayuktaduḥkhānubhavo'tyantāsambhavaduktaka iti sphoraṇāya svātantryasyaiva prathamato grahaṇaṃ kṛtam |
kāśītimmaṇṇācāryakṛtaṭippaṇī --
janmādyasya yataḥ śāstragamyamanvayataḥ pṛthak |
paramaṃ brahma viṣṇvākhyamadhikaṃ tadupāsmahe ||
akhilāpekṣitamokṣaprayojakaparadevatotkarṣapratipattiprativandhakabhedavipratipattipratiṣedhapūrvakavibhādvayavasthāpanāya prakaraṇamārabhamāṇaḥ śrīmadvyāsatīrthamunirnirantarāyaparisamāptaye pracayāya ca parameśvaraṃ prārthayate -- svabhāvata iti || yadyapi bhagavatpādaireva saparikaraṃ bhedo vyavasthāpitaḥ |
tathāpi tadevātra saṅgṛhya saṅkṣepeṇopapādyata ityavaiyarthyam |
viṣṇurjayatāt viśvotkarṣeṇa vijñāto vartatām |
nāto nityasiddhaviṣṇoḥ utkarṣaprārthanānupapattiḥ |
nvasya viśvotkarṣa eva kathamata āha -- jagadīśvara iti || nanvanīśvarajīvādyabhinnasya jagadīśvaratvamapi kathamata āha -- viśvato vibhinna iti || viśvaśabdo jīvajaḍārthaḥ |
tadbhedasyaupādhikasyāprayojakatvāt svābhāvikatvamalābhāya viśabdaḥ |
tādṛśabhedavattve heturguṇairityantam |
guṇairasādhāraṇapramāpakaiḥ |
itthaṃbhūtalakṣaṇe tṛtīyā |
tādṛśaguṇajñāpyaviśvavibhedavānityarthaḥ |
kāni tāni pramāpakāṇītyataḥ || svatantratvapramukhairiti || parānapekṣasattākatvādirūpasvātantryādeḥ kalpitasya svābhāvikabhedasādhakatvāyogādāha -- svabhāvata iti || svābhāvikairityarthaḥ |
anena śrotṛpravṛttyaṅgabhūtaviṣayādikamapi sūcitam |
viśvavibhedavadviṣṇureva viṣayaḥ |
tasya viśvotkarṣajñānaṃ prayojanam |
tadicchamuradhikārī |
yathāyogaṃ sambandha iti ||
śarkarāśrīnivāsācāryaviracitaṭippaṇī --
śrīmaddhanumadbhīmamadhvāntargatarāmakṛṣṇavedavyāsātmakalakṣmīhayagrīvāya namaḥ ||
hariḥ om |
rāmaṃ ramāpatiṃ natvā śrīmadhvaṃ jayayoginam |
vyāsatīrthaguruṃ bhedojjīvanaṃ vyākaromyaham ||
iha khalu sukhameva me syādduḥkhaṃ manāgapi mā bhūditi nikhilāpekṣitamokṣasya
`pañcabhedāṃśca vijñāya viṣṇoḥ svābhedameva ca ||
nirdoṣatvaṃ guṇodrekaṃ jñātvā muktirnacānyathe'ti pañcabhedajñānasādhyatvasmaraṇāttadarthaṃ pravṛttamapi cirantanaśāstramativitatagambhīratayā na mandānāṃ sukhena bhedajñāpakamiti taduktabhedapramāṇānyeva saṅgṛhya saṅkṣipya ca bhedojjīvanākhyaprakaraṇanirmāṇena bhedajñānaṃ janayitvā mandānuddidhīrṣurvyāsatīrthamuniḥ prāripsitaparisamāptyādiphalakaśiṣṭācārādipramāṇakeṣṭadevatāstavanaprārthanarūpaṃ maṅgalaṃ kṛtvā śiṣyāngrāhayituṃ prakaraṇādau nibadhnāti -- svabhāvata iti || nanu devadattaḥ śatrūnjayatītyādau jidhātoḥ sakarmakatvadarśanātkathamatra karmābhāva iti cenna |
jidhātorabhibhavārthe sakarmakatve'pyutkarṣārthe sakarmakatvābhāvāt |
yathoktaṃ jayerityādinā |
prakṛte cotkarṣa eva vivakṣitaḥ |
tathāca viṣṇurjayatāt utkṛṣṭo bhavatādityarthaḥ |
yadyapibhagavānnityotkṛṣṭaḥ tathāpi vyaktiviśeṣābhiprāyeṇotkarṣaprārthanāyā atra kriyamāṇatvādāśīrarthakaloṭprayogopapattiḥ |
yadvā vivakṣābhedena jagatpadaṃ vyastaṃ samastaṃ ca tantranyāyena pūrvottarābhyāṃ sambadhyate |
tathāca viṣṇurjagat duṣṭavighnādikaṃ viśvaṃ jayatādabhibhavatvityarthaḥ |
nanvanirdhāritasvarūpasya stutyādikaraṇamanupapannaṃ, naca lakṣaṇena vinā svarūpanirdhāraṇaṃ bhavatītyato viṣṇorlakṣaṇamāha -- jagadīśvara iti || atra jagacchabdena jamutpattiṃ gacchatīti vyutpattyā cetanācetanātmakaprapañca ucyate |
parādhīnaviśeṣāvāptyādirūpotpatteḥ sarvasādhāraṇyānnātra kaścitkṣudropadravaḥ |
anena jaganniyāmakatvaṃ lakṣaṇamuktaṃ bhavati |
nanu niyamyaniyāmakabhāvasya bhedavyāptatvātprapañceśvarayoścabhedābhāvādidaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ nasambhavatītyata āha -- vibhinna iti || atra viśvapadaṃ cetanātmakaviśvaparam |
ata eva cetanācetanātmakaviśvabhinnatvamityanupadameva vakṣyati |
tathāca cetanācetanātmakaprañcacādatyantabhinnamityarthaḥ |
atra vītyupasargeṇātyantabhedoktyā bhedābhedavādiyādavaprakāśo nirākṛtaḥ |
nanu bhedasyaupādhikatvena muktāvabhāvānmuktacetananiyāmakatvaṃ bhagavato na syādityata āha -- svabhāvata iti || svarūpata ityarthaḥ |
vibhinna ityanenāsya sambandhaḥ |
bhedasya bhedakadharmaniyatvāttānāha -- guṇairiti || teṣāṃ prapañcasādhāraṇyena kathaṃ bhedakatvamityatoguṇānviśinaṣṭi -- svatantratvapramukhairiti || sattātraividhye parānadhīnatvaṃ svatantratvam |
tadeva pramukhaṃ pradhānaṃ yeṣu te svatantratvapramukhāḥ tairityarthaḥ |
nanveṣāṃ guṇānāmaupādhikatvena muktāvabhāvānna svarūpabhedaniyāmakatvamityatopyāha(tovāha) -- svabhāvata iti || ādyādibhya upasaṅkhyānāttṛtīyārthe tasiḥ |
svarūpabhūtairityarthaḥ |
nikhilairiti svarūpasaṅkīrtanam || 1 ||
nanu kathaṃ viṣṇvākhyasya parabrahmaṇaḥ svataścetanācetanātmakaviśvabhinnatvam |
bhede pramāṇābhāvāt |
tathā hi |
na tāvajjīveśvarabhede pratyakṣaṃ sambhavati |
īśvaradharmikasyeśvarapratiyogikasya vā bhedasyeśvarāpratyakṣatvenāpratyakṣatvāt |
yadarthaṃ viśvato vibhinnatvaṃ viṣṇorviśeṣaṇamuktaṃ tatsamarthayitumākṣipati -- nanvityādinā || viśvaśabdena cetanācetanātmakasarvajagato vivakṣitatvāccetanācetanātmaketyuktam |
evaṃ viṣṇuśabdena parabrahmaṇa eva vivakṣitatvādviṣṇvākhyasya parabrahmaṇa ityuktamiti dhyeyam |
kuta evamākṣepa ityata āha -- bheda iti || viṣṇvākhyasya brahmaṇo viśvavibhinnatvāṅgīkāre viśvasyāpi tato bhedo'ṅgīkāryaḥ |
na hi viṣṇurviśvabhinno viśvaṃ tu tato'bhinnamiti sambhavati |
evaṃ cetanācetanātmakaviśvasyāpi mitho bhedo'ṅgīkāryaḥ |
anyathā cetanācetanātmaketi nirdeśasyaivānupapatteḥ |
evameva cetanānāmacetanānāṃ ca parasparabhedo'pyaṅgīkāryaḥ |
anyathā samastavāciviśvaśabdopādānavaiyyarthyāpātāt |
tathā ca cetanācetanātmakaviśvapratiyogikaviṣṇudharmikabhede vā viśvadharmikaviṣṇupratiyogikabhede vā viśvasya parasparabhede vā cetanānāmacetanānāmapi parasparabhede vā pramāṇābhāvādityarthaḥ |
ata eva kathaṃ viśvavibhinnatvamiti viṣṇudharmikabhedasya prastutatve'pi sarvabhedasaṅgrahārthaṃ punarbhedagrahaṇam |
anyathā tasya vaiyyarthyāpatteriti dhyeyam |
yadyāpyādyapadye svatantratvapramukhairityanumānapramāṇamuktam |
tathā'pi tasya pratyakṣādikrameṇaiva dūṣaṇamabhidhāsyan śaṅkotthāpanārthameva muktiḥ |
abhidhāsyati caitaddūṣaṇaṃ vimatau bhinnāvityanumānamāśaṅkyeti |
etadeva krameṇopapādayati -- tathā hītyādinā || tāvacchabdaḥ kramārthaḥ |
na tāvajjīveśvarabhede na tāvatpratyakṣamityubhayatrāpi sambadhyate || jīveśvarabheda iti || jīvādīśvarasya bhede jīvasyeśvarādbhede vetyarthaḥ |
pratyakṣaṃ bhavati |
pramāṇamiti śeṣaḥ |
kutastatra pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇaṃ netyata āha -- īśvaradharmikasyeti || jīvādīśvarabhedasyeśvarājjīvabhedasya vetyarthaḥ |
bhedapratyakṣe dharmipratiyoginorubhayorapi pratyakṣatvaṃ tantramiti bhāvaḥ |
etena viṣṇorviśvato bhinnatvaṃ kathamiti viṣṇudharmikabhedamātrasya śaṅkitatvena tatpratiyogikabhede pramāṇābhāvakathanamasaṅgatamiti śaṅkānavakāśa iti dhyeyam |
kecittu jīveśvarabhede ityetajjīvapratiyogika īśvarabhede iti vyākhyāya tarhīśvarapratiyogikasya vetyaprastutakathanaṃ kuta ityāśaṅkya dṛṣṭāntārthaṃ taduktiriti vyācakṣate |
taccintyam |
īśvarapratiyogikabhedasyāpyuktarītyā prastutatvasattvāt |
kiñca viśvavibhinnatvaṃ viśvapratiyogikabhedavattvaṃ vā viśvaniṣṭhabhedapratiyogitvaṃ vā syāt |
tadubhayamapi na sambhavati |
bhedadvaye pramāṇābhāvādityāśayena na tāvajjīveśvarabheda itvādigrantho yathāśruta evāstviti yadatra vyākhyānāntaraṃ tadapi cintyam |
viśvato vibhinna ityukte viśvapratiyogikabhedasyaivāvabhāsāt |
viśvaniṣṭhabhedapratiyogitvasyānavabhāsāditi |
pūrvapakṣamuṭṭaṅkayati -- nanviti || pareṇa brahmaṇa eva viśvābhedokterviṣṇurvibhinna iti vyadhikaraṇamityato viṣṇvākhyasyeti || kathamiti || kiṃ bhede pramāṇādaikye prāmāṇābhāvādvā |
ādye pratyakṣādanumānādāgamādvā |
dvitīye pratyakṣābhāvādanumānābhāvādāgamābhāvādvetyarthaḥ |
cetanācetaneti viśvapadavyākhyānam |
tadavāntarabhedopakṣepasūcanārthaṃ cetanācetanātmaketi |
anyathā'vāntarabhedabhinnamityevāvakṣyat || bhinnatvamiti || bhedaṃ prati dharmitvaṃ pratiyogitvaṃ cetyarthaḥ |
atra vipratipattiḥ |
īśvaratvopalakṣitā cit jīvatvopalakṣitacitpratiyogikadharmisattāsamānasattākānaupādhikakevalabhedavatī na vetyādirūhyā || īśvarāpratyakṣatveneti || sasamvandhikapadārthapratyakṣe sambandhipratyakṣaṃ tantram |
anyathā vāyvākāśasaṃyogasya vā vāyughaṭasaṃyogasya vā pratyakṣatvāpātaḥ |
atrāpīśvareti śuddhaṃ brahmaiva vivakṣitam |
nanu tattvavidbhiḥ saguṇasya sākārasya bhinnatvamucyate |
parābhipretaṃ tu brahma nirguṇamiti vyadhikaraṇo vivāda iti cenna |
yatparasya nirguṇaṃ tadeva tattvataḥ saguṇaḥ sākāra īśvaro na tato'tiriktaḥ kaściditi viśeṣaikyena sāmānādhikaraṇyāt |
etena jīvajaḍānāmatīndriyāṇāmanatīndriyāṇāṃ cānyonyabhede pratyakṣaṃ nirastam |
na ca sarvatra yogipratyakṣaṃ mānam |
tacca `juṣṭaṃ yadā paśyati' ityādiprasiddhamiti vācyam |
āgamanirāsenaiva nirasiṣyamāṇatvāt |
viśvabhinnatvanirvāhārthamuttaragrantha iti nirvāhakatvameva saṅgatirityāśayena bhagavato viśvabhinnatvamākṣipati -- nanvityādinā || ākṣepo na viṣṇoḥ kintu bhedasyetyāha -- bhede pramāṇābhāvāditi || yadyapi svatantratvapramukhairnikhilairguṇairiti pramāṇamuktaṃ tathā'pi nirguṇe brahmaṇi pakṣīkṛte hetūnāṃ guṇānāmasiddhiḥ |
saguṇapakṣīkāre aupādhikabhedenārthāntaratetyabhisandhinaivamuktiḥ |
cetanācetanātmakaviśvabhinnatve pramāṇābhāvaṃ pariśeṣeṇa sādhayiṣyan cetanabhede pratyakṣaṃ tāvaddūṣayati -- na tāvaditi || jīveśvarabhede jīvapratiyogikeśvarabhede || īśvaradharmikasyeti || jīvapratiyogikasyeśvaradharmikasya bhedasyeśvararūpadharmipratyakṣābhāvenāpratyakṣatvādityanvayaḥ |
īśvarapratiyogikasyeti dṛṣṭāntārtho vā |
vā ivārthe |
tataśca jīvadharmikasyeśvarapratiyogikasya bhedasyeśvararūpapratiyogipratyakṣābhāvena jīvāntararūpadharmipratyakṣābhāvena ca yathā'pratyakṣatvaṃ tathetyarthaḥ |
anyathā tasyāprastutatvena tatra pramāṇanirākaraṇānaucityāt |
yadvā viśvabhinnatvaṃ viśvapratiyogikabhedavattvaṃ vā viśvaniṣṭhabhedapratiyogitvaṃ vā syāt |
tadubhayamapi na sambhavati |
bhedadvaye pramāṇābhāvādityāśayena `na tāvajjīveśvarabheda' ityādigrantho yathāśruta eva |
atra vaktavyaṃ parihārāvasare vakṣyate |
uktārthaṃ pūrvapakṣanirāsādinā dṛḍhīkartumārabhyate || nanvityādinā |
viṣṇorbrahmatvotkīrtanaṃ parairmāyāśabalasya kasyacidviṣṇvākhyasyāsmadādāviva nikhilaprapañcatādātmyānaṅgīkārātkathaṃ tadabhiprāyeṇa pūrvapakṣotthitiriti śaṅkāmapākartum |
viṣṇupadabrahmapadayostrividhaparicchedarāhityarūpavyāptivṛddhyarthayoḥ samānārthatvena tayorekatvāttadupari pūrvapakṣotthitiriti bhāvaḥ |
uttaratra cetanācetanabhedayorvicārayiṣyamāṇatvāttasya viśvapadārthatayā prasaktatvasūcanāya cetanācetanātmakamiti viśvaviśeṣaṇam || īśvarapratiyogikasyeti || bhedaḥ saṃyogādivadubhayavṛttistaddhaṭakapadārthapratiyogikastadanuyogikaśca bhavati |
imau saṃyuktāvayamanena saṃyukto'yamapyanena saṃyukta iti pratītīnāmivemau bhinnāvayamasmādbhinno'yaṃ cāsmādbhinna iti pratītīnāmabādhitānāṃ sattvāt |
ekasyaiva bhedasya ghaṭapratiyogikatvaṃ tadanuyogikatvaṃ cāṅgīkriyate |
ghaṭaḥ svasmādbhinna ityādyāpattiśca svapratiyogikatvaviśiṣṭabhedādhikaraṇatāyāstādṛśapratītau niyamena bhāsamānāyāḥ svasmin bādhitatvādeva na prasajyate |
ghaṭaḥ svena saṃyukta iti pratītyāpattivaditi matenedam |
ata evaitanmatāvaṣṭambhenaiva candre bhedamāśritya dvau candrāviti pratītimupapādayiṣyandṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyaṃ parihariṣyati |
tathā ceśvarapratiyogikatvena pratīyamānasya jagad īśvarādbhinnamiti pratītau bhāsamānasyeśvaro jīvādiprapañcādbhinna iti pratītau bhāsamānasya ca bhedasyaikatveneśvaradharmikatveneśvarāpratyakṣatvenāpratyakṣatvameva |
ghaṭagaganasaṃyogavatsambandhapratyakṣe yāvadāśrayapratyakṣasya kāraṇatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
etena pūrvaśloke jagatpratiyogikeśvarabhedasyaiva prasaktatvena tatraiva vicārasya prakrāntatveneśvarapratiyogikabhedasyāpratyakṣatvavacanamasaṅgatam |
evaṃ pratiyogyapratyakṣatvasya bhedāpratyakṣatvasampādakatve stambhaḥ piśāco neti bhedasya pratyakṣatvānupapattiriti parāstam |
pūrvoktamate īśvarapratiyogikasyāpi bhedasyeśvaradharmikatvāṅgīkāreṇāprasaktavicārarūpatvābhāvādetanmate stambhaḥ piśāco neti pratīteranumitiśābdādirūpatvābhyupagamena pratyakṣarūpatvānabhyupagamācca |
vāśabdaḥ samuccaye |
yadvā bhedo yatpratiyogikaḥ natadvṛttiḥ, kintvanuyogimātravṛttiriti sarvasiddhimatenedaṃ vākyaṃ pravṛttam |
tarhīśvarapratiyogikabhedasyāpratyakṣatvopapādanasya kathamupayoga iti cedittham |
yathā bhāskaramate ghaṭe mṛdā sahātyantābhedo mṛdi tu ghaṭasyātyantabheda eva |
evaṃ kāryakāraṇayorbhedābhedau vyavasthitau |
tathā kāraṇībhūteśvarasya jagatā sahātyantābhede'pi jagadīśvareṇātyantābhinnamiti matamapākartuṃ vibhinna ityuktam |
viśeṣato vibhinnatvaṃ ca svātyantābhinnajagatpratiyogikātyantabhedavattvam |
idaṃ ca pūrvaślokaprasaktaṃ tadanusāreṇa viśvabhinnatvaśaṅkāgranthasya bhinnatvamityasyāpyayamevārthaḥ |
tathā ca bhedadvayasyāpi prakṛtatvādīdīśvarapratiyogikabhedasyāpratyakṣatvopapādanaṃ yuktam |
na ca pratiyogyapratyakṣatvena bhedasyāpratyakṣatve stambhapiśācabhedapratyakṣānupapattiḥ |
iṣṭāpatteḥ |
pūrvoktarītyā tasya śābdādirūpatvopagamāt |
na cātra bījābhāvaḥ |
bhedapratyakṣe pratiyogivṛttidharmaviruddhadharmajñānasya kāraṇatvānubhavena pratiyogina evāpratyakṣatve tadvṛttidharmasyāpyapratyakṣatvena tadviruddhatvasyāpi dharmāntare grahītumaśakyatvāt |
athavā pratiyogitāvacchedakaviśiṣṭapratiyoginirṇayo bhedadhīhetuḥ piśācādīnāṃ lokasiddhavyavahārādinā nirṇayāttatpratiyogikabhedapratyakṣamupapadyate |
bhavadabhimatasya svātantryādiviśiṣṭeśvarasya nānumānādigamyatvam |
tasya satpratipakṣādikabalitatvenānirṇāyakatvāt |
āgamānāṃ tūpakramāliṅgaiścinmātraparatvādavaśiṣṭaṃ pratyakṣameva |
tadapyukteśvare nāstīti pratiyoginirṇayarūpakāraṇābhāvādukteśvarabhedapratyakṣaṃ na yuktamityāśayaḥ |
nanvīśvararūpadharmiṇo'pratyakṣatvāttadvṛttibhedasyāpratyakṣatvakathanamayuktam |
gaganasyāpratyakṣatve'pi tadvṛttyudbhūtarūpābhāvasya pratyakṣaviṣayatāyāstāntrikairabhyupagamātpuṣpasya ghrāṇāviṣayatve'pi tadvṛtticampakagandhābhāvasya ghrāṇajapratyakṣaviṣayatvāṅgīkārāditi cenna |
atyantābhāvasthale yogyapratiyogyanupalabdherhetutvena tathātvasambhave'pi bhedapratyakṣe'dhikaraṇapratyakṣasyaivānvayavyatirekābhyāṃ hetutvamityāśayenoktasaṅgateḥ |
yadvā tādātmyābhāvo'nyonyābhāva eva |
tatpratyakṣe yogyatādātmyānupalabdhiḥ kāraṇam |
yogyatvaṃ ca pratiyogisattvaprasañjitapratiyogikatvam |
tathā ca pratyakṣībhūtādhikaraṇe tanniṣṭhetaratādātmyopalabdherāpādayitumaśakyatvānna prakṛte yogyānupalabdhirityāśaya ityalam |
kecittu vāśabda upamāyām |
tathā ceśvarapratiyogikabhedo dṛṣṭāntatayopātta iti nāprasaktakathanam |
so'pi parajīvānuyogiko'tīndriyajaḍānuyogikaśca grāhyastasya svapratyakṣatvāditi vyācakṣate |
tatreśvarapratiyogikabhedasyeśvarātiriktasāmānyaniṣṭhasyaikasyaivādvaitavādibhirnaiyyāyikaiścāṅgīkṛtatvena svasmin ghaṭādau ceśvarabhedasya pratyakṣaviṣayatvavivādasya sattvena dṛṣṭāntāsaṅgatiḥ |
dharmibhedena bhedāṅgīkāreṇaivedamityuktāvapi bhedasāmānyasya grahaṇāsaṅgatiḥ |
svānuyogikeśvarabhedapratyakṣanirākaraṇaṃ cetyādikaṃ kathaṃ parihartavyamiti vibhāvanīyam |
na ca tasyāprasaktatvādeva na tannirākaraṇamapekṣitamiti vācyam |
tathā'pi svakṛtadvitīyavyākhyāne īśvarapratiyogikabhedapratyakṣasāmānyasyāpi nirākartavyatvena prasaktatayā tatroktadoṣāparihārāditi bhedapratyakṣasāmānye pratiyogipratyakṣasyāpi kāraṇatvamiti śaṅkakasyābhimānasthāpanena yathākathañcidgranthasaṅgamanaṃ tvagatikā gatiḥ |
athaveti kṛtāsmadvyākhyāne ca naiṣo'bhimāna iti |
atrādvaitavādī śaṅkate -- nanviti || parabrahmaṇa ityanena bhedāṅgīkāre vastvaparicchedarūpaparabrahmatvānupapattiṃ sūcayati |
cetanācetane jīvajaḍe |
svabhāvata ityasya bheda ityanenāpi sambandhaḥ |
atreśvaradharmikabhedasyaiva prakṛtatve'pi viśvaśabdasūcitajīvajaḍabhedasyāpi saṅgrahāya bhedamātragrahaṇam || pramāṇābhāvāditi || uktapramāṇasyābhāsatvādityabhimānaḥ |
tadevopapādayati tathāhītyādinā saṅkṣepa ityantena || na tāvaditi || nanvidamasaṅgatam || svatantryādirūpaviruddhadharmahetukānumānasyaivādyapadyoktatayā tatpratikṣepasyaiva kartavyatvāt |
maivam |
tatra pramukhapadena bhedagrāhipratyakṣāgamaviṣayatvasyāpi vivakṣitatvena pramāṇatrayasyaiva lābhāt |
svabhāvataḥ pratyakṣāgamayoḥ prābalye'pi prakṛte pratyakṣasyeśvarīyasya sādhyatayā savivādatvādāgamasya copapattisāpekṣatvādanumānasyaiva prādhānyena parigrahāt, svabhāvaprābalyameva puraskṛtya pratyakṣasya prathamaṃ pratikṣepāt |
ata eva siddhānte'pi pratyakṣādipramāṇatrayaṃ pradarśayiṣyate || jīveśvarabheda iti || ātmadharmikātmāntarapratiyogikabheda ityarthaḥ |
īśvaradharmikajīvabhede jīvadharmikeśvarabhede mitho jīvabhede ceti yāvat |
ata eva sāmānyata ātmanānātsādhakānumānanirākaraṇasaṅgatiḥ |
īśvaretyātmāntaropalakṣaṇam |
yattu -- viṣṇorviśvato bhinnatvaṃ viśvapratiyogikabhedavattvaṃ viśvaniṣṭhabhedapratiyogitvaṃ vā syāt_ |
tadubhayamapi na sambhavatītyāśayena bhedadvaye pratyakṣapratikṣepa iti |
tanna |
bhinnaśabdasya bhedapratiyogyabodhakatvena tādṛśavikalpāyogāt |
anyathā ghaṭo ghaṭabhinna iti prayogāpatteḥ |
vyāsajyavṛttidharmāvacchinnānuyogitākatadekadeśapratiyogitākabhedānupagame'pi ghaṭapaṭobhayaṃ ghaṭabhinnamiti prayogāpatteśca || apratyakṣatvāditi || dharmipratiyogipratyakṣayorabhāvapratyakṣahetutvāditi bhāvaḥ |
viṣṇausvabhāvataścetanātmakaviśvabhinnatvaṃ samarthayitumākṣipati -- nanviti || cetanācetanātmaketi || anyonyabhinnacetanācetanātmaketyarthaḥ |
tenottaratra kriyamāṇe jīvānāṃ jaṭhānāṃ cānyonyabhedanirākaraṇasamarthanesaṅgacchete |
anyathā tayoḥ prakṛtāsaṅgatiḥ syāditi dhyeyam |
bhede svabhāvatobhede |
evamagre'pi |
bhedepramāṇaṃ pratyakṣamanumānamāgamoveti vikalpānmanasi nidhāyādyaṃ dūṣayati -- neti || jaḍabhede'pi pratyakṣaṃ nirākariṣyāmītyāśayena tāvacchabdaḥ |
uttarapakṣadvayamapi nirākariṣyāmītyāśayena vā || jīveśvarabheda iti || jīvānāmanyonyabhede jīveśvarabhede cetyarthaḥ |
pratyakṣaṃ kimīśvaro jīvo netīśvaradharmikajīvapratiyogikabhedaviṣayamabhidhīyate uta jīva īśvaro neti jīvadharmikeśvarapratiyigikabhedaviṣayakam |
ādyaṃ nirācaṣṭe || īśvaradharmikasyeti || īśvaradharmikasya īśvarādhikaraṇasya bhedasyāpratyakṣatvādityanvayaḥ |
tatra hetumāha -- īśvarāpratyakṣatveneti || dharmibhūteśvarasyāpratyakṣatvenetyarthaḥ |
abhāvapratyakṣe'dhikaraṇapratyakṣasya kāraṇatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvabhāvapratyakṣe nādhikaraṇapratyakṣaniyamaḥ |
apratyakṣe'pyākāśe śabdābhāvasya pratyakṣatvāt |
vāyau rūpābhāvasya pratyakṣatvāt |
ato'dhikaraṇajñānamātrasyāpekṣitatvena tasyeśvaraviṣayakasyāpi parokṣasya sattvāttaddharmikasya bhedasya pratyakṣatvamakṣatamiti cenna |
atyantābhāvarūpasaṃsargābhāvasya pratyakṣe'dhikaraṇapratyakṣaniyamābhāve'pi bhedākhyānyonyābhāvapratyakṣe'dhikaraṇapratyakṣāvaśyaṃbhāvāt |
ata evoktaṃ rucidattenopādhyāyamatānuvāde `rūpavadanyonyābhāvastu yogyo'pi vāyāvadhikaraṇe yogyatāvirahānna gṛhyate |
anyonyābhāvagrahe tasyāḥ prayojakatvādi'ti |
uktaṃ ca tṛtīyādhyāye ṭīkākāreṇa |
`saṃsargānyonyābhāvagrahe pratiyogyadhikaraṇayogyatā krameṇa tantrami'ti |
dvitīyaṃ nirāha -- īśvarapratiyogikasyeti || īśvarapratiyogikasya bhedasyāpratyakṣatvādityanvayaḥ |
tatra hetumāha -- īśvarāpratyakṣatveneti || pratiyogibhūteśvarāpratyakṣatvenetyarthaḥ |
abhāvapratyakṣe pratiyogipratyakṣasya hetutvāditi bhāvaḥ |
upalakṣaṇametat |
jīvāntarapratiyogikasya jīvāntaradharmikasya vā bhedasya jīvāntarāpratyakṣatvenāpratyakṣatvādityapi draṣṭavyam |
ata eva yajñadattapratiyogiko viṣṇumitradharmiko bhedo viṣṇumitrasya pratyakṣa ityādyūhyamiti siddhāntagranthe jīvānāmanyonyabhedasya pratyakṣatvasamarthanaṃ kriyate |
ata eva ca nāpyanumānamityādinā''tmamātrabhede'numānamāśaṅkya nirākaraṇaṃ saṅgacchate |
anyathā jīveśvarabhede pratyakṣaṃ nirākṛtya tatraivānumānanirākaraṇasya saṅgatatvenātmamātrabhedasādhakānumānanirākaraṇasyāsaṅgatatvāt |
nacātmamātrabhedāsiddhau jīveśabhedo'pi sidhyatītyāśayena taditi vācyam |
tathāpi caitraśarīramityādinā'nyonyajīvabhedaśaṅkānirāsayoranupapattiriti dhyeyam |
nāpyanumānam |
ātmatvaṃ nānāvyaktiniṣṭhaṃ jātitvāt pṛthivītvavadityasyāsiddheḥ |
ātmaikyavādināṃ(nā) vyaktibhedasya bādhitatvenātmatvasya jātitvānaṅgīkārāt |
ātmā ātmapratiyogikabhedādhikaraṇaṃ dravyatvāt ghaṭavadityasyāpyasiddheḥ |
ātmano nirguṇasyāsmābhirguṇāśrayatvalakṣaṇadravyatvānaṅgīkārāt |
atha sukhaṃ nānāvyaktyāśritaṃ guṇatvāt rūpavat |
sukhatvaṃ nānāvyaktyāśritāśritaṃ guṇaniṣṭhajātitvādrūpatvavaditi |
na ca śabde śabdatve ca (vā) vyabhicāraḥ |
tasya dravyatvāt |
adravyatvapakṣe'pi tadanyatvena hetuviśeṣaṇāditi cenna |
sukhaduḥkhādīnāmantaḥkaraṇāśritatvenāntaḥkaraṇānāṃ ca nānātvena siddhasādhanāt |
svarūpasukhavivakṣāyāṃ tu tasya dravyatvenāsiddheḥ |
caitraśarīraṃ maitraśarīrasvāmibhinnasvāmikaṃ tadviruddhaceṣṭāvattvādityatra vyabhicāraḥ |
ekasvāmikayorapi (sakalaikasvāmikayorapi) ghaṭapaṭayorviruddhaceṣṭā(ceṣṭākatva)darśanāt |
śarīratvena hetuviśeṣaṇe dṛṣṭāntāsiddhiḥ |
vimatau bhinnau viruddhadharmādhikaraṇatvāt dahanatuhinavaditi cenna |
pāramārthikabhedasādhane aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt |
apāramārthika(vyāvahārika)bhedasādhane siddhasādhanatvāt |
tasmānnānumānamātmajīvabhede mānamiti |
|| nāpyanumānamiti || jīveśvarabhede pramāṇaṃ bhavatīti vartate || ātmatvamiti || yadyapīdamanumānamātmamātrabhedasādhakaṃ tathā'pi tadviśeṣajīveśvarabhedo'pyanena sidhyatītyābhiprāyeṇedamabhihitam |
yathā dravyamātrasya guṇitvasiddhau tadviśeṣasya ghaṭasyāpi tatsiddhiriti || jīveśvarabhedasyeva parasparajīvabhedasyāpi prastutatvasya pradarśitatvāttadapekṣayā ceti bodhyam || jātitvāditi || jāteranekānugatatvalakṣaṇakatvāditi bhāvaḥ || asiddheriti || anyatarāsiddherityarthaḥ |
tāmevopapādayati -- ātmaikyavādineti || jātitvānaṅgīkārāditi sambandhaḥ || vyaktibhedasyeti || ātmākhyavyaktibhedasya vakṣyamāṇapramāṇabādhitatvenetyarthaḥ |
asiddhimevopapādayati -- ātmano nirguṇasyeti || nirguṇasyāpyātmano dravyatvaṃ kinnāṅgīkriyata ityata uktaṃ guṇāśrayatvalakṣaṇeti || ātmabhede punaranumānadvayamāśaṅkate -- atha sukhamityādinā || anumānadvaye vyabhicāramāśaṅkya nirākāroti || na ceti || ādyānumāne śabde vyabhicāraḥ |
dvitīyānumāne śabdatve vyabhicāra ityarthaḥ |
ākāśavyakterekatvena nānātvābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
kuto na vyabhicāra ityata āha -- tasyeti || tathā ca śabdasya guṇatvābhāvācchabdatvasya guṇaniṣṭhajātitvābhāvāddhetorevābhāvānna vyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ |
mīmāṃsakamate śabdasya dravyatve'pi naiyyāyikādimate guṇatvāvdyabhicāraḥ sustha ityata āha -- adravyatvapakṣe'pīti || tadanyatveneti || ādyānumāne śabdānyatvena dvitīyānumāne śabdatvānyatvenetyarthaḥ |
evaṃ śaṅkitamanumānadvayaṃ dūṣayituṃ kimatra sukhaśabdenāntaḥkaraṇadharmabhūtaṃ prākṛtaṃ sukhaṃ vivakṣitamuta svarūpasukhamiti vikalpaṃ manasi nidhāyādyaṃ nirācaṣṭe || sukhaduḥkhādīnāmiti || atra duḥkhādigrahaṇaṃ dṛṣṭāntārtham |
dvitīyaṃ nirāha -- svarūpasukheti || guṇaguṇinorbhede svarūpatvasyaivānupapatterabheda evāṅgīkārye ātmana iva tadabhinnasukhasyāpi guṇatvāyogādasiddhirityarthaḥ |
etena dvitīyānumāne'pi sukhatvasyāntaḥkaraṇadharmaniṣṭhatve siddhasādhanatā |
svarūpasukhavṛttidharmatve tasyāguṇatvena guṇaniṣṭhajātitvāyogenāsiddhiriti dūṣaṇaṃ sūcitaṃ bhavati |
upalakṣaṇametat |
atra kimekameva sukhaṃ nānāvyaktyāśritamityucyate |
utānekam |
adye bādhaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyaṃ ca |
na hyekameva sukhaṃ rūpaṃ vā nānāvyaktyāśritamanubhavavirodhatvāt |
ekatvasaṅkhyāyāṃ vyabhicāraśca |
dvitīye hetoyaprayojakatā |
ekasminnevātmani kālabhedenānekasukhāśrayatvadarśanāt |
pādādyavacchedakabhedena yugapadapi taddarśanācca |
svarūpasukhaikyavādinastadvṛttisukhatvasya jātitvānaṅgīkārādasiddhiśca |
kiñcaikasyaiva sukhasya rūpasya vā nānāvyaktyāśritatvapakṣe jātivatparyāptyā nānāvyaktyāśritatvamutānekatṛṇāgraprasāritapaṭavadaparyāptyā |
nobhayamapi |
anubhavavirodhāt |
tathā ca sukhasya nānāvyaktyāśrayatvānupapattau sukhatvasya nānāvyaktyāśritāśritatvamapi dūrotsāritam |
tadadhīnasiddhikatvāttasya |
etena svāśrayadvārakasambandhena nānāvyaktyāśritatvaṃ sukhatvasyaiva sādhyata ityapi nirastam |
uktarītyā'prayojakatvādityādyapi draṣṭavyam |
ata evaivaṃ bahudūṣaṇagrastatvādidamanumānadvayaṃ siddhāntagranthena samāhitamiti jñātavyam |
anyonyajīvabhede'numānamaśaṅkya nirāha -- caitraśarīramiti || maitraśarīreti || maitraśararisvāmī maitrastadbhinnaścaitrastatsvāmikamityarthaḥ || tadviruddheti || maitraśarīraceṣṭāviruddhetyarthaḥ |
atra mitho yuddhyatpālitameṣakukkuṭādidṛṣṭāntaḥ |
yadyadviruddhaceṣṭākaṃ tattatsvāmibhinnasvāmikamiti sāmānyavyāptirabhimatā |
vyabhicāramupapādayati -- ekasvāmikayoriti || atra kvacitsakalaikasvāmikayoriti pāṭhaḥ |
sa tu prāmādika iti prāñcaḥ prāhuḥ |
upalakṣaṇaṃ caitat |
pāramārthikabhedavivakṣāyāmaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā |
apāramārthikabhedasādhane siddhasādhanateti vakṣyamāṇadūṣaṇamapi grāhyam || dṛṣṭāntābhāva iti || caitramaitraśabdayorudāharaṇamātrārthatvena vinyāsabhedena sarvaśarīrāṇāmapi pakṣatvena vivakṣitatvāddṛṣṭāntābhāva ityarthaḥ |
kecittvātmaikyavādino yajñadattādiśarīre'pi maitraśarīsvāmibhinnasvāmikatvasyāsampratipatterdṛṣṭāntābhāva iti vyācakṣate |
jīveśvarabhede'numānāntaramāśaṅkate -- vimatāviti || viruddhadharmādhikaraṇatvāditi || atra pakṣe sarvajñatvāsarvajñatvādirviruddho dharmaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte cauṣṇyaśītatvādirdraṣṭavyaḥ |
virodhasvarūpaṃ tu siddhāntagranthe vakṣyate |
tatra vaktavyamapi tadvyākhyānāvasara eva vakṣyate || aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāditi || pāramārthikabhedasya mayā kutrāpyanaṅgīkṛtatvāditi bhāvaḥ || siddhasādhanatvāditi || jīveśvarabhedasya vyāvahārikatayā mayā'ṅgīkṛtatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
anumānanirākaraṇamupasaṃharati -- tasmāditi ||
|| ātmatvamiti || yadyapi na tāvadityādinā jīveśvarabhedamātropakṣepaḥ kṛtaḥ |
tathā'pi pañcavidhasyāpyādāvupakṣepātsāmānyenātmabhedasādhanaṃ prasaṅgādatra cintyate |
nanu kathaṃ bhedasya pañcavidhatvam |
kiṃ dharmibhedātpratiyogibhedāddharmitāvacchedakabhedātpratiyogitāvacchedakabhedādvā |
nādyau |
jaḍajīveśvarāṇāṃ trayāṇāmeva paryāvṛttyā pratiyogidharmitvāt |
avāntarabhedagrahe tvānantyātpañcatvāyogaḥ |
ata eva nottarau |
jaḍajīveśvaratvānāṃ trayāṇāmeva paryāvṛttyā tathātvāt |
tasmādatra traya eva bhedā vācyāḥ |
kathañciddharmyādipāñcavidhye'pi daśabhedā ityeva vācyam |
pañcapratiyogikānāṃ pañcānuyogikānāṃ ca daśatvāditi |
atra cakāsti pratiyogitāvacchedakabhedādevābhāvabhedaḥ |
ata eva ghaṭānantye'pyavacchedakaikyānna bhūtale ghaṭābhāvānantyam |
prākpradhvaṃsābhāvayostu pratiyogyaikye'pi pūrvottarakālarūpāvacchedakabhedādbhedaḥ |
prakṛte ceśatvaṃ jīvatvaṃ jaḍatvaṃ jaḍatvavyāpyadharmo jīvatvavyāpyadharmaśceti pañcāvacchedakāḥ |
yadyapi vyāpyadharmā aneke tathā'pi tattvenaikīkaraṇānna doṣaḥ |
na hi jīvo jīvājjaḍājjaḍaṃ bhinnamityatra jīvatvajaḍatve'vacchedakau dharmibhūtajīvajaḍeṣvapi sattvāt |
jaḍaṃ jīvādbhinnamityatra tu jīvatvādikamevāvacchedakamiti dharmipratiyogināṃ pratyekaṃ pañcakābhāve'pi tadubhayasamudāyapañcakādbhedapañcakopapattirityāhuḥ |
vibhajya daśavidhatvaṃ tvanumatameva |
dharmitayā pratiyogitayā vā jīvajaḍobhayasambandhī bheda ityādyekīkaraṇasambhavānna pañcatvahāniḥ |
nanu tathā'pi ramāyāḥ pārthakyādīśaramayorjīvaramayorjaḍaramayoścetyaṣṭavidhatvaṃ syāt |
tacca duḥkhāspṛṣṭatvamīśatvamiti tasyā apīśakoṭau praveśa iti vācyam |
tathā satīśayorbhedam iti ṣaḍvidhaḥ syāditi cenna |
asvatantracetanatvaṃ jīvatvamiti vivakṣayā tasyā api jīvaśabdena grahaṇāditi |
ihātmatvaṃ nāma cittvam || nānāvyaktīti || parasparaprayogikadharmisattāsamānasattākānaupādhikakevalabhedādhikaraṇaniṣṭhamityarthaḥ |
evamuttaratrāpi yathāsambhavaṃ jñeyam || jātitvāditi || nanu jātitvaṃ na jātiḥ kintu nityaikānekānugatatvam |
tatrānekānugatatvādityevālam |
tāvanmātre ca sādhyāvaiśiṣṭyam |
maivam |
anekānugatatvaṃ caikatvetarasaṅkhyāvadvartitvam |
sādhyaṃ tu bhedavadvṛttitvamiti bhedāt |
svātantryeṇānugatadhīniyāmakatvaṃ vā jātitvam || vyaktibhedasya bādhitatveneti || ata evānugatatvasya vā'nugatadhīniyāmakatvasya vā'yogāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu tattvavidāṃ jāterekamātravṛttitvena kathaṃ tena nānāvyaktiniṣṭhatvaṃ sādhyate |
kathaṃ ca tānpratyanekānugatatvāsiddhyā parihāra iti cet |
ucyate |
tairapyākāśatvāderajātitvāya jāternānāvyaktiniṣṭhatvamanekānugatatvaṃ vācyam |
parantu yatpadena yadabhivyāhṛtya nānāvyaktiniṣṭhatvamucyate tatpadavācyatāvacchedakadharmeṇa tattatsadṛśānyānyatattaddharmādhikaraṇānyānyatattadvyaktimadhyapatitavyaktiviśeṣaniṣṭhatvameva nānāvyaktiniṣṭhatvam |
anekānugatatvamuktadharmeṇa svasadṛśānekatattaddharmādhikaraṇānekatattadvyaktimadhyapatitavyaktiviśeṣaniṣṭhatvam |
evañcātmatvena dharmeṇaikātmatvasadṛśānāṃ tattadātmatvānāṃ khaṇḍānugatyā'pyabhedalābhānna ko'pidoṣaḥ |
ātmatvapadenetyevoktau sādhyaivakalyaṃ syāditi yattaditi sāmānyāśrayaṇaṃ kṛtam |
ākāśatvasyākaśatvena rūpeṇa sadṛśadharmāntarābhāvānna tasya nānāvyaktiniṣṭhatvamiti |
nanu kimidaṃ sādṛśyam |
tadbhinnatve sati tadgatabhūyodharmavattvamiti cenna |
apasiddhāntāt |
nāpi taddharmasadṛśadharmavattvam |
ātmāśrayāt |
nāpi taddharmanirūpitadharmavattvam |
gauriva gavaya ityādau gogatākārādergavayagatākārādinirūpitatvāditi vācyam |
devadattagatapitṛtvanirūpitatatputratvavati yajñadatte devadattasādṛśyāpatteḥ |
na ca pitṛtvādibhinnavaddharmanirūpitadharmādhikaraṇaṃ yattattena sadṛśamiti vācyam |
ādipadena sādṛśyanirūpakadharmasyāpi grahaṇe'sambhavāpatteḥ |
na ca sambandhibhinnetyādi vācyam |
pitṛtvādivatsādṛśyasyāpyubhayanirūpyatayā sambandhaviśeṣatvāt |
pitṛtvādimātre sambandhatvasya durvacatvācceti |
ucyate |
viśiṣṭapratītyaniyāmakaniṣṭhadharmanirūpitadharmādhikaraṇaṃ yattattena sadṛśam |
pitṛtvādidharmasya putravānityādiviśiṣṭapratītiniyāmakatvānna tatrātivyāptiḥ |
sādṛśyasthale tu gauriva gavaya ityādāvākārāderna viśiṣṭapratītiniyāmakatvam |
gavayavāngaurityādipratityabhāvāt |
na ca sadṛśavāngaurityatrāpi viśiṣṭapratītirastīti vācyam |
tatra parasparapadārthadvayagatasvarūpasambandhaviśeṣasyaiva tanniyāmakatvenākārāderaniyāmakatvāditi |
nanu bhavatāṃ mate jāterakhaṇḍānugatyabhāvena khaṇḍānugateśca rūpādāvapi sattvena lāghavāttenaiva ghaṭādāvanugatapratītyupapattau kiṃ jātyaṅgīkāreṇa |
nanvastvanatiprasaktayatkiñciddharma eva jātipadārtha iti vācyam |
guṇakriyājātipūrvetyādipṛthaggrahaṇa virodhāditi cenna |
na hi vayaṃ prayojanavaśājjātiṃ kalpayāmaḥ |
ghaṭādau guṇādyatiriktā jātiḥ pratyakṣata eva siddhā |
ayaṃ ghaṭa ityatra ghaṭapratītiviṣayabhūtaṃ ghaṭatvaṃ na tāvadrūpādi |
paṭādāvapi sattvena tatrāpi ghaṭatvapratītyāpatteḥ |
nāpi jalādhāratvam |
etadananusandhāne'pi tatpratīteḥ |
hradādāvapi tatpratītyāpatteḥ |
chidraghaṭe tadayogāpatteśca |
nāpi pṛthubudhnodarākāratvam |
citralikhitapratikṛtau peṭikāviśeṣādau ca tatpratītyāpatteḥ |
na ca mṛjjanyatve satīti viśeṣaṇīyam |
suvarṇaghaṭādau tatpatītyayogāpatteḥ |
etadananusandhāne'pi tatpratītidarśanācca |
eteṣāmeva ghaṭapadapravṛttinimittatve uktaśabdānāṃ ghaṭapadena paryāyatvāpatteśca |
yadvā nyāyamatānusāreṇaitadanumānapravṛttirityadoṣaḥ || ātmeti || na ca pratijñā vyāhatiriti vācyam |
ātmanānātvasandehadaśāyāṃ tadabhāvāt |
asiddhiṃ darśayati -- ātmana iti || na ca tarhi dravyatvaṃ nāma samavāyikāraṇatvamiti vācyam |
avikriyasya niṣkriyasyātmano guṇaṃ pratyeva tasya vaktavyatvāt |
nāpi guṇādiṣaṭkabhinnatvaṃ dravyatvamiti vācyam |
bhinnatvasampādakasya guṇāśrayatvasyābhāvena tadvacanasya riktatvāt |
na ca tarhi dravyatvameva heturastviti vācyam |
ātmapratiyogikabhede vyabhicārāt |
svasyaiva svavṛttitvāyogena sādhyābhāvasattvāt |
pakṣadṛṣṭāntānugatavastutvasyānaṅgīkārācca |
|| sukhumiti || asyātmāśritatvena pakṣadharmatābalādātmanānātvasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
nanvatraikātmasukhapakṣīkāre bādhaḥ |
anekātmasukhapakṣīkāre pakṣaviśeṣaṇāsiddhiḥ |
sāmānyataḥ sukhapakṣīkāre'pi bādha eva |
yāvadviśeṣavṛttinaḥ sāmānye'pyabhāvaucityāt |
ataḥ khaṇḍānugatyā samādhānasambhave'pi tuṣyatviti jāterakhaṇḍānugatimabhyupetya prakārāntareṇa prayuṅkte || sukhatvamiti || evañcaikaikatra vidyamānatattatsukhānugatasukhatvasya nānāvyaktyāśritāśritatvaṃ sūpapādamiti bhāvaḥ || śabde śabdatveti || yathāsaṅkhyamuktahetudvayasyeti śeṣaḥ |
tadāśrayasyākāśasyaikatvāditi bhāvaḥ || dravyatvāditi || varṇabhāgasyeti śeṣaḥ |
ataḥ śabdatvasya dravyaniṣṭhatvādityapi grāhyam || adravyatveti || dhvanibhāgasyeti śeṣaḥ |
dravyatvādityekadeśavivakṣayā prauḍhavādaḥ || tadanyatveneti || śabdamātrasyāpi tathātvāṅgīkāra ityapi draṣṭavyam || caitraśarīramiti || yo yadviruddhaceṣṭāśrayaḥ sa tatsvāmibhinnasvāmiko yathā caitraghaṭo maitraghaṭasvāmibhinnasvāmika ityudāhartavyam |
nanu śarīravadghaṭapaṭayorapi tatsvāmibhinnasvāmikatvāsiddhyā sādhyavikalo'yam |
na ca vyāvahārikabhedo'stīti vācyam |
tathātve tenaiva siddhasādhyatāpatteḥ |
na cātra vyatirekadṛṣṭānta evodāhartavya iti ( udāhriyatāmiti ) vācyam |
dṛṣṭāntābhāvādityuttaragranthavirodhāditi cenna |
sāmānyato bhinnasvāmikatvasyaiva sādhyatvena sādhyavaikalyābhāvāt |
prakṛte ca śarīropahitayoreva śarīrasvāmitve ātmāśrayādanupahitabhedasya svābhāvikatvāvaśyambhāvena pakṣa eva paryavasāne pāramārthikabhedasyaiva lābhānna siddhasādhanatā |
na cāntaḥkaraṇopahitayoreva śarīrasvāmikatvamiti śuddhātmabhedālābha iti vācyam |
tatrāpi caitrāntaḥkaraṇaṃ maitrāntaḥkaraṇasvāmibhinnasvāmikamiti sādhanāt |
na cānyopahitayorevāntaḥkaraṇasvāmitvamiti vācyam |
tadupādherapi bhinnopādheyakatvasādhanāt |
śarīrapakṣīkārastu tasyaivādyopādhitvāṅgīkāra iti dhyeyam |
upādhyānantyakalpane cānavasthā |
śarīropalakṣitayoreva śarīrasvāmitvānna śuddhātmabhedo nāpyātmāśraya iti cenna |
upalakṣakasya taṭasthatve upalakṣyaitatsamārthitayatkiñcidviśeṣo vācyaḥ |
yathā kākasya tāṭasthye'pi tatsamārthito tṛṇatvatatsaṃyogadhvaṃsādiviśiṣṭasyaiva gṛhasyopalakṣakam |
evañca tādṛśaviśeṣapakṣīkāreṇa bhinnāśrayatvasādhane śuddhātmabhedasiddhiḥ |
na ca sa viśeṣo'pyupalakṣakatvānna śuddhāśraya iti vācyam |
anavasthāpatteriti |
vyabhicāraṃ vyanakti || sakaleti || sakalā eke bhinnāḥ svāmino yayostau tayorye ghaṭasvāminaste sakalā api paṭasya svāmina ityevamabhinnasvāmikayorityarthaḥ |
avāntarasvāmibhedādinā'pi rahitayoriti |
ekasvāmikayorityevokte pradhānasvāmyaikyai'pyavāntarasvāmibhedānna vyabhicāra ityucyeteti sakaletyuktam || dṛṣṭāntābhāvāditi || śarīramātrasya pakṣatulyatvamiti bhāvaḥ || aprasiddheti || asyādūṣaṇatve'pi svarītyā'bhidhānamiti jñeyam || pāramārthiketi || pāramārthikatvaviśeṣaṇāghaṭitabhedamātretyarthaḥ |
|| ātmatvamiti || nanu jātitvaṃ nāma nityaikatve satyanekasamavetatvam |
tacca nānāvyaktiniṣṭhatvasandehadaśāyāmātmatve sandigdhaṃ niścaye cānumanavaiphalyam |
nityaikatve satītyasya vaiyyarthyācceti cenna |
jātitvasyākhaṇḍopādhitvāt |
vyaktyabhedādibādhakaṣaṭkarahitadharmatvaṃ vā tat |
na cātrāpi vyaktyabhedarāhityamātrasyaiva gamakatve viśeṣavaiyyarthyamiti vācyam |
akhaṇḍābhāvaghaṭakatayā'vaiyyarthyāt |
tāvanmātrasyaiva prakṛte hetutvācca |
na ca tatrāpi sandigdhāsiddhiḥ siddhasādhanatā veti vācyam |
asyaivāgre'bhidhānāt || dhaṭavaditīti |
yadyapi paro ghaṭe'pyātmapratiyogikabhedādhikaraṇatvaṃ na manyata iti dṛṣṭāntaḥ sādhyavikalaḥ |
tathā'pi ghaṭo nāhamiti nirbādhapratyakṣeṇa ghaṭa iva tatsiddhirityāśayenedam |
yadvā prasiddhe'rityasya dṛṣṭānte sādhyāsiddherityapyarthamaṅgīkṛtyedamapi dūṣaṇamuttaratra vakṣyamāṇameveti bodhyam || ātmana iti || yadyapi nirguṇasyātmano guṇāśrayatvarūpadravyatvavattāyoge'pi jātirūpaṃ tadyujyate |
tathā'pi nirdharmake taddravyatvaṃ na sambhavati |
guṇāśrayatvasya tadvyāpakatvāt |
tannivṛttau tadapi na sambhavatītyāśayenedam |
ekasya nānāvyaktyāśritatvaṃ bādhitam |
evaṃ dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyamityasvarasādāha -- sukhatvamiti || yadyapyatraikasyāpi sukhasya rūpasya vā jātiparyāptyā nānātṛṇāgraprasāritapaṭavadaparyāptyā vā na nānāvyaktyāśritatvaṃ bādhitam |
evaṃ rūpasyāpīti dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyamityasvarasādāha -- sukhatvamiti || yadyapyatrāpi kasyāpi sukhasya rūpasya vā jātivatparyāptyā nānātṛṇāgraprasāritaṃ paṭavadaparyāptyā vā na nānāvyaktyāśritatvamiti punarapi bādho dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyaṃ ca |
tathā'pi svāśrayadvārakasambandhena vyaktyāśritatvaṃ sukhatve sādhyamiti na kaścitkṣudropadravaḥ || guṇaniṣṭheti || tadrūpatve vyabhicāravāraṇāya jātitvāditi |
manastve vyabhicāravāraṇāya guṇaniṣṭheti |
śabda ityādyānumāne śabdatva ityuttarānumāne tadanyatveneti |
śabdānyatvenādyānumāne śabdatvānyatvena dvitīye |
ādyānumāne sukhamantaḥkaraṇavṛttirūpaṃ svarūpasukhaṃ vā |
dvitīye'ntaḥkaraṇavṛttidharmo vā svarūpasukhavṛttidharmo vā pakṣatvena vivakṣitamiti vikalpaṃ manasi nidhāyādyayorāha -- sukhaduḥkhādīnāmiti || duḥkhagrahaṇaṃ dṛṣṭāntārtham |
duḥkhapakṣakānumāne'pi evameva dūṣaṇaṃ bodhyamityāśayena ca |
antyayorāha -- svarūpeti || dvitīyānumāne svarūpasukhaikyavādinastadvṛttidharmasya jātitvābhāvenāpyasiddhirbodhyā |
tadviruddhetyatra tatpadena caitraśarīraceṣṭā gṛhyate |
tacceṣṭāviruddhaceṣṭākatvāditi pāṭhaḥ sādhuḥ || sakalaikasvāmikayoriti || ceṣṭāśabdena spandanamātraṃ kathyata ityāśayaḥ |
ata eva śarīratvena viśeṣaṇena hetuśaṅkā'pi yujyate |
atra sakalapadaṃ prāmādikam |
ekasvāmikayorityanena sādhyābhāvasya viruddhaceṣṭādarśanādityanena hetusadbhāvasyopapādanāt |
yadvā yayorghaṭapaṭayormukhyaḥ svāmyeko'vāntarasvāminau ca pratyekaṃ dvau tayorghaṭapaṭayoḥ sādhyamapyastīti na vyabhicāraḥ syādataḥ sakaleti |
sakalo mukhyo'vāntaro vā eka eva svāmī yayoriti || dṛṣṭāntābhāva iti || ātmaikyavādino yajñadattādiśarīre'pi maitraśarīrasvāmibhinnasvāmikatvasyāsampratipatteḥ |
viśeṣavyāptau śarīratvaviśeṣitasya hetordṛṣṭāntābhāva ityarthaḥ |
evaṃ sāmānyavyāptāvapi śarīratvaviśeṣitahetoddraṣṭāntābhāvo boddhavyaḥ |
evamaviśoṣitahetorapi vyāptidvaye'pi dṛṣṭāntābhāvo bodhyaḥ |
evañca śarīratvena hetuviśeṣaṇe vyabhicāravāraṇe'pi dṛṣṭāntābhāvo duruddhara eveti granthārthaḥ |
natvaviśeṣaṇe dṛṣṭāntasattvaṃ viśeṣaṇe dṛṣṭāntābhāva iti bhramitavyam || viruddhadharmādhikaraṇatvāditi || atra virodho vadhyaghātukabhāvaścen `na jāta eko'nyaṃ hanti nāpyanyādhāra' iti nyāyena samānāśrayayorapi jñānecchayorvadhyaghātukabhāvenānaikāntyam |
pratiyogyanuyogibhāvaścetsamānāśrayayorapi jñānatatprāgabhāvayostatsambhavenānaikāntyam |
sahānavasthānaniyamaścedekātmavāde'siddhiriti dūṣaṇe satyeva dūṣaṇāntaramāha -- pāramārthiketi ||
|| ātmatvamiti || ātmetarāvṛttitvamātraṃ pakṣatāvacchedakaṃ na sakalātmavṛttitvamapi |
ātmabahutvasyāsiddhatvena prativādina āśrayāsiddhiprasaṅgāt |
uktapakṣatāvacchedakānusāreṇa tu sāmānādhikaraṇyena sādhyasiddhimātreṇātmanānātvasiddhyākṛtārthatvenāvacchedakāvacchedena sādhyasiddheranapekṣaṇāt ātmetarāvṛttitvarūpoktapakṣatāvacchedakākrāntasukhaduḥkhādau hetusādhyayorabhāve'pi na bhāgāsiddhyaṃśebādhau |
nikhilātmavṛttitvaṃ vā''tmavṛttibhedapratiyogitāvacchedakatvābhāvavattve sati vṛttimattvarūpaṃ (vā) pakṣatāvacchedakamato na pakṣāsiddhiḥ |
dravyatvādikamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāyāvacchedakāvacchedena sādhyaṃ sādhanīyam |
avacchedakāvacchedena sādhyasiddheruddeśyatve'pyaṃśe siddhatādoṣa iti matāśrayaṇe ātmetarāvṛttitvamapi pakṣatāvacchedakakoṭau niveśyam |
nanvetatpakṣe sakalātmavṛttitvamātrasya pakṣatāvacchedakatvapakṣe vā sakalātmavṛttibahutvasyāpi pakṣakoṭiniviṣṭatvātpariṣkriyamāṇajātitvasyāpyasambhavāt bhāgāsiddhiḥ syāt |
svamate tasya bahutvasya siddhatvādviṣayatāsambandhāvacchinnātmatvavṛttyavacchedakatākabhedasyātmani sattvena tatsaṅgrahāya bhedapratiyogitāvacchedakatāyāṃ samavāyasambandhāvacchinnatvasyāvaśyakatvenoktabahutvasyāvāraṇāditi cenna |
ātmaniṣṭhasamavāyasambandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākātyantābhāvāpratiyogitvasyaiva sakalātmavṛttitvapadārthatvena bahutvasya vāraṇāt |
svotpattipūrvakṣaṇe tadatyantābhāvasyāpyātmani sattvāt dhvaṃsaprāgabhāvayoratyantābhāvavirodhāt || nānāvyaktiniṣṭhamiti || svapratiyogivṛttitvasvānuyogivṛttitvobhayasambandhena bhedaviśiṣṭamiti vā svavyāpakatvasambandhena dvitvaviśiṣṭamiti vā'rthaḥ |
tena dvitvāśrayavṛttitvasya vā bhedavadvṛttitvasya vā sādhyatve ghaṭātmadvitvādikaṃ ghaṭādibhedamātmaniṣṭhaṃ cādāyātmaikye'pyātmatve sādhyasambhavena siddhasādhanamarthāntaratā veti nirastam || jātitvāditi || anavacchinnasamavāyasambandhāvacchinnaprakāratāśālitvādityarthaḥ |
na tu nityatve satyanekasamavetatvādityarthaḥ |
anekavṛttitvasyaivedānīṃ sādhyatvena hetuśarīre'dhikapraveśe'pi sādhyāvaiśiṣṭyāparihārāt |
na ca sādhyāvaiśiṣṭyaṃ pakṣe sādhyasandehadaśāyāṃ hetau sandigdhāsiddhatvaparyavasāyi, taddoṣaṃ ca jātitvāsiddheriti svayameva vakṣyatīti nedānīṃ tadanusandheyamiti vācyam |
ādāvatinirabhiprāyakaśaṅkāyā evānutthānāt niścitāsiddhereva svamatamālambya uttaratra vakṣyamāṇatayā sandigdhāsiddherakathanācca |
nāpi jātitvena vyavahāraviṣayatvaṃ tadarthaḥ |
asaduttare'pi(reṣvapi) tathā tāntrikāṇāṃ vyavahāreṇa tatra vyabhicārānistārāt |
nāpi mukhyatayā tathātvam |
kośādyabhāvena jātipadamatra mukhyaṃ tatrāmukhyamiti nirṇayāyogāt |
nāpi vyaktyabhedādibādhakaṣaṭkarahitatve sati dharmatvaṃ tadarthaḥ |
gagane vyabhicāravāraṇāya dharmatvamitīti, gaganatvādau bādhakaṣaṭkasyāpyabhāvena tatra vyabhicārāt |
nāpi bādhakābhāvaṣaṭkasya(ṣaṭkavattvasya) hetutvamātmaikyavādina ekavyaktitvābhāvasyāsiddhyā taddhaṭitaṣaṭkasyāpyasiddhyanistārāt |
asmaduktārthe'ntyaviśeṣeṣu svāṃśe tādātmyenānavacchinnaprakāratāśāliṣu ghaṭarūpaniṣṭhatadvyaktitvādīnāmakhaṇḍatayā svarūpasambandhe(saṃsargeṇa) svarūpato bhānapakṣe teṣu (ca) vyabhicāravāraṇāya prakāratāyāṃ samavāyasambandhāvacchinnatvasya niveśaḥ |
uktahetuśca ghaṭa ityanubhavabalādghaṭa ivātmetyanubhavabalādātmatve(balādevātmatve) uktasādhyasandehadaśāyāmapi siddha iti śaṅkakābhimānaḥ |
samavāyasaṃsargeṇa tadanubhavāpalāpenottaratrāsiddhiriti dhyeyam || ātmana iti || ātmano nirguṇatveneti pāṭhaḥ |
nirguṇatvamātmano vyaktibhedasya bādhitatve hetutvenoktam |
nirguṇe parasparavyāvartakadharmarūpaguṇābhāvena parasparabhedo na ghaṭate |
anyathā ghaṭaḥ svasmādbhidyeteti bhāvaḥ |
nānāvyaktibhedasya bādhitatvenetyatra nāneti prāmādikaṃ jātitvānaṅgīkārāt |
samavetatvānaṅgīkārāt |
tadrūpatvādāviva tasya samavāyāvacchinnānavacchinnaprakāratā na siddheti bhāvaḥ |
|| ātmapratiyogiketi || vyāsajyavṛttidharmāvacchinnabhedastadāśraye nāstīti pratiyogyavṛttitvaṃ vā bhede viśeṣaṇaṃ deyam |
atastādṛśaṃ ghaṭatmobhayabhedamādāya na siddhasādhanateti bhāvaḥ || ghaṭavaditi || ātmanyātmabhedasādhanavelāyāṃ tanmātre vivādo nānyatrāto ghaṭavaditi dṛṣṭāntasaṅgatiḥ |
anyathā siddhāntagranthasthānāmanumānānāṃ prāyaśo bhedaghaṭitatvādanutthitiriti bhāvaḥ |
guṇāśrayatvarūpadravyatvasya tajjñāpyadravyatvasya cāsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
vikalpapūrvakamubhayavidhadravyatvarūpahetudūṣaṇaparatvādetatphakkikāsthalakṣaṇapadaṃ svarūpajñāpakobhayārtha(ka)tvenāpi yojyam || sukhamiti || atra nānāvyaktitvarūpayathāśrutasādhyasya pūrvoktarītyā siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya pūrvoktaniṣkarṣakaraṇe kasmiṃścidapi rūpe sukhe cābhāvena dṛṣṭāntāsiddhirbādhaśca syātāmiti na śaṅkyam |
jagati nīlapītādaya ekaikāḥ sāmagrībalāttattadvyaktiṣu sambadhyante viyujyante ca, tannyāyaḥ sukhādāvapīti matamāśrityaitadanumānapravṛttiḥ |
yadvā'tra nānāvṛttitvaṃ nāma yatkiñciddvitvavyāpakatāvacchedakaguṇatvanyūnavṛttijātimattvarūpaṃ sukhatvajātimādāya sukhe sādhyamupapādanīyam |
rūpe ca rūpatvamādāyedaṃ cātmabhedaṃ vinā na paryavasyatītyātmabhedasiddhiḥ |
guṇatvasatte ādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya guṇatvanyūnavṛttīti |
lāghavamanusṛtyānumānāntaramāha -- sukhatvamiti || svasamavāyisamavetatvasambandhena yatkiñciddvitvavyāpakamityarthaḥ sādhyapadasyāto na kaścitkṣudropadravaḥ |
manastve vyabhicāravāraṇāya guṇaniṣṭheti |
jātipadaṃ samavetamātrārthakam || tadanyatveneti || ādyānumāne śabdānyatvena dvitīyānumāne śabdatvādanyatvenetyarthaḥ |
yadyapyevamapi katvādiniṣṭhavyabhicāro durvārastathā'pi śabdavṛttibhinnatve tātparyānna kaściddoṣaḥ |
śabdānyaguṇasamavetatvāditi paryavasito heturitarāṃśasya vaiyyarthyāt |
kimantaḥkaraṇarūpaṃ sukhaṃ pakṣaḥ utātmarūpaṃ sukhamiti svamate vikalpaṃhṛdi kṛtvā kramāddūṣayati -- sukhamiti || guṇatvenānenāsiddheriti || guṇatvaprakārakasiddhyaviṣayatvādityarthaḥ |
tathā ca guṇatvarūpahetustatrāsiddha iti bhāvaḥ |
tasyāguṇatvenāsiddheriti pāṭhaḥ sugamaḥ |
|| caitraśarīramiti || nanu cetanasyaiva kiñcitsvāmitvena caitanyaikyavādināṃ caitraśarīrasvāmibhinnasvāmighaṭitasādhyāprasiddhiḥ |
na ca vyāvahārikabhedamādāya tatprasiddhiriti vācyam |
hanta tarhi tata eva siddhasādhanatāpattiḥ |
tadvāraṇāya bhede pāramārthikatvaviśeṣaṇadāne punaraprasiddhaviśeṣaṇateti cenna |
caitramaitrapiṇḍayorbhedadarśane'pyantargatacetanānāṃ bhedādarśanena tatra pāramārthikatvāviśeṣitabhedamātre vivādavelāyāmuktānumānapravṛtterna pāramārthikatvaghaṭanāprayuktasādhyā(pra)siddhiḥ |
na ca tathāpyaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatokteti vācyam |
svabhinnasvāmikatvasambandhena maitraśarīrasvāmina eva sādhyatvātsambandhakoṭau svatvasya paricāyakatayā bhinnasvāmikatvamātrasya ghaṭādisambandhatayā prasiddhatvāt || tadviruddhaceṣṭāvattvāditi || tacceṣṭāviruddhaceṣṭāvattvāditi tadarthaḥ |
nanvatra viruddhatvaṃ tadadhikaraṇāvṛttitvam |
caitrīyabālyayauvanakālīnayoḥ śarīrayoḥ parasparaṃ bhinnatvena parasparaviruddhaceṣṭāvattvāduktasādhyābhāvādvyabhicāraḥ |
pariṇāmavādamavalambya tayorabhedāṅgīkāre'pi pūrvāparajanmīyaśarīrāṇyādāya vyabhicāraḥ |
caitraśarīratvarūpa sāmānyadharmapuraskāreṇa hetuśarīraghaṭakatve cidaikyavādināṃ caitraśarīrasyāpi maitraśarīratvena taddhaṭitahetvasiddhyāpatteḥ |
tadatiriktaṃ tu prakṛtopayogi na nirvacanārham |
kiñca taddehāvṛttimattvamātraṃ heturastu |
vyabhicārastu na śaṅkārhaḥ |
ceṣṭātvena tasyā niveśe'pi vyabhicārasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt tasyedānīmananusandheyatvāditi cenna |
maitraśarīrasya paridṛśyamānaśarīratvenaiva niveśo na tu sāmānyarūpeṇāto na prativādyasiddhiḥ |
paridṛśyamānaśarīraceṣṭākāla eva tadadhikaraṇāvṛtticeṣṭāvattvaṃ hetustato na pūrvajanmaśarīrabālyaśarīrayauvanāvasthāpannaśarīrāṇyādāya vyabhicāraḥ |
ayogina eva maitrasya hetuśarīraniviṣṭatvādyogyavasthāpannatadīyaikakālikaśarīradvayakriyopādānena na vyabhicāraḥ |
ceṣṭātvenāniveśe tadvṛttidharmādhikaraṇavṛttidharmatvena niveśe'siddhiḥ |
taccharīra(vṛtti)prameyatvagaganābhāvādyāśrayatvasya caitraśarīre sattvāccaitraśarīrakriyāyāstatra sattvāt |
tadavṛttimātrasya hetutve maitraśarīrāvṛttirūpādiśāliṣu bahuṣu maitravasanādiṣu sphuṭataravyabhicāravato hetūkaraṇāyogena ceṣṭātvena tanniveśaḥ |
tathātve śarīradvārā prayatnasādhyakriyāsveva tādṛśaceṣṭātvaṃ vartate |
tāśca śarīrātirikteṣu na santyeva |
śarīrāntarāṇi tu pakṣatulyānīti na kutrāpi vyabhicāra iti anumānaprayokturabhiprāyaḥ |
tadavṛtticeṣṭāvattvamātraniveśe heturaprayojakaḥ syāt |
etaccharīrakriyāyā api tadātmanā niṣpādayituṃ śakyatvādityāśaṅkā syādatastatkāla eva tacceṣṭādhikaraṇāvṛttitvarūpavirodhasya hetubhūtaceṣṭāyāṃ niveśaḥ |
viruddhakriyayorekadaikaprayatnenotpādāyogādātmani yugapajjīvanayoniyatnaṃ vinā viśeṣaguṇadvayānutpāde tatkriyānukūlaprayatnāśrayo'nyo jīvātmā sidhyati |
īśvaraprayatnasya sādhāraṇyānna tamādāya paryavasyatyato na jīvabhedarūpoddeśyāsiddhirityanumānaṃ kartuṃ hṛdayaṃ |
bhavatu bhavadabhimataśarīrakriyaiva tathā'pi sā kena rūpeṇa hetuśarīre niveśyate |
vaijātyena tanniveśe mānābhāvaḥ |
kriyātvena niveśe ca yatkiñcitkriyāviruddhakriyāvattvaṃ yatra tatsvāmibhinnasvāmikatvaṃ tatreti sāmānyavyāptiḥ prakṛte'bhimatā |
athavā maitraśarīravṛttikriyāviruddhakriyāvattvaṃ yatra tatra tatsvāmibhinnasvāmikatvamiti viśeṣavyāptiḥ |
nobhayamapi sambhavati vyabhicārādityāha -- atreti || vyabhicāramupapādayati -- sakalaiketi || sakalasya ekasvāmī yayoriti vigrahaḥ |
ekapadaṃ mukhyasvāmiparam |
tathā ceśvaramātrasvāmikatayā matadvayasiddhayorityarthaḥ paryavasyati |
na ca viśeṣavyāptau kathaṃ vyabhicārastatreti vācyam |
paramate sarvaṃ pratyapyekajīvasyaiva svāmitvād bhinnasvāminaḥ kasyāpyabhāvāt vyabhicārajñānābhāvaḥ |
nanvaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatāvāraṇāya svabhinna svāmikatvasambandhena maitraśarīrasvāminaḥ sādhyatvāṅgīkārānna viśeṣavyāptau vyabhicāra īśvararūpasvāminamādāya sādhyopapādanasambhavāditi cenna |
parasyaikajīvavādino mate īśvarasyāpi svapnadṛṣṭarājādivatsvarūpata eva kalpitatvena tanmatarītyaiva viśeṣavyāptau vyabhicārasyābhimatatvāt |
dṛṣṭāntābhāvastu paramatarītyā kriyātvena niveśāt sāmānyavyāptau viśeṣavyāptau vā śarīravṛttikriyātvena niveśe vā duruddhara eva |
svamate ca yathākathañcidīśvararūpasvāminamādāya dṛṣṭāntaḥ sambhavatītyāśayenaitadanumānasya śaṅkāviṣayatā sambhavati |
anyathā svamate'pi kathamapi dṛṣṭāntarahitasya nirdalatayā śaṅkā'narhataiva syāt |
tarhīśvararūpasvāminamādāya siddhasādhanatākavalitatvātkathamasya śaṅkāgocaratvamiti vācyam |
viruddhaceṣṭā(dvaya)janakāsādhāraṇaprayatnayoryugapadekātmanyutpattyasambhavarūpātpūrvoktarītyetarabādharūpapakṣadharmatābalājjīvamādāya paryavasyati |
ato jīvamādāya noddeśyāsiddhirityāśayāccharīrakriyātvena hetutve'pīśvararūpasvāminamādāya vā caitraśarīramātrasya pakṣatāvelāyāṃ ḍitthādiśarīreṣu sādhyaṃ sūpapādamityāśayena vā'numānasya śaṅkāpathārohitatvam |
vādinā tvīśvarānaṅgīkārāccharīrāntareṣvapyuktasādhyaniścaye bījābhāvācca dṛṣṭāntābhāvaḥ sthira evetyāśayena tasyaiva dṛṣṭāntatayā kathanamiti na ko'pi doṣaḥ |
na caivamanumānaśaṅkakasya sādhyaprasiddhāvapi svarītyā bhramasambhavena yathāśrutasādhyanirdeśasambhavāttatra bhavaduktasādhyapariṣkāro'kiñcitkara iti vācyam |
sphuṭatarapratijñādoṣaṃ prathamameva vaktavyaṃ vihāya vyabhicārādikathanasyātyantasandarbhaviruddhatvena sādhyasyāsmaduktaparyavasānaparyantaṃ granthakārābhimatatvāt |
athavā'numāne īśvare siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya svāmipadena svaniṣṭhasvatvanirūpakatvaprayuktasukhaduḥkhānyataravattvarūpasvāmitvāvacchinnameva sādhye tatsambandhe ca ghaṭakaṃ dṛṣṭāntāsiddhistu śarīrāntareṣu jīvāntaraṃ vartata iti svamatamātrāvalambanatvabhrameṇa |
tathātve ceśvaramātraniyamyaghaṭādau svamate('pi) viśeṣavyāptervyabhicāro draṣṭavyaḥ |
athavā'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatāvāraṇāya caitraśarīre svasvāmikatvarūpapakṣaviśeṣaṇamupādāya maitraśarīrasvāminiṣṭhasvāmitānirūpakatvābhāva eva sādhyaḥ |
pakṣe sādhyaṃ ca niviṣṭaṃ svāmitvamīśvaravyāvṛttamuktarūpaṃ grāhyam |
īśvarasvāmikatvena paryavasānavāraṇāya bādhavāraṇāya ca pakṣe tadviśeṣaṇānupādāne niḥsvāmikatvena paryavasānāpattyā'rthāntaratā syādatastanniveśaḥ |
granthoktasādhyaṃ tūktarītyā pakṣaviśeṣaṇajñānarūpasāmānyajñāna sahakṛtetaraviśeṣabādhagrahātmakaparāmarśādhīnānumitiviṣayasādhyaparyavasānamādāya |
uktaṃ caitatpratyakṣamaṇau `bahusādhyakānumāne vahnivyāpyavadviśeṣyakatvāvacchinnavahnitvaprakārakavyāpakadāhājanakatvavadviśeṣyakatvāvacchinnavahnitvaprakārakatvasya bādhagraharūpā vahnitvābhāvavatpurovṛttiviśeṣyakatvāvacchinnavahnitvaprakārakatvābhāvasya vyatirekimudrayā'numitisāmagrīvyāpakatvāvacchedakatvenāgṛhītenāpi vahnitvavadviśeṣyakatvāvacchinnavahnitvaprakārakatvena prāmāṇyaṃ gṛhṇāti |
ekaviśeṣabādhagrahasahitasāmānyānumitisāmagryā aparaviśeṣānumitijananena sa yathā sidhyati tadvatsāmānyavattājñānasahitaikaviśeṣābhāvānumitisāmagryā api vyāpakatvenāgṛhīto'pyaparaviśeṣaḥ sidhyati |
viśeṣāntarasaṃśayānutpādasyobhayasthale'pyanubhavasiddhatvā'diti |
idaṃ ca dīdhityādyuktaṃ sarvamanūditaṃ jñeyam |
itthaṃ ca caitraśarīravaccharīrāntare'pi vipratipattisambhavātteṣāṃ dṛṣṭāntatvāyogena ceṣṭāpadena kiṃ kriyāmātraṃ vivakṣyate |
tathātve cāraṇyasthānānāṃ maitrādyasvāmikānāmadvaitamate'pi dṛṣṭāntatvāsambhavāt |
kiṃ vā śarīravṛttitvaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ niveśya veti vikalpau hṛdi nidhāyādye sāmānyavyāptau viśeṣavyāptau ca caitramaitrādisvāmikaghaṭapaṭādāviva vyabhicāraḥ svamatenaikajīvavādinoktaḥ sakalaikasvāmikayoriti |
tatra hetugarbhaviśeṣaṇamanumānadūṣakeṇa dattamanumānaprayoktṛmatenāpi sāmānyavyāptāvīśvaraikasvāmikaghaṭādau vyabhicārastannirūpitaduḥkhitvaghaṭitoktasādhya(śarīra)niviṣṭasvāmitāvato'prasiddheḥ |
viśeṣavyāptau ca maitraśarīrakriyāviruddhakriyāvati maitrasvāmike ghaṭa eva vyabhicāra uktavākyenopapāditaprāya eva |
dvitīyakalpānusaraṇe ca śarīravṛttikriyāyāḥ śarīrātirikteṣvabhāvāditaraśarīrāṇāṃ pakṣatulyatvānna vyabhicāraḥ kintu dṛṣṭāntāsiddhiḥ |
kriyāmātraniveśe ca vyabhicāra eva na dṛṣṭāntāsiddhiriti na pūrvottaragranthasandarbhavirodha iti || śarīratveneti || śarīratve satīti hetuviśeṣaṇa ityarthaḥ |
viśeṣyāṃśānupādāne maitraśarīra eva vyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ |
kvacitpustake ekasvāmikayoriti pāṭho dṛśyate |
sa ca sugamaḥ |
|| vimatāviti || nanūbhayorbhedasiddheḥ prāk kathaṃ dvitvena pakṣatā ? prativādinaḥ pakṣāprasiddhyāpātāditi cenna |
(caitra)maitraśarīravṛttidvitvasya pratyakṣasiddhatvena svāśrayāvacchinnabhogavattvasambandhena pakṣatāvacchedakatvānusaraṇāt |
tasya cobhayaśarīrāvacchedenaika evātmeti mate'pi bādhitatvābhāvāt || viruddheti || viruddhatvaṃ ca naikādhikaraṇāvṛttitvaṃ bhedasiddheḥ prāk hetorubhayasiddhatvānupapatteḥ |
kintu parasparābhāvātmakatvaṃ parasparābhāvavyāpyatvaṃ vā |
tacca sukhaviśeṣatadabhāvaduḥkhādīnāṃ laukikapratītibalātpareṇāpyaṅgīkṛtam |
anyathā (sarva)laukikavyavahārocchedaprasaṅgāt |
tasya ca nāsiddhirmatadvaye'pīti bhāvaḥ || apāramārthiketi || pāramārthikatvaviśeṣaṇaṃ parityajya bhedatvasāmānyarūpeṇa sādhyatve vyāvahārikatvaghaṭitarūpeṇa sādhyatve cetyarthaḥ |
bhedasādhakasakalānumānasādhāraṇyajñāpanāyānumānadūṣaṇāvasāna eva (tad)dūṣaṇoktiriti dhyeyam |
na caivaṃ sati pūrvānumāne sādhyapariṣkārasyātrānumāne pakṣapariṣkārasya bhavatkṛtasya vaiyyarthyamuktadūṣaṇā(na)nusandhānenaivāpātatastaduktisambhavāditi vācyam |
tathā sati pūrvānumāne sādhyaprasiddhimātreṇānumāne pakṣaprasiddhimātreṇa kṛtārthatayā'numānapravṛttereva vaiyyarthyāpattyoktarītyā pariṣkārasyāvaśyakatvāt |
taduttaraṃ tvantaḥkaraṇa eva duḥkhādisattvānnirdharmikātmani hetvasiddhi(riti )dūṣaṇasyaikajīvavādabahujīvavādobhayarītyā sphuṭatvāttadanuktvā bahujīvavādamatenedaṃ dūṣaṇam |
cetanabhedajaḍabhedasādhakasarvānumānasādhāraṇamucyate |
kathañciccetanabhedasādhanamātreṇa tanmātraviṣaye ekajīvavādanirāse'pi na bhavadabhimataviśvabhinnatvasiddhiriti jñāpanāyeti sarvaṃ samañjasam |
yadyapyādyapadyoktamīśvaradharmikaviśvapratiyogikabhedasādhakānumānamevādau pratikṣepyam |
tathāpi sāmānyataścetanabhedasādhakānumānasyābhāsatve viśeṣatastatsādhakasya sutarāmābhāsatvamityetatsūcanāya sāmānyataścetanabhedasādhakānumānāni tāvaddūṣayati -- ātmatvamiti || ātmapadapravṛttinimittamityarthaḥ |
tenātmatvasya jātirūpasya pakṣatve pakṣāprasiddhiḥ |
tadvyaktitvarūpasya tathātve ca bādha iti śaṅkānavakāśaḥ || nānāvyaktiniṣṭhamiti || anekasamavetamityarthaḥ |
svāśrayapratiyogikabhedavadvṛttīti yāvat |
na caivamātmatvasyātmaghaṭobhayasamavetatvasiddhyā'rthāntaramiti vācyam |
ātmatve ghaṭādisamavetatvasya bādhitatvenāsiddheḥ |
siddhau vā'tmatvāśrayanānātvasiddhyoddeśyasiddheḥ |
etacca tārkikarītyā |
siddhānte jāterananugatatvāditi dhyeyam |
vastuto niruktabhedavadvṛttitvaṃ tādṛśabhedavadvṛttibhedapratiyogitāyāṃ svarūpato'navacchedakatvam |
ātmatvasya ca prativyaktibhinnatve'pyayamātmā nātmetyādipratītyabhāvena svarūpatastattadātmavṛttibhedapratiyogitānavacchedakatvānna doṣaḥ |
svarūpata iti viśeṣaṇāttattadvyaktitvenātmatvasyātmāntaravṛttibhedapratiyogitāvacchedakatve'pi na kṣatiḥ |
evamagre'pyūhyam |
hetūkṛtaṃ jātitvaṃ ca na nityānekasamavetatvam |
nityatvavaiyarthyāt |
sādhyāviśiṣṭatvācca |
kiṃ tvakhaṇḍopādhirjātipadavācyatvaṃ veti dhyeyam || ityasyāsiddheriti || iti nyāyapratipādyahetorasiddherityarthaḥ |
vyaktibhedasya svābhāvikātmabhedasya |
gaganatvāderjātitvavāraṇāya svābhāvikavyaktibhedasya jātiniyāmakatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
idamupalakṣaṇam |
sādhyaśarīre pāramārthikatvaviśeṣitabhedaniveśe sādhyāprasiddhiḥ |
tadaviśeṣitabhedaniveśe cāpāramārthikabhedamādāya siddhasādhanamityapi bodhyam |
|| ātmeti || atra ghaṭādibhedasiddhyā'rthāntaravāraṇāyā'tyamapratiyogiketi || ātmatvavyāpyarūpāvacchinnapratiyogitāketyarthaḥ |
tenātmaghaṭobhayabhedamādāya nārthāntaram |
ātmapratiyogikapratiyogyavṛttibhedaḥ sādhya ityapyāhuḥ |
yadyapyatra prameyatvāderapi hetutvaṃ sambhavati |
tathāpi taddhetukānumānasya davyādravyasādhāraṇabahutarabhūyodarśanādhīnatayā na sukaratvamityāśayena dravyatvasyaiva hetutvamanusṛtam |
dravyatvasya jātitve vivādāt |
gurāśrayatvalakṣaṇetyuktam |
guṇāśrayatvasyāśrayatvāpekṣayā gurutayā hetutvānaṅgīkāre tu āśrayatayā guṇasyaiva hetutvaṃ bodhyam |
guṇāśrayatvaṃ lakṣaṇamabhivyanjakaṃ yasyetyarthāddravyatvajātereva hetutvamityapi vadanti || anaṅgīkārāditi || yadyapi svabhāvato nirguṇasyāpyāropitaguṇāśrayatvānna siddhiḥ |
āropitadhūmatvasyaivāgnisādhakatvābhyupagamāt |
tathāpi siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya pāramārthikabhedasyaiva sādhyatayā āropitaguṇāśrayatvasyāprayojakatayā tadapi pāramārthikameva vācyamityasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
|| sukhaṃ nānāvyaktyāśritamiti || yadyapi rūpādeḥ pratyekaṃ nānāvyaktyāśritatvabhāvāt dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyam |
saṃyogāderdṛṣṭāntatvasambhave'pi rūpādāvanaikāntyam |
tathāpi jātyā nānāvyaktyāśritatvasya svāśrayāśrayaniṣṭhabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakavṛttiguṇatvavyāpyajātimattvarūpasya sādhyatvānna doṣaḥ |
rūpatvādeḥ svāśrayanīlaghaṭādiniṣṭhabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakapītarūpavṛtterdṛṣṭānte sattvāt |
pakṣe ca sukhatvamādāya sādhyaparyavasāne tadāśrayāśrayāṇāmātmanāṃ bhedasiddhiḥ |
guṇatvādijātimādāyārthāntaravāraṇāya guṇatvavyāpyeti |
na ca guṇatvasyāpi guṇatvābhāvavadavṛttitayā guṇatvavyāpyatvādarthāntaratādavasthyamiti vācyam |
guṇatvavyāpyetyanena guṇatvanyūnavṛttitvasya guṇaniṣṭhabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakatvaparyavasitasya vivakṣitatvāt |
sukhatvādergandhādiguṇaniṣṭhabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakatvena guṇatvavyāpyatvāditi na kaściddoṣaḥ |
etatpakṣe sādhyagauravādāha -- sukhatvaṃ veti || nānetyādeḥ svāśrayāśrayaniṣṭhabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakavṛttītyarthaḥ |
tena nānāvyaktyāśritaṃ yatsaṃyogādikaṃ tadāśritatvasya rūpatvādāvabhāve'pi na pūrvavaddeṣaḥ |
jātitvamātrasya manastvādau vyabhicāritvādguṇaniṣṭheti |
tāvanmātrasya tadrūpatvādau vyabhicāritvājjātīti |
guṇasamavetatvoktau tu tallābhāyaivajātipadamityavadheyam || vyabhicāra iti || śabdāśrayībhūtagaganasyaikatvena śabde prathamasādhyasya, śabdatve ca dvitīyasādhyasyābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
mīmāṃsakamatenāha -- tasyeti || śabdasyetyarthaḥ |
tathāca śabde śabdatve vā hetvābhāvādeva na vyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ |
naiyāyikanaye'pyāha -- adravyatvapakṣa iti || tadanyatveneti || prathame śabdānyatvena dvitīye śabdatvānyatvena cetyarthaḥ |
na ca dvitīye katvādijātau vyabhicāraḥ |
tatkalpetadanyatvapadena tadvadavṛttitvasya vivakṣitvāt |
katvādyantatvenāpi heturviśeṣaṇīya ityapyāhuḥ |
vastutaḥ kalpadvaye'pi tadanyatvenetyasya śabdānyatvenetyevārthaḥ |
hetuviśeṣaṇādityasya hetughaṭakaguṇaviśeṣaṇatvādityarthakatayā śabdamātravṛttiśabdatvādeḥ śabdānyaguṇavṛttitvābhāvānnoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ |
atra vṛttisukhatvaṃ svarūpasukhatvaṃ vā pakṣatāvacchedakam |
ubhayānugatasukhatvajāterdravyatvādinā saṅkareṇāsambhavāditi bhāvenādyaṃ dūṣayati -- sukhaduḥkhādīnāmiti || vṛtyātmaketyādiḥ |
atra sukhasyaiva prakṛtatve'pi duḥkhādigrahaṇaṃ duḥkhecchādipakṣakānumānāntarāṇāmapi nirāsasūcanāya || antaḥkaraṇāśritatvāditi || teṣāmantaḥkaraṇapariṇāmatvāditi bhāvaḥ || siddhasādhanatvāditi || yadyapi sukhaduḥkhādīnāmātmāśritatvapakṣe'pyanirvacanīyātmabhedamādāya siddhasādhanatvaṃ sambhavatyeva |
pāramārthikatvena bhedaviśeṣaṇe ca sādhyāprasiddheḥ |
antaḥkaraṇāśritatvapakṣe siddhasādhanānupapatteśca |
tadbhedasyāpyapāramārthikatvāt |
tathāpi vastugatimanurūddhyaivāntaḥkaraṇāśritatvamuktam |
vastuto vakṣyamāṇarītyā dharmisattāsamasattākabhedasya sādhyaśarīraghaṭakatāyāmātmāśritatvapakṣe siddhasādhanānavakāśādantaḥkaraṇāśritatvapakṣa eva taduktiḥ |
dvitīyapakṣaṃ dūṣayati -- svarūpeti || ātmasvarūpetyarthaḥ |
dravyasamavetasyaiva guṇatvena svarūpasukhasyātathātvāt svarūpāsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
siddhānte tu tasya svarūpatve'pyātmaviśeṣatayā guṇatvamastyeveti dhyeyam |
|| caitraśarīramiti || atra tatsvāmitvaṃ na tadavacchinnabhogabhoktṛtvam |
ekasvāmikayorghaṭapaṭayorityagrimagranthavirodhāt |
ghaṭādyavacchinnabhogāprasiddheḥ |
kintu svasvāmibhāvavyavahāraniyāmakaḥ tatsattāniyāmakatvādilakṣaṇo dharmaviśeṣaḥ |
yadyapi tatsvāmibhinnasvāmikatvaṃ paramate'prasiddham |
tathāpi prasiddhaghaṭādisādhāraṇasya tatsvāmibhinnatvasyaiva sādhyatāvacchedakatvānna doṣaḥ |
sādhyatā ca svasvāmikatvasambandhena |
ataḥ phalataścaitramaitrātmanorbhedasiddhiḥ |
ghaṭādestādṛśasambandhena siddhyayogāt |
atra tadviruddhaceṣṭāvattvaṃ tadavṛttikriyāvattvaṃ, tatkriyāvijātīyakriyāvattvaṃ veti manvāno dūṣayati -- ityatreti || vyabhicāramupapādayati -- ekasvāmikayoriti || maitrasvāmikayorityarthaḥ |
tathāca maitraśarīraviruddhaceṣṭāvati ghaṭādau tatsvāmibhinnasvāmikatvābhāvāt, yo yadviruddhaceṣṭāśrayaḥ sa tatsvāmibhinnasvāmika iti sāmānyavyāptau ghaṭaviruddhaceṣṭāvati paṭādau tatsvāmibhinnasvāmikatvābhāvādapi vyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ |
nanu śarīratve sati tadviruddhaceṣṭāvattvameva hetuḥ |
ato noktavyabhicāra ityata āha -- śarīratveneti || śarīratvaviśiṣṭaniruktaceṣṭāvattvasya hetutve'pītyarthaḥ |
yadyapi sākṣātprayatnajanyakriyārūpaceṣṭāpadārthavivakṣayaivoktadoṣavāraṇasambhavāt tādṛśakriyāśrayatvarūpaśarīratvasya pṛthagupādānamanucitam |
na ca śarīratvasya jātitvānna gauravamiti vācyam |
pṛthivītvādinā saṅkarāt |
tathāpi sāṅkaryasya jātibādhakatvānaṅgīkāre lāghavasambhavāccharīratvaṃ pṛthagupāttam || dṛṣṭāntābhāva iti || tathāca vyāptigrahavirodhivyabhicāragrahābhāve'pi tatkāraṇībhūtasahacāragrahāsambhavena vyāptigrahāsambhavānnānumānasambhava iti bhāvaḥ |
vipratipannāśeṣabhedasādhakānumānaprakāramāśaṅkate -- vimatāviti || bhinnatvābhinnatvābhyāṃ vipratipannau jīveśvarāvityarthaḥ |
atra dvitvamavivakṣitam |
vimatatvamātrasya pakṣatāvacchedakatvāt || bhinnāviti || mithobhinnāvityarthaḥ |
vimatatvavyāpyadharmāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedavantāviti yāvat |
viruddhadharmādhikaraṇatvāt parasparavṛttyabhāvapratiyogidharmavattvāt |
vimatatvasamānādhikaraṇābhāvapratiyogidharmavattvāditi yāvat |
atra yo yaddharmasamānādhikaraṇābhāvapratiyogidharmavān sa taddharmavyāpyadharmāvacchinpratiyogitākabhedavāniti sāmānyavyāptyā dahanatuhinobhayatvādhikaraṇadahanādivṛtyabhāvapratiyogiśetyādivattuhinādestadubhayatvavyāpyadahanatvādyavacchinnapratiyogitākabhedavattvavat prakṛtasyeśvarāderuktavimatatvādhikaraṇajīvādivṛttyabhāvapratiyogisvātantryādimattvena tādṛśavimatatvavyāpyajīvatvādyavacchinnapratiyogitākabhedavattvaṃ siddhyatītyāśayaḥ |
yadvā , ayaṃ tadbhinnastanniṣṭhābhāvapratiyogidharmavattvāt |
sa caitadbhinna etanniṣṭhābhāvapratiyogidharmavattvāt |
yo yanniṣṭhābhāvapratiyogidharmavān sa tadbhinno dahananiṣṭhābhāvapratiyogiśaityādhikaraṇatuhinavaditi samūhālambanānumāne tātparyam |
tena jīveśvarobhayaṃ pakṣīkṛtya bhedamātrasādhane ghaṭādibhedamādāyārthāntaraṃ, parasparabhedasādhane'pi tasya pratyekaṃ jīvabhedādirūpatvādaṃśe bādhaḥ, evaṃ jīvaviruddhadharmavattvādeḥ pratyekaṃ bhāgāsiddhiḥ, pakṣadṛṣṭāntayoḥ sādhyahetvananugamaścetyādidoṣānavakāśaḥ |
atra pāramārthikatvaviśeṣitastadaviśeṣito vā bhedaḥ sādhyaḥ |
ādye doṣamāha -- pāramārthiketi || antye doṣamāha -- apāramārthiketi || pāramārthikatvāviśeṣitetyarthaḥ |
na tvapāramārthikatvaviśeṣatitetyarthaḥ |
ubhayāviśeṣitabhedasādhanapakṣāpratikṣepeṇa nyūnatāpatteḥ |
sa cāparamārthikatvaviśeṣito na vetyanyadetat |
ātmabhedasādhakānumānanirākaraṇamupasaṃharati -- tasmāditi || uktarītyā bhedasādhakānumānamātrasyābhāsatvādityarthaḥ |
bhede jīveśvarabhede'numānaṃ na pramāṇamityarthaḥ |
dvitīyanirākaraṇaṃ pratijānīte || nāpīti || jīveśvarabheda iti vartate tatra tāvadātmamātrabhedasādhakānumānadvayamanūdya dūṣayati -- ātmatvamityādinā || atrātmatvasya nānavyāktiniṣṭhatvamātmanānātvaṃ vinā na sambhavatītyātmabhedasiddhiḥ || asiddheriti || anyatarāsiddherityarthaḥ |
tāmevopapādayati -- ātmaikyavādināmiti || mata iti śeṣaḥ |
vādineti pāṭhe anaṅgīkārādityanvayaḥ || vyaktibhedasyeti || ātmākhyavyaktau bhedasya nānātvasya pramāṇabādhitatvenetyarthaḥ |
ātmanāṃ bhede hyanekasamavetatvenātmatvasya jātitvaṃ syāt |
bhedābhāve ca vyaktyabhedākhyabādhakasadbhāvādākāśatvavannātmatvaṃ jātiriti bhāvaḥ || nirguṇasyeti || `sākṣī cetā kevalo nirguṇaśce'ti śruteriti bhāvaḥ || guṇāśratvalakṣaṇeti || samavāyikāraṇatvarūpaṃ tu nirvikāratvavādinā nāṅgīkriyata iti bhāvaḥ |
ātmabhede'numānadvayamāśaṅkate -- athetyādinā || anumānadvaye vyabhicāramāśaṅkya nirākaroti || naceti || ākāśasyaikatvena śabdasya nānāvyaktyāśritatvaṃ nāstīti tatrādyānumānasya vyabhicārāt |
ata eva śabdatvasya nānāvyaktyāśritāśritatvābhāvena dvitīyānumānasya tatra vyabhicāra ityarthaḥ |
mīmāsakamatamavalambyāha -- tasyeti || naiyāyikamatamavalambyāha -- adravyatveti || tadanyatveneti || guṇatvahetau śabdabhinnatve satīti guṇaniṣṭhajātitve śabdatvabhinnatve satīti viśeṣaṇānna vyabhicāra ityarthaḥ |
kiṃ prākṛtasukhasya nānāvyaktyāśritatvaṃ sādhyate uta svarūpasukhasya |
ādye doṣamāha -- sukhaduḥkhādīnāmiti || `etatsarvaṃ mana eve'ti śruteriti bhāvaḥ |
sukhamātrapakṣīkāre'pyatra duḥkhādigrahaṇamuktarītyā duḥkhādipakṣakānumānanirāsārthamiti dhyeyam |
dvitīyamāśaṅkya nirākaroti || svarūpasukheti || svarūpasukhasya guṇatve ātmanassaguṇatvāpattyā nirguṇaśrutivyākopāditi bhāvaḥ || anyonyajīvabhede'numānamāśaṅkya dūṣayati -- caitraśarīramiti || maitraśarīreti || maitraśarīrasvāmī maitraḥ tadbhinno yastatsvāmikamityarthaḥ |
anena caitraśarīrasvāmini caitre maitrabhedaḥ sidhyati -- tadviruddheti || maitraśarīraceṣṭāviruddhaceṣṭetyarthaḥ |
atra yat yacceṣṭāviruddhaceṣṭāśrayaṃ tat tatsvāmibhinnasvāmikaṃ yathā paṭaceṣṭāviruddhaceṣṭāśrayo ghaṭaḥ paṭasvāmikuvindabhinnakulālasvāmika iti sāmānyavyāptirdraṣṭavyā |
vyabhicāramupapādayati -- ekasvāmikayorapīti || śarīratveneti || bhogāyatanatvenetyarthaḥ |
dṛṣṭāntāsiddhiḥ |
dṛṣṭāntasyaivāsiddhirityarthaḥ |
dṛṣṭāntābhāvādityapi pāṭhaḥ |
jīveśvarabhede'numānamāśaṅkate -- vimatāviti || pakṣe sarvajñatvālpajñatvādirūpaviruddhadharmo dṛṣṭānte tūṣṇatvaśītatvarūpau draṣṭavyau |
dahano vahniḥ tuhinaṃ himam |
kimatra pāramārthikabhedaḥ sādhyaḥ uta vyāvahārikaḥ |
ādye āha -- pāramārthiketi || sādhyatvenābhimatasya pāramārthikabhedarūpasya dharmiviśeṣaṇasya manmate'prasiddhatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
dvitīya āha -- vyāvahāriketi || vyāvahārikabhedasya mayā'bhyupagamāditi bhāvaḥ |
anumānanirākaraṇamupasaṃharati -- tasmāditi || ātmajīvabheda iti || paramātmajīvabhede jīvānāmanyonyabhede cetyarthaḥ |
`dvā suparṇā' `dve brahmaṇī' ityādyāgamo lokasiddhabhedānuvādakaḥ |
anuvādaśca `neha nānā'stī'ti niṣedhāya |
etena ghaṭapaṭādibhede'pi pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇaṃ nirastaṃ draṣṭavyam |
pratyakṣasya bhrāntitvenāprāmāṇyāt |
ata eva tanmūlānumānamapi na pramāṇam |
abhede tu pratyakṣaṃ tāvatsulabhameva |
ahamityekākāreṇānubhavāt (ātmano'nubhavāt) |
anumānaṃ ca vimatā ātmānaḥ paramārthataḥ paramātmano na bhidyante ātmatvāt paramātmavaditi |
evaṃ bhede pratyakṣānumāne nirākṛtyāgamamapi pramāṇatvenāśaṅkya nirākaroti || dvā suparṇeti || jīveśvarākhyau dvau suparṇāvityarthaḥ || dve brahmaṇī iti || parāpararūpe īśajīvarūpe dve brahmaṇī jñātavya ityarthaḥ || lokasiddhabhedānuvādaka iti || lokavyavahārasiddho yo jīveśvarabhedastasyānuvādaka eva na pratipādako lokavyavahārastu andhaparamparāprāpto na pramāṇamūlaka iti bhāvaḥ |
nanvadhikavidhānādyamāvena vṛthā'nuvādāyogātpratipādakatvamevāsyetyata āha -- anuvādaśceti || yathā kathāyāṃ vādyuktasya dūṣaṇāya prativādino'nuvāda ityarthaḥ |
idānīmacetanānāmapi parasparabhedaṃ pratikṣipati -- eteneti || neha nāneti śrutyā bhedamātrasya nirākṛtatvenetyarthaḥ |
kecittu jaḍānāṃ parasparabhedasyāprastutatvāddhaṭādibheda ityetaddhaṭapaṭādibhedapratiyogikeśvaraniṣṭhabhede ghaṭapaṭādiniṣṭheśvarapratiyogikabhede veti vyākhyāya prasaṅgātparasparajaḍabhedapramāṇanirāso'pyayamiti vadanti |
taccintyam |
jaḍānāṃ parasparabhedasyāpi prastutatvasya prāk pradarśitatvāditi |
ghaṭapaṭādibheda ityupalakṣaṇam |
cetanācetanayorbheda ityapi draṣṭavyam || pratyakṣasyeti || ghaṭaḥ paṭo neti vā nāhaṃ ghaṭa ityādervā pratyakṣasyetyarthaḥ || bhrāntitvāditi || nirdoṣaśrutiviruddhasya prāmāṇyāyogāditi bhāvaḥ || ata eveti || mūlabhūtapratyakṣasyaiva bhrāntitvādityarthaḥ || anumānādiriti || ādipadenāgamagrahaḥ |
tatrānumānaṃ pratyakṣasiddhadharmyādikatvāttanmūlam |
āgamastu pratyakṣasiddhabhedānuvāditvāttanmūla iti vivekaḥ |
na kevalaṃ viṣṇorviśvabhinnatvamuktarītyā'prāmāṇikaṃ kintvabhedagrāhakapramāṇaviruddhaṃ ceti bhāvenābhede pramāṇatrayaṃ krameṇāha -- abhede tviti || jīvātmaparamātmābhede tvityarthaḥ |
tāvacchabdo'trāpi yojyaḥ |
jaḍābhede pramāṇasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt || ahamityekākāreṇeti || sarveṣāmātmā hyahamityekatvenānubhūyate |
yadi paramātmā'pi jīvavatpratyaktvena jīvena saha vidyeta tarhyāvāmityanubhavaḥ syāt |
na caivamasti |
tasmādātmanāṃ paramātmano bhedābhāvarūpābhedasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ || anumānaṃ ceti || abheda iti vartate |
vyāvahārikabhedasya svenāpyaṅgīkṛtatvādbādhādivāraṇāya paramārthata ityuktam |
|| anuvādaka iti || niṣedhyakoṭisamarpaka ityarthaḥ |
aprāptaniṣedhāyogāllokasiddhetyuktam |
bhrāntapratyakṣādibhiḥ siddhetyarthaḥ |
yadvā bhedavākyasyāpūrvatārāhityajñāpanāyaitat || eteneti || pūrvavaddoṣagrāseneti sāmānyataḥ parāmarśaḥ |
ghaṭādibheda itīśvarājjīvātparasparaṃ ceti jñeyam |
yadyapi jaḍe dharmisattāsamānasattākakṛto bhedo'ṅgīkriyate |
tathā'pi dharmadharmiṇorubhayoḥ pāramārthyanirāse tātparyamiti jñeyam || bhrāntitveneti || ekatra vyabhicaritatvenāviśvaviṣayatvenetyarthaḥ |
prabalāgamaviruddhatveneti vā |
|| ata eveti || mūlapratyakṣasya bhrāntitvādityarthaḥ || anumānamapīti || ghaṭaḥ paṭapratiyogikabhedādhikaraṇaṃ paṭāvṛttidharmādhikaraṇatvāt vyatirekeṇa paṭavadityanumānam |
`bhinnā prakṛtiraṣṭadhe'tyāgamaścetyarthaḥ |
aikye pramāṇābhāvāditi dvitīyaṃ pakṣaṃ nirāha -- abhede tviti || eketi || sarveṣāmahamityevollekhena vaicitryānupalambhāditi bhāvaḥ || vimatā iti || vyāvahārikabhedena vā pararītyā vā grāhyasandehasyādūṣaṇatvātsandigdhena vā bhedena vā bahuvacanaṃ pakṣadṛṣṭāntādibhāvaśceti jñeyam |
bādhāpasiddhāntavāraṇāya paramārthata iti |
nanu `virodhe guṇavādaḥ syādanuvādo'dhārite |
bhūtārthavādastattvānāmanuvādastridhā mata' ityukteradhikavidhānārthānuvādasthale arthābādharūpaprāmāṇyadarśanāccānuvādatve'pyarthābādhaḥ kinna syādityata āha -- anuvādaśceti || tathā ca niṣedhārthānuvādenārthābādha ityāśayaḥ |
