evaṃ cetanarūpaviśvabhinnatvaṃ nirasyācetanabhinnatvaṃ nirasyati -- eteneti || ghaṭapaṭādibhede ghaṭapaṭādipratiyogikeśvaraniṣṭhabhede ghaṭapaṭādiniṣṭheśvarapratiyogikabhede vā prasaṅgātparasparapratiyogikaparasparaniṣṭhabhedapramāṇanirāso'pyayam || pratyakṣasyeti || ahaṃ ghaṭo neti ghaṭo'yaṃ neti ghaṭaḥ paṭo neti ca pratyakṣasyetyarthaḥ || ata eveti || pratyakṣasyāpramāṇatvādvyāptigrāhakatvena pakṣagrāhakatve caitanmūlānumānasya śabdasvarūpādigrāhaktvena tanmūlaśabdasya ca na mānatetyarthaḥ |
nanu bhedo nāma tādātmyaniṣedhaḥ |
tādātmyaṃ cābhedastatra pramāṇābhāva eva bhede pramāṇaṃ bhaviṣyati |
na pramāṇāntaraṃ vaktavyamityata āha -- abhede tviti || evañca na tatra pramāṇābhāva iti bhāvaḥ |
etena viśvabhedākṣepasya tatra pramāṇābhāvasamarthanena siddhatvādabhede pramāṇānāṃ kathanamasaṅgatamiti śaṅkā parāstā veditavyā |
nanu ghaṭo ghaṭo'yamityekākāreṇānubhūtānāmapi ghaṭānāṃ bhedavadahamityekākāreṇānubhūtānāmātmanāmapi bhedaḥ syāt |
ākāramātrasyāhantvasya ghaṭatvavatparamaikyaṃ syāt |
tatra ghaṭatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedābhāvavadihāpyātmatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedābhāve'pi tattadātmatvena bhedastu syādityarūcerāha -- anumānamiti || vyāvahārikabhedāṅgīkāreṇa bādhavāraṇāya paramārthata iti |
|| dvā suparṇeti || nanu kathamasya bhede pramāṇatvam |
dvitvasyoktāvapi bhedasyānuktatvāt |
na ca dvitvaṃ bhedavyāptamiti taduktyā bhedaḥ sidhyatīti vācyam |
tathātve'syānumānotthāpakatvāpattyā''gamatayā pramāṇatvenopanyāsāyogāt |
na ca tayoranya ityaṃśena bhedaḥ śābdaviṣayo bhavatīti vācyam |
tarhi tadaṃśasyaivodāharaṇīyatvenānupayuktāṃśodāharaṇāsaṅgatiḥ |
dve brahmaṇītyudāharaṇasya sarvathā'saṅgatiriti cenna |
atra dvau dve iti śabdasyaiva (paraspara)bhedaviśiṣṭabodhakatvamanyathā suparṇāviti brahmaṇīti dvivacanenaitatpadayoranyataravaiyarthyāpatteḥ |
anyatarasyānuvādakatvāśrayaṇasyāgatikāgatitvādityabhiprāyeṇaitadudāharaṇasaṅgateḥ |
na ca kayā vṛtyā dviśabdasya bhedaviśiṣṭabodhakatvamiti cenna nirūḍhalakṣaṇayā śaktyā vā |
śaktistu `dviśabdo dvitvasaṅkhayāyāṃ bhede cāpi prayujyata' iti nānārtharatnamālokteriti brūmaḥ |
tatkathamiti cet |
dvaitaśabdaghaṭakadviśabdasya bhedavadarthakatayaivānādivyavahārāt |
anyathā dviśabdasya bhāvapradhānatayā dvītiśabdasya bhedaviśiṣṭārthakatvena taduttarabhāvārthakāṇpratyayena bhedabodhānupapatteḥ |
bhāvapratyayasya prakṛtyarthatāvacachedakabodhakatvaniyamena dvitvabodhakatvasyaivāpatteḥ |
vyākhyātaścāyaṃśabdo `yatra dvaitamiva bhavati' iti śrutivyākhyāvasare viṣṇutattvavinirṇayaṭīkādāvuktarītyā bhedaparatayaiveti na kiñcidatrāsamañjasam || neha nāneti || iha brahmaṇi nānā bhedaviśiṣṭaṃ kimapi nāstīti niṣedhaḥ bhedasāmānyaniṣedhaparyavasāyī san prapañcasya brahmaṇyadhyastatvarūpaṃ tadātmakatvaṃ gamayatīti bhāvaḥ || eteneti || nirdoṣāgamena samastabhedasya niṣiddhatvenetyarthaḥ |
nanu kathaṃ pramāṇanirāsaḥ ? pratyakṣasya, tanmūlakaviruddhadharmādhikaraṇatvarūpānumānānāṃ ca sattvātpratyuta tadvirodhena śrutireva na svārthaparetyata āha -- pratyakṣasyeti || pratyakṣarūpajñānasyetyarthaḥ || bhrāntitveneti || bhrāntatveneti pāṭhe bhāve ktaḥ |
bhrāntatvasambhāvanāviṣayatvenāpramāṇatvāt pramāṇatvena niścetumaśakyatvāt |
tathā ca śuktirajatādipratyakṣasya karaṇadoṣādimūlatvena bhrāntitvasya dṛṣṭatvādanyatrāpi doṣasambhāvanayā pramāṇatvena niścayāyogāt |
āgamasyāpauruṣeyatvena nirdoṣatvena niścitaprāmāṇyakatvāttena pratyakṣasyaiva bādhastanmūlabhūtānumānasya tu sutarāmeveti bhāvaḥ |
etenetyasyoktārthakatvānaṅgīkāre vakṣyamāṇasyaiva tadvivaraṇarūpatvāṅgīkāre pramāṇasāmānyanirāsaṃ pratijñāya pratyakṣānumānayorubhayoreva nirākaraṇe nyūnatāprasaṅgā(ṅgatā)patteriti dhyeyam |
ghaṭapaṭabhedādau pramāṇanirākaraṇaṃ ca prāsaṅgikatayā vā parasparaṃ ghaṭapaṭādīnāmatyantabhede (tādṛśa)jagatā saha kathaṃ brahmaṇo bhedābhāvaḥ parasparamatyantabhinnayorekavasturūpatvasyānubhavaviruddhatvāditi śaṅkānirāsāya veti bodhyam |
nanu viśvasmādbrahmaṇo bhede kathaṃ pramāṇābhāvaḥ |
tayorabhede pramāṇābhāvarūpahetubalādeva bhedasiddherityāśaṅkāmapākartumabhede pramāṇānyāha || abhede tviti || yadvā pūrvapakṣī parapakṣe pramāṇāni nirākṛtya svamate pramāṇānyāha -- abhede tviti || caitanyaikyādāvityarthaḥ || ahamityekākāreṇeti || anugatapratīterekaviṣayakatvaṃ vinā'nupapatteḥ sarvatra tādṛśapratītau bhāsamānacaitanyaikyaṃ yuktaṃ viśeṣyāṃśe bādha eva |
ghaṭa ityādipratītīnāmiva prakāraikyamādāyānugatākāratvaṃ vaktavyam |
prakṛte ca na tathā |
pratyakṣādīnāṃ bhedaviṣaye nirastatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
(brahmaṇo'pi) `tadātmānamevāvedāhaṃ brahmāsmī'tyādiśrutibhiryasmātkṣaramatīto'hamityādismṛtibhiścāhamiti pratīteḥ siddhatvāttaccetanaikyamate'pi pratyakṣam (eva) pramāṇamiti bhāvaḥ || vimatā iti || vyāvahārikabhedasyāṅgīkṛtatvāttadabhāvasādhane bādhavāraṇāya tattvata iti pāramārthikatvenetyarthaḥ |
na ca vyāvaharikabhedasattve'pi tasya mithyātvānurodhena tadabhāvasyāpyaṅgīkṛtatvātkathaṃ bādha iti vācyam |
avyāpyavṛttitvamādāyārthāntaratāsiddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya bhedābhāve pratiyogivaiyyadhikaraṇyasyāvaśyameva niveśanīyatayā bādhānistārāt |
yadvā mithyātvaśarīraniviṣṭo'bhāvo na pratiyogino vyāvahārika(satva)jñānavirodhī |
tasyedānīmavyāvṛttatvānna tamādāya loke vipratipattyādiprasaktiḥ |
itthaṃ ca paramātmapratiyogikabhedatadabhāvakoṭikavipratipatterlokasiddhabhedavirodhyabhāvāvalambanatvāt tādṛśabhedābhāvasya sādhyatvena tadvirodhivyāvahārikabhedasattvādbādho bhavatyeva |
anyathā vahniranuṣṇa ityādīnāmabādhitatvaprāptyā'bhāvasādhyakabādhitahetumātrocchedaprasaṅgāt |
atastatparāsa iti vaktavyameva |
na ca pāramārthikatvaviśiṣṭabhedarūpapratiyogyaprasiddhyā tadabhāvakoṭeraprasiddhireva |
tārkikādivatsvamate'pi pratiyogisattva evābhāvasattvamityadvaitasiddhitaṭṭīkādāvuktatvādataḥ kathaṃ tasya vipratipattiviṣayatā sādhyatā ceti vācyam |
vyadhikaraṇadharmāvacchinnābhāvasyaivābhyupagamena pāramārthikatvāvacchinnabhedavṛttipratiyogitākābhāvasya vipratipattiviṣayatāyāḥ sādhyatāyāśca sambhavāt |
etatpakṣe (sva)pratiyogitāvacchedakāvacchinnasvapratiyogivṛttyavacchedakatākabhedavadvṛttitvameva pratiyogivaiyyadhikaraṇyaṃ vyadhikaraṇadharmavacchinnāvacchedakatākabhedo'bhyupagamyata iti jñātavyam |
tadanaṅgīkāre tu vakṣyamāṇavipratipattiviṣayatāvacchedakākrānteṣu jīvātmasu pāramārthikanirūpitatvaparamātmapratiyogikabhedanirūpitatvobhayābhāvavatsvavṛttyadhikaraṇa(tā)sāmānyakatvaṃ tadabhāvakoṭikā vipratipattiḥ |
prathamakoṭiradvaitināṃ jīvaniṣṭhādhikaraṇa(tā)sāmānyasyobhayābhāvavattve jīve paramātmapratiyogika(pāramārthika)bhedo nirākṛta eva bhavati |
ghaṭābhāvādinirūpitajīvaniṣṭhayatkiñcidadhikaraṇatāmādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya sāmānyaniveśaḥ |
pāramārthikanirūpitatvaṃ brahmadharmaśuddhatvādiniṣṭhādheyatādiṣvadvaitamate prasiddham |
abhāvakoṭirbhedavādināṃ tādṛśādhikaraṇatāsāmānyakatvābhāvasya jīve paramātmapratiyogikapāramārthikabhedādhikaraṇatāṃ vinā paryavasānāyogāt |
tathā coktasāmānyakatvameva sādhyamiti na kaściddoṣaḥ |
yadi cātra svatvasyānanugatatvātsādhyasyānanugama iti vibhāvyate tadā paramārthatvanirūpitatvaparamātmapratiyogikabhedanirūpitatvobhayasya svāvacchinnanirūpakatādhikaraṇatānadhikaraṇatvasambandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvaḥ sādhyaḥ |
jīve paramātmapratiyogikapāramārthikabhedasattve tādṛśobhayatvāvacchinnanirūpakatākādhikaraṇatā tadubhayanirūpitatādṛśabhedādhikaraṇatāniṣṭhādhikaraṇatā tadvadadhikaraṇatā jīvaniṣṭhatādṛśabhedādhikaraṇatā bhavati |
tatsambandhenobhayatvasattvāttatsambandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākatādṛśobhayatvābhāvarūpasādhyaṃ pakṣe durupapādaṃ syādatastasmiṃstādṛśabhedanirāsasiddhiḥ |
sambandhakoṭau svatvasya paricāyakatvānnānanugamaḥ |
uktasambandho dvaitināṃ mate uktobhayatvasya vyadhikaraṇa eva |
ghaṭanirūpitatvapaṭanirūpitatvobhayatvāderbhūtalādau sambandhatayā prasiddhiḥ |
na ca paramātmapratiyogika(nirūpita)pāramārthikabhedasya jīve sattve'pyuktasādhyopapattiḥ |
bhedasyaiva paramārthatvena nirūpitatvadvayasya bhedādhikaraṇatāyāmabhāvena tādṛśobhayatvābhāvasya pakṣe sambhavāditi vācyam |
pāramārthikatvāvacchinnabhedatvāvacchinnanirūpakatākatvayorbhedāt |
abhede vā pāramārthikatvāvacchedyatvatādṛśabhedatvāvacchedyatvaitadubhayatvasyaiva svaviśiṣṭādhikaraṇatāvannirūpakatānirūpitā(pakā)dhikaraṇatāsambandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāva eva sādhyamityadoṣaḥ |
yadvā jīvavṛttyadhikaraṇatāvacchedena pāramārthikatvāvacchinnanirūpakatākatvaparamātmapratiyogikabhedatvāvacchinnanirūpakatākatvaitadubhayābhāvasādhane'sya tātparyam |
tadanusāreṇa hetvādikamūhyam |
evamanyatrāpi draṣṭavyam |
astu tarhi dvā suparṇetyādyāgama evā'tmabhede pramāṇam |
tatra jīveśvarākhyapakṣiṇordve brahmaṇītyatra parāparabrahmatayā uktayoḥ tayoreva bhedavyāptadvitvaśravaṇāt |
tayoranyaḥ, anaśnannanya iti sākṣādapi tayorbhedābhidhānāt |
bahvīḥ prajāḥ puruṣāt samprasūtā iti jīvānāmapi nānātvāvagamādityāśaṅkyāha -- dvā suparṇeti || dvau suparṇāvityaukārasyākārādeśaḥ |
nanu bhedasya mānāntarāsiddhatāyā uktatvāt kathamāgamasya tadanuvādakatvamityato lokasiddhetyuktam |
lokavyavahārasiddhetyarthaḥ |
sa ca vakṣyamāṇapramāṇavirodhādapramāṇameveti bhāvaḥ |
nanvanubādhamātreṇa kathaṃ bhedasya nirāsaḥ |
anyathā dadhyādirūpaguṇavidhānāya dadhnā juhotītyanūdyamānayāgāderapi nirāsaprasaṅgādata āha -- anuvādaśceti || niṣedhāyeti || brahmaṇi pāramārthikatvākāreṇa bhedaniṣadāyetyarthaḥ |
tathāca bhedānuvādasya tanniṣedha eva tātparyānna tatrāgamasya prāmāṇyamiti bhāvaḥ |
evamātmabhedaṃ nirasya bhedamātrasyāpāramārthikatvasiddhyarthamātmānātmanoranātmanāṃ ca mitho bhedaṃ dūṣayati -- eteneti || bhedamātrasya neha nānāstīti niṣiddhatvenetyarthaḥ || ghaṭapaṭādibheda iti || ghaṭapaṭādibhirātmanasteṣāṃ ca mitho bheda ityarthaḥ |
tarhi ghaṭapaṭau bhinnavityādipratyakṣasya kā gatirata āha -- pratyakṣasyeti || sambhāvitadoṣasya pratyakṣasya nirduṣṭāgamavirodhe satyaprāmāṇyasyaiva yuktvāditi bhāvaḥ || ata eva || bhedagrāhipratyakṣasya bhrāntitvādeva || tanmūlaketi || tādṛśapratyakṣasaṃvādasāpekṣetyarthaḥ || anumānādirityādinā paurūṣeyāgamaparigrahaḥ |
kevalasyānumānādeḥ śaṅkitāprāmāṇyakatvenāsādhakatvāt, pratyakṣādipramāṇasamvādenaiva prāmāṇyaṃ vācyam |
tasya ca pratyakṣāderaprāmāṇye tatsamānaviṣayānumānādiprāmāṇyasya durlabhatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
vyāptigrāhakasahacārapratyakṣasya bhramatvānna tanmūlakānumānādiḥ pramāṇamityapi vyācakṣate |
tatra yadyati vahnivāriṇoḥ sahacārabhrame'pi parvatādau vāriliṅgakavahnyanumānasya prāmāṇyamasti |
tathāpi vyāptipakṣadharmatāvirahe prāmāṇyaṃ duravadhāraṇamityāśayaḥ |
evamabhede bādhakapramāṇaṃ niyasya dārḍhyāya trividhamapi sādhakapramāṇaṃ darśayati -- abheda tviti || yattvabhede pramāṇābhāva eva bhede pramāṇaṃ bhaviṣyatītyata āha -- abhede tvitīti || taccintyam |
tulyanyāyena bhede pramāṇābhāva evābhede pramāṇamiti vaktuṃ śakyatvāt |
abhede pramāṇābhāvasyānumānavidhayaiva bhedasādhakatayā tannirāsāvasara eva nirasanīyatvācca |
nanvātmāntarapratiyogikabhedasya pratiyogyapratyakṣeṇāpratyakṣatvasyoktatvāt kathaṃ tadabhāvarūpābhedasya pratyakṣatvamityata āha -- ekākāreṇeti || ekavastutādātmyenetyarthaḥ |
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ |
bhedābhāvo hi pratiyogitādātmyam |
tathācāhamityekākārānubhavānāmekavastuviṣayakatve lāghavenaikacittādātmyāvagāhino'hamiti pratyakṣasya kathamabhede prāmāṇyasambhava iti |
|| vimatā ātmāna iti || bahuvacanamavacchedakāvacchedena sādhyasiddheruddeśyatvasūcanāya |
anyathā sarvātmanāmaikyāsiddheḥ |
jīvaparamātmanorvyāvahārikabhedābhyupagamāt sāmānyato bhedaniṣedhe bādhaḥ syādataḥ || paramārthata iti || na caivaṃ pāramārthikabhedāprasiddhyā sādhyāprasiddhiriti vācyam |
pāramārthikatvena bhedābhāvasya sādhyatvāt |
tanmate vyadhikaraṇadharmāvacchinnābhāvasattvena sādhyaprasiddheḥ |
etena pāramārthikatvamabhedaviśeṣaṇamityapāstam |
uktabādānistārāt |
na ca pratibhāsikābhedasiddhyā'rthāntaramiti vācyam |
pāramārthikābhedasyaiva sādhyatvāt |
paramārthato na bhidyanta ityanena pāramārthikatādātmyasyaiva sādhyatvaṃ vivakṣitamiti tu na sat |
vyavahārato'pi na bhidyanta iti vakṣyamāṇābhāsasāmyānanuguṇatvāt |
tatra vyāvahārikasya paramātmatādātmyasya dṛṣṭānte paramātmanyasattvena sādhyatvāsambhavāt |
sāmānyataḥ pāramārthikabhedābhāvasya siddhāntinaṃ pratyasiddhirataḥ paramātmapratiyogikatvaṃ bhedaviśeṣaṇam |
tṛtīyaṃ pakṣaṃ nirākaroti || dvā suparṇeti ||
`supāṃ sulukpūrvasavarṇāccheyāḍāḍyāyājāla'
chandasi viṣaye supāṃ sthāne suśca lukca pūrvasavarṇaśca ā ca āt ca śe ca yā ca ḍā ca ḍyā ca yājca ālca ityete ādeśāḥ syuriti sūtrārthaḥ |
udāharaṇantu |
`ṛjavaḥ santu panthā' ityādi |
atra dvāvityaukārasyākārādeśe dvā ityādibhavati |
tataścāyamarthaḥ |
sayujau saṃyuktau |
sakhāyau mitre |
dvausuparṇau vṛkṣāśritatvena pakṣirūpau jīveśau |
samānam ekam |
vṛkṣaṃ dehākhyamaśvattham |
pariṣasvajāte āliliṅgagatuḥ |
tayoḥ suparṇayormadhye |
anyo jīvaḥ |
pippalarūpaṃ dehajanitakarmaphalam asvādveva svādutvenātti |
anya īśvaraḥ |
tadanaśnan |
abhi abhitaḥ |
cākaśīti prakāśate |
natu svasthityai tadapekṣata iti |
`dve brahmaṇī veditavye' ityasya parāparabrahmaśabdāvācye jñātavye ityarthaḥ |
bhedānuvādako natu bhedapratipādakaḥ |
yenāsyāgamasya bhedeprāmāṇyaṃ syāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu siddhasyānuvādaḥ sambhavati |
ata āgamasyānuvādakatvasiddhaye bhedasya yatpramāṇasiddhatvamaṅgīkriyatetadevāsmākaṃ bhedasādhakapramāṇamityata āha -- lokasiddheti || lokavyavahārasiddhetyarthaḥ |
lokavyavahārastu na pramāṇamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanvadhikavidhānārthamanuvādaḥ kriyate yathā dadhnājahotītyatrāgnihotraṃ juhuyādityanena prāptayāgaṃ juhotītyanūdya dadhneti dadhilakṣaṇaguṇo vidhīyate |
yajjuhoti taddadhneti nacātrādhikavidhānamasti yenāgamasya bhedānuvādakatvaṃ yujyata ityata āha -- anuvādaśceti || caśabdastuśabdārthaḥ niṣedhārthamapi kvacidanuvādaḥ kriyate yathā nāstīti niṣedhāya iha bhūtale ghaṭa ityanuvādaḥ |
tathācehāpi nehanānetyaneneha jagati brahmaṇi vā kiñcana kiñcitpadārthapratiyogiko'pi nānā bhedo nāstītyevaṃ niṣedhāya dvāsuparṇetyāgamena lokasiddhabhedānuvādaḥ kriyata ityarthaḥ |
evamātmabhede pramāṇaṃ nirākṛtyānātmabhede tannirākaroti || eteneti || nehanāneti vākyasya bhedaniṣedhakatvakathanenetyarthaḥ |
kathamityata āha -- pratyakṣasyeti || yatprāptaṃ pramāṇena bādhyate tajjñānaṃ bhrāntiḥ |
yathedaṃ rajatamityanena prāptarajatasya rajatamityāptavākyena bādhitatvāttasya bhrāntitvam |
prakṛte ca ghaṭaḥ paṭo netyanena prāptasya ghaṭapaṭādibhedasya neha nānetyanena bādhitatvāttasya bhrāntitvaṃ durvāramiti bhāvaḥ |
bhrāntatveneti pāṭhe bhāve ktaḥ |
bhrāntitvenetyarthaḥ || ata eveti || upajīvyapratyakṣasyāprāmāṇyādevetyarthaḥ |
na kevalamaprāmāṇikatvena bhedābhāvaḥ kintu prāmāṇikatayā'bheda evābhyupagamyata ityāśayenābhede pramāṇatrayamāha -- abhede tviti || ekākāreṇa ekatvena |
bhede āvāṃ vayamiti vāpratyayaḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ |
paramātmanaḥ sakāśātvyāvahārikabhedasya svenāṅgīkṛtatvena paramārthata ityuktam |
nanu kimatra pakṣatāvacchedakam |
na tāvadātmatvam |
paramātmano'pi pakṣatvāpattyā aṃśe siddhasādhanāt |
nāpi paramātmetaratvaviśiṣṭam |
sādhyena sākaṃ pakṣatāvacchedakasya virodhāt |
nāpi bhrāntyā paramātmetaratayā pratīyamānatvaviśiṣṭam |
paramātmanyapi taditaratvabhrāntisambhavenādyapakṣoktadoṣānistarāditi cenna |
paramātmano bhidyante na veti vipratipattigocaratvasyaiva pakṣatāvacchedakatvāt |
anyathā tavāpi paramātmano bhidyanta iti bhedasādhanasyāsambhavāt |
ātmatvaṃ ca na jātiḥ |
kintarhi svaprakāśatvam |
tena hetusādhyayorna virodhaḥ |
nanvatrātmāna ityanenaiva dharmiviśeṣasiddhau vimatapadagrahaṇaṃ vyarthamityatastatkṛtyaṃ vaktumākṣipati -- nanviti || aṃśe siddhasādhanāditi || pakṣaikadeśe paramātmani paramātmapratiyogikapāramārthikabhedābhāvasya siddhatvāditi bhāvaḥ || paramātmetaratvaviśiṣṭamiti || ātmatvamiti vartate |
tathā ca paramātmani paramātmetaratvasyābhāvena pakṣatvānāpattyā noktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ || sādhyapakṣatāvacchedakayoriti || sādhyaṃ paramātmābhinnatvaṃ pakṣatāvacchedakaṃ ca tadbhinnātmatvaṃ tadvirodhādityarthaḥ || bhrāntyeti || paramātmetaratvaprakārakabhrāntirūpapratītiviṣayatvamityarthaḥ |
tathā ca sādhyasya pāramārthikatvātparamātmetaratvasya bhrāntikalpitatvānna virodha iti bhāvaḥ || ādyapakṣoktadoṣeti || aṃśe siddhasādhanatārūpadoṣetyarthaḥ |
yadarthamitthamākṣepastadidānīṃ vakti || paramātmana iti || nānyatpakṣatāvacchedakamatra gaveṣaṇīyamityevaśabdaḥ |
idaṃ ca paramātmanyastīti nāṃśe siddhasādhanatvaṃ nāpi virodha iti bhāvaḥ |
gauravānnedaṃ pakṣatāvacchedakaṃ yuktamityata āha -- anyatheti || ātmatvasya pakṣatāvacchedakatveṃ'śe paramātmani bādhāt |
tadatiriktatvena viśeṣaṇe tasya siddhau siddhasādhanāt |
asiddhau pakṣāprasiddherityarthaḥ |
kecittu nātmatvamaṃśe bādhāt |
nāpi paramātmeratvaviśiṣṭam |
tasyādhunā'pyasiddheḥ |
nāpi paramātmetaratayā bhrāntyā pratīyamānatvam |
paramātmani pūrvavadaṃśe bādhāditi bhāvamāhuḥ |
taccintyam |
paramātmetaratayā bhrāntyā pratīyamānatvasya pakṣatāvacchedakatve paramātmābhinnatvapratītereva pramātvāpattyā sādhyapakṣatāvacchedakayorvirodhasyaivāpatteriti |
atra hetūkṛtasyātmatvasya jātitve ātmatvaṃ nānāvyaktiniṣṭhaṃ jātitvāditi prāk śaṅkitānumāne'siddhikathanāyogātprakārāntareṇātmatvaṃ nirvaktumāha -- ātmatvaṃ ceti || svaprakāśatvamiti || avedyatve satyaparokṣavyavahāragocatvarūpamityarthaḥ |
ātmatvasya jātirūpatvāṅgīkāre kiṃ bādhakaṃ yena svaprakāśatvaṃ tadityucyata ityata āha -- teneti || yadyātmatvaṃ jātiḥ syāt tarhi jāteranekānugatatve vācye'nekātmavyaktīnāmaṅgīkāryatāpatyā'nekatvasya bhedavyāptatvena hetusādhyayorvirodhaḥ syāt |
ato jātirūpaṃ nedamātmatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
hetusādhyayorna virodha ityupalakṣaṇam |
prāk śaṅkitānumāne jātitvasyāsiddhikathanavirodho'pi netyapi grāhyam |
vimatagrahaṇasāphalyaṃ vaktumāha -- nanviti || aṃśe siddhasādhanāditi || atra kecit paramātmanaḥ paramātmatvena rūpeṇa paramātmābhedasiddhāvapyātmatvenāsiddhatvānnāyaṃ doṣaḥ |
pakṣatāvacchedakasāmānādhikaraṇyena sādhyasiddhereva doṣatvāt |
anyathā yatra hetustatra sādhyamiti vyāptigrahaṇadaśāyāmeva pakṣīyasādhyasyāpi siddheranumānamātrocchedaprasaṅgāt |
ataḥ pakṣatāvacchedakanānātvasthala eva sa doṣa ityāhuḥ |
tanna |
pakṣatāvacchedakavattadvyāpyavyāpakānyataradharmapuraskāreṇa siddhirapi dūṣaṇameva |
vaiyyarthyarūpadūṣakatābījasattvāt |
tatra vyāpyadharmapuraskāreṇa cedaṃśe siddhasādhanatā |
parvatatvādikaṃ prati na dhūmavattvādikaṃ vyāpyaṃ vyāpakaṃ veti nātiprasaṅgaḥ |
pṛthivī itarebhyo bhidyata ityādau tu ghaṭādiṣu ghaṭatvādiviśeṣeṇa jalādibhedasiddhāvapi dhaṭatvādeḥ pṛthivītvena saha vyāpyavyāpakabhāvājñānadaśāyāṃ pṛthivī jalādibhinnā na veti vipratipattisattvātpṛthivītvena pakṣīkāraḥ saṅgacchate |
tajjñāne tu ghaṭādītarasthala eva vimatiriti sāmānyataḥ pakṣīkāre doṣa eva |
iha ca nātmatvaviśeṣeṇa vimatiḥ kintu jīvāḥ || paramātmano bhidyante na veti |
eveti |
paramātmano'pi praveśe doṣa eveti virodhāditi |
taditaratvena viśiṣya tadabhedasādhane virodhāditi jñeyam || ādyapakṣoktadoṣa eveti || siddhasādhyatetyarthaḥ || vipratipattīti |
ata evādau vimatapadaprayoga iti jñeyam |
nanu vimativiṣayatāvacchedakaṃ kimapyasti na vā |
ādye tadeva pakṣatāvacchedakamastu |
dvitīye tadīyatānavagamena siddhasādhyatādyāpātāt |
na cānanugatāvacchedikā tṛṇāraṇimaṇinyānenānanugatadharmyāśrayā vimatiḥ sā sugraheti vācyam |
tarhi pakṣatāvacchedakasya vaiyyarthyāt |
maivam |
pakṣatāvacchedakasāmānādhikaraṇyena sādhyasiddheruddeśyatve tadanuktāvuddeśyāsiddhiprasaṅgena tasyāvaśyaṃ vācyatvādvimatyavacchedakānuktāvapi paramātmādervimativiṣayatvavyāhatyādiprāptyā taditaratraiva sā sugraheti nātiprasaṅgaḥ |
nanu tathā'pi na vimatiḥ pakṣatāvacchedikā |
tasyā eva pakṣatātvāt |
yathā''huḥ |
`sandigdhasādhyavānpakṣa' iti |
sandigdhapadasya vimatārthatāvaśyambhāvāditi |
maivam |
sādhyavattayā siṣādhayiṣitatvasyeha pakṣatvāt |
ātmatvamiti hetūkṛtamiti śeṣaḥ || na virodha iti || jātitve'nekānugatatvaniyamena tadabhedarūpasādhyaviparītavyāptatvarūpavirodho netyarthaḥ |
yadyapyatrātmatvasya na viśeṣarūpeṇa sādhyātyantābhāvavyāptiravasiteti kathaṃ virodha iti bhavati śaṅkā |
tathā'pi sādhanatāvacchedakadharmapuraskāreṇa sādhānasādhyātyantābhāvavyāpterapi virodhapadārthatvena yatra jātistatra vyaktyabhedābhāva iti sāmānyato virodhaḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ |
vimativiśeṣāsphoraṇe vimatā ityavyāvartakamiti manvānaḥ śaṅkate -- nanu kimatreti || aṃśe viddhasādhanāditi || yadyapi pakṣatāvacchedakāvacchedena sādhyasiddheruddeśyatve paramātmani paramātmapratiyogikabhedābhāvasiddhāvapyātmatvāvacchedena tadasiddhernāṃśe siddhasādhanatā |
pakṣatāvacchedakasāmānādhikaraṇyena sādhyasiddheruddeśyatve tu sampūrṇaiveti nobhayathā'pyaṃśe siddhasādhanatā |
pakṣatāvacchedakanānātve tu tadavakāśo na tu pakṣatāvacchedakābheda iti nedaṃ yujyate |
tathā'pi yatra pakṣatāvacchedakavyāpakatvaṃ sādhyatāvacchedakasambandaśca sādhyāṃśe saṃsargamaryādayā bhāsate sā pakṣatāvacchedakāvacchedena sādhyasiddhiḥ |
yatra ca pakṣatāvacchedakavati sādhyatāvacchedakasambandhena sādhyaṃ bhāsate pakṣatāvacchedakavati sarvatra noddeśyaṃ sā pakṣatāvacchedakasāmānādhikaraṇyena sādhyasiddhiḥ |
etadubhayabhinnā yā pakṣatāvacchedakarūpeṇa sakalapakṣaviśeṣyakā sādhyatāvacchedakasambandhena sādhyaprakārikā siddhiryatroddeśyā tatraikasminpakṣe sādhyasiddhāvapyaṃśataḥ siddhasādhanatā bhavatīti prācīnāśayāt |
etādṛśasthale'pyaṃśataḥ siddhasādhanatā yujyata eveti bhāvaḥ || sādhyena sākaṃ pakṣatāvacchedakasya virodhāditi || abhedarūpasādhyasya bhedagarbhapakṣatāvacchedakavirodhādityarthaḥ |
sādhyo yaḥ paramātmābhedo yatparamātmetaratvaṃ pakṣatāvacchedakamanayorvirodhādityarthaḥ |
kvacittu sādhyabhedena sākaṃ pakṣatāvacchedakasya virodhāditi pāṭhaḥ |
ṣaṇṇigrahādipakṣe tu pratijñāvirodho'yaṃ virodhāntarbhūtaḥ |
parapakṣe tu bādha evāyam |
vacanadoṣastu vyāghātaḥ |
vimatā iti padoktaṃ vimatiṃ sphoṭayati || paramātmano bhidyante na veti || anyatheti || ātmatvasya pakṣatāvacchedakatveṃ'śe bādhāt |
paramātmetaratvaviśiṣṭasya tattve viśeṣaṇasiddhau siddhasādhanatā |
tadasiddhau pakṣāprasiddhirityarthaḥ |
nanu hetūkṛtamātmatvaṃ yadi jātistadā tenānekāśrayeṇa bhavitavyam |
tena cāśrayaikyasādhane virodha ityata āha -- ātmatvaṃ ceti || svayaṃprakāśatvamavedyatve satyaparokṣavyavahāraviṣayatvādirūpamityarthaḥ |
|| aṃśe siddhasādhanāditi || avacchedakāvacchedena sādhyasiddheruddeśyatve'pi aṃśe siddhasādhanatā doṣa eva |
pakṣasādhyādyaṃśe'dhikāvagāhinyā'numiternyūnāvagāhisiddhyapratibaddhatvaṃ na tu yatkiñcidadhikapadārthāvagāhitvamātre |
tathātve siddhyātmakadhūmaparāmarśabalādghaṭaparāmarśakāle ghaṭavahnyubhayānumānāpatteḥ |
tathā ca pakṣatāvacchedakāvacchedenānumitau pakṣatāvacchedakāśrayanikhilavyaktayo bhāsante na tu (sādhyāṃśe) pakṣatāvacchedakavyāpakatvādikaṃ bhāsate |
sāmānādhikaraṇyenānumitau (tu) katipayavyaktaya eva bhāsante |
etāvatā tayorvailakṣaṇyamiti prācīnamatābhiprāyeṇedam |
navyamate tu sādhyakoṭāvadhikabhānātsa na doṣa iti dhyeyam || sādhyābhedeti || sādhyabhūto'bhedaḥ sādhyābhedaḥ sa ca pakṣatāvacchedakaṃ cetyanayorvirodhādityarthaḥ |
vyāvahārikabhedāderadyāpyaprasiddhyā paramātmetara ityukte paramātmapratiyogikāpāramārthikabhedasyaiva prāptyā virodha ityarthaḥ || vipratipattigocaratvasyaiveti || anatiprasaktatayā'nirvacaneneti śeṣaḥ |
pakṣatāvacchedakatvāt pakṣatāvacchedakatāyā vaktuṃ śakyatvāt |
nanu vipratipatteḥ pakṣatāvacchedakatvaṃ na paramātmabhedābhedaprakārakavipratipattitvena |
paramātmanyapīdantvādiviśiṣṭe uktobhayaprakārakavipratipattisambhavenoktadoṣānistārāt |
nāpi tadvyaktitvena |
itaravyāvṛttatvena tadvṛtteḥ paricayāyogāt |
tathā ca tasyāḥ pakṣatāvacchedakatvoktirayuktaivetyata āha -- anyatheti || etādṛśadūṣaṇena tasyāḥ pakṣatāvacchedakatvakhaṇḍane || tavāpīti || apiśabdo na kevalaṃ mamābhedasādhanasambhava ityarthaṃ vakṣyamāṇasambhave samuccinoti || bhedasādhanasyāpīti || abhedasādhanasamuccāyako'piśabdaḥ |
tathā hi |
ātmatvasya bhedasādhane pakṣatāvacchedakatve tatsāmānādhikaraṇyena sādhyasādhane sarvajīveṣu paramātmabhedāsiddhyoddeśyāsiddhiḥ |
avacchedakāvacchedena tatsādhane paramātmani paramātmabhedābhāvādaṃśe bādhaḥ |
paramātmetaratvasya pakṣatāvacchedakaghaṭakatve uktānumānātprāk tadasiddhyā pakṣatāvacchedakaviśiṣṭapakṣāprasiddhiranyathā'numānavaiyarthyāpatteḥ |
paramātmabhinnatvena pratīyamānatvaṃ bhrāntyā paramātmanyapīti punaraṃśe bādhaḥ syāt |
atastvayā duḥkhibaddhatvādyupalakṣitacetanatvāvacchinnadharmikoktabhedaprakārakavimateranugatoktarūpeṇa vā tadvyatiriktatvena vā pakṣatāvacchedakatvamupagantavyam |
tattulyaṃ mamāpīti bhāvaḥ |
yadyapi tādṛśaduḥkhitvādereva pakṣatāvacchedakatvasambhavaḥ |
tathā'pyanumānapravṛttestadavacchinne anyatarakoṭiniścayadaśāyāmayogāttādṛśavipratipattisādhyasaṃśayasya niyamenāvaśyakatve lādhavāttasyaiva tadvyaktitvena pakṣatāvacchedakatvamucitamiti hṛdayam |
|| svaprakāśatvamiti || avedyatve satyaparokṣavyavahāragocaratvarūpam |
yadvā svātiriktasaṃvidanadhīnasvaviṣayakayatkiñcidvyavahārakatvaṃ matadvaye'pi suṣuptyuttarakālīnavyavahāramādāya siddhamityabhimānastena pūrvapakṣavādimate ātmanaḥ svaviṣayakajñānarūpatvābhāvādasiddhiriti nirastam || na virodha iti || anyathā''tmatvaṃ jātirityaṅgīkāreṇa hetūkaraṇe vyaktyaikyena sādhyena tasya viruddhatvaṃ syāditi bhāvaḥ |
atrānumāne pakṣatāvacchedakaṃ durnirūpamityāśaṅkate -- nanviti || ātmatvasya vakṣyamāṇarītyā pakṣatāvacchedakatve dūṣite tadvyāpakasya sāmānyato vivādaviṣayatvarūpavimatatvasya tadviśiṣṭātmatvasya ca taddūṣitaprāyamityabhiprāyeṇātmatvasyaiva taddūṣayati -- na tāvaditi || pakṣatvāpattyā pakṣatāvacchedakākrāntatvāpattyā || aṃśe siddhasādhanāditi || yadyapyavacchedakāvacchedena sādhyasiddheruddeśyatāyāmaṃśataḥ siddhasādhanaṃ na doṣaḥ |
nyūnaviṣayakasiddheranumityavirodhitvāt |
na cāvacchedakāvacchedena sādhyatadabhāvakoṭikasandehalakṣaṇapakṣatāvighaṭakatvena tasya doṣatvam |
aṃśataḥ siddhisattve'pyavacchedakāvacchedena sādhyaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyena tadabhāvaṃ cāvagāhamānasandehasambhavena tasyaiva tatra pakṣatātvāt |
na caivamanitye vāṅmanase ityādau vāṅmātre sādhyasiddheravirodhitvaprasaṅga iti vācyam |
pakṣatāvacchedakāvacchedena siddhestadavacchedenānumitivirodhitayā vāktvāvacachedena siddhestadavacchedenānumitivirodhitvasambhavāt |
ata evā'ha maṇikāraḥ `pakṣatāvacchedakanānātve hyaṃśataḥ siddhasādhanaṃ doṣa' iti |
tathāpi tadviṣayakānumitau tadviṣayakasiddhisāmānyasya virodhitvamiti matamanusṛtyedamuktam |
astu tarhi paramātmavyāvṛttaṃ tadanyatvaviśiṣṭātmatvameva pakṣatāvacchedakamityāśaṅkāṃ nirasyati -- nāpīti || virodhāditi || bhedābhedāyorvirodhānna pakṣatāvacchedakasādhyabhāvasambhavaḥ |
svavirodhidharmadharmitāvacchedakakasvaprakārakaparokṣajñānānabhyupagamāditi bhāvaḥ |
yadyapi tanmate bhedasāmānyasya na pāramārthikabhedābhāvena virodhaḥ |
tathāpi prātibhāsikabhedasyāvyāvartakatvādvyāvahārikabhedasya ca paraṃ pratyasiddheḥ pāramārthikabhedasyaiva pakṣakukṣau niveśyatayā dhruvo virodhaḥ |
vastutaḥ siddhānte sattātraividhyānabhyupagamena bhedamātrasyaivābhedavirodhitayā tadrītyaitadabhidhānamiti dhyeyam |
vimatapadena vimativiśeṣaviṣayatvasyaiva vivakṣitatayā tāvanmātrasya pakṣatāvacchedakatvaṃ nānupapannamityāha -- paramātmana iti || caitrādyā ityādiḥ |
atra caitratvādīnāmananugatatvāttadavacchinnadharmikasamūhālambanavimativiṣayatvasya pakṣatāvacchedakatvānusaraṇam |
nanvevamātmatvādikameva pakṣatāvacchedakīkṛtya paramātmabhedavādinaṃ prati tadanyadharmāvacchinne tadabhedasādhanamasaṅgatamata āha -- anyatheti || bhedavādinā''tmatvādipakṣatāvacchedakīkaraṇa ityarthaḥ || asambhavāditi || paramātmabhedasyātmatvasāmānādhikaraṇyena sādhane yāvajjīveṣu tadasiddhestadavacchedena sādhane paramātmani bādhāttaditaratvena pakṣaviśeṣaṇe ca paraṃ pratyasiddhestaditaratvena pratīyamānatvoktau punaruktabādhaprasaṅgādbhedavādinā'pyuktavimativiṣayatvameva pakṣatāvacchedakīkartavyamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanvātmatvajātervyaktibhedaniyatatvena vyaktyabhedaparyavasitaniruktasādhyavyāpyatvāsambhava ityata āha -- ātmatvamiti || svaprakāśatvamityavedyatvamabhimatam |
na tu svaviṣayaprakāśatvam |
svasyaivāsiddheḥ |
parasyāsiddhistu vakṣyate |
nanvīdṛśānumānena jīvaparamātmābhedasiddhāvapi kathaṃ mitho jīvābhedasiddhiriti cenna |
sarveṣvekābhedasya sarvaikyaparyavasāyitvāt |
etaccopalakṣaṇam |
vimatā ātmānaścaitrātmano na bhidyante ātmatvāccaitrātmavadityādyanumānamapi grāhyam |
vimatapadaprayogaprayojanaṃ vaktumākṣipati -- nanviti || aṃśe siddhasādhanāditi || nanu yatrānitye vāṅmanase ityatra pakṣatāvacchedakanānātvaṃ tatrānityā vāgiti buddheruddeśyāyāḥ siddheraṃśataḥ siddhasādhanamiti maṇikāreṇoktatvādatrātmatvamekameva pakṣatāvacchedakamiti kathamaṃśataḥ siddhasādhanamiti cet atra praṣṭavyam |
kiṃ pakṣatāvacchedakanānātvamevāṃśe siddhasādhane prayojakamutoddeśyapratītisiddhirapīti |
nādyaḥ |
anvayato vyatirekataśca vyabhicārāt |
tathāhi |
na tāvat yatra pakṣatāvacchekanānātvaṃ tatrāṃśe siddhasādhanamiti niyamaḥ |
vanaparvatau vahnimantau dhūmādityatra vanaparvatatvarūpapakṣatāvacchedakanānātve'pyaṃśataḥ siddhasādhanābhāvāt |
nāpi yatra pakṣatāvacchedakanānātvābhāvaḥ tatrāṃśe siddhasādhanābhāvaḥ iti niyamaḥ |
dravyāṇyanityāni guṇāśrayabhāvāt |
nāpi yatra pakṣatāvacchedakanānātvābhāvaḥ tatrāṃśe siddhasādhanābhāvaḥ iti niyamaḥ |
dravyāṇyanityāni guṇāśrayatvāt ghaṭavadityatra pakṣatāvacchekasya dravyatvasyaikatve'pi pṛthivyādikāryadravye anityatvasya siddhatvenāṃśataḥ siddhasādhanasadbhāvāt |
dvitīye tūddeśyapratītisiddhereva prayojakatvamastu |
āvaśyakatvāllāghavādvyabhicārābhāvācca |
natu pakṣatāvacchedakanānātvasyāpi |
anāvaśyakatvādgauravādvyabhicārācca |
nanvatroddeśyapratītisiddhirapi nāsti |
tathāhi |
kimatrātmatvasāmānādhikaraṇyena sarvatrātmābhedaviṣayā samūhālambanarūpaikā buddhiruddeśyā uta sārvatrikatvādyudāsīnā |
dvitīye paramātmani tadabhedasiddhau siddhasādhanameva |
nāṃśataḥ siddhasādhanaṃ sārvatrikatvādyudāsīnāyā eva buddheruddeśyatvena tasyā ekadeśasiddhāvapi siddha(sādhana)tvāt |
ādye kathamaṃśataḥ siddhasādhanam |
ekadeśasiddhāvapyuddeśyāyāḥ sarvatra samūhālambanabuddherasiddheriti cet ucyate |
uddeśyapratītisiddhirapi hi na svarūpeṇa siddhasādhanaprayojikā |
kintu pakṣatāvighaṭanadvārā'numitipratibandhakatvenaiva |
tacca kvacitsādhyasiddherapyasti |
kvacitsādhyaniścaye tadaṃśe sandehasidhyabhāvayorasambhavena tadubhayaghaṭitapakṣatāvirahāt |
atra ca paramātmani sādhyasiddhau tadaṃśe pakṣatāviraheṇa na sarvātmaviśeṣyakā paramātmābhedaviśeṣaṇakā ekā samūhālambanarūpā'numitirityanumitipratibandhakatayā bhavatyevāṃśe siddhasādhanaṃ doṣaḥ |
na ca siṣādhayiṣādaśāyāṃ sādhyasiddheranumityapratibandhakatvānna tadaṃśe'numitipratibandha iti vācyam |
siṣādhayiṣābhāvadaśāyāmetadanavatārāt |
anyathoktarītyā siddhasādhanasyāpi doṣatvaṃ na syāt |
naca paramātmani paramātmatvarūpeṇa paramātmābhede niścite'pyātmatvena tatsandehādiḥ sambhavatīti vācyam |
viśeṣaniścayasya sāmānyataḥ saṃśayādivirodhitvena tādṛśaniścayesatitathāvidhasandehādyayogāt |
anyathaitatparvatatvena vahnimattayā niścite parvatatvena tatsandehāpattyā tatra vahnyarthino niḥśaṅkapravṛttirna syāt |
vyaktametatsarvaṃ tarkatāṇḍava iti saṅkṣepaḥ |
|| viśiṣṭamiti || ātmatvamiti vartate |
atra tu na pūrvoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ || sādhyeneti || sādhyenābhedena saha pakṣatāvacchedakībhūtaparamātmetaratve satyātmatvasya virodhādityarthaḥ |
tathāca vahnyādisamānādhikaraṇasyaiva parvatatvādeḥ pakṣatāvacchedakatvadarśanena paramātmābhedāsamānādhikaraṇasya paramātmetaratve satyātmatvasya na pakṣatāvacchedakatvamiti bhāvaḥ || bhrāntyeti || tathāca paramātmetaratvasyāvāstavatvānna tasya vāstavābhedena virodha iti bhāvaḥ || bhrāntisambhaveneti || uktabhrāntikāraṇasya paramātmanaḥ sakāśātpṛthagupalabhyamānatvasya tadrūpeṣu matsyādiṣvapi sāmyāditi bhāvaḥ |
vimatapadaprayogalabdhamāha -- paramātmana iti || nanu paramātmano bhidyante na veti vipratipattigocaratvaṃ na pakṣatāvacchedakaṃ gauravāditi cenna |
laghubhūtāvacchedakāntare satyeva gauravasya doṣatvāt |
yathā dhūmatve laghubhūte sati gurūbhūtaṃ nīlaghūmatvaṃ na hetutāvacchedakam |
na cātrāvacchedakāntaraṃ laghubhūtamasti yenāsya gauraveṇa parityāga iti bhāvaḥ |
idameva vipakṣe bādhakena draḍhayati -- anyatheti || uktapakṣatāvacchedakānaṅgīkāra ityarthaḥ |
tatvavādinā hi vimatā ātmānaḥ paramārthato bhidyante tadviruddhadharmavattvāditi sādhanīyam |
tatra praṣṭavyaṃ kimatrapakṣatāvacchedakamiti |
yadyātmatvaṃ tarhi tadavacchinnaparamātmano'pi pakṣatvāpattau tatra paramātmabhedābhāvenāṃśe bādhaḥ |
nāpi paramātmetaratvaviśiṣṭam |
paramātmetaratvasyādhunāpyasiddheḥ |
nāpi bhrāntyā paramātmetaratayā pratīyamānatvam |
paramātmanyapi paramātmetaratvabhrāntisambhavena tasyāpi pakṣatvenādyapakṣoktadoṣatādavasthyāt |
tasmāttenāpi paramātmano bhidyante na veti vipratipattigocaratvasyaiva pakṣatāvacchedakatvamabhyupetya uktarītyā tatra prāptadoṣaḥ pariharaṇīyaḥ |
evamatrāpīti doṣaparihārayorubhayatra sāmye ekatra pakṣapātāyogāt |
yathāhuḥ `yatrobhayoḥ samo doṣaḥ parihāro'pi vā samaḥ |
naikaḥ paryanuyoktavyastādṛgarthavicāraṇa' iti |
nanvātmatvajāteranekasamavetatvanirvāhāyātmanāmanekatvamaṅgīkāryam |
anekatvābhāvaśca sādhyate |
ato hetusādhyayoranyonyavirodhena vyāptigraho'nupapanna ityāśaṅkāṃ parihartumāha -- ātmatveti || jātirna cet tarhi kimiti pṛcchati -- kintarhīti || uttaramāha -- svaprakāśatvamiti || ātmanaḥ svaprakāśatvamavedyatvarūpamabhimatam |
natu svaviṣayaprakātvarūpam |
kartṛkarmavirodhena māyāvādibhirātmanaḥ svaviṣayaprakāśatvānaṅgīkārāditi dhyeyam |
tataḥ kimityata āha -- teneti || ātmatvasya jātitvānaṅgīkāreṇetyarthaḥ |
kiñca vimatāni śarīrāṇi devadattasyaiva bhogāyatanāni śarīratvāddevadattaśarīravat |
atra bhogāyatanatvātyantābhāvānadhikaraṇatvaṃ sādhyam |
tena suptamūrcchitamṛtaśarīrādiṣu na bādhaḥ |
nāpi vyabhicāraḥ |
(na bādho na vyabhicāraḥ) |
nanu daivadattasyaivetyatra bhoktṛtvenāprasaktasyācetanasyaivakāreṇa vyavacchedāyogāccetanāntaraṃ tadvyāvartyaṃ vācyam |
tenātmāntarasiddhiḥ |
evakāraparityāge ca sarveṣāṃ śarīrāṇāṃ pratiniyatabhoktṛkāṇāmapi devadattabhogāyatanatvasyāpyupapattyā'rthāntarāditi cenna |
paramataprāptasyaiva bhoktrantarasyaivakāreṇa vyāvartyatvāt |
ātmābhede'numānāntaramapyāha -- kiñca vimatānīti |
atra devadattaśarīre siddhasādhanātāvāraṇāya vimatānītyuktam |
tathā ca devadattaśarīrātiriktaśarīrāṇīti labhyate |
teṣāṃ ca devadattamātrabhogāyatanatvasādhane yajñadattādisarvātmanāṃ devadattenābhedalābha iti bhāvaḥ |
nanu suptamūcchritamṛtaśarīrāṇāṃ pakṣatve tatra bhogāyanatvābhāvena bādhaḥ pakṣātiriktatve vyabhicāra ityata āha -- bhogāyatanatvātyantābhāveti || suptyādidaśāyāṃ bhogāyatanatvābhāve'pi tatpūrvaṃ vā taduttaraṃ vā bhogāyatanatvasambhavena tadatyantābhāvānadhikaraṇatāyāsteṣvapi sadbhāvena pakṣakoṭipraviṣṭeṣvapi teṣu na bādhaḥ |
pakṣakoṭipraviṣṭatvādeva vyabhicāro'pi netyarthaḥ |
nanvātmanāmabhedasādhanārthamidamanumānam |
tatra devadattasyaivetyevakāreṇaiva tadvyāvartyaṃ devadattātiriktacetanasiddhirbhavati |
ataḥ svavyāhatametadityāśaṅkate -- nanviti || acetanameva tadvyāvartyaṃ kinna syādityata uktaṃ bhoktṛtvenāprasaktasyeti || acetanasyeti śeṣaḥ |
tarhyevakāraḥ parityajyata ityata āha -- evakāreti || pratiniyatabhoktṛkāṇāmapi bhūtāveśanyāyena devadattabhogāyatanatvasyāpyupapattyā'rthāntaratvamityarthaḥ || paramataprāptasyeti || paramataprasaktasyaiva cetanāntarasya vyāvartanānna svavyāghāta iti bhāvaḥ |
vimatānīti |
yajñadattādiśarīrāṇītyarthaḥ |
pakṣadharmatābalādabhedasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
devadattasya parakāyapraveśenārthāntaravāraṇāyaivakāraḥ || na bādho nāpi vyabhicāra iti || pakṣatve bādho na cedvyabhicāraśca netyarthaḥ |
evakāraprayoge'pyanumānaṃ dūṣayati -- nanviti || ātmāntarasiddhiriti || anyathā svato vyāvṛttatadvyāvartakaivakārāyogāditi bhāvaḥ |
niṣedhaprāptireva tantraṃ na svarūpasattvamiti bhāvaḥ |
|| kiñca vimatānīti |
yadyapyanenānumānena jīvānāṃ parasparābhedasiddhāvapīśvarābhedasiddhau na prakṛtopayogaḥ |
tathā'pi prasaṅgāgataṃ bodhyam |
yadvā `śubhaṃ pibatyasau nityaṃ nāśubhaṃ sa hariḥ pibet' ityukterbhagavatastaccharīrāvacchinnasārabhoktṛtvamātmabhedavādibhiraṅgīkāryam |
anena cānumānena devadattamātrabhogāyatanatvasādhane īśvarābhedaḥ sidhyatīti bhāvaḥ || bhogāyatanatvātyantābhāveti || yadyapyatra vyāptirdaiśikī |
tathā ca suptamūrchitamṛtaśarīreṣu pakṣīkṛteṣu sapakṣīkṛteṣu ca tattadavasthāyāṃ bhogābhāve kadācidbhogāyatanatvasya vidyamānatvānna bādho na vyabhicāraḥ |
na ca vācyaṃ parimāṇabhedena dravyabhedātsuptādiśarīrāṇāṃ pūrvottaraśarīrabhedātteṣāṃ kadā'pi bhogāyatanatvābhāvāt bhavati bādho vyabhicāraśceti |
tathātve vivakṣayā'pyanistārāt |
teṣāṃ śarīrāṇāṃ bhogatatprāgabhāvadhvaṃsādhikaraṇatvena tadatyantābhāvānadhikaraṇatvāt |
tathā'pi vyāptidvayena pravṛttamidamanumānamityāśayenedam || nanviti || evakāreṇa bhoktṛtvenāprasaktasya jaḍavyāvartanāyogena bhoktṛtvena prasaktacetanāntarameva vyāvartanīyam |
tathā ca devadattātiriktacetanabhogānāyatanatve sati devadattabhogāyatanatvaṃ sādhyārthaḥ phalitaḥ |
evañca tādṛśasādhyasya prasiddhisampādakena prakṛtānumānena vā devadattātiriktacetanasiddhiḥ syāt |
evakāraparityāge ca pratiniyatabhoktṝṇāmapi bhūtāveśanyāyena devadattabhogāyatanatvasambhavādabhedaṃ vinā'pi sādhyaparyavāsānādarthāntaratetyarthaḥ || paramateti || tathā ca devadattātiriktatvena parābhimatacetanāntarabhogānāyatanatve sati devadattabhogāyatanatvaṃ sādhyārtha iti nobhābhyāṃ devadattātiriktacetanasiddhiḥ |
paramatāvacchedakatvena sādhyakoṭiniveśasyāyameva vadatītyāderivārthasattāparyantatvābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
evaṃ paramātmanā sarvajīvānāmabhedaṃ bahujīvavādena prasādhyaikajīvavādamavalambya sarvaśarīrasya jīvaikyaṃ sādhayati -- vimatānīti || paridṛśyamānadevadattapiṇḍavyatiriktanikhilaśarīrāṇītyarthaḥ |
devadattīyajanmāntarīyaśarīreṣūktasādhyasya siddhatve'pyavacchedakāvacchedena sādhane siddhasādhanatā na doṣa ityāśayenedam |
athavā devadattasambandhipūrvāparanikhilaśarīravyatiriktānyadīyasarvaśarīrasādhāraṇasya rūpasya jñānimukhādinā kathañcidavagame tadrūpaṃ vā tadavacchinnadharmikā vipratipattirvā pakṣatāvacchedikā'to nāṃśe siddhasādhanatā'vakāśaḥ |
ajñānināṃ tu na tādṛśadharmo buddhipathamāroḍhumarhati |
sarvaśarīriṇāṃ devadattaśarīratvapakṣe devadattakarmānirmitatvādidharmāṇāṃ pakṣībhūtaśarīreṣvapi sambhavāt |
athavaikajīvavādapakṣe hiraṇyagarbhanāmakaśarīrābhimānyeka eva jīvaḥ sarvaśarīrābhimānyanādito'nuvartate |
etāvatkālaparyantaṃ na kasyāpi muktistasya muktyanantaraṃ na kimapi jagadavaśiṣyata ityekajīvavādasthitiḥ |
tathā ca devadattapadena na śarīrābhimānī prakṛte vivakṣitaḥ |
tathā ca hiraṇyagarbhaśarīrarūpapiṇḍamanāditaḥ sthitamiti śāstrasiddham |
tadanyaśarīratvāvacchedena hiraṇyagarbhasyaiva bhogāyatanatvaṃ sādhyata iti nāṃśe siddhasādhanatā'vakāśa iti dhyem |
etenaitatprakāreṇa pūrvāparagrantheṣvavacchedakāvacchedena sādhane'pyaṃśe siddhasādhanatāyā doṣatvopagamasyaiva sattvānmadhye'traiva tadanaṅgīkāre sandarbhavirodha iti nirastam || devadattasyaiveti || bhogāyatanatvaṃ bhogāvacchedakatvam |
bhogo'nyaḥ sukhādibhūtāveśanyāyena devadattetarabhogāvacchedake'pi śarīre devadattabhogasambhavena noddeśyasakalajīvaikyasiddhirityarthāntaravāraṇāyaiveti |
devadattetarabhogāyatanatvābhāvaviśiṣṭatvaṃ tadarthaḥ |
nanu devadattetaracetanābhāvājjaḍakartṛkabhogāprasiddheritarakartṛkabhogarūpapratiyogyaprasiddhyā'numātṛmate tadabhāvāprasiddhiḥ |
na ca devadattetaracetanatvaprakārakaprativādyabhimataviṣayībhūtacetanatvena niveśya tatkartṛkabhogāyatanatvābhāvo niveśyaḥ |
parābhimatyavacchedakatayā sādhyakoṭiniveśasyāyamiha vadatītivadarthasattāparyantatvābhāvāditi vācyam |
parābhimaterbhramatve'pi tadviṣayatā cetane vā jaḍe vā vācyā |
ādye devadattacetanasyaiva svātiriktatvabhramagocaratayā tadbhogāyatanatvābhāvasādhane bādhānistārāt |
dvitīye jaḍakartakabhogāprasiddheriti cenna |
svakartṛkabhogavattvasambandhāvacchinnapratiyogitāketarābhāvavaiśiṣṭyasyaiva niviṣṭatvāt |
na ca tasya vyadhikaraṇasambandhāvacchinnapratiyogitāka(kābhāva)tayā kevalānvayitayā'vyāvartakatvamiti vācyam |
pareṣāṃ devadattetarakartṛkabhogamaṅgīkurvatāṃ tasya vyadhikaraṇasambandhatvābhāvena tanmataprasaktārthāntaranirāsakatayā sārthakyādityanupadameva granthakṛtā vakṣyamāṇatvāt |
lokāyatamatarītyaikajīvavādyekadeśimate ekasyaiva śarīrasya sajīvatvamanyeṣāṃ svapnadṛṣṭaśarīravanna jīvatvamityaṅgīkārāttadrītyā vā niścetanatvena paryavasānavāraṇāya viśeṣyabhāgaḥ sārthakaḥ |
nanvevamapyarthāntaravāraṇamaśakyameva |
devadattetarabhogāvacchedake'pi śarīre samayaviśeṣe tadabhāvena tadghaṭitasambandhāvacchinnatadabhāvo'pi vartata eveti tadviśiṣṭabhogāyatanatvarūpasādhyasya tatrābādhāt |
na ca vācyamuktasambandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvo'tyantābhāva eva prāgabhāvādīnāṃ pratiyogitāyāḥ sambandhānavacchinnatvāt |
tasya pratiyogivattatprāgabhāvadhvaṃsābhyāmapi viruddhatvasyāgre grantha eva sphuṭatayā kadācidanyabhogāvacchedakatvasattvena(sattve) tadatyantābhāvāsattvena tadghaṭitasambandhāvacchinnetarātyantābhāvaḥ kathaṃ tatra varteteti bhogātyantābhāvasya tatrāsambhave'pi bhogasyaiva sambandhaghaṭakatayā tatprāgabhāvadhvaṃsayoḥ sambandhāghaṭakatvena tadghaṭitasambandhāvacchinnātyāntābhāvasyāpi bhogānadhikaraṇakāladeśāvacchedena sattvāt |
ghaṭasaṃyogaprāgabhāvadhvaṃsādimatyapi bhūtale saṃyogaśūnya(tā)daśāyāṃ saṃyogena ghaṭo nāstīti pratītyanurodhena ghaṭātyantābhāvavatsaṃyogasambandhāvacchinnendhanatvādyavacchinnaprāgabhāvasyāpi pūrvapakṣagranthādau gadādharaprabhṛtibhirupapāditatvena prāgabhāvadhvaṃsayorapi dharmasambandhāvacchinnatvasattvācca |
tathācārthāntaratā durvāre(raive)ti cenna |
yāvatsvādhikaraṇakālāvacchinnatvasya sādhye viśeṣaṇatvāṅgīkāreṇa vā kālānavacchinnasvarūpasambandhena sādhyatāṅgīkāreṇa voktaviśiṣṭasādhyasya voktasambandhena sādhyasya vā yatkiñcitkāle viśeṣaṇasattve'sambhavenārthāntaraprasakterabhāvādityāśayāt |
tarhi viśeṣyasya sārvakālikatvābhāvādbādho devadattaśarīre vyabhicāraśca |
hiraṇyagarbhavivakṣāyāmapi tasya pralaye suptatayā bhogābhāvādvyabhicāraścetyata āha -- bhogāyatanatveti || bhogāvacchedakatvātyantābhāvasya dhvaṃsaprāgabhāvayoratyāntābhāvavirodhitāmate kathamapyasambhavātsādhyasya tatsambandhasya vā sārvakālikatvena na bādhavyabhicārayoḥ prasaktiḥ |
uktavivakṣāyāmapyevakārārtharūpaviśeṣaṇaṃ deyameva |
anyathā'nyakartṛkabhogadaśāyāmapyuktaviśeṣyabhāgasyā(pya)kṣatatayā'rthāntarānistarāt |
yadvā devadattasyaiva bhogāyatanatvamityatraivakārārthāprasiddhiparihārāya svāvacchinnabhogatvavyāpakadevadattavṛttikatvaṃ sādhyārthaḥ |
svāvacchinnatvasvāvacchinnabhogavṛttibhedapratiyogitānirūpitādheyatāsambandhāvacchinnāvacchedakatāsambandhāvacchinnasvavṛttipratiyogitākābhāvavaddevadattakatvaitadubhayasambandhena śarīraviśiṣṭabhogo'vacchedakatāsambandhena sādhyamiti paryavasitam |
kadācidanyabhogāvacchedakatve tadīyabhoge tatra devadattavṛttitvābhāvena vyāpakatābhaṅgāpattyā'rthāntaratānirāsaḥ |
nanu devadattādisuptyādidaśāyāṃ tādṛśaśarīrāvacchinnabhogābhāvena taddhaṭitasādhyābhāvena bādhavyabhicārau syātāmityāśaṅkāṃ pariharati -- bhogāyatanatveti || tādṛśabhogāyatanatvātyantābhāvānadhikaraṇa(tva)meva tadarthaḥ |
taccoktarītyā kālika(kā)vyāpyavṛttīti na bādhavyabhicārāviti bhāvaḥ |
yadyapi pakṣe hetvadhikaraṇe ca yadā kadā vā sādhyasattvamātreṇa na bādhavyabhicārayoḥ prasaktirbādhaśarīre vyabhicāraśarīre vā sādhyābhāve pratiyogivaiyyadhikaraṇyasya niviṣṭatvāt |
anyathā kapisaṃyogyetadvṛkṣatvādityasyāpi duṣṭatvāpatteḥ |
tathā'pi pratiyogivaiyyadhikaraṇyādiprayojanoktipūrvakaṃ tacchaṅkānirāse gauravaṃ syādityuktaśaṅkāmabhyupetyaiva vyāpyavṛttisādhye paryavasānapūrvakamuktaśaṅkānirāsaḥ kṛta ityavadheyam |
anye tu sādhyaṃ sādhyatulyaṃ sādhyasamaniyatamityarthaḥ |
tathā ca gandhavatī pṛthivītvādityāderbādhitatvavāraṇāya bādhavyabhicārayoratyantābhāvasyaiva niviṣṭatayā kadāciduktasādhyarūpapratiyogimati tadatyantābhāvasambhavānna bādhavyabhicārau |
sādhyaṃ tūktabhogāyatanatvarūpaṃ yathāśrutameveti bhāva ityāhuḥ |
kecid bhogāyatanatvātyantābhāveti yadyapyatra vyāptirdaiśikī |
tathā ca mūrchitatvādidaśāyāṃ bhogāyatanatvāsattve'pi kadācitpakṣasapakṣayoḥ sattvādeva na bādho na vyabhicāraśca |
na cāvayavaparimāṇabhedadarśanādinā pūrvāparaśarīrabhedamaṅgīkṛtyedamiti vācyam |
tādṛśeṣveva katipayaśarīreṣu bhogatatprāgabhāvataddhvaṃsānāṃ trayāṇāmapyabhāvenātyantābhāvasyāvaśyakatayoktasādhyavivakṣāyāmapyetaddoṣatādavasthyāt |
tathā'pi vyāptidvayena pravṛttamidamanumānamityāśayenedamityāhuḥ |
atredaṃ vivecanīyam |
dvitīyavyāptirna kālikī |
atra śarīratvabhogāyatanatvayoḥ kālikasambandhena hetusādhyabhāvānāpannatayā tatsambandhena vyāpteḥ prakṛtānupayogāt |
na ca kālikena sādhyahetubhāvo'pyastīti vācyam |
śarīre kālatvābhāvena tatra hetusādhyayoḥ kālikenābhāvāt |
yathākathañcittatra kālopādhitvamaṅgīkṛtya tasyāpi kālikasambandhena kiñcidadhikaraṇatvamāśritya tatra kālikasambandhena sādhyahetvaṅgīkāre ghaṭāderapi pakṣatāsambhavena tatparityāge bījābhāvaḥ |
kālikasambandhena śarīratvasya hetutve bhogatatprāgabhāvataddhvaṃsānadhikaraṇatvena tadatyantābhāvādhikaraṇe ghaṭādirūpa(kāle)niṣkṛṣṭasādhyasyāpi vyabhicāraḥ |
saṃsārasyānāditayā'nādikālataḥ prāptadevadattabhogadhvaṃsādeḥ kālikasambandhena sattvena na tadatyantābhāvastatretyucyate tadā śarīrarūpakāle devadattetarakartṛkabhogasattve'pyuktasādhyābādhāccetanaikyāsiddhyā'rthāntarataiva |
devadattetarabhogataddhvaṃsādeḥ sattve tadatyantābhāvasya kāle'yogenaivakārārthatayā taditarakartṛkabhogātyantābhāvasya sādhyakoṭau niveśādevārthāntaravāraṇamityaṅgīkāre'pi tāvataiveṣṭasiddhau viśeṣyabhāgavaiyyarthyaṃ niścetanavādinā'pyanubhavasiddhabhogasya kāle'palāpasya kartumaśakyatvena bhogasāmānyābhāvamādāya paryavasānāyogāt |
kiñca śarīrasya kālatvāṅgīkāreṇānumānapravṛttau kālarūpeṣu sarvaśarīreṣu cetanaikyavādināṃ yadā kadācitkālikasambandhena devadattabhogasya sattvātkathaṃ bādhaprasaktiḥ |
nanu bhogasāmānyānadhikaraṇakālamātravṛtti śarīraṃ prasiddhaṃ yena tatra bādhaprasaktiḥ |
suptamūrcchādidaśāpannatacchaśīreṣu bādhodbhāvanaṃ granthakṛtāmatyantāsamañjasaṃ ca syāt |
api ca kāle dhvaṃsaprāgabhāvābhyāṃ sahātyantābhāvasya virodhena sattve'nityavastupratiyogitākātyantābhāvasāmānyasya kālāsaṃsparśaprāptyā teṣāmalīkatvaprāptyā tādṛśamatasya vicārāsahatvena sādhyapariṣkārāyogaḥ |
na hi vimatāni śarīrāṇīti nyāyavākye kālikasambandhena hetusādhyabhāvajñāpakaṃ kimapyasti |
nāpi parakīyaiḥ svagrantheṣu tathoktamapi yena granthakṛtāṃ tadanusāreṇa śaṅkāsamādhānayoḥ prasaktirityapi vaktuṃ śakyeta |
ata eva yadyatkālāvacchedena śarīratvādhikaraṇatvaṃ yatra tatkālāvacchedena devadattasyaiva bhogāyatanatvaṃ tatreti balātsetsyamāne sarvakālāvacchedenoktabhogāyatanatvarūpasādhye bādhodbhāvanaṃ vyāptau vyabhicārodbhāvanaṃ (ca) yuktamiti parāstam |
hetusādhyaśarīre kālāpraveśena tadghaṭitasāmānyavyāptervaktumaśakyatvāt |
asmaduktarītyā'rthāntaravāraṇāya sādhyasya kālaghaṭitatve'pi hetau tanniveśe prayojanābhāva eva |
hetau tanniveśe vaikasyā eva daiśikavyāpteranumānāṅgatvaprāptyā vyāptidvayena pravṛttamidamanumānamityāderasaṅgatireva |
tasmādetadāśayaṃ vayaṃ na vijānīmaḥ kintu mahāntaḥ praṣṭavyā iti |
atredaṃ pratibhāti |
pratiyogivaiyyadhikaraṇyadhaṭitāghaṭitavyāptidvayenetyarthaḥ |
sādhyasya vyāpyavṛttitve vyāptidvayamapi ghaṭata iti pratiyogivaiyyadhikaraṇyāghaṭitavyāptau vyabhicāraśaṅkābādhoktirapi sādhyābhāvamātraṃ pakṣe bādho na tu tallakṣaṇe pratiyogivaiyadhikaraṇyaṃ niveśanīyamityāśayeneti svarītyaivakāravaiyyarthyamāśaṅkya vādiprativādinoranyataramate prayojanasadbhāvamātreṇa tasya prayojanatvamityāśayena prativādimatarītyā'rthāntaravāraṇarūpaṃ prayojanaṃ darśayati -- nanvityādinā || cetanaikyasādhanā(kā)numānena cetanabhedasyaiva siddhāvatyantānabhimatapadārthasyaiva siddhyā'bhimatāsiddhyā'rthāntarataiveti bhāvaḥ |
yadyapyetadanumānaṃ jīvaikyasādhakatvājjīveśvarayoraikyāsādhakatvātprakṛtānanuguṇamiva pratibhāti |
tathā'pi rāmakṛṣṇādiśarīrāṇāṃ līlāgṛhīteśvaraśarīrāṇāmapi pakṣakoṭipraviṣṭatvādabhimatārthasiddhyā prakṛtopayoga iti hṛdayam |
atraivānumānāntaramāha -- kiṃ ceti || bhogo'nubhavaḥ |
āyatanamavacchedakam |
tathāca devadattabhinnabhogānavacchedakatve sati devadattabhogāvacchedakānītyarthaḥ |
satyantavyāvṛttiḥ svayameva vakṣyate |
bhokturdevadattatvasiddhaye viśeṣyabhāgaḥ |
athaivaṃ bhoktraikyasiddhāvapi kathamabhokturīśvarasya jīvābhedasiddhiriti cedatrāha |
īśvarasyāpi tattaccharīrāvacchinnarasabhoktṛtvāttadaikyasiddhiriti |
taccintyam |
asmadādiśarīrasyeśvarabhogaṃ prati sādhanaviśeṣatvarūpāvacchedakatvāsambhavāt |
anyathā devadattaśarīrasyāpyubhayabhoktṛkatvena dṛṣṭāntatvānupapatteḥ |
vastutaścaitanaikye lāghavarūpānukūlatarkabalāt sakalacetanaikyasiddhāvuddheśyasiddhirbodhyā |
jīvaikyasiddhimātramatroddeśyamityapyāhuḥ |
nanvatra vimatatvāvacchedakacaitrādiśarīratvaṃ vimatatvamātraṃ vā pakṣatāvacchedakam |
ādye suptimūrcchāvasthāviśiṣṭaśarīre bādhaḥ |
tatkāle sākṣyadhīnasukhaduḥkhānubhavāṅgīkāre'pi śarīrasya tadavacchedakatve pramāṇābhāvāt |
mṛtaśarīre bādhatādavasthyācca |
evaṃ devadattīyamṛtaśarīre vyabhicāraḥ |
na ca viśiṣṭasya viśeṣyānatiriktatayā viśeṣyībhūtaśarīre ca kadācit sādhyasattvānnoktadoṣa iti vācyam |
viśiṣṭasyaupādhikatvena kevalaviśeṣyānatiriktatvāyogāt |
na caivaṃ vṛkṣaḥ kapisaṃyogī etatvādityādāvapi mūlāvacchinnavṛkṣādau sādhyābhāvasattvādbādhādiprasaṅga iti vācyam |
vṛkṣatvāvacchedena sādhyatāyāmiṣṭāpatteḥ |
sāmānādhikaraṇyena sādhane tasyādoṣatvāt |
dvitīye tu viśiṣṭasya vimatyaviṣayatvena pakṣabahirbhūtatayā bādhānavakāṣe'pi tatraiva vyabhicāraḥ |
na ca mṛtaśarīrādau ceṣṭāvattvarūpaśarīratvābhāvādeva na vyabhicāra iti vācyam |
ceṣṭāśūnyasuptādiśarīre'siddhivāraṇāya pūrvavajjātirūpasya ceṣṭātyantābhāvānadhikaraṇatvarūpasya vā śarīratvasya hetutvāvaśyakatvādityata āha -- bhogāyatanatveti || na bādha iti || suptādiśarīrāṇāṃ bhogāyatanatvadhvaṃsādhikaraṇatvena tadatyantābhāvānadhikaraṇatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
syādetat |
devadattayajñadattādīnāṃ mitho vyāvahārikabhedābhyupagamena yajñadattādiśarīrāṇāṃ devadattādibhinnabhogānavacchedakatvābhāvādbādhaḥ |
na ca pāramārthikatvena bhedo viśeṣaṇīyaḥ |
tathāsati paramārthato devadattabhinnayajñadattādisiddhiprasaṅgenāpasiddhāntāt |
na ca vastuto devadattabhinnaghaṭādibhogānavacchedakatvenāpi sādhyaparyavasānānna jīvabhedasiddhiprasaṅga iti vācyam |
ghaṭādibhogasya kenāpyanabhyupagatatvena tadavacchedakatvasyāprasaktatayā tatpratiṣedhaparyavasānānupapatterityāśaṅkate -- nanviti || nanūktasādhye satyantānupādānānnaitaddoṣāvakāśa ityata āha -- evakāreti || pratiniyatabhoktṛkāṇāṃ mitho vibhinnabhoktṛkāṇām |
tathācaikaikaśarīre bhoktṛdvayasiddhyā'pyanumānaparyavasānānna tadaikyasiddhirityarthaḥ |
na ca satyantopādāne'pi śarīrānavacchinnasauṣuptikabhogabhokturdevadattābhinnatvāsiddhyā'rthāntaratādavasthyamiti vācyam |
suptotthitasya sukhamahamasvāpsamityaikyānusandhānavirodhena tadbhedāsiddheriti bhāvaḥ || paramataprāptasyeti || ayamāśayaḥ |
satyantaghaṭakabhedasya pāramārthikatvena viśeṣaṇe'pi na jīvāntarasiddhiprasaṅgaḥ |
niṣedhyatvāt |
pāramārthikaśuktirajata nāstītivat |
na ca bhogāvacchedakatvamātramatra niṣidhyate, na tu bhoktrantaramiti vācyam |
bhogāvacchedakatvavati viśiṣṭaniṣedhasya bhoktrantaraniṣedha eva paryavasānāt |
niṣedhaśca paramataprāptabhoktrantarapratītinirāsārthamiti || yattu devadattātiriktatvena parābhimatacetanabhogānāyatanatve sati devadattabhogāyatanatvamiha sādhyārtha iti |
tanna |
devadattacetanasyaiva tadatiriktatvena parābhimatatvamaṅgīkarturmāyāvādino bādhaprasaṅgāt |
uktarītyaivopapattau parābhimatatvaparyantaniveśavaiyarthyācca |
-- abhedasādhakānumānāntaraṃ pratijānīte || kiñceti || anyaccābhedasādhakānumānamucyata ityarthaḥ |
kiṃ tadanumānamityata āha -- vimatānīti || atra śarīramātrasya pakṣatvedevadattaśarīrasyāpi pakṣatvāpattyāṃ'śe siddhasādhanam |
ato vimatānīti |
atra vimatānāṃ śarīrāṇāṃ devadattabhogasādhanatvaṃ devadattasya vimataśarīragatatvaṃ vinānopapannam |
taccaikasmin śarīre bhoktṛjīvadvayāyogena taccharīragatātmaikyaṃ vinā nopapadyata iti tatsiddhiḥ |
nanu suptamūrchitamṛtaśarīrāṇāṃ pakṣatvaṃ vā na vā |
ādye teṣāṃ bhogāyatanatvābhāvenāṃśe bādhaḥ |
dvitīye sādhyābhāvavatsuteṣu hetuvṛttyā vyabhicāra ityāśaṅkāṃ parihartumāha -- atreti || na bādho na vyabhicāra iti || pakṣatve bādho'pakṣatve vyabhicāra iti vivekaḥ |
teṣāṃ tadānīṃ bhogāyatanatvābhāve'pi kālāntare tatsattvena teṣu bhogāyatanatvā bhāvābhāvena tadanadhikaraṇatvarūpasādhyasya sattvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu sukhaduḥkhānyatarasākṣātkāro bhogaḥ |
sa ca mṛtaśarīre'nupapanno'pi suptamūrchitayorupapanna eva |
etāvantaṃ kālaṃ sukhamahamasvāpsamiti parāmarśena suptau sukhasākṣātkārāvagamāt |
`mohastu pariśeṣataḥ |
ardhaprāptiriti jñeyo duḥkhamātrapratismṛte'riti mohe duḥkhasākṣātkārāvagamāditi cenna |
muktiyogyasauṣuptikasukhasyātmasvarūpatayā anyasya prājñāliṅganamātrajanyatvena śarīrasya tatra hetutvābhāvāt |
evaṃ maurchikaduḥkhasya prāktanatāḍanādijanyatvena mūrchāvasthāpannaśarīrasya tatrāhetutvāt |
nanvathāpyaṃśe bādhasya kathaṃ doṣatvam |
vṛkṣaḥ kapisaṃyogīdravyatvādityatra bādhitāṃśamūlabhāgamavihāyābādhitāṃśe'grabhāge'numityutpattidarśanāditi cenna |
tatra vṛkṣatvasāmānādhikaraṇyena kvacidvṛkṣāṃśe saṃyogabuddheruddeśyatayā tadavighaṭakatvenāṃśe bādhasyādoṣatve'pi prakṛte vimataśarīratvasāmānādhikaraṇyena sarvaśarīreṣu devadattabhogasādhanatvabuddheruddeśyatayā mṛtādau bādhasya tadaṃśe'numitipratibandhakatvenoddeśyavighaṭakatayā doṣatvāditi dhyeyam |
devadattasyaivetyatraivakārasyāvaśyakatvamutānāvaśyakatvam |
ādye āha -- nanviti || evakārohidevadattānyasya bhoktṛtvaṃ vyāvartayati |
tatrānyo nācetanaḥ |
tasya sarvathā'bhoktṛtvenāprasaktatvāt |
aprasaktasya pratiṣedhāyogāt |
ataścetana evadevadattānya evakāreṇa vyāvartyo vācyaḥ |
naca tadyuktam |
apasiddhāntādityarthaḥ |
ityatra vidyamānaivakāreṇa |
tadvyāvartyam evakāravyāvartyam |
tena evakāravyāvartyacetanāntarakathanena |
dvitīye tvāha -- evakāreti || evakāraparityāge cārthāntarādityanvayaḥ |
tadupapādanāya sarveṣāmityādi |
tattaccharīrāṇāṃ tattadātmabhogasādhanatve'pi devadattabhogasādhanatvamapyupapadyate |
strīśarīrāṇāṃ puruṣabhogasādhanatvasya aśvādiśarīṇāṇāṃ sādyādibhogasādhanatvasya ca darśanāditi bhāvaḥ || pratiniyatabhoktṛkāṇāmiti || pratiśarīraṃ bhoktṛtvena niyatā ya ātmānastadvatāmapītyarthaḥ || paramataprāptasyaiveti || tatvavādimateprāptaṃ yadvimatabhoktṛśarīrāṇāṃ bhoktrantaraṃ devadattānyobhoktācetanastadvyāvartanādityarthaḥ |
cetanāntarasya mayā'naṅgīkārānnāpasiddhānta iti bhāvaḥ |
api ca bhedo mithyā bhedatvāccandrabhedavat |
mithyātvaṃ cādhiṣṭhānayāthātmyajñānanivartyatvam |
nanu prāmāṇikāprāmāṇikayoranugatasāmānyābhāvādasiddho heturiti cenna |
bhedatvena pratīyamānatvasyaiva hetutvāt |
evañca noktadoṣāvakāśaḥ |
pratīyamānatvaṃ hi pratītiviṣayatvam |
tacca pratītisambandhitvam |
pratītisambandhaśca svarūpamevetyatra prāmāṇikatvānapekṣaṇāt |
nanu pratīyamāne candre candrāntarasyāropitatvenāropitānāropitacandrayorbhedasya satyatvena (sattvena) sādhyavikalo dṛṣṭānta iti cenna |
candre bhedāropamātreṇa (candrāropamātreṇa) dvitvapratītyupapattau candrāntarasyāropāṅgīkāre gauravāt |
tarhi śuktikāyāṃ rajatābhedāropamātreṇedaṃ rajatamiti pratītyupapattau rajatāropāṅgīkāro vyartha iti cenna |
tatrādhiṣṭhānasyārajatatvena rajatopasthityarthaṃ rajatāropāṅgīkārāt |
rajatānupasthitau rajatābhedāropāsambhavāt |
naivaṃ prakṛte |
adhiṣṭhānasya candratvāt |
anumānāntaraṃ cāha -- api ceti || vimataḥ sarvo'pi bheda ityarthaḥ |
tathā ca bhedābhedayorviruddhatvābhedamātrasya mithyātve siddhe jīveśvarādīnāmabheda eva sidhyati |
tataścābhede pramāṇopanyāsaṃ pratijñāya bhedamithyātve pramāṇopanyāso'saṅgata iti śaṅkānavakāśa iti dhyeyam |
nanvatra kinnāma mithyātvam |
asattvaṃ cedapasiddhāntaḥ |
anirvācyatvaṃ cedaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā |
dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyaṃ cetyata āha -- mithyātvaṃ ceti || idaṃ candrabhede sampratipannameveti noktadoṣāvakāśa iti bhāvaḥ |
nanvātmatvaśarīratvādivannedaṃ bhedatvaṃ hetūkartuṃ śakyate |
tatra pakṣadṛṣṭāntayorubhayorapi prāmāṇikatvenānugatasāmānyasambhavāt |
atra vimatabhedasya vyāvahārikatvena prāmāṇikatve'pi dṛṣṭāntabhūcandrabhedasya prātibhāsikatayā'prāmāṇikatvenobhayānugata bhedatvarūpasāmānyāsambhavādityāśaṅkya niṣedhati || prāmāṇikāprāmāṇikayoriti || bhedatvena pratīyamānatvasyeti || bhedatvādityasya bhedatvena pratīyamānatvādityarthasya lakṣaṇayā vivakṣitatvādityarthaḥ |
kathamanenāpi kathitadoṣanirāsa ityata āha -- pratīyamānatvaṃ hīti |
nanvevamapyuktadoṣatādavasthyameva |
pratītiviṣayatāyāḥ pratītijanakakaraṇasannikarṣāśrayatā'sambhavādityata āha -- tacceti || sambandhaśca svarūpameveti || nātiriktaḥ saṃyogarūpaḥ kintu pratītivyāvartakatāmātrarūpaḥ pratītyullekhyatvalakṣaṇa eva vivakṣita ityarthaḥ |
etacca prāmāṇikāprāmāṇikayoḥ sādhāraṇa eveti bhāvaḥ |
śaṅkate -- pratīyamānasyeti || āropitānāropitacandrayoḥ prātibhāsikavyāvahārikacandrayoḥ || bhedasya sattveneti || mithyātve tayoranyonyatādātmyāpātāditi bhāvaḥ |
syādidaṃ sādhyavaikalyaṃ yadi candrāntaramāropitaṃ pṛthak syāt |
na caivam |
candrāntarāropasyaivābhāvāt |
imau candrāvityādidvitvapratītistvekasminneva candre bhedāropamātreṇopapadyate |
candrāntarāropamaṅgīkurvatā'pi tayorbhedapratītiravaśyamaṅgīkaraṇīyā |
imau bhinnāviti pratīterapyanubhavasiddhatvāt |
tato varamekasyaiva bhedāropasya kalpanam |
anyathā gauravaprasaṅgāt |
tasya ca bhedasya mithyātvātkvāsti sādhyavaikalyamiti pariharati -- candre bhedāropamātreṇeti || pratibandyā śaṅkate -- tarhīti |
tathā ca tatra yathā rajatābhedavadrajatasyāpyāropamaṅgīkṛtya prāmāṇikatvādgauravaṃ na doṣāyetyucyate |
tadvadatrāpyubhayāropāṅgīkāra evocitaḥ |
tathā cāropitānāropitayorbhedasya satyatvātsādhyavaikalyaṃ duṣpariharamiti bhāvaḥ |
na caivaṃ bhedāṅgīkāre āropitasya mithyātvaniyamātkvāsti sādhyavaikalyamiti vācyam |
ekasmiṃścandre āropitabhedasya mithyātve'pyāropitacandrāntarapratiyogikānāropitacandradharmikabhedasya satyatvāttadvivakṣayaivātra sādhyavaikalyakathanāt |
candre āropitabhedasyaivātra dṛṣṭāntatvena vivakṣitatvānna tatra sādhyavaikalyaśaṅkāvakāśa iti cet tarhi mā'stvāropitabhede sādhyavaikalyam |
āropitānāropitacandrabhede tvanaikāntyaṃ brūma ityāśayāt |
nanvevaṃ candrāntarāropāṅgīkāre tasyādhiṣṭhānādikaṃ vācyam |
` adhiṣṭhānaṃ ca sadṛśatathyavastudvayaṃ vinā |
na bhrāntirbhavati kvāpi svapnamāyādikeṣvapi' iti vacanāt |
na cātrādhiṣṭhānaṃ tāvadasti |
candrasyāropyādbhinnatayā dṛśyamānatvāt |
na hyāropyādbhinnatayā dṛśyamānasyādhiṣṭhānatvaṃ sambhavati |
na hi śuktibhrāntau rajatādbhinnatayā dṛśyate |
na ca candrātiriktamadhiṣṭhānaṃ sambhavati |
nāpyatra pradhānamasti |
satyasya candrāntarasyābhāvāt |
paridṛśyamānacandrasya ca pradhānatve'dhiṣṭhānasya pṛthagavācyatvāt |
tasya cābhāvāt |
ataḥ kathaṃ candrāntarāropāṅgīkāra iti |
maivam |
tādātmyabhramasyaivādhiṣṭhānādiniyamena bhrame tadabhāvāt |
anavaṣṭabdhanayanaraśmibhirgṛhīta eva candro'vaṣṭabdhanayanaraśmibhirvyatyastaiḥ sthānavyatyāsena punargṛhīto bhavati |
evaṃ nayanadvayenaikacandraviṣaya eva jñānadvaye jāte pratyetuḥ puruṣasyemau candrāvityādibhrāntirjāyate |
tatra kā nāmādhiṣṭhānādyapekṣā |
na ca jñānadvaye vivaditavyam |
anavaṣṭabdhanayananimīlane candradvayapratītyabhāvāt |
tadanimīlana eva tadbhāvāt |
na caivaṃ pratītidvayasyāpi satyacandraviṣayatvena pramātvātkathaṃ bhrāntyavakāśa iti vācyam |
ubhayapratītipratisandhāturavivekena jāyamānāyā asyāḥ pratītestābhyāṃ bhinnatvena bhrāntitvopapatteḥ |
ata eva tṛtīyapratītau bhinnatayā bhāsamānasyāropitatvaṃ tatpratiyogikasatyacandrānuyogikabhedasya satyatvena sādhyavaikalyādyuktirapi yujyata ityalam |
pratibandīṃ mocayati -- neti || tatra idaṃ rajatamiti bhrāntisthale rajatāropasthitireva kuto'bhyupeyeti cet |
tadabhāve idaṃ rajatamiti sāmānādhikaraṇyapratītireva na syāditi brūmaḥ |
rajatābhedāropeṇaiva tadupapattirityuktameveti cettatrāha -- rajatānupāsthitāviti || adhiṣṭhānasya candratvāditi || tathā cādhiṣṭhānadarśanenaiva candropasthitisambhavena tadarthaṃ candrāntarāropo nāṅgīkāryaḥ |
bhedamātrāropastu candrāntarāropaṃ vinā'pyadhiṣṭhānatvenopasthite candre yujyata eveti kathaṃ sādhyavaikalyacodyāvakāśa iti bhāvaḥ |
|| bheda iti || kimatra mithyātvam |
vyāvahārikatvaṃ cetsādhyavaikalyaṃ dṛṣṭāntasyāprātibhāsikatvaṃ cedbādha ityato'nugatasādhyaṃ nirvakti || mithyātvamiti || hetuśca mithyābhedatvaṃ cedasiddhiḥ satyabhedatvaṃ cetsādhanavaikalyam |
na ca sāmānyaṃ hetuḥ |
mithyāsatyayoranugatasāmānyābhāvādityāśaṅkya samādhatte || prāmāṇiketi || nanu pratīyamānatvaṃ pratītikarmakārakatvam |
tadasato na yuktamityata āha -- pratīyamānatvaṃ hīti |
tadapi karaṇasannikarṣagocaratvaṃ nāsato yuktamityata āha -- viṣayatvaṃ ceti || sambandhitvaṃ sambandhāśrayatvam |
tadapyayuktamityata āha -- sambandhaśceti || asataḥ sakalaśaktivaidhurye'pi jñānaṃ svasāmagrīmahimāyattasvarūpasambandhena tadviṣayatayocyate |
tadevāsataḥ pratīyamānatvamiti bhāvaḥ || āropitānāropitacandrayoriti || prātibhāsikavyāvahārikayorityarthaḥ || sattveneti || aprātibhāsikatvenetyarthaḥ || sādhyavikala iti || vyāvahārikabhedasya sarvasya pakṣatāvacchedakāvacchinnatayā sandigdhasādhyaka ityarthaḥ || dvitvapratītyupapattāviti || pratīterdvāvityākārakatvopapattāvityarthaḥ |
evañca bhedasyaivāropyatvāttasyaiva prātibhāsikatvānnoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ || gauravāditi || dharmadharmiṇordharmikalpanasya gurutvāditi bhāvaḥ |
yadvā dvitvapratītyupapattau dvitvamātrasya tantratvopapattau dvitvaviśiṣṭadharmidvayasya tantratve gauravādityarthaḥ || idaṃ rajatamiti pratītyupapattāviti || pratīteridaṃ rajatamityākārakatvopapattāviti vyākhyeyam |
tenābhedāropasyaivedaṃ rajatamiti pratītitvena tadupapattāvityuktiḥ kathamiti śaṅkānavakāśaḥ || upasthityarthamiti || āropopasthityorekatve'pi tadarthāṅgīkārādityuktiḥ sāmānyaviśeṣābhiprāyeṇeti dhyeyam |
nanu smṛtyupasthāpitarajatasya tādātmyāropaḥ kinna syāditi cenna |
tādātmyāparokṣe pratiyogyaparokṣasyaiva tantratvāt |
|| api ceti || bhedamātrasya mithyātve viśvaviṣṇubhedasyāpi mithyātvasiddhyā prakṛtopayogaḥ |
nanu mithyātvamasattvaṃ prātibhāsikatvaṃ vā yadi tadā bādhaḥ |
vimatabhedasya vyāvahārikatvāṅgīkārāt |
ādye dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyaṃ cetyata āha -- mithyātvaṃ ceti || idaṃ ca prātibhāsikavyāvahārikasādhāraṇamiti noktadoṣāvakāśa iti bhāvaḥ |
prāmāṇikāprāmāṇikayorvyāvahārikaprātibhāsikayorbhedatvena pratīyamānatvamapi kathamubhayānugatamityata āha -- pratīyamānatvaṃ hīti |
bhedatvena pratītistatsambandhitvaṃ vā vyāvahārika iva prātibhāsike sambhavatīti bhāvaḥ || pratīyamāna iti || imau candrāvityādau pratīyamānasya vyāvahārikacandrānyacandrasya prātibhāsikatvena prātibhāsika vyāvahārikayoḥ prativādimate bhedasya sattvāddṛṣṭāntaḥ sādhyavikala ityarthaḥ || candrābhedāropamātreṇeti || imau candrāvityatra candrāntarāropaṃ vinaiva paridṛśyamānacandre bhedāropamātreṇa dvitvapratītyupapattau na tatkalpanā |
tathā prativādimate'pi tatra bhedo mithyeti na taddṛṣṭāntaḥ sādhyavikalaḥ |
idamatra bodhyam |

candro netyapratīteḥ |

na hi prativādī kasminnapi bhede mithyātvaṃ nāṅgīkarotīti yujyate || tarhīti |
idamāpātataḥ |
yato dvaitavādinastattvavādinaśca rajatābhedasya rajatātmakatvena tadāropāṅgīkāre rajatāropo durvāraḥ |
naiyyāyikasya tu rajatābhedo rajatabhedābhāvo vā rajatatvaṃ tadāropeṇa rajatāropasya nānyathāsiddhiḥ |

ubhayasattve ubhayāropa ityaṅgīkārāt |
tenāropitānāropitayorbhedasya satyatvānaṅgīkāreṇa tasyaitad granthapraveśāyogācca || tatrādhiṣṭhānasyeti || idamapi pūrvanyāyenāpātataḥ || rajatānupasthitāviti || idamapi sthavīyaḥ |
vyāvahārikarajatopasthityaiva rajatābhedāropasambhavena tadarthaṃ prātibhāsikarajatāropānapekṣaṇāt |
bhedasāmānyamithyātvasādhakaikānumānenaiva sarvasvābhimatasiddhirbhavatītyāśayenāha -- bheda iti || atra candrabhedādikamādāyāṃśe siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya vimata iti pūraṇīyam |
pakṣatāvacchedakaṃ tu brahmajñānetarābādhyabhedatvaṃ bodhyam |
avacchedakāvacchedena tasyādoṣatve tu bhedatvamātraṃ pakṣatāvacchedakaṃ bodhyam |
nanu mithyātvamasattvaṃ cedapasiddhānto'rthakriyākāritvādbādhaścetyata āha -- mithyātvaṃ ceti || adhiṣṭhāneti || yāthātmyasvanivartakajñānaviṣayo'(yā)sādhāraṇākāro brahmaṇi svarūpameva tādṛśamanyatra śuktitvādiḥ(di) |
tathā ca jñānatvaghaṭitadharmāvacchinnakāraṇatākadhvaṃsapratiyogitvaṃ mithyātvamanyatsvarūpakathanam |
śuktijñānādrajatamutsannamityādipratītibalādrajatasya nāśasvīkārānnāsambhavaḥ |
mudgarapātādinā ghaṭādeḥ kāraṇātmanā'vasthānarūpatirobhāva eva na dhvaṃsaḥ |
sa tu brahmajñānenaivetyaṅgīkārānna tatrāvyāptiḥ |
ātmaviśeṣaguṇatvenottarajñānanivartyapūrvecchādau mithyātvavyavahāravāraṇāya jñānatvetyādi |
adhikaṃ tu dvaitadyumaṇināmakatattvodyotaṭippaṇyāṃ matkṛtāyāmanusandheyam || bhedatvena pratīyamānatvasyaiveti || nanu pratīyamānatvaṃ (pratītikarmatvaṃ ) kathamaprāmāṇikasya ghaṭate? tasya sakalasāmarthyarahitasya kārakatvagarbhoktakarmatvāyogādityata āha -- pratīyamānatvamiti || bhedatvaprakārakapratīterviṣayatayā hetutvāṅgīkārādityarthaḥ |
asato bhānāṅgīkārānnāsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
nanu prāgabhāvādiṣu bhedatvabhramasambhavāttatra vyabhicāraḥ |
na ca tatrāpi mithyātvasattvānna vyabhicāra iti vācyam |
tathātve pratītiviṣayatvādityetāvatā pūrteriti vaiyarthyāditi cenna |

bhedatvena prāgabhāvāderanyatrāropamādāya vyabhicārastadavastha iti na śaṅkyam |
tatrāpyāropyamāṇo'yaṃ bheda iti vyavahāranirvāhāya tatra bhedatvena tādatmyena bhedabhramastatraiva bhedatvabhānasya svīkārāt |
tatra cāropyamāṇabhedasya mithyātvena vyabhicāraprasakteḥ |
yatra prāgabhāvo mukhyaviśeṣa eva bhāsate tatra tādātmyena bhedatvaṃ bheda eva bhāsate |
tādṛśabhrame'pi bhedānavagāhitvaṃ na svīkriyate kintvayaṃ bheda iti vyavahārabalādbhedasya(syaiva) prāgabhāve bhramaḥ |
tatraiva bhedatvaṃ bhāsata ityādyabhyupagamānna kathamapi vyabhicāraśaṅkā kālikaviṣayatvādinā bhedatvena dhvaṃsajñānādibhedamādāya vyabhicāravāraṇāya tādātmyasaṃsargāvacchinneti bhedatvaprakāratāyāṃ niveśaḥ |
viṣayatvaṃ ca jñānāsādhāraṇakāraṇakaraṇasannikarṣāśrayatvaṃ kathamasatastadyujyata ityata āha -- tacceti || etadapi tādṛgevetyata āha -- svarūpameveti || jñānasvarūpamevetyarthaḥ |
aprāmāṇikasya svarūpāyogādviṣayatājñānasvarūpameva tannirūpakatvameva viṣayasyāyaṃ viṣaya iti vyavahāraheturiti matenedam |
tatreti buddhisthatannirūpakatvaparāmarśaḥ prāmāṇikatvānapekṣaṇādityākare tathā vyavasthāpitatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
aprāmāṇikatvaṃ ca paramate'nirvacanīyasyāpīti na tasyāpasiddhānta iti bhāvaḥ || bhedasya sattveneti || anyathā''ropitānāropitayorabhedāpatterityarthaḥ |
āropite pratīyamānānāropitabhedastadatirikto'taḥ satya evetyanyatra vyavasthāpitam || candrabhedāropamātreṇeti || candrasvarūpamātrasambandhitayā pratīyamānabhedamātrāropeṇa candrasvarūpapratiyogikabhedamātrāropeṇa cetyarthaḥ |
bhedo vyāsajyavṛttirubhayadharmo bhavatyubhayatra pratiyogyanuyogibhāvo'pīti pakṣe candrasvarūpa eva vyāsajyavṛttibhedarūpadharmasya tatpratiyogikatvānuyogikatvayorārope imau candrāvayamasmādbhinna iti pratītyupapattiścandrāntarāropaṃ vinaiva ghaṭate |
avyāsajyavṛttitayā bhedabhāva(bhāna)pakṣe'pi doṣavaśātpratiyogikabhedavyakteranyatrānubhūyamānāyāḥ taccandrasaṃsṛṣṭatayā''ropitatvamātreṇa tādṛśavyavahārādyupapatterna pradeśāntare candrasyāropa iti bhāvaḥ |

tathātve cāyamartho yuktaḥ |
candrāntarāropānaṅgīkārastatra candrasyaivādhiṣṭhānatvena tatroktarītyā bhedāropādinā dvau candrāvityādipratītisambhavāt |
prakṛte ca rajatasyānadhiṣṭhānatvena śukterevādhiṣṭhānatayā laukikasannikarṣeṇa tajjñānāyogāt |
idaṃ rajatamiti vyavahārānirvāhakajñānasya tatkāle siddhyarthaṃ tatkāle rajatāropāṅgīkārasya āvaśyakatvānna tatpratibandiriti bhāvaḥ |
rajatāropānaṅgīkāre na kevalamidaṃ rajatamiti vyavahārānupapattiḥ kintu bhavadabhimatarajatatādātmyatvāvacchinnāropo'pi na siddhyatītyāha -- rajatānupasthitāviti || uktabhramasthale rajataviṣayakajñānābhāve'ṅgīkriyamāṇe bhramasya rajatāviṣayakatveti yāvat || rajatābhedeti || rajatapratiyogikābhedatvena tajjñānāsambhavādityarthaḥ |
na ceṣṭāpattiḥ |
kevalatādātmyādijñānena rajatārthipravṛttyanupapattiḥ(tteḥ) |
na ca tarhyabhedāṃśa eva pratiyogitayā bhāsate(tāṃ) śuktikāyāṃ tadāropo niṣphala iti vācyam |
bhrame rajatabhānasyāvaśyakatve śuktikāyāmevābhedasaṃsargeṇa bhānakalpana eva lāghavamadhikaviṣayatvākalpanāt |
lāghavena tasya (eva) rajatārthaṃ pravṛttau hetutvācca |
na tādātmyasya śuktyaṃśe prakāratvamaṅgīkṛtya tatra rajatasya prakāratvakalpane lāghavam |
rajatatadabhedayorabhedaśuktikayośca saṃsargabhānāvaśyakatayā'dhikaviṣayatākalpanāprayuktagauravaṃ tādṛśasya pravṛttakatvānupapattiśceti bhāvaḥ |
etadvākyasya pūrvaṃ rajatānupasthitau rajatābhedāropāsambhavātpūrvaṃ rajatāropāvaśyaka ityarthabhrāntyā pūrvaṃ rajatāropābhāve'pi vyāvahārikarajatopasthitimātreṇa tādṛśatādātmyabhānasambhavānna rajatāropa āvaśyaka iti dūṣaṇabhrameṇa manaḥkhedo na kāryaḥ |
asmaduktavākyārthe uktadūṣaṇāsaṃsparśāt |
rajatārthipravṛttyādihetvārope rajatapratītirnāstīti vadantaṃ prati tatpūrvaṃ rajatāropopapādanasyākiñcitkaratvādaprasaktatvāccoktavākyasya tādṛśārthakatvatātparyabhramasyaivātisāhasikatvāditi dik |
sarvābhedasiddhaye bhedamātramithyātvānumānamāha -- bheda iti || vimata ityādiḥ |
tena na pūrvavadaṃśataścandrabhede siddhasādhanam |
nanvatra mithyātvamasattvaṃ cedapasiddhāntaḥ, jagataḥ sadasadvilakṣaṇatvāṅgīkārādatha āha -- mithyātvaṃ ceti || yadvā sadasadvilakṣaṇatvarūpamithyātvasādhane paraṃ prati sādhyāprasiddhirata āha -- mithyātvaṃ ceti || yadvā yadi sattvānadhikaraṇatvaṃ mithyātvaṃ tadā nirdharmakabrahmavat sadrūpasyāpi bhedasya sattvādhikaraṇatvaniṣedhasambhavādarthāntaramityata āha -- mithyātvaṃ ceti || yadvā bādhyatvameva hi mithyātvam |
taccānyāthā vijñātasya samyagvijñātatvameveti siddhasādhanam |
kṣaṇikatvādinā vijñātasya jagataḥ sthāyitvādinā vijñātatvābhyupagamādata āha -- mithyātvaṃ ceti || yadvā'jñānatatkāryānyataratvarūpamithyātve sādhye paraṃ prati dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavaikalyaṃ, tanmate'tyantāsataścandrabhodāderajñānatatkāryantarbhāvābhāvādata āha -- mithyātvaṃ ceti || nivartyatvamātrasya mudraraprahāradinā ghaṭāderiva sattvapakṣe'pyupapatterjñānanivartyatvamiti || etadapyuttarajñānanivartyapūrvajñānavat sattvāvirodhītyadhiṣṭhānapadam |
adhiṣṭhānayāthātmyajñānanivartyasya śuktirajatādeḥ sattvādarśane'pi tadbhramanivartyasya sattvasambhavādarthāntaramityatoyāthātmyeti || na ca māyimate candrabhedādeḥ prātibhāsikatvena nivṛttisambhave'pi viddhāṃ timate tucchatvena tasya nivṛttyayogāddṛṣṭāntavaikalyamiti vācyam |
yastannivartyatvaṃ tadadhīnasiddhikābhāvapratiyogitvam |
candrabhedāderabhāvaścandraikatvajñānādhīnasiddhika ityadoṣaḥ || prāmāṇiketi || siddhānte sadasatsādhāraṇabhedatvadharmasyāprasiddhatvenetyarthaḥ || asiddho heturiti || ubhayānugatabhedatvasya hetutve hetvaprasiddhiḥ |
sanmātravṛttibhedatvasya hetutve dṛṣṭāntāsiddhiḥ |
dṛṣṭāntavṛttibhedabhramaviṣayatvarūpabhedatvasya hetutve ca svarūpāsiddhirityarthaḥ |
nanu pratīyamānatvaṃ hi pratītikarmatvam |
tasya ca pratītijanyaphalāśrayatvarūpasya na sadasatsādhāraṇyamata āha -- pratīviṣayatvamiti || etadeva pratītikarmatvaṃ, na tūktarūpam |
jñātatārūpaphalasya nirastatvenāsambhavāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu pratītijanakasannikarṣādimattvarūpaṃ tadviṣayatvamapi na sadasatsādhāraṇamityāśaṅkyāha -- tacceti || nanu sambandhasya sambandhisattāsāpekṣatvāt kathamasataścandrabhedādeḥ pratītisambandhitvamata āha -- sambandhaśceti || syādevaṃ yadyanyaḥ sambandhaḥ syāt |
ayaṃ tu pratīteḥ svarūpameva |
tasya tu viṣayasattānirapekṣatvānna doṣa iti bhāvaḥ |
syādetat |
paramate candrabhedasya pāramārthikatvāddṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavaikalyam |
na caivaṃ candrabhedapratīteḥ pramātvaprasaṅgaḥ |
candrabhinnatvena bhāsamānacandrasyāropitatvena viśiṣṭaviṣayabādhāt |

api tu bhedagraha eveti candrecandrabhedāropāṅgīkāre nānupapattiḥ |
na ca candrāntarasyāropāṅgīkāre'pi bādhakābhāvaḥ |
candraścandrasinna ityādau candrabhedasyaiva sākṣāddharmiviśeṣaṇatvāpekṣayā tādātmyena candrabhinnasya tathātve viṣayatādhikyena gauravādityāśayena samādhatte || candrabhedāropamātreṇeti || atra pratibandīmāśaṅkate -- nanviti || yadyapi rajatāropāpekṣayā viṣayatādhikyena rajatābhedāropa eva gauravannaitatpratibandyavakāśaḥ |
na ca dharmikalpanāto dharmakalpanā ladhīyasīti śaṅkyam |
dharmighaṭitadharmakalpanāyāḥ laghutvāyogāt |
tathāpi rajatābhedasya rajatatvarūpatvena tadviśiṣṭāropāt tadārope lādhavamityabhiprāyaḥ |
rajatatvasya ca na svarūpeṇāropyatvam |
mithyaiva rajataṃ pratyabhādityanusmṛmivirodhāt |
rajatābhedatvenārope ca rajatārepasyāvaśyakatetyāha -- tatretyādinā || rajatābhedāropāṅgīkāre'pi tatkāraṇībhūtapratiyogyupasthityartharajatāropo'vaśyamaṅgīkāryaḥ |
na caivaṃ candrāntarāropasyāpyāvaśyakatā |
adhiṣṭhānabhūtacandrapratītereva pratiyogijñānatvāt |
na caivamihāpyāpaṇastharajatopasthityaiva nirvāhaḥ |
tathāsati tadabhedasyaivāropyatvāpattyā darśitānusmṛtivirodhāt |
tathācāvaśyakarajatāropeṇaivopapattau tadabhedāropāṅgīkāre vaiyarthyaṃ gauravaṃ cetyāśayaḥ |
apicetyetatkiñcetivat vyākhyeyam || bheda iti || vimatabheda ityarthaḥ |
anyathā candrabhedasyāpipakṣatvāpattyāṃśe siddhasādhanāt |
atra bhedasya mithyātve siddhe tadviruddho'bheda eva sidhyatīti dhyeyam |
nanu kimidaṃ mithyātvam |
asattvamanirvacanīyatvaṃ vā |
nādyaḥ |
apasiddhāntāt |
nadvitīyaḥ |
tasya kutrāpyaprasiddhatvenāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvādityata āha -- mithyātvaṃ cādhiṣṭhāneti || āropādhiṣṭhānaśuktiviṣayayathārthajñānanivartyatvaṃ śuktirajate prasiddhamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanvatra vimatabhedatvaṃ hetuścandrabhedatvaṃ vā |
nādyaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte vimatabhedatvābhāvena sādhanavaikalyāpātāt |
na dvitīyaḥ |
hetoḥ pakṣe abhāvena svarūpāsiddheḥ |
nanu yathā parvatīyadhūmasya hetutve sādhanavaikalyaṃ mahānasīyadhūmasya hetutve svarūpāsiddhiriti śaṅkāpariharāyobhayadhūmānugataṃ dhūmatvasāmānyaṃ hetūkriyate tathehāpi vimatabhedacandrabhedānugataṃ bhedatvasāmānyaṃ heturiti cenna |
tatrobhayorapi dhūmayoḥ prāmāṇikatvenobhayadhūmānugatasāmānyasambhavāt |
prakṛte ca vimatabhedasya prāmāṇikatvena candrabhedasyāprāmāṇikatvena prāmāṇikāprāmāṇikānugatabhedatvasāmānyābhāvenāsiddhiprasaṅgādityāśaṅkya nirākaroti || nanviti || vyāvahārikatvena vā tatvavādirītyā vā vimatabhedasyāprāmāṇikatvoktiḥ || evaṃ ceti || bhedatvena pratīmānatvasyobhayasādhāraṇyāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu pratīyamānatvaṃ nāmapramārūpapratītigocaratvam idameva prāmāṇikatvamityaprāmāṇikacandrabhedekathametatsambhavatītyata āha -- pratīyamānatvaṃ hīti || pratītiviṣayatvaṃ natu pramārūpapratītiviṣayatvam |
yenoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ |
nanu yatsannikṛṣṭakaraṇena yajjñānaṃ jāyate sa tasya viṣayo bhavati |
tathācātra pratītiviṣayatvaṃ pratītijanakakaraṇasannikṛṣṭatvaṃ vācyam |
karaṇasannikarṣaśca saṃyogādirūpaḥ |
sa ca prāmāṇikasyaivetyaprāmāṇikekathametatsambhavatītyata āha -- tacceti || pratītisambandhitvaṃ natu pratītijanakakaraṇasannikṛṣṭatvam || yenoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ |
nanu pratītyāsākaṃ sambandhasya saṃyogasamavāyayoranyataratvāvaśyaṃbhāvāduktadoṣatādavasthyamityata āha -- sambandhaśceti || svarūpameva nātiriktaḥ saṃyogādiḥ |
atiriktasambandhamanapekṣya viśiṣṭadhīniyāmakatvaṃ svarūpasambandhatvam |
nanvaprāmāṇikatve'sattvāpattyā svarūpasambandho'pi kathamiti cenna |
pratīyamānacandrabhedasya prātibhāsikatvāṅgīkāreṇāsattvānaṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ || ityatreti || evaṃ niruktabhedatvena pratīyamānatve uktarītyā prāmāṇikatvānapekṣaṇādityarthaḥ |
candrayorbhedasya satyatvaṃ kuta ityata āha -- āropitānāropiteti || āropitānāropitacandrayorbhedasya mithyātve tayoranyonyaṃ tādātmyaṃ syāt |
nacaitadviruddhadharmādhikaraṇayoḥ sambhavatīti bhāvaḥ |
ubhayorapyāropitatvaṃ kiṃ nāṅgīkriyata ityata uktam || pratīyamāne candre candrāntarasyāropitatveneti || candrāntarasyānāropitatve'ṅgulyavaṣṭambhābhāve'pi pratītiḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ |
syādidaṃ yadi candrāntarasyāropitatvaṃ syāt |
nacaitadasti |
naca tathātve imaucandrāviti dvitvapratītyanupapattiḥ |
candrāntarasyāropitasyaivānaṅgīkārāditi vācyam |
ekasminneva candre bhedāropamātreṇa dvitvapratītyupapatteḥ |
naca candrāntarasyaivāropaḥ kiṃ nasyāditi vācyam |
dharmikalpanātovaraṃ dharmakalpanamiti nyāyena siddhecandre bhedarūpadharmamātrāropāṅgīkāreṇa dvitvapratītyupapattau candrāntarākhyadharmyārope gauravāditi pariharati -- candre bhedāropamātreṇeti || candre candrapratiyogikabhedāropamātreṇetyarthaḥ |
pratibandyā śaṅkate -- tarhīti || yadi candre bhedāropamātreṇa dvitvapratītyupapattyā candrāntarāropo nāṅgīkriyate tarhi śuktikāyāṃ rajatāropo na syāt |
naca tathātve idaṃ rajatamiti pratītyanupapattiḥ |
śuktikāyāṃ rajatābhedāropamātreṇa tadupapatteḥ |
naca rajatasyaivāropaḥ kiṃ nasyāditi vācyam |
dharmikalpanātovaraṃ dharmakalpanamiti nyāyena siddheśuktikāśakalerajatābhedākhyadharmāropamātreṇa tādṛśapratītyupapattau rajatākhyadharmyāropāṅgīkāre gauravāt |
yadi tatra prāmāṇikatvena gauravasyādoṣatvamaṅgīkṛtya rajatāropo'ṅgīkriyate tarhyatrāpi tathaivāṅgīkṛtya candre candrāntarasyaivāropo'ṅgīkārya iti bhāvaḥ |
pratibandīṃ mocayati -- neti || tatra udāhṛtasthale |
adhiṣṭhānajñānenaivarajatopasthitiḥ kiṃ na syādityata uktamadhiṣṭhānasyārajatatveneti |
āropādhiṣṭhānasya śuktitvena taddarśane rajatopasthityayogādityarthaḥ |
rajatopasthitireva kuta ityata āha -- rajatānupasthitāviti || sapratiyogikapadārthajñānepratiyogikajñānasya kāraṇatvena rajatarūpapratiyogijñānābhāvetatpratiyogikābhedāropāsambhavādityarthaḥ |
yadyabhedasya sapratiyogikapadārthatvena pratiyogyupasthityarthaṃ rajatāropo'ṅgīkriyate |
tarhi bhedasyāpi tathātvena pratiyogyupasthityarthaṃ candrāntarāropo'ṅgīkārya ityata āha -- naivamiti || yathā rajatopasthityarthaṃ rajatāropo'ṅgīkāryaḥ tathā candropasthityarthaṃ candrāntarāropo'ṅgīkāryo netyarthaḥ |
kuta ityata āha -- adhiṣṭhānasyeti || tatra hyadhiṣṭhānasya śuktitvena taddarśane rajatopasthiterasambhavādrajatāropo'ṅgīkriyate |
atra tu bhedāropādhiṣṭhānasya candratvena taddarśane candrasyopasthitatvena tadarthaṃ candrātarāropo vyartha iti bhāvaḥ |
api ca |
vimataṃ (tattvataḥ) tātvikasvāntarbhedahīnaṃ matatvataḥ |
yaditthaṃ tattathā yadvatkhaṃ tathedaṃ tatastathā ||
yathā gaganamanyonyapāramārthikabhedaśūnyairghaṭamaṭhādyākāśaiḥ paramārthato na bhidyate |
tathā jagadākhyaḥ padārtho'pyanyonyaṃ pāramārthikabhedaśūnyaiḥ svaikadeśairghaṭādibhiḥ paramārthato na bhidyata iti |
nanu ghaṭamaṭhādyākāśānāmanyonyaṃ pāramarthikabhedāṅgīkārātsādhyavikalo dṛṣṭānta iti cenna |
teṣāṃ svabhāvato bhinnatve ghaṭādisambandhātprāgapi bhedapratītiḥ syāt |
ghaṭādīnāṃ vyañjakatvādadoṣa iti cenna |
dīpādivyañjakābhāve'pi kadācitspārśanena ghaṭapratītidarśanāt |
kuṅkumagandhasya ghṛtamivaitadvyañjakam |
tathā ca ghaṭādyabhāve'pi na pratīyate bheda iti cenna |
tatra pṛthivītvena gandhasiddhāvanudbhūtagandhāṅgīkāre gauravāt ghṛtamabhivyañjakamaṅgīkriyate |
na caivaṃ prakṛte |
ākāśa bhede mānābhāvāt |
athācetanānāṃ parasparabhedābhāve'numānaṃ kārikayā saṅgṛhṇāti || vimatamiti || kecittu cetanācetanajātaṃ sarvamapi vimatapadārtha ityāhuḥ |
taccintyam |
pūrvapakṣagranthe vā siddhāntagranthe vā'cetanavācakaghaṭādipadānāmeva śravaṇenobhayavācakapadāśravaṇāt |
asaṅgatiparihārasya ca saṅgatatvapradarśanena prāgeva darśitatvāditi kārikāṃ vyācaṣṭe || yathā gaganamiti || etena vimataṃ jakadākhyavastu tāttvikasvāntarbhedahīnam |
anyonyaṃ tāttvikabhedaśūnyaṃ svaikadeśibhūtaghaṭādimat |
mithastāttvikabhedaśūnyaiḥ svaikadeśibhirghaṭādibhiḥ paramārthato na bhidyata iti yāvat |
kutaḥ |
matatvato dṛśyatvāt |
yaditthaṃ dṛśyaṃ tattathā tādṛśaṃ yadvastu svam |
gaganamanyonyapāramārthikabhadeśūnyairghaṭamaṭhādyākāśaiḥ paramārthato na bhidyate |
idaṃ ca jagattathā dṛśyam |
tato dṛśyatvāttathā tattvataḥ svāntarbhedahīnamiti kārikārthaḥ spaṣṭīkṛto bhavati |
teṣāṃ svabhāvato bhinnatve |
ghaṭādyupādhikatvābhāva iti yāvat |
na cākāśāṃśānāṃ svābhāvikatve'pi ghaṭādisambandhasattva eva hi pratītiḥ |
teṣāṃ pradīpavadvyañjakatvāṅgīkārāditi bhāvena śaṅkate -- ghaṭādīnāmiti || dīpādivyañjakābhāve'pīti |
tathā ca tadvadevātrāpi kadāciddhaṭādyabhāve'pyākāśāṃśapratītiḥ syāt |
na caivam |
ata etena vyañjakāḥ kintūpādhaya eveti bhāvaḥ |
nanvete dīpādivannāniyatavyañjakāḥ kintu kuṅakumagandhādivyañjakaghṛtavanniyatavyañjakā eva |
atastadabhāve ākāśe vidyamāno'pi bhedo na pratīyata ityāśaṅkya niṣedhati || kuṅkumagandhasyeti || yuktaṃ ghṛtasya kuṅkumagandhaṃ prati niyatābhivyañjakatvakalpanam |
tatra gandhasya sattvena tadanupalabdheḥ kāraṇāntarādarśanāt |
na ca kuṅkume gandha eva nāsti |
sattve'pi pāṣāṇādāvivānudbhūto'stu |
ato ghṛtasambandhāttanniṣṭhagandha eva kuṅkume'vabhāsata iti vācyam |
pṛthivītvena hetunā kuṅkume gandhasya siddhatvāt |
tasyānudbhūtatvakalpane mānābhāvāt |
ghṛtasambandhena kuṅkume udbhūtagandhasyaivopalabhyamānatvena tasya ghṛtamātraniṣṭhatvakalpanatvasyāpyaprāmāṇikatvāt |
na caivaṃ prakṛte |
ākāśe svābhāvikabhedasadbhāve pramāṇābhāvāt |
yena ghṛtavaddhaṭāderniyatavyañjakatvena tadabhāve bhedasyānupalabdhiḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ |
|| vimatamiti || jagadityarthaḥ |
bādhavāraṇāya tāttviketi || sarvasyāpi jagattvena jagati jagadbhedānadhikaraṇatvasya siddhatvena siddhasādhanatvāt |
tadvāraṇāya svāntariti || matatvato jñātatvāt |
yadvatkhaṃ yathā''kāśastathedamityupanayaḥ |
tatastatheti nigamanam |
dṛṣṭāntaṃ vivaṇoti -- yatheti || bhedeti || ekadeśirītyā tu bhedaśabdo viśeṣapara iti jñeyam || prāgiti || upādhyabhāvakālopalakṣaṇametat || vyañjakatvāditi || evañca bhedapratīterupādhyanvayavyatirekānusaraṇaniyame'pi na bhedasyopādhijanyatvarūpaupādhikatvasiddhiḥ |
anvayavyatirekayoranyathāsiddhatvāditi bhāvaḥ || spārśaneneti || tathā ca dīpādyabhāvakāle'pi ghaṭasattvaṃ pramāṇāntaravaśādaṅgīkriyate |
prakṛte upādhyabhāvakālīnabhede pramāṇābhāvādupādhinā tadutpattireveti bhāvaḥ || kuṅkumeti || yathā svābhāviko'pi kuṅkumagandho na kadā'pi ghṛtākhyavyañjakaṃ vinopalabhyate |
tathā bhedo'pyastu |
anyathā so'pi na svābhāvikaḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ || pṛthivītveneti || evañca tadvyañjakābhāvakāle'pi gandhasya pratyakṣānupalambhe'pyupalambho'styeveti bhāvaḥ |
