nanvastu pṛthivītvena gandhaḥ sa ca pāṣāṇādāvivānudbhūta evāstu |
ghṛtasambandhātpratīyamānastvanya eva tātkālikopādhijanyaḥ |
evañca tatrāpi na svābhāvikasyopādhyanusaraṇāpattirityevaṃ kuto nāṅgīkriyata ityata uktamanudabhūteti || dharmikalpanāto dharmakalpanā vareti nyāyena sthitasyaiva vyaktimātrato gandhāntarotpattergurutvāditi bhāvaḥ |
|| vimatamiti || tāttvikasvāntarbhedatadabhāvakoṭikasaṃśayaviṣayībhūtaṃ sarvacetanācetanajātamityarthaḥ |
atra gaganasya pakṣāntarbhāve'pi pṛthivītvāvacchinnapakṣaketarabhedānumāne ghaṭasyaiva dṛṣṭāntatvopapattiḥ |
tadvadevāṃśataḥ siddhasādhanatvābhāvo'pi pañcāvayavopanyāsastu nyāyadiśā tathā jagadākhyapadārtha iti jagadrūpasamudāyasya samudāyipratiyogikabhedābhāvaḥ prasaṅgātsādhitaḥ |
samudāyinā viṣṇujīvajaḍānāṃ tāttvikabhedābhāvastu prakṛtopayukta iti bodhyam |
aniyataṃ niyataṃ vā vyañjakaṃ ghaṭamaṭhādīti vikalpaṃ manasi nidhāya nādya ityāha -- dīpādīti |
tathehāpi vyañjakābhāve'pi kadācidbhedapratītiprasaṅgāditi śeṣa iti |
dvitīyamāśaṅkate -- kuṅkumagandhasyeti || tatra pṛthivītveneti || idamupalakṣaṇam |
prakṛte aupādhikabhedapratītivatkuṅkumagandhapratītiraupādhikī nāṅgīkartuṃ śakyā |
upādhau ghṛte tādṛgandhābhāvāditi bodhyam |
jaganniṣṭhabhedasāmānyamithyātvasādhanaṃ prakārāntareṇāha -- vimatamiti || satyatāśūnyasvaghaṭakapadārthapratiyogyanuyogikabhedasāmānyakatvatadabhāvābhyāṃ vipratipattiviṣayībhūtacetanācetanaghaṭitajagatsamudāyaḥ pakṣaḥ |
samudāyaśca samudāyibhyo bhinnābhinnaḥ kaścidapekṣābuddhiviṣayatvaviśiṣṭaḥ |
ato gaganasya tādṛśaviśiṣṭarūpatvābhāvena pakṣabahirbhāvāddṛṣṭāntatvopapattiḥ || tāttviketi || pūrvoktarītyā paramārtha ityarthaḥ |
anumānasya bhāskarābhimatatve tanmate cāpāramārthikabhedābhāvena tasyāvyāvṛttatvāpattyā'naupādhikatvenetyarthaḥ |
matadvaye'pi vyadhikaraṇadharmāvacchinnābhāva eva sādhyam |
bhāskaramate'naupādhikabhedasyāprasiddheḥ || svāntariti || svāṃśapratiyogyanuyogikabhedetyarthaḥ |
ghaṭaṃ prati tantūnāmiva jagadrūpaviśiṣṭarūpasamudāyasya samudāyināṃ ghaṭādīnāmavayavasthānīyānāmaṃśatvameva mahākāśaṃ pratyaupādhikaghaṭādyavacchinnākāśānāmeveti hṛdayam |
ghaṭādinā bhedasya gagane prativādibhistāttvikasya svīkārāddṛṣṭāntāsiddhivāraṇāya svāṃśapratiyogiketi || samudāyarūpe viśiṣṭaikadeśabhedānaṅgīkārātsiddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya svāṃśānuyogiketi || svāṃśapratiyogikasvāṃśānuyogikabhedā yāvantastattanniṣṭhatāttvikatvāvacchinnabhedābhāvakūṭavaditi sādhyārthaḥ |
uddeśyasarvabhedamithyātvasiddhaye kūṭasya sādhyatvānusaraṇam |
ghaṭe āropitasya svabhedasyoktadharmāvacchinnābhāvasya mahārajanādyupādhikabhedasyoktarūpābhāvasya ca siddhatvena siddhasādhanaṃ syāt |
atastadvāraṇaṃ vā (tat)prayojanam |
matatvata iti dṛśyatvādityarthaḥ |
brahmaṇyapyuktasādhyaṃ vādiprativādinoḥ sammatamiti noktasādhyaṃ prati dṛśyatvahetau(toḥ) vyabhicāraprasaktiḥ |
viṣayatayā jñānameva heturato na jñānaviṣayatvarūpahetughaṭakajñānavaiyyarthyaśaṅkā |
yaditthaṃ tattathetyudāharaṇam |
gagane uktasādhyaṃ sādhayitvā taddṛṣṭāntīkariṣyāmītyarthaḥ |
tathedamityupanayaḥ |
tatastatheti nigamanam |
kārikāṃ vivaṇoti -- yathetyādinā || bhedapratītiprasaṅgāditi || indriyasannikarṣasadbhāve tadapratītau bījābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
gaganasya cakṣuravedyatve'pi sākṣivedyatvādāpādanaṃ yuktameva |
paṭādeścakṣurvedyatvena tulyanyāyena tatrāpi paṭāṃśānāṃ svābhāvikatvāṅgīkāre cākṣuṣatvāpādanasambhavastatra draṣṭavyaḥ |
vyañjakatvāttatpratyakṣasāmagrīghaṭakatvād vyañjakasyānugatasyābhāvena yatkiñcidvyañjakābhāvastatpratyakṣābhāvaṃ prati prayojakatayā taṃ prati vyāpya uta svavyañjakasāmānyābhāva iti vikalpau hṛdi nidhāyādyaṃ dūṣayati -- dīpeti || dvitīyaṃśaṅkate -- kuṅkumeti || ghṛtameva yadvyañcakaṃ tadvinā tatpratyakṣābhāva ityanvavavyatirekasiddhavyañjakatākaghṛtābhāve svavyañjakasāmānyābhāvaprāptyā tadabhāvakāle yathā na gandhapratyakṣaṃ tathehāpi parimāṇaviśeṣavaddravyatvenāṃśaviśeṣapratyakṣatvāvacchinnaṃ prati niyamena hetutvāṅgīkārāddhaṭādyabhāve svavyañjakatvāvacchinnābhāvasattvātpratyakṣābhāvo yukta eveti bhāvaḥ |
pratyakṣakāraṇībhūtapadārthaprākkāle tasya pūrvaṃ sattvena dṛḍhatarapramāṇasattve vyañjakasamavadhānātpratyakṣavyañjakasya tadutpattiprayojakatvabādhādvyañjakasya tatpratyakṣajanakatvaṃ kalpayati |
tadabhāve tu tajjanakatvameva kalpayati |
anyathā daṇḍādisāmagryā ghaṭapratyakṣotpattihetutvameva syānna ghaṭotpādakatvam |
tathā ca ghaṭādisambandhātpūrvamaṃśasattve pramāṇābhāvāddhaṭādisambandhakāleṃ'śapratyakṣam |
aṃśasya ghaṭādijanyatvameva kalpayatītyāha -- tatretyādinā |
sarvābhedasādhakānumānāntaraṃ pradarśayaṃstatprayogaprakārajñāpanāya tārkikarītyā pañcāvayavaprayogarūpāṃ prācīnakārikāmudāharati -- vimatamiti || tāṃ vivaṇoti -- yatheti || tathāca vimataṃ jagat parasparapratiyogikapāramārthikabhedaśūnyasvaikadeśapratiyogikapāramārthikabhedaśūnyaṃ dṛśyatvāt |
yad dṛśyaṃ tattādṛśasādhyavat |
yathā bhūtākāśam |
dṛśyaṃ ca jagat |
tasmāttādṛśasādhyavaditi prayogārtha ukto bhavati |
atra sāmānyato bhedaśūnyatvamaprasiddham |
ataḥ svaikadeśapratiyogiketi |
tādṛśavyāvahārikabhedasattvādbādhavāraṇāya pāramārthiketi |
mitho vibhinnaikadeśairabhedasiddhyā'rthāntaravāraṇāya śūnyāntamekadeśaviśeṣaṇam |
pūrvavadaprasiddhivāraṇāya parasparetyādi |
na ca pāramārthikabhedāprasiddhyā sādhyāprasiddhiḥ |
pratiyoginaḥ paraprasiddhimātreṇa niṣedhasādhanasyoktatvāt |
yadi tu mithaḥ svaikadeśabhedasya yaḥ pāramārthikatvenābhāvastadviśiṣṭasvaikadeśabhedasya pāramārthikatvenābhāvaḥ sādhyastadā nāprasiddhiśaṅkā'pi |
vyadhikaraṇadharmāvacchinnabhedābhāvaprasiddheḥ |
na ca niraṃśe guṇādau vyabhicāraḥ |
niraṃśapadārthasyaivānabhyupagamāt |
abhyupagame vā sāṃśatvena matatvasyaiva hetutvāt |
nanvākāśāṃśānāṃ ghaṭādisambandhāt pūrvaṃ bhedarūpaviṣayasattve'pi kāraṇāntarābhāvādeva na bhedapratītiprasaṅga ityāśaṅkate -- ghaṭagrahaṇe dīpādīnāmiveti || dīpādivyañjakābhāve'pīti || ekakāraṇābhāve'pi kāraṇāntareṇa kāryotpattidarśanāddhaṭādivyañjakābhāve'pi kadācidbhedapratītiḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ |
vyañjakatvapadena na pratītiviśeṣakāraṇatvamātraṃ vivakṣitam |
kintu tatsāmānyakāraṇatvam |
ato noktadoṣa ityāśayena śaṅkate -- kuṅkumeti || syādevaṃ bādhakaparihāro yadi kuṅkamagandhādāviva bhede pramāṇaṃ syāt |
tadevatu nāstītyāha -- tatreti || yadyapi pṛthivītvaṃ surabhyasurabhyavayavārabdhāvayavini gandhaṃ vyabhicarati |
tathāpi gandhapratibandhakaśūnyatve sati pṛthivītvasya hetutvaṃ bodhyam |
darśitasthale ca viruddhagandharūpapratibandhakasatvānna vyabhicāraḥ |
nanvevaṃ gandhamātrasiddhāvapi ghṛtābhivyaṅgyodbhūtagandhasya kathaṃ pramāṇasiddhatvamata āha -- anudbhūteti || gauravāditi || ghṛtasambandhe sati pūrvagandhavināśasyodbhūtagandhotpādasya ca kalpanīyatayā gauravādityarthaḥ |
na cāvidyamāna evodbhūtagandho ghṛtopādhisambandhāt pratīyatāmiti vācyam |
bādhakaṃ vinā pratīterbhramatvāyogāt |
atra `pariśeṣeṇodbhūtagandhasyaiva siddhyā ghṛtasambandhāt prāgapi tatpratītivāraṇāye'ti pūraṇīyam || mānābhāvāditi || na ca ghaṭamaṭhādyākāśabhedapratītireva tatra mānamiti vācyam |
tasya bhramatvenāpyupapattervastvantarakalpane gauravāditi bhāvaḥ |
idānīmanātmabhedasādhakaṃ pañcāvayavopetānumānaṃ buddhyārohāya kārikayā saṅgṛhṇāti || vimatamiti || ātmākāśābhyāmanyadityarthaḥ |
tatvataḥ svāntarbhedahīnaṃ paramārthato'nyonyabhedarahitaikadeśaiḥ paramārthato bhedarahitam |
matatvato dṛśyatvāt |
yaditthaṃ yaddṛśyam |
tattathā tatvataḥ svāntarbhedarahitam |
yadvatkhaṃ yathā''kāśam |
tathedam ākāśavadidaṃ dṛśyam |
tato dṛśyatvāttathā tatvataḥ svāntarbhedahīnam |
kārikāṃ vivaṇoti -- yatheti aṅgīkārāt bhedavādinā mayā |
ghaṭamaṭhākāśayoḥ paramārthato bhinnatve paramārthato'nyonyabhedaśūnyasvaikadeśaiḥ paramārthato bhedarāhityarūpasādhyasyākāśe'bhāvātsādhyavikalo dṛṣṭānta ityarthaḥ || ghaṭādisambandhātprāgiti || ghaṭākāśabhedasya ghaṭaupādhikatve khalu ghaṭākhyopādhisambandhaḥ pratīyate tadabhāve tu neti vaktuṃ śakyate |
svābhāvikatve tūpādhisambandhānapekṣaṇāttadabhāve'pi pratītiranivāryeti bhāvaḥ |
nanu ghaṭākāśabhedasya ghaṭaupādhikatvābhāve'pi ghaṭādhīnapratītikatvaṃ yujyate |
ghaṭasya tadvyañjakatvāt |
vyaṅgyapratīteḥ vyañjakādhīnatvāt |
yathā ghaṭasya pradīpopādhikatvābhāve'pi pradīpādyabhivyaṅgyatvena tadadhīnapratītikatvamiti śaṅkate -- ghaṭādīnāmiti || adoṣa iti || tadabhāve'pi pratītiprasaṅgākhyadoṣo netyarthaḥ |
astu ghaṭasyākāśabhedavyañjakatvam |
tathāpi ghaṭābhāve'pi tatpratītiḥ syādeva |
naca vyañjakābhāve kathaṃ vyaṅgyapratītiriti vācyam |
pradīpākhyavyañjakābhāve'pyandhakāre tvagindriyeṇa ghaṭākhyavyaṅgyapratītidarśanena tādṛśaniyamābhāvāditi pariharati -- neti || nanu dvividhaṃ vyajakaṃ niyatamaniyatañca |
vyaṅgyapratītau yanniyamena vartate tanniyatam |
yathā kuṅkumagandhapratītau niyamena sthitaṃ goghṛtam |
vyaṅgyapratītau yanniyamena na vartate tadaniyatam |
yathā ghaṭasya pradīpādikam |
tatra kuṅkumagandhasya goghṛtamiva ghaṭakāśabhedasya ghaṭo niyatavyañjakatvenāṅgīkriyate |
atastadabhāve'pi na tatpratītiprasaṅga iti śaṅkate -- kuṅkumagandhasyeti || bhedaḥ ghaṭākāśabhedaḥ |
pariharati -- neti || sambhavatyeva ghṛtasya kuṅkumagandhaniyatābhivyañjakatvaṃ tadabhāve tasyānupalabdheḥ |
naca na ghṛtābhāvātkuṅkumagandhānupalabdhiḥ kintu kuṅkume gandhasyaivābhāvāditi vācyam |
kuṅkumaṃ gandhavat pṛthivītvādityanumānena tatra gandhasya siddhatvena gandhābhāvādgandhānupalabdhiriti vaktumayuktatvāt |
naca kuṅkumagandhasyānumānasiddhatve'pi tasya pāṣāṇagandhavadanudbhūtatvena tadanupalabdhirapi sambhavatīti vācyam |
ghṛtasambandhena gandhapratīteranubhavasiddhatvena gandhasyānudbhūtatvāṅgīkāre prāmāṇikaparityāgāprāmāṇikakalpanākhyagauravaprasaṅgādityarthaḥ |
nanvatrākāśabhedasya pramāṇasiddhatvena ghaṭasyābhivyañjakatvaṃ kalpyata ityata āha -- naceti || yena ghaṭasya vyañjakatvaṃ syāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu kathamākāśabhede pramāṇābhāvaḥ |
iha pakṣī neha pakṣīti pratītireva mānamiti cenna |
ālokamaṇḍalaviṣayatvāttasyāḥ |
ihā'loka ityatrālokāvayavānāmiheti nirdeśāt |
nehā'loka ityatra gaganasya nirdeśāt |
ihā'loka iti pratītiriha gaganamiti pratītivadaupacārikī vā |
|| iha pakṣīti |
tathāca pakṣyāśraya eko bhāgastadabhāvāśraya eko bhāga iti bhāgabhedaḥ sidhyatīti bhāvaḥ || ālokamaṇḍaleti || tathā ceheti nākāśabhāgapratītiḥ kintvālokamaṇḍalamevehapratītiviṣaya iti bhāvaḥ |
evaṃ tarhīhā'loka ityatrehapratyayasya kā gatiḥ |
nahi tatrāpyālokamaṇḍalameveheti pratīyata iti vaktuṃ śakyam |
ātmāśrayaprasaṅgāt |
atastatrākāśabhāga evehapratyayagocara iti vācyam |
tadvadiha pakṣītyatrāpi sa eva viṣayaḥ kinnāṅgīkriyata ityāśaṅkya tasyāpyanyathopapattimāha -- nehā'loka itīti |
iha tantuṣu paṭa itivadihā'lokāvayaveṣvāloka iti pratītirupapadyata iti bhāvaḥ |
tarhi nehā'loka ityālokābhāvaḥ pratīyate |
na ca tatrāpyālokāvayavā ihapratyayaviṣayaḥ |
ālokāvayaveṣu bhāsamāneṣu satsu nehā'loka iti pratītyanupapatteḥ |
atastatrākāśabhāga evehapratyayaviṣaya ityaṅgīkāryamityata āha -- nehā'loka iti || tatra gaganamevehapratyayaviṣayo na tadbhāga ityarthaḥ |
nanvihā'loka ityatrālokāvayavānāṃ viṣayatā |
nehā'loka ityatra gaganasya viṣayateti dvaividhyakalpane na kiñcinmūlam |
vinā svadṛṣṭipakṣapātādityasvarasādāha -- ihā'loka iti pratītiriti || yathā gaganasya gaganāśrayatābhāve'pīha gaganamiti gaganāśrayatāpratītiraupacārikī evamālokasyālokāśrayatvābhāve'pīhā'loka ityālokasyaivālokāśrayatvena pratīteraupacārikatvopapattyā nānayā pratītyā gagane bhāgabhedasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
atra pratīteraupacārikatvaṃ nāma bhedābhāve'pyādhārādheyabhāvāvagāhitvenāmukhyārthaviṣayakatvam |
ādhārādheyabhāvo hi bhedasamānādhikaraṇo mukhyaḥ |
tadasamānādhikaraṇastvamukhyo vivakṣitaḥ |
evañcopacārasya śabdadharmatvena pratīteraupacārikatvaṃ kathamiti śaṅkānavakāśaḥ |
kecittvaupacārikaśabdaprayogajanakībhūtā buddhiraupacārikītyupacaryata ityāhuḥ |
tanna pratītisamānākārasya prayogasyaupacārikatvāyogena tajjanakabuddheraupacārikatāyāḥ sutarāmayogāt |
|| tasyā iheti pratīterityarthaḥ |
yadyapyaupādhikaviṣayatvādityeva vaktavyam |
pareṇa pakṣitadabhāvopalakṣitayorupādhitvāṅgīkārāt |
tathā'pyapratyakṣatvasyāpi ghaṭanāya keṣāñcidutprekṣāvādo'yamityadoṣaḥ || ihā'loka iti || atra pūrvavadālokapraveśe ātmāśrayāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvihā'lokāvayava ityatrānupapattirityasvarasādāha -- iheti || aupacārikīti |
idaṃ ca svarītyā vastuta'stvīśo diśaśca kālaśca svagatā eva sarvade'ti vacanātsā pratītirmṛkhyaiveti jñātavyam |
ākāśabhede mānamāśaṅkate -- nanu kathamiti || yadyapyanayā pratītyā''kāśe pākṣitadabhāvau gṛhyete na tadbheda iti kathamiyaṃ bhede pramāṇam |
tathā'pi prācīnamate dravyasya svasamānādhikaraṇātyantābhāvapratiyogitvarūpāvyāpyavṛttitvābhāvāt |
ekatra pakṣitadabhāvādhāratvāsambhavāt anumānamudrayā'nayā bhedasiddhiḥ |
na ceyaṃ pakṣisaṃyogatadabhāvaviṣayeti vaktuṃ yuktam |
śabdasyeva pratītervyākhyānāyogāt |
idaṃ pakṣimannedaṃ pakṣimaditi bhedapratītirapi bodhyā || ālokamaṇḍaleti || adhikaraṇatayā''lokamaṇḍalaviṣayatvādasyā nānayā ākāśabheda iti bhāvaḥ |
nanvihā'loko nehā'loka iti pratītirākāśabhedasādhikā |
na cātrehetyālokanirdeśaḥ sambhavati |
ātmāśrayādityata āha -- ihā'loka iti || atrāpi na gaganamadhikaraṇatayā viṣayaḥ |
gaganasya nīrūpatvena cākṣuṣyāmasyāṃ viṣayatā'yogāt |
niyatopanāyakābhāvenopanayakalpanāyogāt |
ālokāvayavānāmeva viṣayatā yujyata iti bhāvaḥ || aupacārikīti |
nanūpacāro gauṇī vṛttirlakṣaṇā tadatiriktā tadantarbhūtā vā |
sā tu śabdavṛttiḥ kathaṃ pratītau syāditi cenna |
yata aupacārikaśabdaprayojakībhūtā buddhiraupacārikītyupacaryate |
evaṃ neha pakṣītyādau gaganasyādhikaraṇatvaṃ vadata iha gaganamiti pratītāvatmāśrayeṇa gaganasya gaganādhikaraṇatvāsambhavena yathākathañcidbhede'pyādhārādheyabhāvo'mukhyo vaktavyastadvadeva prakṛta iti bhāvaḥ |
|| iha pakṣīti |
pakṣitadabhāvayorvirodhenaikapratītāvekadeśabhānānupapatteriyaṃ pratītirākāśāṃśarūpadeśayorbhedaṃ viṣayīkṛtya tatra bhāvābhāvau viṣayīkarotītyaṃśabhede pratyakṣameva mānamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanūktapratīteraṃśabhedaviṣayakatve'pi na svābhāvikāṃśabhedaviṣaya(ka)tvaṃ sidhyati |
pakṣirūpopādhikṛtāṃśe bhedasyaiva viṣayīkaraṇasambhavāt |
maivam |
pakṣisambandhabhāge pakṣirūpopādhikṛtabhedabhānāṅgīkāre'pi tadrahitabhāge mūrtadravyasambandhābhāvena tatraitaddeśabhedasya vaktumaśakyatvāttatrāvidyamānasya tadbhedakatve'tiprasaṅgāt |
na ca pakṣyabhāva eva bhedakaḥ |
pūrvamapi tatra sattvena tadāpi tvanmate bhedaprasaṅgena pūrvameva bhinnatadbhānāpatteḥ |
na ceṣṭāpattiḥ |
anāditastatsattve upādhikṛtatvakalpane gauravaprasaṅgena svābhāvikatvasiddherityāśayāt || ālokamaṇḍaleti || pratīteścākṣuṣatvānubhavena cakṣurgocarālokaviṣayakatvasyaiva yuktatvādgaganasyācākṣuṣatvāt |
na caitatpratītyā''loke svābhāvikāṃśasiddhau gagane'pi tannyāyena tatsiddhiriti vācyam |
ālokamaṇḍalasya tajjanyatvena tadaṃśānāṃ paṭāṃśānāmiva svāṃśyālokamaṇḍalajanakādhīnatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvevamapīhā'loko nehā'loka ityatra gaganāṃśabhedabhānaṃ vinā gatyabhāvaḥ |
āloke ālokasya vṛttitvābhāvādālokabhedamādāya tadupapādanenehā'loka iti pratītyanupapattirityāśaṅkyādhikaraṇatayā bhāsamānaviṣayabhedaṃ darśayati -- ihā'loka ityādi || nanu ālokāvayave ālokasya samavāyena bhāsamānatvenetyatra gaganaṃ nirdiśya tatsambandhāvacchinnābhāvo bhāsata iti vācyam |
tathā cālokamaṇḍalasambaddhagagane'pyuktasambandhāvacchinnālokābhāvasattvānnehā'loka iti pratītyāpattiḥ |
gaganasyācākṣuṣatvenādhikaraṇāṃśe cākṣuṣatvānubhavavirodhaścetyata āha -- iha gaganamitivaditi || pratītistu śābdī vā mānasī veti anyadetat |
sarvathā svasya svādhikaraṇatāyā ātmāśrayaprasaṅgena bādhitatayā taddeśasthamūrtāntarasambandhaṃ viṣayīkarotīti vaktavyam |
tadvadihā'loka iti pratītiriti bhāvaḥ |
aupacārikī mukhyādhikaraṇatvāviṣayiṇī ghaṭe jñānaṃ vaṭe gāva iti śabdollekhipratītivadamukhyasambandhāntaraviṣayiṇītyarthaḥ |
tenopacārapadārthabhūtāmukhyavṛttyoḥ śabdāśritatvena pratyakṣe tadasambhave'pi na kṣatiḥ ||
mānamityasyākāśabheda ityādiḥ |
yadyapīha pakṣī, neha pakṣīti pratīteḥ pakṣitadabhāvayorākāśavṛttitvāvagāhitayā nākāśabhede mānatvam |
tathāpi tādṛśapratītirvibhinnadeśavṛttitvaviṣayā, viruddhadharmaviṣayatvāt |
iha ghaṭatvaṃ neha ghaṭatvamiti pratītivadityanumānotthāpakatayā mānatvaṃ bodhyam |
vastutaḥ pakṣyabhāvasya pakṣimaddeśabhedasamaniyatatayā tadabhinnatvāttadavagāhipratītestatra mānatvamityavadheyam |
atra yadyapi avidyamānamevākāśabhedamālokādyupādhirbhāsayatīti vaktuṃ śakyam |
tathāpi lāghavamanusṛtyāha --
|| āloketi || nanvevamapīhā'loko nehā'loka iti pratītireva mānāntaramāstām |
svasya svādhāratvānupapattyā tasyāḥ pūrvavadālokamaṇḍalaviṣayatvāsambhavādityata āha -- ihā'loka iti || gaganasyālokādhāratvābhāvena tadviṣayatvāsambhavāditi bhāvaḥ || gaganasyeti || ālokāvayavānām ālokābhāvādhāratvāsambhavena tadviṣayatvāyogāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvevamihā'loka iti samyogasaṃsargāvagāhipratyayo na syāt |
avayavāvayavinostadabhāvāt |
na ca gaganasyaiva tadviṣayatvam |
gaganālokayoḥ samyogasattve'pi samyogenādhārādheyabhāvābhāvādata āha -- aupacārikī veti || gaganasambandhitvarūpeha śabdagauṇārthaviṣayaṇītyarthaḥ |
prayojanābhāve kathamopacārikaprayoga ityata uktam || iha gaganamiti pratītivaditi || yathā tatredamityartha iheti prayogo rūḍhopacāratvānna prayojanaviśeṣamapekṣate tathehāpītyarthaḥ |
yadvā , nanvavayavāvayavinorādhārādheyabhāvavyavahāro'nubhavaviruddhaḥ |
kapāle ghaṭa ityādipratīterasiddheḥ |
ataḥ kathamālokāvayavānāmiheti nirdeśa ityata āha -- aupacārikī veti || asyāpyayamāloka ityevārtha iti bhāvaḥ |
kathamityākṣepe |
ākāśabhede mānābhāvaḥ kathaṃ na kathamapi |
kiṃ tvastyevetyarthaḥ |
kiṃ tanmānamityata āha -- iha pakṣīti || iyaṃ hi pratītiriheti nirdiṣṭākāśe pakṣitadabhāvau gṛhṇāti |
nahyekasminnākāśe tayoḥ sattvaṃ yuktam |
pratiyogitadabhāvayorekatra virodhāt |
ata ākāśapradeśe bhedasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
iyaṃ pratītirālokamaṇḍale pakṣitadabhāvau gṛhṇātyato nākāśabhede mānamityāha -- āloketi || nanvihā'loko nehā'loka iti pratītiriheti nirdiṣṭākāśe ālokatadabhāvau gṛhṇāti |
na cātrāpīhetyālokamaṇḍalanirdeśa iti vācyam |
tathātve āloke āloka ityāpattyā''tmāśrayāt |
ato'nayā pratītyā''kāśabhedasiddhirityata āha -- ihā'loka iti || gaganasyetyanantaramihetītyanuvartate |
nanūbhayatrāpīhetyekanirdeśasyānubhavasiddhatvenoktarītyā vyāvasthābhyupagame'nubhavavirodha ityaparitoṣādāha -- ihā'loka iti || nehā'loka itivadihā'loka ityatrāpīheti gaganasyaiva nirdeśaḥ |
naca pratiyogitadabhāvayorekādhiraṇatvānupapattiḥ |
gaganasyālokādhikaraṇatvānaṅgīkārāt |
nacehā'loka iti pratītyanupapattiḥ |
gagane gaganābhāve'pīha gaganamitivadgagane ālokābhāve'pīhāloka ityaupacārikapratītyaṅgīkāre bādhakābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
yadvehā'loka iti pratītāvihetyālokāvayavanirdeśa iti vaktuṃ na śakyate |
laukikānāmavayavāśritatayā'vayavibuddherabhāvāt |
nahi lokā hastādiṣu śarīramiti vyavaharanti |
kintu tadātmatāmevetyata āha -- ihā'loka iti || ihā'loka ityatrāpīhetyālokasyaiva nirdeśaḥ |
nacātmāśrayaḥ |
asyāḥ pratīteraupacārikatvāṅgīkārāt |
upacāraprayojanaṃ vācyamityata uktamiha gaganamiti pratītivaditi |
gagane gaganābhāve'pi yatheha gagane gaganamiti pratītistathetyarthaḥ |
rūḍhopacāratvānna prayojanāpekṣeti bhāvaḥ |
nanvathā'pi ghaṭādirūpopādhiḥ kiṃ gaganamātraṃ spṛśati uta gaganaikadeśam |
nādyaḥ sarvo'pi ghaṭākāśaḥ syāt |
dvitīye svābhāvikāṃśasiddhiriti cenna |
sarvo'pyākāśo ghaṭākāśaḥ syāditi ko'rthaḥ |
kiṃ ghaṭasambandhī syāditi uta ghaṭaparimāṇasadṛśaparimāṇaḥ syāditi, sarvo'pi mahākāśaḥ ghaṭākāśaḥ syāditi vā |
nādyaḥ |
iṣṭāpatteḥ |
na dvitīyaḥ |
upādheḥ pratibimbapakṣapātitvena (pratibimbapakṣapātitvāvaśyambhāvena) ghaṭasyopādheḥ pratibimbasthānīyākāśe svagataparimāṇapratītyādhānāt |
mahākāśaṃ tu bimbasthānīyamiti (bimbasthānāpannamiti) na tatra svadharmoparañjakaḥ |
ata eva na tṛtīyaḥ |
uparañjitaghaṭadharmatvasya ghaṭākāśapadapravṛttinimittasya paricchinnākāśa iva mahākāśe'bhāvena tatra tadavyavahārāt |
saṃyogasya svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvaṃ pramāṇabalādaṅgīkriyate |
pramāṇameva virodhaṃ śamayiṣyati |
`saṃvideva hi bhagavatī viṣayasattvopagame naḥ śaraṇa'miti nyāyāditi |
|| athāpīti || uktarītyā gagane svābhāvikāṃśasiddhāvapi vikalpadvaye dvitīyavikalpe'ṅgīkriyamāṇe sati svābhāvikāṃśasiddhiriti yojanā || gaganamātramiti || kṛtstragaganamityarthaḥ |
tatra prathamapakṣaṃ tāvatkakṣīkṛtya tatroktaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ parihartuṃ vikalpena pṛcchati -- sarvo'pyākāśa iti || sarvo'pi ghaṭākāśo mahākāśaḥ syāditi veti || sarvo'pi mahākāśo ghaṭākāśaḥ syāditi yojanā |
kvacittu tathaiva pāṭhaḥ |
ghaṭākāśaḥ syādityasya ghaṭākāśapadena vyavahriyamāṇaḥ syādityarthaḥ || svagataparimāṇapratītyādhānāditi || svagatavitastyādiparimāṇaṃ ghaṭākāśe ādāya tatpratītijanakatvādityarthaḥ |
ata eva na tatra svadharmoparañjaka iti vakṣyati |
vitastyādiparimāṇatvarūpasvadharmāsañjaka ityarthaḥ |
ata evetyuktameva vivaṇoti -- uparañjitaghaṭadharmatvasyeti || āsañjitavitastyādiparimāṇarūpaghaṭadharmakatvasyetyarthaḥ |
|| tatra tadavyavahārāditi || mahākāśe ghaṭākāśapadavyavahārābhāvādityarthaḥ |
yadyapyādyapakṣo nādvaitavādinaḥ sammatastathā'pi tatroktadūṣaṇamalagnakamityupapādanārthaṃ tatkakṣīkaraṇamiti jñātavyam |
ata eva dvitīyapakṣamabhyupetya tatroktaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ pariharati -- saṃyogasyeti || ayamāśayaḥ |
ghaṭadyupādhirgaganaikadeśaṃ spṛśatīti pakṣamevāṅgīkurmaḥ |
na ca tarhi svābhāvikāṃśasiddhiriti santoṣṭavyam |
gaganaikadeśaṃ spṛśatītyasya gaganaikadeśena saṃyogavānityarthānaṅgīkārāt |
kintu ghaṭasaṃyogo gagane svātyarantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇa ityatra tātparyam |
na ca tathātve'pi svābhāvikāṃśasiddhirdurvārā |
pratiyogitadatyantābhāvayorviruddhatvena sāmānādhikaraṇyāsambhavena virodhaparihārārthamaṃśabhedasyāvaśyamaṅgīkāryatvāditi vācyam |
vṛkṣe kapisaṃyogatadatyantābhāvayoriva gagane'pi ghaṭatadatyantābhāvayoryugapadeva pratyakṣeṇa darśanāt |
pramāṇasiddhe cārthe virodhaśaṅkānavakāśāditi |
atra naiyyāyikanyāyasammatimāha -- saṃvideveti || bhagavatī mānyā |
apalāpānarheti yāvat |
svābhāvikabhede'nupapattimuktvaupādhikabhedavatāmaikyaṃ pariharati -- nanviti || ghaṭasyākāśo ghaṭa ivākāśo ghaṭasyaivākāśa iti tredhā'bhipretya pṛcchati -- ghaṭa ityādinā |
tṛtīyapakṣasya ghaṭākāśo maṭhākāśa iti vyavahārabhedo na syādityarthaḥ || pratibimbeti || idamatra rahasyam |
upādhirekameva bimbaṃ dvedhā pradarśayati |
atra pratibimbaparimāṇādikamādadhāti |
evañca eka eva mahākāśo ghaṭākāśatayā pratibimbatāmākāśāntaratvamāpadyate |
sa eva ghaṭaparimāṇatayocyata iti || ata eveti || pratibimbapakṣapātitvādevetyarthaḥ || pravṛttinimittasyeti || evañca ghaṭasambandhamātrasyānimittatvānna vyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ |
sarvākāśasyāpi ghaṭasambandhitve pradeśāntare tadabhāvadhīrna syādityata āha -- saṃyogasyeti || tatra pradeśe ākāśo ghaṭasaṃyogī atra tu neti sāmānādhikaraṇye pramāṇābhāvādityarthaḥ |
na cātra bhinnādhikaraṇatvameva dṛśyata iti vācyam |
ākāśaḥ saṃyogī ākāśo na saṃyogītyubhayatrākāśasya viśeṣyatayā bhānāt |
atra tatretyavacchedakamātratvāditi || pramāṇameva virodhaṃśamayiṣyatīti |
vaiyyadhikaraṇyapramāṇena kila virodho grāhyaḥ |
yadi tadeva sāmānādhikaraṇyagrāhi tadā kathaṃ virodha iti bhāvaḥ |
gaganamātraṃ kṛtstragaganam || svābhāvikāṃśasiddhiritīti |
tathā ca dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyamiti bhāvaḥ |
|| sarvo'pi ghaṭākāśo mahākāśaḥ syāditi veti || sarvo'pi mahākāśo ghaṭākāśo ghaṭākāśapadavācyaḥ syāditi yojanā |
kvacittathaiva pāṭhaḥ || upādheḥ pratibimbapakṣapātitveti || darpaṇādyupādheḥ svagatamālinyādeḥ pratimukha evonmajjanadarśanena tathā svabhāvakalpanamiti bhāvaḥ |
svadharmoparañjako ghaṭa upādhiriti vibhaktivipariṇāmenāvṛttyā'nvayaḥ |
nanu paricchinnākāśamahākāśayorna mukhapratimukhanyāyena bimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ |
ghaṭasyāsvacchadravyatvenākāśasya ca nīrūpatvena tatra tasya pratibimbatvāyogāt |
yoge vā darpaṇasthamālinyaṃ yathā samastamukhapratibimbe bhāsate na tu tadekadeśe karṇādau tathā'trāpīti sarvaṃ mukhaṃ malinamitivatsarvākāśo ghaṭaparimāṇa iti buddhiḥ syāt |
tasmāddhaṭasambandha ākāśaikadeśavṛttiriti vaktavyam |
tasyāpyekadeśasyaupādhikatve'navasthānātsvābhāvika iti vaktavyam |
tathā ca dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyamityata āha -- saṃyogasyeti |
|| gaganamātramiti || klṛptaṃ gaganamityarthaḥ || ghaṭaparimāṇeti || mahākāśa ityādiḥ |
mahākāśe ghaṭaparimāṇasamānaparimāṇatvaprāptau tasya mahattvābhāvāddhaṭātirikte tatpratītyabhāvāpāta iti bhāvaḥ || mahākāśaḥ syāditi veti || ghaṭāvacchinnākāśa eva mahattvabuddhyāpāta iti bhāvaḥ |
svanimittakākāśāṃśa eva svadharmādhāyakatvena ghaṭaparimāṇasya mahākāśe prāptau bījābhāvaḥ |
tayorbhedasyaivopādhinā kṛtatvāditi samādhiṃ dṛṣṭāntapradarśanapūrvakaṃ vakti || upādheriti || pratibimbeti || bimbapratibimbayoraikye'pyupādhinā tatra bhedāvabhāsapūrvakaṃ pratibimba eva svakīyamālinyādidharmāvabhāsakatvadarśanenopādheḥ pratibimbapakṣapātitvasvābhāvyenetyarthaḥ |
bhavatu prakṛte kimāyātam |
mahākāśaghaṭākāśayorbimbapratibimbabhāvābhāvādghaṭāderasvacchatvādityata āha -- pratibimbasthānīyeti || svanimittakabhedavattvādinā pratibimbasadṛśa ityarthaḥ |
tathā copādherbhedavattvena svakalpitatvāvacchedena svadharmādhāyakatvaṃ lāghavātsvīkriyate na tu tadviśiṣṭapratibimbatvāvacchedena gauravāt |
na tu pratibimbatvāvacchedena svākalpitapratibimbeṣvapi tathātvāpātāditi bhāvaḥ || svādharmoparañjaka iti || upādherityataḥ prathamāntatayā'nuvartate || uparañjitaghaṭadharmatvasyeti || ghaṭenoparañjita uparañjitaghaṭaḥ |
rājadantādivattṛttīyātatpuruṣe'pi paranipātaḥ |
sa cāsau dharmaśceti vigrahaḥ |
athavopañjitaghaṭako ghaṭo yasmindharme ghaṭākāśatvādau sa uparañjitaghaṭaḥ sa cāsau dharmaśceti vigrahaḥ |
tathā ca ghaṭarūpopādhyuparāgātpratīyamānasya ghaṭākāśapadārthatāvacchedakībhūtaghaṭavṛttitva(ghaṭita)ghaṭākāśatvarūpadharmasya mahākāśe'bhāvena ghaṭākāśatvamahattvayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyābhāvena ghaṭākāśo mahānsyādityāpādanamayuktamiti bhāvaḥ |
kuto mahākāśe tadabhāvaḥ |
upādheḥ pratibimbapakṣapātitvena bimbe svadharmānādhāyakatvasyoktatvāt || tatra tadavyavahārāditi || ghaṭākāśe mahattvavyavahārābhāvādityarthaḥ |
a(ta)traiva pūrvavākyopapādito mahattvaghaṭākāśatvayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyābhāvo hetuḥ |
pūrvavākyaitāvatparyantābhiprāyo vaktavyaḥ |
mahākāśe ghaṭākāśatvaṃ kuto na svīkriyata ityata āha -- tatreti || mahākāśe ghaṭākāśatvavyavahārābhāvādityarthaḥ |
vyavahārapadaṃ vyavahāratanmūlapratītyādipramāṇopalakṣaṇam |
svābhāvikāṃśānaṅgīkāre ākāśe ghaṭāsaṃyogadaśāyāmeva pratīyamānatadabhāvo na ghaṭeta |
ekatra bhāvābhāvayorvirodhādaṃśabhedasya svābhāvikasyāṅgīkāre vyavasthayā tadubhayasattvasambhavādityata āha -- saṃyogasyeti || kecittu paricchinnākāśamahākāśayorna mukhapratimukhanyāyena bimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ |
ākāśasya nīrūpatvādghaṭasyāsvacchatvāt |
yoge vā sarvaṃ mukhaṃ malinamitivatsarvākāśe ghaṭaparimāṇadhīḥ syādato ghaṭasyākāśasambandhitvameva vaktavyam |
tasya cāṃśavṛttitvāsiddhyarthaṃ svābhāvikāṃśo'pekṣita ityata āha -- saṃyogasyeti || (tathā ca) saṃyogasya pradeśavṛttitvānaṅgīkārānnāṃśāpekṣeti bhāva ityāhuḥ |
prathamapakṣamāśaṅkyeṣṭāpatteriti |
ghaṭasaṃyogāṅgīkāre tvetaddūṣaṇena tadvyavasthāpanamaprayojanam |
pratibimbasthānīyatvasya tallakṣaṇasyaivoktatvena
pratibimbatvānuktyā taddūṣaṇāsaṅgatiḥ |
aṅgīkṛtyopāgherdūṣaṇapakṣe ca pratibimba eva sarvamukhaṃ malinamiti vyavahārādijanakatvavadatrāpi ghaṭākāśe tatparimāṇapratītijanakatvaṃ vartata eva mahākāśe tadāpādakaṃ tu nāstyeva |
ālokasthadarpaṇe ālokatvapratibimbadarśanena tatpratibimbakatvasya tatsambandhāvirodhitayā tatsambandhavyavasthāpanāya tatpratibimbakatvanirākaraṇaṃ cānupayuktamityādikamatra kathaṃ parihartavyamiti cintyam |
svātyantābhāvetyādyaṃśabhedaṃ vinā'pītyādirviruddhadvayaṃ kathaṃ pramāṇabalādaṅgīkriyate |
svaviṣayībhūtārthaviruddhārthāvagāhinaḥ pramāṇatvasyaiva durghaṭatvādityata āha -- pramāṇameveti || śamayiṣyati nirākariṣyati |
virodho nāmaikādhikaraṇāvṛttitvaṃ tacca na sidhyati |
ekajñāne ubhayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyābhānāt |
tathā ca tayorviruddhatvameva nāstītyarthaḥ |
tarhi nirvahnirvahnimānityekajñāne bhānādetatpratītyā sarvābhāvayorvirodhocchedāpattirityata āha -- saṃvideva hi bhagavatīti |
abādhitasaṃvidityarthaḥ |
tathā cābādhitabuddhireva vastuvyavasthāpikā na buddhimātram |
nirvahnirvahnimānityāderuttaratraikatarabādhapramādarśanena nāvirodhopapādakatvamatra tūbhayāṃśe'pyuttaratra bādhādarśanādavirodha eveti bhāvaḥ |
syādetat |
ghaṭāderghaṭākāśādivyavahārahetutvānyathānupapattyaivākāśabhedaḥ setsyati |
tathāhi -- ākāśāsambaddhasya sarvākāśasambaddhasya vā ghaṭāderghaṭākāśādivyavahārahetutve sarvākāśasya ghaṭākāśatvāpatterekadeśa sambaddhasyaiva tathātvaṃ vācyam |
ekadeśasyaupādhikatve caivamevānavasthānāt svābhāvikatvasyaiva vācyatayā tannirvāhakasvābhāvikabhedasiddhirityāśaṅkate -- nanviti || athāti uktapratītyābhedasiddhyanaṅgīkāre'pi |
upādhirghaṭākāśādivyavahārahetuḥ |
gaganamātraṃ spṛśatītyasya kimakhilaṃ gaganaṃ spṛśatītyarthaḥ kiṃ vā nikhilāvayaveṣu gaganamityuta nikhilopādhyavacchedena gaganamityāhosvidupādhyanavacchinnaṃ gaganamiti ? nādyaḥ |
gaganasyaikatvāt |
na dvitīyaḥ |
niravayavatvāt |
na tṛtīyaḥ |
anubhavavirodhāt |
caturthastāvadangīkriyate |
tatra ca noktadoṣa ityata āha -- neti || dhaṭaparimāṇasamānaparimāṇaḥ syāditi || tādṛśaparimāṇavattvena vyavahartavyaḥ syādityarthaḥ |
upādheḥ svopahite svadharmavyavahārahetutvāditi bhāvaḥ || mahākāśo ghaṭākāśaḥ syāditi || upādhyanavacchinno'pi ghaṭākāśatvena vyavahartavyaḥ syādityarthaḥ |
vyavahāranimittasyopādhisambandhasya mahākāśa eva prāpteriti bhāvaḥ |
nanu bimbapratibimbabhāvasthale hyupādheḥ pratibimbapakṣapātitvam |
darpaṇādinā pratimukha eva svagatamālinyādhānadarśanāt |
prakṛte ca tadabhāvāt mahākāśe'pi ghaṭaparimāṇādhānāpattirityāśaṅkyoktam || pratibimbasthānīyeti || upādheḥ kālpitabhāgamātrapakṣapatitvasvābhāvyāditi bhāvaḥ || ata eveti || ghaṭādyupādheḥ svopahita eva svadharmādhāyakatvādityarthaḥ |
etadeva vivaṇoti -- uparañjiteti || tathāca tatpadapravṛ'ttinimittābhāvādeva tadavyavahāraḥ iti bhāvaḥ ||
nanvevamākāśe ghaṭādisaṃyogasyāvyāpyavṛttitvaṃ na syāt |
tasya svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvarūpasya pradeśabhedanirvāhyatvādityata āha -- saṃyogasyeti || pramāṇabalāditi || iha ghaṭasaṃyogo, neheti pratītibalāddhaṭagaganasaṃyogaḥ svādhikaraṇayāvanniṣṭhābhāvapratiyogī saṃyogatvāt, vṛkṣavānarasaṃyogavadityanumānabalāccetyarthaḥ |
evamupādhirgagaganamātraṃ spṛśatītyādyapakṣāṅgīkāreṇa samādhāya gaganaikadeśamiti dvitīyapakṣāṅgīkāreṇa tadvivaraṇameva svātyantābhāvetyādinā kṛtamiti tūpacintyam |
gaganāvayavasambandhitvarūpamukhyārthābhiprāyeṇa gaganaikadeśaṃ spṛśatīti vikalpitatvāt |
niruktagoṇārthāṅgīkāramātreṇa tatpakṣāṅgīkārāyogāt |
prathamapakṣe'pyetadaṅgīkāreṇaitasya pakṣāntaratvāsambhavācca |
nanvevaṃ sati yo yatpratiyogī sa tadvirodhīti sāmānyataḥ pratiyogyabhāvayorvirodhagrāhakamānavirodha ityata āha -- pramāṇameveti || virodhaṃśamayiṣyati -- tadgrahaṃ nivartayatītyarthaḥ |
viśeṣapramāṇena sāmānyavyāpterābhāsīkaraṇāditi bhāvaḥ |
imameva vṛddhasammatyā draḍhayati -- saṃvideva hīti || bhagavatītyabādhitatvenādaraṇīyatvamāha |
śaraṇaṃ hetuḥ |
prakārāntareṇā'kāśabhedaṃ śaṅkate -- nanviti || athāpi uktarītyā''kāśabhedāsiddhāvapi vakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇa sidhyatītyarthaḥ |
ko'yaṃ vakṣyamāṇaprakāra ityata āha -- ghaṭetyādi || asambaddhasya bhedakatve paṭāsambaddhamahārajanasyāpi tathātvāpattyā sambaddhasyaiva bhedakatvaṃ vācyam |
tathāca ghaṭākhyopādhiyogaḥ kiṃ sarvākāśavṛttiḥ utaikadeśavṛttirityarthaḥ || sarvo'pīti || uktamahārajanasya sarvapaṭasambandhitve sarvasyāpi raktapaṭatvadarśanāditi bhāvaḥ || svābhāvikāṃśasiddhiriti || nanu māstu gagane svābhāvikāṃśaḥ naca tathātve ghaṭasya gaganaikadeśaṃ sambadhya bhedakatvāyogaḥ |
aupādhikāṃśāṅgīkāreṇa taṃ sambadhya bhedakatvopapatteriti cenna |
aupādhikāṃśāṅgīkāre'navasthāprasaṅgāt |
tathāhi |
upādhikṛto hyaṃśa aupādhikaḥ |
tatra praṣṭavyaṃ kimayamupādhiḥ sarvākāśaṃ spṛśatyutaikadeśamiti |
nādyaḥ |
sarvasyāpyākāśasyaupādhikatvaprasaṅgāt |
dvitīye praṣṭavyaṃ sa caikadeśaḥ svābhāvika utaupādhikaḥ |
nādyaḥ |
anaṅgīkārāt |
dvitīye so'pyupādhissarvamutaikadeśamiti vikalpadūṣaṇaparamparāparyavasānāditi bhāvaḥ |
prathamavikalpamaṅgīkṛtya tatroktadūṣaṇaṃ nirākartuṃ pṛcchati -- sarvo'pīti || ghaṭākāśa iti ākāśo ghaṭākāśapadena vyavahartavyaḥ syādityarthaḥ || iṣṭāpatteriti || ākāśe ekadeśānaṅgīkārāt ghaṭasambandhaḥ sarvākāśavṛttiriti bhāvaḥ || upādheḥ pratibimbeti || pratibimba eva svadharmāsañjakatvaṃ hyupādheḥ svabhāvaḥ |
yathā darpaṇarūpopādhiḥ pratibimbe pratimukhe svagatamalinatvādidharmamāsañjayati |
darpaṇasya malinatve pratimukhe'pi malinatatvapratīteḥ |
darpaṇasya malinatve'pi mukhe svacchatvapratīteḥ |
tathātrāpi ghaṭopādhiḥ pratibimbe ghaṭākāśe svagataparimāṇamāsañjayati |
natu bimbe mahākāśe |
ato na tasya ghaṭasamānaparimāṇatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
ādhānāt āsañjanāt |
tatra mahākāśe || ata eveti || ghaṭasyopādhermahākāśe svagataparimāṇāsañjakatvābhāvādevetyarthaḥ |
nanu ghaṭenopādhinā mahākāśe svagataparimāṇānāsañjane'pi mahākāśapadavyavahāraḥ kiṃ na syādityata āha -- uparañjitaghaṭadharmatvasyeti || uparañjitaḥ āhitaḥ ghaṭadharmaḥ parimāṇarūpo yasya sa tathā tasya bhāvastatvam |
tasyetyarthaḥ |
paricchinnākāśe ghaṭākāśe |
tatra mahākāśe |
tadvyavahārāt ghaṭākāśapadavyavahārābhāvāt |
ayamarthaḥ |
padavyavahāro hi padapravṛttirnimittaṃ kāraṇam |
yathā hi paricchinnākāśe ghaṭākāśapadapravṛttinimittasya ghaṭāhitaparimāṇākhyadharmasya sattvena tatra ghaṭākāśaśabdavyavahāraḥ |
naca mahākāśe āhitaghaṭadharmarūpaṃ ghaṭākāśapadapravṛttinimittamasti |
yena mahākāśe ghaṭākāśapadaprayogaḥ syāditi |
evamādyapakṣamaṅgīkṛtya samādhāyedānīṃ ghaṭasaṃyogasyākāśaikadeśavṛttitvamiti dvitīyapakṣamaṅgīkṛtya naiyāyikarītyā samādhatte || saṃyogasyeti || dhaṭasaṃyogasyaikadeśavṛttitvaṃ hi naikadeśe vidyamānatvam |
kintu svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvam |
asyārthaḥ |
svaḥ saṃyogaḥ tatpratiyogiko yo'tyantābhāvaḥ saṃyogātyantābhāvaḥ |
tena samānamekamadhikaraṇaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ tasya bhāvastatvamiti |
samānapadasyaikārthakatvaṃ `samānaṃ vṛkṣa'mityatra dṛṣṭam |
nanu pratiyogitadatyantābhāvayorekatra virodhātkathametadityata uktaṃ pramāṇabalāditi |
iha ghaṭākāśasaṃyogo neha mūle kapisaṃyogo nāgre ityādipratītirūpapramāṇabalādityarthaḥ |
pramāṇapramitevirodhāprasarāditi bhāvaḥ |
atra naiyāyikanyāyasammatimāha -- saṃvideva hīti || no'smākam |
viṣayasattvopagame padārthasatvāṅgīkāre |
bhagavatī pūjyā |
saṃvideva pratītireva |
śaraṇaṃ rakṣakam |
pramāṇamiti yāvaditi nyāyārthaḥ |
evamākāśabhede mānābhāvānna ghaṭasyākāśabhedavyañjakatvamiti sthitam |
nanu vidyamāna eva bhedo ghaṭādibhirnābhivyajyate cettarhyutpadyata iti vaktavyam |
taccāyuktam |
bhedasyautpattikatvenānāgantukatvāditi cet tarhi doṣadvayaparihārāyāvidyamānameva bhedaṃ ghaṭādyupādhiḥ (ghaṭādiḥ) pratibhāsayatītyastu |
evaṃ cedvayaṃ caritārthāḥ |
śrutayaśca (śrutayastu) neha nānā'stītyādyā abhedabodhikāḥ |
tasmānna svabhāvato viṣṇorviśvabhinnatvamiti pūrvapakṣasaṅkṣepaḥ |
evaṃ ghaṭādyupādhergagane vidyamāne bhede vyañjakatvamupapadyata ityuktam |
tatrāśaṅkate -- nanviti || atra yadītyadhyāhāryaṃ tarhīti śravaṇāt || utpadyata iti vaktavyamiti || abhivyaktyutpattī vinopādheḥ kāryāntarādarśanāditi bhāvaḥ || autpattikatveneti || dharmisamasattākatvenetyarthaḥ |
anāgantukatvāt madhye prāptatvābhāvāt |
tathācābhivyaktipakṣamevāśritya tatroktadoṣaḥ kathamapi pariharaṇīya iti bhāvaḥ |
pakṣadvayoktadoṣasyāpyaparihāryatvāttṛtīyapakṣāvalambena śaṅkāṃ samādhatte || tarhīti || doṣadvayeti |
abhivyañjakatvapakṣe kadāciddhāṭādīnāmabhāve'pi bhedapratītiprasaṅgaḥ |
utpattipakṣe bhedasyānāgantukatvabhaṅgaprasaṅga iti doṣadvayetyarthaḥ |
na ca tathaivāstviti vaktuṃ śakyamityāśayenāha -- evaṃ cediti || avidyamānasyaiva bhedasya pratibhāsāṅgīkāre śuktirūpyavanmithyātvasyaiva siddhyā dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyābhāvenoktānumānamasmadīyaṃ nirduṣṭameva jātamiti bhāvaḥ |
evamādyānumānadvayena jīveśvarayorjīvānāṃ cābhedaṃ prasādhya tatsthemne tṛtīyena bhedamātrasya mithyātvamabhidhāya caturthenācetanānāmapi mitho bhedābhāvamabhidhāyedānīṃ tatraiva śrutīrapi pramāṇayati -- śrutayaśceti || iha cetanācetanavarge kiñcana nānā bhinnaṃ nāstītyarthaḥ || abhedabodhikā iti || jīveśvarayorjīvānāṃ jaḍānāṃ ca mithaḥ pāramārthikabhedābhāvabodhikā ityarthaḥ |
upasaṃharati -- tasmāditi || bhede mānābhāvādabhede tadbhāvāccetyarthaḥ |
nanvevamapi pakṣe dṛṣṭānte ca bhedasyaupādhikatvamevāyātam |
na mithyātvamityetacchaṅkāsamādhivyājena vadiṣyannāha -- nanvityādinā || doṣadvayeti |
upādhyabhivyaṅgyatve tadanusaraṇaniyamāyogaḥ |
tadutpannatve autpattikatvahāniriti doṣadvayetyarthaḥ || pratibhāsayatīti |
śuktirajatavatprātibhāsikatvānmithyātvamiti bhāvaḥ || caritārthā iti || mithyātvasādhanāyāsmābhirna yatitavyamityarthaḥ |
upādhighaṭasambandhasya gagane'vyāpyavṛttitvamevāṅgīkriyate |
natvekadeśavṛttitvaṃ yena gagane tāttvikasvāntarbhedaḥ syāt |
kintu svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvam |
na ca pratiyogitadabhāvayoravacchedakabhedaṃ vinaikatra virodha iti vācyam |
bhagavatyāḥ pratītereva virodhaśāmakatvādityarthaḥ |
autpattikatvena kālato dharmivyāpakatvena || śrutayaśceti || tathācābhede pramāṇabāhulyādabhede pramāṇābhāva eva bhede pramāṇamiti nāśaṅkanīyamiti bhāvaḥ |
|| autpattikatveneti || svābhāvikatvenetyarthaḥ || anāgantukatvāditi || ajanyatvādityarthaḥ |
atyantābhāvābhinnatve sati nityābhāvatvasya tallakṣaṇatvāditi bhāvaḥ || doṣadvayeti |
upādhikāla eva pratīyamānatvena pūrvaṃ sthityayogarūpadoṣo nityatvādutpattidoṣa iti doṣadvayetyarthaḥ |
avidyamānamāne prakṛte kimagatamityata āha -- evañceti || pratīyamānatve sati traikālika niṣedhapratiyogitvarūpamasmadabhimatamithyātvaṃ sarvabhedānāṃ prāptamiti siddhamevāsmadabhimatamiti bhāvaḥ |
śuṣkayuktīnāmasādhakatvamiti nāśaṅkanīyamityāha -- śrutayaśceti || ādyapadena `yatra dvaitamiva bhavati' `vibhedajanake jñāna' ityādīnāṃ grahaṇam |
pūrvapakṣaṃ nigamayati -- tasmāditi || iti pūrvapakṣavyākhyānaṃ samāptam |
bhedasyaupādhikatvapakṣe'nupapatteḥ svābhāvikatvasiddhirityāṅkate -- nanu yadīti || autpattikatvena yāvadāśrayapratītibhāvitvasiddhāntena |
anāgantukatvāt upādhijanyatvāyogādityarthaḥ |
yadyuktānupapattyā bhedasyaupādhikatvāsiddhistarhi tata eva svābhāvikatvasyāpyasiddhau mithyātvameva siddhyatītyāha -- tarhīti || doṣadvayaparihārāyeti || atiprasaṅgāpasiddhāntarūpadoṣadvayaparihārayetyarthaḥ |
tataḥ kimityata āha -- evaṃ cediti || yadyākāśabhedasya mithyātvaṃ tarhītyarthaḥ |
caritārthāḥ siddhasvapakṣāḥ |
prāguktānumānasya dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyādyabhāvena svāntarbhedahīnatvarūpasādhyasādhakatvasambhavāditi bhāvaḥ |
evamabhede pratyakṣānumāne upāpādyāgamamapyāha -- śrutayastviti || `neha nānāsti kiñcana', `ekamevādvitīya'mityādiśrutīnāṃ niṣedhaparatayā prāguktatvāttata eva prābalyādbhedaśrutibādhakatvaṃ setsyatītyabhiprāyeṇa tannopapāditam |
pūrvapakṣamupasaṃharati -- tasmāditi || uktarītyā parapakṣe sādhakasya bādhakābhāvasya cābhāvātsvapakṣe tadbhāvāccetyarthaḥ |
pūrvapakṣasaṅkṣepa ityanena nirasanīyayuktyantarasattvaṃ sūcitam |
taccādvaitaśrutīnām upakramādiṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgayuktatvādirūpamupariṣṭāt prapañcayiṣyāmaḥ || itirpūpakṣagranthaḥ ||
idānīṃ vipakṣe bādhakakathanena ghaṭasyākāśabhedavyañjakatvaṃ śaṅkate -- nanviti || tarhītyanantaraṃ ghaṭādibhirityanuvartate |
prakārāntarābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
autpattikatvena svābhāvikatvena |
anāgantukatvāt madhye prāptatvābhāvāt || doṣadvayeti || ghaṭādīnāṃ bhedavyañjakatve tadabhāve'pi kadācitpratītiprasaṅgarūpo mayoktaḥ anabhivyañjakatve utpatyanupapattirūpastvayoktaḥ evaṃ doṣadvayetyarthaḥ |
ghaṭādyupādhyapekṣayā pūrvaṃ bhedānaṅgīkārādādyadoṣaparihāraḥ |
ghaṭādīnāmupattyanaṅgīkārānna dvitīyadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ |
na kevalamevamaṅgīkāre doṣadvayaparihāramātraṃ kintarhītyata āha -- evaṃ cediti || avidyamānaṃ bhedaṃ pratibhāsayatītyaṅgīkāre bhedasya mithyātvaṃ prāptaṃ tathācābhedavādino vayaṃ caritārthāḥ |
bhedasya pāramārthikatvābhāvena dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
evamabhede pratyakṣānumāne abhidhāyāgamaṃ cāha -- śrutaya iti || upasaṃharati -- tasmāditi || bhede mānābhāvādabhede tatsadbhāvāccetyarthaḥ |
atrāyaṃ parihāraḥ || yattāvaduktaṃ pratyakṣaṃ na jīveśvarayorbhede pramāṇamiti tatra brūmaḥ |
īśvaradharmiko jīvapratiyogiko bheda īśvarasya pratyakṣaḥ |
( īśvarasya) tasya tādṛśapratyakṣasadbhāve ` uttamaḥ puruṣastvanya' iti tadvacanaṃ pramāṇam |
īśvarasya jīvādhyakṣīkaraṇakṣamatvānna (jīvādhyakṣīkaraṇe kṣamatvānna) codyāvakāśaḥ |
īśvarapratiyogiko jīvādhikaraṇakastu (jīvadharmiko bhedo) jīvasya pratyakṣaḥ |
sapratiyogikapadārthapratyakṣe na pratiyogipratyakṣaṃ prayojakam (tantram) |
iyaṃ gaurgavayasadṛśī, iha sa paṭo nāsti (iha ghaṭo nāsti), stambhaḥ piśāco netyādau vyabhicārāt |
etenābhāvapratyakṣayogyatāyāḥ (anyonyābhāvapratyakṣayogyatāyāḥ) pratiyogipratyakṣayogyatādhīnatvādayogyeśvarapratiyogikānyonyābhāvasya kathaṃ pratyakṣayogyateti nirastam |
stambhaḥ piśāco na bhavatītyādau vyabhicārāt (stambhaḥ piśāco na bhavati ityanubhavāt)|
anyonyābhāvaviṣayatvācca tasyāḥ (anyonyābhāvaviṣayatvāttasya) |
evaṃ yajñadattapratiyogiko viṣṇumitradharmiko bhedo viṣṇumitrasya, viṣṇumitrapratiyogiko devadattadharmiko bhedo devadattasya pratyakṣa ityādyūhyam |
evaṃ prāptaṃ pūrvapakṣaṃ pratiṣeddhumupakramate || atrāyamiti || bhedapramāṇaviṣaye dūṣaṇoddhārarūpaparihāro'bhedapramāṇaviṣaye dūṣaṇarūpaparihāraścāyaṃ vakṣyamāṇo draṣṭavya ityarthaḥ |
tatra brūma iti iśvaradharmikasyeśvarapratiyogikasya vā bhedasyeśvarāpratyakṣatvenāpratyakṣatvāditi yaddūṣaṇamuktaṃ tatrottaraṃ brūma ityarthaḥ || īśvarasya pratyakṣa iti || īśvarapratyakṣasyāsmādyapratyakṣatve'pi svaṃ prati pratyakṣatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvīśvarasya tādṛśapratyakṣasadbhāva eva kutaḥ |
parabuddherapratyakṣatvādityata āha -- tasyeti || `yasmātkṣaramatīto'ha'mityuttaravākyamapyatrodāhartavyam |
tena jīvādahamanya itīśvarapratyakṣasiddhiḥ |
nanvīśvarasya dharmibhūtasvapratyakṣasadbhāve'pi pratiyogibhūtajīvapratyakṣābhāvātkathaṃ tatpratiyogikasvadharmikabhedapratyakṣamityata āha -- īśvarasyeti || anyathā tasya sārvajñāsiddheriti bhāvaḥ |
yadyapi vakṣyamāṇarītyā bhedapratyakṣe dharmimātrapratyakṣaṃ tantraṃ na pratiyogipratyakṣamapi |
tathā'pi tuṣyatviti nyāyenābhyupetyoktiriyam || jīvadharmika iti || bheda iti vartate || jīvasyeti || pratyakṣa iti vartate |
nanu jīvasyeśvarapratyakṣābhāvatkathaṃ tatpratiyāgikasvadharmikabhedaḥ pratyakṣa ityata āha -- sapratiyogiketi || ayaṃ bhāvaḥ |
loke sapratiyogikāḥ padārthāḥ sādṛśyasaṃyogādayo bahavassanti |
tatra saṃyogapratyakṣe pratiyogipratyakṣasyāvaśyakatvena sādṛśyādipratyakṣe tadāvaśyakatā nāsti |
tathā hi |
iyaṃ gaurgavayasadṛśīti tāvatsādṛśyapratyakṣaṃ jāyate |
na tatra pratiyogipratyakṣamasti |
vane prāgdṛṣṭasya gavayasya tadā vyavahitatvena smṛtimātreṇopayogitayā pratyakṣābhāvāt |
evamiha na ghaṭo'stīti ghaṭābhāvapratyakṣaṃ jāyate |
na ca tatra pratiyogibhūtaghaṭapratyakṣamasti |
tathā stambhaḥ piśāco neti stambhe piśācapratiyogikabhedaḥ pratīyate |
na ca tatra piśācapratyakṣamasti |
kintu tadviṣayakaparokṣajñānenaiva |
ata evaṃ vyabhicāradarśanānneśvarapratiyogikabhedapratyakṣe īśvarapratyakṣaṃ tantraṃ kintu tajjñānamātram |
tattu `yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sa' ityādyāgamena sidhyatīti |
kvacittviha na sa paṭa iti pāṭhaḥ |
ata eva paṭasyāpratyakṣatvasphoraṇāya sa iti prayoga ityāhuḥ |
evaṃ gavayasadṛśītyatrāpi tadgavayasadṛśīti bodhyamityapyāhuḥ |
atra yadyapi stambhaḥ piśāco netyekatraiva vyabhicārodbhāvanamupayuktam |
bhedapratyakṣamātre pratiyogipratyakṣasya tantratvena śaṅkitatvāt |
nānyatra sādṛśyādiviṣaye pratiyogipratyakṣasya tantratvenānāśaṅkitatvāt |
tathā'pi śiṣyavyutpādanārthaṃ vā dṛṣṭāntatvena vā tadudbhāvanamiti jñātavyam |
nanvabhāvapratyakṣe pratiyogipratyakṣameva tantramiti na brūmaḥ |
yeneha na ghaṭa ityādau vyabhicāraḥ syāt |
kintu pratiyoginaḥ pratyakṣayogyatāmātram |
na ca neha ghaṭa ityādau ghaṭasya tadānīṃ pratyakṣatvābhāvavatpratyakṣayogyatā'pi nāsti |
prakṛte ceśvarapratiyogikānyonyābhāvarūpabhedasya pratyakṣatvāṅgīkāre pratiyogibhūteśvarasya pratyakṣayogyatvamaṅgīkāryam |
na ca tadasti |
ataḥ kathaṃ pratyakṣāyogyeśvarapratiyogikabhedasya pratyakṣatvaṃ sambhavedityāśaṅkāmatideśena nirasyati -- eteneti || pratiyogipratyakṣayogyatādhīnatvāditi || pratiyogipratyakṣayogyatādhīnatvādityarthaḥ || ayogyeśvareti || pratyakṣāyogyeśvaretyarthaḥ || kathaṃ yogyateti || pratyakṣayogyatetyarthaḥ |
etenetyuktameva viśadayati -- stambhaḥ piśāco neti || na hi piśācasya ghaṭādivatpratyakṣayogyatā'stīti bhāvaḥ |
nanu nāyamanubhavo'bhāvaviṣayakaḥ kintu piśācapratiyogikabhedaviṣaya eva |
ataḥ kathamatra tadudāharaṇāmiti mandāśaṅkāṃ nirāha -- anyonyābhāveti || anyonyābhāva eva hi bhedo nāma |
ataḥ kathamayamanubhavo'bhāvaviṣayo na bhavediti bhāvaḥ |
evamubhayavidhajīveśvarabhedasya pratyakṣasiddhatvamupapādyedānīṃ parasparajīvabhedo'pi pratyakṣasiddho mantavya ityāha -- evamiti || ceṣṭāliṅgena paradehasya sātmatve sādhite tadviruddhaceṣṭāvato viṣṇumitrasya nāhaṃ devadatta ityanubhavasadbhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
siddhāntayati -- atreti || tadvacanaṃ pramāṇamitīśvarāvatāreṇa kṛṣṇenottamaḥ puruṣastvanya ityuttamatvamuktvā yasmātkṣaramatīto'hamityuktatvātsvabhedaḥ svasya pratyakṣa iti bhāvaḥ |
na ca vācyaṃ tasyāpi bhedaḥ śruto'numito vā ki syāditi || dharmipratyakṣatādipratyakṣasāmagr_yā balavatyā'numityādipratibandhāt |
vastutastvīśvarasya parokṣajñānameva nāstīti tattvavidaḥ || na codyeti || pratiyogipratyakṣābhāvātkathaṃ pratyakṣamiti codyetyarthaḥ |
yadyapi pratiyogipratyakṣaṃ na tantramityanupadameva vakṣyati |
tathā'pyaṅgīkāravādo'yamityadoṣaḥ |
kiñca bhedo yogināmapi pratyakṣo `juṣṭaṃ yadā paśyatyanyamīśamasya mahimāna'miti vacanāt |
yadyapīśvarādipratyakṣaṃ bhedamātre pramāṇīkartuṃ śakyate |
tathā'pyuktabhedasyāsmadādyapratyakṣatvajñāpanāyaivamuktiḥ || jīvasyeti || tattajjīvasyetyarthaḥ |
nāhamīśvaro na nirduḥkha ityādyanubhavādityarthaḥ |
īśvaravajjīvasya pratiyogipratyakṣābhāvātkathamityata āha -- sapratiyogiketi || gavayeti || tasya tatkālāpratyakṣatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
gavayapratiyogikasādṛśyasya bhāvatvāttadupapattāvapyabhāvasya nopapadyata ityata āha -- iheti || nanvanyonyābhāvasyaivāyaṃ niyamaḥ |
kiñca gavayādestatkālāpratyakṣatve'pi tadyogyataiva tantram |
īśvare tu sā'pi nāstītyata āha -- stambha iti || nanvevaṃ vāyvākāśasaṃyogasyāpratyakṣatve'pi vāyughaṭasaṃyogasya pratyakṣatvaṃ syāt |
vāyunirūpitasya ghaṭadharmikasya tasya pratyakṣayogyadharmikatvāt |
na hyasmākaṃ mate dvayorapi dharmitvamaṅgīkṛtam |
`bhinnāśca bhinnadharmāśca padārthā nikhilā apī'ti vacanāditi cet |
satyam |
saṃyogo hyanyapratiyogiko'nyadharmiko'pi padārthadvayasattādhīnasattākaḥ |
anyataranāśe saṃyoganāśāt |
sādṛśyādikaṃ naivam |
naṣṭenāpi gavayena sadṛśī gauriti pratīteḥ |
kintu dharmisattāmātrasāpekṣasattākam |
evañca saṃyogasya dharmipratiyogidvayasattāsāpekṣasattākatvāttadvayapratyakṣasyāpi tadvayapratyakṣādhīnatvam |
sādṛśyādipratyakṣaṃ dharmimātrapratyakṣādhīnamiti na tatpratibandī || eteneti || piśācānyonyābhāve vyabhicāreṇetyarthaḥ || abhāvapratyakṣayogyatāyā iti || bhedasyānyonyābhāvarūpatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
aneneyaṃśaṅkā nirastā |
tathāhi |
na tāvatpṛthaktvaṃ vibhāgo'nekatvaṃ vailakṣaṇyaṃ vā bhedaḥ |
teṣāṃ bhāvatvācca bhedasya ghaṭaḥ paṭo netyādiniṣedhadhīvedyatvāt |
nāpyanyonyābhāvastādātmyapratiyogikābhāvatvaṃ vā tādātmyāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvatvaṃ vā saṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvatvaṃ vā |
nādyaḥ |
tādṛśatādātmyātyantābhāve'tivyāptiḥ |
ghaṭaḥ paṭo netyatra paṭasyaiva niṣedhaviśeṣatvena pratiyogitvadhīvirodhācca |
samānanyāyena saṃsargābhāve'pi saṃsargasyaiva pratiyogitvāpatteḥ |
na dvitīyaḥ |
tadātmanā nāstīti bodhyamānātyantābhāve'tivyāpteḥ |
paṭo na bhavatītyatra pratiyogiśarīre paṭapaṭatvātiriktatādātmyānullekhācca |
pratiyogivṛttyullekhyamānasyaiva pratiyogitāvacchedakatvāt |
ghaṭatādātmyasya paṭāvṛttitvena tasyāvacchedakatvānupapatteśca |
nyāyasāmyena saṃsargābhāve'pi saṃsargasyaivāvacchedakatvaprāptyā ghaṭatvāderanavacchedakatāpātācca |
na tṛtīyaḥ |
ghaṭaḥ saṃsṛṣṭaḥ paṭo na bhavatīti bodhyamānānyonyābhāve'vyāpteḥ |
uktanyāyena saṃsargābhāve'pi saṃsargasyānavacchedakatvenātivyāpteḥ |
nāpi tatra pramāṇam |
ghaṭaḥ paro netyādipratīterlāghavātsaviśeṣaṇe hīti nyāyānugrahācca |
ghaṭe paṭatvasya yo'tyantābhāvastenaiva cāritārthyamiti tadbhedaśūnyasyāpi tanniṣṭhadharmātyantābhāvādhikaraṇatvaṃ dharmāṇāṃ svātyantābhāvasāmānādhikaraṇyavādināmaniṣṭam |
api ca paṭānyonyābhāvasya paṭaḥ paṭatvaṃ tādātmyaṃ vā pratiyogi |
nādyaḥ |
tasya paṭasāmānādhikaraṇyāvirodhāt |
virodhitvasyaiva pratiyogiśabdārthatvāt |
yatkiñcitsambandhamātreṇa pratiyogitve'dhikaraṇāderapi tathātvaprāpteḥ |
na dvitīyatṛtīyau |
paṭasyaiva niṣedhyatvapratīterityuktadoṣānistārāditi || atra cakāsti |
anyonyābhāvo hi bhedaḥ |
tattvaṃ tādātmyāropyasambandhakābhāvatvam |
saṃsargāropyakaśca saṃsargābhāvaḥ |
tādātmyena nāstītyatra tādātmyasyāvchedakatve'pi nāropyasambandhatā saṃsargasyaiva |
evaṃ saṃsṛṣṭo na bhavatītyatrāpi vaiparītyamūhyam |
tādātmyaṃ ca na pratiyogi nāpi tatrāvacchedakaṃ saṃsargānyonyābhāvayoścāropyasambandhabhedādbhedaḥ |
anyonyābhāvasya pratiyogitāvacchedakenaiva virodho na pratiyoginā |
pratiyogitvaṃ cānyonyābhāvāsamānādhikaraṇadharmādhikaraṇatvamiti nātiprasaṅgaḥ |
yadvā''ropyabhūtatādātmyasambandhena pratiyogī na vartate tadanyonyābhāva eva bheda ityastyeva pratiyoginā virodhaḥ |
tādātmyaṃ vā paṭāderdharmaḥ saṃsargatulyayogakṣemaḥ |
tadullekhaśca ghaṭaḥ paṭo na bhavatītyatra padadvayasāmānādhikaraṇyena vyutyattivaśātsaṃsargābhāve'pi saṃsargasya saṃsargatvenābhāne'pi saṃsargamaryādayā bhānamātreṇaivātrāpi tādātmyenābhāne'pyāropyasambandhatvopapatteḥ |
nāpi tatra pramāṇābhāvaḥ |
ghaṭe paṭatvaṃ nāstīti bodhyamānasya paṭatvatvāvacchinnapaṭatvasaṃsargāropyakatvena ghaṭaḥ paṭo na bhavatīti bodhyamānasya paṭatvāvacchinnapaṭatādātmyāropyakatvena pratiyogyavacchedakāropyakāṇāṃ trayāṇāṃ bhedo nāsya tenaiva cāritārthyāt |
aṅgīkṛtaścaikaikabhedena ghaṭābhāvātpaṭābhāvasya dravatvāvacchinnaghaṭābhāvāddhaṭatvāvacchinnābhāvasya ca bhedo na syādityalam |
yadatra valgitamādhunikena bhedapratyakṣe bādhakam |
`na pramāṇaṃ mano'smākamayogyānupalabdhigam |
aupādhikastho bhedo'yaṃ nādhyakṣamatigocaram |
pramāṇasahakāritvādviṣayasyāpyabhāvataḥ |
apramāṇaṃ mano'smākaṃ pramāderāśrayatvata' ityādinā |
tasyāyaṃ niṣkarṣaḥ |
mano naivamatatve'pi neśvarabhedagrāhi |
tathātve'pi bhedo nāsau śuddhacaitanyagata iti |
tattuccham |
manaso'prāmāṇye ātmādyasiddhiprasaṅgāt |
tasya sākṣivedyatve tadbhedo'pi tathaiveti kiṃ manaḥprāmāṇyanirāsavistareṇa |
kutaśca tadaprāmāṇyaṃ pramāṇasahakāritvātkālavaditi cenna |
upanītapratyakṣasahakāryupanāyake vākyasahakāryupakramādiliṅge ca vyabhicārāt |
yāvatpramāṇasahakāritvāditi vācyam |
sākṣiṇaṃ svaṃ pratyanyathātvenāsiddheḥ |
na ca yāvadbāhyapramāṇasahakāritvāditi vācyam |
tvadīyasākṣiṇi vyabhicārāt |
kiñcāprasiddho'yaṃ hetuḥ |
anindriyatvaṃ copādhiḥ |
yaccoktaṃ manasaḥ kvacijjñānakāraṇatve lāghavādrūpādijñāne tasya kāraṇatvāpattyā cakṣurāderālokavat sahakārimātratāpattyā tatprāmāṇyaṃ na syāditi |
tanna |
pratyakṣatvavyāpyayatkiñciddharmāvacchinnakāryatānirūpitakāraṇatāvacchedakajātimattvarūpātiśayasya cakṣurādāveva sattvena tasyaiva kāraṇatvaucityāt |
ālokāderapi cākṣuṣajñānaṃ pratyakāraṇatvena tadayogāt |
uktātiśayābhāve'pi kāraṇatvavyavahāramātrāpādane śabdakalaha evetyupekṣaṇīyam |
yaccābhāṇi |
`saṃyogāderayuktatvāttadanyasyāprasiddhitaḥ |
sannikarṣasya tenedaṃ bhedasaṃjñeva māna'mityādinā manobhedayorna sānnikarṣa iti |
naitajjyāyaḥ |
sākṣiṇaiva bhedagrahaṇenobhayorātmasvarūpatvena tādātmyasyaiva sannikarṣatvāt |
tādātmyaṃ ca na pratyāsattimapekṣata iti tadukteśca |
mā'stu tādātmyasannikarṣaḥ |
ātmani sākṣiṇā sukhābhāvagrahaṇe yo viśeṣaṇatādiḥ sannikarṣastadeva tādātmyābhāvarūpabhedagrahe'pyastu |
astu vā manasaiva bhedagrahastatra saṃyuktaviśeṣaṇatādiḥ sannikarṣaḥ |
yaduktaṃ viśeṣaṇatādeḥ pratyāsattitve mānābhāvāditi |
tanna ghaṭādigrahe saṃyogāderivānvayavyatirekābhyāṃ tathātvasiddheḥ |
yaccātra pralapitam |
yogyānupalabdhirūpasahakāryabhāvānna pratyakṣasyeśvarānyonyābhāvagrāhakatvamiti |
tadasāram |
yogyānupalabdherhetutve'tīndriyārthollekhavikalpānāṃ sāmānyābhāvapramābhāvāpatteḥ |
paramāṇvādyanupalabdheryogyānupalabdhitvābhāvāt |
tasmādanupalabdhimātraṃ hetuḥ |
pratiyogyanupalambhe'pyadhikaraṇasya pratiyogyādhāratvayogyatājñānasya pratibandhakatvānnātiprasaṅgaḥ |
saṃsargābhāvagrahe tathātve'pyanyonyābhāvagrahe'pyanupalabdhimātrasyaiva hetutvānna doṣaḥ |
anyathā stambhaḥ piśāco netyanumitirneti cenna |
pratyakṣe dharmiṇi saṃsargarūpāropyasambandhena vastusattve'pyayogyatayā kvacidanupalabdhivṛttitādātmyāropasambandhakasthale dharmiṇastadātmanā kvacidanupalambhābhāvenātra yogyatāviśeṣaṇasya vaiyyarthyāt |
ghaṭādipratyakṣe tadātmanaḥ svasyaiva punarapratyakṣatādarśanāt |
na ca vācyaṃ parvatasya pratyakṣatve'pi tadātmano vahvimato yogyatayā pratyakṣānupalabdhirdṛśyata iti || parvatavahvimatorjīveśvaravadatyantabhedānaṅgīkārāt |
na ca rāmagrahe kṛṣṇagraho dṛśyata iti vācyam |
nendriyāṇi nānumānam |
śuklamatpakṣasyaiveśvaragrahe paṭutvāvadhāraṇāt |
rāmakṛṣṇayostādātmyagrahapratibandhakaviśeṣasya sattvāt |
jāmbuvatkartṛkapratyakṣaṃ tūttejakasamavadhānānna tatpratibandhaḥ |
na caivaṃ nirviśeṣitacaitanyavādinaḥ parasyāto lāghavādanupalabdhikatvamevāvacchedakam |
saṃsargābhāve tvatiprasaṅgādyogyatāpraveśa iti || astu vā tatpraveśaḥ |
tatra yogyasyānupalabdhirityarthaḥ |
paramāṇau pṛthivītvābhāvasya pratyakṣatāpatteḥ |
na cādhikaraṇayogyatā'pi heturiti vācyam |
gauravāt |
kiṃ svapratiyogisannikarṣetarayāvadupalambhayogyatāsāmagrīsamānakālīnatvamanupalabdhiyogyatvam |
upalambhanayogyatvaṃ cātarkitapratiyogisattvaprasañjitopalambhatātvam |
atra yadi jīva eveśvaro jīvagrāhi mana iśvaratayaiva gṛhṇīyāditi tarkitopalambhatākamanaḥ samānakālīnaivopalabdhiriti |
yadvā pratiyogisattvavirodhitvamanupalabdhiyogyatvam |
virodhitvaṃ yadi pratiyogī syāt tarhi svayaṃ na syādityāpādanīyābhāvapratiyogitvam |
yaccoktamasataḥ pratiyogisattvasya kathamāpādakatvamiti |
tanmandam |
tarkamātre'pi tathātvāt |
yaccoktamasata āpādakatvamarthamahimnota tadvyāptyādijñānamahimnā |
nādyaḥ |
asataḥ śaktimātravikalatvāt |
dvitīye pratiyogī na tatrānupalabdhiriti vyatirekajñānapūrvakatvanaiyyatyenānupalabdhiranumānatayaivābhāvajñāpaketi na tatra bhedapratyakṣamiti || tattu vibhīṣakāmātram |
yogyatālakṣaṇaghaṭanāya vyāptyādisvīkāre'pyanupalabdhīyābhāvabodhane tadanapekṣaṇāt |
anupalabdhau jñānakaraṇatve vivādāt |
aṅgīkṛtyedamuditam |
vastutastu pratyakṣeṇābhāvagrahe nānupalabdheḥ kṛtyamupalabhyate |
na ca pratiyogitadupalambhe cābhāvadhīḥ syāditi vācyam |
viṣayasyaivābhāvāt |
na ca tvanmate viṣayasyākāraṇatvātkinteneti vācyam |
viṣayābhāvenendriyasya sannikarṣarūpasāmagrīvighnāt |
na ca vācyaṃ pratiyogirahitasthale pratiyogipramādaśāyāmabhāvādhīssyāditi yogyupalambhasya pratibandhakatvāditi |
na caivaṃ pratibandhakābhāvatvenānupalabdhahetutvaṃ prāptamiti vācyam |
pratibandhakābhāvasya manmate hetutvāsammateḥ |
pūrvavṛttitāniyamasyaiva kāraṇatvaśabdārthatvāt |
kathaṃ tasya kāraṇatvamiti cenna |
kāryajanakaśaktivaiṣamyasyaiva tadarthatvāt |
anyathā tanturūpāderapi tattvaprasaṅgāt |
na ca tadanyathāsiddhamiti vācyam |
asyāpi tathātvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyatvāt |
nanu kāraṇatayā sammatena yena sahaiva yasya yaṃ prati pūrvavṛttigrahastattenānyathāsiddham |
anyaṃ prati pūrvavṛttitve jñāta eva yasya yaṃ prati pūrvavṛttitvamavagamyate tattenānyathāsiddham |
anyatra klṛptaniyatapūrvavṛttina eva kāryasambhave tatsahabhūtamanyathāsiddhamityanyathāsiddhatrayamadhye'sya kā'nyathāsiddhiriti cedeṣaiveti brūmaḥ |
maṇyabhāvasamavahito vahnirdāhapūrvavṛttī maṇyabhāvādervahnyādinā sahaiva pūrvavṛttitāgrahaṇāt |
maṇyabhāvo dāhakāraṇamiti pṛthaggrahaṇānanubhavāt |
kiñca prakṛte bhāvagrahe klṛptendriyasannikarṣādinaivābhāvasyāpi grahopapattau tatsahabhūtānupalabdhestṛtīyānyathāsiddhatvaṃ yujyate |
anyathā tatrāpyabhāvānupalabdheḥ kāraṇatvaṃ syāt |
api ca prakriyāmātrasya nirmūlatvenoktānyathāsiddhyayoge kāryābhāvaprayojakībhūtaṃ yadvastu pratiyogikatvābhāvatvena yasya yaṃ prati pūrvavṛttitvagrahastattenānyathāsiddhamiti caturthānyathāsiddhatvaṃ kalpyatām |
kiṃ pratibandhakābhāvasya kāraṇagataśaktyāvaraṇāpasaraṇatāmātraṃ ca tena kāryabhāvaprayojakībhūtāvaraṇāpasaraṇe karmābhāvavasthāne svata eva kāryotpattisambhave pratibandhakābhāvasyāpi kāraṇatve gauravam |
kāraṇagataśaktyuttejakatvenānyathāsiddhaśca |
tasmādyogyānupalabdherahetutvena prakṛte tadabhāve'pi pratiyogyupalambharūpapratibandhakābhāvādanyonyābhāvapratyakṣopapattirityuktam |
yaccoktaṃ puruṣagrahe'pi sahakāryabhāvādbrahmaṇatvasyaivaikyamātropapattiriti |
tadasaṅgatam |
aikyagrahānupapattyā bhedanasya sādhanāt |
kintu nāhamīśvara iti pratyakṣeṇetyuktaṃ bhavati |
pratītajīvatanmātraikyavādinastaddṛṣṭānto'pyayuktaḥ |
etenaikyāntasyopaniṣadatvādajñātatvādvā'nupalabdhiriti nirastam |
yaccoktamahamityaupādhikapratīteḥ nāyaṃ bhedo'paricchinnaśuddhātmagata iti |
tanna |
jaḍeśavṛttitvasyaiva jīvasvabhāvatvāt |
nanvatroktānubhavasyaiva mānatvāt |
na cāparicchinnabrahmātmanaḥ kathamayaṃ svabhāva iti vācyam |
tadbhāvasyāpyadyāpyaprasiddheḥ |
yacca pralapitaṃ nāhamīśvara iti ahaṅkāradhīriti tadidamatimāndam |
ahaṃ brahmāsmītyādivilayāpatteḥ |
nāhamīśvara iti vannāmīśvareṇa sthīyata ityādāvahamullekhābhāvācca |
ahamiti prayokta ityādiprayogasiddho'haṃśabdasyāsmacchabdābhinnārthakatvaprasiddheścetyalaṃ durbalahiṃsayā || evamiti || devadattasya yogena parajīvādhyakṣīkaraṇadaśāyāmiti śeṣaḥ |
evaṃ devadatto'haṃ na yajñadatto nāhaṃ jaḍo ghaṭo neśvara ityādyūhyam |
tasmādbhede pramāṇābhāvādīśvaradharmikabhedasyāsmatpratyakṣāviṣayatve'pi tatpratyakṣaviṣayatvaṃ bhaviṣyatītyata āha -- īśvaradharmika iti || īśvarasyeti || idaṃ prauḍhivādena |
vastutastu janyalaukikapratyakṣe pratiyogiyogyatādestantratveneśvarapratyakṣasyānevaṃvidhatvenātra na tadapekṣā |
apekṣāyāmapi vakṣyamāṇarītyā stambhe piśācānyonyābhāvapratyakṣadarśanena tarkitapratiyogisattvasañjanaprasañjitapratiyogikānupalabdherevāpekṣitatvena tasyāśca jīvādyakṣābhāvenāpi sambhavena codyānavakāśāt |
jīvasya pratyakṣa ityanuṣajyate |
nanvīśvarasyāyogyatvena tatpratiyogikabhedaḥ kathaṃ pratyakṣa ityāśaṅkya pratiyogipratyakṣatvasya kiṃ sati pratiyogikapadārthapratyakṣatve kāraṇatā kiṃ vā'bhāvapratyakṣe utānyonyābhāvapratyakṣe iti vikalpaṃ manasi nidhāyātra krameṇa vyabhicārānāha -- sapratiyogiketi || idamupalakṣaṇam |
vastutastu vikalpadvayamapi bodhyam |
sa paṭa iti paṭasya pratyakṣatvasphoraṇārtham || sa iti || ` idamaḥ pratyakṣagataṃ samīpataravṛtti caitado rūpam |
adasastu viprakṛṣṭaṃ taditi parokṣe vijānīyādi'tyukteḥ |
evaṃ gavayasadṛśītyatrāpi tadgavayasadṛśīti bodhyam |
nanu yasya puṃso'bhāvapratyakṣaṃ tatpuruṣīyapratyakṣayogyatvaṃ pratiyoginastadabhāvapratyakṣatāyāṃ tantram |
parokṣaghaṭābhāvapratyakṣe tu tadasti |
nāsti ca jīvadharmikeśvarabhedasthala iti kathaṃ pratyakṣamityāśaṅkāmantimapakṣoktadoṣeṇa pariharati -- eteneti || pratiyogipratyakṣayogyatā pratiyoginaḥ pratyakṣayogyatā |
ata evottaratrāyogyeśvareti || etenetyuktaṃ darśayati -- stambha iti |
bhedasya vyāsajyavṛttitvādipakṣamavalambya pūrvapakṣāvasare dūṣaṇakathane'pi siddhāntakathanāvasare vastusthitimālambyaiva samādhānamāha -- īśvaradharmika ityādinā |
kasyacitpratyakṣasattvamātreṇa bhede pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇamityukteryuktatvānna sarveṣāṃ tadasattvaṃ bādhakamiti bhāvaḥ |
asmākaṃ tanniścāyakapramāṇapraśne tatpratyakṣopanyāso'kiñcitkara iti cenna |
āptavākyasiddhatatpratyakṣeṇa deśāntarasthavastuniścayavadatrāpyupapatteḥ |
tatpratyakṣaṃ nāsmākaṃ pratyakṣavidhayā ghaṭapratyakṣavatsaṃśayocchedahetuḥ kintu liṅgavidhayeti cet |
atrāpi tathā'stu kā kṣatiḥ sabhāyāṃ vastuviśeṣe laukikapratyakṣasya madhyasthaṃ prati pramāṇatvenopanyāsavadatrāpi tathoktau bādhābhāvāt |
īśvarasya pratyakṣasadbhāve kiṃ mānamityata āha -- īśvarasyeti || tadvacanamiti || āptavākyena tatpratyakṣasiddhivadatrāpi tatheti bhāvaḥ |
nanu janyabhedalaukikapratyakṣe pratiyogipratyakṣasya kāraṇatvaṃ bhedasya vyāsajyavṛttitvenaikasyaivobhayavṛttitvapakṣe yāvadāśrayapratyakṣaṃ vinā tatpratyakṣaṃ na (saṃ)bhavatītyeva vaktavyam |
anyathā gaganaghaṭadvitvasaṃyogādipratyakṣāpatteḥ |
pratiyogyavṛttitvena dharmimātravṛttitvapakṣe'pyanubhavasāmyāya pratiyogyanuyogipratyakṣayo rūpāntareṇa kāraṇatayā'vaśyaṃ pūrvaṃ sattvamāvaśyakam |
ata eva stambhe piśācabhedo'pi na pratyakṣaḥ |
matāntare piśācarūpāśrayapratyakṣābhāvena tatpratyakṣābhāvasyāvaśyakatvenaitanmate'pi tādṛśānubhavasāmyāya tathāṅgīkāraucityāt |
tathaiva bhedalaukikapratyakṣaṃ prati taddharmitatpratiyogyubhayaniṣṭhalaukikaviṣayatāśālipratyakṣatvaṃ vyāpakam |
avyabhicaritasahacāradarśanena vahnerdhūmavyāpakatāyā iva tasya niścayāt |
evañceśvarajñānasya māyāvṛttyātmakatvasya janyatvāttaṃ prati jīveśvarobhayapratyakṣasya pūrvaṃ sattvamāvaśyakam |
tasya nityatve'pyuktavyāpyavyāpakabhāvanirvāhāya tatpratyakṣe'pyubhayorlaukikaviṣayatvamāvaśyakam |
taccātra na ghaṭate |
aṇutvena laukikacākṣuṣāyogyasya jīvasya paramātmacākṣuṣe'pi bhānāsambhavāt |
paramātmanaḥ paramānasaviṣayatvādarśaneneśvaramānasagocaratvasyāpyasambhavādityata āha
|| īśvarasya jīvādhyakṣīkaraṇakṣamatvāditi || kṣamatvātsamarthatvādityarthaḥ |
anyathā'ghaṭitaghaṭanāśaktimattvarūpeśvaratvasyaiva hāniprasaṅgāt |
ata eveśvarasyeti hetugarbhaviśeṣaṇam |
hanta tarhi cetanamapi ghaṭaṃ kuryājjīvamapi svātmānaṃ kuryāt |
maivam |
pramāṇasiddhasya lokarītyā ghaṭanāvaidhuryeṇa pratīyamānasya ghaṭakatayā `vicitraśakti'rityādipramāṇaistacchaktau siddhatvāt |
paramātmanaśca `sarvapadārthaviṣayakasākṣāddraṣdṛtvaṃ ' `sākṣī cetā kevalo nirguṇaśca' yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sa sarvavidityādiśrutismṛtisahasrasiddhamiti bhāvaḥ || na codyāvakāśa iti || idānīmīśvarapratyakṣe jīvāviṣayakatvamavalambya codyasyotthāne khalu tannirāsāya pratiyogipratyakṣakāraṇatvakhaṇḍanasyānyatra
tādṛśakāraṇatvamaṅgīkṛtyeśvarapratyakṣanityatvavicārapūrvakaṃ tatra pratiyogipratyakṣānupayogasya vā kathanāvasaraḥ |
ādau tacchaṅkaiva nāvatarati |
tasya māyikasarvajñatve vā svābhāvikasarvajñatve vā tatpratyakṣāviṣayavastunaḥ kenāpi vādinā'naṅgīkṛtatvāt |
ata evokteśvaravacanāt (tasya) svavyavahāraprayojakajñānasiddhāvapi tasya pratyakṣarūpatvamasiddhamiti nirastam |
prathamatastasyaiva prāptatvena tatparityāge bādhakābhāvāt |
tathā ca tannirāsārthaṃ pratiyogipratyakṣakāraṇatvādikhaṇḍanasyānākāṅkṣitatvāttannirākaraṇaṃ na kriyate kintu jīvasya bhedapratyakṣavyavasthāpanāvasara evānupadaṃ kriyate |
tata idānīmevākāṅkṣitamakṛtyottaratra 
tatkaraṇe sandarbhāśuddhiriti manaḥ khedo na kārya iti bhāvaḥ |
kecittu janyapratyakṣe pratiyogijñānasya kāraṇatveneśvarapratyakṣe tasya kāraṇatvāprasaktyā vakṣyamāṇanyāyena tasya khaṇḍitatvena ca codyaprasaktiviraheṇa jīvaviṣayakapratyakṣarūpakāraṇakathanena tadanavakāśakathanaṃ prauḍhivādamātramityāhuḥ 
asmaduktarītyaitatpaṅktyuthānasambhavenottaragranthe tatkāraṇatākhaṇḍane'pīdānīmīśvarapratyakṣasya janyatvabhrameṇa prāptaśaṅkāmaṅgīkṛtya samādhisaukaryeṇa parihārakaraṇasya ca sambhavena cāvimarśapūrvakoktiparyavāsāyi prauḍhivādatvaṃ kathamasya granthasyeti ta eva praṣṭavyāḥ |
|| īśvarapratiyogika iti || jīvasyeti || jīvasyāpītyarthaḥ |
nanu pūrvapakṣoktarītyā pratiyogipratyakṣakāraṇatāyā vyavasthāpitatvājjīvasya tadabhāvātkathaṃ tatpratyakṣasambhava ityāśaṅkya kiṃ sapratiyogika iti pratyakṣamātre pratiyogipratyakṣakāraṇatvamutābhāvapratyakṣa iti vikalpau hṛdi nidhāyādye vyabhicāramāha -- iyaṃ gauriti || sannikṛṣṭagavi smṛtāraṇyakagavayasādṛśyapratyakṣe vyabhicārānnedaṃ yuktamiti bhāvaḥ |
dvitīye dārḍhyāyātyantābhāvasthale'nyonyābhāvasthale'pyubhayatra vyabhicāramāha -- iha sa paṭa iti || paṭasya tatkāle parokṣatvadyotanāya sa iti nirdeśaḥ |
tena (pratiyogi)pratyakṣarūpakāraṇābhāvasya tatkāla upapādanam |
sapratiyogikapratyakṣe vā'tyantābhāvapratyakṣe'thavā'nyonyābhāvapratyakṣa iti vikalpān hṛdi nidhāya kramānnirākarotīti nāvataraṇaṃ kāryam |
atyantābhāvapratyakṣe pratiyogipratyakṣakāraṇatvākāraṇatvayoḥ pūrvapakṣasiddhāntayoranupayogenātyantābhāvapratyakṣatvena vikalpakoṭau niveśāsaṅgateḥ |
abhāvapratyakṣatvena dvitīya(vikalpa)koṭiṃ parikalpyānyonyābhāvapratyakṣatvena tṛtīyakoṭiṃ niveśya kramānnirāhetyavataraṇamapi yuktam || stambha iti || nanvetatpratyakṣarūpaṃ na bhavatītyuktameveti vācyam |
yata ekasyaiva ghaṭapratiyogikatvena taditarasarvānuyogikatvenāsmadabhyupagatabhedasya vyāsajyavṛttitve dvitvavatpratiyoginyanuyogikavyaktāveko bhedaḥ |
evaṃ pratiyogini tadanyāparānuyogivyaktāveko bheda ityucyate |
uktapratiyogini (tadanya)sarvānuyogiṣveka eva bheda ityucyate |
ādye ghaṭapratiyogikabhedasyaivānuyogikabhedena nānātvasyāprāmāṇikasyaiva kalpanāprasaṅgaḥ |
dvitīye ghaṭabhedasyāpratyakṣatvāpattiḥ |
janmasahasreṇāpi tadāśrayayāvadvyaktīnāṃ yugapatpratyakṣāsambhavāt |
pakṣadvaye'pi ghaṭaḥ svasmādbhinna iti pratītivāraṇāya pūrvoktarītyā vilakṣaṇādhikaraṇatvādikalpanāgauravaṃ cetyādyuktyā tanmatasya heyatvena tanmatāṅgīkṛtānubhavasāmyasyākiñcitkaratvena stambhe piśācabhedapratyakṣasya sarvānubhavasiddhasyāpalāpānarhatvāt |
tathā ca pratiyogipratyakṣasya bhedapratyakṣahetutvābhāvāttajjñānamātrasya hetutvādīśvarapratyakṣaṃ vinā'pyāgamajanyajñānena tatsmaraṇasambhavānnāhaṃ sarvajñaḥ sarveśvara ityādirūpamīśvarabhedapratyakṣaṃ yuktameva(meveti bhāvaḥ) |
āgamasya cinmātraparatvānneśvaraniścayaḥ sambhavatīti na yuktam |
uttaratrāgamasya bhedaparatvavyavasthāpanena tasya nirasanīyatvāt |
āgamatātparyanirṇayātprāgeva sārvajñādivaidikapadaistajjñānamātreṇāpyuktapratiyogismṛtisambhavācca |
mā'stu tarhi pratiyogipratyakṣamabhāvapratyakṣatvāvacchinne'nyonyābhāvapratyakṣatvāvacchinne vā kāraṇam |
tathā'pyabhāvapratyakṣatvāvacchinnaṃ prati laukikapratyakṣaviṣayapratiyogijñānatvena hetutā vācyā |
anyathā bhūtalādau piśācābhāvapratyakṣāpatteḥ |
tathā ceśvarasya pratyakṣāviṣayatvāttajjñānena na tadbhedapratyakṣamityāśaṅkyātyantābhāvapratyakṣasthale tathā kāryakāraṇabhāvo nānyonyābhāvasthale stambhe piśācabhedapratyakṣādau vyabhicārāt |
kintvadhikaraṇapratyakṣatvena pratiyogijñānatvena ca kāraṇatā |
anubhavānusāreṇa sarvapadārthavyavasthāṅgīkārātprakṛte na kā'pyanupapattirityāha -- etenetyādinā |
etena piśācabhedapratyakṣe vyabhicāreṇa |
abhāvapratyakṣayogyatāyā abhāvaniṣṭhapratyakṣayogyatāyāḥ |
yogyatā ca viṣayatā vā yāvaddravyapadārthāntaraṃ vetyanyadetat |
pratiyogipratyakṣādhīnatvāt uktarītyā pratiyogipratyakṣayojyatvāt |
pratyakṣaviṣayapratiyogijñānatvena hetutve pratiyogipratyakṣasya kāraṇatāvacchedakaghaṭakatayā paramparayā bhāvaviṣayatārūpakāryatāvacchedake tatsamaśīlayogyatayā vā prayojakatvamavyāhatam || stambha iti || tasyāḥ pratiyoginiṣṭhapratyakṣayogyatāyāḥ |
caśabdaḥ pūrvoktavyabhicārasamuccāyakaḥ |
anyonyābhāvāviṣayatvāt anyonyābhāvapratyakṣe'prayojakatvābhāvāt 
tatraivānubhavādityantaṃ hetuḥ |
tathā ca pūrvoktavyabhicārādanubhavānusāreṇānyonyābhāvasthale tathā kāryakāraṇabhāvākalpanācca tadavaṣṭambhena śaṅkā nirasteti bhāvaḥ |
anyonyābhāvaviṣayatvācca tasyā iti strīliṅgāntaṃ prāmādikaṃ, bahupustake pulliṅgāntapāṭhadarśanāt |
tatpakṣe cāyamarthaḥ |
etenetyuktaṃ vivaṇoti -- stambha iti || tādṛśānubhavasattve'pi prakṛte kimāyātamityata āha -- tasyeti || tasya cetyanvayaḥ |
castvarthaḥ |
tathā ca vyabhicārānnedaṃ yuktamiti bhāvaḥ |
yogyānupalabdherabhāvapratyakṣe hetutvapakṣe'pyahaṃ yadi sarvajñaḥ syāt tathā'nubhūtaḥ syāmityāpādanayogyopalabdhipratiyogikānupalabdhiratrākṣataiveti na bhedapratyakṣasyānupapattiḥ |
anupalabdhisvarūpaṃ tu dvaitadyumaṇau pratyakṣayogyasattvaniruktyavasare vicāritaṃ tatraivānusandheyam |
evaṃ jīvadharmikeśvarabhedeśvaradharmikajīvabhedayormukhyataḥ prāptayorviṣaye pratyakṣaṃ samarthya prasaṅgato vā paramparayā vā yathākathañcittadupayogitvena (vā) prāpte jīvapratiyogikajīvadharmike bhede tadupapādayati -- evamiti || evaṃ yajñadattapratiyogikadevadattadharmiko bhedo devadattasya pratyakṣa ityādyūhyamiti pāṭhaḥ sārvatrikaḥ susaṅgata eva |
kvacittu viṣṇumitradharmikaḥ yajñadattapratiyogikaḥ bhedaḥ ityādyūhyamiti pāṭhaḥ |
yadyapi devadattaparidṛśyamānayoryajñadattaviṣṇumitraśarīrayoreva bhedo'nubhūyate na taccetanayorbhedaḥ sākṣātkriyate |
cakṣurādyayogyatvena taccākṣuṣatvāyogāt |
tadīyajīvasya devadattasākṣyavedyatvena sākṣiṇo'pi tatrāpravṛtteḥ |
tathā cedamayuktamiti bhāti |
tathā'pi tadindriyajanyābhāvapratyakṣe pratiyogisattvaprasañcanaprasañcitatattadindriyajanyapratyakṣarūpapratiyogikānupalabdhireva heturiti na |
kintu yatra yasya pratiyogino yajjanyajñānayogyatvaṃ tatsattayā''pādanayogyatānniścayarūpapratiyogipratiyogikābhāva eva yogyānupalabdhipadārthaḥ |
sa evābhāvapratyakṣe hetuḥ |
tatsattāyāmapi tajjanyaniścayābhāvastatra tadabhāvapratyakṣahetuḥ |
evañca yajñadattaśarīrasthacetanasya pūrvakālopalakṣitasyaitatkālīnasya ca tadanubhūtasmaraṇādibhiḥ(dinā) tadābhisaṃhitaphalavirodhiceṣṭābhāvādiliṅgaiścābhedo niścīyate |
na ca yajñadattaviṣṇumitrayorabhedo'sti |
abhede tādṛśānumāpakaliṅgāni tajjanyānumitiśca syādityāpādyamānānumityabhāvasahakṛtacakṣuṣaiva tanniṣṭhabhedena saha yathākathañcit saṃyuktaviśeṣaṇatāsannikarṣasahitena dharmyupasthitisahitena dharmyaṃśe upanayarūpaṃ bhedāṃśe laukikaṃ viṣṇumitracetane yajñadattacetanabheda ityākārakaṃ pratyakṣaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ |
adhikaraṇasyāpratyakṣatve'pi gagane udbhūtarūpābhāva iti pratyakṣānubhavabalenātyantābhāvapratyakṣe nādhikaraṇasya yogyatvaṃ tantraṃ kintu yogyānupalabdhimātramiti svīkāravadgagane udbhūtarūpavadbheda iti pratyakṣānubhavabalenātrāpi tathā(tvā)'ṅgīkārasyāvaśyakatvāt |
na ca tatrodbhūtaliṅgakānumitiviṣaya evodbhūtarūpavadbhedo na pratyakṣaviṣaya iti vācyam |
vinigamakābhāvena vaiparītyasyāpi vaktuṃ śakyatvāt |
vyāpyavṛttidharmātyantābhāvatadavacchinnapratiyogitākabhedayoḥ samaniyatatvenaikyapakṣe tasyāvarjanīyatvācca |
yadvā yajñadattapiṇḍe viṣṇumitrapiṇḍe ca ceṣṭāliṅgena sātmakatvasiddhyanantaraṃ tattadātmaviśiṣṭapiṇḍayoḥ parasparaṃ (bhedasya) laukikapratyakṣe viśeṣaṇatāvacchedakayorubhayorbhedo'pyalaukikasannikarṣeṇa bhāsate |
taduttaraṃ svātmatadātmanoriva tayorapi parasparamabhedasaṃśayānudayānubhavāt |
anyathā laukikānāṃ tvacchāstrajanitāprāmāṇyaśaṅkāṃ vinā saṃśayādinā vyavahārocchedaprasaṅgāt |
tathā ca tayorbhede'pi devadattapratyakṣaṃ pravartata eva |
parantu tadaṃśe laukikatvaśūnyam |
taccānupayuktamasmābhirlaukikamevetyapratijñānāt |
na cālaukikapratyakṣasyāpyarthasādhakatve gagane kālatvasiddhirapi syāt kadācittādṛśopanītabhānasyāpi sambhavāditi vācyam |
prabalapramāṇāntarabādhe syādeva |
na ca tathā'sti |
prakṛte tu prabalapramāṇāntarabādho nāstyeva |
pratyutāhametasmādbhinna ityāditadvākyairviruddhadharmavattvādiliṅgaiśca bhedasyaiva siddhyā tatsaṃvādasyaivātra sattvāt |
yadvā devadatto yogī cet cetanadvayabhedaviṣayakaṃ pratyakṣaṃ yuktameva īśvaravat |
yogipratyakṣasyāpi sākṣātkaromīti tadīyavyavahāranirvāhakatvāt |
laukikapratyakṣatulyatvamevetīśvarapratyakṣavattasyāpi prakṛte pramāṇatvenopanyāso yuktaḥ |
svātmadharmikānyātmapratiyogikabhedastu sarveṣāṃ (svīya)laukikapratyakṣaviṣayo bhavatyeveti nopapādanāpekṣā'tretyāśayavānāha -- ityādyūhyamiti ||
siddhantaṃ pratijānīte || atrāyamityādinā || atra pūrvapakṣe'yaṃ vakṣyamāṇaḥ parihāro dūṣaṇoddhāra ityarthaḥ |
pūrvapakṣakrameṇaiva siddhāntābhidhānamiti jñāpanāya prathamataḥ prathamadūṣaṇoddhāraṃ pratijānīte || yattāvaditi || īśvaradharmika iti || yadyapi bhedasāmānyamīśvarapratyakṣaviṣayaḥ |
tatpratyakṣe ca `dvāvimau puruṣau loka' ityādi tadvacanameva pramāṇam |
tathāpi `svadharmikasvetarapratiyogikabhedaḥ svapratyakṣa' iti sāmānyato niyamamabhipretyedaṃ nidarśitam |
prathamaśloka īśvaradharmikabhedasyaiva prādhānyena prastutatvādvā |
yadyapi tatra jaḍapratiyogikabhedo'pi prakṛtaḥ |
tathāpi prathamaṃ jīveśvarabheda eva pratyakṣapramāṇābhāvasya śaṅkitatvājjīvapratiyogika ityevoktam |
evamuttaratreśvarapratiyogikādigrahaṇasya saṅgatiravaseyā |
nanvīśvarasyaitādṛśabhedapratyakṣasadbhāve pramāṇabhāvaḥ |
parabuddherapratyakṣatvāt |
na ceśvaraḥ svadharmikasvetarabhedapratyakṣavān, ātmatvāt asmadādivadityanumānaṃ mānamiti vācyam |
anukūlatarkābhāvenāprayojakatvāt |
nāpyāgamaḥ |
tasya tādṛśārthe prāmāṇyānavadhāraṇādityata āha -- īśvarasyeti || tadvacanamiti || kṛṣṇarūpeśvaravacanamityarthaḥ |
tathācedaṃ vākyaṃ vākyārtha viṣayakeśvarīyapratyakṣapūrvakam |
īśvarīyapramāṇavākyatvāt |
tādṛśavākyāntaravat |
parokṣajñānarahita īśvaro vaitadvākyārthajñānavān |
etadvākyaprayoktṛtvāt |
yo yadvākyaprayoktā sa tadarthajñānavān |
yathā yajñadatta ityādyanumānamīśvarasyā jīvapakṛtibhinnatvarapavākyārthapratyakṣasadbhāve pramāṇam |
etadvākyaprāmāṇyaṃ cobhayavādisiddham |
etādṛśagamānugṛhītatvāduktānumānamapi pramāṇamevetyātaśayaḥ |
yadyapi bhedapratipādakatadvākyena tatpratyakṣaṃ prasādhya tena bhedasādhanāpekṣayā tadvākyenaiva bhedasādhane lāghavam |
tathāpi pareṇa jīveśvarabhede pratyakṣapramāṇāsambhavasyoktatayā tanniṣedhāya paramparāśrayaṇam |
atha vyāvahārikabhedasyaiveśvarīyapratyakṣaviṣayatvānna tataḥ pāramārthikabhedasiddhiriti cet sa kiṃ pāramārthikatvena pratīyate vyāvahārikatvenaiva vā |
nādyaḥ |
īśvarasyāpi bhrāntatvāpātāt |
dvitīye ca tadvyāvahārikatvaṃ bādhyamabādhyaṃ vā |
ādye bādhyārthagrāhitvātsa eva doṣaḥ |
tattvato bhedasya vyāvahārikatvāpattiśca |
ante tvapasiddhāntaḥ |
kiñcoktavacanānāṃ vakṣyamāṇarītyā vyāvahārikabhedaparatvānupapattau tadanumeyatadarthabhūtabhedapratyakṣasya kathaṃ vyāvahārikaviṣayatvamiti dik |
nanu pratiyogyādipratyakṣābhāvena jīveśvarabhedapratyakṣaṃ prāgeva dūṣitamataḥ kathametadityata āha -- īśvarasyeti || anyathā sarvajñatvānupapatteriti bhāvaḥ |
etaccābhyupetyaivoktam |
uktapratiyogipratyakṣāpekṣāyā anupadameva dūṣyatvādīśvarapratyakṣasya kāraṇānapekṣatvācceti sūcanāya na doṣāvakāśa ityanuktvā na codyāvakāśa ityuktam |
jīvadhikaraṇakastu bhedo jīvasya pratyakṣa iti sambandhaḥ |
nāhaṃ sarvajño nāhaṃ nirduḥkha ityanubhavāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvīśvarasyāpratyakṣatvātkathaṃ tatpratiyogikabhedasya pratyakṣatvamiti cet tatkiṃ pratiyogipratyakṣaṃ sapratiyogikapadārthapratyakṣamātre'bhāvapratyakṣamātre'nyonyābhāvapratyakṣa eva vā kāraṇam ? kiṃ vā yogyānupalabdherabhāvapratyakṣahetutvādabhāvayogyatve pratiyogiyogyatāyāḥ prayojakatvamiṣyate ? ādyapakṣatrayaṃ dūṣayati -- sapratiyogiketi || ityādau pratyakṣe vyabhicārāt |
pūrvakṣaṇe pratiyogibhūtagavayādipratyakṣābhāve'pi tādṛśapratyakṣajananāditi bhāvaḥ |
caturthaṃ tṛtīyadoṣātideśena dūṣayati -- eteneti || etadeva vivaṇoti -- stambha iti || pratiyoginaḥ piśācasyāyogyatve'pītyādiḥ |
ityanubhavāt ityākārakapratyakṣajananāt |
tathāca tatra vyabhicāreṇa yogyānupalabdherabhāvapratyakṣasāmānyahetutvāsambhavānnābhāvayogyatāyāḥ pratiyogiyogyatādhīnatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
yadvā, nanu piśācabhedapratyakṣameva nāstīti noktavyabhicāra ityata āha -- stambha iti || ityanubhavāt iti pratyakṣasyānubhavasiddhatvāt |
anyathā'nubhavavirodha iti bhāvaḥ |
nanu piśāco na bhavatīti pratītistadvirodhaviṣayaivāstu |
virodhasyāpi nañarthatvādata āha -- anyonyābhāveti || arthāntarānupasthitāvapi tasyānubhavasiddhatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvabhāvayogyatāyāḥ pratiyogiyogyatānadhīnatve gurutvādyabhāvapratyakṣāpattiḥ |
na ca saṃsargābhāvapratyakṣe pratiyogiyogyatvam, anyonyābhāvapratyakṣe cādhikaraṇayogyatvaṃ prayojakamiti vācyam |
stambhe piśācatvātyantābhāvasya vāyāvudbhūtarūpavadbhedasya ca pratyakṣānupapatteriti cenna |
anubhavānusāreṇa tattadabhāvatvasyaiva tattadyogyatāprayojakatvāt || viṣṇumitrasyeti || nāhaṃ yajñamitra ityādyanubhavāditi bhāvaḥ |
ityādītyādinā sarvasyāpi svadharmikasvetarapratiyogikabhedaḥ svapratyakṣa ityetatparigrahaḥ |
siddhāntayati -- atreti || atra bhedapramāṇadūṣaṇaviṣaye |
ayaṃ vakṣyamāṇaḥ |
parihāraḥ dūṣaṇoddhāraprakāraḥ |
atrābhedasādhakapramāṇaviṣaye parihāro nirākaraṇaprakāra iti cārthaḥ |
tatra tāvadbhedapramāṇadūṣaṇaparihāraprakāraṃ darśayati -- yattāvadityādinā || īśvarasya pratyakṣa iti || tasya nāhaṃ jīva ityanubhavasiddhatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvīśvarabuddherapratyakṣatveneśvarasya tādṛśapratyakṣaṃ vartata ityasmābhiḥ kathaṃ jñātavyamityata āha -- tasyeti || kṛṣṇarūpeṇeśena `dvāvimau puruṣau loka' ityādinā kṣarākṣarapuruṣadvayaṃ nirūpya uttamaḥ puruṣa ābhyāmanyo na tvenayoranyatara ityabhihitam |
naca tasyātādṛśapratyakṣābhāve evaṃvidhavacanaprayogaḥ sambhavati |
ato jñāyate astīśasyaitādṛśapratyakṣamitīti bhāvaḥ |
jīvasyāṇutvenāpratyakṣātkathaṃ tatpratiyogikabhedasya pratyakṣatvamityata āha -- īśvarasyeti || kṣamatvāt samarthatvāt |
sarvajñatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
idaṃ cābhyupetyoktam |
sapratiyogiketyādinā'nupadameva tādṛśaniyamanirākaraṇāditi dhyeyam || jīvasyeti || tasya nāhaṃ sarvajño na nirduḥkha ityanubhavasadbhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
pratiyogibhūteśvarasyāpratyakṣatve kathaṃ tatpratiyogikabhedasya pratyakṣatvamityata āha -- sapratiyogiketi || pratiyoginirūpyetyarthaḥ || iyamiti || vane gavayamanubhūya nagaragatasya godarśane iyaṃ gaurgavayasadṛśīti gavayapratiyogikasādṛśyapratyakṣaṃ jāyate |
naca tatra pratiyogibhūtagavayapratyakṣamasti |
tasya vanasthitatvena vyavahitatvāt |
ato'tra vyabhicārānnoktaniyama ityarthaḥ |
nanvabhāvarūpasapratiyogikapadārthapratyakṣe pratiyogipratyakṣaniyamo'ṅgīkriyate |
sādṛyasya bhāvarūpatvānna tatpratyakṣe vyabhicāra ityata āha -- iha ghaṭo neti || iha bhūtale ghaṭo nāstīti ghaṭapratiyogikābhāvapratyakṣaṃ jāyate |
naca tatra pratiyogibhūtalaghaṭapratyakṣamasti |
bhūtale ghaṭābhāvena tatsannikarṣābhāvāt |
ato'tra vyabhicārānnoktaniyama iti bhāvaḥ |
nanvanyonyābhāvarūpasapratiyogikāpadārthapratyakṣe pratiyogipratyakṣaniyamo'ṅgīkriyate |
iha ghaṭo nāstīti pratyakṣaṃ ca ghaṭasaṃsargābhāvaviṣayamato na tatra vyabhicāra ityata āha -- stambha iti || stambhaḥ piśaco neti piśācapratiyogikānyonyābhāvapratyakṣaṃ jāyate |
na ca tatra pratiyogibhūtapiśācapratyakṣamasti |
tasyātīndriyatvāt |
ato'tra vyabhicārānnoktaniyama ityarthaḥ |
nanvabhāvapratyakṣayogyatāyāṃ pratiyogipratyakṣayogyatā prayojikā |
yathā ghaṭasya pratyakṣayogyatvena tadabhāvasya pratyakṣayogyatvaṃ paramāṇoḥ pratyakṣāyogyatvena tadabhāvasya pratyakṣāyogyatvam |
ataḥ pratiyogibhūteśasya pratyakṣāyogyatvātkathaṃ tatpratiyogikānyonyābhāvasya pratyakṣayogyatvam |
pratyakṣayogyatābhāve ca kasya pratyakṣatvamityāśaṅkyātideśena dūṣayati -- eteneti || ayogyeti || pratyakṣāyogyetyarthaḥ |
etenetyuktaṃ vivaṇoti -- stambha iti || piśācapratiyogikānyonyābhāvasya tāvatpratyakṣayogyatvamasti |
naca tatra piśācasya pratyakṣayogyatā'sti |
ato'tra vyabhicārānnoktaniyama ityarthaḥ |
nanu tarhi paramāṇvabhāvapratyakṣaṃ syāditi vācyam |
kiṃ paramāṇusaṃsargābhāvasya pratyakṣatvamāpādyate utānyonyābhāvasya |
nādyaḥ |
saṃsargābhāvapratyakṣayogyatāyāṃ pratiyogipratyakṣayogyatāyāḥ kāraṇatvena tatra tadabhāvāt |
na dvitīyaḥ |
iṣṭāpatteḥ |
ghaṭaḥ paramāṇurneti paramāṇvanyonyābhāvapratyakṣābhyupagamāditi bhāvaḥ |
parasparaṃ jīvabhedasyāpi pratyakṣasiddhatvamāha -- evamiti || viṣṇumitrasya nāhaṃ yajñadatta ityanubhavasadbhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu nāhaṃ nirduḥkho na sarvajña iti nirduḥkhatvādisaṃsargābhāva evātmanyanubhūyate neśvarapratiyogikānyonyābhāva iti cenna |
mayi nirduḥkhatvaṃ sarvajñatvādikaṃ nāstītyanubhava eva tatsaṃsargābhāvaviṣayakaḥ |
nirduḥkhatvasyaiva pratiyogitayā tatropādānāt (pratiyogitayopādānāt)|
na cāsya nirduḥkhatvātyantābhāvo viṣayaḥ |
dharmipratiyoginostādātmyaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyena prasajya tasyaiva niṣedhāt |
pratyakṣaprāmāṇyaṃ tu sudṛḍhamanyatropapāditamasmābhiḥ |
nanvīśvarapratiyogiko jīvadharmiko bhedastu jīvasya pratyakṣa iti yaduktam |
tanna |
nāhamīśvara iti kasyāpyanubhavābhāvāt |
na ca nāhaṃ nirduḥkho na sarvajña ityasti tāvatsarveṣāmanubhavaḥ |
sa eva hi nāhamīśvara ityanubhavaḥ |
tallakṣaṇatvādīśvarasyeti vācyam |
asyānubhavasya nirduḥkhatvādisaṃsargābhāvaviṣayakatvena nirduḥkhādirūpeśvarapratiyogikānyonyābhāvaviṣayakatvābhāvādityāśaṅkate -- nanviti || evaṃ śaṅkamāno na laukiko nāpi parīkṣaka iti bhāvena samādhatte || maivamiti || iha ghaṭo nāstītivanmayi nirduḥkhatvaṃ sarvajñatvaṃ vā nāstīti nirduḥkhatvādikaṃ pratiyogitayopādāya yadyayamanubhavaḥ pravarteta syāttadā saṃsargābhāvaviṣayakaḥ |
na caivam |
kitvahaṃ nirduḥkha sarvajña iti sāmānādhikaraṇyena dharmipratiyoginorjīvośvarayostādātmyaṃ prasajya tanniṣedhaviṣayatvādanyonyābhāvaviṣayaka eveti bhāvaḥ |
atra sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ nāmāhantvanirduḥkhatvayorekādhikaraṇyaṃ na tvekavibhaktyantatvam |
pratītau vibhaktyāderabhāvāt |
tasya śabdadharmatvāt |
tathā'pi kecidekavibhaktyantatvameva sāmānādhikaraṇyamatra vivakṣitamiti vyācakruḥ |
nanu nāhaṃ nirduḥkha ityādyanubhavasya bhedaviṣayatve'pi nānena tatsiddhiḥ |
tasyābhedasādhakabahupramāṇaviruddhatvena prāmāṇyābhāvādityata āha -- pratyakṣaprāmāṇyaṃ tviti || anyatra nyāyāmṛte prathamaparicchede pratyakṣasya jātyopakramādinyāyaiśca prābalyavyutpādanavādamārabhya pratyakṣasya pāramārthikasatvagrāhitvavyutpādanavādaparyantaṃ navaprakaraṇeṣu nipuṇataramupapāditamityarthaḥ |
tatrāyaṃ vivekaḥ |
bāhyapratyakṣasya balavatpratyakṣetareṇa na bādhaḥ |
sākṣipratyakṣasya na kenāpi bādha iti || tatra nāhaṃ nirduḥkho na sarvajña iti tu sākṣipratyakṣameva |
ato na kenāpi tasya bādho'stīti |
nirbādhasākṣipratyakṣaprāmāṇyasya niyatatvāttena jīveśabhedaḥ sidhyatyeveti bhāvaḥ |
|| saṃsargābhāva iti || saviśeṣaṇe hīti nyāyānugrahāt duḥkhābhāvādirūpayogyāpratiyogitvena pratyakṣavattvasambhavādabhāvaikavidhyarūpalāghavācceti bhāvaḥ |
nanvevamapīśvarīyadharmādabhedaḥ prāpta iti vācyam |
tathā'pi bhedapratyakṣatābhaṅgāt |
vastutastu taddharmāṇāṃ svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatāṅgīkāreṇa na kaściddoṣaḥ |
tādātmyaṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyena prasajyeta |
nāhaṃ nirduḥkha ityatrāhaṃnirduḥkhapadayoḥ samāmānādhikaraṇyena tayostādātmyamāropyetyarthaḥ |
