evañca mayi nirduḥkhatvaṃ sarvajñatvaṃ vā nāstītyatra vyadhikaraṇānvayasya saṃsargavyutpannatayā'sya saṃsargāropyakatvena saṃsargajñāpakatvaṃ yuktam |
uktapratītestu tādātmyāropyakatvena tadvilakṣaṇābhāvaviṣayatyameva vācyamityadoṣaḥ |
bhedapratyakṣaṃ bhrāntamityuktamityata āha -- pratyakṣeti || anyatreti |
nyāyāmṛta ityarthaḥ |
tathā hi |
pratyakṣaprāmāṇyaṃ bādhakaṃ tūṣṇīṃ vā |
ādye pratyakṣeṇānumānenāgamena bādhakaśaṅkāmātrādvā |
nādyaḥ |
tadabhāvāt |
bhāve vā vaiparītyasambhavāt |
ādyasyāsañjātavirodhitvena balavattvācca |
na dvitīyaḥ |
kālātyayāpadiṣṭamātrocchedāpatteḥ |
pratyakṣasya dharmigrāhakatvenopajīvyatvācca |
na tṛtīyaḥ |
yajamānaḥ prastara iti śrutyā tadbhedapratyakṣabādhopapatteḥ |
candrapādeśapratyakṣaṃ tvāgamavirodhātprāgevānyatra klṛptapariṇāmasāmagrīvirahādapaṭutayā jātaṃ paścādāgamena bādhyate |
na turyaḥ |
atiprasaṅgāt |
ata eva na dvitīyaḥ |
vartamānamātragrāhipratyakṣeṇottarakālikasattā na sidhyatīti cenna |
tāvatā'pi trikālaniṣedharūpamithyātvāsiddheriti |
|| mayīti |
anuyogini saptamyāḥ saṃsargābhāvollekṣaniyatatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu nāhaṃ nirduḥkha ityādikamastu bhedaviṣayakaṃ tathā'pi nānena tatsiddhirasyāpramāṇatvādityata āha -- pratyakṣaprāmāṇyamiti || sākṣirūpatvādasya pratyakṣasya sākṣiṇaśca pramāṇarūpatvādbādhakābhāvācca |
neha nānā'sti tattvamasītyādyāgamasya tvanmate'khaṇḍārtha paratvenābādhakatvāt |
upajīvyapratyakṣavirodhena yajamānaḥ prastara ityādyāgamavadgauṇārthatāsambhavāt |
`bhinnā jīvāḥ paro bhinnastathā'pi jñānarūpataḥ |
procyante brahmarūpeṇa vedavādeṣu sarvaśaḥ' |
`tadguṇasāratvāt tadvyapadeśa' iti smṛtisūtrayorgauṇārthatāyā uktatvāccetyādinā nyāyāmṛte pratyakṣaprāmāṇyamupapāditamityarthaḥ |
nanu pratiyogipratyakṣasya vyabhicāreṇāhetutve'bhāvapratyakṣe kiṃ prayojakam |
indriyasambaddhaviśeṣaṇatādikameveti cenna |
piśacābhāvādeḥ pratyakṣatāpātāt |
na ca yogyapratiyogikatvaṃ saṃsargābhāve tantram |
yogyādhikaraṇatvaṃ cānyonyābhāvapratyakṣe tena na piśācābhāvādeḥ pratyakṣatvam |
na vā stambhe piśācānyonyābhāvasyāpratyakṣatvamiti vācyam |
ghaṭe manastvātyantābhāvāderapratyakṣatāpātāt |
ghaṭe paramāṇu bhedādeḥ pratyakṣatāpātāt |
kiñca yatkiñcitpratiyogiyogyatvaṃ yāvattadyogyatvaṃ vā tantram |
ādye mūrtasāmānyābhāvapratyakṣāpātaḥ |
dvitīye mahati vāyau rūpasāmānyābhāvapratyakṣāpātaḥ |
evamadhikaraṇayogyatāyāmapi vikalpaḥ |
ādya mūrtasāmānye piśācānyonyābhāvapratyakṣaprasaṅgaḥ |
dvitīye mahati vāyau rūpavadanyonyābhāvapratyakṣāpāta iti cet |
atrodayanācāryāḥ |
pratiyogitadvyāpyetarayāvatpratiyogyupalambhakasamavadhānaṃ saṃsargābhāve yogyatā |
tena nātīndriyānyonyābhāvāpratyakṣāpātaḥ |
na vā piśācābhāvādeḥ pratyakṣatāprasaṅga iti vadanti |
tanna |
anyonyābhāvapratyakṣe yogyatāntarasyānuktatvena tatrendriyasambandhaviśeṣaṇādereva hetutvāṅgīkāre gurutvavadanyonyābhāvāpratyakṣatāpatteḥ |
kiñca yāvattadupalambhakasamādhāne sambhavati yāvattadupalambhakatāvacchedakaṃ yattadavacchinnasamavadhānamapi tathā |
bhūtale ghaṭābhāvapratyakṣasthale yāvaddhaṭopalambhakatāvacchedakānāṃ tattatparāmarśatvatattacchabdajñānatvatattasaṃskāratvādīnāmāśrayāṇāmabhāvāt |
kiñca pratiyogivyāpyatvaṃ kālikena daiśikena vā sambandhena |
nādyaḥ |
ghaṭasannikarṣasya ghaṭāvyāpyatāpātāt |
ghaṭanāśottaraṃ tannāśāt |
kālikena nityavyāpyetarāprasiddhyā nityapratiyogikasaṃsargābhāvātpratyakṣatāpātācca |
na dvitīyaḥ |
ghaṭendriyasannikarṣasyāpi ghaṭāvyāpyatvāpattyā tatsamavadhānābhāvena ghaṭābhāvasyāpratyakṣatāpatteḥ |
pratiyogimadasādharaṇakāraṇaṃ vyāpyapadena vivakṣitamiti cenna |
ālokādivyāvṛttāsādhāraṇyasya durvacatvāt |
kiñca yāvattadupalambhakasamavahitābhāvatvena vā |
abhāvasamavahitayāvattadupalambhakatvena vā hetutvamitvatra vinigamanābhāvaḥ |
indriyāderabhāvapratyakṣahetutayā yogyatārūpakāraṇatāvacchedakakukṣinikṣepāyogāt |
saṃyogināśajanyanāśāpratyatyakṣatā ca saṃyoginostatprakṣasya vā saṃyogādvyāpyatvena saṃyogopalambhakatvena samavadhānābhāvāditi dik |
maṇikṛtastu |
pratiyogisattvaprasañjana prasañjitapratiyogyupalambhakābhāvavattvaṃ saṃsargābhāvānyonyābhāvasādhāraṇī yogyatā |
yatrādhikaraṇe saṃyogatādātmyādisambandhāvacchinnapratiyogisattvenāpāditena tatsambandhena pratiyogyupalambho yasyābhāvasyāpādāyituṃ śakyate so'bhāvo'trādhikaraṇe yogya ityarthaḥ |
pratiyogisattvaṃ cāvinaśyadavasthapratiyogisattvaṃ tena saṃyogināśajanyasaṃyoganāśapratyakṣopapattiḥ |
piśācānyonyābhāvatadatyantābhāvayoḥ pratyakṣāpratyakṣopapattiśceti vadanti |
tadapi na |
tathā hi |
pratiyogisattvasya pratiyogyupalambhaprasañjakatvaṃ tadupahitatvaṃ tadyogyatvaṃ vā |
nādyaḥ |
tarkarūpasya tasyābhāvapratyakṣātpūrvaṃ niyamenābhāvāt |
na dvitīyaḥ |
vyāpyatvātiriktasya tasyānirukteḥ |
astu tadeva kiṃ bādhakamiti cet |
ucyate |
śuddhaṃ pratiyogisattvaṃ vyāpyaviśeṣitaṃ vā'bhimatam |
ādye paṭādyabhāvapratyakṣaṃ na syāt |
kevalasattvasyopalambhāvyāpyatvāt |
dvitīye jalaparamāṇau pṛthivītvābhāvāpratyakṣāpatteḥ |
tatrāpi mahattvaviśiṣṭapṛthivītvasyopalambhavyāpyatvāt |
na ca dharmyavṛttiviśeṣaṇāvacchinnapratiyogisattvaṃ vyāpyaṃ vivakṣitaṃ mahattvādikaṃ ca paramāṇvavṛttīti noktadoṣa iti vācyam |
indriyasannikarṣapārthivāṇubhinnatvādisamavahitapṛthivītvasattvasya tathātvenoktadoṣānivṛtteḥ |
na ca tatrādhikaraṇe yatpratiyogyupalambhakaṃ tadatiriktānavacchinnaṃ tattathā |
pārthivāṇubhinnatvādestadatiriktatvānnoktadoṣa iti vācyam |
tatrādhikaraṇe pratiyogyupalambhātiriktānavacchinnaṃśuddhaṃ pratiyogisattvaṃ tu na tatheti yāvatpratiyogyupalambhakāvacchinnaṃ vyāpyaṃ vācyam |
tatrāpi yatkiñcitpratiyogyupalambhakāvacchinnaṃ na tatheti yāvatpratiyogyupalambhakāvacchinnatayā vācyam |
evañca prāptāprāptavivekena yāvatpratiyogyupalambhakasamavādhānameva yogyatetyuktaṃ syāt |
sa ca prācīnairyogyatā'tra ca doṣāṇāmuktatvāt |
kiñcāpādyopalambho laukiko'laukiko vā |
nādyaḥ |
stambhe piśācānyonyābhāvāpratyakṣatāpātāt |
tatra tādṛśopalambhāsiddheḥ |
na dvitīyaḥ |
tādātmyena piśācasattvasyālaukikopalabhbhāvyāpyatvāt |
na dvitīyaḥ |
tādātmyena piśācasattvasya laukikopālambhāvyāpyatvāt |
na cālaukikopālambhakasamavahitaṃ tatsattvaṃ tatheti vācyam |
piśācātyantābhāvapratyakṣāpatteriti dik |
tasmādindriyasannikarṣādyaviśeṣe kaścidevābhāvaḥ kasyacit pratyakṣa ityatra niyāmakaṃ durvacanīyamitīśvarānyonyābhāvapratyakṣe kā pratyāśeti cet |
ucyate |
anugataniyāmakamabhāveṣu nāstyeva |
kintu bhāveṣviva tattadabhāvapratyakṣe bhinnabhinnaiva sāmagrī niyāmikā |
gurutvādivat keṣāñcidabhāvānāṃ svarūpeṇa pratyakṣāyogyatvādevāpratyakṣatvam |
sāmagrī cānvayavyatirekābhyāṃ yathāyathamunneyā |
stambhaḥ piśāco neti vannāhaṃ sarvajña ityanubhāvānneśvarānyonyābhāvasyāpratyakṣatvasambhāvanā |
itarathā ghaṭānyopyābhāvapratyakṣatve'pi kā pratyāśeti sarvaṃ sustham |
|| na cāsyeti || idānīmahaṃ na śikhīti pratīterivānuyoginaḥ saptamyantapadenānupādāne'pi pratiyogina āśrayāṃśe bhānena pratiyogitayā prathamāntapadenānupādāne'pi tatsaṃsargābhāvaviṣayakatvasambhavāt |
na ca dṛṣṭānte vivādaḥ |
bhedasya vyāpyavṛttitayā śikhibhedasya tatra bhānāyogādityāśayaḥ || tādātmyamiti || sāmānādhikaraṇyena samānavibhaktikapadenollekhabalena |
niṣedhāt niṣedhānubhavāt |
tṛtīyāntamatraiva hetuḥ |
tādātmyābhāva eva hi bhedaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte tvagatyā tatra tādṛśavyavahārasya pāribhāṣikatvaṃ vā yathākathañcidatyantābhāvabodhakatvaṃ vā bheda(sya)vyāpyavṛttitvaṃ svīkṛtya tadbādhakatvaṃ vā'bhyupagamyata iti bhāvaḥ |
nanu sarvamapi pratyakṣaṃ śrutivirodhādapramāṇameveti na sādhakam |
īśvarapratyakṣasyāpi vikṣepaśaktimanmāyārūpadoṣamūlakatvādityata āha -- pratyakṣaprāmāṇyamiti || pratyakṣasya śrutiṃ pratyupajīvyatvaniravakāśatvasvabhāvaprābalyādibhistatprāmāṇyaṃ sudṛḍhatayā nyāyāmṛte upapāditamityarthaḥ |
syādevamuktabhedasya pratyakṣatvaṃ, yadi nāhaṃ nirduḥkha ityādi pratyakṣaṃ bhedaviṣayaṃ syāt, na tvevamityāśaṅkate -- nanviti || pratiyogitayopādānāditi || abhāvāṃśe pratiyogitāsaṃsargeṇa viśeṣaṇatvādityarthaḥ |
asya nāhaṃ nirduḥkha ityādipratyakṣasya || dharmipratiyāginoriti || nāhaṃ nirduḥkha iti vākyenetyādiḥ |
dharmiṇi jīve pratiyogina īśvarasya tādātmyaṃ dharmipratiyogipadayoḥ samānavibhaktikatvenāropya |
tādṛśatādātmyāropajanaka samānavibhaktikadharmipratiyogipade samullikhyeti yāvat |
tasya tādātmyasyaiva niṣedhāt abhāvabodhanādityarthaḥ |
ayamāśayaḥ |
yādṛśaśabdādyādṛśārthasya bodhastādṛśaśabdollikhitapratītau tādṛśārtha eva bhāsate |
nañasamabhivyāhāre yatra yādṛśadharmaḥ pratīyate nañsamabhivyāhāre tatra tadabhāvo budhyate |
tathāca mayi nirduḥkhatvamastītyādau dharmiṇi nirduḥkhatvabhānāttadabhāvasyaiva nirduḥkhatvaṃ nāstītyādau bhānasambhavaḥ |
na ca mayi nirduḥkhatvamastītyādiśabdānnirduḥkhatvādau dharminirūpitāstitvasyaiva bodhāt kathamuktapratītau dharmiṇi nirduḥkhatvabhānamiti vācyam |
nirduḥkhatve dharminirūpitāstitvabhāne dharmiṇi nirduḥkhatvabhānasyāpyanubhavasiddhatvāt |
evamahaṃ nirduḥkha ityādau dharmiṇi nirduḥkhatādātmyabhānāttadabhāvasyaiva nāhaṃ nirduḥkha ityādau bhānamityuktānyonyābhāvasya pratyakṣatvamāvaśyakam |
tādātmyaniṣedhasyaivānyonyābhāvatvāt |
na ca nirduḥkhatādātmyasya nirduḥkhatvarūpatayā tadatyantābhāva evāyamiti vācyam |
tādātmyatvena tādātmyaniṣedhasyānyonyābhāvatvāt |
evaṃ nāhaṃ nirduḥkha ityādau nirduḥkhapratiyogikatvena bhāsamānabhede tattādātmyapratiyogikatvamapi bhāsate |
nirduḥkhatvaṃ nāstītyādau tu na tatheti naikaviṣayatvamiti dhyeyam |
nanvastu jīveśvarabhedapratyakṣam |
kiṃ tvapramāṇameva |
darśitapramāṇabādhitatvādityata āha -- pratyakṣaprāmāṇyamiti || sudṛḍhamupapāditaṃ mānāntarābādhyatvenopapāditaṃ nyāyāmṛtādau |
anyathā vahniranuṣṇaḥ kṛtakatvādityādau bādhocchedāpatteriti bhāvaḥ |
asyārthasya pūrvācāryairevoktatvāduktamityanuktvopapāditamityuktam |
jīveśābhedapratyakṣaṃ tu nāstyeveti vakṣyate |
yadvā , prakṛtasya jīveśvarabhedapratyakṣasyaiva prāmāṇyaṃ sudṛḍhamavyabhicāryupapāditam |
asya sākṣipratyakṣatvāt sākṣiṇaśca niyataprāmāṇyakatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
yaduktamīśapratiyogiko jīvadharmiko bhedo jīvasya pratyakṣa iti tanna |
tasya tādṛśapratyakṣābhāvāt |
naca nāhaṃ sarvajño na nirduḥkha iti pratyakṣameveśabhedāvagāhīti vācyam |
tasya jīve nirduḥkhatvātyantābhāvaviṣayakatvena nirduḥkheśānyonyābhāvaviṣayakatvābhāvāditi śaṅkate -- nanviti || yadyuktapratyakṣasya nirduḥkhatvātyantābhāvaviṣayakatvaṃ syāt tarhi mayi nirduḥkhatvamiti nirduḥkhatvaṃ pratiyogitvenāpādya tanniṣedhaḥ kriyeta |
nacaivam |
kintvahaṃ nirduḥkha ityasmacchabdavācyajīvasya nirduḥkhaśabdāvācyeneśenaikyaṃ pratiyogitvenāpādya neti niṣidhyate |
tādātmyaniṣedharūpaścānyonyābhāva eva |
ata īśapratiyogikānyonyābhāvaviṣayakatvamevāsyeti || parihārati || mayīti || asya nāhaṃ nirduḥkha iti pratyakṣasya || dharmipratiyoginoriti || dharmī asmacchabdavācyo jīvaḥ |
pratiyogī nirduḥkhaśabdābhidheya īśaḥ |
tādātmyaprasañjanajñāpakamuktam || sāmānādhikaraṇyeneti || atra sāmānādhikaraṇyamekavibhaktyantatvam |
tathāca śuklaḥ paṭa ityādivadahaṃ nirduḥkha ityanayorapi prathamāntatvena tādātmyapratipādakatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanu nīlaṃ nabha iti nabhonailyagrāhipratyakṣasya nabho nīrūpaṃ vibhutvādātmavadityanumānaviruddhatvena prādeśaparimitaścandramā iti candraprādeśaparimitatvāvagāhipratyakṣasya `aṣṭāśītisahastrāṇi vistīrṇo yojanāni tu |
pramāṇaṃ tatra vijñeyaṃ kalāḥ pañcadaśaiva tu |
' ityādyāgamabādhitatvena yathā'prāmāṇyaṃ tathā jīveśabhedagrāhipratyakṣasya vimatā ātmānaḥ paramātmanastatvato na bhidyante ātmatvāt paramātmavadityanumānabādhitatvena nehanānetyāgamabādhitatvena cāprāmāṇyamityata āha -- pratyakṣaprāmāṇyamiti || pratyakṣasyānumānāgamābādhyatvaṃ kutrāpi na dṛṣṭam |
nabhonailyacandraprādeśatvāvagāhipratyakṣaṃ tu dūrasthatvādidoṣayuktatvādapaṭvevotpannam |
anantaraṃ pravṛttāvanumānāgamau tena dattāvakāśau tadbādhakāvivocyete |
naca bhedāvagāhipratyakṣasyāpaṭutve mānamasti yenoktarītyā'prāmāṇyamityanyatra nyāyāmṛte samarthitamityarthaḥ |
anumānāni ca |
jīvaḥ brahmapratiyogikadharmisattāsamānasattākabhedādhikaraṇaṃ brahmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvāt vyatirekeṇa brahmavat |
brahma vā jīvapratiyogikadharmisattāsamānasattākabhedādhikaraṇaṃ jīvānusaṃhitaduḥkhānanusandhātṛtvāt vyatirekeṇa jīvavaditi || atra jīvabrahmapratiyogikabhedayoḥ ghaṭasattayā vyāvahārikayā samānasattākayorvyāvahārikayorbhedayorghaṭe prasiddhatvānnāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam |
jīvabrahmaṇośca pāramārthikatvāttatsattāsamānasattākatvena siddhyan bhedaḥ pāramārthika eva siddhyatīti na siddhasādhanam |
duḥkhādīnāmantaḥkaraṇāśritatve'pi jīvasyānusandhātṛtvāt |
brahmaṇo duḥkhānusandhātṛtve tadabhāvalakṣaṇamukterapuruṣārthatvaprasaṅgāt nirañjana(tva)śrutivirodhācca tadabhāvāvaśyambhāvānnāsiddhiḥ ||
evaṃ jīveśayorjīvānāṃ ca mitho bhede pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇamabhidhāyānumānānyapyāha -- anumānāni ceti || jīveśvarayorjīvānāṃ jaḍānāṃ ca bhede pramāṇatvenopanyasyata iti śeṣaḥ || dharmisattāsamānasattāketi || dharmī svayaṃ jīvastatsattāsamasattāketyarthaḥ || brahmānanusaṃhiteti || atrānusandhānaṃ nāma na sākṣātkāramātraṃ yena sarvajñatvādbrahmaṇaḥ kathaṃ duḥkhasya tadananusaṃhitatvamiti śaṅkāvakāśaḥ |
kintvahaṃ sukhī duḥkhītyanubhava eva |
na hīśvarasyaitādṛśo duḥkhānubhavo'sti |
kinnāma ghaṭagatarūpādikamiva jīvagataṃ sukhādikaṃ sākṣātkaroti |
ato brahmānanusaṃhiteti yuktam |
kecittu duḥkhādau svātmīyatvābhimānenātmani tatkṛtanīcoccatvalakṣaṇā vikriyaivānubhava iti vadanti |
taccintyam |
vikriyāyā anubhavānantarikatvāt |
na hi tajjanyasya tattvaṃ sambhavati |
nāpyahaṃ sukhītyādau sukhādivannīcoccatā'vabhāsate |
anubhavavadvikriyāyāḥ sakarmakakriyātvānanubhavācca |
na hi duḥkhaṃ sukhaṃ vā'nubhavāmītyanuvyavasāyaviṣayānubhavasyeva nīco'smītyādijñānaviṣayanīcatvādivikriyāyāḥ sakarmakatā cakāsti |
`nīcoccataiva duḥkhāderbhoga ityabhidhīyata' iti vacanaṃ tu `trapusaṃ pratyakṣo jvara' itivadgauṇam |
jñānināṃ duḥkhādyanubhave'pi kadācinnīco'smītyādijñānānudayācca |
yathoktam |
`brahmaṇo'pyalpaduḥkhaṃ syāttadapyanabhimānataḥ |
nāstyātmasambaddhataye'ti |
`nidrā ratirmanyurahaṃ madaśca dehena jātasya hi me na santī'ti bhāgavate jaḍabharatavacanācca |
dehena jātasya dehābhimānina ityarthaḥ |
nanvahaṃ sukhādimānityanusandhānamīśvarasyāpi sambhavati |
sukhādimānityasya sukhādisvāmitvamevārthaḥ |
na tu tatsamavāyaḥ |
teṣāmantaḥkaraṇadharmatvena jīve'pi tadabhāvāt |
asti ca tatsvāmitvaṃ bhagavataḥ |
pratyuta jīvāpekṣayā mukhyaṃ ca |
ataḥ kathaṃ brahmānanusaṃhiteti viśeṣaṇamiti cenna |
ahaṃ sukhītyādyanusandhānaṃ nāma mabhedaṃ sukhamanukūlaṃ mabhedaṃ duḥkhaṃ pratikūlamityanubhavaḥ |
` anyānandādisādṛśyamānukūlyādinā para'mityukteḥ |
na caitādṛśaṃ duḥkhānusandhātṛtvamīśvarasyāsti |
jīvaduḥkhasyeśaṃ prati pratikūlatvābhāvāt |
tasmādyuktaṃ brahmānanusaṃhiteti duḥkhaviśeṣaṇam |
kecittu paratantraduḥkhānusandhānasya vā'sarvaviṣayaduḥkhānusandhānasya vā'mukhyasvāmitāsambandhena duḥkhakṛtanīcatāvattvasya vā hetutvaṃ vivakṣitamityāhuḥ |
tadapi cintyam |
anusandhānaśabdasyoktarītyā'nubhavaviśeṣārthakatvena pāratantryādiviśeṣaṇakṛtyādarśanāt |
nanu sapakṣabhūte jaḍe lakṣmyāṃ cāsya hetorabhāvādasādhāraṇyamiti cenna |
anukūlatarkasadbhāve tasya pratipakṣānunnāyakatvenādoṣatvāt |
anukūlatarkāṇāṃ cātra vakṣyamāṇatvāt |
nanu cātra duḥkhānusandhātṛtvamātrasya hetutvopapattau brahmānanusaṃhiteti viśeṣaṇaṃ vyarthamiti cenna |
hetoraprayojakatāparihārārthamanukūlatarkasphoraṇārthatvāt |
yathā ` īkṣaternāśabda'mityatrāstvīkṣaṇīyatvaṃ mā'stu vācyatvamityaprayojakatāśaṅkāyāṃ pratyakṣādyavedyatve satīti viśeṣaṇaṃ hetūcchittirūpānukūlatarkavyutpādanārtham |
tadvadatrāpi yadi duḥkhānusandhātṛtve'pi jīvasya brahmabhedo na syāt tarhi jīvavattadabhinnasya brahmaṇo'pi duḥkhānusandhānaṃ syāditi || jīvamātrasya pakṣīkṛtatvādanvayadṛṣṭāntābhāvādāha -- vyatirekeṇeti || evaṃ jīvadharmikabrahmapratiyogikabhede'numānamabhidhāya brahmadharmikajīvapratiyogikabhede'numānamāha -- brahma veti || evamanumānadvaye'pi kiṃ pāramārthikabhedaḥ sādhyate |
uta vyāvahārikaḥ |
nādyaḥ |
aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt |
na dvitīyaḥ |
siddhasādhanatvāditi śaṅkaparihārāya sādhyadvaye dharmisattāsamānasattāketi viśeṣaṇamupāttam |
tena kathaṃ doṣadvayaparihāra ityataḥ krameṇa tadubhayaparihāraṃ darśayati -- atreti || jīvabrahmaṇośceti || dharmibhūtayoriti śeṣaḥ |
nanu duḥkhāderantaḥkaraṇadharmatvātkathaṃ jīvasya tadanusandhātṛtvam |
brahmaṇo vā kutastadananusandhātṛtvam |
jīvavadduḥkhānusandhātṛtvāṅgīkāre bādhakābhāvāt |
ato'numānadvaye'pi hetvorasiddhirityata āha -- duḥkhādīnāmiti || ādipadena sukhādīnāṃ kṣuttṛḍādidehendriyādidharmāṇāṃ ca grahaṇam |
antaḥkaraṇādītyatrādipadena dehendriyādigrahaṇam |
duḥkhasyaikadeśasyaiva prakṛtatve'pi sukhādīnāṃ dṛṣṭāntatvena grahaṇam || jīvasyānusandhātṛtvāditi || nādyānumāne'siddhirityuttareṇānvayaḥ |
na hi jīvagatameva jīvenānusandheyaṃ nānyagatamiti niyamo'sti |
mabhedaṃ pratikūlamityādyanubhavamātrasyānusandhānaśabdārthatvāt |
tasya cāntaḥkaraṇagataduḥkhādāvapi sambhavāditi bhāvaḥ || tadbhāvalakṣaṇeti || duḥkhādyanusandhātṛbrahmabhāvalakṣaṇetyarthaḥ |
māyimate brahmabhāvāpattereva mokṣatvenāṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ || tadabhāveti || duḥkhādyanusandhātṛtvābhāvetyarthaḥ || nāsiddhiriti || dvitīyānumāne'pītyarthaḥ |
|| jīvo brahmapratiyogiketi || jīvatvopalakṣitā cid brahmatvopalakṣitacitpratiyogiketyarthaḥ |
aprastutajaḍabhede'rthāntaratāvāraṇāya brahmapratiyogiketi || siddhasādhanātāvāraṇāya dharmītyādi |
dharmī jīvacit tatsattā pāramārthikī tatsamānetyarthaḥ |
samānasattvaṃ ca sattātvasākṣādvyāpyadharmeṇeti jñeyam |
tena vyāvahārikyāḥ sattāyāḥ padārthatvena dharmeṇa tatsamānatvātsiddhasādhanateti nirastam |
pāramārthiketyaprasiddhirityevamuktiḥ |
tathā'pi pakṣadharmatābalātpāramārthyasiddhiḥ |
upalakṣaṇametat |
dharmisattāsamānasattāketi padasthāne dharmijñānākāryeti vā |
dharmijñānābādhyeti yāvaddharmikālamanuvartamāneti vā'naupādhiketi vā padaprakṣepe'pyuktabhedasiddhiriti jñeyam |
brahmānanusandhātṛtvādityeva hetuḥ |
duḥkhapadaṃ pakṣadharmatāsphuraṇārtham |
duḥkhānanusandhātṛtvādityeva hetuḥ |
śiṣṭapadaṃ prayojakatāsphuraṇārtham |
anumānadvayābhiprāyeṇaivamuktiḥ |
yadvā'trānusandhānaṃ nāma sākṣātkāramātraṃ vivakṣitam |
evañca duḥkhānusandhātṛtvaṃ sarvajñe brahmaṇyasmadrītyā vyabhicaritam |
brahmānanusaṃhitaṃ yathā tathā duḥkhānusandhātṛtvāditi kriyāviśeṣaṇam |
brahmajñānavijātīyanīcatāhetubhūtajñānavattvādityarthaḥ |
evamapi duḥkhapadaṃ vyarthamiti cenna |
hetuvākyaparyavasitasya duḥkhabhoktṛtvasya vivakṣitatvāt |
evamuttaraprayoge'pi |
yadyapi dvitīyaprayoge ghaṭādāvanvayavyāptireva sugrahā |
tathā'pi kadācitpāramārthiketi viśeṣaṇadāne'pyaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatāyā na vyāptivighaṭakatvam |
vyatirekavyāptigrahasambhavāditi sūcayituṃ vyatirekapradarśanam |
dharmītyādiviśeṣaṇābhiprāyamāha -- atretyādinā |
nanu tarhi prathamaprayoge brahmapratiyogikadharmisattāsamānasattākabhedavati ghaṭe hetorabhāvādasādhāraṇyamiti cenna |
vādivipratipatteḥ |
asādhāraṇyaṃ na hetvābhāsa ityeke vyāpyatvāsiddhāvantarbhavatīti kecit |
anye tvavidyamānasapakṣasyaiva tathātvena vidyamānasapakṣastu vyabhicārī |
anucitasthalavṛttitvavaducitasthalāvṛtterapi vyabhicārapadārthatvāt |
eke tu satyapyanyatra sādhyatatsambandhādarśanena vyāptisandehajanakatvātpṛthagghetvābhāsa ityāhuḥ |
vastutastu nāyaṃ hetvābhāso vyatirekamukhena vyāptisambhavāt |
nāpi saṃśayajanakatvamanukūlatarkeṇa tanniścayopapatteḥ |
anyathā dhūmādeḥ katipayasapakṣavartino'pi vyāptisandehataulyāditi || astu vā'yaṃ doṣaḥ |
duḥkhānusandhātṛtvaṃ nāma doṣādhikaraṇatvaṃ vivakṣitam |
tacca ghaṭe'stīti na kaściddoṣaḥ |
nanu jīvabrahmaṇorabhedavāde jīvagatānusandhānasyaiva brahmagatatvenāsiddhiriti cenna |
evaṃ sati kutrāpi tuhinadahanādau viruddhadharmeṇa vyāvahārikabhedasādhanamapi na syāt |
tatrāpi dahanagatoṣṇatvasya tuhinagatatvāditi || kiñca prakṛtaheturna niścitāsiddhaḥ kintu sādhyabhūtasandehāhitasandehagrastaḥ |
evañca sādhyasandehānāhitasvārasika eva |
tathāca sādhyasandehāhitasaṃśaye heturasaṃśayādiviṣayatvaṃ hetorasiddhirnāma |
anyathā vahnisaṃśayāhito dhūmasaṃśayo'pi tathā syāt |
tatrāpi kāraṇībhūtavahnisandehaitatkāryadhūmasandehasya nyāyyatvāt |
nanu sandigdho'pi vahnirna pratyakṣapramitadhūmasandehasyeṣṭe |
kintu hetuniścayabalāttatsandeha eva līyata iti kecittarhyatrāpi niraniṣṭādiśrutibalādabhedasandeha eva līyata iti || nanu dvitīyaprayoge'nanusandhānātyantābhāvarūpahetorjīve vyabhicāraḥ |
anusandhānasya svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvāditi cenna |
pratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇātyantābhāvasya vivakṣitatvāt || nāsiddhiriti || jīvasyānusandhātṛtvādityuktyā''dyanumāne hetusvarūpasya hi dvitīye tadviśeṣaṇasya brahmaṇaśca tadabhāvāvaśyambhāvādityuktyādyaviśeṣaṇasya dvitīye tatsvarūpasya nāsiddhirityarthaḥ |
|| anumānāni ceti || mukhye jīveśvarabhede prāsaṅgike jīvānāṃ parasparabhede jaḍānāṃ ca parasparabhede ca pramāṇānītyarthaḥ || jīva iti || pakṣavicārastu mūla evāgre bhaviṣyati |
brahmānanusaṃhiteti hetāvanukūlatarkasphoraṇārtham |
na tu hetuśarīrāntargataṃ vaiyyarthyāt |
na tu brahmaṇi vyabhicāraḥ |
tasya sarvajñatvena sarvaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvāt |
na ca tasya duḥkhānusandhātṛtve tadbhāvalakṣaṇamukterapuraṣārthatvāpattiḥ |
bhavanmate tadbhāvasyāmuktitvena bhavanmate'naikāntyāt |
na ca `niraniṣṭo niravadya' iti śrutivirodhādasmābhirduḥkhānusandhātṛtvaṃ brahmaṇo nāṅgīkriyata iti vācyam |
`yaḥ sarvajña' ityādiśrutivirodhādapasiddhāntācca |

bhavanmate parameśvarasyaiva mukhyaṃ sarvasvāmitvaṃ taddattamamukhyaṃ `jīvānāmiti jyotirādyadhiṣṭhānaṃ tu tadāmananādi'tyadhikaraṇe nirūpitatvāt |

|| anumānāni ceti || pūrvoktabhedeṣvityādiḥ || brahmapratiyogiketi || svasminvyāsajyavṛttidharmāvacchinnapratiyogi(tāka)bhedānaṅgīkāreṇedam |
ato noddeśasya brahmavṛttitadvyaktitvabrahmatvādyavacchinnapratiyogitākabhedasyāsiddhiprasaṅgenārthāntarāpātaḥ |

siddhasādhanatā tu nāsti |
vakṣyamāṇajīvatvopalakṣitacaitanye svasamānasattākasya kasyāpi (bhedasya) pareṇa naṅgīkṛtatvena sādhyasyobhayasiddhatvābhāvāt || brahmānanusaṃhiteti || duḥkhānusandhātṛtvamātrasya vyabhicārādyabhāve'pi brahmānanusaṃhiteti duḥkhādisvarūpakathanamaikye jīvānusaṃhitaduḥkhe brahmaṇo'nusandhānābhāvānupapattirityanukūlatarkotthāpanāya |
evamuttaratrānumāne'pi |
athavā tasya vyabhicāravārakatve'pyuktānukūlatarkotthāpanadvārā vyāptigrahopayogitvāt hetuśarīre niveśo yukta eva |

dharmisattāsamānasattākatvasya sādhyadvaye niveśastu jīvabrahmaṇoḥ parasparavyāvahārikabhedamādāya paraṃ prati siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya |
sādhyaprasiddhirghaṭādāveveti svayaṃ vakṣyati |
vyatirekeṇa jīvavaditīti |
atra ghaṭādāvanvayavyāpteḥ sambhave'pi sā śiṣyaireva jñātuṃ śakyetyāśayenānuktvā pūrvottarānumānasāhacaryānurodhādvyatirekavyāptirevoktā |
yadvoktahetorasati sattve'pi svadharmisattāghaṭitasādhyāprasiddhyā vyabhicāravāraṇāya hetau cetanatvaṃ lāghavānniveśanīyamato nānvayasambhava iti vyatirekavyāptirevokteti draṣṭavyam |
nanu duḥkhāderantaḥkaraṇadharmatvātsvāśritaduḥkhajñātṛtvarūpaṃ vā tatpratyakṣarūpaṃ vā tadanusandhātṛtvaṃ prathamānumāne jīve'bhāvādasiddham |
dvitīyānumāne ca jīvānusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvābhāvasya jīve'pi sattvādvyabhicāra ityata āha -- duḥkhādīnāmiti || antaḥkaraṇāśritatve'pi tādātmyena tanniṣṭhatve'pi || jīvasyeti || tadanusandhātṛtvāditi || anusandhātṛtvaśarīre tādātmyena khāśritatvasya duḥkhādāvaniṣṭatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu tadanusandhātṛtvaṃ nāma tatpratyakṣavattvamityuktaṃ syāt |
jñānatvena tanniveśasyātiprasaktatvāt |
itthaṃ ca brahmapadārthabhūtabhavadīyeśvarasyāpi sākṣāddarśitvāttadananusaṃhitaduḥkhāprasiddhyā prathamānumāne hetusvarūpasyaivāsiddhiḥ |
brahmānanusaṃhiteti viśeṣaṇasya hetuśarīraniveśapakṣe tadabhāvapakṣe brahmaṇyeva vyabhicāraḥ |

anusandhānasya tatpratyakṣāpekṣayā'tiriktatvāt |

tacca jīvasvarūpa eva vartate na paramātmani |
taduktaṃ bhāṣye |
`nīcoccataiva duḥkhāderbhoga ityabhidhīyata' iti |
sudhāyāṃ `mithyātvaṃ yadi duḥkhāde'rityanuvyākhyānavivaraṇāvasara eva `satye eva nīcoccatvalakṣaṇe vikṛtī anubhavati |
tādṛśavikriyāyāśca na jīvasvarūpahānisampādakatva'miti |
nanvetāvatā hetuśarīre kathaṃ niveśaḥ |
na ca brahmavṛttivikriyājanakatvābhāvādduḥkhapratyakṣādhīnavikriyāvattvaṃ heturniṣpadyata iti vācyam |
tanmate brahmavṛttivikriyāprasiddhyā prāmāṇikapratiyogikābhāvānaṅgīkāreṇa tajjanakatvābhāvaghaṭitahetoraprasiddhiḥ |
svavṛttivikriyājanakatvaṃ saṃsargāvacchinnābhāvaṃ niveśya vā svajanyavikriyātvāvacchinnābhāvavadbrahmakatvena duḥkhapratyakṣaṃ vā niveśya hetupariṣkāre jīvasvarūpe mayā tadanaṅgīkārādasiddhiriti cenmaivam |
tvanmate'pyupāsyabrahmarūpasya saviśeṣasya rāmakṛṣṇādernityanirduḥkhatvasya śrutipuraṇādiśatasiddhatvena jīvānāṃ duḥkhitāyāścānubhavasiddhatvena tadvyavasthāyāstvayā'pyaṅgīkartavyatvena mayā'pi tvatsiddhaṃ niveśyaiva hetu(śarīra)pariṣkārasya kartuṃ śakyatvāt |
na ca mayā duḥkhādiviṣayakāntaḥkaraṇavṛttyātmakapratyakṣavattvaṃ tadanusandhātṛtvam |
tacca jīvānāṃ vartate |
īśvarapratyakṣaṃ tu māyāvṛttyātmakamiti na tasyānusandhātṛtvamiti tasyānusandhātṛtvamiti vyavasthā kriyata iti vācyam |
tasyoktisahatve mayā (mayāpi) hetuśarīre tathaiva niveśaḥ kartuṃ śakya eva tadeva na sambhavati |
duḥkhādeḥ sākṣivedyatvena tvayā mayā'pi tatrāntaḥkaraṇavṛtteranaṅgīkārāt |
aṅgīkāre vā yogināmapi paraduḥkhaviṣayakāntaḥkaraṇavṛttirūpasākṣātkārādivyavahārabhājanasya pratyakṣasya sattvena teṣāmapi duḥkhānubhavitvāpatteḥ |
na ca pratyakṣe laukikaviṣayatāṃ vā tadatiriktavaijātyaṃ vā (kiñcit) yogīśvarādipratyakṣavyāvṛttaṃ duḥkhānubhavitṛpratyakṣe svīkṛtya tadavacchinnamevānusandhānamucyata iti vācyam |
duḥkhādeḥ sākṣivedyatvamiti niṣkṛṣṭasiddhānte gatyabhāvāt |
aṅgīkāravādapakṣe'pyantaḥkaraṇavṛtterantaḥkaraṇadharmatvena jīvasya tadvattvarūpatadanusandhātṛtvāyogācca |
svāmitāsambandhena tadvattvasyaiva tathātve sarvasya vaśī sarvasyeśāna ityādiśrutirīśvarasya svāmitāyā bhavadaṅgīkṛtatayā tasya duḥkhitvaṃ durvāram |
anyādhīnasvāmitvameva tathātvaṃ cenmamāpi hetuśarīre tadeva niviṣṭamastu |

na ca lakṣmyāstvanmate duḥkhitvāpattiḥ |
tatsvarūpabhūtaparakīyaduḥkhapratyakṣasyeśvarādhīnasvarūpabhūtasvāmitāsambandhena lakṣmyāmapi jīva iva viśeṣabalena sattvāditi vācyam |
tavāpi katham ? anekaśrutipurāṇasiddheśvarādhīnetarasvāmitvanirduḥkhatvayorhātumaśakyatvāt |
nanu paramārthato mayā kimapi nāsatyāpātata eva sarvavyavasthā'ṅgīkriyata iti nātikṣodo madvyavasthāyāmiti vācyam |
tathātve sarvatrāpi kṣodasya parityāgāpattyā tava vicārasyaiva vaiyyarthyāpātāt |
bhāskareṇa sarvaprapañcasatyatāmabhyupagacchatā'vaśyaṃ vyavasthāyāḥ kartavyatvācca |
uktānumānayostu taṃ pratyeva mukhyataḥ pravṛttiḥ |
uktaṃ hyanyatra ṭīkākāraiḥ -- `māyāvādinā sarvasya kalpitatvaṅgīkārātkālpanikī vyavasthā ghaṭetāpi na tu bhāskarasyātastaṃ pratīdaṃ duḥkhānusandhānādyāpādana'mityādi |
upādhikhaṇḍane'pi bhaskaramatanirāsāvasara evedaṃ dūṣāṇamabhihitam |
evaṃ tattvodyote |

gītātātparye'pi satyopādhipakṣa evāparasya sukhaduḥkhādyanusandhānaprasaṅgādidūṣaṇānyuktvā mithyopādhipakṣe'pyete doṣā bhavantyevetyuktatvāttadanusāreṇa mithyātvavādinaṃ pratyapi duḥkhānusandhānādivyavasthāyātrāpādanaprasaktiḥ |

yādṛśadeśādivṛttitvaviśiṣṭahetunā yādṛśadeśādiṣu sādhyānumitirānubhāvikītattatsthalīyavyāptistādṛ'śadeśādikaṃ niveśya nirvācyā |
yatra kevalānvayini sādhyābhāvo'prasiddhastatsthale taddhaṭitā vyāptiraprāmāṇikapratiyogikābhāvavādināṃ sulabhaiva |
saddhetusthale sarvatra tādṛśayogyatā vartate |
vyabhicārādyanupalambhādanukūlatarkasahitapratyakṣādipramāṇaistanniścayasya sarvalokānubhavasiddhatvāt |
kvacidbralavanmantrauṣadhādikāraṇaviśeṣāddhetuvyaktiviśeṣasya sādhyābhāvavadvṛttitvaprāptāvapi pramāṇena (hetusāmānye) siddhā tādṛśayogyatā nāpanīyate |
yadyapīyaṃ vyaktiḥ sādhyābhāvādhikaraṇāvṛttitvasambandhayogyaiva tathā'pi balavatkāraṇavaśādasyāḥ sādhyābhāvasambandhaḥ prāptaḥ sarvalokānubhavāt |
anyathā bhojanādirūpasāmagrīsattve'pi balavanmantrauṣadhādivaśāt tṛptyabhāve'pi tṛptiyogyamevedaṃ balavanmantroṣadhādibalāttṛptyanupādhāyakaṃ jātamiti sarvavyavahārasyāpyaprāmāṇyāpātāt |
na coktapratītivyavahārayoḥ prāmāṇye balavadbādhakamasti yeneṣṭāpattiḥ syāt |
yogyatā ca tattatsvabhāvabhūtastattanniṣṭhapadārthaviśeṣaḥ |
sa ca tattanniṣṭhatādṛśavṛttitvādhikaraṇatāṃ prati prayojako bhavati |
kuto dhūmasyaiva vahnyabhāvavadavṛttitvaṃ na ghaṭādīnāmiti praśne tatsvabhāva eva niyāmaka iti sarvairapi vaktavyatvāt |
svabhāvasya ca `svabhāvajīvakarmāṇi dravyaṃ kālaḥ śrutiḥ kriyā |
yatprasādādime santi na santi yadupekṣaye'ti pramāṇabalādīśvarādhīnatvāt |
na coktādhikaraṇatā sākṣādīśvaraniyamyā'stu kiṃ (madhye) svabhāvarūpaśaktermadhyetanniyāmakatvamalpaneneti vācyam |
bhagavacchakterbāhyakāraṇadvāraiva padārthaprayojakasya bahuśo darśanāt |
anyathā daṇḍe'pi ghaṭānukūlaśakterapyevaṃ pratyākhyānaprasaṅgāt |
na ca dhūmatvameva tādṛśayogyatārūpamastyeveti vācyam |
dhūmatvasyālokādiniṣṭhatādṛśādhikaraṇatānyūnavṛttitvena tatprayojakatvābhāvāt |
pratyutādhūmatvādayaṃ tādṛśāvṛttitvayogya iti pratītyā tādṛśayogyatājñāpakatayaiva pratītestato'tiriktatvāt |
itthaṃ ca sva(svā)bhāvamapyabhibhūya balavatkāraṇādhīnatādṛśavṛttitvavatyapi (hetau) tādṛśayogyatāsattvānna sambhavaḥ |
tata eva vyāptijñānasya na pratibandho virodhyaviṣakatvāt |
tādṛśayogyatābhāvajñānasyaiva virodhitvāt |
viṣayābādhācca na tatsthalīyavyāptijñānasya bhramatvam |
na ca tasya pramātve bādhānavatāradaśāyāmapavādaviṣaye jāyamānānumitiḥ pramā syāt |
saliṅgaparāmarśasya bhramānumitijanakatvābhāvāditi vācyam |
sādhyābhāvavadviṣayakatvarūpadoṣāhitabhramajananaśaktyā bhrabhajanakatvasambhavāt |
naiyyāyikaiḥ pṛthivītvarūpasalliṅgakaparāmarśajanyāyā apyutpattikṣaṇāvacchinnaghaṭe gandhānumiterbhramarūpāyā aṅgīkṛtatvāt |
nanvapavādaviṣayahetubhinnatvameva vyāpyatāvacchedakakoṭau niveśyatāṃ kimuktaprayāseneti cenna |
aindriyakānaindriyakamantrauṣadhādirūpāpavādānāṃ bhāvikālīnānāṃ vartamānakālīnānāṃ cābhāvasya niścayāyogena tatsamavahitabhedaviśiṣṭatvāvacchinnasya niścetumaśakyatvena tadrūpāvacchinnahetau vyāptigrahānupapatteḥ |
apavādādisphūrtyabhāvakāle vyāptipramānutpattyāpatteśca |
yatra yatrāpavādasya pravṛttistattadbhedakūṭasyāsarvajñadurgrahatayā tadajñānināmasarvajñānāṃ vyāptipramāvilayāpātāt |
na ceṣṭāpattiḥ |
keṣucidbhojaneṣu dṛṣṭidoṣādinā maraṇādijanakatvadarśanātkeṣucinmantrauṣadhādibalāttṛptyajanakatvadarśanācca (bhojanamātrasya) balavadaniṣṭājanakatvaviśiṣṭeṣṭasādhanatvavyāpyavattājñānādbhojane tadanumitipūrvakaṃ pravartamānānāmevaṃ bhojanamātrasya tṛptivyāpyatājñānāt bhojanaliṅgena tṛptyanumitimatāmasarvajñānāṃ bhramatvaprasaṅgaḥ |
na ca tatreṣṭāpattiḥ |
prekṣāvatā svoktanirvāhārthameva kartuṃ śakyate |
nānābhedakūṭaviśiṣṭahetujñānatvena kāraṇatve kāraṇatāvacchedakasyātiguruśarīratvaṃ syādityuktarītireva samyak |
svarasavyabhicārasthale'pyevaṃ syāditi nāpādyam |
pramāṇabalenoktayogyatārūpavyāptisiddhyanantaramevoktarītyāśrayaṇāt |
tasmātsudhoktaṃ yuktameva |
tadvākyasthavyabhicārapadamapi sādhyābhāvavadvṛttitvaparameva na tūktayogyatvābhāvaparam |
ato'pavādaviṣaye vyabhicāra eva nāstīti tadaṅgīkāreṇa dūṣaṇatvakathanamasaṅgatamiti śaṅkānakāśa iti |
nanu tathā'pīdamanumānamuyuktam |
āśrayatvarūpadharmitvenānugatarūpeṇa niviṣṭatvena ghaṭarūpadharmisamānasattākavyāvahārikabhedasya jīvādau siddhatvenānumānadvaye'pi siddhasādhanataiva |
tattaddharmiṇāṃ viśiṣya sādhyaśarīre niveśe punaḥ siddhasādhanataiva |
brahmarūpadharmiṇo viśiṣya niveśe tatsamānasattākabhedāprasiddhyā punaraprasiddhaviśeṣaṇataiva |
svadharmīti niveśe'pi svapadena brahmajīvamātradharmikajaḍabhedagrahaṇe pūrvavadaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇataiva |
ghaṭādimātradharmikacidbhedagrahaṇe jīveṣu brahmaṇi cābhāvenānumānadvaye bādha eva |
cetanajaḍobhayasādhāraṇabhedamādāya siddhasādhanatā durvāraiveti cenna |
prācīnamate yathākathañcitsvatvasyānugatatvena svatvena (bheda)niveśe sādhyāprasiddhivirahāt |
parantu brahmajaḍobhayasādhāraṇabhedamādāya siddhasādhanatā(di)vāraṇāya tattaddharmikabhedasya vastusvarūpatayobhayaniṣṭhaikabhedo nāstīti svapakṣa evāśrayaṇīyaḥ |
svatvena sādhye niveśasthale'prasiddhasyāpi sādhyasya pakṣe siddhiprakārastu dvaitadyumaṇau darśitastatraivānusandheyaḥ |
ādhunikanaiyyāyikādimanaḥsamādhānaṃ tu svavṛttitvasattāsamānasattākatvobhayasambandhena dharmiviśiṣṭabheda eva sādhyaḥ |
ghaṭavṛttibhede ubhayasambandhena ghaṭarūpadharmivaiśiṣṭyasya sattvena sādhyaprasiddhiḥ |
sapakṣe coktasādhyasiddhyā pāramārthikabhedasya siddhiḥ |
anyavṛttitvaviśiṣṭasyānyatrāvṛttitvavadādheyatāsambandhenānyaviśiṣṭasyāpyanyatra vṛtterabhāvādubhayasambandhena dharmiviśiṣṭasādhyasya pakṣe sattāyāḥ pakṣībhūtajīvabrahmarūpadharmiṇamādāyaivopapānīyatayā pāramārthikatvasyāvaśyaṃ prāpteḥ |
bhedasya dharmibhedena bhinnatvamekatvaṃ vā'stu na kācitkṣatiḥ |
uktobhayatvāvacchinnasya saṃsargatayābhānamapramāṇikamityaṅgīkāre tu pāramārthikavṛttitvapāramārthikānyatvobhayābhāvatvameva prakṛte dharmisattāsamānasattākatvena vivakṣitam |
itthaṃ ca tādṛśobhayābhāvavadbhedavattvaṃ sādhyam |
yadvā pāramārthikānyanirūpitatvapāramārthikavṛttitvobhayābhāvavadbhedādhikaraṇatvaṃ sādhyam |
bhedasyāśrayabhedena bhedānaṅgīkāre paṭavṛttighaṭabhede jīvādivṛttitvasyāpi sattvena tādṛśobhayābhāvasya tatrāsattve'pyacetanamātravṛtticetanabhede pāramārthikavṛttitvābhāvādubhayābhāvatvaṃ prasiddham |
adhikaraṇatāyāḥ sādhyatvapakṣe bhedasyāśrayabhedena bhedābhāve'pi tadadhikaraṇatānāmāśrayabhedena bhinnatvāt |
ghaṭādibhedanirūpitapaṭādiniṣṭhādhikaraṇatāsu pāramārthikavṛttitvābhāvadubhayābhāvatvaṃ prasiddham |
tathā ca nāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā |
pakṣe uktasādhyaṃ tu bhedasya pāramārthikatvaṃ vinā na paryavasyati |
bhede tadadhikaraṇatve vā pāramārthikavṛttitvasyaiva sattvenāpāramārthikatvābhāvaprayuktasya vā pāramārthikanirūpitatvābhāvaprayuktasya vobhayābhāvasya vaktavyatvāditi rītyā kartavyamityeṣā dik |
nanvatra lakṣmyā jīvatvābhāvenāpakṣatvād yathākathañcidalpaśaktitvādinā tasyā api pakṣakoṭau niveśe duḥkhānusandhātṛtvarūpahetvabhāvena bhāgāsiddhiprasaṅagāt sapakṣatve tatra hetvabhāvādasādhāraṇyamiti cenna |
pakṣatve'pi tatsādhāraṇānusandhātṛtvasyaiva hetutvāṅgīkāreṇa bhāgāsiddhivirahāt |
tadvyāvṛttahetupariṣkārapakṣe tasyāḥ pakṣabahirbhāve'pi tanmātre uktasādhyaniścaye bījābhāvena sandigdhasādhyavattayā pakṣatulyatvena sapakṣatvābhāvena tatra hetoravṛttāvapyasādhāraṇyavirahāt |
tatra sādhyasiddherapekṣitatve etadanumānasiddhajīva dṛṣṭāntenaivālpaśaktikatvādinoktasādhyasya sādhayituṃ śakyatvāt na doṣaḥ |
vyatirekadṛṣṭāntabhūte brahmaṇi hetvabhāvo na siddha ityāśaṅkya niṣedhati || brahmaṇaśceti || yadvā brahmānanusaṃhitatvaghaṭitādyānumānahetorbrahmapakṣakānumānahetoścāsiddhimāśaṅkya nirākaroti || brahmaṇaśceti || apuruṣārthatvaprasaṅgāditi || saṃsāradaśāyāṃ svakīyaduḥkhamātrabhoktṛtvaṃ brahmabhāve tu tattādātmyāpanna sakalajīvaduḥkhabhoktṛtvāpattyā mukteratyantaheyatāprasaṅgaśca jñātavyaḥ || nirañjanatvaśrutīti |
nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaitīti śrutyā nirañjanapadena duḥkhabhoktṛtvādi sakaladoṣarāhityoktestadvirodha iti bhāvaḥ |
pañcamyantadvamavaśyambhāve hetuḥ || nāsiddhiriti || uktāvatārikādvayānurodhena na dṛṣṭāntāsiddhiḥ hetvasiddhirityarthadvayamanusandheyam |
|| anumānāni ceti || bheda ityādiḥ |
ucyanta iti śeṣaḥ |
yadyapi pūrvapakṣakrameṇa sāmānyata evātmanānātvasādhakānumānamādau vaktavyam |
tathāpi pradhānasiddhau sutarāṃ guṇasiddhiriti bhāvenāha -- jīva iti || atra duḥkheti svarūpakīrtanam |
na tu hetughaṭakam |
evaṃ ca nityasukhādyanusandhātari brahmaṇi vyabhicāravāraṇāya brahmānanusaṃhiteti sārthakam |
na ca brahmaṇaḥ sarvajñatvena brahmānanusaṃhitāprasiddhiḥ |
svīyatvenānubhavasyaivānusandhānatvāt |
svīyatvaṃ ca svanaicyādyāpādakatvam |
na tu svadharmatvam |
ato nāntaḥkaraṇavṛttiduḥkhāsaṅgrahaḥ |
etallābhārthaṃ ca duḥkhapadamiti dhyeyam |
yadyapi arjunānanusaṃhitakiñcitsukhādyanusandhāturindrasya tadabhedavadatrāpyabhedopapatteraprayojakatvam |
tathāpi bhedatatpratinidhiviśeṣobhayasādhāraṇabhedavyavahāraviṣayamātrasya sādhyatvānna doṣaḥ |
jīvabrahmaṇorbādhakābhāvena bhedasyaiva siddheḥ |
svābhāvikaviśeṣasiddhyā'pi paranivṛtteśca |
atrānvayavyāptau dṛṣṭāntābhāvādāha -- vyatirekeṇeti || yadyapi prathamamīśvaradharmikabheda eva pratyakṣasyoktatvādanumānamapi tatraivādau vaktumucitam |
tathāpi jīvadharmikabhedasya jīvaprayakṣasiddhatvena tatrānumānavyutpādanasaukaryāttadevādāvuktam |
pūrvapakṣakrameṇa ca pūrvamīśvaradharmikabhede pratyakṣaṃ pradarśitam |
pūrvapakṣiṇā ca prādhānyāttadevādau pratikṣiptamiti nāsaṅgatiḥ |
|| brahma veti || atra jīvatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedaḥ sādhyaḥ |
tena jīvapratiyogikabhedasya jīvasādharaṇye'pi na vyatirekāprasiddhiḥ |
tattajjīvapratiyogikabheda eva sādhya ityapyāhuḥ |
atrāpi hetau duḥkhapadaṃ svarūpakīrtanameva |
na caivaṃ jīvānusaṃhitaduḥkhābhāvānusandhātṛtvasya brahmaṇyapi sattvādasiddhiriti vācyam |
duḥkhadhvaṃsaprāgabhāvayoreva jīvānusaṃhitatvāt |
īśvarasya ca duḥkhātyantābhāvānusandhātṛtvena doṣābhāvāt |
evaṃ cānanusandhātṛtvamātrasya brahmaṇyabhāvādasiddhirato jīvānusaṃhiteti sārthakam |
na ca vyabhicārāvārakatvāttadvaiyarthyamiti śaṅkyam |
vyabhicāravadasiddherapyanumitivirodhitvāviśeṣeṇa vimataṃ kartrajanyaṃ śarīrājanyatvādityādau śarīrādiviśeṣaṇavadasiddhivārakatve'pyavaiyarthyasya śāstre samarthitatvāt |
akhaṇḍābhāvasampādakatvenāvaiyarthyaṃ cobhayatra tulyam |
kecittu duḥkhānusandhātṛtvatadabhāvayorevātra hetutvam |
duḥkhe brahmānanusaṃhitatvādyabhidhānaṃ tu vyatirekadṛṣṭānte hetvabhāvasattvapradarśanaparamityāhuḥ |
taccintyam |
tatpradarśanasya duḥkhādīnāmityagrimagranthenaiva lābhena brahmānanusaṃhitatvādyabhidhānavaiyarthyasya durvāratvāt |
yadyapi dvitīyānumāne ghaṭāderanvayadṛṣṭāntatvaṃ sambhavatyeva |
tathāpi anvayadārḍhyāyaiva vyatirekeṇa jīvavadityuktam |
yadyapi ca prathame brahmānusaṃhitasārvajñādyananusandhātṛtvasya, dvitīye jīvānanusaṃhitasārvajñādyanusandhātṛtvasya ca hetutvaṃ sambhavati, tathāpi paramate śuddhasyānusandhātṛtvābhāvādasiddhiḥ syādato na tadanusaraṇam |
na caivaṃ prathame brahmānusaṃhitāprasiddhyā parasya hetvaprasiddhiriti vācyam |
brahmānanusaṃhitetyanena brahmaniṣṭhābhāvapratiyogyanusandhānaviṣayatvasya vivakṣitatvena doṣābhāvāt |
atra sādhyaśarīre dharmisattāsamānasattākatvaviśeṣaṇavyāvṛttiṃ vyañjayan pāramārthikatvena bhedaviśeṣaṇāviśeṣaṇayoḥ pūrvapakṣoktasādhyāprasiddhisiddhasādhanarūpadoṣānavakāśaṃ darśayati -- atreti || etadanumānadvaya ityarthaḥ |
asya ca `nāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ' `na siddhasādhana'mityābhyāmanvayaḥ |
atha sattātvādinā brahmasattāsamānasattākavyāvahārikabhedamādāya siddhasādhanavāraṇāya pāramārthikatvenaiva samānatvaṃ vivakṣaṇīyam |
tathā ca paraṃ prati sādhyāprasiddhistadavasthaiva |
pāramārthikatvavyāvahārikatvānyatararūpeṇa samānatvoktau tu siddhānte vyāvahārikatvāprasiddhyā sādhyāprasiddhiḥ |
sattātvavyāpyarūpeṇa tadvivakṣaṇe ca pāramārthikavyāvahārikānyataratvena brahmasattāsamānatvamādāya siddhasādhanam |
yattu samānatvamabheda eva |
siddhānte bhedasya dharmisvarūpatvena tatra dharmisattātiriktasattāvirahāditi |
tanna |
paramate ghaṭādau sādhyaprasiddhyanupapatteḥ |
maivam |
tadadhikaraṇayāvatkālavṛttitvaṃ tatsamānatvamityaṅgīkārāt |
vyāvahārikasya vyavahāranivṛttikālāvṛttitvena siddhasādhanādyanavakāśāt |
yadvā , brahmasattābhinnatvatadabhinnatvānyatararūpeṇa samānatvaṃ vivakṣaṇīyam |
brahmasattābhinnatvena dharmisattāsamānasattākabhedasya ghaṭādau prasiddhatvānnāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam |
brahmaṇi brahmasattābhinnatvena brahmasattāsamānatvāsambhavena tadabhinnatvena tatsamānasattākabhedaḥ siddhyan brahmābhinnaḥ pāramārthika eva siddhyatīti dik |
atra ca ghaṭādisattāsamānasattākabhedasya brahmaṇyabhyupagamāt siddhasādhanamato dharmīti |
yadyapi ghaṭādermeyatvādikaṃ prati dharmitvamastyeva |
tathāpi svadharmitvaṃ vivakṣitam |
svapadasya ca tattadbhedaparatvānna doṣaḥ |
na caivamātmadharmikabhedasya paramate svadharmisattāsamānasattākatvābhāvāt sādhyāprasiddhiriti vācyam |
sajātīyasādhyasya ghaṭādau prasiddhyaivānumānanirvāhāt |
anyathā pakṣīyavahneḥ prāgasiddhatvenānumānocchedaprasaṅgāt |
vastutaḥ svaniṣṭhatvasvaniṣṭhasattāsamānasattākatvobhayasambandhena dharmiviśiṣṭo bhedaḥ sādhyaḥ |
evaṃ ca sādhyasyānugatatvāddhaṭādau tatprasiddhiḥ sulabhaiva |
etena bhedasyānugatatvapakṣe ghaṭādisattāsamasattākataddharmikabhedasyaivātmanyabhyupagame'pi na siddhasādhanāvakāśaḥ |
guṇādau dravyavṛttitvaviśiṣṭasattāyā iva ghaṭādivṛttitvaviśiṣṭabhedasyātmanyabhāvāditi |
nanu jñānasukhaduḥkhādīnāmaikādhikaraṇyaniyamena duḥkhānadhikaraṇe jīve tadanusandhānasyāpyabhāvāt prathamānumāne svarūpasiddhiḥ |
anyathā brahmaṇo'pyaviśeṣeṇa duḥkhānusandhānāpattyā dvitīye taddoṣa ityata āha -- duḥkhādīnāmiti || vṛttyātmakānāmeva jñānasukhādīnāmaikadhikaraṇyaniyamāt tadanusandhānasya ca sākṣipratyakṣatvena jīvagatatve bādhakābhāvānna prathame'siddhiḥ |
brahmaṇaśca duḥkhānusandhātṛtve tadbhāvaṃ prāptasyāpi tadāpattyā, muktitvena parāṅgīkṛtabrahmabhāvasya puruṣārthatvānupapatteḥ `niraniṣṭaḥ' ityādiśrutyaprāmāṇyaprasaṅgācca brahmaṇo duḥkhānanusandhātṛtvāvaśyakatvānna dvitīye'pi doṣa iti bhāvaḥ |
evaṃ jīveśayorjīvānāṃ ca bhede pratyakṣapramāṇamabhidhāyānumānānyāha -- anumānāni ceti || bhede santīti śeṣaḥ |
tatra tāvadbrahmapratiyogikajīvadharmikabhede'numānamāha -- jīva iti || dharmisattāsamānasattāketi || dharmī jīvaḥ |
tasya yādṛśī sattā tatsamānasattāko yo bhedaḥ tadadhikaraṇamityarthaḥ |
brahmānanusaṃhitaṃ yadduḥkhaṃ tadanusandhātṛtvāt |
tadanubhuvitṛtvādityarthaḥ |
atrānusandhānaśabdoktānubhavo nāma duḥkhādau svātmīyatvābhimānenātmani tatkṛtanīcoccatālakṣaṇā vikriyaiva natu jñānamātram |
tathāca sarvajñasya brahmaṇaḥ kathaṃ duḥkhānubhavābhāva iti na śaṅkyam |
anvayadṛṣṭāntābhāvādāha -- vyatireketi || brahmaṇi brahmapratiyogikabhedābhāvena sādhyābhāvo duḥkhānusandhātṛtvasyābhāvena hetvabhāvaśca draṣṭavyaḥ |
idānīṃ jīvapratiyogikeśvaradharmikabhede'numānamāha -- brahma veti || atra dharmi brahma |
śiṣṭaṃ pūrvavat |
jīvānusaṃhitam |
jīvenānusaṃhitam anubhūtaṃ yadduḥkhaṃ tadananusandhātṛtvāt |
tadananubhavitṛtvādityarthaḥ || vyatirekeṇeti || jīve jīvapratiyogikabhedābhāvena sādhyābhāvaḥ |
jīvānusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvasyaiva vidyamānatvena tadananusandhātṛtvābhāvāddhetvabhāvaśca draṣṭavyaḥ |
atrānumānadvaye kiṃ pāramārthikabhedaḥ sādhyaḥ uta vyāvahārikaḥ |
nādyaḥ |
aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt |
nadvitīyaḥ |
siddhasādhanāditi śaṅkāparihārāya sādhyadvaye dharmisattāsamānasattāketi viśeṣaṇamupāttam |
tena kathaṃ doṣadvayaparihāra ityataḥ krameṇa tadubhayaparihāraṃ darśayati -- atretyādinā na siddhasādhanamityantena || ghaṭo brahma neti pratītibalāt ghaṭe brahma pratiyogikabhedastāvadasti |
tasya dharmī ghaṭaḥ tasya yādṛśī sattā vyāvahārikī tatsamānasattākatvaṃ prasiddhameva māyāvādinaḥ |
ato nādyānumāne'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam |
tathā ghaṭo jīvo neti pratītibalāt ghaṭe jīvabhedaḥ siddhaḥ |
tasya ghaṭarūpadharmisamānasamattākatvaṃ prasiddhameveti na dvitīyānumāne'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam |
tathā jīvasya dharmiṇaḥ pāramārthikatvena tatsamasattākatvena sidhyanbhedaḥ pāramārthika eva sidhyatīti nādyānumāne siddhasādhanam |
brahmaṇo dharmiṇaḥ pāramārthikatvena tatsamasattākatvena sidhyanbhedaḥ pāramārthika eva sidhyatīti na dvitīyānumāne siddhasādhanamityarthaḥ |
nanu duḥkhasyāntaḥkaraṇavṛttitvājjīve duḥkhameva nāstīti tadanusandhātṛtvasyāpyabhāvāddhetoḥ svarūpāsiddhirityata āha -- duḥkhādīnāmiti || anusandhātṛtvādityasya nāsiddhiriti vakṣyamāṇenānvayaḥ |
jīvasya duḥkhānusandhāne duḥkhasya jīvagatatvaṃ na prayokajakam |
jīvāvartino'pi ghaṭāderanusandhānadarśanāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu brahmaṇo'pi duḥkhānusandhātṛtve bādhakābhāvāddvitīyahetoḥ svarūpāsiddhirityata āha -- brahmaṇa iti || māyāvādimate jīvasya brahmabhāva eva mokṣa ityaṅgīkriyate |
tatra yadi brahmaṇo'pi duḥkhānusandhānantarhi tadbhāvāpannajīvasyāpi tatsyāt |
tathāca tādṛśamokṣasya puruṣairarthyatvābhāvena puruṣaprayojanatvaṃ na syādityarthaḥ || nirañjaneti || niraniṣṭo niravadya ityādinirduḥkhatvādipratipādakaśrutivirodhādityarthaḥ || tadabhāveti || duḥkhānusandhātṛtvābhāvetyarthaḥ |
nanvatra jīvaśabdenānavacchinnacaitanyavivakṣāyāṃ tasya duḥkhānanusandhātṛtvenāsiddhiprasaṅgādantaḥkaraṇena vā'jñānena vā'vacchinnacaitanyaṃ vivakṣaṇīyam |
na ca tadyuktam |
avacchinnasya mithyātvena tatsattāsamānasattākatvena sidhyato bhedasya pāramārthikatvāsiddhiriti cet kastarhi tavāpi jīvaśabdārthaḥ |
avacchinnameveti cet tarhi jīvasya muktirmithyā syāt |
nanu jīvabhāva eva mithyā caitanyaṃ tu satyameveti cenna yaccaitanye (yatra caitanye) mithyābhūto jīvabhāvastatsatyameva |
tadeva caitanyaṃ jīvaśabdenāsmābhirvivakṣitamiti santoṣṭavyam |
tadeva caitanyaṃ duḥkhānusandhātriti nāsiddhiḥ |
duḥkhānusandhātṛtvaṃ mithyaiveti cetkimanenākāṇḍatāṇḍavena |
na hyasmābhissatyatvaṃ (tatsatyatvaṃ) hetau viśeṣaṇīkṛtam |
nanu jīvabrahmaṇorbhedasiddheḥ pūrvaṃ kathaṃ pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāva iti cenna |
tvayā'bhyupagatena vyāvahārikabhedenaiva pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāvopapatteḥ ||
nanvatrādyānumāne dharmisamasattāketi bhedasya viśeṣaṇe'pi na bhavadabhimatapāramārthikatvasiddhirityāśayena śaṅkate -- nanvatretyādinā |
paramukhenaiva samādhiprakāraṃsūcayituṃ pṛcchati -- kastarhīti |
tava jīvabrahmaṇorabheda iti vadataḥ |
mata iti śeṣaḥ || avacchinnamevetīti |
antaḥkaraṇādyavacchinnaṃ caitanyamevetyarthaḥ || tarhīti |
avacchinnasya mithyātvāṅgīkārānmithyābhūtasya ca brahmabhāvarūpasatyamuktyayogājjīvasya śāstreṣūcyamānā muktirmithyā syāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvavacchinnasya jīvatve'pi tasya mithyātve'pi na tasyocyamānā muktirmithyā''padyate |
jīvaśabdārthasya sarvasyāpi mithyātve hi tathā syāt |
na caivam |
yatra caitanye jīvabhāva āropitastaccaitanyaṃ satyameva |
tasya ca muktiḥ sambhavatveveti bhāvena śaṅkate -- nanviti || tarhi vayamapi vadāmastadeva caitanyaṃ jīvaśabda vācyam |
yatra jīvabhāva āropita iti brūṣa iti bhāvena samādhatte || yatra caitanya iti || tathā ca dharmipadena tādṛśacaitanyasyaiva lābhena tatsamasattākatvasiddhau bhedasya pāramārthikatvameva sidhyatīti bhāvaḥ |
nanvevaṃsatyacaitanyasyaiva jīvaśabdārthatvena vivakṣitatve tasya manmate duḥkhānusandhātṛtvābhāvena hetorasiddhiḥ syādityata āha -- tadeveti || āropitajīvabhāvaṃ caitanyamityarthaḥ |
tasya ca duḥkhānusandhātṛtvaṃ tvayā'pyaṅgīkṛtameveti bhāvaḥ |
nanvaṅgīkṛtamapi tasya duḥkhānusandhātṛtvaṃ manmate na satyam |
kintu mithyaiveti kathaṃ nāsiddhirhetoriti śaṅkate -- duḥkhānusandhātṛtvamiti || mithyaivāropitamevetyarthaḥ |
āropitamapyanusandhātṛtvaṃ caitanye tvayā'ṅgīkṛtameveti tasya satyatānaṅgīkāramātreṇa kathamasiddhiḥ |
na hi tatra satyatvaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ dattamasmābhiriti bhāvena pratyāha -- kimaneneti || akāṇḍatāṇḍaveneti || vādyādivirahitanartanatulyenānupayuktena tanmithyātvavarṇanenetyarthaḥ || pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāva iti || ādyānumāne jīvasya pakṣatvaṃ brahmaṇo dṛṣṭāntatvam |
dvitīyānumāne brahmaṇaḥ pakṣatvaṃ jīvasya dṛṣṭāntatvamityarthaḥ |
nanvatreti || anumānadvaye'pītyarthaḥ || antaḥkaraṇena veti || ajñānamātrasya jīvabhāvaṃ pratyaprayojakatvādanusandhānaṃ pratyanupayogācca pakṣāntaraparigrahaḥ |
pareṇaiva parihāraṃ vācayati -- kastarhīti |
vaidikavyavahāreṣviti śeṣaḥ || muktirmithyā syāditi || avacchinnasya mithyātvena svarūpata eva tasya bādhyatve kasya muktiriti bhāvaḥ |
santoṣṭavyamiti || uktānumānadūṣaṇāya vṛthā vyathā kāryeti bhāvaḥ |
nanvatrāpyuktaṃ dūṣaṇamityata āha -- tadeveti || satyacaitanye mithyābhūto jīvabhāva āropyata iti tvayaivoktatvāt |
duḥkhānusandhātṛtvasyaiva jīvaśabdārthatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
śuddhasya mokṣānaṅgīkāre duḥkhānusandhānānvayo'pi tasyaiva |
anyathā vaiyyadhikaraṇyāditi bhāvaḥ || akāṇḍeti || avicchinnetyarthaḥ || kathamiti || anyonyāśrayāditi bhāvaḥ |
vyāvahāriketi || pratibhāsikādvailakṣaṇyamātreṇa pareṇa vyavahārāṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ |
vastutastu sandigdhenāpi bhedena pṛthivī itarebhyo bhidyata ityādāviva pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāvopapattiḥ |
asya grāhyasandehatvenāpratibandhakatvādityādyūhyam |
pratyadhikaraṇaṃ brahmabhedasyānyatve mānābhāvena jīvagatasya vyāvahārikabrahmabhedasya vyāvahārikaghaṭe'pi sattvena ghaṭāpekṣayā dharmisattāsamānasattākatvena punastenānyathāsiddhiḥ |
|| nanvatreti || prathamānumāne pakṣavācakajīvaśabdenetyarthaḥ || pāramārthikatvāsiddhiriti || tathāca siddhasādhanamiti bhāvaḥ |
gūḍhābhisandhiḥ pṛcchati -- kastarhīti |
jīvaniṣṭhā muktirmithyā syāt |
na hi mithyābhūtadharminiṣṭho mithyātvatatsamaśīladharmātirikto dharmo na mithyeti yujyata ityarthaḥ |
yadvā jīvasya muktiriti vākyaṃ mithyā syāt |
aprāmāṇyāt |
yadi jīvo mithyābhūtastadā brahmajñānena tannivṛttiḥ syāt |
tathā ca brahmajñānena jīvo mucyata iti vākyamapramāṇaṃ syāt |
muktyādhārasya jīvasyābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ || nanviti || tasya muktyādhāratvānnoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ |
āśayamuddhāṭayati -- yatreti || nanu tasya duḥkhānusandhātṛtvābhāvādasiddhirityata āha -- tadeveti || upādherjaḍasyānusandhātṛtvāyogenopādhinā nimittena caitanya evānusandhātṛtvasya vācyatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
pakṣaniṣkarṣārthaṃ śaṅkate -- nanviti || anavacchinneti || avidyayā'ntaḥkaraṇopādhibhirvā'navacchinnetyarthaḥ |
prasaṅgāditi vivakṣaṇīyamityanenānveti tavāpīti |
`aduḥkhamitaratsarvaṃ jīvā eva tu duḥkhinaḥ |
teṣāṃ duḥkhaprahāṇāya śrutireṣā pravartata' ityādau duḥkhitvena muktatvarūpaduḥkhaprahāṇavattvena ca pratipādyate |
jīvaśabdārthaḥ sa ka ityarthaḥ || avacchinnamevetīti |
avidyayā'ntaḥkaraṇena cetyarthaḥ |
jīvaśabdoktānāmeva teṣāmiti parāmarśena muktirduḥkhaprahāṇarūpocyate |
sā mithyā syāt prātibhāsikī syāt |
idamuktaṃ bhavati |
avidyādyupādhyapagama eva jīvasya muktirūpaduḥkhanāśahetustadapagame tu viśeṣaṇanāśena tadviśiṣṭasvarūpātmakajīva eva naṣṭa iti jīvaniṣṭhamukterabhāvāduktaśrutirghaṭasya caitanyamiti vākyavadapramāṇameva syāditi || nanviti || jīvabhāva eveti || jīvatvaṃ caitanye āropitaṃ tattādātmyāpannaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ vetyarthaḥ |
|| caitanyaṃ tu satyamiti || tathā ca yo bālastasyaidānīṃ sthaviratvamityādivākyena yathā bālyopalakṣitapiṇḍa eva sthaviratvaṃ pratīyate na tadviśiṣṭe bādhāt |
tathā prakṛte teṣāmiti jīvatvopalakṣitānparāmṛśya muktirucyate |
tatra muktisambandhasyābādhānna tadvākyāprāmāṇyamiti bhāvaḥ |
paraṃ prati samādhiṃ vaktuṃ tena vivādaṃ kartumaśakyatvasthāpanāya gūḍhābhisandhinā yatpṛṣṭaṃ tadidānīmuddhāṭayati -- yatretyādinā |
jīvo jīvatvaviśiṣṭo mithyābhūta āropita iti vyutkrameṇoddeśyavidheyabhāvaḥ || taccaitanyamiti || tathā ca jīvatvopalakṣitañcaitanyameva pakṣaḥ |
jīvatvaviśiṣṭasya tādātmyena tatrādhyāsena taddharmabhūtajīvatvavattvasyāpi tatrādhyastatayā taddharmatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu jīvavattvasya svopahita eva sattvāttadvattvenopahitasyaiva pakṣatvaprāptyā tatroktasādhyatvābhyupagamātsiddhasādhanateti cenna |
śuddhe jīvavattvasaṃsargasyābhāve pūrvoktajīvamuktivacanāprāmāṇyāpattyā tasmiñjīvavattvasambandhasyāvaśyakatve svopahitapratiyogikena svopahitāvṛttinā tādātmyena jīvavattvasya pakṣatāvacchedakatve upahitavyāvṛtteḥ śuddhasaṅgrahācca |
upahite upahitapratiyogikatvaviśiṣṭatādātmyābhāvācchuddhe tatsattvācca |
tadupahitatvaṃ svaviṣayatāvyāpakatatprakārakatvarūpamevobhayamatānurodhānniveśyam |
ataḥ siddhānte viśiṣṭātiriktopahitāprasiddhāvapi na pakṣāsiddhiriti jñeyam |
tarhi caitanye duḥkhānusandhātṛtvamasiddhamityata āha -- tadeveti || jaḍasyānusandhānarūpajñānāyogāt |
anyathe`kṣaternāśabda'mityatrekṣatyāśrayatvena pradhānavyāvṛttyayogāt |
anusandhātṛtvamapi cetana eva vaktavyamamiti nāsiddhirityāśayaḥ |
asiddhyabhiprāyeṇa śaṅkate -- duḥkheti || mithyaiveti || svātyantābhāvavatyeva pratīyamānamityarthaḥ |
tathā cāsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
kiṃ vastuto mithyeti kṛtvā svarūpāsiddhirityabhiprāyaḥ? uta satyatvaṃ hetuviśeṣaṇamavaśyaṃ vācyam, anyathā''ropitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvamādāya brahmaṇyeva vyabhicārāpatterataḥ sādhanāprasiddhiriti ? || nādyaḥ |
tvanmate savānumānasthale'pyasiddherbādhasya ca durvāratayā'numānaprāmāṇyavārtocchedāpatteratastadvāraṇāya pratiyogivaiyyadhikaraṇyādikaṃ niveśyāsiddhibādhādilakṣaṇaṃ |
) pariṣkāryamiti kathamasiddhyudbhāvanamityāśayenāha -- kimaneneti || anavasare'nupayuktavacanenetyarthaḥ |
anumānadoṣakathanāvasare tadanupayuktamithyātvakathanamasaṅgatamiti bhāvaḥ |
dvitīyaṃ nirācaṣṭe || na hīti |
anusandhātṛtvaṃ caitanye vartata iti tava mama ca sammatam |
tatra mithyātvasatyatvaviṣaye punarāvayorvivādaḥ |
tatra vivādaviṣaye satyatvarūpaṃ vā mithyātvarūpaṃ vā dharmamaviśeṣaṇīkṛtyānusandhātṛtvamātrasya hetutvenopādānānna sādhanā prasiddhiranyataramatenodbhāvayituṃ śakyeti bhāvaḥ |
tvanmate āropitadhūmamādāya dhūmatvāvacchinnasya vahnivyabhicāravāraṇāya prātibhāsikavyāvṛttasya vyāvahārikadhūmamātravṛtterdhūmatvasya hetutāvacchekatvam |
manmate ca tasyāsattvena tatra dhūmatvasyaivābhāvāddhūmatvasya hetutāvacchedakatvam |
nanu dhūme satyatvaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ deyam |
tadvatprakṛte'pi brahmaṇyāropitānusandhātṛtvasya manmate'līkatvena tvanmate prātibhāsikatvena tamādāyāvayormate vyabhicāraprasaktyabhāvena kuto hetau satyatvaviśeṣaṇaṃ deyamiti bhāvaḥ || pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāveti || kevalapāramārthikadṛṣṭyā tava pravṛttau mama tava caikyena vādiprativādibhāvasyaivānupapattiḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ |
ādyānumāne pakṣaṃ pariṣkartumāśaṅkate -- nanviti || anavacchinneti || yadyapi yāvadviśeṣaṇānavacchinnasya bodhayitumaśakyatvānna pakṣatvasambhavaḥ |
siddhānte pakṣāprasiddhiśca |
tathāpyanavacchinnapadasyāntaḥkaraṇādyanavacchinnaparatayā tadanavacchinnatvena pakṣatve tātparyānna doṣaḥ |
bahujīvapakṣe'ntaḥkaraṇeneti, ekajīvapakṣe'jñāneneti |
avacchinnaṃ viśiṣṭam |
tasya ca mithyātvamapāramārthikatvaṃ, vyāvahārikatvamiti yāvat |
tathāca tatsattāsamānasattākabhedasya siddhatvāt siddhasādhanaṃ, pāramārthikabhedāsiddherarthāntaraṃ cetyāśayaḥ |
atra viśiṣṭaghaṭakaviśeṣyāṃśasyaiva pakṣatvamaṅgīkṛtya samādhāsyan uktasiddhasādhanādyabhāvasphuṭīkaraṇāya pṛcchati -- kastarhi taveti || avacchinnamiti || sāmānye napuṃsakam |
avacchinnaṃ caitanyamiti vā || mithyā syāditi || śaśaśṛṅgādivadasatī syādityarthaḥ |
jīvasya mithyātvenājñānavat pravilīnasya bahmabhāvāyogāditi bhāvaḥ |
tathāca niṣprayojanatvena vedāntānāmanārambhaṇīyatvaprasaṅga iti dhyeyam |
jīva śabdārthaviśiṣṭaghaṭakaviśeṣyāṃśasya brahmatvāpattireva jīvamuktiśabdārthaḥ |
tatra ca noktadoṣa ityāśaṅkate -- nanviti || jīvabhāvopalakṣitacaitanyasya pakṣatvānnārthāntarādītyāśayena samādhatte || yatreti || santoṣṭavyamityanenābhyupetyaivedamuktam |
vastuto viśiṣṭasyāpi satyatvameveti sūcitam || tadeveti || duḥkhānusandhātṛtvasya jīvabhāvaniyatatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvapāramārthikaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvasya brahmaṇyanaikāntyāt pāramārthikatvena heturviśeṣaṇīyaḥ |
tathāca paramate hetvasiddhirityāśaṅkate -- duḥkhānusandhātṛtvamiti || syādevaṃ yadi pāramārthikatvena heturviśeṣitaḥ syānna caivaṃ, prātibhāsikānaikāntyasyādoṣatvāt |
anyathā'numānamātrocchedāpatteriti bhāvenāha -- kimaneneti || anavasaratāṇḍavāyitenānenānuktopālambhena kimasmākamaniṣṭam ? na kimapītyarthaḥ |
athāyaṃ pūrvapakṣābhiprāyaḥ |
duḥkhānusandhātṛtvasya mithyātvānna pakṣadharmatvam |
satyānṛtayoḥ sambandhānupapatteḥ |
yadyapyadhyastādhiṣṭhānabhāvarūpaḥ sambandho'sti |
tathāpi nāsau hetutvaniyāmakaḥ |
siddhānte duḥkhānusandhātṛtvasyānāropitatvena tadanupapatteḥ |
kintvāśrayāśrayibhāvaḥ |
tathāca pareṣāmasiddhiriti |
atra brūmaḥ |
anusandhātṛtvasambandhena duḥkhavattvasyaivātra hetutvam |
anusandhātṛtvaṃ ca paramate svādhiṣṭhāna eva, siddhānte ca svādhāra eva duḥkhādervṛttiniyāmakamiti na ko'pi doṣaḥ |
|| kathaṃ pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāva iti || yadyapi prakṛte vādivipratipattyā bhedasya sandigdhatvānna sandigdhasādhyakatvādilakṣaṇapakṣatvānupapattirasti |
tathāpi pakṣatvasahitadṛṣṭāntatvānupapattervivakṣitatvānna doṣaḥ |
yadvā , bhedasiddheḥ pūrvamabhedaniścaye yathā jīvasya pakṣatvamanupapannaṃ tathā tatsandehe brahmaṇo dṛṣṭāntatvamanupapannamityarthe tātparyam |
ata eva bhedasiddheḥ pūrvamityapi saṅgacchate |
anyathā bhedasiddhyuttaramapi pakṣatvānupapattisattvena tadasaṅgateḥ |
atra jīve brahmabhedasandehe'pi brahmaṇi tadabhedāniścayānnoktadoṣaḥ |
na cābhede tadanupapattiḥ, vyāvahārikabhedenaiva tadupapatterityāśayena samādhatte || tvayeti || yadyapi kevalacaitanyabhedaḥ pareṇa nābhyupagataḥ |
tathāpi viśiṣṭabhedasya viśeṣyībhūtacaitanyapratiyogikatvamastīti sa eva pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāvopapādaka iti bhāvaḥ |
yadvā , sārvajñādiviśiṣṭacaitanyameva dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
tatpratiyogikabheda eva sādhya iti na kaściddoṣaḥ |
atha tathāpi jīvabrahmaṇorabhedavādināṃ brahmacaitanye'pi duḥkhānusandhātṛtvasatvādvyabhicāraḥ |
abhedasya vipratipattyā sandigdhatve'pi sandigdhānaikāntyaṃ durvāramiti cenna |
hetumati sādhyābhāvasandehādhīnavyabhicārasandehasyādoṣatvāt |
prakṛte ca tasya hetumati jīve brahmābhedasandehamūlatvāt anyathā pakṣīyavyabhicārasandehasya sārvatrikatvenānumānocchedaprasaṅgāditi |
jīvapakṣakānumāne dharmisamasattāketi viśeṣaṇe'pi na siddhasādhanatvaparihāra iti śaṅkate -- nanvitvādinā pāramārthikatvāsiddhirityantena || antaḥkaraṇeneti bahujīvavādimatena ajñānenetyekajīvavādimatasādhāraṇyeneti dhyeyam |
atra pratibandyā samādhatte || kastarhītyādinā santoṣṭavyamityantena || tava jīvasya brahmabhāvo muktiriti vadataḥ || tarhīti || yadyavacchinnaṃ jīvaśabdārthastarhi tasya mithyātvena mithyābhūtasya brahmajñānanivartyatvena brahmātmaikyāparokṣajñānasamaye jīvasyaivābhāvājjīvasya brahmabhāvo muktirityetanmṛṣā syādityarthaḥ |
nanu jīvaśabdārthabhūtamavidyādiviśiṣṭaṃ nasarvaṃ mithyā kintu viśeṣaṇabhāga eva |
viśeṣyabhāgasya tu satyatvena nivṛttyabhāvāttasya brahmabhāvarūpā muktiryuktaiveti śaṅkate -- nanviti || jīvabhāvaḥ jīvatvam || yatra caitanya iti || tathāca jīvasyapāramārthikatvena tatsamasattākatvena sidhyanbhedaḥpāramārthika eva sidhyatīti na siddhasādhanamiti bhāvaḥ |
mithyābhūto jīvabhāva iti paramatānuvādaḥ |
svamate jīvabhāvasya satyatvāditi dhyeyam |
nanu yadi siddhasādhanatāparihārāya satyacaitanyameva jīvaśabdārtho'ṅgīkriyate |
tarhi tasya duḥkhānusandhātṛtvābhāvātsvarūpāsiddhiḥ syādityata āha -- tadeveti || ahaṃ duḥkhītyatra caitanyasyaivāsmacchabdārthatvāditi bhāvaḥ || akāṇḍatāṇḍaveneti || atra tāṇḍavāṅgādikaṃ kāṇḍaśabdena vivakṣitam |
tathāca kāṇḍābhāve'pi tāṇḍavāyitena dukhānusandhātṛtvamithyātvena kimityarthaḥ |
yathā nartanāṅgamṛdaṅgādisadbhāvena nartanaṃ saṅgataṃ tadabhāve tvasaṅgataṃ tadvadatrāpi duḥkhānusandhātṛtvasyāsmābhiḥ satyatve varṇite tanmithyātvakathanaṃ saṅgataṃ, tadabhāve tatkathanamasaṅgatameveti bhāvaḥ |
etadevāha -- nahīti || hetau duḥkhānusandhātṛtve |
yena tanmithyātvavarṇanaṃ saṅgataṃ syāditi bhāvaḥ |
vastugatyā bhavadabhimataṃ satyatvaṃ nirākṛtamiti cenna |
tasya prasaṅgāntaratvena prakṛtāsaṅgatyaparihārāditi hṛdayam |
nanvādyānumāne jīvasya pakṣatvaṃ brahmaṇo dṛṣṭāntatvaṃ dvitīye brahmaṇaḥ pakṣatvaṃ jīvasya dṛṣṭāntatvaṃ na sambhavati |
pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāvasya bhedasāpekṣatvāt |
jīvrabrahmaṇorbhedasyādyāpyasiddhatvāditi śaṅkate -- nanviti || nacānenaivānumānena tayorbhedasiddhyā pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāvopapattiriti vācyam |
bhedasiddhau pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāvopapattyā'numānapravṛttiranumānapravṛtau ca tena bhedasiddhirityanyonyāśrayāpātāditi bhāvaḥ |
pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāve hi bhedamātramapekṣitaṃ nanu tasya pāramārthikatvamapi |
tathātve tvanmate parvatamahānasayoḥ pāramārthikabhedābhāvena pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāvānupapattiprasaṅgāt |
bhedamātraṃ tu jīvabrahmaṇostvayāpyaṅgīkṛtamiti tena pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāvopapattiriti pariharati -- tvayeti ||
yadvā jīvo brahmapratiyogika(tāttvika)tādātmyādhikaraṇaṃ na bhavati brahma vā jīvapratiyogika(tāttvika)tādātmyādhikaraṇaṃ na bhavati |
pūrvahetvoreva sādhyanirdeśo draṣṭavyaḥ |
na caivamaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ doṣāya |
taddhi sādhyakoṭeraprasiddheḥ(ddhau) sādhyatadabhāvavattvena sandehānudayātpakṣatāvighaṭanadvārā pakṣadharmatāvighaṭakatvena doṣa utāprasiddhe sādhye tena hetorvyāptigrahaṇāyogena vyāptivighaṭanena vā ubhayavighaṭanena vā |
nādyaḥ |
brahma jīvapratiyogika(tāttvika)tādātmyāpannaṃ(tādātmyāvacchinnaṃ) na veti saṃśayābhāve'pi jīvapratiyogika(tāttvika)tādātmyaṃ brahmaṇyasti na veti sandehamātreṇābhidheyatvasādhakānumāna iva pakṣatopapatteḥ (pakṣadharmatopapatteḥ)|
na dvitīyaḥ |
kevalavyatirekiṇi sādhyābhāvasyaiva vyāptigrahāt |
atra sādhyābhāvasya jīvatādātmyasya jīve prasiddheḥ |
yatra vyatirekiṇi bhāvaḥ sādhyastatrābhāvasya vyāptyupayogino bhāvajñānādhīnatvātsādhyāprasiddhirdoṣāya |
prakṛte tvabhāvasya sādhyatvānna doṣaḥ |
na tṛtīyaḥ |
ubhayāvighaṭanenādūṣaṇatvāt |
vyāptipakṣadharmatānyataravighaṭakatve(vighaṭakatvenaiva) hi dūṣaṇam |
pūvoktānumānadvaye dharmisamasattāketi viśeṣaṇapraveśena gauravamityaparitoṣādāha -- yadveti || pūrvahetvorevoti |
pūrvoktahetubhyāmevetyarthaḥ |
ādyānumāne brahmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvāditi || dvitīye ca jīvānusaṃhitaduḥkhānanusandhātṛtvāditi pūrvoktāveva hetū bodhyāviti bhāvaḥ || na caivamaprasiddhaviśeṣatvamiti || sarvatrādvaitavādinā brahmajīvapratiyogikatāttvikatādātmyasyaivāṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ |
yadyapyaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvamasti |
tathā'pi na tenānumitipratibandharūpadoṣo'stīti bhāvenoktaṃ doṣāyeti |
taddhi pakṣadharmatāvighaṭanena vā vyāptivighaṭanena vobhayavighaṭanena vā doṣa iti yojanā |
kathaṃ pakṣadharmatāvighaṭanamityata uktaṃ pakṣatvavighaṭanadvāreti |
tadapi kathamityata uktaṃ sādhyakoṭītyādi |
sādhyarūpaikakoṭītyarthaḥ |
tathā ca sandigdhasādhyavata eva dharmiṇaḥ pakṣatvāttadvṛttihetoreva pakṣadharmatvātsandehebhāve pakṣatvāsiddhau hetoḥ pakṣadharmatā na ghaṭata ityarthaḥ |
vyāptivighaṭanopapādanāyoktamaprasiddhanetyādi |
jīvo brahmapratiyogikatāttvikatādātmyādhikaraṇaṃ na bhavatītyādyanumāne īśvarapratiyogiko jīvadharmikastu bhedo jīvasya pratyakṣaḥ |
nāhaṃ nirduḥkho na sarvajña ityanubhavāditi prāk pakṣa eva sādhyaprasiddherupapāditatvena kiṃ parāṅgīkāramātreṇa sādhyāprasiddhimabhyupagamya tasya doṣahetutānirāsāyāseneti bhāvena brahmapakṣakadvitīyānumāna eva sādhyāprasiddheradūṣaṇatvamupapādayati -- nādya ityādinā |
yadyapīśvaradharmiko jīvapratiyogiko bheda īśvarasya pratyakṣa iti prāguktatvāddvitīyānumāne'pi sādhyāprasiddhirnāsti |
tathā'pi parabuddherapratyakṣatvāduttamaḥ puruṣastvanya itīśvaravākyena tatpratyakṣānumāne punastatrāpi sādhyāprasiddhiśaṅkāyāṃ tannirāse prayāsagauravamālocya dvitīyānumāne sādhyāprasiddhimabhyupagamya tasyā adūṣaṇatvakathanamidamiti bodhyam |
kecittūpalakṣaṇābhiprāyeṇa dvitīyānumāne'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvasya doṣatvābhāvakathanamidamityāhuḥ |
yadyapi nāyaṃ panthāḥ saiddhāntikaḥ |
āśrayavyāśrayāsiddhī sādhyāsiddhiśca dūṣaṇam |
keṣāñcinna ca te doṣā vyāptau satyāṃ kathañcanetyukteḥ |
tathā'pi paramatānusāreṇedamiti bodhyam || brahmeti || tatra jīvapratiyogikatāttvikatādātmyapannamiti sādhyābhāvakoṭipradarśanam |
na veti sādhyakoṭipradarśanam |
tathā ca brahmarūpapakṣadharmikasādhyatabhāvakoṭikasaṃśayāsambhave'pi sādhyābhāvadharmikapakṣavṛttitvatadabhāvakoṭikasandehamātreṇa pakṣatvopapattyā hetoḥ pakṣadharmatvopapatterityarthaḥ |
kvacitsādhyadharmikapakṣavṛttitvatadabhāvakoṭikasaṃśayenāpi pakṣatopapattirdraṣṭavyā |
neyaṃ rītiḥ śāstrānubaddheti mantavayamiti bhāvenātra dṛṣṭāntamāha -- abhidheyatvasādhakānumāna iveti || śabdo'bhidheyaḥ prameyatvādityatrābhidheyatvasya kevalānvayitvena tadabhāvāprasiddhyā śabdo'bhidheyatvāvānna veti santehesambhave'pyabhidheyatvaṃ śabdavṛtti na veti sandehamātreṇa yathā śabdasthapakṣatopapattistathā'trāpīti bhāvaḥ |
idamapi pakṣatāsampādanaṃ paroktānusāreṇaiva |
na svamatarītyā |
svamate liṅgajanyapratītiviṣayarūpasādhyadharmavattāmātrasya pakṣatārūpatvena sandigdhasādhyadharmavata eva pakṣatvaniyamābhāvāt |
ghanagarjitaśravaṇena ghanānumāne jñātvā'pi parīkṣārthaṃ pṛcchantaṃ prati prayuktānumāne pratyakṣādinā niścite'pyarthe dārḍhyārthaṃ prayujyamānānumāne ca sādhyasandehābhāvena sādhyadharmavataḥ pakṣatābhāvaprasaṅgāt |
svakāraṇāyattatayā jāyamānāyāmanumitau prāk sādhyasiddherapratibandhakatvācca |
siddhyabhāvasyānumitāvanaṅgatvācca |
na hi sāmagrī buddhimatī yena yanmayā kartavyāṃ tadanyenaiva kṛtamityālocyodāsīta |
ata eva siṣādhāyiṣā sahakṛtasiddhyabhāvaḥ pakṣatetyādivādā api sthavīyāṃsa eva |
parvate vahniṃ dṛṣṭvā kiñciddūragatasya sahasā dhūmadarśane vahnyanumiteranubhavasiddhatvāt |
na hi tatra siṣādhayiṣā'sti |
nāpi siddhyabhāva iti || kevalavyatirekiṇīti |
hetoḥ sādhyena vyāptigrahe hi sādhyaprasiddherupayogaḥ |
sādhanābhāvena sādhyābhāvasya vyāptigrahe tu na sādhyaprasiddhirupayoginī |
kevalavyatirekī cāyaṃ heturiti bhāvaḥ |
nanu kevalavyatirekiṇi vyatirekavyāptigrahe sādhyaprasiddheranupayoge'pi sādhyābhāvaprasiddhistvāvaśiyakyeva |
sā'pi kathamatretyata āha -- atreti || jīvatādātmyābhāvasyaiva sādhyatvena tadabhāvo jīvatādātmyaṃ jīve prasiddhameveti bhāvaḥ |
nanu jīvaccharīraṃsātmakaṃ prāṇādimattvādityatra vyatirekavyāptigrahe pratiyogitayā sādhyaprasiddherapyāvaśyakatvena kathaṃ sā nopayoginīti tatrāha -- yatra tviti || tathā ca sātmakatvānumāne sātmakatvasya bhāvarūpatvena vyatirekavyāptigrahe pratiyogibhūtasātmakatvājñānasyāpekṣitatvena tadaprasiddhau vyāptigrahāyogena sādhyāprasiddherdoṣatve'pi na prakṛtānumāne sādhyāprasiddhirdoṣāya |
jīvatādātmyābhāvasya sādhyatvena tasyāprasiddhatve'pi vyatirekavyāptau jīvatādātmyābhāvābhāvarūpajīvatādātmyasya jīve prasiddhatvena tasya hetvabhāvena vyāptigrahasya suśakatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
abhāvasyābhāvajñānasya |
bhāvajñānādhīnatvāt sādhyarūpapratiyogijñānādhīnatvāt || ubhayavighaṭaneneti || asiddhyādihetvābhāsadoṣeṣu pakṣadharmatāvyāptyubhayavighaṭanena dūṣaṇatvasyādarśanādityarthaḥ |
tatkuta ityata āha -- vyāptīti |
anyataravighaṭakatvamātreṇaiva dūṣaṇatvaṃ na tūbhayavighaṭakatvenāpi gauravāt |
ato'trāpi nobhayavighaṭakatvena dūṣaṇatvamaṅgīkāryamiti bhāvaḥ |
evaṃ dvitīyaprayoge'pītyataḥ satyeva samādhāne viṣayavyāptisūcanārthaṃ prakārāntareṇa prayuṅkte || yadveti || samādhānaṃ tu brahmapratiyogikābhedo na jīvaghaṭayorekaḥ |
tasya dharmisvarūpatvena pratyadhikaraṇamanyānyatvāt |
tathā hi |
anyonyābhāvo dharmisvarūpo dharmyabhinnatvenāropyamāṇapratiyogikaḥ saḥ svayamapi dharmiṇā tadavasthānāpannaḥ |
yathā dharmibhinnatayā''ropyamāṇapratiyogikaḥ saṃsargābhāvo dharmibhinna iti || vipakṣe bhedaparamparayā'navasthetyanukūlatarkaḥ |
astu vā'sanneka eva bhedaḥ |
tathā'pi yāvaddharmisattāsamānasattāketi viśeṣaṇānna doṣaḥ |
na jīvaghaṭobhayadharmikasya viruddhasattādvayapratyāhatyā vyāhatiriti vācyam |
pakṣadharmatābalādvyāhatārthasyāparyavasānāt |
kiñca jīvajaḍayoḥ pāramārthikatvaikarūpasattākatvasyāpyanena lābhāt |
na caivaṃ jīvasyāpi vyāvahārikaparyavasānaṃ kinna syāditi vācyam |
sandigdha eva paryavasānasya nyāyyatvāditi || tādātmyābhāvasyaiva bhedatvena pūrvānumānāpekṣayā'tra ko bheda iti cenna |
tādātmyāropyakasyaiva bhedatvāt |
iha ca tādātmyābhāvasādhyatvāt |
na caivaṃ bhedasādhanāsaṅgatiriti vācyam |
tādātmyavirodhimātrasyaivādau bhedapadena vivakṣitatvāt |
vyāvahārikatādātmyasyābhāvena siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya tāttviketi || aprasiddheti || sarvasyāpi tattvato brahmapadābhidheyacaitanyātmakatvāṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ |
sādhyāsiddhirna dūṣaṇamiti bhāvenāha -- taddhīti |
pakṣatvavighaṭanadvāreṇa pakṣadharmatāvighaṭanadvāreṇetyekavidho vikalpaḥ |
aprasiddhakoṭikasaṃśayāsambhave sandigdhatvarūpapakṣatvābhāvenetyarthaḥ || ubhayeti || uktavyāptipakṣadharmatārūpadvayetyarthaḥ |
prakāratrayaṃ vinā doṣatāyogāditi bhāvaḥ |
sādhyāprasiddhau pakṣaviśeṣyakasādhyatadabhāvobhayakoṭikasaṃśayābhāve'pi sādhyatadabhāvānyataraviśeṣyakena pakṣavṛttitvāvṛttitvakoṭisaṃśayenālamityāha || brahmaṇyasti na vetīti |
evañca brahmavṛttitvatadavṛttitvayoreva koṭitvena tayoḥ sattvajñānatvādau prasiddhatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
abhidheyatveti śabdo'bhidheya ityatrābhidheyatvasya kevalānvayitvena neti koṭyaprasiddhyā'bhidheyo na veti saṃśayāyoge'pi abhidheyatvaṃ śabde'sti na veti sandehamātreṇaivetyarthaḥ |
yathākathañcitpakṣatāvacchedakasāmānādhikaraṇyasaṃśayo'numityaṅgam |
na tu tasyānumitisamānaviśeṣyaprakāratāpekṣeti bhāvaḥ |
vastutastu jijñāsuṃ pratyanumānapravṛttāveva saṃśayo'ṅgam |
prativādinaṃ prati siṣādhayiṣāyām |
vādiprativādimadhyasthānāṃ svasvasiddhāntaniścayitvāt |
kiñca koṭijñānasyaiva saṃśayaṃ prati hetutvaṃ lāghavāt |
ataścāroparūpāprasiddhiḥ sarvatra yujyate |
na hi parasya viśeṣadarśanavataḥ sā na yukteti vācyam |
tasyāpyāhāryopapatteḥ |
prativādinaṃ prati pravṛttau tu saṃśayasyāpyāhāryahetutvāṅgīkārāt |
api ca tādātmyādhikaraṇaṃ na bhavatīti pratijñāvākyādeva koṭiprasiddhirastu |
ayogyatājñānaṃ vākyārthajñānaprāmāṇya eva pratibandhakam |
tajjñānaṃ tu viśeṣadarśanaṃ candraprādeśena candraprādeśatvapratyakṣasāmagrīvaśādbhavatyeveti || sādhyābhāvasya vyāptigrahaṇāditi || evañca sādhyavyāpteragrahaṇe tadaprasiddhiradoṣa iti bhāvaḥ |
idaṃ ca pararītyaiva |
vastutastvadhikaraṇaviśeṣasyāprasiddhatve'pyadhikaraṇānubandhenaivedamanena vyāptaṃ tadabhāvavyāpakībhūtābhāvapratiyogitvādityanvaya eva sugrahaḥ |
upalakṣaṇametat |
viśeṣatatsādhyāprasiddhāvapi yo yadavṛttidharmādhikaraṇaṃ sa tattādātmyādhikaraṇaṃ na bhavatīti sāmānyavyāptistu sugraheti || abhāvavyāptigrahe'pi pratiyogiprasiddhirāvaśyikītyata āha -- yatretyādinā || doṣāyeti || kathañciditi śeṣaḥ |
tatrāropyadvayaprasiddherūpapādayituṃ śakyatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
anyathā sādhyāprasiddhirna doṣa iti virodhaḥ || abhāvasya sādhyatvāditi || abhāvābhāvasya bhāvatvena pratijñānānapekṣaṇāditi bhāvaḥ |
idaṃ cāpātato'bhāvābhāvasya bhāvatve'pi na vidhimukhena vyatirekadhīḥ |
kintvabhāvābhāvatvena |
īdṛśyā eva vyabhicāradhīvirodhitvāditi || vastutastūktarītyā''roparūpāprasiddhiravaseyā |
astu prakārāntareṇa duṣṭatvamityata āha -- vyāptīti |
nanu dharmitvamekaṃ nānugatam |
tathā ca yadi ghaṭasattāsamānasattākaṃ bhedaviśeṣaṇaṃ tadā siddhasādhanatā viśeṣaṇavaiyyarthyaṃ ca |
yadi brahmasattāsamānasattākatvaṃ tadā sādhyāprasiddhirityata oha || yadveti || brahma vā jīvapratiyogiketi || idaṃ dvitīyānumānābhiprāyeṇa |
evaṃ prathamānumāne'pi jīvo brahmapratiyogikatātvikatādātmyādhikaraṇaṃ na veti sandehāsambhave'pi brahmapratiyogikatāttvikatādātmyaṃ jīvavṛtti na veti sandehasambhavena pakṣatā bodhyā |
na ca pakṣaviśeṣyakasādhyatadabhāvakoṭikasaṃśayānyasaṃśayenāpi pakṣatāyāṃ siddhidaśāyāmapi yādṛśatādṛśasaṃśayena pakṣatāpattiriti |
prakṛtānumitinivṛttyasaṃśayasyātiprasaṅgabhañjakatvāt |
prakṛtānumitiśca bhinnaviśeṣyakaprakārakā'pyanubhavavirodhāt tādṛśasaṃśayanivṛttiketi bhāvaḥ |
idamupalakṣaṇam |
vastutastu ghanagarjitena ghanādyanumānānurodhena viśiṣṭasiddhyabhāvaḥ pakṣatā sā tu saṃśayābhāve'pyakṣatā |
pakṣadharmatā tu na tāvattattaddharmadharmitā |
kintu pakṣatābhimatadharmavṛttitaiva sā tu saṃśayanirapekṣitaiveti na tadvighaṭakatā sādhyāprasiddherityapi bodhyam |
tatrābhāvajñānasya vyāptyupayogino bhāvādhīnatvāditi pāṭhaḥ sādhuḥ |
kvacittatrābhāvavyāptipratiyogino bhāvādhīnatvāditi pāṭhaḥ |
tatra vyāptipratiyogino vyāptyāśrayasyābhāvajñānādhīnatvādityarthaḥ || ubhayavighaṭaneneti || yadyapyubhayavighaṭane'numitipratibandho bhavatyeva tathā'pyubhayavighaṭakatvaṃ na dūṣakatābījaṃ gauravādavyāpteśceti bhāvaḥ |
śiṣyavyutpādanāyoktārthadārḍhyāya cānumānāntarāṇyāha -- yadveti || kecittu dharmitvena niveśe ghaṭādirūpadharmisattāsamānasattākabhedaṃ vyāvahārikamādāya siddhasādhanatā |
svadharmīti niveśe pakṣadṛṣṭāntayorekasādhyālābha ityarucerāha yadvetītyāhuḥ |
iti sādhyanirdeśaḥ pūrvoktahetoḥ kartavya ityarthaḥ || brahmapratiyogikatāttvikatādātmyādhikaraṇaṃ na bhavatīti || nanu tādātmye tāttvikatvaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ vyartham |
sāmānyatastādātmyānādhikaraṇatvamātrasādhanenāpyabhimatārthasiddheḥ |
yathākathañcittattādātmyādhikaraṇatve tatsāmānyānāśrayatvabādhāpattyā'tyantabhedasiddheḥ |
na cāropitasya jīvatādātmyasya brahmaṇi, brahmatādātmyasya ca jīve sattvādbādhavāraṇāya tatsārthakyamiti vācyam |
āropitānāropitayormilitvā tādātmyatvākhyaikadharmasyaivābhāvāt |
āropitasyaivāsattvena kiñcidadhikaraṇāvṛttitadvattāmādāya bādhaprasaktyabhāvācca |
nāpi paraṃ prati bādhasiddhasādhanatāvāraṇāyeti vācyam |
tāttvikatādātmyasya ta(tva)nmate'ṅgīkṛtatvena tādatmyasāmānyābhāvasya tanmate'naṅgīkāreṇa siddhasādhanānavakāśāt |
tāttvikatādātmyamaṅgīkurvatastāttvikatvadāne'pyadāne'pi bādhasyāvaśyakatvena tasyādūṣaṇatvāt |
anyathā sarvatra prativādinā'nabhyupagatasyaiva sādhanīyatvena bādhāvaśyakatayā parārthānumānakathocchedaprasaṅgagāt |
tādātmye taddāne'pi vyāvahārikatadabhāvamādāya siddhasādhanānistārācca |
tasmāttāttvikatvasya na tādātmyaviśeṣaṇatāsambhavaḥ |
nāpi tadabhāvaviśeṣaṇatvamapāramārthikam |
tādātmyābhāvamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāyeti vācyam |
samāsamadhyapatitasya padasya samāsabahirbhūtasvāsamabhivyāhṛtanañarthānvayyabodhakatvāt |
paramārthatvaviśiṣṭatādātmyābhāvarūpabhedasya paraṃ pratyasiddhatvācca |
vyatirekavyāptimūlakānumānenāpi yaddharmāvacchinnavyāpakatāvacchedakatvaṃ hetvabhāvatve gṛhitaṃ taddharmāvacchinnābhāvānumāpakatvasya hetau sattve'pi pāramārthikatvaviśeṣitatādṛśābhāvānumitau bījābhāvāt |
tasmāttāttvikapadamaphalameveti cenna |
atra tāttvikapadaṃ na pāramārthikaparaṃ kintu mukhyaparam |
sa tu tādātmyapadasyaikatvaparatātātparyagrāhakam |
anyathā jīvabrahmaṇoḥ sādṛśyābhedāditātparyeṇa śāstre tādātmyaikyādipadānāṃ bahuśaḥ prayogāt |
tādātmyetyetāvata evokte teṣāmapyupasthitau tadabhāvasya vā mukhyāmukhya sādhāraṇarūpāvacchinnābhāvasya vā prakṛte sādhyatvabhrameṇa prakṛte bādhaśaṅkā syāt |
tāttvikapadadāne tu mukhyaikyamityarthalabdhyā tadabhāvasyaiva sādhyatālābhānna bādhaśaṅkāprasaktiḥ |
tathā ca jīvapratiyogikaikyatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvo brahmapratiyogikaikyatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvaścānumānadvaye yathāyathaṃ sādhye |
ayamabhāvastādātmyaniṣṭhasvarūpasaṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitāko'tyantābhāva eva bhedādanya evādhikaraṇībhūtabrahmādisvarūpatayā paramate pāramārthika eva sidhyati |
tena tasya vyāvahārikatvamādāya siddhasādhanatāparsaktiḥ |
|
bhedastu tādātmyasambandhāvacchinnapratiyogitāko vā tādātmyāvacchinnapratiyogitāko vā bhedatvamakhaṇḍopādhirvā |
abhedasāmānyamapāramārthikamiti parābhyupagamapakṣe'tyantābhāvasyaiva sādhanenāsmākaṃ kṛtārthatetyāśayenaitadanumānaṃ pravṛttamiti na ko'pi doṣaḥ |
ata evottaragrantheṣu tāttvikatvaviśeṣaṇaṃ tādātmye'nupādāyaiva vyavahāraḥ |
pūrvaṃ tādātmyābhāvasya bhedatvena vyavahārādatra tasyātiriktatvāṅgīkāre pūrvottaravirodha iti cenna |
bhedasya tasya ca samanaiyyatyenaikyamiti mataviśeṣābhiprāyeṇa tathā vyavahṛtatvena virodhābhāvāt |
kecittu parārthānumānasya vipratipattipūrvakatvādvipratipattau ca tāttvikatādātmyatvena pareṇa svapakṣotkīrtanaṃ kāryam |
anyathā'nirvacanīyatādātmyamādāya paryavasānātparasyārthāntarāpātāt |
tathā ca tadabhāvarūpasiddhānta(siddhāntyabhimata)koṭistāttvikatvaghaṭitaiveti tāttvikapadaṃ sārthakamityāhuḥ || na caivamaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvamiti || na ca brahmaikyasya jīvaikyasya vā ghaṭādāvabhāvāddhaṭādāveva tadabhāvaḥ prasiddha iti kathamaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇateti vācyam |
tādātmyamaikyamityanarthāntaraṃ tadvṛtyasādhāraṇadharmasyaiva (tat)tādātmyarūpatvāt |
bhedābhāvarūpaikyasya ca pratiyogitāvacchedakasvarūpatvena tatraiva paryavasānāt |
itthaṃ gandhavattvalakṣaṇakathanātprāk pṛthivītvasya sarvatra prasaktau satyāṃ jalādyasaṅkīrṇavastujñānābhāvājjalādibhedasya kutrāpi prasiddhyabhāvājjalādibhedavyāpyavattājñānāsambhavamāśaṅkyābhāvasādhyakavyatirekitvāttādṛśasādhyābhāvabhūtajalatvavyāpakībhūtābhāvapratiyogigandhavāniti jñānājjalabhedānumiteḥ parairaṅgīkārāttadbrahmatvasya jīvasya ca sarvatra prasaktidaśāyāṃ tādātmyābhāvasya kvacidadhikaraṇe niścayābhāvādaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā tadrītyaiva samādhānaṃ vaktuṃ śakyatetyadoṣaḥ |
pṛthivītvasya sarvatra prasaktidaśāyāṃ jalāditrayodaśānyatamabhedādhikaraṇatvena vyaktiviśeṣānirṇayāttatra jalabhedāderanupasthitivadihāpi daśāviśeṣe tādṛśatādātmyābhāvasyānupasthitisambhavāt |
yadvā'dvaitavādibhāskarasya mate samastajagataḥ sacchaktimadbrahmapariṇāmitvāṅgīkārātsamastajagati brahmatādātmyamevaṃ jīvacita eva brahmatvāttadupādānakatvena jīvatādātmyamapi sarvatra vartate |
māyāvādimate'pi sarvatra brahmacaitanyāropitaprapañcādhiṣṭhānabrahmāsādhāraṇadharmarūpasattāyā aṅgīkṛtatvāttādātmyaṃ tadabhinnajīvatādātmyaṃ ca kevalānvayītyaṅgīkṛtamiti tenāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatodbhāvayituṃ śakyata eva |
sattāyāśca brahmāsādhāraṇadharmatvaṃ `tvānando viṣayānubhavaḥ sattā ceti santi trayo dharmāḥ, apṛthaktve'pi caitanyātpṛthagivāvabhāsanta' iti pañcapādikokteḥ siddhamiti na ko'pi kleśaḥ || doṣāya ceti || na cetyasyātrānvayaḥ |
prakṛtānumānavighaṭanarūpadoṣāya na bhavatītyarthaḥ || ubhayavighaṭaneneti || pratyekaṃ vyāptigrahavighaṭanena pakṣadharmatāvighaṭanena ceti dvitvāvacchinnasampādānena cetyarthaḥ || brahmeti || jīvapratiyogiketi || pūrvaṃ jīvarūpadharmiṇaḥ pratyakṣaviṣayatvena tatra hetvādeḥ sugrahatvādanumitirjhaṭiti bhavatīti tātparyeṇa jīvapakṣakānumānasya prathamata uktatve'pīdānīṃ saṃśayadharmiṇo jīvapratiyogikatādātmyasya pratyakṣasiddhatvāttaddharmikasaṃśayasya saukaryamiti prādhānyamanusṛtya dvitīyānumāna eva saṃśaya upapāditaḥ || pakṣatopapattiriti || sādhyānumitinivartanīyasaṃśayamātrasya pakṣatātvābhyupagamādasamānadharmitāvacchedakakasyāpyasya saṃśayasyānumitinivartyatvānubhavātpakṣatātvamiti bhāvaḥ |
idantu saṃśayasya pakṣatātvamabhyupetya |
siddhyabhāvapakṣatātvamate tu na ko'pi doṣaḥ || abhāvaḥ sādhya iti || tatrābhāvarūpasādhyābhāvajñānasya bhāvarūpapratiyogijñānādhīnatvāditi tatra sādhyopasthiterāvaśyakatvamiti bhāvaḥ || vyāptyupayogina iti || vyāptijñānaṃ prati viśeṣaṇajñānamudrayā hetubhūtaṃ yajjñānaṃ tadviṣayībhūtasyetyarthaḥ || abhāvasyeti || abhāvābhāvasya bhāvarūpatayā tasya svasādhyābhāvatvena jñānārthaṃ sādhyopasthiterapekṣaṇe'pi sādhyībhūtābhāvīyapratiyogitāvacchedakarūpeṇaiva vyāptiśarīre bhānābhyupagamena tatra sādhyopasthiteranapekṣaṇāditi bhāvaḥ |
na ca jalabhedānupasthitidaśāyāṃ jalatādātmyarūpajalatvasya sarvatra grahe gandhābhāvasya tadvyāpakatā grahītuṃ śakyate |
gandhavatyapi tatprasaktyā vyabhicāragrahāditi vācyam |
jalatvasya pratyakṣeṇa jale grahakāle gandhābhāvasyāpi tatra pūrvameva vyāpakatvādergṛhītatvenedenīṃ tatsmaraṇarūpaparāmarśenoktānumitisambhavāt |
pūrvamagṛhītatve'pīdānīṃ jalatādātmyādau pṛthivyanuyogitvādisandeharūpatadatiprasaktasya sattve'pi tasya gandhābhāvanirūpitavyabhicārasya jalatvasaṃśayarūpatvena paryavasāne'pi grāhyasaṃśayasya pakṣaniścaye pratibandhakatvābhāvāt |
tathā yathākathañcidanukūlatarkeṇa tadapasaraṇopapatteśca vyāpakatvagrahe yujyata eva |
evaṃ śābdaniścaye'pyuktavyabhicārasaṃśayasyāpratibandhakatvāt |
pratyuta yogyatājñānamudrayākāraṇatvādāptavākyādinā'pi tanniścayo yujyata iti na ko'pi doṣaḥ || ubhayavighaṭanenādūṣatvāditi || vyāptivighaṭakatvena pakṣatāvighaṭanadvārā |
yatpakṣadharmatāvighaṭanaṃ tena ceti yaddūṣaṇatvaṃ tadabhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
sādhyasandehasya pakṣatātvamate tadvighaṭanena pakṣadharmatāvighaṭakatve'pi tasya pakṣatātvapakṣaṃ dūṣayitvā siddhyabhāvarūpapakṣatātvasyaiva siddhāntitatvena tadvighaṭanasyāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇena kartumaśakyatvena vighaṭanadvayasyaivāyogāt |
saṃśayapakṣatāmate vighaṭanadvayasattve'pi tayorna samucitatvena dūṣakatvaṃ samucitatvenodbhāvane bādhaviśiṣṭavyabhicārodbhāvakasyaivādhikyena nigrahāpatteḥ |
kintu bādhavyabhicārayoriva pṛthagevodbhāvanīyatvātpṛthageva doṣatvamityarthaḥ |
vikalpakoṭau niveśastu yathākathañcitsambhāvitatvamātreṇeti draṣṭavyam || vyāptipakṣadharmatā'nyatareti || pratyekavighaṭakatvenetyarthaḥ |
tathā caikadaikamevodbhāvanīyamityarthaḥ |
tṛtīyakalpadūṣaṇaṃ śiṣyavyutpādanāyaiva |
na prakṛtopayuktatvena |
prakṛte vyāptipakṣadharmatayorubhayorapyupapāditatvena samucitayordvayordūṣakatve'pi kṣativirahāditi draṣṭavyam ||
bhede dharmisattāsamānasattākatvamaniveśyāpi pāramārthikabhedasādhanaprakāramārabhate || yadveti || pūrvoktahetvoḥ krameṇeti śeṣaḥ |
tathāca jīvo brahmapratiyogikatādātmyādhikaraṇaṃ na bhavati, brahmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvādvyatirekeṇa brahmavat |
brahma vā jīvapratiyogikatādātmyādhikaraṇaṃ na bhavati, jīvānusaṃhitaduḥkhānanusandhātṛtvādvyatirekeṇa jīvavadityuktaṃ bhavati |
na ca brahmatādātmyādhikaraṇībhūtabrahmapratiyogikavyāvahārikabhedasya jīve'bhyupagamātsiddhasādhanamiti vācyam |
brahmatādātmyādhikaraṇatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedasya sādhyatvāt |
ghaṭākāśe mahākāśapratiyogikabhedābhyupagame'pi mahākāśatādātmyānadhikaraṇatvāpratītyā tadadhikaraṇatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedasyeva jīve vyāvahārikasyāpi brahmatādātmyādhikaraṇatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedasya parānabhyupagatatvena doṣābhāvāt |
nanvevaṃ paramate sarvasyaiva brahmatādātmyādhikaraṇatvāttadavacchinnapratiyogitākabhedarūpasādhyāprasiddhirata āha -- na caivamiti || yadyapi kāñcanamayavahnyādeḥ sādhyatāyāmiva sādhye sādhyatāvacchedakābhāvarūpasādhyāprasiddhiḥ sākṣādanumitivirodhitayā doṣa eva |
tathāpi prakṛte na tādṛśāsiddhirudbhāvayituṃ śakyā |
vipratipattyā bhede brahmatādātmyādhikaraṇatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvasandehena tadanirṇayāt |
tatsandehasya ca sādhyasandehādhāyakatvenāvirodhitvāt |
kintūktasādhyasya kvacidagṛhītatvādajñānarūpāsiddhirevodbhāvyā |
sā ca nābhāvasādhyakavyatirekyanumāne doṣa iti bhāvaḥ |
etadevopapādayati -- taddhīti || pakṣadharmatāvighaṭanena vā vyāptivighaṭanena vobhayavighaṭanena vā doṣa iti sambandhaḥ |
nanu sādhyasyāprasiddhāvapi hetoḥ pakṣadharmatvasambhavāt kathaṃ tasyāstadvighaṭakatvamityata uktam || pakṣatāvighaṭaneneti || tadapi katham ? ataḥ sādhyakoṭītyādi |
atha pakṣatāviśiṣṭapakṣadharmatājñānaṃ nānumitihetuḥ |
pakṣatāyāḥ svarūpasatyāḥ pṛthageva hetutvāt |
kintu pakṣatāvacchedakavati hetordharmatvajñānameva heturiti kathaṃ pakṣatāvighaṭakasyānumityaupayikapakṣadharmatāvighaṭakatvamiti cet satyam |
tathāpi pakṣatāyāḥ svātantryeṇa hetutve tadviśiṣṭapakṣadharmatāyā anumitiprayojakatvānapāyāt ekadeśavighaṭakasya viśiṣṭavighaṭakatvamavyāhatameva |
aprasiddhenetyādyayogādityantaṃ vyāptivighaṭane hetuḥ |
sādhyasyāprasiddhau tannirūpitavyāptigrahāyogādityarthaḥ || ubhayavighaṭaneneti|| uktarītyā vyāptipakṣadharmatobhayavighaṭanenetyarthaḥ |
