brahmadharmikabhedānumānasya prādhānyādanyopalakṣakatayā tatra sādhyāprasiddheradoṣatvamupapādayati -- nādya ityādinā || nanu pakṣadharmikasādhyatadabhāvakoṭikasandehasyaiva pakṣatātvāt kathamanyavidhasaṃśayena pakṣatopapattirata āha -- abhidheyatvasādhyakānumāna iti || abhidheyatvaṃ śabde'sti na veti saṃśayeneti śeṣaḥ |
yathā śabdo'bhidheyaḥ prameyatvādityādau sādhyābhāvāprasiddhyā sādhyatadabhāvakoṭikasaṃśayāsambhave'pi sādhye pakṣaniṣṭhatvatadabhāvakoṭikasaṃśayasya pakṣatātvamupeyate, tathā'tra jīvatādātmyarūpasādhyābhāve pakṣaniṣṭhatvatadabhāvakoṭikasaṃśayasya pakṣatātvamurarīkriyate, viśeṣābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
idamupalakṣaṇam |
vipratipattyadhīnasādhya saṃśayasya siṣādhayiṣāvirahaviśiṣṭasiddhyabhāvasya ca pakṣatātvasambhavo draṣṭavyaḥ |
nanu sādhyāprasiddhau kathaṃ sādhyābhāvasyāpi vyāptigrahaḥ |
pratiyogiprasiddherabhāvadhīhetutvādityata āha -- yatreti || śabdo gaganaviśeṣaguṇaḥ śabdatvādvyatirekeṇa rūpavadityādau |
vyāptyupayoginaḥ sādhyābhāve hetvabhāvanirūpitavyāptigrahaprayojakasya |
abhāvasya sādhyābhāvajñānasya |
bhāvajñānādhīnatvāt sādhyajñānaprayojyatvāt |
sādhyāprasiddhirdoṣāya vyāptigrahavighaṭanadvārā'numitivighaṭanāyetyarthaḥ |
ayamāśayaḥ |
vyatirekiṇi taddharmāvacchinnābhāvasādhyakānumitau taddharmāvacchinnābhāvābhāvatvena vyāptijñānavattaddharmeṇa vyāptijñānamapi hetuḥ |
vahnyabhāvābhāvatveneva vahnitvena vyāptijñānādapi vahnyābhāvānumitidarśanāt |
tathāca prakṛte prasiddhajīvatādātmyatvena vyāptigrahāttadavacichannābhāvasādhyakānumitirna sādhyasiddhimapekṣata iti |
atha siddhānte vyatirekasyānvayamukhenaiva sādhyasādhakatayā kathamaprasiddhasādhyavyāptyanumāpakatvaṃ tasyeti cenna |
yo yavdyāpakībhūtābhāvapratiyogī sa tadabhāvavyāpyaḥ |
vahnyabhāvavyāpakībhūtābhāvapratiyogidhūmavaditi sāmānyavyāptyā pakṣadharmatābalādaprasiddhasādhyavyāptyanumitisambhavāditi vibhāvanīyam || ubhayāvighaṭaneneti || uktarītyā vyāptipakṣadharmatāgrahāvighaṭakatvena anumityavighaṭakatvādityarthaḥ |
nanu tathāpi bādhādivadasiddherapi sākṣādanumitivirodhitvenaiva doṣatvamastvata āha -- vyāptīti || bādhāderapi sādhyasandeharūpapakṣatāvighaṭakatvena doṣatvaṃ bodhyam ||
pūvoktānumānadvaye dharmisattāsamānasattāketi viśeṣaṇapraveśena gauravamityaparitoṣādāha -- yadveti || pūrvahetvoriti || tathāca jīvaḥ brahmapratiyogikaṃ yattādātmyamaikyaṃ tadāśrayo na bhavati brahmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvāt vyatirekeṇa brahmavat |
brahma vā jīvapratiyogikaṃ yattādātmyaṃ tādāśrayaṃ na bhavati jīvānusaṃhitaduḥkhānanusandhātṛtvāt vyatirekeṇa jīvavadityanumānadvayamuktaṃ bhavati |
nanu sarvatra jīvabrahmapratiyogikatādātmyādhikaraṇatvasyaiva mayā'ṅgīkāreṇa tadabhāvarūpasādhyāprasiddhyobhayatrāpyaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvamityāśaṅkya nirākaroti || naceti || evamuktarītyā pratijñākaraṇe'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvasya sphuṭatvena kathaṃ tadapalāpa ityata uktam || doṣāyeti || tat vidyamānamapyaprasiddhiviśeṣaṇatvam |
pakṣadharmatāvighaṭakatvena doṣa ityanvayaḥ |
kathaṃ tasya pakṣadharmatāvighaṭakatvamityata uktam || pakṣatāvighaṭanadvāreti || pakṣatāśrayavṛttitvaṃ pakṣadharmatvam |
pakṣatāyā abhāve tadāśrayavṛttitvarūpapakṣadharmatvamapi netyarthaḥ |
pakṣatāvighaṭakatvameva kathamityata uktam || sādhyatadabhāvavattvena sandehānudayāditi || sandigdhasādhyadharmavattā hi pakṣatā |
sādhyasandehasyaivābhāve taddhaṭitapakṣatāpi netyarthaḥ |
sādhyasandehānudaya eva kuta ityata uktam || sādhyakoṭeraprasiddheriti || tathātvekoṭismṛterabhāvāditi bhāvaḥ || vyāptigrahaṇāyogeneti || vyāptirūpasapratiyogikapadārthajñāne sādhyarūpapratiyogijñānasyākāraṇatvāditi bhāvaḥ || ubhayeti || vyāptipakṣadharmatārūpobhayetyarthaḥ |
upalakṣaṇābhiprāyeṇa dvitīyānumāne'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvasya doṣatvābhāvamāha -- nādya ityādinā || iti sandehamātreṇeti || pakṣadharmikasādhyatadabhāvakoṭikasandehavatsādhyatadabhāvānyataradharmikapakṣavṛttitvatadabhāvakoṭikasaṃśayasyāpi pakṣatāghaṭakatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
atraiva dṛṣṭāntamāha -- abhidheyatvasādhaketi || śabdo'bhidheyaḥ prameyatvādityatrābhidheyatvasya kevalānvayitvena tadabhāvāprasiddhyā śabdo'bhidheyatvavānna veti pakṣadharmikasādhyatadabhāvakoṭikasandehābhāve'pi sādhyatadabhāvānyataradharmikapakṣavṛttitvatadabhāvaprakārakasandehamātreṇa yathā pakṣadharmatā tathā'trāpi sādhyābhāvadharmikapakṣavṛttitvatadabhāvakoṭikasaṃśayena pakṣatopapatterityarthaḥ || atreti || dvitīyānumāne hi jīvatādātmyānadhikaraṇatvaṃ sādhyaṃ tadabhāvaśca jīvatādātmyādhikaraṇatvaṃ tacca jīve prasiddhamatastatra sādhanābhāvena vyāptigrahassambhavatītyarthaḥ |
nanvabhāvajñāne pratiyogijñānasya kāraṇatvena pratiyogibhūtasādhyasyāprasiddhatve tadabhāvajñānāyogātkathaṃ tatra vyāptigraha ityata āha -- yatreti || śabda ākāśaviśeṣaguṇaḥ ākāśamātravṛttiguṇatvāt vyatirekeṇa rūpavaditi kevalavyatirekyanumāne sādhyasyākāśaviśeṣaguṇatvasya bhāvarūpatvena tadabhāvasyābhāvarūpatvāt |
pratiyogibhūtākāśaviśeṣaguṇatvasyāprasiddhatvena tadabhāvasya jñātatvena tatra vyāptigrahāsambhavātsādhyāprasiddhirdoṣāya |
prakṛte jīvatādātmyasya bhāvarūpatvena tajjñānasya pratiyogijñanānadhīnatvāttatra vyāptigrahasambhavena sādhyāprasiddhirna doṣāyetyarthaḥ |
abhāvasya sādhyābhāvajñānasya |
bhāvajñānādhīnatvāt sādhyarūpapratiyogijñānādhīnatvāt |
kuta ityata āha -- vyāptīti || asiddhyādihetvābhāsānāṃ hi vyāptipakṣadharmatānyataravighaṭakatvenaiva doṣatvaṃ prasiddhaṃ natu prakārāntareṇa |
ato'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvasyāpi vyāptyādivighaṭakatvābhāvena dūṣaṇatvameva netyarthaḥ |
yadvā brahmapratiyogikābhedo na jīvadharmiko brahmadharmiko vā'bhedo na jīvapratiyogikaḥ |
jīvānusaṃhitaduḥkhānanusandhāturabhedatvāt |
vyatirekeṇa jīvapratiyogikajīvadharmikaikyavat |
jīvapratiyogikamaikyaṃ vā na brahmadharmikaṃ jīvadharmikamaikyaṃ vā na brahmapratiyogikaṃ brahmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhāturabhedatvāt |
vyatirekeṇa brahmadharmikabrahmapratiyogikaikyavat (brahmapratiyogikabrahmadharmikaikyavat)|
evaṃ yajñadattādibhyo devadattādīnāṃ bhedaḥ sādhyaḥ |
etasminnapyanumānadvaye'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaśaṅkānirāsaprayāsagauravamityaparitoṣādāha -- yadveti || ananusandhāturabhedatvātiti || ananusandhātṛ yadbrahma tatsambandhyabhedatvādityarthaḥ |
sambandhaścādye pratiyogyanuyogibhāvarūpo dvitīye dharmidharmabhāvarūpo bodhyaḥ |
evamuttaratrāpi || vyatirekeṇa jīvapratiyogiketi || tatra hi jīvadharmikatvaṃ jīvapratiyogikatvaṃ ca vartate na tu tadabhāvaḥ |
evaṃ jīvānusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛjīvasambandhitvameva vartate |
na tu tadananusandhātṛmasambandhitvamiti sādhyasādhanābhāvo'vagantavyaḥ |
evamuttaratrāpi |
evaṃ jīveśabhede'numānāṣṭakamabhidhāya parasparajīvabhede'pi tameva prakāramatidiśati || evamiti || devadatto yajñadattapratiyogikadharmisamasattākabhedādhikaraṇam |
yajñadattānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvāt vyatirekeṇa yajñadattavadityādirūpeṇa bhedaḥ sādhya ityarthaḥ |
nanu bhūḥ śaśaviṣāṇollakhitā bhūtvādityādau ko doṣa iti cet |
pramāṇabādha iti samādheyam |
yasyāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatve doṣābhimānastenāpi santoṣṭavyamiti prakārantareṇa prayuṅkte || yadveti || jīvadharmikatvādyabhāvasya sārvajñādau prasiddherna doṣaḥ || ananusandhāturabhedatvāditi || atra dharmitvapratiyogitvodāsīnasambandhamātraṃ ṣaṣṭhyarthaḥ || brahmapratiyogiko vā jīvapratiyogika iti || jīvabrahmaṇorabhede jīvo brahmaṇaiko brahma vā jīvenaikamiti tayoraikyaṃ prati dharmitvapratiyogitve syātām |
tanniṣedhe tvaikyameva niṣiddhaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ || evamiti || devadatto yajñadattapratiyogikadharmisattāsamānasattākabhedādhikaraṇam |
tattādātmyādhikaraṇaṃ vā na bhavati |
tatpuṇyābhoktṛtvād vyatirekeṇa tadvat |
anvayena ghaṭavatyādyūhyam |
anyapuṇyāderanyabhoktṛtve kṛtahānākṛtābhyāgamaprasakternāsiddhiḥ |
sādhyaprasiddhisaukaryāyāha -- yadveti || anumānacatuṣṭaye pakṣe tāttvikatvaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ bodhyam |
tena na siddhasādhanatā |
jīvānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhāturiti ṣaṣṭhī |
ādye sādhyeti pratiyogyanuyogibhāvo dvitīye dharmidharmabhāvo bodhyaḥ |
evamuttaratrāpīti |
na ca ghaṭādau ghaṭapratiyogighaṭadharmikayorbhedayorvā sādhāraṇyamiti vācyam |
abhedasya ghaṭātmakatvena ghaṭapratiyogikadhaṭadharmikabhedasya paṭādyātmakatvena paṭādyātmakatayā ghaṭadharmikabhedasaypyevaṃrūpatvena hetorati sattvāt |
yadyapyuttarānumānayogenaiva sādhāraṇyaṃ na supariharam |
tathā'pyanukūlatarkasanāthatvāt vyatirekiṇi satpratipakṣonnāyakatā na sambhavatīti na tasya doṣatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
prāsaṅgikabhedasādhanamāhaḥ || evamiti || devadatto yajñadattapratiyogikadharmisattāsamānasattākabhedādhikaraṇaṃ yajñadattānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvāt |
vyatirekeṇa yajñadattavadityādirūpeṇetyarthaḥ |
|| uktaprayāsapradveṣādanumānāntaraṇi darśayati -- duḥkhānusandhāturabhedatvāditi || prathamānumānapakṣe pratiyogitvaṃ ṣaṣṭhyarthaḥ |
dvitīyānumānapakṣe ādheyatvaṃ ṣaṣṭhyartha iti dhyeyam || jīvapratiyogiketi || prathamānumānatātparyeṇa jīvapratiyogiketi || dvitīyatātparyeṇa jīvadharmiketi || jīvapratiyogiketyādyatrāpyanusandhāturiti ṣaṣṭhyā'numānabhedenārthadvayaṃ draṣṭavyam |
anumānacatuṣṭaye'pyasmanmate paramate cābhedasya dharmisvarūpatvena satyatvāttatra sādhanīyasyātyantābhāvarūpasādhyasyādhikaraṇasvarūpatvamatyantābhāvasyābhyupagacchatā mithyātvasya vaktumaśakyatayā na vyāvahārikasādhyamādāyārthāntaratodbhāvanāvakāśaḥ |
jīvānāṃ parasparaṃ bhedasādhane'pīmaṃ prakāramatidiśati || evamiti || devadatto yajñadattapratiyogikaikyābhāvavān yajñadattānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānanusandhātṛtvāt vyatirekeṇa yajñadattavaditi prakāro draṣṭavyaḥ |
aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaśaṅkānāskanditamanumānāntaramāha -- yadveti || abhedastādātmyam |
yadyapi brahmapratiyogikābhedo na jīvapratiyogikaḥ, brahmadharmikābhedo vā na jīvadharmika ityuktāvapyarthājjīvabrahmaṇorbhedaḥ siddhyati |
tathāpi abhedasādhakatvena parābhimatasya tattvamasītyādivākyasya brahmaṇi jīvapratiyogikābhedo jīve brahmapratiyogikābhedo vā pratipādyaḥ |
sākṣāttadvirodhitvaṃ ca yathoktānumānānāmeveti tadanusaraṇam |
tatra prathamacaturthayoḥ dvitīyavirodhitvaṃ, dvitīyatṛtīyayoḥ prathamavirodhitvamiti draṣṭavyam |
hetau tādātmyatvamātrasya dṛṣṭānte'naikāntyādananusandhātṛtādātmyatvasya cāsiddherjīvānusaṃhitetyādi vyākhyātārthakam |
atra jaḍatādātmyasya pakṣasamatvenānvayadṛṣṭāntābhāvādāha -- vyatirekeṇeti || na ca paramate jīvapratiyogikaikyasya brahmapratiyogikatvamapyastyeveti na vyatirekidṛṣṭāntasyāpi sambhava iti vācyam |
jīvaikyāderjīvatvādirūpatayā tatra duḥkhānanusandhātṛtvarūpahetvabhāvasyobhayavādisiddhatvāt || evamiti || devadatto yajñadattapratiyogikadharmisattāsamānasattākabhedādhikaraṇaṃ tadananusaṃhitaduḥkhādyanusandhātṛtvādvyatirekeṇa yajñadattavadityādiprakāreṇetyarthaḥ |
pūrvoktānumānadvaye'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvasya dūṣaṇatvanirāsaprayāsagauravamityaparitoṣātpakṣāntaramāha -- yadveti || jīvapratiyogikajīvadharmikaikyavaditi || tatra hi jīvadharmikatvaṃ vartate jīvānusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātā jīvaḥ tatpratiyogikābhedatvamevāsti natu tadananusandhāturabhedatvam |
tathā jīvapratiyogikatvaṃ vartate |
jīvānusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātā jīvaḥ |
taddharmikābhedatvaṃ ca siddhamiti bhāvaḥ |
evaṃ brahmābhedasya jīvasambandhitvaniṣedhakānumānamabhidhāya jīvābhedasya brahmasambandhitvaniṣedhakamapi tadāha -- jīvaprītayogiketi || brahmadharmikabrahmapratiyogikaikyavaditi || tatra hi brahmadharmikatvaṃ vartate |
brahmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānanusandhātṛ brahma |
tatpratiyogikābhedatvaṃ cāsti |
nānyathā tviti |
tathā brahmapratiyogikatvaṃ vartate brahmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānanusandhātṛ brahma taddharmikābhedatvamevāstinānyatheti sādhyasādhanābhāvasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
jīveśayorbhedasādhanaprakāramanyonyajīvabhedasādhane'pyatidiśati || evamiti || jīvabrahmapadasthāne devadattayajñadattādipadāni prakṣipya devatatto yajñadattapratiyogikadharmisavāsamānasattākabhedādhikaraṇam |
yajñadattānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvāt |
vyatirekeṇa yajñadattavadityādayaḥ sarve prayogā atra draṣṭavyā iti bhāvaḥ |
nanvaupādhikabhedamātreṇa (upādhibhedamātreṇa) duḥkhānusandhātṛtvānanusandhātṛtvopapatteḥ pāramārthikabhedasādhane'prayojakatvameveti cenmaivam |
aupādhikabhede'pi yogino nānādehajanyasukhādyatusandhānasyaiva darśanāt |
atra dehasya cāntaḥkaraṇasya copādherbhinnatvāt |
na hyanyadehasthamantaḥkaraṇamanyadehasthaṃ bhavati |
tatrāvidyopādhireka eva bhavatīti cet |
ihāpyekajīvavādina eka eva |
bahujīvavādinastu suptāvapi kāraṇātmanā'ntaḥkaraṇānuvṛttiṃ vadato nāvidyāvacchedo jīvabhāvahetuḥ |
kintarhyantaḥkaraṇameva |
tataśca tadeva niyāmakaṃ vācyam |
tacca yogiṣvapi (yoginyapi) vidyata (bhidyata) ityuktam |
nanu jīvabrahmaṇorvā parasparajīvānāṃ vā duḥkhādyanusandhānānanusandhāne na pāramārthikabhedaprayojake |
svarūpaikye vidyamāne'pyaupādhikabhedamātreṇa tadupapatteḥ |
ato'prayojakā ete sarve'pi hetava ityāśaṅkya niṣedhati || nanu ceti || upādhibhedamātreṇa upādhikṛtabhedamātreṇa |
upādhīnāṃ bhedamātreṇeti vā || aupādhikabhede'pīti |
upādhikṛtabhede'pītyarthaḥ |
abhyupagamyoktiriyam |
svamate upādherbhedaṃ prati kārakatvābhāvāt |
upādhīnāṃ sambandhī aupādhika upādhiniṣṭha iti yāvat |
tādṛśabhede'pīti vā |
yoginaḥ saubharyādeḥ |
tathā caupādhikabhedo'prayojaka iti pāramārthikabheda eva parasparamananusandhāne prayojaka iti svīkārya iti bhāvaḥ |
nanu deha eva nopādhiḥ kintvantaḥkaraṇamapi |
tathā ca dehabhedasadbhāve'pi saurbhādāvantaḥkaraṇasyaikatvenānusandhānaṃ sambhavati |
na tathā prakṛte |
antaḥkaraṇānāṃ bhinnatvādityata āha -- dehasyeti || na saubharidehe'pyantaḥkaraṇamekaṃ kintu bhinnameva kathamanyathaikadehasthāntaḥkaraṇasyānyadehe bhogāvacchedakatvaṃ sambhavediti bhāvaḥ |
nanu dehānmanaso na bhinnatvamaṅgīkartumucitam |
dehavattasyotpattyabhāvāt |
mano nityamanāditvāditi śruteḥ |
na hyanāderekasya manasaḥ pratiśarīraṃ bhinnatvaṃ sambhavati |
ataḥ kathaṃ tasya bhinnatvamucyata iti cet |
satyam |
tathā'pyekasyaiva manaso bahudhāvibhaktatvena tattadbhautikāṃśairūpacitatvācca sītābhūṣaṇavadbhedāṅgīkāravadiyaṃ bhinnatvoktiriti bodhyam |
ata eva nyāyamṛte dvitīyaparicchede ātmabhede'nukūlatarkakathanāvasare na cāntaḥkaraṇarūpopādhibhedo duḥkhādyanusandhāne tantraṃ yoginaścāntaḥkaraṇamekameveti vācyamityāśaṅkya caitanyaikye'ntaḥkaraṇabhedasyāprayojakatvādityevoktam |
na tu yogino'pyantaḥkaraṇabhedaḥ sādhitaḥ |
tena jñāyate yogināmantaḥkaraṇamekamevetyetadanumitamiti |
nanvantaḥkaraṇādyupādherbhinnatve'pyavidyālakṣaṇopādheḥ saubhayodiyoginyekatvādanyonyamanusandhānam |
prakṛte tvavidyābhedānnānusandhānamiti śaṅkate -- tatreti || yoginītyarthaḥ |
uttaramāha -- ihepīti |
devadattādiṣvapītyarthaḥ || ekajīvavādina iti || mata iti śeṣaḥ |
eka eva |
upādhiriti vartate |
avacchedyajīvasyaikatvādavacchedakāvidyā'pyekaiva |
tathā cānusandhānaṃ durvāramiti bhāvaḥ || bahujīvavādinastviti |
mata iti śeṣaḥ |
ayamāśayaḥ |
yadyapi bahujīvavāde'vidyābhedo'sti |
tathāpi sā na jīvabhāvahetuḥ kintvantaḥkaraṇameva |
yadyantaḥkaraṇameva jīvabhāve hetutvenābhimataṃ na syāttarhi suptāvantaḥkaraṇasya kāraṇātmanā'nuvṛttirnāṅgīkriyeta |
avidyāvacchedādeva suptāvapi jīvabhāvāvasthityupapatteḥ |
aṅgīkriyate ca sā tena suptāvantaḥkaraṇavilayājjīvabhāvāpagamena tadaiva brahmabhāvarūpā muktiḥ syāditi śaṅkānirāsaḥ |
kāryātmanā vilaye'pi kāraṇātmanā'vasthiteḥ |
tathā ca yoginyavidyopādherekatve'pi jīvabhāvahetvantaḥkaraṇasya pratiśarīraṃ bhinnatvāddevadattādivadanusandhānābhāva eva yuktaḥ |
asti cānusandhānam |
tena jñāyate svarūpaikyamevānusandhāne prayojakaṃ nopādhibhedābhāva iti || tathā ca duḥkhādyanusandhātṛtvatadabhāvau pāramārthikabhedamevākṣipata iti |
nanvetaddhetavo'prayojakāḥ |
yadi bhedo na syāt tarhi sukhādyanusandhānaṃ syāditi hetūcchittitarkasya mithyābhedenānyathāsiddhiriti śaṅkate -- nanviti || tasya tadvyabhicāritvena na tenānyathāsiddhirityāha -- aupādhiketi || aupādhikabhedasyānvayavyabhicāreṇāprayojakatvāditi bhāvaḥ || atreti || yoginyaupādhikabhedaṃ darśayati -- atreti || bahurūpāṇi gṛhītavati yoginītyarthaḥ |
astu bhedamātraṃ nāntaḥkaraṇabheda ityata āha -- na hīti |
aṇutvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu tarhīśajīveṣvantaḥkaraṇābhāvaḥ syāt |
tātkālikāntaḥkaraṇajananāṅgīkārāditi cenna |
caitanyena saha tasyāpyaṃśabhedāṅgīkaraṇāt |
antaḥkaraṇāṃśānāṃ cānyonyatvāt |
tathā'pi yogino'nusandhānasattve ko heturiti cenna |
anyathā śarībāhulyagrahaṇecchānupapatteḥ |
nanvaṃśānāṃ parasparānusandhānābhāve'pyaṃśino yāvadavayavagatānusandhānaṃ syādeva |
tadaṃśānāmantaḥkaraṇatayāṃ'śāṃśinorabhedasyāpi sattvenopādhyaikyāt |
tāvanmātreṇa bahurūpeccheti || viśleṣāvasthāyāmupādhyaikyābhāvāt |
upādhyaikyamāśaṅkate -- tatreti || ihāpīti |
ayogiṣvityarthaḥ |
eka eva jīva īśvaramāyākalpito'nye tadavidyākalpitāstadaṃśā ityaṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ |
aupādhikabhede'pīti |
tathā ca na so'nusandhānānanusandhānaprayojaka iti tannirvāhāya svarūpabheda eva tāttvikaḥ svīkārya iti bhāvaḥ |
suptāvapi kāraṇātmanā'ntaḥkaraṇānuvṛttiṃ vadanta iti suptāvantaḥkaraṇāvyāptivāraṇāraya kvacittvantaḥkaraṇānuvṛttiṃ vadanto nāvidyāvacchedo jīvabhāvahetuḥ kintarhyantaḥkaraṇameva vadantīti pāṭhaḥ |
tatra bahujīvavādina iti prathamābahuvacanam |
pāṭhāntare tu vadantīti nāsti |
tatra bahujīvavādina iti ṣaṣṭhyekavacanaṃ bodhyam |
anumāne'prayojakatāmāśaṅkate -- nanvaupādhiketi || tathā ca svābhāvikaikye'nusandhānānanusandhānavyavasthānupapattirūpatarkānutthānādaprayojakateti bhāvaḥ || aupādhikabheda iti || anusandhāne svābhāvikaikyameva prayojakaṃ na tūpādhinimittakabhedābhāvaḥ |
tatsahitamaikyaṃ vā yogānugṛhītanānāśarīrāvacchinnasukhānusandhātṛṣu vyabhicārāt |
tasya cānyatra vyavasthāpitatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvantaḥkaraṇakṛtabhedābhāva eva prayojakaḥ |
sa ca yogiṣu vartata ityato'ntaḥkaraṇaikyaṃ nirākaroti || antaḥkaraṇasya ceti || nahīti |
dehānāṃ parasparaṃ viśliṣṭatvena tatraikāntaḥkaraṇasya sthityaṅgīkāre tasya dīrghatvāpattyā puruṣāntaraśarīre'pyavasthānāpatteriti bhāvaḥ |
jīvopādhitvaṃ nāntaḥkaraṇasya kintvavidyāyāstasyā niraṃśatvenāpratipādyatvena sarvaśarīreṣvekatvaṃ yujyata ityāśaṅkya nirākaroti || avidyopādhiriti || ekajīvavādina iti || tathā caikajīvavāde dūṣaṇaṃ duṣpariharameveti bhāvaḥ |
idaṃ ca yogiviṣaye dūṣaṇaparihāramabhyupagamyoktam |
ghaṭapaṭādyupādhibhirghaṭākāśādibhedavadyugapannānādehasambandhādavidyāyāmeva bhedasyāvarjanīyatayā tanmūlakajīvabhedasyāpyavarjanīyatvamiti jñeyam |
suptāvapīti || kāraṇātmaneti || sūkṣamarūpātmasaṃskārarūpeṇetyarthaḥ |
tathā cāntaḥkaraṇatatsaṃskārānyataropādhyavacchinnacetanasyaiva jīvatvātsuṣuptau jīvasiddhiḥ |
anyathā jīvasyaiva layāpatterityarthaḥ || nāvidyeti || tathātve suṣuptāvavidyāsattvādeva jīvasambabhave suṣuptāvantaḥkaraṇasya sūkṣmarūpeṇa kathanamayuktaṃ syādvaiyyarthyāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvantaḥkaraṇakāraṭamavidyaiva |
antaḥkaraṇātmanā'vāsthāne'vidyaiva seti prāptamityavidyopādhikatvamāgatamiti vācyam |
satyam |
avidyaivāntaḥkaraṇakāraṇamiti || tathā'pi tadānīmapi kalpitabhedena kāryāvasthāpannameva sa tu jīvopādhiḥ |
anyathā caitramaitrayoryugapatsuptikāle śuddhekāvidyopādhibalādaikyaprāptyā parasparasuṣuptismaraṇādyāpātāt |
tathā ca bahujīvavāde'ntaḥkaraṇasyaiva jīvopādhitvāttatroktadūṣaṇaṃ dṛḍhameva |
bahujīvavāde pratiniyatabahujñānāṅgīkāre naiṣa doṣa iti cet |
tatra pramāṇābhāvagauravādidūṣaṇasattvāt |
śarīrabhedaprayuktāvidyābhedādityuktadoṣāparihārāccetyalam |
atrāntaḥkaraṇavṛttiṃ vadata iti pāṭhe bahujīvavādina iti ṣaṣṭhayaikavacanaṃ vadanta iti pāṭhe prathamābahuvacanameva |
tasya kiṃ tarhyantaḥkaraṇamevetyanantaramityāhurityadhyāhṛtya tena saha tasyānvayo vācyaḥ |
nanūktānumāneṣu vipakṣe bādhakābhāvādaprayojakatvam |
na caivamekasyaiva svānanusaṃhitānusandhātṛtvāpattiḥ |
tatpratiyogikabhedamātrasya tatprayojakatvenāpyupapatteḥ pāramārthikabhedasyānāvaśyakatvādityāśaṅkate -- nanviti || upādhibhedamātreṇa pāramārthikabhedābhāve'pyavidyādyupādhikṛtabhedena |
duḥkhetyāderjīveśvarayorityādiḥ |
aprayojakatvam uktahetūnāmiti śeṣaḥ |
tadananusaṃhitānusandhātṛtvādau tatpratiyogikapāramārthikabheda eva prayojako vācyaḥ |
anyathā yogasiddhasaubharyādīnāṃ svecchāgṛhītanānādehāvacchinnacaitanyānusaṃhitānusandhātṛtvānupapatteḥ |
tathāca vipakṣe hetvanupapattireva bādhiketyāśayena samādhatte || maivamiti || upādherbhinnatvādityantamaupādhikabhede hetuḥ |
nanvantaḥkaraṇakṛtabhedasyaivānanusandhānaprayojakatvānna dehabhede'pyanusandhānānupapattirityata āha -- antaḥkaraṇasya ceti || nanu dehabhede'pyantaḥkaraṇaikyamastvata āha -- na hīti || antaḥkaraṇasyākāśādivadavyāptatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
atha tasya yāvaddehavyāpitve ko doṣa iti cet tarhi tadantarānuvartipuruṣāntarasyāpi tadantaḥkaraṇagrastatvena tatkṛtabhedābhāvāttatpuruṣīyānusandhānāpatteḥ |
kiṃ ca kimantaḥkaraṇaikye caitanyaikyaṃ niyāmakamuta dehaikyam |
nādyaḥ |
ayogināmapi antaḥkaraṇaikyaprasaṅgāt |
nāntyaḥ |
yogināmapi tadanupapatteḥ |
na ca tattaddehagatabhogānusandhānaṃ tattaddehāgatāntaḥkaraṇaikyaniyāmakamato noktadoṣa iti vācyam |
bhogānusandhānasyāntaḥkaraṇaikyanibandhanatvenānyonyāśrayāt |
siddhānte tvantaḥkaraṇāṃśānāṃ dehabhedena bhede'pyātmaikyasyānusandhānaniyāmakatvānna kaściddoṣaḥ |
astu tarhyavidyopādhikṛtabheda evānanusandhānaniyāmakaḥ |
caitramaitrāderavidyābhedādananusandhānasyāyogino dehabhede'pyavidyaikyādanusandhānasya copapatteḥ |
na ca bhedakābhāvātkathamavidyābheda iti vācyam |
tasyānāditvābhyupagamādityāśayenāśaṅkate -- tatreti || nānādehavati yoginītyarthaḥ |
avidyābhedasyānanusandhānaniyāmakatvaṃ kimekajīvavādamāśrityocyate kiṃ vā bahujīvavādam |
ādye doṣamāha -- iheti || ayogini caitrādāvityarthaḥ || eka eveti || avidyopādhirityanuvartate |
mata iti śeṣaḥ |
tathācaika eva hiraṇyagarbho jīvastadanye tu ghaṭādivatsvarūpeṇaiva mithyābhūtā iti mate parasparānanusandhānaprayojakasyāvidyopahitaparasparabhedasya caitramaitrādiṣu durvacatvena parasparānanusandhānānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ |
dvitīye doṣamaha || bahujīveti || suptau kāryātmanā'ntaḥkaraṇavṛttyasambhavāt kāraṇātmanetyuktam |
tathācāvidyopādherjīvabhāvahetutve tata eva suptau jīvabhāvopapatterantaḥkaraṇānuvṛttivaiyarthyamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanvavidyopādherjīvabhāvahetutvābhāve'pyuktavyavasthāniyāmakatvamastvata āha -- tataśceti || vyavasthāniyāmakamiti || caitanyaikye'pi parasparabhogānanusandhānaniyāmakaparasparabhedaprayojakatayā tasyaiva tadanusandhānamiti niyamanirvāhakamityarthaḥ |
aikyavāde niruktavyavasthānirvāhārthameva jīvabhedasya kalpitatvena tanniyāmakasyaiva tanniyāmakatvaprāpteriti bhāvaḥ || tacceti || svasambaddhadehabhedena ananusandhānaṃ parasparaprayojakamantaḥkaraṇaṃ cetyarthaḥ |
tathāca yogino nānādehagatabhogānusandhānānupapattitādavasthyamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanu pāramārthikabhedasādhakeṣūktānumāneṣu sarvatra heturastu sādhyaṃ mā bhūditi śaṅkāyāṃ bādhakābhāvādaprayojakatvam |
naca yadi jīvabrahmaṇorjīvānāñcānyonyaṃ pāramārthikabhedo na syāt tarhi jīvasya duḥkhānusandhānaṃ brahmaṇaśca tadananusandhānam ekasya jīvasya sukhamaparasya duḥkhamityādivyavasthā na syāditi bādhakaṃ vācyam |
uktavyāvasthāyāḥ pāramārthikabhedābhāve'pyaupādhikabhedenopapattyā tarkasyānyathāsiddhatvāditi śaṅkate -- nanvaupādhikabhedamātreṇeti || upādhikṛtabhedamātreṇetyarthaḥ || aupādhikabhedepīti || saubharyādiyogino hi yogaprabhāvādyugapadanekaśarīrāṇi bibhrati |
tatraikadehasthasya duḥkhe'nyadehasthasyāpi duḥkham ekasya sukhe'parasyāpi sukhaṃ bhavatīti vastugatiḥ |
tatropādhikṛtabhedasadbhāve'pyanyonyāduḥkhānusandhānadarśanenaupādhikabhedasya noktavyavasthāniyāmakatvamiti na tarkasyānyathāsiddhirityarthaḥ |
nanu yogisthale aupādhikabheda eva nāstītyabhedato'nyonyānusandhānaṃ yuktamityata āha -- atreti || dehasyopādherbhinnatvāditi sambandhaḥ |
nanu tatra dehalakṣaṇopādhisadbhāve'pyantaḥkaraṇopādherekatvādanyonyānusandhānamityata uktamantaḥkaraṇasyeti || antaḥkaraṇabhedamevopapādayati -- nahīti || antaḥkaraṇasya manorūpatvena manaādīndriyāṇāṃ pratiśarīraṃ bhinnatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu dehāntaḥkaraṇopādherbhede'pyavidyākhyopādherekatvādyogiṣvapyanyonyamanusandhānaṃ prakṛte tvavidyābhedānnānusandhānamiti śaṅkate -- tatreti || yogisthala ityarthaḥ |
kimekajīvavādimatānusāreṇocyate, uta bahujīvavādimatānusāreṇa ? ādya āha -- ihāpīti || tanmate'vacchedyajīvasyaikatvādavacchedakāvidyāyā apyekatvam |
tathācoktarītyā'pyanusandhānandurvāramiti bhāvaḥ |
dvitīye āha -- bahujīvavādinastviti || avidyāvacchedaḥ avidyāyāḥ paricchedaḥ |
satyaṃ bahujīvavādimate'vidyopādherbhinnatvamiti |
tathāpyavidyāvacchedasya jīvabhāvahetutvābhāvādevāvidyābhedasya noktavyavasthāniyāmakatvamityarthaḥ || antaḥkaraṇameveti || antaḥkaraṇāvaccheda eva || kuta etadityata uktam || suptāvapīti |
bahujīvavādī svagranthe suptikāle'pi kāraṇātmanā'ntaḥkaraṇaṃ tiṣṭhatīti pratipāditavān |
taccāntaḥkaraṇāvacchedasya jīvabhāvahetutve saṅgacchate |
yadyantaḥkaraṇāvacchedasya jīvabhāvahetutvantarhi suptikāle'ntaḥkaraṇavilayenāvacchedakābhāvājjīvatvāpagame tadānīmeva brahmaikyānubhavarūpamuktiprasaṅga iti śaṅkāyāṃ kāryātmanā'ntaḥkaraṇavilaye'pi kāraṇātmanā vidyamānatvena tadadhīnajīvatvānivṛtterna tadānīṃ muktiprasaṅga iti parihārasambhavāt |
nacaitadavidyāvacchedasya jīvabhāvahetutve saṅgacchate |
tathātve'vidyāyā evasuptāvavasthānakathanaprasaṅgāditi bhāvaḥ |
tadeva bhinnāntaḥkaraṇameva |
na hyekasya jīvabhāvahetutvamaparasya vyavasthāniyāmakatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
tataḥ kimityata āha -- tacceti || vyavasthāniyāmakaṃ bhinnāntaḥkaraṇamityarthaḥ |
uktam anyadehetyādinā |
tathāca yogiṣvantaḥkaraṇabhedasadbhāve'pi vyavasthābhāvānna tasya vyavasthāpakatvam |
tataśca vyavasthāniyāmakatvena pāramārthikabhedasyāvaśyakatvānnāprayojakatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
api ca pṛthivītvaṃ jalatvatejastvavāyutvamanastvetaradravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātibhinnam |
jātitvāt prameyatvādvā jalatvavat |
na ca pṛthivītvetaratvamupādhiḥ |
tasya pakṣetaratvāt |
anukūlatarkāṇāṃ vakṣyamāṇatvācca |
evaṃ jalatvatejastvavāyutvamanastvapakṣīkāreṇānumānacatuṣṭayamūhanīyam |
yatprāguktamātmatvaṃ nānāvyaktiniṣṭhaṃ jātitvātpṛthivītvavatityasyāsiddhiriti tatrātmatvasya jātitvasādhanadvārā''tmabhedaṃ sādhayitumanumānānyāha -- apicetyādinā ityādyanumānānyātmabhedaṃ sādhayantītyantena || naiyyāyikamatāvaṣṭambhenaivaitānyanumānāni pravṛttāni |
itthaṃ hi tanmataprakriyā |
pṛthivyaptejovāyvākāśakāladigātmamanāṃsi nava dravyāṇi |
tatrākāśatvakālatvadiktvāni na jātayaḥ |
ekavyaktikatvāt |
pṛthivītvādiṣaṭkaṃ tu jātiriti |
tatra pṛthivītvasya jalatvādicatuṣṭayetaradravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātibhedasiddhau tādṛśajāterātmatvānatirekāttasya jātitvasiddhau tannirvāhāyātmanānātvamaṅgīkāryamevetyātmabhedasiddhiḥ |
evamuttaratrāpi || jalatveti || jalatvādicatuṣṭeyatarā yā dravyatvasākṣadvyāpyā
jātistadbhinnamityarthaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte pṛthivītvajātibhinnatvena sādhyaparyavasānam |
sādhye sattājātivyudāsāya vyāpyeti viśeṣaṇam |
na ca tathā'pi prameyatvādivyāpyā bhavatyeveti vācyam |
jātivyāpyatvasya vivakṣitatvāt |
prameyatvāderajātitvāt |
vyāpyajātibhinnamityevokte rūpatvajātibhinnatvasiddhyā'rthāntaratā |
ato dravyatveti |
tāvatyevokte ghaṭatvamādāyārthāntaratā |
ataḥ sākṣāditi |
ghaṭatvaṃ ca dravyatvavyāpyapṛthivītvavyāpyamiti na dravyatvaṃ prati sākṣādvyāpyam |
tadvyāpyajātyavyāpyatve sati tadvyāpyasyaiva tatsākṣādvyāpyatvāt |
tāvatyevokte jalatvādijātibhinnatvasiddhyā'rthāntaratā |
ato jalatvatejastvavāyutvamanastvetareti jātiviśeṣaṇam |
nanu dravyatvasyāpi dravyatvavyāpyatvamāvaśyakameva |
anyathā śabdo'nityaḥ kṛtakatvādityatra kṛtakatvasyaivopādhitvāpatteḥ |
sādhyasamavyāptatvena tasyānityatvavyāpakatvāt |
svavyāpyatvānaṅgīkāre svābhinnasādhanāvyāpakatvācca |
ato'bhede'pi vyāpyavyāpakabhāvamaṅgīkṛtya kṛtakatve sādhanavyāpakatvamaṅgīkaraṇīyam |
tadvadatrāpi dravyatvasya dravyatvavyāpyatve'ṅgīkārye tadbhinnatvasiddhyā'rthāntarateti cenna |
vyāpyavyāpakabhāvasya bhinnāśrayatvaniyamāt |
prapañcamithyātvānumānakhaṇḍanaṭīkāyāṃ tattvodyotaṭīkāyāṃ ca tathaivoktatvāt |
na ca tathātve śabdānityatvānumāne kṛtakatvasyopādhitvāpattiḥ |
aniṣṭābhāvāt |
sādhanasya sādhyavyāpyatvagrāhakapramāṇavirodhena sādhanasadbhāve tadvyāpakasya sādhyasyāpi sattvaniyamātpakṣe vyabhicāravyāpyatvāsiddhyorvā bādhapratipakṣayorvā'nunnāyakatvāt |
ato dravyatvasya dravyatvavyāpyatvābhāvānna tadbhedamādāyārthāntarateti |
abhyupagamya cedamuktam |
vastutastu tatra kṛtakatvasyopādhitvaṃ nāstyeva |
sādhanāvyāpākatvasyaivābhāvāt |
na hi sādhanāvyāpakatvaṃ sādhanavyāpakatvābhāvaḥ kintu sādhānavadavṛttitvam |
na hi kṛtakatvaṃ śabdāvṛtti |
tadvati tadabhāvāyogāditi |
kecittvabhede'pi vyāpyavyāpakabhāvamabhyupetya dravyatvasya dravyatvavyāpyatve'pi dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvaṃ nāsti |
tadvyāpyāvyāpyatve sati tadvyāpyatvaṃ hi sākṣādvyāpyatvam |
na ca dravyatve tadasti |
dravyatvavyāpye dravyatve tadavyāpyatvābhāvāt |
ato nārthāntaratetyāhuḥ || pakṣetaratvāditi || pakṣetaratvarūpatvādityarthaḥ |
pakṣetaratvasyāpyupādhitve'numānamātrocchedaprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ |
nanu pakṣetaratvasyāpi vahniranuṣṇaḥ padārthatvādityādau vahnītaratvāderivopādhitvaṃ kinna syādityata āha -- anukūlatarkāṇāmiti || vakṣyamāṇatvāditi || ātmapadapravṛttinimittasyātmatvasya jātitvāṅgīkāre lāghavamityādineti śeṣaḥ |
tathā ca pṛthivītvaniṣṭhabhedapratiyogina ātmatvasya jātitvamanaṅgīkṛtyaupādhikadharmatvaṃ yadyaṅgīkriyeta tarhi tasyopādhijñānādhīnajñānatvena gauravaprasaṅga ityādyanukūlatarkasadbhāvādātmatvasya jātitve'vaśyamaṅgīkārye tadbhinnatvasya pṛthivītve sattvena pṛthivītvetaratvasya pakṣa eva sādhyavyāpakatābhaṅgānnopādhitvam |
vahnyanauṣṇyādyanumāne tvanukūlatarkābhāvādvahnītaratvāderupādhitvaṃ sambhavatīti bhāvaḥ || evamiti || jalatvaṃ pṛthivītvatejastvavāyutvamānastvetaradravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātibhinnam |
jātitvātprameyatvātpṛthivītvavadityādirūpeṇohyamityarthaḥ |
siddhāntyutprekṣitānumānāni prayuṅkte || pṛthivītvamityādinā || jātibhinnamiti || bhedapratiyogitvenātmatvajātisiddhāvanekānugatatvaniyamenātmanānātvasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
pṛthivītvasya svena bhedāyogājjalatvādīnāmitarāntaviśeṣaṇenaiva vāraṇādākāśakāladiśāmekadravyatve vivādābhāvena tatrāparyavasānāt |
dravyāṇāṃ navatvaniyamena jātyantarakalpane gauravāt nānyathāsiddhiḥ |
guṇatvādinā'nyathāsiddhivāraṇāya dravyatvavyāpyeti |
ghaṭatvādinā tadvāraṇāya sākṣādvyāpyeti |
buddhiguṇatvādinā tadvāraṇāya jātīti |
nanvasmābhirasatpratiyogikabhedasyāṅgīkārāduktajāterasattve'pi tadbhinnatvenānyathāsiddheriti cenna |
tādṛśasajjātibhinnatvasyaiva vivakṣitatvāt |
nyāyamatarītyā vaitadanumānapravṛttirityadoṣaḥ |
ata eva dravyanavatvādyupapattiḥ |
asmadrītyā tvahantvāhaṅkāratvādītaretyapi grāhyam || jalatvavaditi || pakṣīkṛtapṛthivītvabhinnaṃ taditi jñeyam || pakṣetaratvāditi || tasyopādhitve'numānamātrocchedaprasaktiriti bhāvaḥ |
nanu tejo'nuṣṇaṃ padārthatvādityādau bādhādyunnītatejovyatiriktatvamupādhirbhavatyeva bādhakābhāve pakṣetaratvasyopādhitvaṃ nyāyyamevetyata āha -- anukūlatarkāṇāmiti || evamiti || jalatvaṃ pṛthivītvatejastvavāyutvamanastvetaradravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātibhinnaṃ jātitvātpṛthivītvavadityādyūhyam |
ātmatvajātisādhanenātmanānātvaṃ setsyatītyāśayenāha -- api ceti || dṛṣṭānte pṛthivītvamādāya sādhyaṃ bodhyam |
pakṣe cātmatvajātimādāyetyātmanānātvasiddhiḥ |
na ca kālatvādibhedamādāyārthāntaratā |
dharmigrāhakamānena lāghavatarkasanāthena tadaikyasiddheḥ |
jalatvādītaratvaviśeṣaṇaṃ tu tadbhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya |
sattābhedamādāya tadvāraṇāya dravyatvavyāpyeti |
ghaṭatvādibhedamādāya tadvāraṇāya sākṣāditi |
nanvanukūlatarkābhāve pakṣetaratvasyāpi satpratipakṣonnāyakatayā dūṣaṇatvaṃ syādevetyata āha -- anukūlatarkāṇāmiti || evamiti || tadā pṛthivītvānyatvaṃ sādhye deyam |
jalatvādi ca pakṣīkartavyam |
pṛthivītvādi dṛṣṭāntīkartavyamiti bhāvaḥ |
|| api ceti || pṛthivītvamityādi |
jalatvādibhinnā yā dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyā jātiḥ pṛthivītvaṃ tadbhinnatvaṃ jalatvarūpe dṛṣṭānte hetvadhikaraṇajātyantareṣvapi vartate'to na kutrāpi vyabhicāraśaṅkā |
pakṣe ca hetubalāduktasādhyasiddhau satyāṃ pṛthivītve svabhedamādāyopapādayitumśakyatvādatiriktātmatvarūpajātisiddhidvārā''tmabhedasiddhiḥ |
atra jalatvādibhedapratītyasiddhatvātsiddhasādhanamarthāntaratā ca syāditi jalatvādiniveśaḥ |
pratiyogitayā jāteranupādāne gaganaparimāṇapṛthivījalādivṛttitritvabahutvādibhedamādāyokta(eva) doṣa iti jātitvena jāterniveśaḥ |
ghaṭatvādibhedamādāya taddoṣavāraṇāya dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyeti |
tattvaṃ ca dravyatvabhinnā svabhinnā ca dravyetarāvṛttiryā yā jātistattadavacchinnabhedakūṭavadvṛttitve sati dravyetarāvṛttitvam |
idaṃ ca jalatvapṛthivītvādau sphuṭam |
ghaṭatvādiṣu tādṛśabhedakūṭāntargatapṛthivībhedavatyavṛttitvānna teṣāṃ sākṣādvyāpyatvam |
na cedaṃ pṛthivītvajalatvādāvapi durghaṭam |
pṛthivītvabhinnā dravyatvabhinnā yā jātayo ghaṭatvādirūpāḥ pṛthivītvavyāpyā jalatvavyāpyāḥ kāścana jātayaśca |
tattadavacchinnabhedakūṭavattvaṃ na pṛthivyāṃ nāpi jalādau |
sarvasyā api pṛthivyā jaladeśca kiñcijjātyākrāntatvāt |
atastādṛśakūṭavadgaganādirguṇādikaṃ vā tatra pṛthivītvāderavṛttitvāditi vācyam |
dravyatvavyāpyavyāpyajāteḥ paramāṇuṣvasvīkāreṇa paramāṇumādāyaivopapādanasambhavāt |
kecittu tattadavacchinnabhedasāmānādhikaraṇyakūṭasyaiva niveśanīyatvādadoṣaḥ |
pṛthivītve ghaṭatvāvacchinnabhedasāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ paṭāntarbhāveṇa paṭatvāvacchinnabhedasāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ ghaṭāntarbhāveṇetyevaṃ krameṇa tattajjātyavacchinnabhedakūṭasāmānādhikaraṇyakūṭhaṃ vartata iti pṛthivītvajalatvādikaṃ dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyaṃ ghaṭatvādikaṃ tu naivaṃ svaśabdena ghaṭatvopādane tadbhinnadravyatvabhinnadravyetarāvṛttijātimadhye pṛthivītvasyāpi niviṣṭatayā tadavacchinnabhedasāmānādhikaraṇyavirahādityāhuḥ |
svabhinnatvadravyatvabhinnatvayordravyetarāvṛttijātau niveśānna svamādāya dravyatvamādāya ca pṛthivītvāderuktadravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvabhaṅgaḥ |
dravyetarāvṛttitvaniveśānna sattāmādāyoktadoṣaḥ |
jātitvena niveśānna pṛthivīmātravṛttiyāvattvarūpasaṅkhyāvacchinnabhedasāmānādhikaraṇyābhāvāt uktadoṣaḥ |
sattāyā guṇatvasya ca dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvavāraṇāya viśeṣyadalam |
yadvoktabhedakūṭaniveśe gauravātsvabhinnadravyatvabhinnadravyetarāvṛttijātitvāvacchinnābhāvavadvṛttitvaṃ viśeṣaṇadalārthaḥ |
tādṛśapaṭatvarūpayatkiñcijjātyabhāvavati ghaṭavattvasya vṛttitvāt sākṣādvyāpyatvavāraṇāya tādṛśajātisāmānyābhāvaniveśaḥ |
dravyatvavyāpyavyāpyajātīnāṃ paramāṇuṣvabhāvādevoktalakṣaṇanirvāhāya dravyatvasya dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvamuktarūpamiṣṭameva |
prakṛtānumāne dravyatvamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya dravyatvaniṣṭhabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakam adhikaṃ niveśya tādṛśavyāpyatvaṃ dravyatvādvyāvartanīyam |
nanūktasākṣādvyāpyatvasya svatvaghaṭitatvenānanugatatvaprāptyā dṛṣṭāntānurodhena svapadārthatayā pṛthivītvasyaiva niviṣṭatvena tena kathamātmatvasya siddhiḥ |
pratyuta pṛthivītvarūpapakṣe svabhedābhāvādbādha eva iti cenna |
svābhāvavadavṛttitvasvabhinnatvobhayasabhbandhena svaviśiṣṭajātimattvasambandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākasvābhāvavattvasvābhāvavadavṛttitvobhayasambandhena dravyatvavajjātitvameva dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātitvamanugatam |
sambandhaśarīre jātimattvaṃ ca svābhāvavadavṛttisvabhinnatvobhayasambandhenaitādṛśamahāsambandhaḥ dvitīyasambandhaścetyubhayaṃ dravyatvasya lakṣyabhūtajātiṣu niveśe bodhyam |
atra dravyatvasādhāraṇyena dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātitvarūpatvam |
prakṛte tadvāraṇāya svāśrayavṛttibhedapratiyogitāvacchedakatvaṃ vātṛtīyasambandhena vā niveśyoktasambandhatrayeṇa dravyatvaviśiṣṭajātitvaṃ tatsākṣādvyāpyajātitvaṃ vācyam |
yathāśrutānusāreṇa niṣkarṣe sambandhaprayojanādikamanusandheyam |
svatvasya sambandhaśarīre niveśābhāvaprayuktabādhakānusandhāne svābhāvavadavṛttitvasthāne'bhāvavadvṛttitvasambandhāvacchinnasya svavṛttyavacchedakatākabhedavattvameva sambandhaghaṭakaṃ bodhyamityeṣā dik || jātitvāditi || atra prameyatvarūpakevalānvayihetvantaropādānamuktasādhyasya vyabhicāraśaṅkā kutrāpi na kartavyeti sūcanāya |
jātitvahetorvyarthaviśeṣaṇakatvādidoṣābhāvastu `yaddhetuvyavacchedakatvetyādinā tattvodyotaṭīkāyā'muktaprakāreṇa bodhyaḥ |
pakṣetaratvasyāpyanukūlatakārbhāve'pyupādhitvaṃ vartata evetyāśaṅkyāha -- anukūlatarkāṇāmiti || ātmapadapravṛttinimittasya jātitve lādhavamupādhitve gauravamityādīnāmityarthaḥ |
idānīmātmatvaṃ nānāvyaktiniṣṭhaṃ jātitvāt ghaṭatvavadityādyanumāneṣu pūrvapakṣoktadoṣamuddidhīrṣurādyānumāne'siddhiśaṅkāparihārāyā'tmatvasya jātitvasādhakapramāṇaṃ tāvadāha -- api ceti || ityanumānānyātmatvajātisādhanadvārā''tmabhedaṃ sādhayantīti sambandhaḥ |
atra pṛthivītvabhedamādāya dṛṣṭānte sādhyasattvaṃ, pakṣe tu tadayogādātmatvajātibhedamādāyaiva sādhyaparyavasānāduddeśyasiddhiḥ |
na cākāśatvādijātyantarasiddhyā'rthāntaramāśaṅkyam |
tarkavirodhena tadasiddhervakṣyamāṇatvāt |
ātmaikyagrāhakatarkāṇāṃ ca balavatpramāṇaviruddhatvenābhāsatvāditi |
etena vibhutvādijātyantarasiddhyā'rthāntaramityapāstam |
dravyatvayāpyajātirākāśādyavṛttiḥ sattādravyatvātiriktajātitvāt pṛthivītvavadityāditarkaviruddhatvāt |
saṃskāraśūnyāvṛttitvena jātirviśeṣaṇīyetyapyāhuḥ |
atra ca jalatvādijātibhedamādāya siddhasādhanavāraṇāya jalatvādyatiriktattvaṃ jātiviśeṣaṇam |
ghaṭatvādibhedamādāya taddoṣavāraṇāya dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyeti || dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvaṃ ca dravyatvavyāpyajātyavyāpyatve sati dravyatvavyāpyatvam |
ghaṭatvādivāraṇāya satyantam |
dravyatvavyāpyabhūtatvādivyāpyasya pṛthivītvādeḥ saṅgrahāya tatra jātipadam |
guṇatvādivāraṇāya viśeṣyadalam |
atha vyāpyatvasya tadabhāvavadavṛttitvarūpatvena dravyatvasyāpi dravyatvavyāpyatvasambhavāttadbhedamādāya siddhasādhanam |
yattu dravyatvasya dravyatvyāpyadravyatvarūpajātivyāpyatayā na dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvamiti |
tanna |
tathāsati pṛthivītvāderapi dravyatvavyāpyadravyatvarūpajātivyāpyatayā dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātyasiddhyāpatteḥ |
dravyatvavyāpyadravyatvabhinnajātyavyāpyatvavivakṣaṇe tu dravyatvasyāpi dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvākṣateḥ |
evaṃ dravyatvavyāpyajātyavyāpyatvasya dravyatvavyāpyajātyabhāvavadvṛttitvarūpatayā pṛthivītvāderasaṅgrahaḥ |
pṛthivyāṃ dravyatvavyāpyajātisattvena tadabhāvāsambhavāt |
dravyatvavyāpyayatkiñcijjātyabhāvavadvṛttitvavivakṣaṇe ca ghaṭatvāderapi saṅgrahaprasaṅga iti cenmaivam |
nyūnavṛttitvarūpavyāpyatvasya vivakṣitatvāt |
nyūnavṛttitvaṃ ca tatsamānādhikaraṇabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakatve sati tatsamānādhikaraṇatvam |
sattāguṇatvayoḥ dravyatvavyāpyatvavāraṇāya satyantaviśeṣyabhāgayoḥ praveśaḥ |
dravyatvasya ca dravyatvasamānādhikaraṇabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakatvābhāvānna dravyatvavyāpyatvam |
dravyatvavyāpyajātyavyāpyatvaśarīre ca dravyatvavyāpyatvaṃ dravyatvasamānādhikaraṇabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakatvamātram |
adhikasya vaiyarthyāt |
tadavyāpyatvaṃ ca tatsamānādhikaraṇatve sati tatsamānādhikaraṇabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakaṃ yattadanyatvam |
svasamānādhikaraṇabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakatvasvasamānādhikaraṇatvobhayasambandhena tādṛśajātiviśiṣṭānyatvamiti yāvat |
pṛthivītvāderdravyatvavyāpyajalatvasamānādhikaraṇabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakatvāt tathāvidhaghaṭatvādisamānādhikaraṇatvāccāsaṅgrahāpattirato daladvayaniveśaḥ |
itthaṃ ca dravyatvavyāpyaṃ pṛthivītvādi |
tadvyāpyaṃ ghaṭatvādi |
tadanyatvaṃ ca pṛthivītvāderiti tatsaṅgrahaḥ |
gaganāvṛttiviśeṣaguṇavattvādidharmabhedamādāya siddhasādhanavāraṇāya jātipadam |
yadyapi tādṛśajātiniṣṭhapratiyogitākasya pṛthivītvaghaṭobhayabhedasya pṛthivītve'pi sattvātsiddhasādhanam |
tādṛśajātitvānadhikaraṇatvasādhane ca pṛthivītvasyāpi tādṛśajātitvādhikaraṇatvādbādhaḥ |
tathāpi tādṛśajātipratiyogikapratiyogyavṛttibhedasādhanādadoṣaḥ |
ubhayabhedasya pratiyogivṛttitvāt |
jātitvaṃ cākhaṇḍopādhiḥ |
ato gurudharmasya hetutānavacchedakatve nityānekasamavetatvasya hetutvāsambhave'pi na kṣatiḥ |
ādheyatvamātraṃ vā hetutvena vivakṣitam |
prameyatvādvetyatra praśabdaḥ prakṛtasādhanasya prāmāṇikatvadyotanārthaḥ |
|| na ceti || yadyapi pakṣe sādhyasandehānna pakṣetaratve sādhyavyāpakatvaniścayaḥ sambhavati |
tathāpi tasya sandigdhopādhitvamavyāhatam |
na ca tasya na pratipakṣonnāyakatvaṃ, tadadhīnapratipakṣasandehasya ca sādhyasandehatayā na virodhitvamiti vācyam |
svārasikasandehasyāvirodhitve'pyāhitasandehasyānumānavirodhitvāditi bhāvaḥ |
pakṣetaratvasya sandigdhopādhitve'pi na dūṣakatvam |
anyathā'numitimātrocchedāpatterityāśayenāha -- tasyeti || nanvanukūlatarkasattva eva sandigdhopādheradūṣakatvam |
anyathā vahniranuṣṇaḥ kṛtakatvādityādau vahnītaratvopādherdūṣakatvānupapatterityata āha -- anukūlatarkāṇāmiti || `vipakṣe gauravaprasaṅgarūpāṇā'mityādiḥ |
|| evamiti || jalatvaṃ pṛthivītvatejastvavāyutvamanastvetaradravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātibhinnaṃ jātitvāt pṛthivītvavadityādiprakāreṇetyarthaḥ |
idamupalakṣaṇam |
pṛthivītvaṃ jalādyavṛttidravyavṛttikiñcijjātimadavṛtti, dravyatvavyāpyajātitvājjalatvādivat |
pṛthivītvaṃ gandhajñānānyataravadvṛttidravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātibhinnaṃ, meyatvāddhaṭatvavadityādikamapyātmatvajātisādhakamūhanīyam |
idānīmātmatvasya jātitvasādhanadvārā''tmanāṃ bhedasādhakānumānānyāha -- apicetyādinā ātmabhedaṃ sādhayatītyantena || atra pṛthivītvasya jalatvādicatuṣṭayetaradravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātibhinnatvasiddhau tādṛśajāterātmatvānatirekāttasya jātitvasiddhau tannirvāhāyā''tmanāmanekatvamaṅgīkāryamityātmabhedasiddhiḥ |
evamagre'pyupayogo boddhavyaḥ || jalatveti || jalatvādibhya itarā yā dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātiḥ tadbhinnamityarthaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte pṛthivītvabhinnatvena sādhyaparyavasānam |
sādhye jātibhinnamityukte sattājātibhinnatvasiddhyā'rthāntaram |
ato vyāpyeti || tasyāḥ dravyādītaravṛttitayā vyāpakatvāttatparihāraḥ |
naca sāpi prameyatvavyāpyajātirbhavatyeveti vācyam |
jātiṃ prati vyāpyatvasya vivakṣitatvāt |
vyāpyajātibhinnamityukte rūpatvabhinnatvasiddhyā'rthāntaram |
tasya rūpamātravṛttitvena vyāpyajātitvāt |
ato dravyatveti || tasya ca guṇatvavyāpyatvāttatparihāraḥ |
dravyatvavyāpyajātibhinnamityukte ghaṭatvabhinnatvasiddhyā'rthāntaramityata uktam || sākṣāditi || ghaṭatvaṃ ca na dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātiḥ |
tadvyāpyajātyavyāpyatve sati tadvyāpyatvaṃ hi tatsākṣādvyāpyatvam |
yathā pṛthivitvaṃ dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyam |
tat dravyatvam |
tadvyāpyajātiḥ jalatvādiḥ |
tadvyāpyatve sati dravyatvavyāpyatvāt |
na tathā ghaṭatvam |
tat dravyatvam |
tadvyāpyaṃ pṛthivītvam |
tadvyāpyatvena tadvyāpyāvyāpyatvābhāvāt |
dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātibhinnamityukte jalatvādīnāmapi dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātitvena tadbhinnatvasiddhyā''rthāntaramato jalatvatejastvavāyutvamanastvetareti |
ataḥ sarvaṃ sārthakam |
nanu tathāpi jalatvādītarasākṣādvyāpyajātirdravyatvaṃ tadbhinnatvasiddhyā''rthāntaram |
naca dravyatvasya dravyatvaṃ na vyāpyam |
abhede vyāpyavyāpakabhāvānaṅgīkārāt |
anyathā śabdo nityaḥ kṛtakatvādityatra kṛtakatvasyopādhitvaprasaṅgāt |
kṛtakatvasyānityatvavyāpakatvāt |
abhede vyāpyavyāpakabhāvānabhyupagamena kṛtakatvāvyāpakatvācca |
atastadaṅgīkāre tu nāyaṃ doṣaḥ |
kṛtakatvasya kṛtakatvavyāpakatvena sādhanavyāpakatvāt |
ataḥ kathamiti cenna |
dravyatvasya dravyatvavyāpyatve'pi sākṣādvyāpyatvābhāvāt |
tathāhi |
tadvyāpyajātyavyāpyatve sati tadvyāpyatvaṃ tatsākṣādvyāpyatvam |
naca dravyatve tadasti |
tat dravyatvam |
tadvyāpyaṃ dravyatvaṃ tadvyāpyatvaṃ dravyatve vartate natu tadvyāpyatvam ato na doṣa iti jalatvavaditi || naceti || jalatve sādhyavyāpakatvāt pakṣe sādhanāvyāpakatvāt pṛthivītve taratvamupādhiriti netyarthaḥ |
kuta ityata āha -- pakṣetaratvāditi || pakṣetaratvasyāpyupādhitvaṃ kiṃ na syāditi cet tathātve parvato vahnimāndhūmavattvādityatra mahānase sādhyavyāpakatvātpakṣe sādhanāvyāpakatvācca parvatetaratvamupādhissyāt |
tathācānumānamātrocchedaḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu viṣamoyamupanyāsaḥ parvato vahnimānityatra yadi vahnirna syāt tarhi dhūmo'pi na syādityanukūlatarkabalātparvate vahnisiddhau tatra parvatetaratvābhāvena sādhyāvyāpakatvātparvatetaratvasya nopādhitvam |
yathāhuḥ |
`anukūlena tarkeṇa sanāthe sati sādhane |
sādhyavyāpakatābhaṅgātpakṣe nopādhisambhava' iti |
sanāthe sasvāmika sādhane sati pakṣe sādhyavyāpakatābhaṅgāditi sambandhaḥ |
prakṛte cānukūlatarkābhāvāt |
vahniranuṣṇaḥ padārthatvādityatra vahnītaratvasyeva (pakṣetaratvasyāpi) pṛthivītvetaratvasyāpyupādhitvaṃ yuktamityata āha -- anukūlatarkāṇāmiti || ātmapadapravṛttinimittasya jātitvāṅgīkāre lāghavamityādineti śeṣaḥ |
tathā cātrāpyanukūlena tarkeṇa balena pakṣe sādhyasiddhau tatra pṛthivītvetaratvābhāvena sādhyavyāpakatvānnāyamupādhiriti bhāvaḥ |
pṛthagupanyāse gauravādāha -- evamiti || jalatvaṃ pṛthivītvatejastvavāyutvamanastvetaradravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātibhinnaṃ jātitvāt pṛthivītvavadityādyūhyamityarthaḥ ||
dravyatvaṃ pṛthivītvajalatvatejastvavāyutvamanastvetarajātiṃ prati sākṣādvyāpakaṃ sattāsākṣādvyāpyajātitvāt guṇatvavat |
sarvāṇi manāṃsi sparśānadhikaraṇadravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātimadvyatiriktāni dravyatvāt sattādhikaraṇatvāt prameyatvādvāpṛthivīvat |
digbhedavādināṃ tu sarvatra diktvānadhikaraṇatvagrahaṇaṃ kāryam |
sarvā pṛthivī jalatvatejastvavāyutvamanastvānadhikaraṇadravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātimadvyatiriktā dravyatvāt sattādhikaraṇatvāt jalavat |
sattā dravyatvānyātmaniṣṭhajātyanyā jātitvāt prameyatvāt ghaṭatvavat |
dravyatvaṃ vā sattānyātmaniṣṭhajātyanyat (ātmaniṣṭhasattānyajātyanyat) jātitvāt ghaṭatvavat |
ātmāṇutvavādināṃ tu ātmā dravyatvavyāpyajātyadhikaraṇam avibhudravyatvāt pṛthivīvadityadyanumānānyātmabhedaṃ sādhayanti |
|| guṇatvavaditi || atra rūpatvajātimādāya tadvyāpakatvena sādhyaparyavasānaṃ bodhyam |
sādhye jātiṃ prati vyāpakamityevoktau ghaṭatvamādāyārthāntaratā |
ataḥ sākṣāditi |
tāvatyevokte pṛthivītvādipañcakamādāyārthāntaratā |
tannirāsāyetarāntam |
hetau jātitvādityevoktau sattāyāṃ vyabhicāraḥ |
vyāpyajātitvādityuktāvapi punaḥ sattāyāmeva vyabhicāraḥ |
prameyatvaṃ prati vyāpyatvāttasyāḥ |
ataḥ sattāvyāpyajātitvāditi |
tāvatyevokte ghaṭatvādau vyabhicārastadvāraṇāya sākṣāditi || sarvāṇi manāṃsīti || atra manāṃsītyeva nirdeśe katipayamanasāṃ pakṣatvasambhavena teṣu sparśānāghikaraṇāni yāni dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyamanastvajātimantyavaśiṣṭamanāṃsi tadvyatiriktatvasiddhyā'rthāntaratā |
ataḥ sarvāṇīti |
itthaṃ ca jātipadasyātmatvajātimādāyaiva paryavasānam |
sparśānadhikaraṇeti viśeṣaṇena pṛthivītvajalatvatejastvavāyutvajātimatāṃ pṛthivyādīnāṃ vyāvartitatvāt |
sarveṣāmapi manasāṃ pakṣīkṛtatvena manastvajātimatāṃ manasāmapi grahītumaśakyatvāt |
svasya svavyatiriktatvāyogāt |
ākāśakāladiśāṃ ca dravyatvavyāpyajātyanāśrayatvāt |
sādhye jātimadvyatiriktānītyevokte sattādijātimadguṇakarmavyatiriktatvasiddhyā'rthāntaram |
ato vyāpyeti |
tarhyapi sattājātivyāpyaguṇatvakarmatvajātimadguṇakarmavyatiriktatvasiddhyā'rthāntaram |
ato dravyatveti |
tarhyapi dravyatvavyāpyaghaṭatvādijātimaddhaṭādivyatiriktatvasiddhyā'rthāntaram |
ataḥ sākṣāditi |
athā'pi dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyapṛthivītvādijātimatpṛthivyādicatuṣṭayātiriktatvasiddhyā'rthāntaram |
ataḥ sparśānadhikaraṇeti |
nanvevaṃ sparśānadhikaraṇadravyatvavyāpyajātimatpṛthivyādicatuṣṭayātiriktamityevālam |
sākṣāditi vyartham |
dravyatvaparamparāvyāpyaghaṭatvādijātimatāṃ ghaṭādīnāṃ sparśānadhikaraṇapadenaiva vyāvartitatvāditi cet |
satyam |
tathā'pyātmatvasya dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvasiddhyarthaṃ taditi kecidāhuḥ |
eke tvabhede'pi vyāpyavyāpakabhāvamaṅgīkṛtya dravyatvasyāpi dravyatvavyāpyatvamastyeveti dravyatvavyāpyajātipadena dravyatvajātereva grahaṇasambhavena tatsiddhyā punararthāntaratā syāt |
atastannirāsāya sākṣāditi |
sākṣādvyāpyatvaṃ ca tadvyāpyāvyāpyatve sati tadvyāpyatvam |
dravyatvasya ca dravyatvavyāpyatvādeva tadvyāpyāvyāpyatvābhāvena dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvābhāvāt |
ātmatvajātimādāyaiva tasya paryavasānamiti sākṣātpadakṛtyāmācakṣate |
vastutastvabhede vyāpyavyāpakabhāvo nāstyeveti pakṣe sākṣātpadamanapekṣitameveti bodhyam || pṛthivīvaditi || tatra tādṛśamanastvajātimādāya tadvanmanovyatiriktatvena sādhyaṃ bodhyam |
nanu dikṣu svābhāvikabhedavādināṃ mate diktvasya jātitvopagamena pṛthivītvādipakṣakānumāneṣu diktvabhinnatvasiddhyā dravyatvapakṣakānumāne diktvajātiṃ prati sākṣādvyāpakatvasiddhyā manaḥpakṣakānumāne diktvajātimaddigbhedasiddhyā'rthāntarataiveti tatrāha -- digbhedavādināṃ tviti || tānuddiśyetyarthaḥ || diktvānadhikaraṇatvagrahaṇamiti || upalakṣaṇametat |
diktvagrahaṇamityapi grāhyam |
atra sarvatretyuktatvāt |
tathā ca manaḥpakṣakānumāne sparśānadhikaraṇetivat diktvānadhikaraṇeti pūraṇīyam |
itarānumāneṣu jalatvādisamakakṣatayā diktveti pūraṇīyamiti bhāvaḥ |
evameva vakṣyamāṇapṛthivīpakṣakānumāne'pi diktvānadhikaraṇamityapi draṣṭavyam |
ata evātra sarvatretyuktam || sarvā pṛthivīti || atrāpi sarvapadakṛtyaṃ yatkiñcitpṛthivīpakṣīkāre uktarūpapṛthivītvajātimadavaśiṣṭapṛthivīvyatiriktatvasiddhyā'rthāntaravāraṇameva pūrvavadanusandheyam |
sādhye jalādibhedamādāyārthāntaratānirāsāyānadhikaraṇāntam |
guṇādivyatiriktatvamādāya tadvāraṇāya dravyatvavyāpyeti jātiviśeṣaṇam |
atrāpi sākṣātpadamātmatvasya dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvasiddhyarthamityāhuḥ || jalatvavaditi || atra pṛthivībhedamādāya sādhyaparyavasānam || satteti || dravyatvānyā ātmaniṣṭhā yā jātistadanyetyarthaḥ |
atrātmaniṣṭhadravyatvajātivyudāsāya dravyatvānyeti || pṛthivītvādivāraṇāyātmaniṣṭheti || sā ca jātirātmatvaṃ vinā nānyā bhavitumarhatīti bhāvaḥ || ghaṭatvavaditi || atra sattājātimādāya tadanyatvaṃ sādhyaṃ bodhyam || dravyatvaṃ veti || atrāpi dravyatvabhedamādāya dṛṣṭānte sādhyaparyavasānam || avibhudravyatvāditi || atrākāśādāvanaikāntyavāraṇāyāvibhviti |
guṇādau tadvāraṇāya dravyatvāditi |
naiyyāyikādibhirātmano'ṇutvānaṅgīkārāttadrītyā hetorasiddhiśaṅkāvāraṇāyādāvevātmāṇutvavādināmityuktam || ityādyanumānānīti || atrādipadenātmatvaṃ jātiḥ svāśrayarūḍhapadapravṛttinimittatvāt ghaṭatvavat |
ātmapadaṃ svavācyaniṣṭhasattādravyatvānyajātipravṛttinimittakaṃ rūḍhatvāt ghaṭapadavat |
ātmā vā ātmapadapravṛttinimittajātyāśrayastatpadarūḍhārthatvāt |
yo yatpadarūḍhārthaḥ sa tatpadapravṛttinimittajātyāśrayaḥ |
yathā ghaṭapadarūḍhārtho ghaṭo ghaṭapadapravṛttinimittaghaṭatvarūpajātyāśraya ityādīnyanumānāni grāhyāṇi |
sākṣādvyāpakamiti || tadvyāpyatayā''tmatvajātisiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
ghaṭatvādivyāpakatvenānyathāsiddhivāraṇāya pratijñāyāṃ sākṣāditi |
vyāpyāntaraśūnye caramavyāpye śarāvodañcanatvādau vyabhicāravāraṇāya hetau sākṣādvyāpyeti |
jātipadaṃ svarūpakathanam || jātimadvyatiriktānīti || vyatirekapratiyogiviśeṣaṇatayā''tmatvajātisiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
mano'ntarādanyathāsiddhivāraṇāya sarvāṇīti pakṣaviśeṣaṇam |
athā'pi manasāmanyonyaṃ bhedenānyathāsiddhiriti cenna |
manastvāvacchinnavyatirekādhikaraṇatvasya sādhyatvāt |
na caivaṃ sādhyavaikalyam |
manastvāvacchinneti sthāne dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyeti sāmānyāśrayaṇopapatteḥ |
yadvā dravyatvasākṣādyāpyajātimadvyatiriktānītyasyoktajātyavacchinnapratiyogitākavyatirekādhikaraṇānītyarthaḥ |
pṛthivyādicatuṣṭayavāraṇāya sparśānadhikaraṇeti |
jātyādivyatiriktasiddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya jātimaditi |
guṇādibhyastadvāraṇāya dravyatvavyāpyeti || pṛthivīvaditi || uktarūpamanobhinnā seti jñeyam |
bhedavādināṃ tasyāpi jātitvena sarvatrānthāsiddhirityata āha -- satteti || atra dravyatvavyāpyātmaniṣṭhatvenānyathāsiddhivāraṇāya dravyatvānyeti |
uktarūpasattābhinnaṃ taditi jñeyam |
dravyatvamiti pūrvavat || ātmeti || sattādravyatvābhyāmarthāntaravāraṇāya dravyatvavyāpyeti |
ākāśādau vyabhicāravāraṇāya hetāvavibhviti |
etadasiddhirmābhūdityādāvātmāṇutvavādināmityuktam || ityādīti || pṛthivīguṇā jalatejovāyumanastvāsamānādhikaraṇajātisamānādhikaraṇaguṇabhinnā guṇatvādrūpavat |
buddhiḥ sattādravyatvānyajātisamānādhikaraṇā śabdānyaguṇatvādrūpavat |
pṛthivī jalādītaraviśeṣaguṇādhikaraṇajātimadvibhinnā prameyatvājjalavat |
ātmatvamātmasamānādhikaraṇapratiyogitākasamānādhikaraṇaṃ dharmatvātpaṭavadityādyūhyam |
|| dravyatvamiti || ghaṭatvādivyāpakatvamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya sākṣāditi |
itaraṃ tadvyāpakatvamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya |
vyāpakatvamadhikadeśavṛttitvam |
tena dravyatvasya svavyāpakatvamādāya na siddhasādhanatā |
jātiṃ prati vyāpakatvameva viśiṣṭajātivyāpakatvam |
tena guṇakarmapṛthivyanyatvaviśiṣṭasattāvyāpakatvamādāya na siddhasādhanatā |
aparajātau vyabhicāravāraṇāya sattāsākṣādvyāpyeti |
tadvyāpyajātyavyāpyatve sati tadvyāpyatvaṃ tasmātsākṣādvyāpyatvam || guṇatvādivaditi || guṇatvādisākṣādvyāpakatmādāya sādhyaṃ bodhyam |
karmatve'pyutkṣepaṇatvādisākṣādvyāpakatvamādāya sādhyasattvānna vyabhicāraḥ || sarvāṇi manāṃsīti || katipayamanasāṃ pakṣīkāreṇa mano'ntaravyatiriktatvamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya sarvāṇīti |
sparśānadhikaraṇeti jātimato viśeṣaṇam |
rūpādivyatiriktatvamādāya tadvāraṇāya dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyeti |
sākṣātpadamātmatvasya dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvasiddhyarthamiti || pṛthivīvaditi || manovyatiriktatvamādāya sādhyaṃ bodhyam || digbhedavādināmiti || svābhāvikadigbhedavādināmavyavahitānumāne sparśānadhikaraṇadiktvānadhikaraṇadravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātimadvyatiriktānīti sādhyaṃ kartavyam |
diktvabhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya sarvānumāneṣu diktvetaratvaṃ sādhye viśeṣaṇaṃ deyamiti bhāvaḥ || sarvā pṛthivīti || yatkiñcitpṛthivīpakṣīkāre pṛthivyantarabhedamādāya siddhasādhanamiti sarveti |
adhikaraṇāntaṃ jalādibhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya |
guṇatvādivyatiriktatvamādāya tadvāraṇāya dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyeti |
sākṣātpadamātmatvasya sākṣādvyāpyatvasiddhyartham || jalatvavaditi|| pṛthivībhedamādāya sādhyaṃ bodhyam || satteti || dravyatvabhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya dravyatvānyeti |
guṇatvādibhedamādāya tadvāraṇāyātmaniṣṭheti |
ātmabhedābhāve'pyātmavṛttibhedamādāya tadvāraṇāya jātīti || ghaṭatvavaditi || sattābhedamādāya sādhyaṃ bodhyam || dravyatvaṃ ceti || pūrvavadeva kṛtyāni |
dṛṣṭānte tu dravyatvamādāya sādhyaṃ bodhyam || ātmāṇutvavādināmiti || svarūpāsiddhiparihārāyedam |
gaganādau vyabhicāravāraṇāyāvibhviti |
guṇādau vyabhicāravāraṇāya viśeṣyam || ātmabhedaṃ sādhayantīti || ātmatvajātisādhaneneti bhāvaḥ |
|| dravyatvamiti || sākṣādvyāpakamiti || tadvyāpakībhūtasvabhinnaṃ pratyavyāpakatve sati tadvyāpakamityarthaḥ |
idaṃ ca ghaṭatvādivyāpakatāmādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya sākṣādvyāpyeti |
ghaṭatvādau vyabhicāravāraṇāya samavāyena sattānirūpitavyāpyatvalābhāya hetau jātitvotkīrtanam |
anyathā kālikena ghaṭatvasya janyamātre sattvātsattāvyāpyajāteḥ samavāyena kasyā api sarvatrābhāvāddhaṭatvasyāpi sattāvyāpyajātyavyāpyatvagarbhitasākṣādvyāpyatvaṃ prasajyeta |
sattāvyāpyatvasya sambandhasāmānyena niveśe kālikena tathātvābhāvādasiddhiśca syādatastadāvaśyakam || sarvāṇi manāṃsīti |
atraitadanumānātpūrvaṃ dravyatvaṃ pṛthivītvajalatvatejastvavāyutvamanastvabhinnadravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātibhinnaṃ prameyatvāditi pāṭho dṛśyate |
tatra dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvaṃ dravyatvasādhāraṇameva nirvācyam |
tenātmatvajātisiddheḥ prāk sādhyāprasiddhiḥ prakṛtānumānasiddhātmatvajātāveva vyabhicārādasyānumānasyāsadanumānatvamiti śaṅkānavakāśaḥ |
na cānumityutpatteḥ pūrvamātmatvajāterevānupasthitatvānna tatra vyabhicāragrahāvakāśaḥ siddhyanantaraṃ tasyā jāteḥ siddhatvenaiva vyabhicāragrahaṃ pratyupajīvyatvena vyabhicāragraheṇa tadvighaṭanānupayoga iti vācyam |
tatsiddhyuttaramapyuktavyabhicāragrahasya parāmarśāprāmāṇyagrahasampādanadvāroktānumityaprāmāṇyagrahajananena prakṛtasādhyāsiddhivighaṭakasyā'karasiddhatvāt |
sarvāṇi manāṃsītyatra manastvāvacchedena sādhyasiddheruddeśyatālābhāya sarvāṇīti |
tatsāmānādhikaraṇyena sādhyasiddheruddeśyatve 'numityanantaraṃ manastvasāmānādhikaraṇyena sādhyābhāvasandehānivṛttau tatraiva prakṛtahetorvyabhicārasandehāhitāprāmāṇyasandehasya prakṛtaparāmarśajananenānumityaprāmāṇyasandehaḥ syāt |
evaṃ kiñcinmanovattadanyamanasāmapi hetu(mattva)sattve'pi sādhyābhāvaḥ kinna syādityaprayojakatāśaṅkā ca syāttannirāsāya yatnāntarakaraṇam |
prakṣālanāddhīti nyāyenānucitamiti manastvāvacchedenaiva sādhyasādhanasyocitatvāt |
yatkiñcinmanobhedasyānyamanasi sattvātsiddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya sarvāṇītyuktamiti tu na sat |
tathātve'pi tanmanasyetanmanobhedarūpasādhyasyaitanmanasi tanmanobhedarūpasādhyasya sattvena siddhasādhanatā'nuddhārāt |
na caika eva bhedaḥ sarvapakṣeṣu nāstīti vācyam |
kimataḥ sādhyatāvacchedakākrāntavyaktīnāṃ madhye ekaikatra pakṣe ekaikasyā vyakteḥ sattvamātreṇa siddhasādhanatāyā āvaśyakatvāt |
anyathā parvatatvāvacchedena vahniniścayadaśāyāṃ siddhasādhanatābhāvaprasaṅgāt |
na caikabheda evasādhya iti vācyam |
bhede ekatvasaṅkhayārūpaguṇābhāvena sādhyāprasiddheḥ |
anyādṛśasya niveśe guṇasya vā tatrāṅgīkāre'pyekatvasyāvyāvartakatayaikabhedatvarūpasādhyatāvacchedakasyāpi sarvabhedeṣvavyāhateḥ |
anyādṛśaṃ tu prakṛtopayogi nirvoḍhumaśakyam |
niyataikavyakterekasādhyatetyasat pakṣe dṛṣṭānte caikabhedasyānupādānāt |
na ca manastvāvacchedena sādhane'pi tattanmanobhedāḥ tādṛśajātimatsparśānadhikaraṇapratiyogitākā bhavantyeveti tānādāya siddhasādhanatā |
tādṛgjātimatsparśānādhikaraṇatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedastu na niveśayituṃ śakyate |
tādṛśajātimatsparśānadhikaraṇaṃ neti pratītisiddhabhedarūpasya sādhyamanastvajātimati manasyabhāvenābādhāt |
atastādṛśayatkiñcidekajātīyāvyāpakapratiyogitākabheda eva sādhyata iti vācyam |
tathā ca kathaṃ tanmanobhedamādāya siddhasādhanatā sarvatvena niveśottaramiti vācyam |
hanta tarhi tadadāne'pi kathaṃ siddhasādhanatā |
bhavaduktasādhyapariṣkāre vyāsajyavṛttidharmāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedasya pratyekāṅgīkāre siddhasādhanataiva |
manoghaṭabhedasya manasyabādhāt |
yatkiñcittvaniveśādananugame gauravaṃ ca |
sparśānadhikaraṇavṛttitādṛśajātyavacchinnapratiyogitākabhedatvena sādhyatvāttatra tanmanobhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāprasakteḥ |
tasmāduktaprayojanameva yuktam |
pṛthivībhedādikamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya sparśānadhikaraṇeti |
teṣāmapyutpattikālāvacchedena sparśābhāvavattvātpunastaddoṣavāraṇāya sparśānadhikaraṇatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvaniveśaḥ |
dravyavṛttijātiniveśe'pi sattāyāstathātvāttadāśrayaguṇabhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya dravyatvavyāpyatvaniveśaḥ |
dravyatvasyāpi dravyatvavyāpyatvāttadāśraya gaganādibhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya sākṣāditi |
sākṣāttvaṃ ca mukhyatvaṃ prakṛte vivakṣitam |
tacca bhedaghaṭitam |
abhede vyāpyavyāpakabhāvasya prācīnairanaṅgīkṛtatvena tasya mukhyatvāt |
tathā ca dravyatvabhinnadravyatvavyāpyajātimatsparśādhikaraṇamiti labhyate |
gaganādikaṃ ca na tatheti siddhasādhanatāvakāśaḥ |
idaṃ tu yathāśrutasādhyānusāreṇāpātataḥ sparśānadhikaraṇadravyavṛttijātyavacchipratiyogitākabhedaniveśasyāvaśyakatvena sattādravyatve samudāyoktadoṣāsaṃsparśāt |
parantu guṇabhedādikamupādāya doṣavāraṇāya dravyavṛttitvamātraṃ jātau deyamityāhuḥ |
anye tvatra vyāsajyavṛttidharmāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedānaṅgīkārāttādṛśajātimatpratiyogitākabhedatvenaiva(tvena) sādhyatā |
tattanmanobhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇaṃ tu sādhyībhūtabhede pakṣatāvacchedakavyāpakatābhānasyāvaśyakatvāttatra pakṣatāvacchedakībhūtamanastvavanniṣṭhabhedapratiyogitānavacchedakatvalakṣaṇaṃ bhedaghaṭitameva vyāpakatvaṃ sādhye vā tatsādhyatāvacchekasambandhe vā bhāsate na tu manoniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitānavacchedakasādhyatāvacchedakavattvarūpam |
idaṃ caikaikabhedasya tādṛśabhedaghaṭitavyāpakatvābhāvādeva na siddhasādhanatāvakāśaḥ |
tathā ca sparśānadhikaraṇībhūtadravyatvasattādijātimadgaganādibhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvaṃ jātau deyameva |
parantu dravyatvasya svavyāpyatvavatsvasākṣādvyāpyatvasyāpyabhedenoktadoṣānistārāt sākṣātpadasya mukhyārthakatvamāśritya dravyatvabhinnatve sati tadvyāpyatvārthakatve paryavasānaṃ kartavyamityāhuḥ |
pare tu avacchedakāvacchedenānumitau sādhye vā sādhyatāvacchedakasambandhe vā yadvyāpakatvaṃ bhāsate tanna parāmarśe sādhye vā tatsambandhe vā tadbhānamanurudhya kintu parāmarśasyāvacchedakāvacchedena hetvavagāhitvamanurūddhyaiva |
taccānyatra yathāklṛptaṃ tathā'trāpītyaṅgīkartavyam |
tathā ca ghaṭā rūpadhvaṃsavantaḥ parvato vahnimānityanumitiṣu sarvatrātyantābhāvaghaṭitavyāpakatvabhānasyaivānumiteraprāmāṇyavāraṇāyāvaśyakatvamabhyupagantavyatayātrāpi tadbhānasyaiva prāptyā'vacchedakāvacchedena sādhyasādhane'pi siddhasādhanatā'rthāntarayorāvaśyakatvāt |
na hīdaṃ vākyaṃ yena vaktṛtātparyānusāreṇārthāvagāhinīmanumitiṃ parāmarśo janayet |
sparśānadhikaraṇadravyaniṣṭhajātyavacchinnabhedasādhanenaivābhimatārthasiddhau vyāsajyavṛttidharmāvacchinnabhedānabhyupagamasākṣādvyāpyatvaniveśasākṣātpadārthāntarāditātparyādyanekaprayāsasya granthakṛtāmanaucitī ca |
tasmānnedaṃ sākṣādvyāpyatvapadaprayojanakathanaṃ yuktaṃ kintu hetau vyarthaviśeṣaṇaghaṭitatve yathā vyāpyatvāsiddhyā na tathā sādhyaśarīre vaiyarthyaṃ sādhyaviśeṣaṇānāmuddeśyapratītyarthatvāt |
uddeśyatāpi kuta ityetāvanmātramavaśiṣyate ta(a)tra itthaṃ brūmaḥ |
pṛthivītvādisamaśīlatvasiddhyasiddhiśaṅkāntaranirāsadvāreṇetyabhiprāyeṇeti |
śaṅkāntaraṃ ca yathā ghaṭadau ghaṭatvavyāpyaccaitrajanyadaśaghaṭeṣu caitrajanyatāvacchedakajātayo'pi prācīnairaṅgīkṛtā lipyādāvanubhavasiddhāśca |
jāne'haṃ caitrasyeyaṃ lipirna maitrasya tasya tu vilakṣaṇeti sarvalokasiddhavyavahārāt |
utpattikṣaṇadvitīyakṣaṇa eva na(nā)śyamānā api padārthāḥ prācāṃ granthe vyavahṛtāḥ |
tathā surabhyasurabhyavayavārabdhadravye'vayavagandhena kārye gandhajanane viruddham gandhadvayasya kārye āpattirekasyaivotpattirityatra niyāmakābhāvāt |
citragandhasyāpi siddhānte'naṅgīkārāttatrāvayavini gandho nāstyeva kintvavayavagandhasyaiva pratyakṣaṃ na caivaṃ citrarūpasyāpyasiddhiriti vācyam |
bahirdravyapratyakṣe udbhūtarūpasya kāraṇatāyāḥ klṛptatvena dharmiṇi rūpābhāve tatpratyakṣānupapattyā tatsiddhyarthaṃ citrarūpasyābhyupagamāditi prācāṃ granthe vyavahāraḥ |
tathoktaṃ tarkadīpikāyām -- `surabhyasurabhyavayavārabdhe dravye parasparavirodhe gandhānutpādāccitragandhānaṅgīkārāt |
tatrādyakṣaṇāvacchinne ghaṭe utpannavinaṣṭaghaṭādau ca gandhavattvalakṣaṇamavyāptamiti' |
itthaṃ ca prakṛte caitrajanyeṣūtpannavinaṣṭadaśaghaṭeṣvevāsambhāvitā(nāṃ) śītānuṣṇāśītavajjalapṛthivyubhayārabdhacaitramaitrajanyeṣu keṣuciddravyeṣu citrasparśasyāpi siddhāntaviruddhatayā kasyāpi sparśasyābhāvena sparśānadhikaraṇeṣu vā caitramaitrajanyatāvacchedakajātīnāmanubhavasiddhānāṃ cāpahnotumaśaktatvāttā jātīrupādāya siddhasādhanam |
tāsāṃ sparśānadhikaraṇavṛttitvāt |
tadavacchinnabhedasya manasyabhāvāt (abādhāt) |
etena sparśāsāmānādhikaraṇyasya jātau niveśenaiva dravya(tva)sattvayorvāraṇāt dravyatvavyāpyatvameva nopādeyaṃ kutastarāṃ sākṣātpadamityapi pratyuktam |
uktajātīrupādāya siddhasādhanatāyā avāraṇāditi |
tacchaṅkānirāsastu tādṛśapadārthasvarūpāṇāṃ balavatībhiryuktibhirnirākaraṇapūrvakaṃ tādṛśajātikhaṇḍanena vā tā aṅgīkṛtya vā prakṛtānumāne jātiṣu dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvaviśeṣaṇaprakṣepeṇa vā bhavati (bhavatīti) tatra tatkhaṇḍanādīnāṃ prakṛtānupayogenānavasaraduḥsthatvāttadaṅgīkṛtyaiva samānādhikaraṇe siddhānte utkarṣasattvātsvābhimatātmatvajāteḥ pṛthivītvasamaśīlatvasyāpi siddheścaitadeva yuktamityāśayena dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvopādānamityāhuḥ || digbhedavādināṃ tviti || prācīnaikadeśisiddhāntinetyarthaḥ |
atra sarvatra diktvānadhikaraṇatvamityetadanumānapūrvottarānumānasahitaitadanumānābhiprāyeṇa |
jātibhedasādhakānumāneṣu diktvamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya diktvabhedo'pi jātau niveśya iti jñeyam || sarvā pṛthivīti |
atra sarvapadaprayojanaṃ pūrvavadeva || sākṣādvyāpyetīti || anye tvityuktamatarītyā'pare tvityuktamatarītyā kevaloddeśyapratītyarthakatvena vā |
sārthakamiti dhyeyamityāhuḥ |
vastutaḥ pṛthivītvāvacchedena sādhyasya sādhane'pi pakṣatāvacchedakavyāpakatāvacchedakatvasyātyantābhāvaghaṭitasyaiva sādhyatāvacchedakabhānasyānumitau vyavasthāpitatvāt |
jalatvādijāticatuṣṭayānadhikaraṇapṛthivīrūpadravyavṛttighaṭatvapaṭatvādyavacchinnapratiyogitākabhedānāmuktajāticatuṣṭayānadhikaraṇapṛthivīrūpadravyavṛttijātyavacchinnapratiyogitākabhedatvena pṛthivītvavyāpakatvātsiddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya sākṣādvyāpyatvaviśeṣaṇaṃ jātau sārthakamityanusandheyam |
anye tu `sarvāṇi manāṃsi' ityatra tādṛśajātimadvyaktibhedasya sādhyatve tattanmanobhedamādāya siddhasādhanatā |
tādṛśajātimattvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedasādhane bādhaḥ |
tādṛśajātiśarīre manastvasyāpi ghaṭakatvāt |
atastādṛśayatkiñcijjātisamaniyatavyāsajyavṛttidharmāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedaḥ sādhyam |
tacca pratyekaṃ sarvamanassu vartate'taḥ siddhasādhanaṃ durvāramiti manastvasamaniyatavyāsajyavṛttidharmasya pakṣatāvacchedak(tā)alābhāya sarvāṇi manāṃsīti |
vyāsajyavṛttidharmāvacchinnānuyogikapratyekavṛttidharmāvacchinnabhedāmyupagamapakṣe manastvāvāntarajātisiddhyā'pi sādhyasyopapannatayā'rthāntaratā syāditi jātirdravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvena viśeṣitā |
tanniveśe'pi pṛthivītvādikamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya sparśānadhikaraṇavṛttīti |
sparśādhikaraṇatvaṃ ca (sarvavādyabhyupagata)sambandhasāmānyena bodhyam |
tenātmaikye'pyākāśakāladigātmasvātmātiriktoktatrayeṣu kāladiśorbahutvamaṅgīkṛtya pratyekaṃ tayorvā gaganakālayorvā kāladiśorvo gaganadiśorvā jātisiddhyā'pi sādhyasattvanirvāhādarthāntarateti śaṅkānavakāśaḥ |
kāle kālikasambandhena diśi ca dikkṛtaviśeṣasparśasattvenoktajātiṣu sparśasāmānādhikaraṇyavirahāt |
dikkṛtaviśeṣaṇatayā digupādhiṣu vṛttyabhyupagamānmanastvasaṅgrahaṇasambhavena dṛṣṭānte sādhyasattvopapattiḥ |
na ca tathā'pi gaganātmanorjātisiddhyā'pi sambhavādarthāntaratā durvāreti vācyam |
śabdāsāmānādhikaraṇyasyāpi jātiviśeṣaṇatvāt |
na cātmaikye'pi manaḥ sādhāraṇyena jātyabhyupagamasambhavātpunararthāntaratā durvāreti vācyam |
tathā sati mūrtatvasyāpi jātitvāpatyā sāṅkaryasambhave tathāvidhajātikalpanāsambhavābhāvadityabhiprāyāt |
mūrtāmūrtobhayavṛttibhinnatvaṃ vā deyam |
vastutastu yathāśrute pṛthivītvādīnāṃ dravyatvavyāpyamūrtatvavyāpyatayā dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvaśeṣaṇenaiva sparśānadhikaraṇetyādi vyarthameva syāt |
ataḥ sākṣādvyāpyetyetaddravyavibhājakopādheḥ paricāyakameva |
tathāca sparśāsamānādhikaraṇadravyavibhājakajātiparyāptāvacchedakatākabhedaḥ sādhyam |
tatra pratyekaṃ kasyāpi manasaḥ pakṣatvā'ṅgīkāre'pi na doṣa iti sūcanāya sarvāṇi manāṃsītyuktam |
sarvāṇyeva manāṃsītyarthaḥ |
`sarva eva śubhaḥ kāla' ityādāviva tadvyaktitvena manasāmupasthitisambhavaḥ |
sarvā pṛthivītyādāvapyevameva prayojanamūhyamityāhuḥ |
ātmāṇutveti vakṣyamāṇahetorasiddhivāraṇāyāvibhviti gaganādau guṇādau ca vyabhicāravāraṇāya hetughaṭakaviśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāgau || ityādyanumānāniceti |
ātmā dravyatvanyūnavṛttijātimān ghaṭātmasaṃyogādghaṭavat |
ātmā(na)ṇutvapakṣe śarīrātmasaṃyogāccharīravadityādīni draṣṭavyāni |
aṇupakṣe tu pūrvoktasādhya evāṇutvaṃ kriyāvattvaṃ vā heturmanadidṛṣṭāntaḥ |
kriyāvattvaṃ ca `so'smāccharīrādutkramye'ti śrutisiddhameva |
aṇutvapakṣe ātmasaṃyogāntaḥkaraṇa eveti vaktuṃ śakyatvādidaṃ na ghaṭate |
kaṇṭhoktānumānānāmivaiṣāmapyanukūlatarkasānāthyenārthasādhakatvāt |
|| dravyatvamiti || atra rūpatvādijātimādāya dṛṣṭānte, ātmatvajātimādāya pakṣe sādhyasattvaṃ bodhyam |
dravyatvasya pṛthivītvādisākṣādvyāpakatayā siddhasādhanavāraṇāyetarāntam |
aṣṭadravyavṛttyupādhivyāpakatvamādāya taddoṣavāraṇāya jātipadam |
tādṛśajātitvāvacchinnavyāpakatvaṃ jāterbādhitamatastādṛśayatkiñcidekajātivyāpakatvalābhāya samāsamupekṣya jātiṃ pratītyekavacanāntaṃ pṛthagupāttam |
pṛthivītvādibhinnaghaṭatvavyāpakatvamādāya siddhasādhanamataḥ sākṣātpadam |
sākṣādvyāpakatvaṃ ca tadvyāpakajātyavyāpakatvam |
vyāpakatvaṃ cādhikadeśavṛttitvam |
tacca tadavacchinnabhedavadvṛttitve sati tadvadvṛttitvam |
pṛthivītvādibhinnaṃ yadrūpatvādi tadvadvṛttirūpatvādikaṃ prati tadavacchinnabhedavadvṛttirasatvādikaṃ prati ca guṇatvasya vyāpakatvāt dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavaikalyāpattirataḥ satyantaviśeṣyadalayorniveśaḥ |
tādṛśadharmāvyāpakatvasya sādhyatve guṇatvasya viśeṣaguṇatvādirūpatādṛśadharmavyāpakatayā sādhyavaikalyamatastādṛśajātyavyāpakatvamanusṛtam |
na ca pṛthivītvādivyatiriktarūpatvādivyāpakaguṇatvāvyāpakatvasya dravyatve siddhatvātsiddhasādhanamiti vācyam |
tadavyāpakatvasya tadavacchinnabhedavadavṛttitvarūpatayā tādṛśaguṇatvāvyāpakatvasya dravyatve bādhitatvāt |
na ca pṛthivitvādivyatiriktaguṇatvavyāpakasattājātyavacchinnabhedavadavṛttitvamādāya siddhasādhanamiti vācyam |
tadavacchinnabhedavadasamavetatvasya vivakṣitatvāt |
sattājātyavacchinnabhedavatsamavetatvasyaivāprasiddhyā siddhasādhanānavakāśāt |
evaṃ ca svādhikaraṇavṛttitvasvāvacchinnabhedavadvṛttitvobhayasambandhena pṛthivītvādivyatiriktakiñcijjātiviśiṣṭajātyavacchinnabhedavadasamavetatvaṃ sādhyamiti niṣkarṣaḥ |
ubhayasambandhapraveśāt pṛthivītvādivyatiriktarūpatvādyavacchinnabhedavadvṛttistādṛśarasatvādimadvṛttiśca yā rasatvādijātiḥ tadvyāpakatve'pi guṇatvarūpadṛṣṭāntasya na sādhyavaikalyam |
tena rūpatvavyāpakaguṇatvaṃ prati guṇatvasya svādhikaraṇavṛttyabhāvāpratiyogitvena vyāpakatvādavyāpakatvarūpasādhyavaikalyaṃ dṛṣṭāntasyeti nirastam |
adhikadeśavṛttitvarūpavyāpakatvasyaiva vivakṣitatvāditi |
hetau jātitvamātrasya sattāyāmanaikāntyāt sattāvyāpyeti |
vyāpyatvaṃ ca pūrvavannyūnavṛttitvarūpaṃ grāhyam |
ataḥ svasya svavyāpyatvamādāya na doṣaḥ |
ghaṭatvādau vyabhicāravāraṇāya sākṣātpadam |
yadyapi ghaṭatvādau pṛthivītvādibhinnaghaṭatvādivyāpakatvarūpasādhyasattvānna vyabhicārāvakāśaḥ |
tathāpi pṛthivītvādibhinnadravyatvavyāpakadravyaguṇānyataratvarūpadharmāntarāvyāpakatvasiddhyā'rthāntaravāraṇāya tadvyāpakajātyavyāpakatve sati tadvyāpakatvameva sākṣādvyāpakatvapadena vivakṣaṇīyamityāśayena tadupādānāt |
anyathā tu nopādeyameva sākṣātpadamityavadheyam |
gaganāvṛttidharmasamavāyitve vyabhicāravāraṇāya jātitvaniveśaḥ |
etaccopalakṣaṇam |
dravyatvaṃ mūrtāvṛttijātimadvṛtti, sattāsākṣādvyāpyajātitvādamūrtavṛttijātitvādvā guṇatvādivat |
dravyatvamātmavṛttijātivyāpakam, ātmānātmavṛttijātitvāt sattāvadityādikamapyūhyam |
|| sarvāṇi manāṃsīti || dṛṣṭānte manastvajātimadvyatiriktatvena pakṣe cātmatvajātimadvyatiriktatvena sādhyaparyavasānaṃ bodhyam |
atra yatkiñcinmanasaḥ pakṣatve mano'ntaravyatiriktatvamādāya siddhasādhanāt sarvāṇītyuktamiti kecit |
taccintyam |
sarveṣāmapi manasāṃ cālinīnyāyena tādṛśajātimanmanaḥpratiyogikabhedavattvena sarvapadopādāne'pi siddhasādhanānuddhārāt |
tādṛśajātyavacchinnapratiyogitākabhedavattvasya sādhyārthatve ca sarvapadavaiyarthyāt |
na ca tādṛśajātimatpratiyogikayatkiñcidekabhedasya sādhyatvānna doṣaḥ |
ekamanobhedasya sarvamanassvabhāvāditi vācyam |
tādṛśabhedapratiyoginyanaikāntyāt |
sādhye sākṣātpadavaiyarthyaprasaṅgācca |
manastvāvāntarajātimatprayogikaikabhedasya sarvamanassvabhāvādeva siddhasādhanānavakāśāt |
yattu dravyatvavyāpyadravyatvavadgaganādibhedamādāya siddhasādhanavāraṇāya sākṣātpadam |
dravyatvasya ca dravyatvavyāpyāvyāpyatvābhāvānna doṣa iti |
tanna |
dravyatvasādhāraṇadravyatvavyāpyatvavivakṣāyāṃ dravyatvavyāpyāvyāpyatve sati dravyatvavyāpyajātyaprasiddhyā sādhyāprasiddheḥ |
yadapyātmatvasya dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyatvasiddhyarthaṃ sākṣātpadamiti || tadapi na |
tatsiddheḥ prakṛtānupayuktatvāt |
tasmāttādṛśajātyavacchinnapratiyogitākabheda eva sādhyaḥ |
sarvapadaṃ tvavacchedakāvacchedena sādhanasyāpi sādhutvajñāpānartham |
pṛthivyādivyatiriktatvamādāya siddhasādhanavāraṇāya sparśānadhikaraṇeti |
jātiguṇādivyatiriktatvena siddhārthatānirāsāya dravyatvavyāpyajātimaditi |
manastvāvāntaracittatvādijātimadvyatiriktatvamādāya taddoṣabhaṅgāya sākṣātpadam |
etatsūcanārthaṃ sarvāṇi manāṃsītyuktam |
cittādibhedena nānājātīyānītyarthaḥ |
tena manastvāvāntarajāteraprāmāṇikatvāt sākṣātpadavaiyarthyamiti śaṅkānavakāśaḥ |
yadyapyatrātmapṛthivyādisādhāraṇajātyantarasiddhyā'rthāntarasambhavaḥ |
tathāpi sparśānadhikaraṇetyanena sparśāsamānādhikaraṇatvasya jātau vivakṣitatvānna doṣaḥ |
pṛthivītvādikaṃ dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyaṃ, dravyavibhājakajātitvāt manastvavadityanumānavirodhena tādṛśajātyantarāsiddheśca |
ātmākāśādimātrasādhāraṇajātyantarasiddhistu prāgeva dūṣiteti |
nanvevamapi svābhāvikadigbhedavādināṃ mate diktvajātimadvyatiriktatvamādāya siddhasādhanamata āha -- digbhedavādināmiti || atra dravyapakṣakānumāne |
upalakṣaṇametat |
dravyatvādipakṣakoktānumāneṣvapi diktvetaratvagrahaṇaṃ kāryam |
|| sarvā pṛthivīti || jalatejovāyumanasāṃ tvapratyayenānvayaḥ |
anyatpūrvavat |
anayaiva rītyā pratyekaṃ jalādipakṣakānumānānyūhyāni |
evaṃ pṛthivyādidravyāṇi ātmavṛttijātimadvyatiriktāni anātmatvāt guṇādivadityanumānaṃ bodhyam |
|| satteti || atra dṛṣṭānte sattānyatvamādāya pakṣe cātmatvānyatvamādāya sādhyasattvaṃ draṣṭavyam |
dravyatvapṛthivītvādibhedamādāya siddhasādhanavāraṇāya dravyatvānyatvātmaniṣṭhatvayoḥ jātau, jāteśca guṇabhedamādāya taddoṣanirāsāya niveśaḥ |
yadyapyātmatvasiddheḥ prāgātmaniṣṭhatvasya niveśāsambhavaḥ |
jātiṃ vinā vyaktibhānāyogāt |
tathāpi tadvyaktitvenāṣṭadravyānyadravyatvena vā tanniveśānna doṣaḥ |
na caivamapyātmapṛthivyubhayānugatajātyantarasiddhyā'rthāntaramiti vācyam |
tasya vinigamanāviraheṇa yāvaddravyavṛttitve tulyavyaktikatayā dravyatvānatirekāt |
etena dravyatvaṃ vetyādikaṃ vyākhyātaprāyam |
|| ātmeti || jīvātmetyarthaḥ |
tena neśvare bhāgāsiddhiḥ |
atra dravyatvādijātyā siddhārthatāvāraṇāya dravyatvavyāpyetyuktārthakam |
ekatvādinā taddoṣavāraṇāya jātīti |
gaganādau vyabhicāravāraṇāyāvibhviti |
vibhutvaṃ ca sarvamūrtasaṃyogitvaṃ paramamahattvavattvaṃ vā |
tacchūnyatvarūpāvibhutvasya guṇādāvanaikāntyāt dravyeti |
etādṛśahetorasiddhiparihārāyaivātmāṇutvavādināmityuktam |
natvātmavibhutvapakṣe hetvantarāsambhavāt |
pratyakṣadravyatvāderhetutvasambhavāt |
evaṃ paṭo paṭātmānyataravṛttijātiśūnyo meyatvāddhaṭavat |
sattādravyatvābhyāmanena jñāyamānamidamidānīmātmavṛttijātyā na jñāyate, vastutvādvastvantaravadityādikamātmatvajātisādhakamanumānaṃ draṣṭavyam || dravyatvavyāpyajātīti || tādṛśajātyantaraviśiṣṭadaśamadravyasiddhyā pṛthivītvādipakṣakānumāneṣvarthāntaramityarthaḥ || gauravāditi || dharmadharmyubhayakalpanāto dharmamātrakalpanāyā laghutvāditi bhāvaḥ |
na caikavyaktau jātikalpanāyogādvyaktyantarakalpanamāvaśyakameveti vācyam |
aklṛptānekavyaktikalpanāpekṣayā klṛptavyaktyantarbhāvena jātikalpane lāghavānapāyāt |
|| satteti || sattājātisākṣādvyāpyajātitvādityarthaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte rūpatvavyāpakatvena sādhyaparyavasānam |
sādhye jātiṃ prati vyāpakamityevokte ghaṭatvaṃ prati vyāpakatvasiddhyā'rthāntaratvam ata uktaṃ sākṣāditi || sākṣādvyāpakatvaṃ ca tadvyāpakāvyāpakatve sati tadvyāpakatvam |
dravyatvasya ghaṭatvavyāpakapṛthivītvavyāpakatvena tadvyāpakāvyāpakatvābhāvena sākṣādvyāpakatvābhāvānna doṣaḥ |
jātiṃ prati sākṣādvyāpakamityukte pṛthivitvādipañcakaṃ prati sākṣādvyāpakatvādarthāntaram |
ataḥ pṛthivītvajalatvatejastvavāyutvamanastvetareti |
ataḥ sarvaṃ sārthakam |
atrāpi pūrvoktarītyā dravyatvasya sākṣādvyāpakatvābhāvānna dravyatvamādāyārthāntaraṃ śaṅkyam |
sādhane jātitvādityukte vyāpyajātitvādityukte sattāvyāpyajātitvādityukte ca ghaṭatve vyabhicāraḥ |
tasya vyāpyajātitvena vyāpakatvarūpasādhyābhāvāt |
ataḥ sākṣāditi |
ghaṭatvaṃ tu pṛthivītvadravyatvadvārā sattāvyāpyamiti na doṣa iti dhyeyam || sparśānadhikaraṇeti || sparśānadhikaraṇaṃ yaddravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātimattadvyatiriktānītyarthaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte tādṛśajātimanmanovyatiriktatvena sādhyaparyavasānaṃ pakṣe sarvapadaparityāge yatkiñcinmanasāṃ pakṣatvāpattyā tatra sparśānadhikaraṇadravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātimadyanmanastadvyatiriktatvasidvyārthāntaram |
ataḥ sarvāṇīti |
sādhye jātimadvyatiriktānītyukte sattājātimatkarmavyatiriktatvasiddhyā'rthāntaram |
ato vyāpyeti |
vyāpyajātimadvyatiriktānītyukte sattāvyāpyakarmatvajātimatkarmavyatiriktatvasiddhyā'rthāntaramato dravyatveti |
dravyatvavyāpyajātimadvyatiriktānītyukte uktarītyā dravyatvavyāpyadravyatvajātimadvyatiriktatvasiddhyā'rthāntaramataḥ sākṣāditi |
dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātimadvyatiriktānītyukte dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyapṛthivītvādijātimatpṛthivyādicatuṣṭayavyatiriktatvasiddhyā arthāntaram |
ataḥ sparśānadhikaraṇeti |
pṛthivyādicatuṣṭayasya sparśādhikaraṇatvānna doṣaḥ |
nanu pṛthivītvapakṣakānumāne jalatvādicatuṣṭayetarajātirdiktvaṃ tadbhinnatvasiddhyā'rthāntaram |
evaṃ jalatvādicatuṣṭayapakṣakānumāneṣvapi |
tathā dravyatvapakṣake pṛthivītvādipañcaketaradiktvajātiṃ prati sākṣādvyāpakatvasidvyā'rthāntaram |
tathā manaḥpakṣakānumāne sparśānadhikaraṇadravyatvasākṣādvyāpyadiktvajātimadvyatiriktatvasiddhyā'rthāntaram |
tathā vakṣyamāṇe pṛthivīpakṣakānumāne jalatvatejastvavāyutvamanastvānadhikaraṇadravyatvasākṣādvyāpyadiktvajātimadvyatiriktatvasiddhyā'rthāntaram |
naca diśa ekatvena vyaktyabhedāddiktvaṃ jātireva na bhavatīti vācyam |
yanmate diśi svābhāvikabhedo'ṅgīkriyate tanmate'rthāntarāparihārādityata āha -- digbhedavādināmiti || atra manaḥpakṣakānumāne sparśānadhikaraṇadiktvānadhikaraṇeti kartavyam |
sarvatra pṛthivītvapakṣakānumāne manastvadiktvetareti kartavyam |
dravyatvapakṣakānumāne manastvadikatvetarajātiṃ pratīti kāryam |
vakṣyamāṇe pṛthivīpakṣakānumāne manastvadiktvānadhikaraṇeti kartavyamityarthaḥ || jalatveti || jalatvatejastvavāyutvamanastvānadhikaraṇaṃ yaddravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātimadvyatiriktetyarthaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte tādṛśajātimatpṛthivīvyatiriktatvena sādhyaparyavasānam |
atra pṛthivītyeva pakṣanirdeśe yatkiñcitpṛthivyāḥ pakṣatvāpattyā tatra jalatvatejastvavāyutvamanastvānadhikaraṇadravyatvasākṣādvyāpyapṛthivītvajātimatpṛthivyantaravyatiriktatvasiddhyā'rthāntaram ataḥ sarveti |
sādhye dravyatvetyādermanaḥpakṣakānumānoktakṛtyameva draṣṭavyam |
dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātimadvyatiriktetyukte dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajalatvādicatuṣṭayavadvyatiriktatvasiddhyā'rthāntaram ato jalatvatejastvavāyutvamanastvānadhikaraṇeti || dravyatveti || dravyatvānyā yā''tmaniṣṭhā jātistadanyetyarthaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte sattānyatvena sādhyaparyavasānam |
sādhye jātyanyetyevokte ghaṭatvajātyanyatvasiddhyā'rthāntaram |
ata ātmaniṣṭheti |
ātmaniṣṭhajātyanyetyevokte dravyatvajātyanyatvasiddhyā'rthāntaram |
ato dravyatvānyeti |
dravyatvānyajātyanyetyuktaghaṭatvānyatvamādāyārthāntaram |
ata ātmaniṣṭheti |
dravyatvānyātmaniṣṭhānyetyukte sukhānyatvasiddhyā'rthāntaram |
ato jātīti || dravyatvamiti || atra dṛṣṭānte dravyatvānyatvena sādhyaparyavasānam |
sādhye ātmaniṣṭhajātyanyadityukte sattānyatāsiddhyā'rthāntaram atassattānyeti |
jātipadakṛtyantu viśiṣṭasattāpakṣakānumānavaddraṣṭavyam |
ātmano vyāptatvādviśeṣaṇāsiddhirityata uktam ātmāṇutvavādināmiti |
mata iti śeṣaḥ |
sādhye jātyadhikaraṇatvasiddhyā'rthāntaramato dravyatvavyāpyeti |
hetau dravyatvādityukte gagane dravyatvaṃ vartate dravyatvavyāpyajātyadhikaraṇatvābhāvādvyabhicāraḥ |
ato'vibhviti |
avibhutvādityukte guṇe ato dravyatveti |
upanyastānumānānāṃ kutropayoga ityata āha -- ityādīti ||
na cātra dravyatvavyāpyajātimatpadārthāntarasiddhyā'rthāntaratvam |
klṛpte dharmiṇi dharmaviśeṣasādhanamātreṇānumānaparyavasānasambhave tadviśiṣṭe dharmiṇyanumānaparyavasāne gauravāt |
na cātmano dravyatvāsiddhyā'siddhyādiḥ |
ātmani nirguṇe rūpādiguṇābhāve'pyayāvaddravyabhāvināṃ dvitvādiguṇānāṃ vyāvahārikāṇāmaṅgīkāre bādhakābhāvāt |
guṇādyantarbhāvaprasaṅgācca dravyatvasiddheḥ(siddhiḥ) |
na cānukūlatarkābhāvādaprayojakatvābhāsasamānayogakṣematve |
ātmapadapravṛttinimittasya ātmatvasya jātitvāṅgīkāre lāghavam |
aupādhikatve tu (aupādhikadharmatvāṅgīkāre tu ) tasyopādhijñānasāpekṣatvena gauravamityanukūlatarkasanāthatvāt(tarkarakṣakopetatvāt) |
tulyavyaktikatvādīnāṃ ca bādhakānāmabhāvāt |
|| na cātreti || manaḥpakṣakānumāne pṛthivīpakṣakānumāne cetyarthaḥ || jātimatpadārthāntareti || ātmatvātiriktajātimaddṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyam |
tadyadātmātiriktapadārthāntaraṃ tatsiddhyetyarthaḥ || klṛpte dharmiṇīti |
navadravyapatite'trātmākhye dharmiṇi dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātiviśeṣarūpadharmasādhanamātreṇetyarthaḥ || na ca dravyatvāsiddhyeti || ātmano nirguṇatvādguṇavatvādhīnaṃ dravyatvaṃ kathaṃ tasyopapadyeteti bhāvaḥ || asiddhyādiriti || ātmapakṣakānumāne'siddhiḥ sattāpakṣakānumāne sādhye dravyatvānyeti viśeṣaṇavaiyarthyaṃ dravyatvapakṣakānumāne'tiriktasarvānumāneṣvapi bādha ityarthaḥ || rūpādiguṇābhāve'pīti |
rūpādiguṇānāṃ paṭādiṣu svarūpānubandhitvadarśanena yāvaddravyabhāvitayā''tmani tadaṅgīkāre ātmavatpāramārthikatvāpattyā nirguṇatvaśrutivyākopāpātena teṣāmanaṅgīkāryatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
dvitvādītyādipadena tritvādīnāṃ grahaṇam |
teṣāmapekṣābuddhijanyatvena tadvināśe vināśādayāvaddravyabhāvitvamupapannamiti bhāvaḥ |
ayāvaddravyabhāvināmapi pāramārthikatve punarnirguṇatvaśrutivirodha ityata uktaṃ vyāvahārikāṇāmiti || bādhakābhāvāditi || nirguṇatvaśrutistu pāramārthikaguṇābhāvamātraviṣayiṇīti bhāvaḥ || guṇādyantarbhāvaprasaṅgācceti || ātmanaḥ padārthatvane dravyatvānaṅgīkāre guṇādyantarbhāvaḥ syāt |
sa ca na yuktaḥ |
na tāvadguṇe'ntarbhāvaḥ |
jātyāśrayatvānaṅgīkārāt |
nāpi karmaṇi |
saṃyogavibhāgau pratyasamavāyikāraṇatvābhāvāt |
nāpi sāmānye |
anekasamavetatvābhāvāt |
na ca samavāye |
asambandharūpatvāt |
nāpi ca viśeṣe |
nityadravyāśritatvābhāvāt |
nāpyabhāve |
pratiyogyanirūpyatvāt |
ato dravyatvasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
nanveteṣvanumāneṣvaprayojakatvaśaṅkāyāṃ na kiñcidbādhakamasti |
jalatvatejastvasamakakṣatayā''tmatvamapi niveśya taditaradravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātibhinnatvasyāpi sādhayituṃ śakyatvenākāśatvāderapi jātitvasiddhiprasaṅgenābhāsasāmyaṃ cetyāśaṅkya nirākaroti || na ceti |
atra yogo nāma vyāptyādisampattiḥ |
kṣemastu pramāṇabādha iti jñeyam || ātmapadapravṛttinimittasyeti || ayamāśayaḥ |
ātmatvarūpadharma eva nāstīti tāvadvaktuṃ na śakyam |
tathā sati pravṛttinimittābhāvenātmanyātmaśabdaprayoga eva na syāt |
sa ca jātirnacennirvaktavyaḥ |
na ca svaprakāśatvamevātmatvamiti vācyam |
paramate parāvedyatvameva svaprakāśatvamityaṅgīkārātsvaprakāśatvasyaivādhikadharmatvaprāptyāpararūpopādhijñānasāpekṣatvena gauravāpātāt |
jātitvāṅgīkāre tu lāghavam |
tasyā akhaṇḍatvenānyānapekṣaṇāt |
tathā ca gauravāpattirūpānukūlatarkopodbalitatvenānumānānāṃ nāprayojakatvādīti |
nanu sabādhakaṃ lāghavamātramakiñcitkaram anyathā gaganapadapravṛttinimittasya gaganatvasyāpi jātitvaṃ syādityata āha -- tulyavyaktitvādīnāmiti || ātmatvasya jātitvāṅgīkāra iti vartate |
`vyakterabhedastulyatvaṃ saṅkaro'thānavasthitiḥ |
rūpahānirasambandho jātibādhakasaṅgraha' iti vacanādvyaktyabhedādīni ṣaḍ jātibādhakāni |
tatra tāvattulyatvādīni pañcabādhakānyātmatvasya jātitvāṅgīkāre na santi |
tathā hi |
tulyatvaṃ nāma tulyavyaktikatvam |
tulyadeśavṛttitvamiti yāvat |
tathā ca ghaṭatvāśrayaghaṭamātravṛttitvātkarīratvasya na jātitvam |
paraspaparihāreṇa vartamānayorekatra samāveśaḥ saṅkaraḥ |
yathā bhūtatvamūrtatvayoḥ |
bhūtatvaṃ gagane'sti na mūrtatvam |
mūrtatvaṃ ca manasyasti na bhūtatvam |
