tayośca pṛthivyādibhūtacatuṣṭaye samāveśaḥ |
ato na te jātī |
ghaṭatve punaḥ sattājātyaṅgīkāre tatrāpi punaḥ sattājātyaṅgīkāraprasaṅgādanavasthiterjātau na jātiraṅgīkāryā |
viśeṣeṣu viśeṣatvarūpajātyaṅgīkāre rūpahānirbādhikā |
rūpahānirnāma svabhāvahāniḥ |
paramāṇugataviśeṣāṇāṃ ca paramāṇuṣvanyonyavyāvṛttidhīhetutvarūpamantyavyāvartakatvasya bhāvaḥ |
ātyantikatvaṃ ca kadā'pi saṃśayādyahetutvam |
ata eva paramāṇupratyakṣavatāṃ yogināṃ tattadgataviśeṣadarśināṃ kadā'pi paramāṇuṣu saṃśayaviparyayau na jāyete |
yadi viśeṣeṣvapi viśeṣatvajātiḥ syāt tarhi sarveṣāṃ viśeṣāṇāṃ viśeṣatvarūpasamānadharmākrāntatvena ghaṭādiṣviva kadācitsaṃśayādisambhavena tadāśrayaparamāṇuṣvapi saṃśayādiprāptau teṣāmuktasvabhāvahāniḥ syāt |
ato na viśeṣeṣu jātiriti || samavāye samavāyatvajātyaṅgīkāre'sambandho bādhakaḥ |
yadi samavāye jātyaṅgīkriyeta tarhi jātivyaktyoḥ samavāyaniyamātsamavāye punaḥ samavāyāntaramaṅgīkaraṇīyam |
samavāyaścaika eva |
ataḥ samavāye samavāyābhyupagame ātmāśrayāpattyā na samavāyatvaṃ samavāye jātiriti || tathā cātmatvasya jātitṅgīkāre na tāvattulyavyaktitvaṃ bādhakam |
ātmanyātmatvasamaniyatadharmāntarābhāvāt |
sadbhāve vā tasyaiva jātitvāpātāt |
nāpi saṅkaraḥ |
ātmatvasyātmamātravṛttitvāt |
nāpyanavasthitiḥ |
tadabhāvāt |
nāpi rūpahāniḥ |
ātmasvabhāvasya kasyāpyanapāyāt |
nāpyasambandhaḥ |
samavāyasambhavāditi bhāvaḥ |
antimatrayavyatiriktānumānānāmātmano jātimattva iva jātimaddaśamadravyasiddhau paryavasānaṃ kinna syādityata āha -- na cātreti || antimatrayavyatiriktānumāneṣvityarthaḥ || asiddhyādiriti || ātmano nirguṇasyetyādyuktadiśā'ntyānumāne'siddhiḥ |
ata evopānte sattādravyatvarūpātmaniṣṭhajātyanyatvena dṛṣṭānte sādhyasya vaktavyatvena dravyatvasyātmanyabhāve sādhyavaikalyaṃ cetyarthaḥ || ayāvaddravyeti || yāvaddravyabhāviguṇāṅgīkāre'sya tu kadā'pyanivṛttyā nirguṇatvabhaṅgaḥ syāt |
uktaguṇāṅgīkāre tūttaratra bhāvinirguṇatvavirodhāditi bhāvaḥ |
dravyatvānaṅgīkāre bādhakaṃ cāha -- guṇānāmiti || adravyatve guṇādyanyatamatvaṃ syāt |
anyathā'padārthatvāpattiriti || nanu dravyādiṣaṭkātiriktatve'pi padārthatvamaṅgīkriyate ko doṣa iti cet |
tarhi guṇādipañcakabhinnatvameva dravyatvaṃ vivakṣitamiti kathamasiddhyādiḥ || aprayojakatvābhāsasamānayogakṣematva iti || anyatra sahacārasambhave'pi pakṣe vyabhicārasambhavādaprayojakatvam |
na ca hetumati sādhyābhāvenopādhisahacārabhaṅgenānumānamātrocchedaḥ syāditi tarko'stīti vācyam |
vyabhicārocchedakasya vyabhicāre'vaśyamupādhirityanaupādhikatvasyāsammateḥ |
na copādhirna dṛśyata iti vācyam |
bādhakābhāve pakṣetaratvādīnāmapi tathātvāt |
kiñcādyānumāne jalādītareti vadātmatvetaretyapi praveśe jātimaddaśamadravyasyāpyāpattiḥ |
tatrānadhikaraṇatvasyāpi praveśe punardravyāntarasiddhyāpattiḥ |
tathā''kāśādīnāmapi jātimatvaṃ susādhyam |
tathā manovyatiriktapakṣakeṣvanityasamānādhikaraṇe jātiviśeṣaṇe ātmānityatvaṃ syādityābhāsasāmyaṃ cetyarthaḥ || lāghavamiti || jātyupādhyorjātirbalīyasīti nyāyāditi bhāvaḥ || upādhīti |
buddhiguṇakatvasvaprakāśatvādyupādhyadhīneti bhāvaḥ |
yadyapyuktopādhinaivānugatapratītyupapattāvanaupādhikatvāṅgīkāro vyarthaḥ |
tathā'pyakhaṇḍavyavahārasiddhyarthaṃ parāṅgīkṛtānuvādo'yamiti jñātavyam |
idaṃ gauravaṃ na phalamukhaṃ yena doṣaḥ syātkintu kāraṇamukhamiti darśayitumādāvātmapadapravṛttinimittasyetyuktam |
nanu vyaktibahutvaniścaya eva jātyupādhyoriti nyāyāvatārikā |
iha tu tatsandehātkālaparyantatvena jātitvameva gurviti cenna |
vyaktibhedasya pratyakṣasiddhatvenākalpanāt |
pratyutaupādhikatvakalpanasyaiva vyaktibhedagrāhakapratyakṣādyaprāmāṇyakalpanaparyantatvena tato'tigauravamiti || evañca yadyātmatvaṃ na jātistarhi kalpanāgauravamiti tarkasattvānna doṣaḥ |
|| asiddhyādiriti || avyavahitānumāne svarūpāsiddhiḥ sattāpakṣakānumāne sādhye viśeṣaṇavaiyarthyaṃ katipayeṣu bādha iti bhāvaḥ || bādhakābhāvāditi || tathā ca guṇāśrayatvarūpavyāpakanivṛttyabhāvānna dravyatvāsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ || guṇādyantarbhāveti || ātmano vyāvahārikadvitvādiguṇānāśrayatve sattāśrayatve karmaṇo vyatiriktatvena jātimātrāśrayo guṇa iti guṇalakṣaṇākrāntatvādguṇāntarbhāvaḥ syāt |
sattānāśrayatvetvabhāvāntarbhāvaprasaṅgāt |
dvitvādiguṇāśrayatvamaṅgīkāryam |
tataśca dravyatvasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ || na cānukūlatarkābhāvāditi || pṛthivītvajalatvatejastvavāyutvamanastvātmatvetaradravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātibhinnajātitvājjalatvavaditi || sattā dravyatvānyākāśaniṣṭhajātyanyetyābhāsasamānayogakṣematvamityarthaḥ |
nanu sabādhakaṃ lāghamavakiñcitkaram |
anyathā gaganapadapravṛttinimittaṃ gaganatvamapi jātiḥ syādityata āha -- tulyavyaktikatvādīnāmiti |
|| na ceti || padārthāntareti || ātmasahitanavadravyātiriktetyarthaḥ || klṛpte dharmiṇīti |
sarvapratyakṣasiddhātmasveva tajjātikalpane lāghavam |
anyathā dharmadharmiṇorubhayoḥ kalpane gauravaṃ syādityarthaḥ |
nanu tarhi klṛpteṣu gaganādiṣveva tatsidhyet |
na ca teṣāṃ dharmigrāhakapramāṇenaikatvenaiva siddhatvājjātikalpanā'yukteti vakṣyatīti vācyam |
tādṛśayuktyaikaikagaganādau tādṛśajātisiddhyabhāve'pyākāśakāladikṣu vā vinigamakābhāvānusandhāne'pyātmasāhityena vā teṣu jātyaṅgīkārasambhavāt |
ātmabhedāsiddhyā'rthāntarateti cenna |
sandigdha eva nyāyapravṛttirna tu bādhita iti nyāyāt |
gaganakāladikṣu dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajāterabhāvasyaiva niścitatayā tatra vivādābhāvena bādhitārthānvihāyānyasandigdhātmasveva jātisiddheḥ |
ata eva sarvāṇi manāṃsītyādyanumitiviṣayasādhyasattvanirvāhāya keṣucinmanassveka(kā)dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyā jātirekā'paramanassvekā tādṛśī jātirmanastvākhyaikā tadubhayavyāpi(kātā) mā'stveva |
pakṣatā tu sarvamanasāṃ manaḥpadārthatvenāstu |
sādhyībhūtabhedadvayasyaiva tādṛśasādhyatāvacchedakarūpeṇa pakṣatāvacchedakavyāpakatvādavacchedakāvacchedena sādhyasiddherapi nānupapattiḥ |
ata ātmatvāsiddhyā'rthāntarateti śaṅkā'pi nirastā |
niṣprāmāṇikajātidvayāṅgīkāre gauravājjāticatuṣṭayāṅgīkārasyāpi sambhavādatiprasakteśca |
tādṛśajātidvayādyabhāvena manastvarūpasākṣādvyāpyajātimaṅgīkṛtyaiva tasya pakṣatāvacchedakatvenopādānāttatraitacchaṅkanasyaiva sāhasikatvāt |
gaganādāvapi bādhitatvābhāvāttatra jātisiddhyā'rthāntaratetyāgrahaścet |
sādhyaśarīre pūrvatarānumāneṣu sarveṣu dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātau śabdānyaviśeṣaguṇaśūnyamahadavṛttitvaviśeṣaṇaṃ deyamato nārthāntaratāprasaktiriti draṣṭavyam |
na ca dravyatvetyavibhudravyatvādityanumatā(manā)bhiprāyeṇāsiddhyudbhāvanamitarānumānābhiprāyeṇa tvādīti |
ātmanyapi dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātervyāpakadravyatvābhāvena bo(bā)dhitatvagrahāttādṛśānumānabalādātmātirikte tādṛśajātimaddravyāntarameva siddhyedityarthāntaratā |
dravyāntarasyāpyabhāvaniścaye bādho vetyetadubhayamādipadārthatayā bodhyam |
ātmani vyāpakībhūtadravyatvābhāvasiddhau bījābhāvaḥ |
nirbījaśaṅkāyāḥ paryavasānābhāvena tādṛśaśaṅkasya kathakatāyā evābhāvāt |
na ca dravyatvalakṣaṇatvena dravyatvaṃ prati vyāpakībhūtaguṇābhāvādeva tatsiddhiḥ |
tatraiva bījābhāvāt |
nanvātmani tattadguṇābhāvasiddhyā guṇasāmānyābhāvasiddhiḥ |
jñānecchādīnāṃ bhavato manaḥpariṇatatayā manoguṇatvāṅgīkārāt |
anudbhūtarūpādisattve mānābhāvaḥ |
udbhūtarūpādisattve mahata ātmanaścākṣuṣādyāpatteḥ |
mahattvānaṅgīkāreṇa tadāpattivāraṇe tatra tacchabdābhāva eva mānābhāvaḥ |
pūrvamātmani rūpaṃ sthitaṃ tadā cākṣuṣamapi sthitamidānīṃ tannāśānna cākṣuṣatvamapītyasat |
tatra rūpādināśotpādasyaivābhāvāt |
ātmano nityatvenāśrayanāśarūpakāraṇābhāvāttatra pākāderaprāmāṇikatvāt |
tathātve rūpāntarotpādāvaśyakatvācca |
pākādirūpakāraṇāsambhavācca |
tathaikatvaparimāṇādiguṇānāmapi pratyakṣādyaviṣayatvenāprāmāṇikatvenaiva tadabhāvasiddheḥ |
tathā ca viśeṣābhāvakūṭabalādeva sāmānyābhāvasiddhiriti cenna |
tāvatā'pi dvitvasaṃyogādisadbhāve dvā suparṇetyādyāgamavyavahārasya dviśabdapariṣasvajāta iti śabdabalācca siddhatvenāyāvaddravyabhāvitayā teṣāmapekṣābuddhyanyadīyakriyābhirutpattivināśāṅgīkāreṇa dvitvasaṃyogādiguṇābhāvasiddhyā viśeṣābhāvakūṭarūpaheturasiddha eva |
ata ātmani dravyatvābhāvaśaṅkaivātyuktetyāśayenāha || ātmanīti || nirguṇa iti || nirguṇatvena śrautavyavahārādiviṣaye'pītyarthaḥ |
idaṃ ca nirguṇa iti śrutibalādeva nirguṇatvaṃ svīkriyata iti śaṅkādyotanāya dvitvādīnāmiti |
tathā dvitvādibodhakoktaśrutisadbhāvena nirguṇatvaśrutessatpratipakṣatatvena na svārthanirṇāyakatvam |
vicāradaśāyāṃ tu dvitvādyaṅgīkāre bādhakābhāvena tacchruteḥ svārthatātparyanirṇayaviṣayatvena balavatyāḥ nirguṇatvaśruteḥ guṇasāmānyābhāvaparatve tadbādhāt rūpādirūpaguṇābhāvaparatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanu neha nāneti śrutyā sarvasya niṣiddhatvātkathaṃ dvitvādikamātmanyaṅgīkartuṃ śakyamityato vyāvahārikāṇāmiti |
pṛthivyādiṣvapi pāramārthikaguṇābhāvena vyāvahārikaguṇāśrayatvenaiva dravyatvasyaivātrāpi dravyatvasyāvaśyakatvamiti bhāvaḥ || guṇādyantarbhāveti || dravyatvānaṅgīkāra iti śeṣaḥ || prasaṅgāśceti || guṇādyantarbhāvāpādakatarkabalādityarthaḥ |
dravyabhinnatve sati padārthatvāt guṇādivadguṇādyanyatamatvāpattiḥ |
na ceṣṭāpattiḥ |
asamavetatvātsatpratiyogikatvābhāvācca guṇādyabhāvāntarbhāvasya vaktumaśakyatvāt |
tasmādāpādyābhāvādviśiṣṭāpādakābhāvo'stvityāpādakābhāvasiddhau tasya padārthatvarūpaviśeṣyābhāvena paryavasāne ātmano'līkatvāprāptyā viśeṣaṇābhāvena paryavasāne dravyatvasiddheriti bhāvaḥ |
yadvā nirguṇatvapratipādakaśrutiviṣaya ātmani śrutiprāmāṇyāya guṇābhāvasattvamapekṣitam |
tacca yāvaddravyabhāviguṇavati na sthātuṃ śaknoti |
ayāvaddravyabhāviguṇasattve'pi tatsattākālātiriktakālāvacchedena guṇābhāvasattvena śrutiprāmāṇyābhāvarūpabādhakābhāvena tatsattvasambhava ityarthaḥ |
sarvathā tadanaṅgīkāre bādhakamāha -- guṇeti || teṣāṃ nirguṇala(tva)kṣaṇatvāditi bhāvaḥ || na cānukūlatarketyādi || ābhāsasamāneti || ātmatvajātisiddhyanantaramapi tadbhinnatvamapi dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātau niveśya sādhyopādānena jātyantarādisādhakānumānairātmā na jātimān ātmābhāvānyataratvādabhāvavadityādibhiśca samānayogakṣematvamityarthaḥ |
yadyapyābhāsasāmyamaprayojakatāparyavasānenaiva dūṣaṇam |
anyathā sāmyamātrasadbhāvasya dūṣakatve dhūmānumānasyāpi prameyatvenābhāsasāmyādduṣṭatvāpatteḥ |
tathā'pi sudhoktarītyā'sādhakatvāpādaka(tva)mapi tasya vartata ityāśayena pṛthagutkīrtanam || upādhijñānasāpekṣatveneti || aupādhikadharmatvaṃ nāma nānāpadārthaghaṭitadharmatvam |
tajjñānena ca taddhaṭakanānāpadārthajñānasyāvaśyakatvena tajjñānādhīnapravṛttinimittajñānādhīnaśaktijñānāṅgīkāre upasthitigauravaṃ śakyatāvacchedakagauravaṃ ca syādityanukūlatarkeṇāprayojakatvaśaṅkānirāsādeteṣāṃ sādhakatvamitareṣāṃ punaranukūlatarkābhāvātpratyutāprāmāṇikabahujātigauravamityādipratikūlarkotthānācca na sādhakatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
na ca tarhi vibhupadaśakyatāvacchedakalādhavānusārādvibhutvajātirapi sidhyeduktānumānādineti vācyam |
kevalānumānasyākāmagāmitvena kasyāpyarthasya na sādhakatvaṃ bādhakatvaṃ ca |
kintu pratyakṣādisiddhārthe sandehe yuktistatsahāyenārthasādhikā bhavatīti svaparayoḥ siddhāntaḥ |
itthaṃ cātmatvajāterahamahamityādipratyakṣaviṣayatvāttatra sandehe uktānumānāni tadanugrāhakatayā nirṇāyakāni |
na ca tathā vibhutvasthale tasya pratyakṣavedyatvābhāvācchakyatāvacchedakalāghavamātrānusāreṇa jātikalpane nānārthocchedakaprasaṅgādityāśayāt |
nanu tarhi ghaṭaṃ dṛṣṭvā lokānāmayaṃ ghaṭa iti karīra iti vyavahārānusārātkarīratvādikamiti sidhyet |
bādhaka(sādhaka)sadbhāvātteṣāṃ jātitvaṃ nāstītyaṅgīkāre prakṛte'pi tathā'stvityāśaṅkya tulyavyaktikatvasyābādhakatvamityabhiprāyapūrvakaṃ nirācaṣṭe || tulyeti |
māyināmāśaṅkāṃ māyirītyaiva nirasyati -- na ceti || nirguṇaśrutivirodhenātmani guṇavattvāsiddhyā tadvyāpyadravyatvajātirapyasiddhetyarthaḥ || asiddhyādiriti || avyavahitapūrvānumāne svarūpāsiddhirbādhaśca |
tatpūrvānumāne sādhyāprasiddhiḥ |
tatpūrvānumāne dravyatvānyeti viśeṣaṇavaiyarthyam |
tatpūrvānumāneṣvātmani dravyatvavyāpyajātisiddhimādāya sādhyaparyavasānāyogāduddeśyāsiddhirityarthaḥ || ātmanīti || ayaṃ bhāvaḥ |
nirguṇaśrutyā pāramārthikaguṇaniṣedhe'pi vyāvahārikaguṇāṅgīkāre bādhakābhāvaḥ |
na ca guṇānāṃ yāvaddravyabhāvitvena pāramārthikatvāpattiḥ |
yāvaddravyabhāvirūpādiguṇāṅgīkārāyoge'pyapekṣābuddhijanyadvitvādyaṅgīkāre bādhakavirahāditi |
nanvatra sādhakābhāva eva bādhaka ityata āha -- guṇādyantarbhāveti || avṛttitvena guṇādyanantarbhūtasya pariśeṣāddravyatvasiddherityarthaḥ |
nanūktānumāneṣu heturastu sādhyaṃ mā'stvityaprayojakaśaṅkāvārakatarkābhāvādasādhakatvam |
evaṃ pṛthivītvaṃ jalatvādiklṛptajātivyatiriktadravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātibhinnaṃ jātitvāt, sattā dravyatvānyākāśaniṣṭhajātyanyā jātitvāddhaṭatvavadityādyābhāsasāmyaṃ cetyāśaṅkāṃ nirāha -- na ceti || ābhāsasamāneti || yogakṣemayoḥ sādhakabādhakabhāvābhāvayorābhāsasamānatvamityarthaḥ |
tathācoktānumānasyābhāsatvādasyāpyabhāsatvamaviśeṣāditi bhāvaḥ |
ābhāsasāmyaṃ vighaṭayiṣyannanukūlatarkamupapādayati -- ātmapadeti || ātmapadaśakyatāvacchedakasyetyarthaḥ || lāghavamiti || jāterakhaṇḍatvena viśeṣaṇajñānādyanapekṣatvāditi bhāvaḥ || aupādhikatveti || viśiṣṭadharmasvarūpatvetyarthaḥ |
anekavastughaṭitasvaprakāśatvāderātmapadapravṛttinimittatva iti yāvat || gauravamiti || yadyapi svaprakāśatvāderātmapadapravṛttinimittatvābhāve'pi svaghaṭakopādhijñānasāpekṣajñānakatvamastyeveti na lāghavāvakāśaḥ |
pratyuta jātyantarakalpanādeva gauravam |
tathāpyātmapadaśaktijñānāderanekopādhijñānādhīnatvakalpanādanekopādhyantarbhāvena śakyatāvacchedakatvakalpanācca gauravaṃ bodhyam |
evamātmatvajātau sādhakasattvamupapādya bādhakāsattvamapyupapādayati -- tulyavyaktikatvādīnāmiti || ghaṭatvakalaśatvayorbhinnajātitve jātisamanaiyatyarūpatulyavyaktikatvaṃ bādhakam |
bhūtatvamūrtatvayorjātitve parasparābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatve sati parasparasamānādhikaraṇatvarūpasāṅkaryam |
jāterjātimattve jātiparamparayā anavasthā |
viśeṣasya jātimattve jātereva vyāvartakatayā svatovyāvṛttatvarūpatatsvarūpasya, jātyanyatve jātiśūnyatve ca sati samavetatvarūpatallakṣaṇasya vā hāniḥ |
abhāvasya jātimattve samavāyavaidhuryaṃ bādhakam |
ātmatvajātyaṅgīkāre caiteṣāmabhāvādityarthaḥ |
nanūpanyastasarvānumānaiḥ pṛthivītvajalatvatejastvavāyutvamanastvetaradravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātisiddhau tādṛśajāterātmatvānatirekāttasya jātitvasiddhāvātmabhedasiddhiriti matam |
tanna ātmānyatādṛśajātyantaraviśiṣṭadaśamadravyasiddhyā'rthāntarādityāśaṅkya nirākaroti || naceti || pṛthivītvādipañcakānyadravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātimadātmānyavyaktisiddhyā'rthāntaratetyarthaḥ || klṛpta iti || ātmā dharmī klṛptaḥ tatrātmatvajātirūpadharmaviśeṣānumānaparyavasāne lāghavam |
dharmamātrasya kalpyatvāt |
kalpyaviśeṣadharmaviśiṣṭavyaktyantaraparyavasāne gauravam |
dharmadharmiṇorubhayorapi kalpyatvāt |
dharmikalpanāto varaṃ dharmakalpanamiti nyāyāduktarītyaiva sādhyaparyavasānamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanvātmani nirguṇe guṇāśrayatvarūpadravyatvasya manmate'siddhyā''tmatvasya dravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātitvāsiddhiḥ |
tathācānumānasya viśeṣyāsiddhirityāśaṅkya nirākaroti || naceti || vyāvahārikāṇāmiti || tathāca `kevalo nirguṇaśce'ti śrutiḥ pāramārthikaguṇaniṣedhapareti bhāvaḥ |
guṇānāṃ trikālābādhyayāvadbrahmabhāvitve teṣāmapi pāramārthikatvamevetyata uktamayāvaddravyabhāvināmiti || satyevadravye'pekṣābuddhināśena dvitvānāśāddravyaṃ yāvatkālaṃ tiṣṭhati tāvadasatāmityarthaḥ || bādhakābhāvāditi || tvatpakṣepīti śeṣaḥ || guṇādīti || yadyātmano dravyatvaṃ na syāttadā tasya guṇādipadārtheṣvantarbhāvaḥ padārthāntaratvaṃ vā vaktavyam |
tatrādye na tāvadguṇentarbhāvaḥ dravyāsamavetatvāt |
tata eva na karmaṇi acalanātmakatvācca |
nāpi sāmānye anekānugatatvābhāvāt |
nāpi samavāye asambandharūpatvāt |
nāpi viśeṣe nityadravyānāśritatvāt |
nāpyabhāve pratiyogyanirūpyatvāditi |
nāpi dvitīyaḥ padārthānāṃ saptatvaniyamāt |
ataḥ pariśeṣāddravyatvasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
nanvapicetyādinokteṣvātmatvajātisādhakeṣvanumāneṣu sarvatra heturastu sādhyaṃ māstvityaprayojakatāśaṅkāyāṃ bādhakatarkābhāvādaprayojakatvam |
tathā pṛthivītvaṃ jalatvatejastvavāyutvamanastvātmatvetaradravyatvasākṣādvyāpyajātibhinnaṃ jātitvādityākāśatvāderjātitvasādhakābhāsānumānasāmyaṃ cetyata āha -- naceti || aprayojakatveti || aprayojakatvaṃ cābhāsasamānayogakṣematvaṃ ceti vigrahaḥ |
yogaśabdena vyāptyādisādhakasampattirvivakṣitā |
kṣemaśabdena ca pramāṇabādhādyabhāvaḥ |
tathācā'bhāsānumānatulyasādhakabādhakābhāvavattvamityarthaḥ |
ātmapadapravṛttinimittasya ātmatvasya || lāghavamiti || jāterakhaṇḍatvenānyajñānanirapekṣaraṇāditi bhāvaḥ |
aupādhikatve svaprakāśatvarūpatve || upādhīti || svaprakāśatvasya tvanmate itarāvedyatvarūpatvenetararūpopādhijñānādhīnatvādityarthaḥ |
upādhipratyayaṃ vinopahitapratyayānupapatteriti bhāvaḥ || ityanukūlatarkarakṣakopetatvāditi || anumānānāṃ vipakṣe kalpanāgauravarūpānukūlatarkākhyarakṣakopetatvādityarthaḥ |
nacaivamākāśapadapravṛttinimittasya jātitvānaṅgīkāre gauravamiti tatrāpi vaktuṃ śakyamiti vācyam |
tatra jātibādhakasadbhāvena jātitvasyāṅgīkārānarhatvena gauravasyādoṣatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
yadā hodayanaḥ |
vyakterabhedastulakyatvamityādinā |
tatra na tāvadātmatvasya jātitvāṅgīkāre tulyatvādīni pañca bādhakāni santītyarthaḥ |
ayaṃ praghaṭṭakaḥ dṛśyate -- `yadāhodayanaḥ |
vyakterabhedastulyatvaṃ saṅkaro'thānavasthitiḥ |
rūpahānirasambandho jātibādhakasaṅgrahaḥ || iti |
tatrānekeṣu ghaṭeṣu `ghaṭo'yaṃ ghaṭo'yaṃ' iti anugatapratyayavyavahārayornirvāhāya ghaṭatvarūpānugatajātiraṅgīkriyate |
anyathā tayornirnibandhanatvāpatteḥ |
na ca ghaṭarūpādinibandhanatvaṃ tayorvācyam |
teṣāmananugatatvāt |
ato'nugatajātiraṅgīkāryā |
evaṃ vyaktyanekatvasya jātitvaprayojakatvāt vyaktyabhedo jātibādhakaḥ |
yathā''kāśasyaikatvāt ākāśatvaṃ na jātiḥ |
tatra jātyāpādakānugatapratyayāderabhāvāt |
dravyatvāpekṣayā sattāyāḥ adhikadeśavṛttitvena paratvāt, pṛthivītvasya nyūnadeśavṛttitvena aparatvāttayoḥ jātitvamaṅgīkriyate |
tathā ca kiñcijjātyapekṣayā parāparabhāvāpannatvasya jātiprayojakatvāt tulyadeśavṛttitvākhyatulyatvaṃ jātibādhakam |
yathā ghaṭatvatulyadeśavṛttikarīratvaṃ na jātiḥ |
parasparaparihāreṇa vartamānayoḥ ekatra samāveśaḥ saṅkaraḥ |
yathā bhūtatvamūrtatvayoḥ |
bhūtatvaṃ mūrtatvaparihāreṇa gagane asti |
mūrtatvaṃ ca bhūtatvaparityāgena manasi |
tayoḥ pṛthivyāṃ samāveśāt |
jātau jātyaṅgīkāre anavasthitiḥ bādhikā |
viśeṣeṣu jātyaṅgīkāre viśeṣasvarūpahāniḥ bādhikā |
tathā hi |
āpyaparamāṇuprabhṛtīnāṃ samānajātyākrāntatvena jātyādeḥ vyāvartakatvāsambhavāt, teṣu yogināṃ anyonyavyāvṛttidhījananena antyavyāvartakatvasvabhāvākhyāḥ viśeṣāḥ aṅgīkriyante |
teṣāmantyavyāvartakatvaṃ ca kadāpi saṃśayādyahetutvam |
na caitadviśeṣeṣu jātyaṅgīkāre sambhavati |
tathātve sarveṣāṃ viśeṣāṇāṃ viśeṣatvarūpasādhāraṇadharmāpannatvena kadācit saṃśayaviṣayatvāpattyā svāśrayanityadravyeṣu saṃśayahetutvāpatteḥ |
ataḥ viśeṣeṣu jātyaṅgīkāre antyavyāvartakarūpahāniḥ anivāryā |
samavāyatvajātyaṅgīkāre asambandho bādhakaḥ |
samavayasya ekatvena samavāye samavāyābhyupagame ātmāśrayāpattyā samavāyatanniṣṭhajātyoḥ sambandhābhāvāt | -- iti |
vyaktyabhedo'pi na bādhakaḥ |
tathā hi |
ātmatvajātisādhanadvārā''tmabhedaḥ sādhyaḥ |
sa ca sandigdhaḥ |
na ca sādhyābhāvasandeho'numānaṃ niruṇaddhi (anumānaviruddhaḥ)|
(anyathā) anumānamātrocchedaprasaṅgāt |
evaṃ tarhi gaganatvādikamapi jātiḥ syāditi cenna |
teṣāṃ dharmigrāhakamānena lāghavānugṛhītenaikatvasiddheḥ(siddhau bādhāt) |
na caivaṃ prakṛte dharmigrāhakapratyakṣasya bhedagrāhakatvenopapādanāt |
na cāpāramārthikabhedenāpyupapattyā'rthāntaratvam (ātmatvajātyupapattyā'rthāntaratvam)|
jāterdharmisattāsamānasattākabhedavadvyaktyāśrayatvāt(vyaktyāśritatvāt)|
anyathā gaganatvasyāpi jātitvāpatteḥ |
pañcabādhakānāmabhāve'pi vyaktyabhedo bādhako'stvityata āha -- vyaktyabhedo'pīti |
yadyapyaikyavādinā''tmavyakterabhedo'ṅgīkriyate |
tathā'pi sa nedānīmapi niścitaḥ kintu sandigdhaḥ |
ata evātmatvasya jātitvasādhanadvārā''tmabhedaḥ sādhyate |
abhedaśca bhedābhāvarūpaḥ |
na ca sādhyābhāvasandeho'numitivirodhī |
anumānamātrocchedaprasaṅgāt |
pratyuta pakṣatāghaṭakatvenānumityaṅgam |
ato nātra vyaktyabhedo'pi bādhaka ityarthaḥ |
idamapi nyāyamatānusāreṇaiva |
siddhānte vyaktyabhedāderajātibādhakatvāt |
tathā hi |
vyaktyabhedastāvanna jātibādhakaḥ |
yathoktaṃ tattvanirṇaye |
`yataḥ svarūpataścānyo jātitaḥ śrutito'rthataḥ |
kathamasmi sa ityeva sambandhaḥ syādasaṃhita' iti |
vyākhyātaṃ ca taṭṭīkāyāṃ `jātito jīvatvādijātibhedata' iti |
ādipadena viṣṇutvagrahaḥ |
tathā ca viṣṇutvasya jātitvena pramāṇasiddhatvāttatra vyaktyanekatvābhāvānna vyaktyabhedo jātibādhaka ityavagamyate |
evaṃ tulyatvamapi na bādhakam |
tulyavyaktikayorubhayorapi jātitvāṅgīkāre bādhakābhāvāt |
na ca ghaṭatvavatkarīratvasyāpi jātitvāpattiḥ |
ghaṭatvātiriktasya karīratvasyābhāvāt |
ghaṭakarīraśabdavadetayorapi śabdayoḥ paryāyatvāt |
evaṃ saṅkaro'pi na bādhakaḥ |
mānuṣeṣu parasparaparihāreṇa vartamānayorapi yatitvagārhasthyayordeveṣu sāṅkaryasya śrutisiddhatvāt |
ata eva te na jātīti iti cet |
tarhi brāhmaṇatvādīnāmapi jātitvābhāvāpātāt |
na ca sāṅkaryābhāvātteṣāṃ jātitvamiti vācyam |
sāṅkaryasya jātibādhakatāsiddhyuttarakālīnatvādasyāḥ kalpanāyāḥ |
na caivaṃ bhūtatvamūrtatvorapi jātitvāpattiḥ |
mūrtatvasya paricchinnaparimāṇarūpatvenaupādhikadharmatayā jātitvaśaṅkānarhatvāt |
ata eva na parāparabhāvānupapattiriti |
anavasthitirapi na bādhikā |
jātau jātyaṅgīkāre'pyanavasthābhāvāt |
yathoktamanuvyākhyāne |
`asmatpakṣe guṇādyāśca tadvanto hi viśeṣataḥ |
ananyatvānnā'navasthe'ti |
viśeṣasamavāyayoḥ svarūpasyaivābhāvāttatra viśeṣatvasamavāyatvarūpadharmasyāpyabhāvena tayorjātitvanirāsārthaṃ rūpahānyasambandhayorbādhakatvakalpanaṃ tu dūrotsāritamiti saṅkṣepaḥ || evaṃ tarhīti || vyaktyabhedasya bādhakatvābhāva ityarthaḥ || gaganatvādikamiti || kālatvaṃ diktvaṃ cādipadārthaḥ |
nirākaroti || teṣāmiti || gaganakāladiśāmityarthaḥ || teṣāmiti || sāmānyena parāmarśānnapuṃsakatā |
gaganakāladigrūpapadārthānāmiti vā || dharmigrāhaketi || śabdaḥ kvacidāśrito guṇatvādrūpavadityanumānena sāmānyataḥ śabdāśrayadravye siddhe tasya pṛthivyādyaṣṭānyatamatvasya bādhitatvāttadatiriktadravyaṃ yatsiddhyati tadevākāśaṃ bhavati |
tasya cānekatve pramāṇābhāvena tatkalpane gāraivāpattyā lāghavādekatvamevāṅgīkāryam |
evaṃ kāladiśorapi |
tathā ca lāghavānugṛhītena dharmigrāhakamānenaikatvasyaiva siddhergaganādivyaktinānātvābhāvena tatra gaganatvādijātyaṅgīkāre vyaktyabhedo bādhako bhavatyeveti bhāvaḥ |
prakṛte'pi tathaivāstvityata āha -- na caivamiti || upapādanāditi || `tatra brūma' ityādigranthenopapāditatvādityarthaḥ |
nanu pratyakṣasya bhedāvagāhitve'pi na pāramārthikabhedāvagāhitvam |
advaitaśrutyādivirodhāt |
ato'pāramārthikabhedenāpyātmatvajātyupapattyā punararthāntarataiveti || tatrāha -- na ceti || jāteriti || tvanmate'pi ghaṭatvādijāterdharmisattāsamānasattāko yo bhedo vyāvahārikastadvatyo yā vyaktayo ghaṭādirūpāstadāśritatvāṅgīkārāt |
prakṛte'pyātmatvajāterdharmibhūtātmasattāsamānasattākabhedavatyo yā vyaktaya ātmarūpāstadāśritatve'ṅgīkārye ātmavattadbhedasyāpi pāramārthikatvameva paryavasyatīti bhāvaḥ |
vyaktau bhedamātreṇa jātitvopapattyā dharmisamasattākatvaviśeṣaṇamatantramevetyata āha -- anyatheti |
gagane'pi prātibhāsikabhedasya tvanmate sattvena tāvanmātrasyaiva jātitvaprayojakatve gaganatvasya jātitvaṃ durvārameva |
ato dharmisamasattākatvaviśeṣaṇamāvaśyakameva |
tataśca dharmiṇo gaganasya vyāvahārikatvena tatsamasattākatvasya prātibhāsikabhede'yogena na gaganatvasya jātitvamiti vācyam |
tadvadatrāpi tādṛśabheda eva jātitvaprayojaka ityaṅgīkāryamiti nārthāntarateti bhāvaḥ |
nanu jātibādhakābhāva evoktanyāyāvatāra ityataḥ prakṛte'pi tulyavyaktikatvādikaṃ nāstītyāha -- vyaktyabhedo'pīti |
prakṛte sādhyābhāvarūpatayā sandigdhatvātsandehasya sādhyasandehatvenāpratibandhakatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
jātereva sādhyatvātkathametadityata āha -- ātmatveti || ayaṃ bhāvaḥ |
iha cātmatvajātisādhanaṃ na tāvanmātratātparyakam |
vaiyarthyāt |
kintu taddvārā vyaktibheda eva sādhyaḥ |
evañca paramparayābhedasya sādhyatvena na bhedasandeho'numitipratibandhakaḥ pratyuta guṇa eveti || nanu sākṣātsādhyasandeha evāpratibandhaka iti cenna |
sādhyaniyatasaṃśayamātrasya tathātvāt |
anyathā vahnisāmagrīsaṃśayaḥ pratibandhakaḥ syāt |
nanu satpratipakṣādisthale sādhyasandeho'pyanumānaṃ niruṇaddhīti cet |
satyam |
virodhyāhitasya tathābhāve'pi svārasikasaṃśayasya guṇatvāt || gaganatvādikamiti || uktanyāyādeveti bhāvaḥ || dharmigrāhaketi || śabdasyopādānakāryatvādinetyarthaḥ || na ca pāramārthiketi || ghaṭādāvapāramārthike bhedasya ghaṭatve'pi kṛtenetyarthaḥ || dharmisatteti || dharmiṇāmeva vyāvahārikatvena bhedasya tathātvopapattāvātmano dharmitvena pāramārthikatvasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
evañca jātilakṣaṇe'nekānugatatvaṃ dharmisattāsamānasattākabhedavadvṛttitvamiti jātisiddhyaivabhedapāramārthyasiddhiḥ |
yadvā || gaganatvasyāpīti |
tatrāpi prātibhāsikavyaktibhedasyaiva jātighaṭakatvasambhavāditi bhāvaḥ ||
|| anyatheti || tatrāpi prātibhāsikadharmibhedasya sattvāditi bhāvaḥ |
śaṅkakābhiprāyamāviṣkṛtya nirākaroti || vyaktyabhedo'pīti || sādhyābhāvasandeha iti || svārasikasandeha ityarthaḥ |
āhitastu bhavatyeva sa ca prakṛte nāstyeva |
tasya parāmarśādau aprāmāṇyaśaṅkāmūlakatvāt |
teṣāṃ ca prakṛte'nukūlatarkabhayenānutthiteriti bhāvaḥ || dharmigrāhakamāneneti || śabdarūpaguṇāśrayatayā gaganasya siddhiḥ |
tatra pṛthivyādicatuṣṭayakalpane gauravapratisandhānenaiko vibhurākāśaḥ sidhyati |
evamidānīṃ ghaṭa ityādipratītiṣu taraṇiparispando ghaṭādīnāṃ sambandhaghaṭakatayā lāghavādeko vibhuḥ kālaḥ sidhyati |
iha ghaṭa ityādipratītinirvāhāya mūrtasambandhaghaṭakatayā lādhavādekā vibhurdik sidhyati |
tatra jātyaṅgīkāre nānāgaganādiprāptyā tasya dharmigrāhakamānabādhādityarthaḥ |
na cetītyarthāntaramityanena sambandhaḥ |
nanu tathāpi vyaktyabhedo bādhakaḥ |
yathoktam |
`vyakterabhedastulyatvaṃ saṅkaro'thānavasthitiḥ |
rūpahānirasambandho jātibādhakasaṅgraha' ityata āha -- vyaktyabheda iti || ātmatvasya jātitve vyaktyabhedaḥ svāśrayapratiyogikabhedavadavṛttitvaṃ virodhīti na vācyam |
tasya sākṣājjātitvānumitivirodhitve mānābhāvāt |
phalībhūtātmabhedānumitau ca tanniścayasyaiva bādhavidhayā virodhitvāt, tasya ca prakṛte'bhāvādityāha -- ātmatveti || ātmabhedaḥ ātmatvasya svāśrayapratiyogikabhedavadvṛttitvam |
sa ca sandigdha iti || tatsandehasya ca vyaktyabhedasandehatayā na virodhitvamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanu tatsandeha evānumitipratibandhako bhaviṣyatītyata āha -- na ceti || anumānamātrocchedaprasaṅga iti || sarvatra sādhyasandehasattvāditi bhāvaḥ |
etacca sādhyasandehaḥ pakṣateti matenoktam |
siddhyabhāvapakṣatāpakṣe tu sādhyasandehasyānumityavyavahitapūrvamanāvaśyakatvāt sandigdhasādhyakānumānocchedaprasaṅgo draṣṭavyaḥ |
nanu saṃśayasādhāraṇavyaktyabhedajñānamātrasya jātitvānumitau maṇimantrādivat svātantryeṇaiva virodhitvam |
anyathoktakrameṇa gaganatvādijātisiddhiprasaṅgāt |
tathāca nātmatvajātisiddhirityāśaṅkate -- evamiti || vyaktyabhedasandehasya jātitvānumityavirodhitva ityarthaḥ |
syādevaṃ yadi prakṛta iva gaganavyaktyabhedaḥ sandigdhaḥ syāt |
na caivam |
api tu pramita eva |
tathāca tata evātiprasaṅgavāraṇāduktavirodhitve mānābhāva iti bhāvena samādhatte || dharmigrāhaketi || śabdo dravyāśrito guṇatvādrūpavadityanumānena śabdasyaikadravyāśritatve lāghavamiti lāghavagrahasahakṛtena pṛthivyādyaṣṭadravyāśritatvabādhe satyaṣṭadravyātiriktaikadravyāśritatvasyaiva siddhyā gaganatvāderjātitvābhāvaniścayādityarthaḥ |
dharmigrāhaketyanenopajīvyatayā prābalyaṃ sūcitam |
tena vaiparītyaśaṅkānavakāśaḥ || pratyakṣasyeti || sākṣiṇo nāhaṃ nirduḥkha ityādirūpasya bhedagrāhakatvenaiva pūrvamuktatvādityarthaḥ |
evaṃ ca noktābhāsasāmyamityāśayaḥ |
ātmatvaṃ nānāvyaktiniṣṭhaṃ jātitvāditi phalībhūtānumāne sādhyaśarīre bhedasāmānyapraveśādāropitabhedasiddhyā'pyanumānaparyavasānādarthāntaramityāśaṅkya nirācaṣṭe || na ceti || ātmatvajātyupapattyā ātmatvasya jātitvopapattyā || jāteriti || tathāca dharmisattāsamānasattākabhedasyaiva sādhyaghaṭakatvādātmani pāramārthikabhedaṃ vinā sādhyāparyavasānānnārthāntaratvamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanu jāterbhedavadvyaktyāśritatvamityeva niyamo na tu dharmisattāsamānasattākatvamapi tatra niveśyata ityata āha -- anyatheti || jātitvāpatteriti || gagane'pyaupādhikabhedasattvāditi bhāvaḥ |
etacca māyimatenoktam |
siddhāntibhiraupādhikabhedānaṅgīkārāt |
tarhi vyaktyabhedo bādhako'stvityata āha -- vyaktyabhedo'pi na bādhaka iti || ayamarthaḥ |
vyaktyabhedo hi nedānīmapi niścitaḥ āvayostatraiva vivādāt |
kintu sandigdhaḥ |
tathācātrabhedasya sādhyatvādabhedasya tadabhāvarūpatvāt tatsandehasya sādhyatadabhāvakoṭikasaṃśayatvāt tasya ca sārvatrikatvenānumānamātrocchedakatayā dūṣaṇatvāt pratyuta pakṣatāghaṭakatvenānumityaṅgatvāt bhedānumityapratibandhakatvāt vyaktyabhedo nātra bādhaka iti || evantarhīti || tatrāpi vyaktibhedasyoktarītyā bādhakatvaparihārasambhavāditi bhāvaḥ || dharmigrāhaketi || ākāśo dharmī |
śabdaḥ kvacidāśritaḥ guṇatvādityanena śabdāśraye sāmānyataḥ siddhe tasya pṛthivyādyaṣṭānyatamatvabādhe tadatirikto navama ākāśākhyaḥ padārtho'ṅgīkriyate |
lāghavādanekatve pramāṇābhāvāccaikatvaṃ ca tasya niścitam |
tathācaikatvarūpasādhyābhāvasya niścitatvāt bhavatyeva tatra vyaktyabhedo bādhaka iti bhāvaḥ |
tarhyatrāpi tathaivāstvityata āha -- nacaivamiti || ātmagrāhakaṃ sākṣipratyakṣam |
tacca bhedagrāhakameveti nāhaṃ sarvajña ityādinā prāgupapādanādityarthaḥ |
tasmānnābhāsasamānayogakṣematvamiti bhāvaḥ |
upapattyā ātmatvajāteḥ || jāteriti || jāterhi dharmiṇo vyakteḥ yadṛśī sattā tatsamānasattāko yo bhedaḥ tadvatyo yā vyaktayaḥ tadāśritatvaṃ niyatam |
ghaṭatvajāterdharmiṇo ghaṭasya yā sattā vyāvahārikī tatsamasattāko yo bhedo vyāvahārikaḥ tadvatyo ghaṭavyaktayaḥ tadāśritatvadarśanāt |
tathā''tmatvajāterapi dharmiṇa ātmano yādṛśī sattā pāramārthikī tatsamasattāko yaḥ pāramārthikabhedaḥ tadvadātmavṛttitve ātmanāṃ pāramārthikabhedasiddhyā'rthāntaratā netyarthaḥ |
jātitve vyaktibhedamātrasya prayojakatvamastu natu bhedasya dharmisattāsamasattākatvamityata āha -- anyatheti || vyaktibhedabhāvādeva hi gaganatvasya jātitvaparityāgaḥ |
nacāsau yuktaḥ |
tatrāpi prātibhāsikabhedasadbhāvāt |
ato gaganatvamapi jātiḥ syāt |
ato'tra prātibhāsikabhedasadbhāve'pi ākāśo dharmī tasya yā sattā vyāvahārikī tatsamasattākabhedābhāvāt na gaganatvaṃ jātiriti parihārāya jāterdharmisattāsamānasattākabhedasāpekṣatvamavaśyamaṅgīkāryamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanvetāvatā''tmatvasya jātitvamātraṃ sidhyati nātmabhedaḥ |
na ca tathātve vyaktyabhedo bādhakaḥ syāt |
sa hyanugatavyavahārābhāvenānugatavyavahārarūpajātiviṣayakapramāṇavighaṭanena dūṣaṇam |
na cātmatvajātāvanugatavyavahāraḥ |
yena tadvighaṭanena jātireva na sidhyediti vyaktyabhedo dūṣaṇaṃ syāt |
kintu tvaduktānumānānyeva |
tataḥ kathaṃ bhedasiddhiriti cet |
satyam |
nātra vyaktyabhedo jāteḥ pramāṇavighaṭanena dūṣaṇam |
yenaivamupalabhye(bhe)mahi |
kintarhi lakṣaṇavighaṭanadvārā |
nityamekamanekānugatamiti hi tallakṣaṇam |
tathātve ātmabhedābhāve |
vyaktyabhedo hi na sākṣādeva jātibādhakaḥ |
kintu jātiviṣayakapramāṇavighaṭakatvena |
pramāṇaṃ ca nānāviśeṣyakaikaprakārakābhijñābhivādanarūpānugatavyavahāra eva |
ata eva ghaṭādiṣu ghaṭatvādijāteraṅgīkāro na gaganādiṣu gaganatvādijāteḥ |
na ca prakṛte ātmani tādṛśavyavahāro'sti |
svātmātiriktasyātmanaḥ pratyakṣatvābhāvāt |
anumānādeścādyāpi gahananiviṣṭatvāt |
ataḥ kathaṃ vyaktyabhedastatra tadvighaṭakatvena jātibādhakaḥ syāt |
na ca tatrānugatavyavahārābhāve'pi tatsādhakānyanumānānyupanyastānyeviti vācyam |
tairjātimātrasiddhāvapyātmabhedasiddheriti bhāvena pūrvapakṣī samādhatte |
sa hītyādinā kathaṃ bhedasiddhirityantena |
ātmatvajātāviti viṣayasaptamī |
anugatavyavahāraḥ pramāṇamiti śeṣaḥ |
tathā cātmatvarūpānugatajātyaṅgīkāre'yamātmā'yamātmeti nānātmaviśeṣyakātmatvarūpaikaprakāravyavahāra eva na syāt |
ata ātmatvajātāvanugatavyavahāra eva pramāṇamiti na vācyamityarthaḥ |
tvaduktānumānānyeva |
pramāṇamiti śeṣaḥ || yenaivamupalabhemahīti |
yenānugatavyavahārarūpapramāṇavighaṭakatvābhāvena vyaktyabhedasyādūṣaṇatvaṃ pratīyeta ityarthaḥ || anekānugatamiti || anekasamavetamityarthaḥ |
anugatamityanantaramiti śabdo'dhyāhāryaḥ |
tataśca nityatve satyekatve satyanekānugatatvaṃ hi jātilakṣaṇamityarthaḥ sampadyate |
tathācātmabhedānaṅgīkāre ātmatvasyānekānugatatvānupapattyā jātitvameva na syāditi bhāvaḥ |
atrānekānugatatvamityanekasamavetatvamucyate |
anyathā gaganātyantābhāve'nekānugate'tivyāptiḥ syāt |
samavetatvasya tvabhāve'bhāvānnātivyāptiḥ |
tāvatyevokte ākāśasamavete śabde'tivyāptiḥ |
ato'neketi |
paramāṇusamavetaviśeṣeṣvativyāptinirāsāyaikamiti |
saṃyoge tadvyudāsāya nityeti |
nanvathā'pi nityamanekānugatamityevālam |
anekānugatatvamityasyānekatvasaṅkhayāvadanugatatvavivakṣāyāṃ guṇatvādijātāvavyāpteḥ svāśrayabhinnasamavetatve vivakṣaṇīye tenaikatvasya svata eva lābhāt |
na hi prativyaktibhinnasya svāśrayabhinnasamavetatvaṃ sambhavati |
ata ekamiti mā'stviti cet |
satyam |
tathā'pi na sāmānyaṃ viśeṣavatprativyaktibhinnaṃ kintvekameva sarvatrānugatavyavahāraheturiti svarūpakathanārthametaditi naiyyāyikasampradāyavidaḥ |
|| etāvateti || nirbādhena lāghavānugṛhītenoktānumānajātenetyarthaḥ || vyaktibhedo bādhaka iti || evaṃ svayaṃ sidhyato jātiḥ svabādhakavyaktyabhedaṃ bādhata eva |
yāvatā vinā na pakṣe sādhyaparyavasānaṃ tatsarvaṃ pakṣadharmatābalalabhyamiti nyāyāditi bhāvaḥ |
anugatavyavahāraprāmāṇyakajātāveva tadbādho nānyatretyata āha -- sa hīti |
ekasminnanugatavyavahārāyogāditi bhāvaḥ |
anugatavyavahāra iti pramāṇīkṛta iti śeṣaḥ || tvadukteti || pṛthivītvamityādyairanumānairevānugatavyavahāraṃ vinā jātisiddhau vyaktibhedasya kutropayoga iti bhāvaḥ |
tathātve dharmibhedābhāve || sa hīti |
nānāviśeṣyakaikaprakārakajñānarūpo'nugatavyavahāro hi saṅkarādyabhāve jātau pramāṇam |
vyaktyabhede hi sa na syāditi |
vyaktyabhedasya pramāṇavighaṭakatvena dūṣaṇatvam |
prakṛte tvanugatavyavahārarūpapramāṇābhāve'pi tvadudāhṛtānumānānāmeva pramāṇatvāt na tasya bādhakateti samudāyārthaḥ |
ātmabhedavicārāvasare uktānumānaistadasiddhyā'rthāntaratetyāśaṅkate -- nanvityādinā |
ekavyaktimātraviṣayakānugatapratītestadviṣayakatvenaiva sambhavāttannirvāhāya jātirna sidhyati |
vyaktibahutve tu ghaṭo ghaṭa ityanugatavyavahārasyaikavyaktyā nirvoḍhumaśakyatvāttādṛśanikhilapratītiṣu bhāsamānā ekā (jātiḥ) sidhyatīti vyaktyaikyaṃ jātigrāhakapramāṇavighaṭakamiti bhāvaḥ |
evamupalabhyemahi evamākṣepagocarā bhavāma ityarthaḥ || kintarhīti || lakṣaṇeti || tathā cāropitavyaktibhedamādāya gaganatve jātilakṣaṇātivyāptiparihārāya svasamānasattākabhedavannānāvyaktivṛttitvaghaṭitaṃ jātitvaṃ tadeva sādhyaśarīre jātitvaṃ niveśanīyam |
anyathā jātitvasyākhaṇḍopādhitve mānābhāvena tena rūpeṇa niveśāyogaḥ |
tathā'pyuktānumānabalādeva pāramārthikātmabhedamādāyātmatvaṃ sidhyatīti bhāvaḥ |
nanūktānukūlatarkasahakṛtenoktānumānajātenātmatvajātisiddhāvapi nātmabhedasiddhiḥ |
na ca tata eva tadanumānasambhavaḥ |
aprayojakatvāt |
uktaniyamasya nirmūlatvādityāśayena darśayati -- nanviti || tathātve ātmabhedābhāve || bādhaka iti || ātmatvajātisiddhivirodhītyarthaḥ |
tathā ca vipakṣe bādhakatarkasambhavānnāprayojakatvamityabhisandhiḥ |
vyaktyabhedo hi na sākṣājjātisiddhivirodhī |
mānābhāvāt |
api tu kāraṇavighaṭanadvārā |
kāraṇaṃ ca kvacidanugatavyavahāraḥ kvacidanumānam |
prakṛte tvanumānameva |
na ca tatra tasya vyaktyabhedasya virodhitvaṃ mānābhāvādityāha -- sa hīti || anugatavyavahāro nāma nānekeṣvekadharmajñānam |
anugatadharmasiddheḥ pūrvaṃ tadasiddheḥ |
kintvanekeṣvekākārajñānam |
vyaktyabhedaśca vyaktyabhedaghaṭitatādṛśapramāṇavirahasampādakatvena jātisiddhivirodhītyarthaḥ || na ceti || anyathā'numānavaiyarthyamiti bhāvaḥ |
yaduktaṃ na cātmatvajātāvityādi tatsatyameva |
kintu vyaktyabhedasya jātilakṣaṇavirodhitayā tadabhāvadhīsampādakatvājjātisiddhivirodhitvamiti samādhatte || satyamiti || atra jāteḥ prakṛtasādhanaviṣayībhūtātmatvajāterityarthaḥ |
yadyapi vyaktyabhedasya jātitvābhāvavyāpyatayā sākṣādapi jātisiddhivirodhitvasambhavaḥ |
tathāpyanukūlatarkāsphūrtau tādṛśavyāpyatvasya durgrahatvāllakṣaṇavighaṭanadvāretyuktam |
lakṣaṇasya ca lakṣyatāvacchedakavyāpakatvaṃ, vyāpakābhāvasya vyāpyābhāvasādhakatvaṃ ca prasiddhameva |
tathāca vyaktyabhede lakṣaṇābhāvāpattyā jātitvānupapattirūpānukūlatarkasattvāduktajātitvahetukānumānenātmabhedasiddhirāvaśyakītyāśayaḥ |
nanu kathaṃ vyaktyabhedasya jātilakṣaṇavighaṭakatvamiti cettasya vyaktibhedaghaṭitatvādityāśayena tārkikoktaṃ jātilakṣaṇamāha -- nityamiti || saṃyogādāvativyāptivāraṇāya nityamiti || gaganaparimāṇādāvativyāptivāraṇāyānekānugatamiti || anekasamavetamityarthaḥ |
tenātyantābhāve nātivyāptiḥ |
anekasamavetatvaṃ ca na padārthavibhājakopādhirūpeṇa |
tathā satyanekavṛttitāvacchedakapadārthavibhājakopādhimattvarūpasya tasya viśeṣe'pi sattvādativyāpteḥ |
kintu pratyekaṃ svasamavāyipratiyogikabhedavatsamavetatvaparyavasitameva vivakṣitamiti jñāpanāyaikamityuktam |
ekamiti kriyāviśeṣaṇam |
tenaikasambandhena samavāyenānekavṛttitvalābha ityapyāhuḥ |
pūrvamuktānumānairātmabhedamātrasiddhimaṅgīkṛtya tasya pāramārthikatvāsiddhyā'rthāntaramāśaṅkya nirākṛtam |
adhunābhedasyaivāsiddhyā'rthāntaramityāśaṅkate -- nanviti || etāvatā uktānumānasamudāyena |
tathātve ātmabhedamanaṅgīkṛtyātmatvasya jātitvāṅgīkāre tasya prakṛte bādhakatvaṃ nirākaroti || sahītyādinā || sa cānugatapratyayasādhyaḥ |
anugatapratyayopyanugatadharmasādhya ityanugatāghaṭatvādijātiraṅgīkriyata ityanugatavyavahāro'tra jātyaṅgīkāre pramāṇam |
vyaktibhedābhāve cānugatavyavahārasyaivābhāvādanugatajātyaṅgīkaro vyartha iti khalu vyaktyabhedasyānugatavyavahārarūpajātighaṭakapramāṇavighaṭakatvena dūṣaṇatvam |
naca prakṛte'nugatavyavahārabalādātmatvasya jātitvamaṅgīkriyate |
yenānugatavyavahārarūpajātighaṭakapramāṇavighaṭanena vyaktyabhedo dūṣaṇaṃ syāt |
kintu anumānabalādevātmatvasya jātitvasvakāraḥ |
nacoktarītyā vyaktyabhedasya tadvighaṭakatvamastīti samudāyasyārthaḥ |
vyavahāra ityanantaraṃ pramāṇamiti śeṣaḥ |
jātireva ātmatvarūpajātireva |
tvadanumānānyeva pramāṇamiti śeṣaḥ || tataḥ kathamiti || vyaktyabhedasya dūṣaṇatve ātmatvasya jātitvanirvāhāya vyaktāvātmani bhedo'ṅgīkāryaḥ tadabhāve tu nimittābhāvādātmabhedasiddhiḥ kathamityarthaḥ |
atra prakṛte || lakṣaṇavighaṭanadvāreti || vyaktyabhede anekānugatatvarūpalakṣaṇāyogāditi bhāvaḥ |
kiṃ tajjātilakṣaṇaṃ yadvighaṭakatvena vyaktyabhedasya dūṣaṇatvaṃ jānīma ityato naiyāyikoktajātilakṣaṇamupanyasyati -- nityamiti ||
anekānugatamiti || anekasamavetamityarthaḥ |
anyathā ākāśātyantābhāve'tivyāptiḥ |
tatra nityatve satyekatve satyanekānugatatvasya satvāt |
samavetatvamityuktau ca nātivyāptiḥ |
tatra nityatve satyekatve satyapi anekasamavetatvābhāvāt |
abhāvasya kvacidapyasamavāyāt |
tathā ca nityatve satyekatve satyanekasamavetatvaṃ sāmānyalakṣaṇam |
atra samavetamityevoktau śabde'tivyāptiḥ |
tasyākāśasamavetatvāt |
ato'neketi |
ākāśasyaikatvena śabdasyānekasamavetatvābhāvāt parihāraḥ |
anekasamavetamityukte viśeṣeṣu |
teṣāmapi anekaparamāṇusamavetatvāt |
ataḥ eketi |
teṣāmanantatvāt |
ekamanekasamavetamityukte saṃyoge |
tasya dviṣṭhaguṇatvenānekasamavetatvādekatvācca |
ato nityeti |
tasyānityatvāt |
nityamityukte viśeṣeṣu |
ata eketi |
nityamekamityukte gagane |
ato'nekasamavetamiti |
nityatve satyanekasamavetatvamityukte viśeṣeṣu |
ata eketi |
atra sampradāyavidaḥ atraikatvaṃ na lakṣaṇaśarīrāntargatam |
kintu sāmānyaṃ sarvatrānugatavyavahārahetutvādekameva na viśeṣavatprativyaktibhinnabhinnamiti svarūpakathanārthameveti |
atredaṃ cintyaṃ kimidamanekasamavetatvaṃ nāmānekatvasaṅkhyāvatsamavetatvaṃ vā svāśrayapratiyogikānyonyābhāvavatsamavetatvaṃ vā ādye rūpatve'vyāptiḥ |
rūpe'nekatvasaṅkhyābhāvena rūpatve'nekatvasaṅkhyāvatsamavetatvābhāvāt |
dvitīye ekapadavaiyarthyam |
naca viśeṣeṣvativyāptiparihārāya tat |
teṣāṃ prativyaktiniyatatvena kutrāpi viśeṣe svāśrayapratiyogikānyonyābhāvavatsamavetatvābhāvenānativyāpteḥ |
naca viśeṣasamudāye'stīti vācyam |
samudāyibhinnasamudāyābhāvāt |
bhāve vā tasya samavetatvābhāvenātivyāptyabhāvācca |
samudāyasyāpyekatvena datte'pi viśeṣaṇe'tivyāptyaparihārācceti |
nanu kālākāśasaṃyogasyājanyatvena nityatvādekatvātkālākāśarūpānekasamavetatvātsamagralakṣaṇasya tatrātivyāptiriti cenna |
tādṛśasaṃyogasyaivābhāvāt |
kālākāśayoramūrtatvena tatsaṃyogānaṅgīkāre vyāptatvabhaṅgābhāvācceti saṅkṣepaḥ |
evañcātmā dharmisattāsamānasattākātmapratiyogikabhedādhikaraṇaṃ dravyatvāddhaṭavadityādīnyanyathāsiddhāni(dīnyapi susthāni) |
caitraśarīraṃ vastuto maitraśarīrasvāmibhinnasvāmikaṃ tadviruddhaceṣṭākaśarīratvādityatra maitraśarīraṃ vyatirekadṛṣṭāntaḥ |
jīveśau dharmisattāsamānasattākabhedavantau viruddhadharmādhikaraṇatvātsammatavat |
virodhaśca na sahānavasthānaniyamalakṣaṇaḥ |
aikyavādino'siddheḥ |
kiṃ tarhi nityatvānityatvādāviva vadhyaghātukabhāvalakṣaṇaḥ |
tādṛśaśca dharmaḥ sarvajñatvāsarvajñatvādiḥ |
na copādhibhedamātreṇa tadvyavasthopapattiryogini tadadarśanāt |
evamupanyastānumānānāmābhāsoddhāreṇa pūrvapakṣiṇā''śaṅkya dūṣitānyapyanumānāni nirdoṣāṇi siddhānītyāha -- evañceti || ātmano dravyatve ātmatvasya jātitve ca siddhe dharmisamasattāketi bhedaviśeṣaṇe ca vivakṣite satītyarthaḥ |
ādipadenātmatvaṃ nānāvyaktiniṣṭhaṃ jātitvātpṛthivītvavadityādyanumānagrahaṇam |
caitraśarīrapakṣakānumānadoṣaṃ pariharati -- caitraśarīramiti || vyāvahārikabhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya vastuta iti || atra balavatkāraṇābhāve satīti yugapaditi ca hetau pūraṇīyam |
tenendrānabhimatakhāṇḍavadahanodyoginyarjunaśarīre kālabhedena gamanaśayanavati maitraśarīre ca na vyabhicāra iti dhyeyam |
balavatkāraṇaṃ ceśecchādi || vyatirekadṛṣṭānta iti || tathā cānvayadṛṣṭāntābhāvavivakṣayā yaddṛṣṭāntābhāva ityuktaṃ tatparihṛtaṃ bhavati |
idānīṃ vimatau bhinnāvityanumānadoṣaṃ pariharati -- jīveśāviti || dharmisattāsamānasattāketi bhedaviśeṣaṇadānena pāramārthikabhedasādhane'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā |
vyāvahārikabhedasādhane siddhasādhanamiti codyānavakāśa iti dhyeyam || aikyavādina iti || ātmaikyavādino mate'jñatāsārvajñādidharmāṇāmekatraivāvasthānāṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ || vadhyaghātukabhāvalakṣaṇa iti || atra vadhyatvaṃ nāma paraprayojyābhāvapratiyogitvam |
ghātukatvaṃ ca parapratiyogikābhāvaprayojakatvam |
ayaṃ ca govyāghrādau nityatvānityatvādau sarvajñatvāsarvajñatvādau ca samānaḥ |
nanu na samānaḥ |
govyāghrādau tamaḥ prakāśādau vadhyaghātukabhāvasya samānāśrayāśritatvaniyamāt |
na ca nityatvānityatvādau samānāśrayatā'sti |
na hi nitye gagane kadā'pyanityatvamasti |
nāpi sarvajña īśvare'sarvajñatvam |
evamanitye ghaṭādau nityatvaṃ vā'sarvajñe jīve sārvajñaṃ vā kadā'pyastīti cet |
satyam |
atrāpi samānāśrayatayā prasaktayoreva nityatvānityatvayoḥ sarvajñatvāsarvajñatvayorvā'bhāvapratiyogitvāṅgīkāreṇāvirodhāt |
etāvāṃstu viśeṣaḥ |
govyāghrādau vadhyaghātukabhāvapraviṣṭo'bhāvo dhvaṃsarūpaḥ |
nityatvānityatvādau cātyantābhāvarūpa iti || nanvevaṃ tarhyatyantābhāvapratiyoginoraprāmāṇikatvaniyamānnityatvānityatvayorvā sarvajñatvāsarvajñatvayorvā'prāmāṇikatvāpattyā kathaṃ tadadhikaraṇatvaṃ gaganaghaṭayorjīveśayorveti cet |
satyam |
anyatra prasaktānāmabhāvapratiyogitvenāprāmāṇikatve'pi svasvādhikaraṇe gaganaghaṭādau jīveśādau ca nityatvānityatvādīnāṃ sarvajñatvāsarvajñatvādīnāṃ ca prāmāṇikatvāt |
na ca teṣāmuktarūpavadhyaghātukabhāvānupapattiḥ |
svasvādhikaraṇe parasparasajātīyasattā'sahiṣṇutvarūpatvāttasya |
na hi nityatvaṃ svādhikaraṇe gagane prasaktamanityatvaṃ sahate |
nāpyanityatvaṃ svādhikaraṇe ghaṭādau prasaktaṃ nityatvam |
evaṃ sarvajñatvamapi na svādhikaraṇa īśvare prasaktamasarvajñatvaṃ sahate |
nāpyasarvajñatvaṃ svādhikaraṇe jīve prasaktaṃ sarvajñatvam |
atra vadhyamapi tadevābhipretam |
yaddhi prasaktaṃ sajātīyam |
tataśca vadhyābhimatasyāprāmāṇikatve'pi ghātukābhimatasya prāmāṇikatvameva |
tathā ca kā nāmānupapattiḥ |
jīve prasaktaṃ yatsarvajñatvaṃ tatsattā'sahiṣṇutayā tadghātukābhimataṃ yadasarvajñatvaṃ tasyaiva tatra viruddhadharmatvena vivakṣitatvāt |
evamīśvare'pi yatprasaktamasarvajñatvaṃ tatsattā'sahiṣṇutayā tadghātukābhimataṃ yatsarvajñatvaṃ tasyaiveśvare viruddhadharmatvena vivakṣitatvāditi || nanvevaṃ virodhasya vadhyaghātukabhāvalakṣaṇatvāṅgīkaraṇaṃ svavyāhatam |
svenaiva nyāyāmṛte dvitīyaparicchede jīvabrahmaṇorbhedasādhakānumānanirūpaṇāvasare virodhaśca yadyapi na sahānavasthānam |
advaitinaṃ pratyasiddheḥ |
nāpi vadhyaghātukabhāvaḥ |
nājāta eko'nyaṃ hanti nāpyanyādhāra iti nyāyena tasyābhedāsādhakatvādityādyuktvā tathā'pi parasparātyantābhāvarūpatvaṃ vā parasparātyantābhāvavyāpyatvaṃ vā kālabhedenāpi sāmānādhikaraṇyāyogatvaṃ vā virodha ityuktatvāt |
tathā ca tatra vadhyaghātukabhāvalakṣaṇaṃ virodhaṃ niṣidhyātra tadaṅgīkāre kathaṃ na svavyāhatiriti || ucyate |
dvividho hi vadhyaghātukabhāvaḥ |
parasparavirahasāmānyaprayojakatvarūpaḥ |
parasparadhvaṃsamātraprayojakatvarūpaśceti |
tatrādyavivakṣayaitadgranthapravṛttiḥ |
dvitīyavivakṣayā nyāyāmṛte tanniṣedha iti na svavyāhatiriti || atra kecit |
nājāta eko'nyaṃ hanti nāpyanyādhāra iti nyāyātsarvajñatvāsarvajñatvayorbhinnādhikaraṇatve vadhyaghātukabhāva evānupapanna iti kathaṃ tena jīveśayorbhedasamādhānamityālocyātra vadhyaghātukabhāvaśabdena jahallakṣaṇayā parasparaviraharūpatvaṃ vivakṣitamityācakṣate |
anye tu parasparavirahavyāpyatvamityāhuḥ |
tadubhayamapi cintyam |
uktarītyā mukhyārthasambhave'nupapatterabhāvāt |
lakṣaṇābījasya sambandhasya lākṣaṇikaprayoge prayojanasya vā'nirūpaṇācca |
asarvajñatvapadenālpajñatvavivakṣāyāṃ tasya sarvajñatvaviraharūpatvāyogācceti |
nanvatra vadhyaśabdenātyantābhāvapratiyogyaprāmāṇikameva vivakṣitamiti yadaktam |
tanna |
atyantābhāvapratiyoginaḥ kvacitprāmāṇikatvasyāpyaṅgīkāryatvāt |
anyatheśe'lpaśaktirnāstīti buddhernirviṣayatvaṃ syāditi cenna |
bhūtale śaśaviṣāṇaṃ nāstīti buddherapi nirviṣayatvāpātāt |
jīve'lpaśakteḥ prāmāṇikatve'pīśe jīvasāmyena prasaktāyāstasyā aprāmāṇikatayā''vaśyakatvācca |
nanu ca sattvāsattve tāvatprāmāṇike |
te ca parasparātyantābhāvarūpatvātparasparapratiyogibhūte |
tathā ca kathamatyantābhāvapratiyoginaḥ prāmāṇikatvābhāvaniścaya iti cenna |
ghaṭādeḥ sattvasya tadasattvātyantābhāvarūpatve'pi na tatrāsattvasya pratiyogibhūtasya prāmāṇikatvam |
evaṃ śaśaviṣāṇāderasattvasya tatsattvātyantābhāvarūpatve'pi na tatra pratiyogibhūtasya sattvasya prāmāṇikatvam |
evañca sati kathamatyantābhāvapratiyoginaḥ prāmāṇikatvaṃ pratyāśākartuṃ śakyate |
ata eva nyāyāmṛtādau vimataṃ mithyetyatra sattvāsattvātyantābhāvayoḥ sādhyatve vyāhatidānaṃ ca saṅgacchate |
`trikālasarvadeśīyaniṣedhāpratiyogitā |
sattocyate'dhyastatucche taṃ pratiyoginī' iti nyāyāmṛte'dhyastatucchayordvayorevātyantābhāvapratiyogitvakathanācca |
na ca tatraiva tatparīkṣāgranthe'śve gotvaṃ kadā'pi nāstītyādau tatsaṃsarga eva niṣidhyata iti mate deśapadamanapekṣitamityuktam |
tena jñāyate svamate'śve niṣidhyamānagotve'vyāptivāraṇārthaṃ sarvadeśīyapadamapekṣitamiti |
tataścātyantābhāvo dvividhaḥ |
sārvadaiśikasārvakālikarūpa ekaḥ |
sārvadaiśikamātro'nyaḥ |
tatra prathamābhāvaṃ pratyevādhyastatucchayoḥ pratiyogitvaṃ dvitīyābhāvaṃ prati tu prāmāṇikasyāpi gotvādeḥ pratiyogitvamiti setsyatīti vācyam |
abhāvadvaividhye pramāṇābhāvāt |
aśve niṣidhyamānasya gotvasya prāmāṇikatvāyogāt |
tathā hi |
kiṃ gotvamaśve prasaktaṃ niṣidhyate'prasaktaṃ vā |
ādye'dhyastameva tat |
na dvitīyaḥ |
aprasaktapratiṣedhāyogāt |
pratiṣedhapratipādanameva niyamena prasaktisāpekṣaṃ na tatpratipattiriti cet |
tathā'pi yadgogataṃ gotvaṃ tadevāśve nāstīti pratīyate |
uta tatsajātīyamanyadeva |
ādye'pi sakalagogataṃ gotvamutaikagogatam |
nādyaḥ sakalagotvānāmajñātatvena tannāstitāpratītyayogāt |
aśve gotvāni na santīti pratītyāpātācca |
na dvitīyaḥ |
tathā sati tadgotvaṃ nāstīti pratītyāpatteḥ |
antye tu tucchamevaitat |
sakalasadgotvasajātīyasyānyasyābhāvāt |
tasmādaśve nāstīti niṣedhyaṃ gotvamasadeva |
tataścāśve gotvasaṃsarga eva niṣidhyate na gotvamiti matameva svamatam |
tatra sarvadeśīyapadamanapekṣitameva |
saṃsargātyantābhāvapratiyoginaḥ saṃsargasyāprāmāṇikatvena tadatyantābhāvasya sarvadeśīyatāniyamāt |
kārikāyāṃ sarvadeśīyapadaṃ tvabhyupagamavādena |
atyantābhāvasvarūpakathanārthaṃ ca |
anyathā sarvātyantābhāveṣvaprāmāṇikapratiyogitvānugatisūcanāya śaśaviṣāṇādīnāmiti bahuvacanaṃ prayuktamiti tattvavivekaṭīkīyamandāramañjaryāṃ spaṣṭamevoktattvāttadvirodhāpātāt |
siddhānte'prāmāṇikasyaiva niṣedhapratiyogitvāditi pramāṇalakṣaṇaṭippaṇyāṃ śrīrāghavendratīrthakṛtāyāmuktatvācca |
vistṛtaṃ caitadasmābhirnirdoṣatvavāde |
ato'trātyantābhāvasya siddhānte tucchātucchobhayapratiyogikatvamapyastītyabhiyogena sarvajñatvāsarvajñatvādeḥ parasparaviraharūpatvāṅgīkāre'pi na prāmāṇikatvabhaṅgo mantavya ityupapadyate |
upādhikhaṇḍanamandāramañjarīgranthavirodhamāśaṅkya tatra kathañcidanupapattyādikamabhidhāya prauḍhivādenaivāsau granthaḥ pravṛtta ityādi yatkaiściduktaṃ tadaśraddheyamivābhātīti cintyam |
na ceti || svābhāvikaikyasadbhāve'pi māyāvidyārūpopādhikalpitabhedasya sattvāttanmātreṇaiva jīveśayoḥ sarvajñatvāsarvajñatvādiviruddhadharmavyavasthopapattiḥ |
tathā ca nānena pāramārthikabhedasiddhirityarthaḥ || tadvati yoginīti |
aupādhikabhedavati saubharyādiyogini parasparamaṃśānāmanusandhānasyaiva darśanena jñānājñānasukhaduḥkhādivyavasthāyā adarśanādityarthaḥ |
|| evañceti || dravyatvādeḥ sādhitatvenāsiddhyāderabhāvātpūrvapakṣadūṣitānyapi punaḥ siddhānīti bhāvaḥ |
ātmatvaṃ nānāvyaktiniṣṭhaṃ jātitvātpṛthivītvavadityādirādipadārthaḥ || caitreti || sakaletyuktadoṣagrāsāya hetau śarīratvapraveśaḥ |
tarhi dṛṣṭāntābhāvādityuktamityato vyatireketi |
śiṣṭaṃ vicāritam |
nanvindrārjunādiśarīreṣu svāmyaikye'pi viruddhāyā rāvaṇādiśarīreṣu tadbhede'pyaviruddhāyāścecchāyā darśanādaprayojako hetuḥ |
maitrasyaiva janmāntarīyaśarīreṣu vyabhicāraśceti cenna |
pratijñāyāṃ bhedapadasya dvitvanirvāhakadharmasāmānyavācitvenendrārjunādiśarīrāvacchinne tāvadviśeṣasadbhāvāt |
na caivaṃ caitramaitrayorapi viśeṣeṇārthāntaratā |
tāvatā'pi nirviśeṣavādavilayāpatteḥ |
vastutastvindrārjunādiśarīreṣvaikyasya pramitatvādbhede pramāṇamātrābhāvācca |
somābhāve pūtikasyeva viśeṣaparigrahaḥ |
iha tu bhedasyaiva svarasatā prāptā |
caśabdādbādhakānāṃ nirasiṣyamāṇatvānmukhyabhedasyaiva graho yuktaḥ |
rāvaṇādiśarīre vyāpakavyabhicārastu nāsmākaṃ paripanthī |
na caivaṃ janmāntarīyaśarīre vyabhicāraḥ sustha eva |
tatsvāmino viśeṣasyābhāvāditi vācyam |
hetau samānakālīnatvasya viśeṣaṇāt |
yadvā mukhyabhedaḥ sādhyaḥ |
tatra viruddhaceṣṭākatvaṃ nāmānyonyāyatanakasukhādyanusandhānānadhikaraṇatvaṃ vivakṣitam |
uktasthale'nusandhānasyaiva sattvānna vyabhicārādi |
janmāntarīyaśarīrādiṣvapi stanyādinā pravṛttyā kiñcidanusandhānāṅgīkārāt |
uttaraśarīragatasukhaduḥkhādyanusandhānaṃ pūrvaśarīre nāstīti tadā vyabhicāra iti cenna |
anyatarānusandhānasyāpi virodhaśabdārthatvādityalam || jīveśvarāviti || pāramārthikabhedasādhane'prasiddhiḥ |
na cetsiddhasādhanatetyuktadoṣagrahāya dharmītyādi |
virodhassahānavasthānaniyamaścetsa hi bhinnādhikaraṇatvaniyato jīveśvarabhedasiddheḥ 
pūrvamasiddhamityata āha -- virodhaśceti || nanvevaṃ cedviruddho hetuḥ |
nājāta eko'nyaṃ hanti nāpyanyādhāra iti nyāyena vadhyaghātukayorekādhikaraṇyāvaśyambhāvāt |
kiñca sarvajñatvāsarvajñatvayoruktavirodhāsiddhiḥ |
nityatvānityatvayorapyuktavirodhābhāvāddṛṣṭāntāyogaśceti cenna |
iha vadhyaghātukatvaṃ nāma pratiyogyabhāvatvamiti vivakṣaṇāt |
aṅgīkṛtyedamuditam |
sahānavasthāna eva virodhastadvāṃśca dharmo jīvatveśvaratvaniyamyatvaniyāmakatvarūpa evāstu |
na caivamasiddhiḥ |
sādhyasandehāhito hetusandeho na doṣa ityuktatvāt |
sahānavasthānaniyamo nāma nedaṃ sahāvasthātumarhatīti nirbādhaviṣayatvam |
bādhakaṃ nirākariṣyate |
athavā pāramārthikatvavyāvahārikatvaudāsīnabhedamātrasāpekṣatvaṃ virodhaḥ sa ca vyāvahārikabhedavādinastu nāsiddhaḥ |
yadvā jīveśvarayorasaṅkaravyavahārārthaṃ vādidvayāṅgīkṛtatadasādhāraṇaśabdaghaṭitalaukikavaidikavyavahāreṣvanyonyaniṣṭhatvenāvyavahriyamāṇadharmādhikaraṇatvamiti dik || tadvyavastheti || sārvajñādivyavahārasyetyarthaḥ |
tathā ca bhedābhāve vyavasthā na syāditi hetūcchittitarkasyāprasiddhatvādaprayojako heturiti bhāvaḥ |
nānādehagatāṃśeṣvanyonyamaupādhikabhedavatyapi yoginyaṃśabhedena sārvajñāsārvajñarūpavailakṣaṇyādarśanādaupādhikabhedenānyathāsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
nanvārope sati nimittānusaraṇaṃ na tu nimittamastītyāropa iti cenna |
vipratipannasthale kvāpi vyāvahārikabhedenoktavyavasthānupalambhena nimittatvasyaiva bhañjanāt |
nanu viruddhadharmayorviśiṣṭavṛttitvena jīveśvarapadābhipretacaitanye tadasiddhiriti cenna |
viśiṣṭapadoktaviśeṣaṇadvayasyaiva hetutvāt |
na ca tadapi viśiṣṭayoreveti vācyam |
avidyāviśeṣaṇayoreva hetutvāt |
|| evañceti || ātmano dvitvaguṇāśrayatvasamarthanena dravyatvasiddhāvityanvayaḥ |
guṇāśrayatvasamarthanena dravyatvasiddhāvityarthaḥ || caitraśarīramiti || vyāvahārikabhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya vastuta iti |
na cāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇateti vācyam |
vastuto maitraśirīrasvāmitādātmyāpannasvāmikaṃ na bhavatītyabhāvasādhyakatvāttatra ca sādhyāprasiddhirna doṣa ityuktaṃ prāk || viruddhaceṣṭākatvāditi || maitraśarīraceṣṭāviruddhaceṣṭākaśarīratvādityarthaḥ |
evañcaikasvāmikayorghaṭapaṭayorna vyabhicāraḥ |
prāguktamanvayadṛṣṭāntābhāvamaṅgīkṛtya vyatirekadṛṣṭāntamāha -- maitraśarīramiti || na ca kālabhedena maitraśarīra eva gamanāgamanayorviruddhaceṣṭayordarśanādvyabhicāra iti vācyam |
ekakālāvacchedeneti hetuviśeṣaṇāt |
na ca tathā'pi khāṇḍavadahanājñānadarśanānukūlaviruddhaceṣṭādarśanādindrārjunaśarīrayorvyabhicāra iti vācyam |
ubhayavādisampratipannaṃ yadekasvāmikaṃ tacchūnyatve satīti viśeṣaṇāt |
indrārjunaśarīrayostvāvayorekasvāmikatvasampratipatterna vyabhicāraḥ |
ata eva na saubharyādiśarīre vyabhicāraḥ |
na cāprayojakatā |
parasparagataduḥkhānusandhānaprasaṅgarūpavipakṣe bādhakatarkasanāthatvāt |
ata eva nopādhisatpratipakṣābhāsasāmyaśaṅkā || jīveśvarāviti || na ca jīvasyāpi dharmitvāttatsattāsamānasattākabhedavattvaṃ parasyābhimatamiti siddhasādhanateti vācyam |
dattottaratvāt || vadhyaghātukabhāvalakṣaṇa iti || parasparātyantābhāvarūpatvalakṣaṇastadvyāpyatvalakṣaṇo vetyarthaḥ |
tena nājāta eko'nyaṃ hanti nāpyanyādhāra iti nyāyena vadhyaghātukabhāvāpannajñānecchādhāre tadāpannatamaḥ prakāśādhāre na vyabhicāraḥ |
saṃyogatadatyantābhāvayostu nāvayorekāśrayatvamiti tadādhāre na vyabhicāraḥ |
ata eva nityatvānityatvādāviveti saṅgacchati |
na hi tatra nāśyanāśakabhāvaḥ sambhavati |
ata evetthameva nyāyāmṛte'pi bodhyam |
alpaśaktirasārvajñaṃ pāratantryamapūrṇatā |
upajīvakatvaṃ jīvatvamīśatvaṃ tadviparyayaḥ |
svābhāvikaṃ tayoretannānyathā tu kathañcanetyādiśruternāsiddhiḥ |
nanu siddhānte'tyantābhāvasya tucchamātrapratiyogikatvena sarvajñatvādīnāṃ parasparātyantābhāvarūpatve tadvyāpyatve vā tucchatvāpattiḥ |
tadādhāratvasya pakṣe dṛṣṭānte cāsiddhiḥ |
tucchasyetarātyantābhāvasādhyayorvyāpyatvāyogaśca |
na ca tārkikarītyā'yaṃ prayoga iti noktadoṣa iti vācyam |
saṃyogatadatyantābhāvayostu nāvayorekāśrayatvamiti na vyabhicāra iti nyāyāmṛtīyottaravākyavirodhāt |
tataḥ kathamiti cet |
ucyate |
atyantābhāvasya na tucchamātrapratiyogikatvaṃ kintu tucchapratiyogikatvaṃ na tu tāttvikamata ivātucchamātrapratiyogikatvaṃ na vā samavāyaviśeṣyakasaṃsargitvamityāśayenaivāyaṃ prayogaḥ |
ata eva niravadhikābhāvaḥ sadā bhāva iti lakṣaṇāt |
na tu tucchapratiyogika iti || bhavati ca jīve sarvajñatvābhāvo niravadhika evamīśe jīvadharmābhāvo'pi |
anyatheśe'lpaśaktirnāstītyādibuddhernirviṣayatvaṃ syāt |
na ceyamīśālpaśaktisaṃsargābhāvaviṣayiṇīti vācyam |
saṃsargasya pratiyogikoṭāvanullekhāt |
buddheḥ śabdavat vyākhyānāyogāt |
īśatvaṃ tadviparyaya iti udāhṛtaśrutivirodhācca |
anyatheha dhaṭo nāstītyagre bhaviṣyatītyādiprāgabhāvādibuddherapi saṃsargaprāgabhāvaviṣayatvāpātena ghaṭādīnāṃ prāgabhāvādipratiyogitvamapi na syāt |
tasmādatucchapratiyogiko'pi siddhānte'tyantābhāvo'styevetyāśayo granthakārasya |
ata eva nyāyāmṛte sattvāsattvātyantābhāvayoḥ sādhyatve vyāhatidānaṃ lāghavādāvaśyakatvādasattvātyantābhāva eva sattvamiti svīkārāt |
dvau nañau prakṛtamarthaṃ sātiśayaṃ gamayata iti nyāyenaikataraniṣedhasyānyataravidhirūpatvāt |
me mātā vandhyetivadvyāghāta iti |
mayā trikālasarvadeśīyaniṣedhāpratiyogitvatatpratiyogitve sattvāsattvasvīkārāditi iti ca saṅgacchate |
parantu kadāciddhaṭavatyeva bhūtale samayāntare pratīyamānastadabhāvo na siddhānte'tyantābhāvaḥ |
tasyāmūrtatvenānyatra gamanāgamanāyogena ghaṭavatyapi tadabhāvabuddheḥ pramātvāpātāt |
na ca samavāyaviśeṣasya saṃsargatayā tena sambandhena sa nāstīti kathaṃ prameti vācyam |
samavāyaviśeṣasya saṃsargatve pramāṇābhāvāt |
anyathā sarvaṃ sarvatrāstyeva |
samavāyaviśeṣarūpasaṃsargābhāvādapratītiviruddhapratītirityapi syāt |
kintu yathāyathaṃ ghaṭatadbhūtalasaṃsargaprāgabhāvādiḥ |
na ca śabdavattatpratītivyākhyānamanupapannamiti vācyam |
ananyagatyā tatra tathā svīkārāt |
etadabhiprāyika eva bhūtale ghaṭābhāvabuddherviṣayo na vācyaḥ prācāṃ yatra tadbhūtale ghaṭasaṃsargaḥ kadā'pi na tatra tatsambandhātyantābhāvaviṣayoktistvanavasthaiva |
uktarītyā tatra ghaṭātyantābhāvaviṣayatvasyāpi sambhavāt |
athavā tatrāpi pratītirna vyākhyeyā |
pratiyogisamavāyideśa iva tadanyadeśe'pi kālādādiva ghaṭaprāgabhāvādisattvaṃ tayorna viruddham |
iha bhūtale idānīṃ ghaṭo nāsti agre bhaviṣyati prāgabhūdveti pratīteḥ |
na ca tathā'pyavidyamānasyaiva ghaṭādeḥ samavāyānyadeśavṛttiprāgabhāvapradhvaṃsapratiyogitvamāgataṃ na tu deśāntaravṛtteḥ |
pratiyogitvaṃ vartamānasyaiva prāgabhāvādivirodhitvāditi tadarthapratītivyākhyānaṃ durvāramiti vācyam |
etasminbhūtale sa ghaṭo bhaviṣyadabhūdveti |
asmākaṃ samagro bhaviṣyatyabhūdveti vidyamānasyāpi prāgabhāvādipratiyogitvabuddheraviśeṣeṇa nyāye'tyantābhāva iva tattadadhikaraṇe tattatkālāvacchedena tattatsambandhena pratiyogitvasyaiva prāgabhāvādivirodhakalpanayā tatra hi tatsaha virodhena tatrāpratīteravyākhyānāt |
na caivamekaghaṭapratiyogikanānāprāgabhāvāpātaḥ pratīterupalambhāyogāt |
saṃsargaprāgabhāvaviṣayatvādīti cenna |
pratītivyākhyānāyogena prāmāṇikagauravasya soḍhavyatvāt |
anyathā prāgabhāvādāvivātyantābhāve'pi pratiyogivartamānatvasyaiva virodhikalpanena tadbuddherapi saṃsargātyantābhāvabuddhitvaṃ ko vārayitā |
ati lāghavena sattvamātrasyaivātyantābhāvavirodhakalpanayā sarvatra satpratiyogikātyantābhāvabuddhiḥ saṃsargātyantābhāvaviṣayatvamatyantābhāvasya tvasanmātrapratiyogikatvaṃ ko nivārayiteti digityapi kecit |
nanu granthakṛta evamāśayavarṇane mandāramañjarīvirodhaḥ |
tathā hi tatra bhedagrāhipratyakṣe bādhābhāvavyutpādanāvasara uktam |
svaṃ bādhābhāva eva hetuḥ |
svaśabdaśca bhedagrāhipratyakṣaparaḥ |
na ca tatprasiddhyaprasiddhibhyāṃ tadabhāvasya durvāramiti vācyam |
atyantābhāvasya pratiyogisattvānapekṣaṇāt |
jñānantvasato'pi sādhitameva |
nanu kathamabhāvo niṣpratiyogikaḥ syāditi cenna |
kimidaṃ niṣpratiyogikatvam |
pratiyogipratītinirapekṣatvaṃ vā prāmāṇikapratiyogihīnatvaṃ vā |
nādyaḥ |
anuktopalambhāt |
na hyasmābhiratyantābhāvapratītiḥ pratiyogipratītinirapekṣetyuktam |
kintarhi prasaktapratiṣedha ityuktam |
na dvitīyaḥ |
abhāvatvaṃ niṣpratiyogitvavat |
sattvasākṣādvyāpyadharmatvādbhāvatvavadityanumānena niṣpratiyogikābhāvasādhanāt |
atha bhūtalāntare ghaṭe vidyamāne iha bhūtale ghaṭo nāstīti ghaṭasyābhāvo'numīyate |
ayaṃ prāgabhāvadhvaṃsābhāvayoranyataraḥ pratiyogyasamānakālīnatvāttayornāpyanyonyābhāvaḥ |
bhūtalaṃ ghaṭo na bhavatīti prasaṅgāt |
tataḥ pariśeṣādatyantābhāva eva gamyate |
tataścātyantābhāvaḥ kathaṃ niṣpratiyogikaḥ syāditi cenna |
iha bhūtale ghaṭo nāstītyādau bhūtalaghaṭasaṃyogasyaiva niṣidhyamānatvāt |
avaśyaṃ caitattvayā'pyaṅgīkaraṇīyam |
ghaṭasya bhūtalāntare sattvena svarūpeṇa niṣedhāyogāt |
etadbhūtalasaṃsṛṣṭatvākāreṇa niṣidhyata iti cet |
tarhi prāptāprāptavivekena saṃsarga eva niṣidhyata ityāyātam |
saviśeṣaṇe hi vidhiniṣedhau sati viśeṣyabādhe viśeṣaṇamupasaṅkrāmata iti nyāyāt |
kathaṃ tarhi ghaṭo nāstītyatra ghaṭaśabdopādānamiti cet |
bhūtala ityadhikaraṇasaptamyā bhūtalasyāśrayatve'bhihite āśrayatvapratisambandhinyādheyatve cārthādyukte kinniṣṭhādheyatetyakāṅkṣāyāṃ ghaṭaniṣṭhetyāvedayituṃ ghaṭaśabdopādānāt |
evañceha bhūtale ghaṭo nāstītyatrāpi bhūtalaghaṭayorādhārādheyabhāvo niṣidhyata iti sthitam |
tatra yadi nāma tayorādhārādheyabhāvaḥ prāgabhūttadā'yamanubhavastatpradhvaṃsaviṣayakaḥ |
yadi tu bhaviṣyati tadā prāgabhāvaviṣayakaḥ |
yadi na jāto na bhāvī tadā tadatyantābhāvaviṣayakaḥ |
evañca śaśaviṣāṇaṃ nāstītyādeḥ śaśaviṣaṇaṃ nāstītyādyapi na kalpanīnayam |
tatrāpyaprāmāṇikasya śaśaviṣāṇasyaiva pratiyogitvopapatteḥ |
tasmādatyantābhāvasya niṣpratiyogikatvātsvabādhātyantābhāvo heturiti na kaściddoṣa iti |
tataḥ kathamevamāśayavarṇanamiti cet |
ucyate |
na tāvadanumānavirodhaḥ |
tucchapratiyogikātyantābhāvasyāpi svīkāreṇābhāvatvasya niṣpratiyogikaniṣṭhatvāt |
niṣpratiyogikamātraniṣṭhatvaṃ na tu sādhyārthaḥ |
bādhāt |
bhāvatve ca vyabhicārācca |
hetūkṛtasattvasākṣādvyāpyadharmatvaṃ yadyapi sattvavyāpyatve sati tadvyāpyatvam |
abhāvapṛthivyanyataravyāpyatve sati tadabhāvasyāsiddhyāpatteḥ |
nāpi tadvyāpyajātyavyāpyatve sati tadvyāpyatvam |
prāgabhāvatve vyabhicārāt |
tathā'pi sattvasākṣādvibhājakopādhitvam |
tadapi bhāvābhāvabhedena sadvibhāgaṃ kurvato mate svatantrasadviruddhavibhāgavādādasiddhiriti na mantavyam |
tathā ca nānumānavirodhaḥ |
atha bhūtalāntara ityādi granthastu na sādhusandarbhaḥ |
tathā hi |
abhāvatvasya niṣpratiyogikaniṣṭhatvaṃ tucchapratiyogikaniṣṭhatvaṃ tucchapratiyogikātyantābhāvatve tadanyatve vā satyabhāvāt |
prakṛtasya svabādhakavirahasya hetūkṛtasya niṣpratiyogikatve'bhāvatvakathantāyāḥ samāhitatvāt |
na hyatyantābhāvamātrasya niṣpratiyogitvamanumānārtho yena granthasandarbhaḥ śuddhaḥ syāt |
avaśyaṃ caitattvayā'pyaṅgīkaraṇīyamityādyapi na naiyyāyikabhayāvaham |
tenādhikaraṇāntare sata eva ghaṭasya sthalāntare svarūpeṇaiva niṣedhāṅgīkārāt |
etāvānparaṃ viśeṣaḥ |
śaśaśṛṅgādikaṃ sadbhiḥ sarvatra niṣidhyata ityucyate na tu ghaṭo'dhikaraṇaviśeṣe svarūpeṇa niṣidhyata ityucyate |
na punarbhūtalasaṃsṛṣṭatvarūpaviśeṣāṅgīkāreṇa sundaro devadatto nāstītyādāviva viśeṣaṇasya pratiyogitāvacchedakakoṭāvanupādānāt |
abhimatasaṃsargāvacchinnā pratiyogitā paraṃ saṃsargamaryādayā bhāsata ityaṅgīkārāt |
yadi cādheyatāyā ghaṭaniṣṭhatājñāpanāya ghaṭaśabdopādānaṃ syāttarhīha ghaṭo nāstītyagre bhaviṣyatītyādāvapi ghaṭaśabdopādānasya tadeva phalaṃ bhaviṣyatīti prāgabhāvāderapi na ghaṭapratiyogikatvaṃ syāt |
api tvādhārādheyapratiyogikatvameva syāditi na tasmādayaṃ granthaḥ prauḍhivādena prāguktasāmagrikātyantābhāvanāstitvatātparyaka eva vaktavyaḥ |
ata eva mandāramañjaryāmeva satyatvaniruktiprastāve niṣedhāpratiyogitvamiti cenna |
dhaṭāderapi niṣedhapratiyogitvena lakṣaṇāsambhavāt |
traikālikaniṣedhāpratiyogitvamiti cenna |
aśvatvasya gavi traikālikaniṣedhapratiyogitvena tatrāvyāpteḥ |
na hi gauḥ kadācidapyaśvatvāśraya ityādigranthaḥ |
aśvatvāderatyantābhāvapratiyogitvāṅgīkāraparo dṛśyate |
anyathā tatraiva pūrvottaravirodha udāhṛtanyāyena nyāyāmṛtavākyavirodhaḥ syāditi || ata evānirvācyatvasādhakaniṣedhapratiyogitvānyathā'nupapattibhaṅge nyāyāmṛte |
kiñceha bhūtale ghaṭo nāstītyādinā parābhimatasāmayikātyantābhāvabuddhereva saṃsargābhāvaviṣayatvavyutpādanaṃ na tu gavyasattvābhāvaṃ buddherdṛśyate |
tathā hi |
kiñceha bhūtale ghaṭo nāstīti dhiyaḥ saṃsargābhāvo viṣayo na tu ghaṭābhāvaḥ |
iha bhūtale ghaṭo nāstītyatra saṃyogo niṣidhyata ityudayanokteḥ |
bhūtale ghaṭo'stīti buddhestatsambandhaviṣayatve nāstīti buddherapi tadabhāvaviṣayatvācca |
kiñca na tāvadayaṃ ghaṭasya prāgabhāvo dhvaṃso vā pratiyogisamānakālīnatvāt |
nāpyatyantābhāvaḥ |
kadācittatra ghaṭasya sattvāt |
yadi caitatkālāvacchedenātyantābhāvastarhi atyantābhāvātiriktaḥ prāgabhāvādirna sidhyet |
saṃyogasya tu bhūtale bhūtalatve dhvaṃso bhāvitve prāgabhāvaḥ kadā'pyabhāve tvatyantābhāva ityasataḥ saṃsargasya pratiyogitvasiddhiḥ |
na ca ghaṭe sanneva saṃsarga iha niṣedhyaḥ |
tasyāpi ghaṭatulyatvena prāgabhāvādikalpanāprasarāt |
yaduktaṃ vardhamānena ghaṭātyantābhāvastarhyatyantābhāvātiriktaḥ prāgabhāvādirna sidhyet |
saṃyogasya tu bhūtala evāyam |
ghaṭakāle tvasambandhānna bhavati |
ghaṭātyantābhāvasya bhūtalena sahasambandhaśca tatsaṃyogadhvaṃsādiḥ |
athavā pratiyogibhedeneva pratiyogitāvacchedakabhedenāpyabhāvabhedāt |
saṃyuktaghaṭābhāvo'yaṃ viśiṣṭāntarābhāvavadutpādavināśaśālyapyanya eveti || tatra nādyaḥ |
abhāvādhikaraṇayoḥ sambandhāntaramantareṇa tadupaśliṣṭasvabhāvatvarūpaklṛptasambandhatyāgena saṃyogadhvaṃsādeḥ sambandhatvakalpane''vaśyakasaṃyogadhvaṃsādereva taddhīviṣatvopapatteḥ |
anyathā ghaṭasya kapāleṣvatyantābhāva eva ghaṭakālatvāśrayāśrayibhāvadhvaṃsādirūpasambandhābhāvādapratīteriti || ghaṭātyantābhāvaḥ kevalānvayī syāt |
na dvitīyaḥ |
saṃyogasya niṣedhyakoṭitvādiṣṭāsiddheḥ |
daṇḍe satyapi puruṣābhāvena daṇḍyabhāvavadbhūtalasaṃyogavatyapi ghaṭābhāvena saṃyuktābhāvasyādarśanena klṛptaiḥ saṃyogapradhvaṃsādibhireva sarvopapattāvutpādavināśaśīlasaṃyuktābhāvāntarakalpanāyogācceti |
ata eva nyāyāmṛte sattvaniruktiprastāve trikālasarvadeśīyaniṣedhāpratiyogitā sattocyate |
adhyastatucchetaṃ prati pratiyoginīti sattvalakṣaṇamabhidhāya parīkṣāvasare'śve gotvaṃ kadāpi nāstītyādau tatsaṃsarga eva niṣidhyata iti mate deśapadamanapekṣitamityuktam |
na ca tadapi svamatameva nyāyarītyā deśapadaṃ manmate tu nāpekṣitamiti tadartha iti vācyam |
tathātve gaganāderapyatyantābhāvo kevalānvayītyuktatvānna gaganādāvavyāptiritivadaśvāderatyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ neti na tatrāvyāptiriti vaktavyam |
na taduddhārāya sarvadeśīyapadaṃ deyam |
anyathā nyāyanaye gaganādāvavyāptyuddhārāya padāntaraṃ deyaṃ syāditi |
tasmādatyantābhāvasya tucchamātrapratiyogikatvaṃ granthakarturabhimata eveti |
kecittvatyantābhāvasya tucchamātrapratiyogikatvaṃ sādhayanta ityāhuḥ |
pradhvaṃsaprāgabhāvayostāvatpratiyoginā saha kāliko virodha iti parasyāpi sammata iti || tatra pratiyoginā saha kālikavirodhitāvacchedakaṃ pradhvaṃsatvaṃ prāgabhāvatvaṃ ceti nānā vaktavyam |
tato varaṃ lāghavāt |
saṃsargābhāvatvamevaikamiti ghaṭakālīnatadatyantābhāvaḥ sidhyati |
ekarūpeṇa virodhakalpanopapattāvatyantābhāvasya daiśiko virodha itarayostu kālika ityardhajaratīyānupapatteḥ |
nanvevaṃ tato'pi lāghavādabhāvatvameva virodhitāvacchedakamastu |
evaṃ sati ghaṭakāle'pi ghaṭānyonyābhāvo'pi na siddhaḥ syātkintvatyantābhāva ivānyonyābhāvo'pyasatpratiyogika eva syāditi cenna |
manmate'nyonyābhāvasyābhāvatvābhāvenābhāvasya trividhatvāt |
tadudbhāvitāniṣṭāpādanamāpādakābhāvādabhāvatvasyaiva lāghavādvirodhitāvacchedakatve asmadiṣṭasyaiva siddheḥ |
taduktaṃ bhagavatpādaiḥ |
`prāk pradhvaṃsasadātvena trividho'bhāva iṣyate |
bhāvābhāvasvarūpatvānnānyonyābhāvatā pṛthagi'ti |
nanvetāvatā'pi dharmyātmakatvenānyonyābhāvasya bhāvaniṣṭhe tasminbhāvātve'pyabhāvaniṣṭhe tadastyeveti cet tāvatā'pi prāgabhāvāditrayāṇāmevānyonyābhāvarūpatvena teṣāṃ pratiyogisamānakālīnatvaṃ nāstītyuktaṃ syāt |
tadasmākamiṣṭamevetyavocāmeveti |
nanvevaṃ vāyau rūpātyantābhāvo na syāditi cenna |
iṣṭāpatteḥ |
na caivaṃ vāyau rūpāpattiḥ |
kiṃ rūpamātrasadbhāva āpādyate vā vāyurūpasadbhāvo vā |
nādyaḥ |
ghaṭādau rūpasya sattveneṣṭāpatteḥ |
na dvitīyaḥ |
vāyāvityadhikaraṇarūpakārakavibhakterullekhātkārakāṇāṃ kriyayaivānvayāt |
vāyvadhikaraṇarūpakartṛsadbhāvarūpakriyāviśeṣaṇasyaiva śabdamaryādayā pratīterjanakajñānasya ca śābdabodhasamānākārakatayā vāyurūpasaṃsargaviśeṣaviṣayatvāttasya tucchatvena tadatyantābhāvasyāpi vāyau sambhavānna ko'pi codyāvakāśaḥ |
nanu tādṛśasaṃsargasyākālikatvena kathaṃ tajjñānaṃ yenāpādanamapi vaktuṃ śakyata iti cet |
kavikāvyamūlabhūtotprekṣājñānaṃ cedāhāryatvāttasya |
ata eva pratiyogyadhikaraṇasaṃsargāropo'pi tatra kāraṇamiti tvadīyasāmpradāyikāḥ |
etāvānparaṃ viśeṣaḥ |
taistādṛśajñānasya svatantrakaraṇataḥ svīkriyate |
asmābhistu tadeva pratiyogijñānamityāsthīyata iti |
ata eva vāyau rūpaṃ nāstītyabhāvapratītirapi vāyvadhikaraṇakarūpakartṛkasattāmeva niṣedhyatvena viṣayīkaroti śābdabuddhisamānākāratvācchabdaprayogakaraṇabuddheḥ |
anyathā'tiprasaṅgāt |
śabdāttu vāyau rūpamastīti yattannetyeva pratītiḥ |
adhikaraṇe saptamyanuśāsanāt |
vāyvādyadhikaraṇakarūpakartṛkasattāyāstvalīkatvādyuktaṃ sārvātrikātyantābhāvapratiyogitvam |
ata eva na surāṃ pibedityādāvapi balavadaniṣṭānanubandhitvarūpaliṅantakriyāpadārthāṃśa eva niṣedhānvaya iti tavā'pi sammatam |
rūpābhāvavānvāyurityādāvapi niṣedhyatayā pratīyamānaṃ rūpaṃ vāyavīyameva |
vāyau svīyaṃ rūpaṃ nāstītyapratīteḥ |
anyaniṣṭharūpasyānyatrābhāvamādāya tatpratītau ghaṭādāvāpi rūpaṃ nāstītyādipratītyāpatteḥ |
ata eva tādṛśasthale nañprasajyapratiṣedhamākṣipati |
uktaṃ ca |
`aprādhānyaṃ vidheryatra pratiṣedhe pradhānatā |
prasajyapratiṣedho'yaṃ kriyayā saha yatra nañi'ti |
ata eva saṃyogābhāvaṃ bubodhayiṣavo bhūtale ghaṭo nāstītyeva pravaktāro nirastāḥ |
etena bhūtale ghaṭo netyeva prayoktāro nirastāḥ `avyayaṃ vibhaktī'tyādinā'rthābhāve yadavyayaṃ tattena saha nityasamāsāmnānāvdyastaprayogasya sādhutvāt |
dhaṭo nāstītyādau `supsupe'tyanuvṛttestiṅantena saha samāsāprasaṅgādeva nāsādhutvaśaṅkāvakāśaḥ |
na ca ghaṭaḥ ghaṭo netyatrāsādhutvāpattiḥ |
vibhāṣādhikāre nañsamāsāmnānāt |
na ca tatrāpyavyayībhāvaprasaṅgaḥ |
arthābhāva ityarthaśabdopādānamahimnā saṃsargābhāvavācakanañaiva samāsavidhānenānyonyābhāvasthale tatprāpterevābhāvāditīti |
tasmādvāyau rūpaṃ nāstīti śabdajanyā buddhirvāvyadhikaraṇakarūpakartṛkasattvaniṣedhameva gocarayatīti tādṛśollekhavatī pratyakṣabuddhirapi prameyaṃ gocarayatīti siddhamatyantābhāvasyāsatpratiyogikatvam |
avaśyaṃ caitadevam |
anyathā ghaṭo nāsti ghaṭau na staḥ ghaṭā na santī vacanavyavasthā |
devadatto nāsti tvaṃ nāsyahaṃ nāsmīti puruṣavyavasthā ca na syāt |
astitvaṃ pratyakṣābhāvasya kartṛtvaṃ sarvatra nāstītyeva prayogāpatteḥ |
yuṣmadyupapade samānādhikaraṇe'smadi ca tathā vidhermadhyamottamayoranuśāsanāditi || atrocyate |
yattāvaduktaṃ prāgabhāvadhvaṃsayoḥ pratiyoginā saha kālikavirodhitāvacchedakaṃ na prāgabhāvatvaṃ dhvaṃsābhāvatvaṃ ca nānā gauravāt |
kintu lāghavāt saṃsargābhāvatvamekameva |
evañca trayāṇāmekarūpeṇa kāliko virodho na punaratyantābhāvasya daiśiko'nyayostu kālika ityardhajaratīyamiti siddhamatyantābhāvasyāsatpratiyogikatvamiti |
tanna pratiyogyadhikaraṇasaṃsargāropapūrvakaniṣedhadhīviṣayābhāvatvarūpasaṃsargābhāvatvāpekṣayā laghoḥ kvācitkābhāvatvasyobhayānugatasya virodhitāvacchedakatvāt |
tantupaṭādīnāṃ saṃsargasyaivābhāvānna prāgabhāvadhvaṃsayoḥ saṃsargābhāvatvamiti ṭīkākāraistayoḥ saṃsargābhāvatvasyaiva niṣedhena tasya virodhitāvacchedakatvāyogācca |
kāryakāraṇabhedavāde pratiyoginā saha kālikavirodhasya durvacatvācca |
bhedena kathañcidupapādane'pi tantūnāṃ pratiyogikoṭiniveśasya tyaktumaśakyatvāt |
pratiyogitāyā dvitvādivadvyāsajyavṛttitvābhāvena pratiyogitāparyāptyadhikaraṇavirodhasyāpyayogāt |
idānīṃ mayi dhanaṃ nāstītyagre bhaviṣyatītyādipratītyanuredhāt |
vartamānasyāpi prāgabhāvapratiyogitvāderdarśitatvācca |
atyantābhāvasya kālikavirodhasvīkāre'pi daiśikavirodhasyāvaśyaṃ vaktavyatvenārdhacaratīyanyāyānistārāt |
anyathā prāgabhāvadeśa ivātyantābhāvadeśe pratiyogisattvaṃ syāt |
na cātyantābhāvasya sarvakālavṛttitvāttatkāle taddeśe kathaṃ pratiyogī syāditi vācyam |
yata evaṃ sati kālikavirodho daiśikavirodhasyopodbalaka eva na tu nivartakaḥ |
yadapi nanvevaṃ tato'pi lāghavādityārabhya tadasmākamiṣṭamityavocāmetyantaṃ tadapi na ramaṇīyam |
tathā hi |
abhāvatvaṃ kiṃ yatkiñcitpratiyogitāśrayavirodhitāśrayavirodhitāvacchedakam |
kiṃ vā tadabhāvaniṣṭhaprāgabhāvādikā yāvatyaḥ |
nādyaḥ |
tathā sati satpratiyogikātyantābhāve'pi svamate tucchānyonyābhāvasambhavena tasya ca tadātmakatvena tadabhāvaniṣṭhānyonyābhāvanirūpakayatkiñcitpratiyogitāśrayatucchena kālikavirodhe'pi tasya virodhitāvacchedakatvasambhavena svābhimatāsatpratiyogikatvānirvāhāt |
na dvitīyaḥ |
abhāvatvasya prāgabhāvādiniṣṭhaprāgabhāvatvanirūpakapratiyogitāśrayavirodhitāśrayavirodhitāvacchedakatve'pi tanniṣṭhānyonyābhāvatvanirūpakapratiyogitāśrayaghaṭādivirodhitāvacchedakatvābhāvenābhāvatvasya virodhitāvacchedakatvāyogāt |
yadapi nanvevaṃ vāyāvityārabhyāsthīyata ityantam |
tanna |
yadi parvato niragniḥ syāt tarhi nirdhūmaḥ syādityatrāpi dhūmābhāvamātrasyāpādyatvena tasya ca jalādau sattveneṣṭāpatteḥ |
na ca tatrāpi parvatavṛttidhūmābhāvo dhūmābhāvavānparvato vā''pādyaḥ |
sa ca na jala iti neṣṭāpattiriti vācyam |
vahnyabhāvamātreṇa tadāpādanā'yogāt |
na ca nirvahniparvatādikamevāpādakamiti vācyam |
nirvahnimatparvatasya nirdhūmavatparvatena vyāpteragrahāt |
dharmaviśeṣe'niṣṭāpādanābhāve tena tanniṣṭhavipakṣajñānasyocchedāyogāt |
adyaprabhṛtyetādṛśāpādyāpādakabhāvasyāśrutatvācca |
na ca dhūmābhāvasya jala iva parvate neṣṭatā iti neṣṭāpattiriti vācyam |
ihāpi rūpasya vāyāvaniṣṭhatvena tadabhāvāt |
tathā ca parvato yadi vahnyabhāvavānsyāt tarhi dhūmābhāvavānsyāditivadvāyuryadi rūpātyantābhāvavānna syāt tarhi rūpavānsyāditi tarkaḥ sustha eveti |
avacchedakabhedādāpādyāpādakabhāvopapatteḥ |
athavā rūpātyantābhāvo yadi vāyuvṛttirna syāt tarhi tadvṛttirūpapratiyogikaḥ syāditi tarkākāra iti |
vāyāvityadhikaraṇarūpāyāḥ kārakavibhakterityādi yadvikalpitaṃ dvitīye tadapi na yuktam |
vāyurūpasadbhāvasyālīkatvena vāyau tadatyantābhāvasya sattvena tadāpādanāyogādityeva vaktuṃ yuktam |
na hi vikalpasthale upādānāpekṣā |
ayuktaṃ ca tadupapādanamudāhṛtatarkaśarīradvaye saptamyādyabhāvāt |
prasiddhatarka ivāpādyāpādakayordharmyaghaṭitatvācca |
tucchātyantābhāvasya vāyuvṛttitve'pi tasya kevalānvayitvena tasya rūpāvirodhitvena tadāpādanarūpacodyāvakāśāsattvācca |
alīkasya jñānasambhave'pi tena vyāptigrahāyogācca |
tarkavicārāvasare'bhāvajñānakaraṇapārthakyāpārthakyavicārasyāsaṅgatatvācca |
yadapi ata eva vāyau rūpaṃ nāstītyādyanuśāsanādityantaṃ tadapi na |
tathā hi |
ata evetyanena saṃsargābhāvatvasya virodhitāvacchedakatvaṃ vopaṣṭambhakatvena parāmṛśyate |
śābdabuddhestadākāratvaṃ vobhayaṃ vā |
nādyaḥ |
nirastatvāt |
na dvitīyaḥ |
asiddheḥ |
tathā hi |
kārakāṇāṃ kriyayaivānvaya ityatra na kiñcitpramāṇamasti |
na hi kārakavibhaktīnāṃ kriyānvita eva svārthe śaktiḥ |
kintu yogyetarānvite |
tatra yatkārakaṃ sākṣātkriyānvayayogyakartrādi tasya kriyānvayaḥ |
adhikaraṇaṃ tu sarvaṃ na sākṣātkriyānvayayogyam |
yathā'dhvani devadattaścarati gaṅgāyāṃ devadattāya gāṃ dadāti vṛkṣādbhūmau parṇaṃ patatītyādāvadhvagaṅgābhūmyādīnāṃ calanadānapatanādyādhāratvābhāvāt |
na ca devadattādidvārā kriyādhāratā'dhikaraṇānāmiti vācyam |
āvaśyakatvena devadattādyanvayasyaiva vaktumucitatvāt |
ata evāhuḥ |
yadyadākāṅkṣitaṃ yogyaṃ sannidhānaṃ prapadyate tena tenānvitaḥ svārthaḥ padairevābhidhīyata iti || evañca prakṛte'pi rūpaniṣṭhavartanabhāvanāparaparyāyasattākriyāyā vāyuvṛttitvāyogāt |
na ca vāyostathā'nvayaḥ |
sattājātistu na kriyā svamate'nugatāyāstasyābhāvācca |
evañca vāyvadhiraṇakasattetyanvayabodhāṃśo na yujyate |
na vāyurūpakartṛkasatyetyaṃśo'pi karturākhyāto'bhihite tvitarapadottarakartṛkartṛkaviṣayavibhakterabhāvena rūpakartṛkaniṣedhāyogāt |
ākhyātārthastu kartā tu prakṛtipratyayau sahārthaṃ bhūtastayoḥ pratyayārthaḥ prādhānyeneti niyamānna kriyopasarjanatāyogaḥ |
ata eva sattāniṣedha ityaṃśo'pi na yujyate |
na hyevaṃ sambhavati |
tathātve yā nañarthaṃ pratiyogitayā'nveti || ākhyātārthastu dhātvarthe upasarjanatāmaśnuta iti || kiñcaivaṃ bodhāṅgīkāre vāyau rūpaṃ nāstyukte'pi punaradhikaraṇāntarākāṅkṣā syāt |
tadvāraṇārthaṃ sarvatretyuktirapi syāditi || tasmādvāyvadhikaraṇarūpakartṛkasattāniṣedha iti na śābdabuddherākāraḥ |
kintu caitrasyedaṃ dhanaṃ na maitrasyetyādau ṣaṣṭhyarthaṃ maitrasvatvasya nañarthe'nvayena maitrasvatvābhāvavaddhanamiti buddhivatpṛthivyāṃ rūpaṃ na vāyāvityādāvapi saptamyarthādheyatvasya nañarthe'nvayena vāyvādyādheyatvābhāvavadrūpamityevānvayabodhaḥ |
evamanvayabodhe vacanavyavasthā puruṣavyavasthā ca samyagupapadyate |
ghaṭādīnāmevāsti kriyākārakakartṛkatvāṅgīkārāt |
tathā hi |
ghaṭādīnāṃ sarvātmanā niṣedhāyogādadhikaraṇaviśeṣāpādanasyāvaśyakatvena tadādheyatvaniṣedhaviśiṣṭaghaṭapaṭatvavadātmatvāderviśeṣyatvasyaucityāt |
na caivaṃ bhūtale ghaṭo nāstītyatra ghaṭātyantābhāvo na viṣayaḥ syāditi vācyam |
na bhavatyeva śābdabodhe prātyakṣike tvindriyasambaddhaviśeṣyatādisadbhāve bhavatyeva viṣayaḥ |
sa tātparyaśabdaprayogakāraṇībhūtā'stu śābdabuddhisamānākāro mānasādirbhinna eva bodha iti na kiñcitsaṅkaram |
astu vā vāyau rūpaṃ nāstīti śabdasthale vāyvadhikaraṇakarūpakartṛkasattāniṣedha ityākāro bodhastādṛśaśabdaprayogakāraṇībhūto'pi mānasādistadākāraḥ |
tathāpi sarvaireva prātyakṣikairbodhaistadākāraireva bhavitavyamiti na niyamaḥ |
svasyānuvyavasāyasākṣikāṇāṃ tattatsāmagrībalalabhyānāmākārāntarāṇāmapi sambhavāt |
na hi te sarve'pyākārāḥ śabdenābhilapanīyā iti niyamo'sti |
keṣāñcidākārāṇāṃ śabdenābhilapanāyogāt |
yathā jātiviśeṣaviśiṣṭaputrādisvaralipyādibodhādyākārāḥ |
yathā vā bhūtalaviśeṣyakaghaṭaprakārakapratyakṣabodhākāro na tatsamānākāraśābdabuddhijanakaśabdenābhilapituṃ śakyaḥ |
ghaṭābhāvavadbhūtalamityanena ghaṭābhāvasambandhyabhinnaṃ bhūtalamiti bodhāt |
evaṃ bhūtalaviśeṣyakaghaṭaprakāraketyāderapi śabdo vilakṣaṇākārameva bodhaṃ janayatīti śābdabodhakriyāvidāṃ sujñānameva |
vāyvādheyatvābhāvavadrūpamiti bodhāṅgīkāre vāyau rūpaṃ nāstīti prayoktāro nirākṛtāḥ |
saptamyarthasyaiva nañarthapratiyogitvenānvayenopapadena sāmarthyābhāvena samāsāprasakteḥ |
saptamī tu na subantamiti na tayā'pi samāsaprasaṅgaḥ |
ata eva ṭīkākāravyāsatīrthānāṃ bahavaḥ prayogāḥ |
astu vā vāyau rūpaṃ nāstītyatra yatkiñciccaitrasyaidaṃ na maitrasyetyādau satpratiyogikātyantābhāvabodhena yatkiñcidbādhakam |
na hi tatrāpi kārakavibhaktirasti ṣaṣṭhyā akārakatvāt |
anyathā rājñaḥ puruṣa ityatrāpi ṣaṣṭhyarthasya puruṣe'nvayo na syāt |
ata eva na tṛtīyaḥ |
kiñcaivaṃ sati idānīṃ ghaṭo nāstyagre bhaviṣyati idānīṃ tvamasyagre na bhaviṣyasi idānīmahamasmyagre na bhaviṣyāmi |
evamidānīṃ ghaṭo prāṅnābhūt tvaṃ nābhūḥ ahaṃ nābhūvamityādau ghaṭapratiyogikadhvaṃsaprāgabhāvabodho jāyata ityavivādam |
so'pi bhavaduktarītyā na syāt |
tatrāpi kārakavibhaktiśravaṇenāgrimakālādhikaraṇakaghaṭādikartṛkālikasattāniṣedha eva pratīyeta |
na caivam |
anubhavavirodhāt |
ghaṭādidhvaṃsādīnāṃ sattākartṛkatve ca dhvaṃsaprāgabhāvādivyaktīnāṃ nānātvena vacanavirodhābhāve'pi puruṣavirodhaḥ syādeva |
sarvatrāpi bhaviṣyati abhūdityeva syāt |
na ca tatrāpi dhvaṃsādipratiyogivyavasthākṛtaiva puruṣavyavasthā |
`yuṣmadyupapade samānādhikaraṇa' ityādau prathamaikavacanāntaśabdapratipādyatvarūpaṃ yadākhyātārthakartrādyabhedānvayayogatāvacchedakaṃ rūpaṃ tadvattvameva sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ vivakṣitam |
na tvabhedānvayopahitatvaparyantamiti vācyam |
bhūtale ghaṭo nāstītyādāvapi tulyatvāt |
evañca bhūtale vṛttirghaṭābhāvaḥ sattā kartetyapi bodhākāraḥ samāhito bhavati |
vacanavyavasthāyāḥ puruṣavyavasthāsamānayogakṣematvāditi |
kiñca rūpābhāvavānvāyurvāyau nīrūpaṃ rūpābhāvo vetyādyabhilāpasthale kā gatiḥ |
na hi tatroktakusṛṣṭiścalati |
ādye'dhikaraṇavibhakterabhāvāt |
agrime puruṣapadasya nañādibhiḥ samāsāt |
na cātrāpi rūpapadaṃ vāyurūpaparam |
anyathā'nyadīyarūpābhāvenaitādṛśabodhe'bhilāpe vā pṛthivyādāvapi tathā bodhābhilāpāpāta ityuktamiti vācyam |
anvayitāvacchedakāvacchinnapratiyogitāvacchekatvasya vyutpattibalalabhyatvena tata eva pṛthivyādau tathā bodhābhilāpayoraprasaṅgena vṛthā lakṣaṇāṅgīkārāyogāt |
viśeṣābhāvabodhastviṣṭa eva |
api ca ghaṭaḥ paṭo na bhavatīti yattanneti yojanayā ghaṭakartṛkapaṭabhāvaniṣedha eva pratīyate |
na paṭānyonyābhāvaḥ |
na ca paṭapadasya kārakapadavibhaktyantatvābhāvenaitadarthasya nañarthe'nvayabodhātpaṭānyonyābhāvabodha iti vācyam |
vāyau rūpaṃ nāstītyādāvapi rūpapadasya tathātvena tadarthasya nañarthe'nvaye bādhakābhāvāditi sāmyāditi dik |
tasmādevamāśayavarṇanena kiñcidbādhakamiti sthitam |
evañca sati pāratantryādidoṣātyantābhāvānāṃ bhagavallakṣaṇatā'pi samāhitā bhavati |
atyantābhāvasya tucchamātrapratiyogikatve tu teṣāṃ kevalānvayitvena bhagavallakṣaṇatvaṃ na sambhavati |
astu vā'tyantābhāvasya tucchamātrapratiyogikatvaṃ siddhānte tathā'pi na kṣatiḥ |
tathā hi |
harāveva sarvajñatvamanyasminneva tadabhāvaḥ |
evaṃ jīvādāvevāsarvajñatvaṃ harāveva tadabhāvaḥ |
evaṃ gavyeva gotvaṃ tadanyasminneva tadabhāva ityādipratītiḥ sarvajanasiddhā nirbādhā sarvasamādheyā |
sā tu sarvajñatvāsarvajñatvagotvātyantābhāvānāmāropitatucchatatpratiyogitve tucchatattatsaṃsargapratiyogikatve vā teṣāmabhāvīyaviśeṣaṇatāsambandhena kevalānvayitvānna samādhātuṃ śakyā |
āropitasarvajñatvādyabhāvānāṃ haryādiṣvapi sattvāt |
atasteṣāṃ vyatirekitānirvāhakaṃ sambandhāntaramāvaśyakam |
yatheśvarajñānasya viṣayatāsambandhena kevalānvayino'pi vyatirekitāyai samavāyānyadveti |
yathā vā paramate ākāśātyantābhāvasya kevalānvayitve'pi pratiyogitāvacchedakasambandhenākāśamātraniṣṭhatvārthaṃ pratiyogitāsambandhaḥ |
taccātyantābhāvasyāśvādyāropitagotvapratiyogikatve svapratiyogyadhiṣṭhānatvamaśvādimātraniṣṭhaṃ vyāvṛttamanyadvā'stu |
aśvādyanuyogikagotvapratiyogikasaṃsargapratiyogikatve tu svapratiyogyanuyogitvaṃ govyāvṛttamanyadvā'stu tena vinoktapratītyanirvāhāt |
evañca sarvajñatvābhāvasya jīvamātraniṣṭhasambandha āvaśyakaḥ |
asarvajñatvābhāvasyeśvaramātraniṣṭhaḥ |
sa ca svarūpa eva lāghavāt |
tathā ca parasparātyantābhāvasvarūpamasādhāraṇasambandhastadātmakatvāt |
tena sambandhenātyantābhāvasya vyāpakatānirūpitavyāptimattvāt |
parasparātyantābhāvarūpatvaṃ tadvyāpyatvaṃ ca yuktamiti |
evaṃ sarvadoṣātyantābhāvasyāpi nṛharimātraniṣṭhaḥ sambandha āvaśyaka iti tena sambandhena tasya lakṣaṇatā'pi yujyate |
