evaṃ rūpātyantābhāvasya vāyvādimātraniṣṭhaḥ pṛthivyādivyāvṛttaḥ sambandha āvaśyaka iti tena sambandhena tasya tasya rūpavirodhitvāt na vāyvādau rūpāpattiriti doṣasaṃsargātyantābhāve'pi paramparayā doṣasambandhitvāddoṣatyantābhāvatayā vyavahriyata iti na kiñcidanupapannam |
nanvatyantābhāvasya tucchamātrapratiyogikatvaṃ niryuktikaṃ siddhānte kathamaṅgīkriyata iti ceducyate |
tucchamātrapratiyogikatvaṃ tāvatkvāpi śaśaśṛṅgaṃ nāstīti pratītibalātsiddham |
śaśaśṛṅgasya tucchatvāt |
tasya caitatpratītibalātpratiyogikatvasiddheḥ |
na ca tatra śaśaśṛṅgaṃ pratiyogi kintu śṛṅgamātraṃ na tattuccham |
tanniṣṭhā pratiyogitā paraṃ vyadhikaraṇena śaśīyattvenāvacchidyate |
tadavacchinnapratiyogitākaḥ śṛṅgapratiyogitāka evābhāvo'nayā pratītyā gṛhyata iti vācyam |
tathā sati śaśīyaṃ nāstīti pratītyākāraḥ syāt |
śṛṅgatvasya pratiyogitāvacchedakāghaṭasya pratiyogidiśibhānāyogāt |
anyatra tathā'darśanāt |
bādhakaṃ vinā viśiṣṭapratiyogitābuddheḥ svarasabhaṅgāyogācca |
anyathā devadattavati daṇḍī devadatto nāstītyādibuddherapi devadattamātrapratiyogitvaṃ daṇḍitvaṃ tadavacchedakamiti syādviśiṣṭasya pratiyogitvaṃ na syāt |
viśiṣṭaṃ daṇḍadevadattātmakamatiriktaṃ vetyanyadetat |
nanu tatra viśiṣṭaṃ sattvamatrāsaditi vailakṣaṇyamiti cet |
astu kimetāvatā |
na jñānamabhāvapratītikāraṇaṃ na sambhavatīti cet |
asato'pi śabdābhāsādinā pratītisambhavāt |
anyathā tavāpi pratiyogitāvacchedakaprakārakapratiyogijñānaṃ na syāt |
nanu yogyānupalabdhirabhāvapratyakṣe kāraṇam |
yogyatā ca tadvyāpyetarayāvattadupalambhakasamavadhānarūpasadupalabhbhakatvadoṣaghaṭitam |
tatsattve'nupalambhāyogaḥ |
tadasattve na yogyateti nāsadviśiṣṭābhāvaḥ pratyakṣa iti cenna |
pratyakṣāsambhave'pi śābdabuddhereva sambhavāt |
kiñca yogyatāyāṃ doṣātiriktatvamevopālambhakaṃ vaktavyam |
anyathā ghaṭābhāvo'pi pratyakṣo na syāt |
yuktitaulyāt |
kiñca saṃyogināśajanyasaṃyoganāśapratyakṣasthale etadyogyatānupalabdherabhāvāt |
tarkitapratiyogisattvaprasañjanaprasañjitapratiyogikānupalabdhirabhāvapratyakṣe kāraṇaṃ vācyam |
sā cātrāstyeveti na kiñcidanupapannam |
yogyānupalabdhivicārastu prāgeva kṛta iti neha prapañcyate |
vistṛtaṃ caitannyāyāmṛte'khyātivādānyathā'nupapattibhaṅge |
niṣedhyatvānupapattibhaṅge ca |
tathā ca siddhamatyantābhāvasya tucchapratiyogikatvam |
nanu tathā'pi tanmātrapratiyogikatvaṃ kuta iti cet |
śṛṇu |
tucchapratiyogikātyantābhāvasyāsti dauśika iva kālikapratiyoginā virodhaḥ |
tatrāpi kālikavirodhitāvacchedakaṃ na tucchābhāvatvaṃ gauravāt |
kintu lāghavādatyantābhāvatvameva |
tathā ca nātyantābhāvakāle pratiyogī |
atyantābhāvasya tvanādinityatvāt sarva eva kāla iti sakalakālāvṛttipratiyogikatvasiddhau tucchamātrapratiyogikatvameva sampannamiti |
athavā tvanmate pratiyogitāvacchedakāvacchinnasya pratiyoginastaddeśakālāvacchedena tattatsambandhena tatrādhikaraṇe sattvaṃ tatrādhikaraṇe'tyantābhāvavirodhitvam |
tatra yathā lāghavātpratiyogitāvacchedakāvacchinnāṃśatyāgena pratiyogisattvaṃ virodhi kalpayitvā vyāsajyavṛttidharmāvacchinnābhāvasya vyadhikaraṇadharmāvacchinnābhāvasya cāsambhava ucyate |
tathā tato'pi lāghavādyatra kutrāpi pratiyogisattvamātrasyātyantābhāvavirodhitvamāsthāyāsmābhiḥ satpratiyogikātyantābhāvāsambhava ucyata iti || aśve gotvaṃ nāstītyādipratītistu vyāsajyavṛttidharmāvacchinnābhāvavyadhikaraṇadharmāvacchinnābhāvavatpratītivadviṣayāntaramāsādayiṣyati |
tacca viṣayāntaramaśve gotvādisaṃsargātyantābhāva eva |
na ca śabdavatpratītivyākhyānamayuktamiti vācyam |
tvanmate vyāsajyavṛttidharmāvacchinnābhāvādisthala ivānanyagatikatvāt |
anyathedānīmapi dhanaṃ nāstyagre bhaviṣyati prāgabhūdvetyādipratītibalādvartamānapratiyogikau prāgabhāvadhvaṃsābhāvāvapyaṅgīkāryau syātām |
yadi ca tatra svarūpasaṃsargasya prāgabhāvaḥ pradhvaṃsaśca viṣayo lāghavena pratiyogivartamānatvamātrasya prāgabhāvapradhvaṃsavirodhikatvāt |
na tu tena tena sambandhena tattatkālāvacchedena tatrādhikaraṇe pratiyogisattve virodhi gaurava ityaṅgīkriyate tarhi prakṛte'pi dīyatāṃ dṛṣṭiriti saṅkṣepaḥ || naceti || jīveśvarayormāyāvidyārūpopādhikṛtabhedasya sattvātsarvajñatvāsarvajñatvavyavasthopapattirityarthaḥ || yoginīti |
aupādhikabhedavati pratiyogini saubharyādau tattatpadārthaviṣayakajñānājñānayoradarśanādityarthaḥ |
evañceti || ātmano guṇāśrayatvavyavasthāpanena dravyatvasiddhau satyāmityartha iti kecit |
vastutastu dharmisattāsamānasattākānekāśritatvasyāvaśyakatvaṃ tatraiva vyaktyabhedasya bādhakatvamityasya siddhau satyāmityarthaḥ || ityādīnyapīti || ādinā''tmatvaṃ svāśrayasamānasattākasvāśrayapratiyogikabhedavannānāvyaktivṛtti jātitvāddhaṭatvavadityādergrahaṇam |
susthāni pāramārthikātmabhedaparyavasāyīnītyarthaḥ |
āropitabhedavannānāvyaktiniṣṭhatvaṃ jāteryadi syāt tarhi ātmani dravyatva(tvātmatvādi)jātaya ātmāntaraghaṭāntarapratiyogikapāramārthikabhedena vināpyupapanna, na tasyātmāntaraghaṭādibhyaḥ pāramārthikabhedavyavasthāpanakṣamāḥ |
yadi ca vyaktyaikyaṃ pratyakṣādipramādyavighaṭakatvena dūṣaṇam |
tadā'numānasiddhātmatvasyātmanyātmāntarapratiyogikapāramārthikabhedavyavasthāpakatvaṃ na sidhyet |
tadvyavasthāpane tvidaṃ siddhaṃ bhavatītyupaṣṭambhasaṅgatiḥ || caitraśarīramiti || atra vastuto maitraśarīrasvāmibhinnasvāmikamityeva pāṭhaḥ |
vyāvahārikabhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya vastuta ityuktamiti prācīnaṭippaṇyukteḥ |
idaṃ ca bahujīvavādinaṃ prati jīvānāṃ parasparaṃ pāramārthikabhedasādhanāya pravṛttamityāśayenaikajīvavādinirāsāyaitadanumānaprayoge tu tena siddhasādhanatā vyāvahārikabhedamādāyodbhāvayituṃ na śakyate |
ekameva śarīraṃ sajīvamanyāni śarīrāṇi svapnadṛṣṭakaśarīrāṇīva nirjīvāṇyeveti vā sajīvatve'pi sarvaśarīrādhiṣṭhitaika eva hiraṇyagarbho nāma jīva iti vā tairaṅgīkṛtatvenaikaśarīrasvāmini śarīrāntara[re ]svāmipratiyogikavyāhārikabhedasyāpi tairanaṅgīkṛtatvāt |
tadā prātibhāsikabhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya vastuta ityetadaprātibhāsikaparam |
ata eva keṣucitkośeṣu tatpadāghaṭita eva pāṭho dṛśyate |
tatra prātibhāsikabhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya vastuta iti na vaktavyam |
padānāṃ prātibhāsikavyāvṛttadharmāvacchinnopasthāpana eva [sthāpakasyaiva] samarthatvena bhedādipadena tadupasthāpanasyaivābhāvāt |
anyathā vastutaḥ parvato vastuto vahnimānityeva prayogāpatterityabhiprāyaḥ |
[nanu] atra vastuta ityanena pūrvoktamatabhedena pāramārthikatvasyāprātibhāsikatvasya vā niveśe maitraśarīrasvāmipratiyogikapāramārthikabhedavat [bhedena] svāmirūpasādhyāprasiddhyā'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā |
asya bhāvasādhyakatvena sādhyāprasiddherdoṣatvasyāvaśyakatvāt |
na ca vastuto maitraśarīrasvāmitādātmyāpannasvāmikatvābhāva evātra sādhyatvena vivakṣito'(to')bhāvasādhyakavyatirekyevedamiti vācyam |
tathā ca 
vastuta iti viśeṣaṇaṃ pratiyokoṭau abhāve vā |
nādyaḥ |
viśiṣṭapratiyogina evāprasiddhyā tannirūpitavyāpakatāyā hetvabhāve durgrahatvāt |
pratiyogiprasiddheratrā(ddhestatrā)ṅgīkāre'pi tadabhāvarūpasādhyasya vyāvahārikatvena vā prātibhāsikatvena vā siddhasādhanatāyā anuddhārāt |
asya sādhyasya śarīrādhikaraṇatvena siddhasyāpi pāramārthikatvāyogāvyāvahārikatvasyaivocitatvāt |
sarvasyāpyatyantābhāvasya pāramārthikatvameveti matāśrayaṇe pratiyogikoṭau vastuta ityanuktāvapi tādṛśasya svāmikatvasāmānyabhāvasya pāramārthikatvasyaiva (pra)siddhyā pratiyogikoṭau tadvaiyarthyāt |
vastutaḥ pāramārthikasvāmi(ka)tvasyaiva tanmate prasiddhireva |
etena pratiyogini pāramārthikatvānupādāne svāmitvasāmānyābhāve pāramārthikatvatādṛśasvāmitvamithyātvaparyavasāyi syāttena parābhimatasyaiva siddhyā'rthāntaratā syādatastanniveśanaṃ tatpratiyogini taddāne tu tasya pāramārthikatvenaiva gṛhītatvāt na tanmithyātve paryavasānamiti nirastam |
atyantābhāvasya pāramārthikatvasiddhāvapi tasya tādṛśasvāmikatvaṃ 
sāmānādhikaraṇyāsiddhyā mithyātvāparyavasānācca |
atyantābhāvasyāprāmāṇikatvapratiyogikatvameveti 
mate uktasādhyībhūtābhāvasya pāramārthikapratiyogikatvāyogācca |
nāpyuktasiddhasādhanatāvāraṇāyābhāva eva vastuta ityetadviśeṣaṇamiti pūrvoktadvitīyakṣaḥ |
yaddharmāvacchinnavyāpakatā hetvabhāvasya gṛhītā tadabhāvena taddharmāvacchinnābhāvatvena sidhyatu pāramārthikatvena siddhau tu bījābhāvaḥ |
anyathā vyāvahārikatvena kuto na sidhyet |
na ca pra[bhra]mātmakānumitisāmānyaṃ svaviṣayaṃ pāramārthikatvenaiva viṣayīkarotīti vācyam |
tathātve vastuta iti viśeṣaṇopādānamanarthakameva |
anyānumitīnāmivāsyāpyanumiteḥ svamahimnaivoktasādhyasya pāramārthikatvavyavasthāpakatvasambhavāditi cenna |
ata eva vastuta iti padāghaṭito'pi pāṭhaḥ pustakeṣu dṛśyate |
taddāne tu tasya pāramārthikatvenaiva gṛhītatvānna mithyātvaparyavasānam |
tatsattve'pi yathāśrutasādhyamevedaṃ nābhāsasādha(dhya)kam |
tatra vyāvahārikasādhyādikamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya vastuta iti sārthakam |
tacca maitraśarīrasvāmibhede'nveti |
na cāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā doṣaḥ |
naiyāyikaireva tadabhyupagamāt |
vedāntimate tasyā adūṣaṇatāyā `āśrayavyāśrayasiddhī sādhyāsiddhiśca dūṣaṇam |
keṣāñcinna hi te doṣāḥ vyāptau satyāṃ kathañcane'tyādinā bhaktipādīyānuvyākhyāne sudhāyāṃ ca nipuṇataramupapāditam |
advaitacandrikākāro'pi sādhyasyāprasiddhirna doṣaḥ |
tatkāle yathākathañcidvādivākyādinā tadupasthitimātreṇa sādhyasaṃśayasya vyatirekavyāpteścānukūlatarkādisāhityena grahītuṃ śakyatvādityupapāditavāndvitristhaleṣu |
tathā ca kvacinnaiyāyikarītimavalambya granthapravṛttāvapyatra śuddhavādiprativādirītimavalambyaiva granthapravṛttau bādhakābhāvāt |
yadi cā [vā]'yamapi grantho'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatāyā dūṣaṇatvamavalambyaiva lāpanīya ityāgrahastadā nairātmyaṃ kiñcinniṣṭhābhāvapratiyogi vyatirekidharmatvādityādinā sāmānyataḥ sātmakatvasiddhyanantarameva jīvaccharīraṃ sātmakaṃ prāṇādimattvāditi vyatirekyanumānaṃ pravartate |
tathā'trāpi maitraśarīrasvāmibhedaścidvṛttitvapāramārthikatvobhayavān apāramārthikānirūpakakatve sati cidvṛttitvāt cinniṣṭhānandasattādivat |
cidvṛttiśuddhatvādau vyabhicāravāraṇāya satyantam |
teṣāṃ jaḍāmiśritatvādirūpatayā'pāramārthikānirūpitatvābhāvāt |
sattā'pi brahmasvarūpa[tva]mevāto na deśanirūpitāto na dṛṣṭāntāsiddhiḥ |
tasyāstathātve'pyānandajñānayordṛṣṭāntatvasambhavācca |
gagane svābhāvādyanātmakatayā'pāramārthikānirūpite vyabhicāravāraṇāya viśeṣyadalam |
citi ḍitthapiṇḍaniṣṭhāyāṃ bhedasyājñakalpitasya parairapyaṅgīkārānna pakṣā(pra)siddhiḥ |
nāpi hetvasiddhiḥ |
viśeṣyībhūtacidvṛttitvasyopapāditatvāt |
svāmipadena tattvopalakṣitacetanasyaiva grahaṇāt |
tasya pāramārthikatvenāpāramārthikānirūpakatvarūpaviśeṣaṇabhāgasyāpi sattvāt |
tathā coktānumānena maitraśarīrasvāmipratiyogikapāramārthikabhedavatsiddhyanantaraṃ pratiśarīraṃ jīvabhedasiddhyarthaṃ tādṛśacideva svāmitānirūpakatvasambandhena caitraśarīre pradhānato vyatirekaliṅgena sādhyata iti na kaściddoṣaḥ |
yadvā maitraśarīrasvāmī caitraśarīre pāramārthikapratiyogikatvāpāramārthikatvobhayābhāvavatsvapratiyogikabhedavatsvāmikatvasambandhena sādhyate |
ayaṃ ca maitrasvāmikaghaṭe ghaṭāntarasya sambandhaḥ prasiddhaḥ |
ghaṭāntarapratiyogikamaitraniṣṭhe bhede pāramārthikapratiyogikatvābhāvaprayuktobhayābhāvasattvāt |
anena sambandhena maitraśarīrasvāminaḥ sādhyatve sambandhaghaṭakatadbhede'pāramārthikatvābhāvaprayuktasyaivobhayābhāvasya vaktavyatayā maitraśarīrasvāmipratiyogikapāramārthikabhedavatsvāmikatvasyoktānumānabalādeva siddheḥ |
paramate uktasambandhasyoktasādhyaṃ prati vyadhikaraṇasambandhatayā tatsambandhāvacchinnābhāvasya kevalānvayitayā'ṅgīkṛtatve'pyuktavicāradaśāyāṃ maitraśarīrātirikteṣu sandigdhatvena maitraśarīramātre tadabhāvasya hetvabhāvasya vādipratipādyubhayasammatatvena niścitasahacāre'pyubhayoḥ vāstavabhedo na syāt, tarhi parasparasukhaduḥkhānubhavaḥ syādityādirūpānukūlatarkasahitena
vyabhicārasaṃśayamanādṛtya vyāptigrahopapatteḥ |
uktatarkabalādevānumānahetorapi nāprayojakatāśaṅkā'pi |
adhikaṃ pūrvapakṣavivaraṇenānusandheyam |
uktānukūlatarkānusandhānābhāvādeva sāmānyato dṛṣṭāntābhāvakathanam |
atra tu tadabhyupagamena dṛṣṭāntavyavasthāpanābhiprāyeṇa vyatirekidṛṣṭāntakathanamiti na pūrvottaravirodhaḥ || tadviruddheti || ekakālāvacchedena tacceṣṭāsādhyaphalotpattivirodhivyāpārāśrayatvaṃ tadviruddhaceṣṭākatvam |
prakṛte tādṛśavyāpāravantāveva caitramaitrau grāhyāviti nāsiddhiḥ |
vāyūpaghātādivaśāt maitraceṣṭāvirodhiceṣṭāvati maitrasvāmike ghaṭe vyabhicārāpatti[cāra]vāraṇāya śarīratvamupāttam |
tāvatyukte maitraśarīra eva vyabhicāravāraṇāya viśeṣaṇadalam |
ekasvāmikatve viruddhakriyotpattirna syādityanukūlatarkotthāpakatayā vyāptigrahaupayikatvaṃ sarvaviśeṣaṇaghaṭakānāṃ vartata iti na kasyāpi vaiyarthyam |
etattarkopaṣṭambhitatayā'pi nāsya hetoraprayojakatvam |
yattvatra kathitamindrārjunayorekakāla eva khāṇḍavavanadahanatacchamanarūpaviruddhaceṣṭāvatorekasvāmikatvasyaiva sattvādvyabhicāra iti tanna |
uktānumānasya prakṛtahetusādhyaghaṭitavyatirekavyāptimūlakasya vyabhicārāprasakteḥ |
yaccharīraṃ yaccharīraceṣṭāviruddhaceṣṭakaṃ tattatsvāmibhinnasvāmikamiti sāmānyavyāptyā'numānasyāpravṛttatvena sāmānyavyāptau vyabhicāracodanasyākiñcitkaratvāt |
na caivaṃ sati ḍitthaviṣṇumitraśarīrasya jīvabhedasiddhiḥ kathaṃ syāditi vācyam |
tatrāpyetādṛśavyatirekyanumānena tatsiddhyuttarataddṛṣṭāntakānvayyanumānāttatsiddhisambhavāt |
nanu tarhyarjunādiśarīrādidṛṣṭāntenoktahetoraprayojakatvameva śaṅkyata iti vācyam |
anukūlatarkayoruktatvāt |
tatraikasminneva viruddhaceṣṭānukūlaprayatnotpādakabhagavadicchārūpaviśeṣakāraṇarūpabalādekasvāmikayoḥ kadācidviruddhaceṣṭotpattāvapyanyatra bhinnasvāmikatvaṃ vinā tannirvāhāyogāt |
asyā vyāpterautsargikatvena balavadbādhakaikāpodyatvena sopādhikavyabhicārasyādūṣakatvādityuktatvāt |
anyathā yāgīyahiṃsāyāḥ pāpājanakatvena hiṃsātvasyāpi pāpājanakatvayogyatvānupapatteḥ |
tatra yāgīyahiṃsābhinnatvaviśeṣaṇaṃ deyamiti caittarhyatrāpyekatra viruddhaceṣṭotpādakaprayatnajanakabhagavacchaktyādirūpaviśeṣakāraṇā(ṇāna)dhīnatvena pramitatvaviśeṣaṇaṃ datvā sāmānyavyāptyādirapi nirvoḍhuṃ śakya (śakyata) eva |
viśeṣavyāptistu saulabhyāya kutrāpi vibhinnasvāmikatvasiddhyabhāvadaśāyāṃ sāmānyavyāpterdarśayitumaśakyatvāccopāttetyalam |
iyatā bhedasya vyāsajyavṛttitvamavalambya tatsiddhaye dvitvādyavacchinnapakṣakānumānandarśayati -- jīveśāviti || jīve īśe ceti bhedasiddhau dvitvena pakṣīkaraṇasambhavaḥ |
tatsiddhau ca siddhasādhanateti śaṅkānavakāśaḥ |
dvitvāvacchedenaikabhedasiddherabhāvāt |
tatsiddhistu dvitvasya pakṣatāvacchedakatvamahimnā dvitīyakṣaṇāvacchinno ghaṭo gandhavānitivat |
atra vyāptistu yadubhayaṃ parasparaviruddhadharmādhikaraṇaṃ tadubhayaṃ svaniṣṭhobhayatvāvacchedena parasparasambandhabhedavadityuktavyāptijñānasya dvitvāvacchedenaiva bhedānumitijanakatvānurodhenaiva (tat)siddhiḥ |
etena pakṣatāvacchedakatvamāhimnā siddhirityanena tadbhānaṃ na parāmarśakāryatāvacchedakamiti samprāptam |
tathātve uktadvitvopalakṣite'pi bhedānumitijanakatvaṃ syāt |
viśiṣṭaghaṭe bādhadaśāyāmutpattikālopalakṣitaghaṭe tatsthalīyaparāmarśabalenānumitidarśanāt |
tatreṣṭāpattau ca caitramaitrau caitrabhedavantāvityatrānumitirdvitvāvacchedena bādhagrahaṇadaśāyāṃ tadupalakṣite syāt |
na ca tadākārānumitiḥ kasyāpyanubhavasiddhā |
tathānyāyo'pi sa nyāya iti kasyacidanumita iti śaṅkānavakāśaḥ |
uktajñānadaśāyāmanumitijanakatvasyaiva sattvāt || sammatavaditi || jalānalobhayavadityarthaḥ |
anyatra dvitvāvacchedena bhedamaṅgīkṛtya jīveśadvitvāvacchedena parasya bhede vipratipattidaśāyāmevānumānasya pravṛttiriti sammatavadityanena sūcitatvānna dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyaśaṅkāvakāśaḥ || vadhyaghātukabhāvalakṣaṇa iti || parasparābhāvātmakatvaṃ parasparavirahavyāpakatvaṃ vā |
taccaikatvavādinā'pi laukikavyavahārādaṅgīkāryam |
anyathā sarvamaryādollaṅghane kathakatāyā evāsiddhiprasaṅgādityāśayaḥ |
siddhante (abhāvasya) prāmāṇikapratiyogitvanaiyatye'pi yathedaṃ ghaṭate tathopapāditaṃ purastāt (dvaitadyumaṇau) |
nanvekasminnapyāropitayorviruddhadharmayordvitvasya sattvādvyabhicāraḥ |
tadarthaṃ dharmisattāsamānasattākatvasya hetuviśeṣaṇe parasyāsiddhiriti cenna |
pallavājñānakāryatvābhāvarūpaprātibhāsikatvābhāvasya hetuviśeṣaṇatvāṅgīkāre doṣābhāvāt |
manmate ca pallavājñānasyāprasiddhyā taddhaṭitapratiyogitākābhāvasya satpratiyogikatvenāropitaviruddhadharmayoḥ sattve'pi tayorasattvena tadvatvasyaiva kutrāpyabhāvādeva na vyabhicāraprasaktiriti jñeyam |
yadvā bhedasyā[sya ]vyāsajyavṛttitvapakṣe'pi pūrvaṃ brahmapakṣakānumānajīvapakṣakānumāne tayo parasparaṃ pāramārthikabhedasiddhyarthaṃ pṛthaguktvedānīmekenaiva tatsādhanārthamāha -- jīveśāditi || atra dvitvāvacchedena sādhyasādhanaṃ nāma tadvyāpakatvena sādhanam |
svarūpasambandharūpāvacchedakatvasyaiva tatpakṣe bādhitatvāt |
vyāpakatā ca pratyekavṛttyorapi parasparabhedayoḥ parasparapratiyogikaparasparānuyogikabheda[va]ttvarūpasādhyatāvacchedakarūpeṇaivānumānāntareṣviveti bodhyam |
saṃyogatadabhāvādhikaraṇatvayoraṃśabhedenaivāṅgīkṛtatvādvṛkṣarūpadharmiṇi saṃyogasattvameva na saṃyogābhāvasyāpi tatra sattvamiti dvaitadyumaṇau vyavasthāpitatvānna tadubhayamupādāyoktavyāptibhaṅgaḥ || na ceti || upādhimātreṇeti || bhedasampādakadvāretyādiḥ || tatpratiyoginīti || upādhipratiyogini tadabhāva iti yāvat |
tadadarśanāt upādhisāmānādhikaraṇyādarśanādityarthaḥ |
bhavedupādhibhedasampādanadvārā'nyayorvirodhamekādhikaraṇavṛttitve'pyupapādanakṣamaḥ |
yadi svābhāvavirodhaṃ svasminsvābhāvaṃ svaṃ caikasminghaṭamānaḥ syāt |
na caitadasti |
ghaṭāvacchinnākāśo ghaṭāghaṭabhāvasyāṃśabhedamanupādāyākāśamātre vopādhyabhāvāvyavahārāt |
ākāśe ghaṭasaṃyogo nāsti |
ghaṭāvacchinnākāśe ghaṭasaṃyogo nāstītyapratīteḥ |
ākāśāntare tu svābhāvikatvānnopādhikṛtatvamato'svasvābhāvayorevaikavṛttitvaṃ viruddhatvaṃ copapādayitumakṣamaḥ |
svayamanavatīrṇa iti nyāyena kathamanyatra vyavasthāṃ kuryādityāśayaḥ || upādhikṛtabhedamātreṇeti pāṭhe tatpratiyoginyaupādhikaparasparasvapratiyogyanuyogikabhedapratiyogitvena tvadabhyupagateṣu sarvajñatvālpajñatvayoradarśanādaupādhikabhedaprayojaka ityarthaḥ |
tadvati yoginīti pāṭhe aupādhikabhedavatsaubharyādiyogini sarvajñatvālpajñatvayoradarśanādityarthaḥ |
|| evaṃ ceti || uktarītyā prathamānumāne vyavasthāpite cetyarthaḥ |
apāramārthikabhedena siddhasādhanavāraṇāya dharmisattāsamānasattāketi |
ityādītyādinā sukhaṃ nānāvyaktiniṣṭhaṃ guṇatvādityādīnāmupasaṅgrahaḥ |
ādipadenātmatvaṃ nānāvyaktiniṣṭhaṃ jātitvādityasya grahaṇamiti tu mandam |
etāvatā prabandhena tasyaiva vyavasthāpitatvāt |
prāthamikatvena tasyaiva kaṇṭhata upādātumucitatvācca || susthānīti || uktavidhayā dravyatvasyātmani siddhatvenoktāsiddhyanavakāśāt |
bhede dharmisattāsamānasattākatvaniveśena siddhasādhanasya cānavakāśāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvetāvatā sukhādipakṣakānumāne kathaṃ pūrvoktadoṣaparihāra iti cedittham |
sukhādīnāmapyayāvaddravyabhāvitvenātmanyaṅgīkāre bādhakābhāvāt |
teṣāṃ pratyakṣatvena tatprayojakamahattvaśūnyamanoguṇatvāsambhavācca |
sukhaṃ manobhinnanānāvyaktiniṣṭhaṃ guṇatvādrūpavadityanumīyate |
tathāca na siddhasādhanam |
na caivamapasiddhāntaḥ |
tārkikarītyaitatprayogāt |
āśrayatvānusandhātṛtvānyatarasambandhasya sādhyaghaṭakatvādvā doṣābhāvāt |
manovṛttermanobhinnāśritatvānupapattyā manobhinnānusandhātṛkenaiva sādhyaparyavasānāduddeśyasiddhariti |
asyārthasya dvitvādīnāmiti granthenaiva lābhānna pṛthaguktiḥ |
nanu caitraśarīramityādyanumāne prāguktadoṣāparihārāt kathamasya susthātvamata āha -- caitraśarīramiti || uktarītyā siddhasādhanavāraṇāya vastuta iti bhedaviśeṣaṇam |
dharmisattāsamānasattāketyarthaḥ |
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ |
tadviruddhaceṣṭākatvaṃ tatkriyājanakaprayatnānusandhātrananusaṃhitaprayatnajanyakriyāvattvam |
ato noktavyabhicāraḥ |
ghaṭādau prayatnajanyakriyāvirahāt |
etadarthameva ceṣṭāpadam |
na caivaṃ ghaṭāderdṛṣṭāntatvāsambhava iti vācyam |
vyatirekeṇa maitraśarīrasyaiva dṛṣṭāntatvasambhavāditi |
yattu tadviruddhaceṣṭāvattvaṃ tatkriyākāryapratibandhakakriyāvattvam |
śarīratvena hetuviśeṣaṇānna ghaṭādau vyabhicāra iti |
tanna |
indrārjunayoḥ khāṇḍavadāhādau viruddhakāryakriyāvatorvyamicārāt |
na cobhayasampratipannaikasvāmikatvābhāve satīti viśeṣaṇānna doṣa iti vācyam |
viśeṣaṇavaiyarthyādaprayojakatvācca |
vimatau bhinnau viruddhadharmādhikaraṇatvāddahanatuhinavadityasyoktadoṣavāraṇāya vivakṣitārthamāha -- jīveśāviti || nanu viruddhadharmādhikaraṇatvasya parasparadharmavadavṛttidharmavatvarūpasya hetorasiddhiḥ |
māyimate jīveśvaradharmayorekādhikaraṇakatvādityāśaṅkāvāraṇāya virodhaṃ vivaṇoti -- virodhaśceti || sahānavasthānaniyamaḥ parasparādhikaraṇāvṛttitvam |
vadhyaghātukabhāvaśca na nāśyanāśakabhāvarūpaḥ |
sarvajñatvāsarvajñatvādīnāṃ tadabhāvāt |
kintu bhāvābhāvatvarūpa iti jñāpanāya nityatvānityatvādāvivetyuktam |
atha siddhānte atyantābhāvasyāprāmāṇikapratiyogikatvaniyamāt kathamīśavṛttisarvajñatvātyantābhāvarūpatvamasarvajñatvasya , kathaṃ vā jīvagatāsarvajñatvātyantābhāvarūpatvaṃ sarvajñatvasyeti cet ittham |
jīve āropisarvajñatvapratiyogikātyantābhāva evāsarvajñatvam |
sa ca matāntare pratiyogineva siddhānte pradhānena viruddhyate |
sarvajñatvavatīśvare sarvajñatvaṃ nāstītyapratīteḥ |
īśagatasarvajñatvasyāsarvajñatvaṃ prati pradhānatvādeva pratiyogitvavyavahāraḥ |
evamasarvajñatvaṃ sarvajñatādātmyaniṣedhaḥ |
sarvajñatādātmyaṃ ca sarvajñatvameveti tatpratiyogikatvamasarvajñatvasya |
anyonyābhāvasya prāmāṇikapratiyogikatvopapatteḥ |
tadabhāvaśca sarvajñatvameva |
bhāvabhinnātyantābhāvasyaiva prāmāṇikapratiyogikatvaniṣedhāt |
`niṣedhasya niṣedhaśca bhāva eva balādbhavedi'ti vacanāt |
adhikamanyatrānusandheyam |
|| sarvajñatveti || nanu kimidaṃ sarvajñatvam |
na tāvat sarvaviṣayakajñānavattvam |
meyatvādinā sāmānyataḥ sarvaviṣayakajñānasya jīvasādhāraṇyāt |
nāpi yāvantaḥ padārthāḥ tattadvyaktitvena tāvadviṣayakajñānavattvam |
asarvajñādīnāṃ viśiṣya tāvadviṣayakajñānābhāvena durjñeyatvāpatteḥ |
ucyate |
viṣayitāsambandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvānadhikaraṇajñānavattvaṃ sarvajñatvam |
meyatvena sarvaviṣayake'pi jñāne ghaṭatvena ghaṭābhānādviṣayitāsambandhāvacchinnaghaṭatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvasyaiva sattvānnātivyāptiḥ |
asarvajñasyāpyetādṛśasarvajñatvajñānasambhavānna durjñeyatvam |
ādipadena svātantryāsvātantryādiparigrahaḥ |
nanvaprayojako hetuḥ |
dharmisattāsamānasattākabhedābhāve'pi tannyūnasattākabhedenaiva viruddhadharmavattvopapatterityāśaṅkāṃ nirācaṣṭe || na ceti || jīveśayoravidyādyupādhikṛtabhedenaivoktaviruddhadharmavattvopapattirityarthaḥ |
yogini yogopāttanānādehavati |
tadadarśanāt uktavidhaviruddhadharmavattvādarśanāt |
tathācaupādhikabhedasya viruddhadharmavattvanirvāhakatve yogino'pi tatprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ |
pūrvamaupādhikabhedasya viśiṣya duḥkhānusandhātṛtvatadabhāvobhayanirvāhakatvaṃ nirākṛtam |
idānīṃ tu sāmānyato viruddhadharmadvayanirvāhakatvamiti na punaruktiḥ |
idānīṃ pūrvapakṣadūṣitānumānānyuddharati || evaṃ ceti || ātmano dravyatve ātmatvasya jātitve ca siddhe satītyarthaḥ |
asiddhyabhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
ādipadenā'tmatvaṃ nānāvyaktiniṣṭhaṃ jātitvādityādyanumānagrahaṇam |
caitraśarīrapakṣakānumānoktadoṣaṃ pariharannāha -- caitraśarīramiti || vyākhyātametadadhastāt |
siddhasādhanavāraṇāya vastuta iti |
atra ekasvāmikayorghaṭapaṭayorvyabhicāraparihārāya hetau śarīrapadapraveśe'pi na dṛṣṭāntāsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
vimatapakṣakānumānoktadoṣaṃ pariharannāha -- jīveśāviti || dharmisattāsamānasattākabhedasādhanāt pāramārthikabhedasādhane'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvam |
vyāvahārikabhedasādhane siddhasādhanamiti codyānavakāśa iti bhāvaḥ |
sammatavat dahanatuhinavat |
anabhimatapakṣaṃ dūṣayannabhimatapakṣamāha -- virodha iti || sahānavasthānaniyameti || niyamenaikatrānavasthitatvarūpetyarthaḥ |
kuta ityata āha -- aikyavādina iti || tanmate ātmanāmekatvena viruddhadharmatve vakṣyamāṇayorekatraivāvasthānenaikatrānavasthānāsambhavena svarūpāsiddherityarthaḥ || vadhyaghātuketi || nanu nājāta eko'nyaṃ haṃti nāpyanādhāra iti nyāyena vṛścikādīnāṃ svajanmanā mātrādihantṛtvaṃ bhaṭādīnāṃ samarabhūmaughaṭitānāṃ hantṛtvamiti loke dṛṣṭam |
tatra vakṣyamāṇasarvajñatvādervadhyaghātukabhāve ekasmādanyajanmābhāvena ghaṭādīnāmivaikasthānasambandho vācyaḥ |
sa ca tadādhārajīveśayoraikyamevāpekṣata iti kathaṃ vadhyaghātukabhāvavirodhaviśiṣṭadharmādhikaraṇatvena bhedasādhanamiti cenna atra jahallakṣaṇayā vadhyaghātukabhāvaśabdena parasparaviraharūpatvasya vivakṣitatvāt |
vakṣyamāṇasarvajñatvāderapi tathātvāt |
saṃyogatadatyantābhāvāvayoścasāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ netyāvayormatamiti na tadāśraye vyabhicāraḥ |
ata evaitādṛśavirodhaviśiṣṭaṃ dharmaṃ dṛṣṭāntatayopādatte || nityatveti || sukhaduḥkhādāvuktavirodhaviśiṣṭatvābhāvāttādṛśaviruddhadharmaḥ ka ityapekṣāyāmāha -- tādṛśa iti || tadvyavasthā viruddhadharmavyavasthā |
tathāca dharmisattāsamānasattākabhedāsiddhyā'rthāntaramiti bhāvaḥ || yoginīti || yogiṣu dehāntaḥkaraṇādyupādhikṛtabhedasadbhāve'pi sukhaduḥkhādiviruddhadharmavyavasthāyā adarśanānnopādhibhedamātreṇa tadvyavasthopapattirityarthaḥ |
evaṃ devadattāntaḥkaraṇaṃ yajñadattāntaḥkaraṇāvacchinacaitanyādvastuto bhinnacaitanyāvacchedakaṃ tadvṛttyanivartyanivartakatvāt |
vyatirekeṇa yajñadattāntaḥkaraṇavat |
evaṃ devadattasya smaraṇaṃ vastuto na yajñadattīyaṃ yajñadattānubhavājanyatvāt |
vyatirekeṇa yajñadattasmaraṇavadityādīni bhedasādhakānyūhanīyāni |
na cāsiddhiḥ |
devadattānubhūte yajñadattasya smaraṇānudayāt |
ātmanāṃ ca svābhāvikābhede sukhaduḥkhādivyavasthā'nupapattirbādhikā
mandāramañjaryāmasmābhirupapāditā draṣṭavyā |
evaṃ jīveśvarabhede'numānamabhidhāyānyonyajīvabhede'pyanumānānyāha -- evaṃ devadattāntaḥkaraṇamiti || caitanyādvastuto yadbhinnaṃ caitanyaṃ tadavacchedakamityarthaḥ |
nityasāpekṣatvādbhinnapadasya samāsaḥ |
tadvṛttyanivartyanivartakatvāditi || yajñadattāntaḥkaraṇavṛttijñānenānivartyaṃ yadajñānaṃ tannivartakavṛttimattvādityarthaḥ || evaṃ devadattasya smaraṇamiti || devadattayajñadattayorabhedasya pāramārthikatve devadattasmaraṇasya vyavahārato'yajñadattīyasyāpi vastuto yajñadattīyatvaṃ sambhavatyeva |
atastannirāsāya vastuta iti || tena ca devadattayajñadattayorvastuto bhedaḥ sidhyatīti bhāvaḥ |
devadattayajñadattayorvastuta aikyāddevadattīyasmaraṇasya yajñadattānubhavajanyatvamevāstvityāśaṅkya nirākaroti || na cāsiddhiriti || nanu devadattasmaraṇasya yajñadattīyānubhavajanyatāyāstayorvyāvahārikabhedenāpyupapatternasvābhāvikaikyaṃ viruṇaddhīti tatrāha -- ātmanāṃ ceti || mandāramañjaryāmiti || `sukhaduḥkhādibhogaśca kharūpaikyena bhedataḥ |
dṛśyo hī'tyādimūlīyaṭīkāvyākhyāvasara ityarthaḥ |
|| tadvṛttyanivartyanivartakatvāditi || yajñadattamanovṛttyanivartyaprāgabhāvaviṣayāvaraṇājñānādinivartakatvādityarthaḥ |
na ca viśeṣaṇāsiddhiḥ |
yajñadattasya jñānotpattau devadattagatājñānānivṛtteḥ || vastuto na yajñadattīyamiti || yajñadattīyaṃ netyetadvastuta ityarthaḥ |
tena sarvamithyātvavādino yajñadattīyatvasyāpyavāstavavattvātsiddhasādhanateti nirastam |
vyāvahārikeṇa yajñadattīyatvābhāvena siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya vastuta iti || ityādīnīti |
devadattecchā na vastuto yajñadattīyā tajjñānājanyatvāt |
tadbhogānivṛttyatvācca |
devadattabrahmahatyā na vastuto yajñadattīyā |
tatsetudarśanānivṛttyatvādityādyūhyam |
vipakṣe bādhakābhāvādaprayojakāhetava ityata āha -- ātmanāmiti || mandāramañjaryāmiti || tathā hi |
yadyātmāno na bhinnāḥ syustarhi parasparasukhādyanusandhānaṃ syāt |
na ca śarīrabhedenopapattiḥ |
tathā'pi yoginastaddarśanāt |
tadabhāve'pi mātṛgarbhasthitayo rāvaṇādiśarīrasthayośca tadadarśanāt |
na cāntaḥkaraṇabhedenopapattiḥ |
yoginyapi tasyopapāditatvāt |
ekasyāpyaharahaḥ suptavataḥ kāraṇavilayāṅgīkārāt |
uttarāhe pūrvāthīyānusandhānaṃ na syāt |
nāpyavidyābhedānupapattiḥ |
muktasya saṃsārāvidyābhinnāvidyābhāvena tadduḥkhādyanusandhānāpatteḥ |
na cāvidyaikyamanusandhāne prayojakam |
muktasya svānandānubhavasyāpyabhāvāpatteriti |
|| evaṃ devadattāntaḥkaraṇamiti || bhinnacaitanyāvacchedakamiti || samāsastu nityasāpekṣatvādbodhyaḥ || tadvṛttyanivartyanivartakatvāditi || tadvṛttyā'nivṛttyajñānasaṃśayaviparyayāditannivatakavṛttimattvādityarthaḥ |
|| evaṃ devadatteti || vastuto bhinneti || ekajīvavādinaṃ prati prayoge pūrvoktarītyā vastuta ityasyāprātibhāsika ityevārthaḥ |
tanmate'ntaḥkaraṇasya jīvaupādhitvābhāve'pyekamahāvidyopādhikatvena mahatastatpariṇāmabhūtanānāntaḥkaraṇāvacchinnatvasyaikasminnapi sambhavāt |
bahujīvavādinaṃ prati tu prāgeva vastuta ityasya paramārthata ityarthaḥ |
uddeśyasidhyarthamekajīvapakṣe'pi paramārthata ityevārtho vā |
aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatoddhāraprakārastu caitraśarīramityuktānumānavivaraṇoktakrameṇa bodhyaḥ (krameṇohyaḥ) || tadvṛttīti |
tadvṛttiryajñadattāntaḥkaraṇavṛttirūpaghaṭābhāvajñānādiḥ |
tadanivartyasya paṭasaṃśayaviparyādernivartakatvāt paṭjñānotpattidvārānivartakatvādityarthaḥ |
tadvṛttyanivṛttyanuvartakatvāditi pāṭhe tadvṛttibhiḥ srakcandanabhogarūpapratyakṣādibhiraniṣpādyasukhāderviṣayāntarabhogadvārā niṣpādakatvādityarthaḥ |
na vastuto yajñadattīyamiti yajñadattīyatvapratiyogikapāramārthikābhāvavadityarthaḥ |
na ca mithyātvavādinā mithyātvasiddhaye tadvattve'pi tadabhāvasya pāramārthikasyāṅgīkāratsiddhasādhanatā |
pāramārthikatvāprakṣepe'pi tamevābhedamādāya siddhasādhanatā |
yajñadattasmaraṇe'pi pāramārthikasya yajñadattīyatvābhāvasya sattvādvyatirekāsiddhiśca |
pratiyogivaiyadhikaraṇyāvacchinnasya tasya sādhyatve'pi siddhasādhanataiva |
devadattasmaraṇe tathābhūtasyaiva yajñadattīyatvapratiyogikapāramārthikābhāvasyāṅgīkṛtatvācceti vācyam |
na vastuto yajñadattīyamityasya yajñadattapiṇḍāvacchinna[nne ]bhoktṛtvopalakṣitacitsvāmikatvābhāvavadityarthaḥ |
devadattasmaraṇe tādṛśopetacitsvāmikatvaṃ ca pareṇāpyaṅgīkṛtam |
anyathaikyamityasya pāribhāṣikatvāpatteḥ |
tathā ca tasya pratiyogivaiyadhikaraṇyāvacchinnasya sādhyatvānna siddhasādhanatvādikaṃ vyatirekāsiddhiśceti draṣṭavyam || ityādīnīti |
devadattasukhādipakṣakā(numānā)dīnītyarthaḥ || draṣṭavyeti || tathā cānukūlarkasanāthatvānnāprayojakateti bhāvaḥ ||
mitho jīvabhedakānumānāntaramāha -- evamiti || atra yajñadattāntaḥkaraṇāvacchinnacaitanyapratiyogikadharmisattāsamānasattākabhedaviśiṣṭasyaiva sādhyatvam |
caitanyāvacchedakapadaṃ tu caitanyanirūpitasya svabhogyavṛttikatvarūpāvacchedakatvasya sambandhatvalābhārtham |
nātaḥ sādhyāprasiddhiḥ |
tādṛśabhedaviśiṣṭasya ghaṭādeḥ prasiddhatvāt |
tasya coktasambandhena vṛttyasambhavāccaitanyāvacchedakatvamādāyaiva sādhyaparyavasānāduddheśyasiddhiḥ || tadvṛtyanivartyanivartakatvāditi || yajñadattāntaḥkaraṇavṛttirūpaṃ yajjñānaṃ tadanivartyaṃ yadajñānādi tannivartakavṛttyārambhakatvādityarthaḥ || devadattasmaraṇamiti || devadattakartṛkasmaraṇaṃ yajñadattapratiyogikavāstavatādātmyāpannakartṛkasmaraṇānyat yajñadattakartṛkānubhavājanyatvādityarthaḥ |
atra yadyapi ghaṭāderanvayadṛṣṭāntatvaṃ sambhavati |
tathāpi yajñadattīyasmṛtestadīyānubhavajanyatvena vyatirekavyāpteḥ sugrahatvāttadanusaraṇam |
atra ca phalato devadattayajñadattayorbhedasiddhiḥ |
ādipadena devadattānubhavo na vastuto yajñadattīyo yajñadattasmaraṇājanakatvād vyatirekeṇa yajñadattānubhavavadityādeḥ saṅgrahaḥ |
devadattasmaraṇasya yajñadattānubhavajanyatvādasiddhirityāśaṅkāṃ vārayati -- na ceti || na ca māyimate devadattānubhavasyaiva yajñadattīyatvādasiddhirdurvāraiveti vācyam |
caitanyamātrābhede'pi viśiṣṭabhedena tava tadīyatvāsambhavāt |
anyathā devadattānubhūte yajñadattasmaraṇaprasaṅgāt |
uktānumāneṣu vipakṣe bādhakamāha -- ātmanāmiti || sukhaduḥkhavyavasthānupapattiḥ || tatsukhaṃ tenaivānubhūyata ityādiniyamānupapattiḥ |
caitrādermaitrādisukhādyanubhavasyāpattiriti yāvat |
nanu sukhādivyavasthāyāḥ pāramārthikatve bhavetsvābhāvikabhedāpekṣatvam |
na caivam |
tasyāḥ kālpanikatvādityāśaṅkāpanodāya mandāramañjaryāmupapāditetyuktam |
tathāhi |
kālpanikī vyavasthā kiṃ vyavasthāpakameva nāpekṣate |
kiṃ vā pāramārthikam |
ādye kālpanikajīvatvādivyavasthānirvāhāyāvidyādikalpanānarthakyaprasaṅgaḥ |
dvitīye kālpanikasyāpi vyavasthāpakasya svābhāvikabhedaṃ vinā'nupapatterityādikaṃ tatra draṣṭavyam |
jīvanāmanyonyabhedasādhakānumānānyāha -- evamiti || atra yajñadattāntaḥkaraṇasya devadattāntaḥkaraṇāvacchinnacaitanyabhinnacaitanyāvacchedakatve yajñadattadevadattayorbhedasiddhiḥ || devadatteti || yajñadattāntaḥkaraṇāvacchinnaṃ paricchinnaṃ yaccaitanyaṃ yajñadattacaitanyaṃ tasmādvastutobhinnaṃ yaccaitanyaṃ devadattacaitanyaṃ tasyāvacchedakaṃ paricchedakamityarthaḥ || tadvṛttīti || tadvṛttyā yajñadattāntaḥkaraṇavṛttirūpajñānenānivartyaṃ devadattājñānaṃ tannivartakatvādityarthaḥ || yajñadattāntaḥkaraṇavaditi || tatra sādhyābhāvarūpayajñadattāntaḥkaraṇāvacchinnacaitanyāvacchekatvaṃ vartate |
tādṛśacaitanyaṃ devadattacaitanyameva |
tadanavacchedakatvāt |
tathā tadvṛtyanivartyanivartakatvābhāvastadvṛttinivartyanivartakatvaṃ cāsti |
tadvṛttyā yajñadattāntaḥkaraṇavṛttijñānena |
nivartyaṃ yadyajñadattājñānaṃ tannivartakatvāditi dhyeyam |
atraivānumānāntaramāha -- evamiti || atra devadattayajñadattayorabhedena devadattasmaraṇasyāpi yajñadattānubhavajanyatvena dvitīyahetoḥ svarūpāsiddhirityāśaṅkya nirākaroti || naceti || nanu jīvānāmanyonyabhedasādhakānumāne vipakṣe bādhakābhāvādaprayojakatvam |
naca tathātve sukhaduḥkhavyavasthānupapattirbādhiketyuktamiti vācyam |
pāramārthikabhedābhāvena pāramārthikavyavasthānupapattāvapi tadbhinnavyasthābhyupagame bādhakābhāvādityata āha -- ātmanāmiti || pāramārthikavyavasthāsambhave'pi kālpanikīvyavasthā sambhavatīti cet sā kiṃ vyavasthāpakameva nāpekṣate uta pāramārthikam |
nādyaḥ |
yakṣānurūpabalivadanuguṇavyavasthāpakasyāvaśyakatvāt |
anyathā'pāramārthikajīvādivyavasthāsiddhaye'vidyānaṅgīkāraprasaṅgāt |
na dvitīyaḥ |
apāramārthikasyāpi vyavasthāpakasya svābhāvikabhedātirekeṇa nirākariṣyamāṇatvādityādimandāramañjaryāmupapāditā kālpanikavyavasthāyā anupapattirdraṣṭavyetyarthaḥ |
śrutayaśca dvā suparṇetyādikāḥ |
vyāvahārikabhedaparāstā iti cet (na)|
sa kiṃ bādhyaḥ ? bāḍhamiti cet |
tarhi śruteḥ prāmāṇyaṃ na syāt |
bādhyārthagrāhitvāt śuktirūpyādhyakṣavat |
prāmāṇyaṃ (ca) vyāvahārikamiti cet |
kintarhyaprāmāṇyaṃ pāramārthikam? kaḥ (kasya) sandehaḥ? asti mahānsandehastathāgatacaraṇaśaraṇatvamajānataḥ (tathāgatacaraṇaśaraṇaṃ prāptavānyataḥ) |
nanu śrutyupa(sthita)sthāpitārthānaṅgīkāre hi śrutyaprāmāṇyaṃ syāt |
na cāsmābhiḥ śrutipratipādito bhedo nāṅgīkriyate |
nāpyasmābhiranaṅgīkṛtaṃ tatsatyatvaṃ śrutau pratipādyam |
tatra satyapadāśravaṇāditi cet |
naiṣa doṣaḥ |
svagocarārthābādho hi prāmāṇyam |
tadbādhaścāprāmāṇyam |
tatkathaṃ śrutivedyasya bhedasya bādhe śruteḥ prāmāṇyam (prāmāṇyaprasaṅgaḥ) |
satyapadāśravaṇamātreṇa tadanaṅgīkāre ekamevādvitīyaṃ brahmetyādāvapi satyapadāśravaṇenādvaitamapi pāramārthikaṃ na syāt |
advaitaśruteḥ ṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgayuktatvāttadarthaḥ pāramārthika iti cenna |
upakramādīni hi svārthe tātparyaṃ jñāpayanti |
evañcādvaitamātraparamastu(mātraṃ sidhyati) tātparyaliṅgavaśān |
na tatsatyatvaparaṃ (tatsatyatvaṃ) tasyātadarthatvāt |
bheda eva ṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgayuktatvamasmābhiranyatropapāditam |
evaṃ jīveśabhede pratyakṣānumāne abhidhāyāgamaṃ cāha -- śrutayaśceti || jīveśvarabhede pramāṇamiti śeṣaḥ |
vyāvahārikabhedaḥ kiṃ bādhya utābādhya iti vikalpaṃ hṛdi nidhāyādyaṃ dūṣayituṃ pṛcchati -- sa kiṃ bādhya iti || taṃ pakṣaṃ pūrvapakṣī kakṣīkaroti || bāḍhamitīti |
bādhya ityetadeva dṛḍhamityarthaḥ |
dūṣayati -- tarhīti |
śuktirūpyādhyakṣavadbādhyārthagrāhitve'pi na śruteḥ sarvathā prāmāṇyābhāvaḥ |
kintu pratipādyabhedavatprāmāṇyamapi vyāvahārikameva |
naivaṃśuktirūpyādhyakṣasya |
kintvaprāmāṇyamevetyāśayena śaṅkate -- prāmāṇyamiti || śrutiprāmāṇyasya vyāvahārikatvāṅgīkāre'prāmāṇyasya vyāvahārikatve tadabhedasya pāramārthikatvamiti bhāvena solluṇṭhaṃ pṛcchati -- kintarhīti |
apahāsamajānānastameva pakṣamaṅgīkṛtya śaṅkate -- kaḥ sandeha iti || aprāmāṇyasya pāramārthikatvaviṣaye na ko'pi sandeha ityarthaḥ |
uttaramāha -- astīti |
kuta ityata āha -- tathāgateti || tathāgato bauddhaḥ |
ajānato'manyamānasya |
ātmanaḥ |
taveti śeṣaḥ |
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ |
na hi bhavān bauddha iva sarvathā śrutyaprāmāṇyavādī |
kintvadvaitabhāgasya tattvāvedakatvalakṣaṇaṃ bhedabhāgasyātattvāvedakatvalakṣaṇaṃ prāmāṇyamaṅgīkurute |
yadīdānīmaprāmāṇyameva pāramārthikamityucyate tarhi bauddhamatapraveśaḥ sphuṭameva tavāpadyata iti || śaṅkate -- śrutyupasthāpiteti || śrutipratipāditetyarthaḥ |
nāṅgīkriyate kintu bhedamaṅgīkṛtya tatsatyatvaṃ nāṅgīkriyata ityarthaḥ |
bhedasatyatvaṃ śrutipratipādyaṃ neti kathaṃ jñāyata ityata āha -- tatreti || śrutāvityarthaḥ |
dvetyādipadaṃ tu bhedavyāpyadvitvamātrābhidhāyīti bhāvaḥ |
uttaramāha -- naiṣa iti || śrutau satyapadāśravaṇe'pi tatsatyatvasya durvāratvātsatyatvānaṅgīkāre śruteraprāmāṇyāpattirdurvāretyarthaḥ |
etadevopapādayati -- svagocarārtheti || svapratipādyetyarthaḥ |
tathā cābādhitārthapratipādakatvaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ bādhitārthapratipādakatvamaprāmāṇyam |
tathā ca śrutipratipāditabhedasya tvayā bādhyatvāṅgīkāre kathamaprāmāṇyaṃ na syāditi bhāvaḥ |
satyapadāśravaṇādityuktadoṣaṃ pratibandyā pariharati -- satyapadeti || tadanaṅgīkāre satyatvānaṅgīkāre |
prameyasāmye hi pratibandīgrahaṇam |
nātra prameyasāmyamastīti bhāvena śaṅkate -- atheti || ṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgayuktatvāditi || ekamevādvitīyamityupakramaḥ |
tattvamasītyupasaṃhāraḥ |
tattvamasi tattvamasīti navakṛtvo'bhyāsaḥ |
advaitasya pramāṇāntarāprāptatvenāpūrvatvam |
tasya tāvadevaciramiti phalam |
yenāśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavatītyarthavādaścetyevaṃṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgayuktatvādityarthaḥ |
upalakṣaṇametat |
mṛtpiṇḍādidṛṣṭāntairaikyasyopapāditatvādupapattirapi grāhyā |
upakramādīnāmapyupapattiviśeṣatvādupapatteḥ pṛthagavivakṣayā ṣaḍvidhetyuktiḥ |
tathā cādvaitaśrutau satyapadābhāve'pi tātparyaliṅgabalenādvaitasya satyatā sidhyati |
bhedaśrutestu tātparyaliṅgābhāvena na tadarthasya bhedasya satyatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
etacca vaiṣamyamarthasatyatve'prayojakamiti bhāvenāha -- upakramādīnīti || svārthe śrutyarthe |
|
atadarthatvāt śrutyarthatvābhāvāt |
abhyupagamyoktiriyam |
vastutastu vaiṣamyamasiddhamevetyāha -- bhede'pīti || anyatreti || nyāyāmṛte dvitīyaparicchede |
santi ca bhedaśruterapi ṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgāni |
tathā hi |
ātharvaṇe dvā suparṇetyādyupakramaḥ |
paramaṃ sāmyamupaitītyupasaṃhāraḥ |
tayoranyo'naśnannanyo'nyamīśamityabhyāsaḥ |
śāstraikagamyeśvarapratiyogikasya taddharmikasya vā kālatrayābādhyabhedasya śāstraṃ vinā'prāpterapūrvatā |
puṇyapāpe vidhūyeti phalam |
asya mahimānamiti stutirūpārthavādaḥ |
attyanaśnannityupapattirityādinopapāditamityarthaḥ |
atrāpi saptavidhatātparyaliṅgayuktatvamiti vaktavye ṣaḍvidhetyukterukta evābhiprāyaḥ |
|| śrutayaśceti || śrutayaḥ sūtrāṇi ceti grāhyam |
|| vyāvahāriketi || yadyapyanuvāditvameva pūrvapakṣoktaṃ tadevādau parihartavyam |
tathā'pi prāsaṅgikatayā tasyāpi parihāralāghavādevamuktiḥ |
yatnaśatenāpi śruteḥ pāramārthikatvānaṅgīkāre bauddhavailakṣaṇyaṃ bādhyārthatvasyaivāprāmāṇyapadārthatvāditi bhāvenāha -- kimityādinā || bādhyārthagrāhitvāditi || nanvidamevāprāmāṇyamiti cenna |
pramājanakatvābhāvasyehāprāmāṇyatvāt |
aṅgīkaroti kaḥ sandeha ityuktavatastvāsmābhistadudbhāvane kṛte mahānsandehaḥ kartavyo'stītyarthaḥ |
nanu śrutestadarthasya tatprāmāṇyasya caikasattākatvena dharmisattākaṃ prāmāṇyamaṅgīkriyate |
tathā ca śruterjagataśca vyāvahārikatvāṅgīkāre'pi jagataḥ svāgamoktaprakāreṇaiva vyavasthāmaṅgīkṛtya śruteścātyantāprāmāṇyamaṅgīkṛtamiti cetkimanenākāṇḍatāṇḍavena |
śuktirajatavadbādhyārthatvāparihārāt |
brahmajñānamātrabādhyārthamapi pramāṇamiti cenna |
brahmaṇi kṣaṇikatvādyāropasyāpi prāmāṇyāpatteḥ |
kiñca prāmāṇyadvaividhye gauravamanugataniṣprakārakajñānetarajñānābādhyārthatvaṃ prāmāṇyāṅgīkaraṇaṃ sambhavatīti cenna |
tarhi dvaitādvaitayoruktarūpaikaprāmāṇyakatvena tadarthayorvyāvahārikatvapāramārthikatvabhedānupapatteḥ |
sarvathā'bādhyārthatvaṃ mukhyaprāmāṇyam |
brahmajñānabādhyārthatve'pi taditarābādhyārthatvamamukhyaprāmāṇyamityastyeva prāmāṇye'vāntarabheda iti cet tarhi vivakṣāmātrasya niraṅkuśatvena bauddho'pi vedārthasya jñānaviśeṣabādhyatvaṃ vadanvedavādī syāt |
svabādhātprāgevābādhyārthatvaṃ prāmāṇyam |
rajatasthale tu rajatapratyakṣasya prātibhāsikatayā pratyakṣabādhātprāgeva rajataṃ bādhyata iti cenna |
niṣprakārakabrahmāparokṣāvyavahitapūrvotpannarajatādibhrame'tivyāptiḥ |
kiñcārthabādhonāmārthanāśo bhāvajñānaṃ vā tatpratyakṣaṃ vā |
nādyaḥ |
śrutyarthabhūtasya ghaṭādeḥ prapañcasya śrutibādhātprāgeva nāśāṅgīkārāt |
na dvitīyaḥ |
viditavedāntasya nehanānā'stītyādinā prāgevāparokṣato'rthāsattvajñānāt |
ata eva na tṛtīyaḥ |
candraprādeśapratyakṣasya tādṛśabādhābhāvena prāmāṇyāpatterityalam |
nanu yāvacchrutyuktaṃ tanmātrānaṅgīkāre hi doṣaḥ |
iha tu śrutyuktaṃ sarvamaṅgīkriyate |
tatpāramārthyaṃ na tacchrutyartha ityāśaṅkate -- śrutīti || svagocareti || evañca śrutyuktaṃ sarvamaṅgīkriyate tatpāramārthyaṃ neti riktaṃ vacaḥ |
abādhyapratipatterevāṅgīkārapadārthatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
kiñca sattvāsattvaudāsīnyena pratipādanāyogātsarvavākyānāṃ satyapadābhāve svārthasattāparyantatvaṃ vyutpattisiddham |
anyathā satyaṃ jñānamityādāvapi satyapadasattve'pi satyataiva syānna tu satyatāyāḥ satyatvamiti śaṅkāpiśācī durūccāṭā syāt |
dṛśyate ca satyaṃ bhidetyādau satyapadamapīti |
nanu satyaṃ padaṃ vinā'pyasatye tātparyāyogādaikyasya satyatāsiddhiḥ |
tātparyaṃ ca talliṅgādgamyata iti śaṅkate -- atheti || chāndogye ekamevetyupakramaḥ |
tattvamasītyupasaṃhāraḥ |
tattvamasi tattvamasīti navakṛtvo'bhyāsaḥ |
aikyasya pramāṇāntarāprāptatvādapūrvatā |
atha sampatsyata ityaikyajñānasya phalam |
yenāśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavatītyaikyārthavādaśceti jñeyam |
svārtheti svaprakaraṇe yāvaduktaṃ tāvati tātparyamasti |
na tūktimātramityetāvanmātramupakramādīni jñāpayanti |
evañca tātparyameva syānna tu satyatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanvityetattātparyayogātsattvamapi labhyata iti cet tarhi bhrāntādivākyasyopakramādiliṅgāvagatatātparyavadupapatteḥ |
vastutastvekamevādvitīyamityisya samābhyadhikarāhityasyānyatroktatvādviśeṣaniṣedhaḥ śeṣavidhiṃ gamayatīti bhedopakramatvādupasaṃhārasyāpi dṛṣṭāntānuguṇyāyātattvamasīti vicchedena vaikapadatvamabhyupetya tasya bhāvastattvamiti vigrahāśrayaṇena vā napuṃ kasakopapattyarthaṃ tatvampadasyedaṃ sarvamiti jagatparāmarśakatvena vā sādṛśya ityādyarthakatvena vā bhedaparatvāt |
ata evābhyāsasya bhedaparatvādvedaikavedyeśvarabhedasyāpyapūrvakatvātprakaraṇasya bhedaparatve phalasyāpi tadanvayāt |
bhedajñānasya heti tatpadādikādiśravaṇācca bheda eva tātparyamiti ca jñeyam || bheda eveti || evañca tātparyaliṅgātsatyatāsiddhiścetsamaṃ mamāpīti bhāvaḥ || anyatra nyāyāmṛte |
tathā hi |
ātharvaṇe dvāsuparṇetyupakramaḥ |
sāmyamupaitītyupasaṃhārastayoranyaḥ anaśnannanyaḥ, anyamīśamityabhyāsaḥ |
uktarītyā''pūrvatā |
puṇyapāpe vidhūyeti phalam |
asya mahimānamiti vītaśoka ityarthavāda iti |
vyāvahārikabhedo bādhyo'bādhyo veti vikalpaṃ manasi kṛtvā''dhyamāśaṅkate -- sa kimiti || bhrāntyoddeśayati -- nanviti || bhrāntimāviṣkaroti || svagocarārthābodho hīti |
śrutiprāmāṇyāṅgīkāre tadarthaṃ sattvāṅgīkāra āvaśyaka ityarthaḥ |
vaiṣamyamāśaṅkate -- athaitacchruteriti || atha prayojakaṃ vaiṣamyamityāha -- upakramādīnīti |
evañcopakramādibalādadvaitaśrutestātparyamātramāyāsyati |
na tu tadarthaṃ satyatāpratārakavākye satyapi tātparyārthasatyatvābhāvāt |
advaitamātramastu na tu satyatvamiti parabhāṣyoktiḥ |
asiddhaṃ ca vaiṣamyamityāha -- bhede'pīti |
anyatra nyāyāmṛte |
|| dvā suparṇetyādikā iti || āsāmāgamatayā bhedāvagamakatvamupapāditaṃ pūrvapakṣavivaraṇāvasare || kintarhīti |
prāmāṇyaṃ vyāvahārikamityukte vyavahāramātraṃ tatra na vastutastadastītyarthaḥ || tathāgateti || bauddha ityarthaḥ || pratipādita iti || yasmindharmiṇi yo dharmaḥ śrutyā bodhyate tadviśiṣṭadharmiṇo'tyantābhāva ityarthaḥ |
anyathā śrutighaṭakavākyabodhyasya kasyacidanaṅgīkārasyāprāmāṇyahetutve vedaghaṭakasatvādipadabodhyasyābhāvāttavāpyaprāmāṇyaṃ syāditi bhāvaḥ || nāṅgīkriyata iti || kintu mithyātvenābhyupagamyata iti śeṣaḥ |
jīveśau dharmīkṛtya dvā suparṇetyādinā tatra bhedokte tatra tadanaṅgīkāre'prāmāṇyaṃ durvāramityāśayenāha -- naiṣa doṣa iti || sattvena prakāravidhayā pratipādyasyāsattva eva doṣo na prakṛte tathetyāśaṅkyāha -- satyapadeti |
ṣaḍvidhetyupapatterbahiraṅgatvādantaraṅgavivakṣayā vā'rthavādaphalayoḥ sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvenaikyamāśritya vā ṣaḍvidhatvoktiḥ |
upakramādayastu nyāyāmṛte draṣṭavyāḥ || na tatsatyatvamiti || anyathā śūnyavādigranthasthopakramādibhistattātparyaviṣayasya śūnyasya satyatvaṃ syādityarthaḥ || bheda eveti || tathā ca tvadrītyaiva satyatvaṃ śrutipratipādyaṃ jātamityarthaḥ |
evaṃ jīveśabhede pratyakṣānumāne abhidhāyāgamamapyāha -- śrutayastviti || na śrutayaḥ pāramārthikabhede pramāṇamityāśaṅkate -- vyāvahāriketi || śrutyukto bhedo bādhyo'bādhyo veti vikalpena dūṣayituṃ pṛcchati -- sa kimiti || prāmāṇyam abādhyārthakatvam |
nanvāropitameva śruteḥ prāmāṇyam |
kiṃ tvabrahmajñānābādhyatvāt vyavahārārhamityaśaṅkate -- prāmāṇyamiti || aprāmāṇyaṃ prāmāṇyābhāvaḥ || pāramārthikamiti || bhāvābhāvayorekasya bādhe'nyasyābādhyatvaniyamāditi bhāvaḥ |
atreṣṭāpattimāśaṅkate -- kaḥ sandeha iti || tarhi bauddhamatapraveśaprasaṅga ityāha -- astīti || tathāgato bauddhaḥ svasya tadanuyāyitvamanabhyupagacchata ityarthaḥ |
śuktirūpyādhyakṣavadityuktadṛṣṭāntābhiprāyamavijñāya śaṅkate -- nanviti || satyapadāśravaṇāt || bhede satyatvasamarpakapadāśravaṇāt |
abodhakatvameva hyaprāmāṇyaprayojakam |
śrutīnāṃ ca bhedasvarūpabodhakatvānna tadaṃśe'prāmāṇyam |
tasya pāramārthikatvaṃ tu na śrutibodhitam |
atastadaṃśe tadaprāmāṇyamiṣyata eveti bhāvaḥ || svagocareti || abādhyārthabodhakatvamityarthaḥ |
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ |
abodhakatvavadvādhyārthabodhakatvamapyaprāmāṇyaprayojakam |
anyathā śuktirajatagrāhakaduṣṭendriyāderapi prāmāṇyaprasaṅgāt |
tacca śruterapyaviśiṣṭamiti tadaprāmāṇyaṃ durvārameva |
na ca brahmajñānetarābādhyabodhakatvameva prāmāṇyam |
tacca śrutisādhāraṇamiti vācyam |
śuktijñānetarābādhyabodhakatvameva prāmāṇyaṃ, tacca duṣṭendriyasādhāraṇamityasyāpi suvacatvāt |
kiñca yacchruteḥ prāmāṇyaṃ tadbādhyameveti tadabhāvalakṣaṇāprāmāṇyaprasaṅgastadavastha eveti |
bhedasatyatvaṃ na śrutyuktamityetaddūṣayati -- satyapadāśravaṇāditi || dvā suparṇetyādāvityādiḥ |
tadanaṅgīkāre bhedasatyatvānaṅgīkāre || na syāditi || ayamāśayaḥ |
sattvāsattvodāsīnapratītyabhāvāduktaśrutyā'pi sattvenaiva bhedapratīteḥ sambhavati śrutestatsatyatve prāmāṇyam |
anyathā śruteradvaitasvarūpamātragrāhakatvāpattyā satyatvāgrāhakatvādadvaitamapi tava bādhyaṃ syāditi |
nanu śruteradvaitasvarūpamātragrāhakatve'pi tatsatyatvamupakramāditātparyaliṅgairavasīyate |
na tu bhedasatyatvamityāśaṅkate -- atheti || `sadeva somyedamagra āsī'`dekamevādvitīya'mityupakramaḥ |
`aitadātmyamidaṃ sarva'mityupasaṃhāraḥ |
`tattvamasī'ti navakṛtvo'bhyāsaḥ |
aikyasya mānāntarāprāptatvādapūrvatā |
`atha sampatsyata' iti phalam |
`yenāśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavatī'tyarthavāda iti ṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgayuktatvādityarthaḥ |
yadyapyupapattyā saha tātparyaliṅgāni saptavidhāni |
asti cādvaite'pi śrautopapattiryathā somyaikena mṛtpiṇḍena sarvaṃ mṛṇmayaṃ vijñātaṃ syādityādiḥ |
tathāpyupakramādeḥ sarvasyāpi upapattirūpatvenārthasiddhatvāt sā na pṛthaguktā |
syādevam |
yadyupakramādipratipādyamaikyasatyatvaṃ syāt |
na caivam |
api tvaikyamātram |
ato na tatsatyatvasiddhirityāha -- upakramādīnīti ||
nanūpakramādīnāmaikyasatyatvāpratipādakatve'pi tadvyāpyatayā tatsādhakatvamavyāhatamityata āha -- bheda iti || `dvā suparṇe'tyupakramaḥ |
`paramaṃ sāmyamupaitī'tyupasaṃhāraḥ |
`tayoranyaḥ' `anaśnannanyaḥ' `anyamīśa'mityanyaśabdābhyāsaḥ |
śāstraikasamadhigamyeśvarabhedaparatvādapūrvatā |
`puṇyapāpe vidhūye'ti phalam |
`asya mahimāna'mityarthavādaḥ |
anaśnannityupapattiścetyupapattisahitaṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgayuktatvaṃ bhedaviṣayaśruterapi nyāyāmṛte upapāditam |
tataśca bhedasatyatvasiddhirityāśayaḥ |
yadvā bhedaviṣaya eva śruteḥ ṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgayuktatvamityarthaḥ |
tathāhi |
ekamevādvitīyamiti sajātīyadvitīyarāhityenaivopakramaḥ |
kālādeḥ sattvaśravaṇena dvitīyamātraniṣedhāyogāt |
aitadātmyamityetatsvāmikatvenopasaṃhāro na tvetatsvarūpatvena |
taddhitavaiyarthyāt |
tattvamasītyanenāpi tatsadṛśatvādikamevābhyasyate |
tattvaṃ padamukhyārthaviśiṣṭābhedabādhena padadvayasya cinmātralakṣaṇāpekṣayā sāmānādhikaraṇyamātrasya gauṇatvaucityāt |
atatvamasīti cchede tu sphuṭameva bhedaparatvam |
mānāntarāgamyeśvarabhedaparatvādapūrvatvam |
atha sampatsyata iti brahmaprāptiḥ phalamucyate |
yenāśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavatītyarthavādaśca sādṛśyādinopapadyate |
tathaiva mṛtpiṇḍādidṛṣṭāntairupapādanāt |
sarvamṛṇmayānāmekamṛtpiṇḍavikāratvābhāvena tadabhede tātparyāyogādityādi draṣṭavyam |
evaṃ jīveśabhede pratyakṣānumāne abhidhāyā'gamañcāha -- śrutaya iti || jīveśayoḥ pāramārthibhede mānamiti śeṣaḥ || vyāvahāriketi || tathāca pāramārthikabhede pramāṇatvena etacchrutyudāharaṇamayuktamiti bhāvaḥ |
vyāvahārikabhedaḥ kiṃ bādhya utābādhya iti vikalpaṃ hṛdinidhāyādyaṃ dūṣayituṃ pṛcchati -- sa kimiti || uttaraṃ śaṅkate -- bāḍhamiti || vyāvahārikabhedo bādhya ityetat dṛḍhamityarthaḥ |
dūṣayati -- tarhīti || kuta ityata āha -- śuktirūpyeti || śuktirajatapratyakṣasyāpi śuktirajatarūpabādhyārthagrāhitvādeva prāmāṇyābhāvo dṛṣṭaḥ |
tathaivāgamasyāpi vyāvahārikabhedarūpabādhyārthagrāhitve prāmāṇyaṃ durlabhamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanu bādhyārthagrāhakasya pāramarthikaprāmāṇyavirodhitvena tadasambhave'pi vyāvahārikaprāmāṇyāvirodhitvāttādṛśaprāmāṇyamāgamasya sambhavatīti śaṅkate -- prāmāṇyamiti || tathātve śuktirajatādhyāsasyāpi vyāvahārikaprāmāṇyaṃ syāditi doṣe satyeva doṣāntaraṃ vaktuṃ pṛcchati -- kintarhīti || parasparaviruddhayoranyataraniṣedhasyānyataravidhivyāptatvāditi bhāvaḥ || kasyeti || vedaprāmāṇyasya vyāvahārikatvaṃ vadato mama tadaprāmāṇye pāramārthikatvaniścaya evāsti na tu saṃśaya ityarthaḥ |
dūṣayati -- astīti || tathāgataḥ bauddhaḥ |
bauddhena vedāprāmāṇyaṃ pāramārthikamityaṅgīkriyate tvayā'pi tadabhyupagame tanmatābhyupagamaprasaṅgaḥ |
tathāca vedaprāmāṇyasya vyāvahārikatvena tadaprāmāṇyaṃ kiṃ pāramārthikaṃ uta tathāṅgīkāre bauddharāddhāntābhyupagamaprasaṅgena pāramārthikaṃ neti saṃśaya eva syānna pāramārthikamiti niścaya ityarthaḥ |
yadvā sandehaḥ śaṅkā aniṣṭamiti yāvat || śrutyupasthāpiteti || śrutipratipāditetyarthaḥ |
nanu śrutipratipāditabhedāṅgīkāre'pi tatpratipāditabhedasatyatvānabhyupagamādaprāmāṇyaṃ syādevetyata āha -- nāpīti || kuta ityata āha -- tatra satyapadeti || dvetyādipadaṃ tubhedāparaparyāyadvitvamātravācakamiti bhāvaḥ |
naca prāmāṇyāprāmāṇyayoḥ pratipādyārthāṅgīkārānaṅgīkārau prayojakau |
apitu pratipādyārthabādhābādhāveva |
tathāhi pratipādyārthābādhādeva vahniruṣṇa ityasya prāmāṇyaṃ pratipādyārthabādhādeva vahniranuṣṇa ityasyāprāmāṇyam |
prakṛte ca śrutipratipādyārthasyabhedasya bādhyatve'ṅgīkṛte satyaprāmāṇyaṃ durvāramiti pariharati -- naiṣadoṣa ityādinā || svagocareti || svaṃ prāmāṇatvenābhimatam |
tadbodhyetyarthaḥ |
śrutivedyasya śrutipratipādyasya |
tatra satyapadāśravaṇādityetatpratibandyā dūṣayati -- satyeti || tadanaṅgīkāre bhedasatyatvānaṅgīkāre |
nanvekamevādvitīyamiti śrutau satyapadāśravaṇe'pi tatpratipādyaikyasya pāramārthikatvameva |
tasyāḥ śruteraikye upakramādiṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgayuktatvāt |
tathāhi |
ekamevādvitīyamityupakramaḥ |
tattvamasītyupasaṃhāraḥ |
tattvamasi tattvamasīti navavāramabhyāsaḥ |
aikyasyānyapramāṇenāprāptatvādapūrvatā |
atha sampatsyata iti phalam |
yenāśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavatīti stutirūpo'rthavādaḥ |
yathā somyaikena mṛtpiṇḍeneti mṛtpiṇḍādidṛṣṭāntairupapāditatvādupapattiścādhiketi |
naca bhedaśruterbhede ṣaḍvidhatātparyamasti |
yena tadarthasya pāramārthikatvaṃ syāditi śaṅkate -- atheti || yadyapi tātparyaliṅgānāmupapattyā saha saptavidhatvam |
yathoktaṃ `upakramopasaṃhārāvabhyāso'pūrvatā phalam |
arthavādopapattī ca liṅgantātparyanirṇaya' ityādinā |
tathāpyupapattiviśeṣāṇāmupaka amādīnāṃ ṣaḍvidhatvādevamuktamityadoṣaḥ |
ekamevādvitīyamityupakramādīnyaikyaśruteraikye tātparyaṃ jñāpayanti |
na tvaikyasatyatve |
kuta iti cet aikyasya śrutyarthatvāt |
tatsatyatvasya ca śrutyarthatvābhāvāt |
upakramādīnāṃ śrutyartha eva tātparyajñāpakatvasvābhāvyāditi pariharati -- upakramādīnītyādinā tadarthatvādityantena || svārthe svaṃ śrutiḥ tadarthe |
tātparyaṃ śruteriti śeṣaḥ |
advaitamātraparam aikyavākyamiti śeṣaḥ || tātparyeti || aikyavākyasyaikye upakramāditātparyaliṅgayuktatvādityarthaḥ |
mātrapadavyāvartyamāha -- neti || atadarthatvāt śrutyarthatvābhāvāt |
idaṃ cābhyupetyoktam |
vastutastu śakunisūtrādidṛṣṭāntabalena sa ātmātattvamasīti vākyasyātattvamasīti padacchedena bhedapratipādakatvameva yuktam |
nahyabhedaṃ pratijñāya bhede dṛṣṭāntābhidhānaṃ saṃgacchate |
ata evaikamevādvitīyamityetsamādhikarāhityapratipādanena bhedaparam |
evamevaitadātmyamityapi |
yathā somyetyādiprādhānyajñāpanāya sṛṣṭipratipādakamityaṅgīkāryam |
evaṃ caikamevādvitīyamityasyopakramāt aitadātmyamidaṃ sarvamiti tasyaivopasaṃhārāt atattvamasīti navavāramabhyāsāt śāstraṃ vinā śāstraikagamyeśvarabhedasyāprasaktyā'pūrvatvāt atha sampatsyata iti phalaśravaṇāt yenāśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavatītyarthavādāt śakunisūtrādidṛṣṭāntairupapādanāt upakramāditātparyaliṅgāni bhedaparāṇyeveti draṣṭavyam |
yadi satyapadāśravaṇe'pyaikyaśruteḥ ṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgavattvāttadarthaḥ pāramārthika ityaṅgīkriyate tarhi satyapadāśravaṇe'pi bhedaśruteḥ ṣaḍvidhatātparyaliṅgopetatvāttadarthaḥ pāramārthika ityaṅgīkriyatāmityāha -- bhede ṣaḍvidheti || ātharvaṇe dvāsuparṇetyupakramaḥ, paramaṃ sāmyamupaitītyupasaṃhāraḥ, tayoranyaḥ anaśnannanyaḥ anyamīśamityabhyāsaḥ, śāstraikagamyeśvarapratiyogikasya taddharmikasya vā bhedasya śāstraṃ vinā'prāpterapūrvatā, puṇyapāpe vidhūyeti phalam, asya mahimānamiti stutirūpo'rthavādaḥ, atti anaśnanniti viruddhadharmoktyopapattiriti bhedaśruterbhede tātparyaliṅgavattvaṃ nyāyāmṛte dvitīyaparicchede upapāditamityarthaḥ |
yadi punarabādhyo bhedastarhyavivādaḥ |
kutaśca vyāvahārikabhedaparatvam |
advaitaśrutivirodhāditi cettarhyadvaitaśruteḥ prātibhāsikādvaitaparatvamastu |
viroghaparihārasya sāmyāt |
nanu na śrutipratipāditasya (śrutyupapāditasya) bādhādaprāmāṇyaṃ |
kintarhyabhipretasya (kintu tadabhipretasya)|
anyathā mīmāṃsakamate'rthavādabhāgasyāprāmāṇyaprasaṅgaḥ |
na cātra bhedaḥ śrutitātparyagocaraḥ |
śrutīnāṃ neha nāneti niṣedhāya pratyakṣaprāptabhedānuvāditvāditi cenna |
neha nānāstīti vedavākyāni vimatā ātmānaḥ paramatmānaḥ paramārthato na bhidyante ātmatvāt paramātmavadityanumānaprāptābhedaṃ dvā suparṇeti niṣedhāyānuvadantīti vaiparītyasambhavāt (vaiparītyaprasaṅgāt)|
bhedasya tādātmyaniṣedhātmakatayā bhedapratipādakānāṃ tādātmyaniṣedharūpatvāt |
etena niṣedhavākyatvādadvaitavākyaṃ prabalamiti nirastam |
na hi pratyakṣeṇa prāptirevānuvāde (prāptiranuvāde) prayojikā |
tathā satya`gnirhimasya bheṣaja'mityādāvagnerhimanivṛttiṃ (śītanivṛttiṃ) prati kāraṇatvamanvayavyatirekarūpānumānaprāptaṃ pratipādayatsarvasammatamanuvādakaṃ na syāt |
evaṃ vyāvahārikabhedasya bādhyatvapakṣaṃ nirasyābādhyatvapakṣamāśaṅkya nirākaroti || yadi punariti || avivāda iti || abādhyatvamaṅgīkāre tadeva pāramārthikatvamiti nāvayorvivādo'stīti bhāvaḥ |
evaṃ paroktavyāvahārikabhedaparatvaṃ śrutīnāmaṅgīkṛtya bādhyatvābādhyatvavikalpena dūṣaṇamabhihitam |
idānīṃ tadevānupapannamiti bhāvenāha -- kutaśceti || uttaraṃśaṅkate |
advaiteti || advaitasya pāramārthikatvāttadviruddhasya bhedasyāpi pāramārthikatvāyogādvyāvahārikatvamaṅgīkṛtya bhedābhedaśrutyorvirodhaḥ parihariṣyata iti bhāvaḥ |
evaṃ tarhi mā'stvetayā bhedaśrutīnāmadvaitaparatvaṃ kinnāma sādṛśyādyarthakatvamevāstu |
evañca bhedaśrutīnāṃ pāramārthikabhedaparatve'pi nādvaitaśrutivirodha iti bhāvenāha -- kiṃ tarhīti || virodhaparihārasya sāmyāditi || yathā dvaitādvaitaśrutyorvirodhaprāpte'dvaitasya pāramārthikatvaṃ dvaitasya vyāvahārikatvamabhipretya virodhaḥ parihriyate evaṃ dvaitasyaiva pāramārthikatvamaṅgīkṛtvā'dvaitaśruteḥ sādṛśyādyabhedārthakatvena na dvaitādvaita śrutyorvirodha ityapi virodhaparihārasya sāmyādityarthaḥ |
nanu svagocarārthābādho hi prāmāṇyamiti yaduktaṃ tatsatyameva |
tathā'pi na bhedaśruteḥ prāmāṇyam |
svagocarārtho nāma svatātparyagocara eva |
na svābhihitamātro bhedaśca na śrutitātparyagocaraḥ |
yena tasya bādhyatvāṅgīkāre śrutīnāmaprāmāṇyaṃ prasajyeta |
kinnāma nāhaṃ sarvajña ityādipratyakṣaprāptaṃ bhedaṃ niṣedhārthamanuvadantyeva śrutaya iti bhāvena śaṅkate -- nanviti || abhihitabādhe'pyaprāmāṇyaṃ kuto netyata āha -- anyatheti || yadi śrutyuditasya bādhācchruteraprāmāṇyamaṅgīkriyate |
tarhyuttānā vai devagavā vahantītyarthavādabhāgasyāprāmāṇyaṃ syāt |
śrutyuditasya devagavānāmūdhrvavāhitvasya laukikagoṣvadṛṣṭatvena bādhitatvāt |
atastatra mīmāṃsakamate prathamādhyāyatṛtīyapāde prayogacodanābhāvārthakatvamavibhāgāditi sūtravyākhyānāvasare yajñadravyārthatvena devasambandhino ye manuṣyāṇāṃ gāvastatstutiparamevedaṃ vākyamityabhipretārthamupapādāyaiva prāmāṇyamupapāditamiti bhāvaḥ |
kecittu so'rodīdyadarodīdityarthavādeṣu mantreṣu ca śaktyā pratipāditānāmatātparyaviṣayībhūtānāmarthānāṃ bādhādityartha ityāhuḥ || vaiparītyasambhavāditi || yaducyate tanneti vākyaṃ parihāre yuktiviśeṣaṃ ca vinā nirnimittamabhede śrutīnāṃ niṣedhakatvasya bhedaśrutīnāmanuvādakatvasyāṅgīkāre uktarītyā vaiparītyasyāpyaṅgīkāraprasaṅgādityarthaḥ |
nanu bhedaśrutīnāṃ bhedamātrapratipādakatvātkathamabhedaniṣedhaparatvam |
yena vaiparītyaprasaṅga ityata āha -- bhedasyeti || bhedo nāmānyonyābhāvaḥ |
sa ca tādātmyapratiyogikaḥ |
tathā ca tādṛśabhedapratipādakaśrutīnāṃ kathamabhedaniṣedhaparatvābhāva iti bhāvaḥ |
niṣedhavākyatvādadvaitavākyaṃ prabalaṃ bhedavākyaṃ tu na niṣedhakamiti tato durbalamiti kaścit |
tadapyanena nirastam |
bhedasyāpi tādātmyaniṣedharūpatvādityāha -- eteneti || nanu bhedaśruteranuvādakatvavadabhedaśruterapyanuvādakatvameveti vaktuṃ na śakyate |
anuvāde pratyakṣaprāptereva prayojakatvāt |
na ca bhedasyevābhedasya pratyakṣataḥ prāptiḥ kintvanumānata evetyata āha -- na hīti || tathā satīti |
kāryakāraṇabhāvasyātīndriyatvādagnerhimanivṛttikāraṇatvaṃ na pratyakṣasiddhamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanu śrutyuktabhedo'bādhya eva nāprāmāṇyamityata āha -- yadīti |
pratītabhedapāramārthatyāgo nirbījaścetyāha -- prātibhāsiketi || virodhyanyatarasyārthāntarameva vācyaṃ na tu pratītārthasya mukhyasattākatvakalpanaṃ yuktamiti bhāvaḥ |
yathā''haiko mīmāṃsako brūyādvirodhe śāstrayormithaḥ |
ekaṃ pramāṇamitaraṃ tvapramāṇaṃ bhavediti |
kiñcaivaṃ śrutyuktajagato mithyātve brahmāpi mithyā syāt |
satyaṃ jñānamityādivirodha iti cenna |
viśvaṃ satyamityādivadupapatteḥ |
anṛtena hi pratyūḍhamiti jagato'sattvamucyate'dvaitaṃ paramārthata iti brahmaṇaḥ pāramārthyamucyata iti cenna |
`yāthātathyato'rthāni'tivadupapatteḥ |
asadvā idamagra āsīnna tatsannāsaducyate |
na sanna cāsañchiva eva kevalaṃ sanna cāsanniṣedhaśeṣo jayatādaśeṣa iti brahmaṇo'pi tathātvāpatteḥ |
āhurasadityādinā brahmāsattvaṃ nindyata iti cenna |
prakṛte'pyasatyamāhurjagadetadajñā asatyamapratiṣṭhaṃ ta ityādi nindyate ca |
etacchūnyavādinindanamiti cet |
samaṃ tavāpi |
adhiṣṭhānajñānāya brahmaikaṃ satyamāvaśyakamiti cet |
prādhānyāya jagadekamapi tathā syāt |
niṣpradhānakabrahmāṅgīkāre niradhiṣṭhānako'pi kinna syāt |
muktau brahmāvaśeṣaḥ śrūyata iti cet |
strībhirvā yānairvā jñātibhirveti jagadavaśeṣo'pi muktau śrūyate |
saguṇamuktāveva jagadavaśeṣo na nirguṇamuktāviti cet |
sanmuktāveva brahmāvaśeṣo nāsanmuktau |
sanmuktiraprāmāṇikīti cet |
nirguṇamuktirapi tathaiva |
yatra tvasya sarvātmaivābhūdityādinā nirguṇamuktiḥ śrūyata iti cet |
asanneva sa bhavati asadbrahmeti veda ceti brahmāsattvajñānenāsanmuktirapi mahattamapuruṣārtho śrūyate |
svarūpāsattvaṃ na puruṣārtha iti cenna |
nairguṇyamapi tathaiva |
muktau svarūpasattāvabodhakapramāṇabāhulyādetadvacanamanyathā neyamiti cet |
evaṃ bhogapratipādakavacanabāhulyādernirguṇavacanamapyanyathā neyamityalam |
api ca śrutyarthānāṃ vyāvahārikatvaṃ svarasataḥ pratīyate brahmavatpāramārthikatvaṃ vā svarasataḥ pratīyate |
nādyaḥ |
anubhavavirodhāt |
vimatamithyātvasādhane vaiyyarthyāpatteśca |
aikyāderapi svarasataḥ pāramārthikatvānāpatteśca |
dvitīye jitamasmābhiḥ |
sarvatra pratītapāramārthyaṃ bādhavati tyajyate |
iha tu bhedavākyamaikyavākyabādhitamiti cenna |
vaiparītyasya prātibhāsikatvasya vā pāramārthika eva dvaividhyasya vā trividhātiriktasattāntarasya vā sambhavenānyathāsiddhiḥ |
kiñca vyāvahārikatvasyaiva durvacatvena śruteḥ satyatvamaṣṭamarasāritamiti || bhedasyābhimatatve'pi tātparyagocaratvābhāvena nāprāmāṇyamityāśaṅkate -- śrutīti || arthavādeti || uttānā vai devagavā vahantītyādāvabhihitārthabādhe upalakṣitaprāśastyarūpaṃ tātparyagocarābādhamātreṇa prāmāṇyāṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ |
vākyānyanuvadantīti sambandhaḥ |
tarhi vinigamanāviraha ityata āha -- bhedasyeti || niṣedhātmakatayeti || anyonyābhāvaviṣayatayetyarthaḥ || niṣedharūpatvāditi || nirākaraṇarūpatvādityarthaḥ |
abhāvapratipādanasya pratiyogiprasañjanapūrvakaṃ tadapanhavarūpatvenaiva pravartanānniṣedhakatvamucitamiti bhāvaḥ |
etena pūrvapakṣoktamanuvāditvaṃ nirastaṃ jñeyam || eteneti || bhedavākyasya niṣedhakatvasvarūpapratipādanenetyarthaḥ || niṣedhavākyatvāditi || advaitapadasya nārthagarbhitatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
yadvā neha nānā'stītyadvaitavākyasya nārthagarbhitatvāditi bhāvaḥ || nirastamiti || śabdaniṣedharūpatvādarthato niṣedharūpatvasya prabalatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
bhedasyevaikyasya pratyakṣaprāptyabhāvātkathamanuvāditvamityata āha -- na hīti |
svānupajīvimānāntaraprāptirevānuvāditve tantramiti bhāvaḥ || anumānaprāptamiti || kāryakāraṇabhāvasyātīndriyatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
dvitīyamāśaṅkyeṣṭāpattimāha -- yadi punariti || bhedaśrutīnāṃ vyāvahārikabhedaparatvaṃ niṣpramāṇikamityāha -- kutaśceti || arthavādādibhāgasyeti || so'rodīdyadarodīdityādyarthavādeṣu mantreṣu ca śaktyā pratipāditānāmatātparyaviṣayībhūtānāmarthanāṃ bādhādityarthaḥ |
vaiparītyevocitamityāha -- bhedasya tādātmyaniṣedhātmakatayeti || niṣedhavākyatvādadvaitavākyaṃ prabalamiti vācaspatyuktamapyata eva nirastamityāha -- eteneni |
nanu bhedasya pratyekṣeṇa prāpterbhedavākye'nuvādakatā yujyate nābhedavākye |
abhedasya pratyakṣeṇāprāpterityāśaṅkāmāprayojakatvena pariharati -- na hīti |
avivāda āvayoriti śeṣaḥ |
asmābhirapi tathaivāṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ |
bhedo vyavahārakālābādhya eva na vastuta ityāśaṅkya dvaitādvaitayoḥ śrutipratipādyatvasāmye'pi uktabādhyatvameva kuta ityāśayena dūṣayituṃ pṛcchati -- kutaśceti || pūrvaṃ bhedasya śrutyarthatve'pi na satyatvena śrutyarthatvamityāśaṅkā nirākṛtā |
idānīṃ śrutyarthatvameva nāstīti śaṅkānirāsārtho nanvityādigranthaḥ || śrutyabhipretasyeti || tātparyapramādhīnaśrutijanyabodhe niṣedhāpratiyogitvena bhāsamānasyetyarthaḥ || arthavādabhāgasyeti || prajāpatirātmano vapāmudakhidadityādīnāmityarthaḥ |
prajāpativapotkhananasya tātparyaviṣayasyāsattve'pi teṣāṃ prāśastyalakṣakatayā prāmāṇyaṃ vartate |
tasyaiva tātparyaviṣayatvādityarthaḥ || na ceheti || niṣedhāpratiyogitveneti śeṣaḥ |
tasyaivāsattvāṅgīkāre'prāmāṇyaṃ dvaitavākyāni tu niṣedhapratiyogitayaiva dvaitaṃ bodhayantītyarthaḥ |
anuvādasya prāptimūlakapratyakṣeṇetyuktam astu tarhyabādhya eva śrutyukto bheda ityata āha -- yadi punariti || avivāda iti || abādhyatvasyaiva pāramārthikatvarūpatayā'smadabhimatasiddheriti bhāvaḥ |
bhedaśrutervyāvahārikabhedaparatve bādhakamabhidhāya sādhakābhāvamāha -- kutaśceti || advaitasya vyāvahārikatvāsambhavāt prātibhāsiketyuktam |
nanvevamadvaitaśrutīnāmaprāmāṇyaprasaṅga iti cet tvanmate dvaitaśrutīnāmaprāmāṇyaprasaṅgena tulyatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
syādevam |
yadyabādhyārthapratipādakatvaṃ prāmāṇyaṃ syāt |
na caivam |
api tvabādhyārthaparatvam |
dvā suparṇetyādīnāṃ ca bhedānuvādakānāṃ tanniṣedhaparatvādeva prāmāṇyopapattiḥ |
ata eva tatpratipādyabhedasya bādhyatvamapītyāśayena śaṅkate -- nanviti || arthavādabhāgasyeti || tasya pratipādyārthābādhaniyamābhāve'pyabādhyavidhyarthakāryaparatvenaiva prāmāṇyāṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ |
matāntare pratipādyārthe'pi prāmāṇyāt mīmāṃsakamata ityuktam |
evaṃ satyadvaitaśrutīnāṃ prātibhāsikādvaitaparatve'pi nāprāmāṇyaprasaṅga ityāśayenāha -- neheti || vākyānyanuvadantītyanvayaḥ |
bhedavadabhedasya pratyakṣeṇa prāptyabhāvādāha -- vimatā iti || nanu dvāsuparṇetyādīnāṃ kathamabhedaniṣedhaparatvam ? tadarthakapadābhāvādata āha -- bhedasyeti || tādātmyaniṣedharūpatvāt abhedābhāvarūpatvāt |
tathāca bhedārthasyaiva tadarthakatvānnoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ |
evaṃ bhedaśruterabhedaniṣedhakatvasamarthanena vācaspatyuktamapi pratyuktamityāha -- eteneti || prabalamiti || tathāca tena bhedaśrutereva bādhaḥ |
anyathā nedaṃ rajatamiti pratyakṣasyāpi prābalyāsiddheridaṃ rajatamiti pratyakṣabādhakatvānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ || pratyuktamiti || bhedaśruterapi niṣedhakatvasāmye durbalatvāyogāditi bhāvaḥ |
atha bhedasyābhedaniṣedharūpatve'pyabhedaniṣedhatvena bhedāpratipādakatvāt dvā suparṇetyāderdurbalatvameveti cedatra vaktavyam |
kimabhedajñānaṃ prati abhedaniṣedhatvenaiva bhedajñānaṃ virodhyuta bhedatvenāpi |
ādye'nubhavaviredhaḥ |
ghaṭaḥ paṭabhinna ityādijñānadaśāyāṃ jātvapi tadabhedajñānānudayāt |
dvitīye tu kathaṃ dvā suparṇetyāderdurbalatvam ? nirodhitvāviśeṣāt |
na caivamuktātiprasaṅgaḥ |
niścitaprāmāṇyakatvena prābalyāt |
parīkṣayā ca tanniścayāt |
niṣedhakatvamātrasyāprayojakatvāt |
anyathā `na hiṃsyā'dityāde`ragnīṣomīya'mityādiśrutibādhakatvaprasaṅgāt |
na caivaṃ bhedābhedavākyayoḥ prābalyadaurbalyānirṇayaḥ |
niravakāśatvādinā tannirṇayāt |
niravakāśatvaṃ ca bhedavākyānāmevetyanyatra prapañcitam |
nanvabhedaśrutīnāṃ nānuvādakatvasambhavaḥ |
abhedasya pratyakṣeṇāprāptatvāt |
na ca prāptimātramanuvādaprayojakam |
sarvasyāpyanumānataḥ prāptisambhavena śrutimātrasyāpyanuvādakatvaprasaṅgādityata āha -- na hīti || iti vākyamanuvādakaṃ na syādityanvayaḥ |
iṣṭāpattinirāsārthamanuvādakatvena sarvasammatamiti || pratyakṣaprāptapratipādakatve doṣābhāvādanvayattyatirekarūpānumānaprāptaṃ kāraṇatvamarthaṃ pratipādayaditi || kāraṇatvasya kālādighaṭitatvena pratyakṣāsambhavāditi bhāvaḥ |
yatsattve yatsattvamityanvayamātrasya prameyatvābhidheyatvayoḥ vyabhicaritvāt vyatirekaniveśaḥ |
yadabhāve yadabhāva iti vyatirekasya naraśṛṅgaśaśaśṛṅgayorvyabhicāritvādanvayaniveṣaḥ |
na caivamapyanyathāsiddhānvavyātirekavati vyabhicāraḥ |
ananyathāsiddhatvenāpi viśeṣaṇīyatvāt |
vastutastu anvayavyatirekavattvasya niyatapūrvavṛttitvavyāpyatayā tena tasmin sādhyamāne himanivṛttiṃ pratyagneranyathāsiddhaniyatapūrvavṛttitvabādhāt ananyathāsiddhaniyatapūrvavṛttitvarūpakāraṇatvasiddhirityavadheyam |
evaṃ vyāvahārikabhedasya bādhyatvamityādyapakṣaṃ dūṣayitvā dvitīyamanūdya dūṣayati -- yadīti || avivāda iti || śrutipratipādyabhedasya pāramārthikatvāparaparyāyābādhyatvāṅgīkāre'smadabhimatasyaivāṅgīkārādāvayorvivādo na syādityarthaḥ |
evaṃ bhedasya paroktavyāvahārikatvamabhyupetya dūṣaṇamabhihitam |
idānīṃ vyāvahārikatvameva bhedasya netyāha -- kutaścetyādinā sāmyādityantena || śrutipratipādyabhedasya vyāvahārikatvaṃ kasmādaṅgīkriyata ityarthaḥ |
uttaraṃ śaṅkata || advaiteti || pāramārthikādvaitetyarthaḥ || virodhaparihārasyeti || ubhayorapi śrutyoḥ pāramārthikabhedābhedapratipādakatve'nyonyavirodhāt khalu bhedaśrutervyāvahārikabhedaparatvamabhedaśruteḥ pāramārthikābhedaparatvamaṅgīkṛtya tatparihāraḥ kriyate |
tasya ca virodhaparihārasya bhedaśruteḥ pāramārthikabhedaparatvamabhedaśruteḥ prātibhāsikābhedaparatvamityaṅgīkāre'pi samānatvādityarthaḥ |
nanu vyāvahārikabhedasya bādhyatvamityādyapakṣa eva svīkriyate |
naca tathātve śruterbādhyārthagrāhitvenāprāmāṇyaprasaṅga iti vācyam |
śrutitātparyaviṣayārthabādhasyaivāprāmāṇye prayojakatvena śrutipratipādyabādhasyāprayojakatvāt |
bhedasya ca śrutipratipādyatve'pi śrutitātparyagocaratvābhāvāditi śaṅkate -- nanviti || upapāditasya pratipādyasya |
abhipretasya tātparyagocarasya || anyatheti || yadi tasya bādhācchruteraprāmāṇyamaṅgīkriyate ta`rhyuttānā vai devagavā vahantī'tyādyarthavādabhāgasyāpyaprāmāṇyaṃ syāt |
śrutyuditasya devagavānāmūrdhvavāhitvasya bādhitatvāt |
matāntare'rthavādāprāmāṇyasyeṣṭatvānmīmāṃsakamata ityuktam |
kuta ityata āha -- śrutīnāmiti || śrutīnāṃ bhedānuvāditvāditi sambandhaḥ |
aprāptasyānuvādāsambhavādāha -- pratyakṣaprāpteti || nāhaṃ sarvajña ityādipratyakṣaprāptetyarthaḥ |
anuvāde prayojanamāha -- neha nāneti || pratibandyā samādhatte || neti || nanu neha nāneti vākyasyeha brahmaṇi jagati vā bhedo nāstīti bhedaniṣedhakatvena tena niṣedhāya dvāsuparṇetyāderbhedānuvādakatvaṃ yujyate |
dvā suparṇetyādestu bhedamātrapratipādakatvenābhedaniṣedhakatvābhāvātkathaṃ tena niṣedhāya neha nānetyāderabhedānuvādakatvamityata āha -- bhedasyeti || bhedo hyanyonyābhāvarūpaḥ |
sa ca tādātmyapratiyogikatvena tanniṣedharūpa eva |
tathā ca tādṛśabhedapratipādakavākyasya tādātmyaniṣedharūpatvena niṣedhāya neha nānetyāderbhedānuvādakatvaṃ yuktamevetyarthaḥ || eteneti || bhedaśrutivirodhādabhedaśrutyarthatyāgo vā utābhedaśrutivirodhādbhedaśrutyarthatyaga iti sandehe niṣedhavākyatvenābhedaśruteḥ prābalyāttadvirodhena bhedaśrutyarthasyaiva bhedasya parityāga iti yaduktamadvaitavādinā tadbhedavākyasyāpi niṣedhakatvakathanena nirastamityarthaḥ |
nanu nābhedavākyasyānuvādakatvaṃ yuktam |
abhedasya prāmāṇāntarāprāpteḥ |
nacānumānataḥ prāptirukteti vācyam |
anuvāde pratyakṣataḥ prāptereva prayojakatvenānumānena prāpteraprayojakatvādityata āha -- nahīti || tathā satīti || pratyakṣataḥ prāpterevānuvādaprayojakatve `agnirhimasyabheṣaja'miti vākyamanuvādakaṃ na syādityanvayaḥ |
kuta ityata uktamagnerityādipratipādayadityantam |
agnerhimanivṛttikāraṇatvaṃ hi satyagnau himanivṛttiranyathā netyanvayavyatirekadarśanena himanivṛttiragnito janyā |
tadanvayavyatirekānuvidhāyitvādityānumānaprāptam |
tadevapratipādayadidaṃ vākyamanuvādakamaṅgīkriyate |
na ca tatrāpi pratyakṣaprāptasyaivānuvāda iti vācyam |
kāryakāraṇabhāvasya kālādighaṭitatvenātīndriyatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
iṣṭāpattiparihārāyoktaṃ sarvasammatamiti |
neha nāneti vākyasyānumānenaikyaprāptirityabhihitam |
yadvā tattvamasīti śrutyarthāparijñānādadvaitaprāptirastu |
asti ca śrutyarthāparijñānaprāptasya śrutyantareṇa niṣedhaḥ |
yatheṣṭāpūrtayoḥ paramapuruṣārthatvaniṣedhaḥ |
athavā pravāhato'nādirūpayā duḥśāstraparamparayā'dvaitaprāptirastu |
asti ca kumataprāptasya niṣedhaḥ |
`taddhaika āhurasadevedamagra āsī'`dekamevādvitīyaṃ' `tasmādasataḥ sadajāyata' |
`kutastu khalu somyaivaṃ syāditi hovāca' |
`kathamasataḥ sajjāyete(sadajāyate)'tyādau |
atra hi bauddhamataprāptamasataḥ kāraṇatvaṃ niṣidhyata iti ||
tasmāt dvā suparṇetyādyāḥ śrutayo jīveśvarabhede pramāṇam |
paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti saha brahmaṇā vipaścitā |
mama sādharmyamāgatā ityādyāstu na vyāvahārikabhedaparatvena śaṅkitumapi śakyāḥ |
muktyantaraṃ bhedapratipādakatvāt |
ghaṭapaṭādi bhede tu pratyakṣaṃ sulabhameva |
advaitaśruteranuvādakatvasiddhyarthaṃ prakārāntareṇādvaitasya prāptiṃ darśayati -- yadveti || tattvamasīti śrutestātparyaviṣayībhūto'rthastu tadguṇasāratvāttadvyapadeśa iti sūtrakāreṇaiva siṃho devadatta ityādivajjīvabrahmaṇoḥ sādṛśyarūpo darśitastadaparijñānenetyarthaḥ |
śrutyarthāparijñānaprāptasya śrutyantareṇa niṣedhaḥ kva dṛṣṭa ityata āha -- astīti |
`darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajete'tyādiśrutitātparyāparijñānaprāptamanīśvaraviṣayaṃ kāmyakarme`ṣṭāpūrtaṃ manyamānā variṣṭhaṃ nānyacchreyo vedayante pramūḍhāḥ |
nākasya pṛṣṭhe sukṛte te'nubhūtvā imaṃ lokaṃ hīnataraṃ vā viśantī'ti śrutiḥ |
pūrvārdhenānūdyottarārdhena tasya paramapuruṣārthasādhanatvaṃ nirākaroti |
nindāyāḥ pratiṣedhe tātparyāt |
na caivaṃvidhakarmaṇo'nyataḥ prāptirasti |
ataḥ śrutitātparyāparijñānameva tatprāpakaṃ vācyam |
tadvatprakṛte'pīti bhāvaḥ || iṣṭāpūrtayoriti || iṣṭaṃ yajanaṃ pūrtamannadānādikaṃ tayorityarthaḥ |
anyeṣāmapi dṛśyata iti dīrghaḥ śrutyanusāreṇa |
śrutau ca dvandvaikavadbhāvaḥ |
sarvo dvandvo vibhāṣayaikavadbhavatīti vacanāt |
variṣṭhaṃ paramapuruṣarthasādhanam |
anyacchreyaḥ parabrahmāpūjyam |
tatprasādamiti yāvat |
na vedayante na jānanti |
variṣṭhamiti vartate |
prakārāntareṇāpyadvaitaprāptiṃ darśayati -- athaveti || kathamādhunikaduḥśāstraparamparayā prāptasyānādinā vedena niṣedha ityata uktaṃ pravāhato'nādirūpayeti |
anādikakālato vṛttāḥ samayā hi pravāhata iti vacanāditi bhāvaḥ |
asyāṃ śrutau kiṃ mataprāptaṃ kiṃ niṣidhyata ityataḥ spaṣṭayati -- atra hīti |
iti śabdaḥ praptipradarśanaprakaraṇasamāptiparaḥ |
upasaṃharati -- tasmāditi || uktatītyā bhedasya vyāvahārikatvābhāvadanuvādakatvābhāvāccetyarthaḥ |
vyāvahārikabhedaparatvena śaṅkitumapyaśakyamauktabhedaśrutayaḥ smṛtayaśca santītyāha -- paramamiti || na hi muktau vyāvahārikabhedo yujyata iti bhāvaḥ |
evamātmabhede pramāṇatrayamupanyasyaitena ghaṭapaṭādibhede pramāṇaṃ nirastamityādinā prāk kṛtāṃśaṅkāṃ nirāha -- ghaṭapaṭādīti || sulabhameveti || nopapādanasāpekṣaṃ dharmipratiyoginorubhayorapi pratyakṣayogyatvena pūrvaśaṅkānavakāśāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu kevalānumānānneśvaraprāptiḥ |
śāstraikasamadhigamyatvādīśvarasya |
āgamamūlatve svānupajīvatvābhāvātkathamanuvāditvamityataḥ prāptyantaramāha -- yadveti || aparijñānāditi || tātparyāparijñānādāpātapratītimātrādityarthaḥ |
ādhunikapuruṣajñānātprāptamarthaṃ kathamanādiśruteranuvāda ityata āha -- astīti |
ajñānasyāpi pravāhato'nāditvāditi bhāvaḥ || niṣedha iti || iṣṭāpūrtaṃ manyamānā iti śeṣaḥ |
atra sākṣānniṣedhaśravaṇe'pi nindārthavādādgamyata iti jñeyam |
nanviyaṃ prāptiḥ śrutyarthāparijñānādeveti kutaḥ śāstrādito'pi sambhavāditi cenna |
yāmimāṃ puṣpitāṃ vācaṃ pravadanti vipaścita iti samākhyānāt |
viṣayavyāptyarthaṃ prāptyantaramāha -- athaveti || samayasyādhunikatvātkathametadityāśaṅkyoktaṃ pravāhata iti || eke śūnyavādina idamagre'sya jagataḥ pūrvamevādvitīyamasacchūnyamevāsīttasmādasataḥ sajjagadāsīt |
ajāyatetyāhuḥ |
he somārha kutaḥ kāraṇādevaṃ syāditi hovāca guruḥ śiṣyaṃ prati kathamasataḥ sajjāyeteti bhāvenetyarthaḥ |
kiñca bhedābhedavākyayoḥ paurvāparyaniyamābhāvādyattacchabdabhāvadbhedavākye vidheyāntaraśravaṇāt |
bhedābhedavākyayorasannihitatvādbhedasyāṅgīkṛtārthāntareṇa sahapāṭhācca nānuvādaniṣedha iti bhāvaḥ |
yaccoktaṃ siddhānuvādakatvādbhedavākyaṃ durbalamiti |
tanna |
brahupramāṇasaṃvādasya dāḍharyahetutvena guṇatvāt |
etena bhedavākyasya vaiyarthyamiti nirastam |
vyarthatve'pi tadarthasya satyatvāvirodhācca na doṣaḥ |
vastutastvanuvāditve'pi na vaiyarthyam |
adhikāribhedena pravṛttasamānārthakaśākhāntare vyabhicārāt |
adhikāribhedenāpravṛtte satīti viśeṣaṇe tvasiddhiḥ |
yasya vādimatyā pratyakṣaṃ bhedaniścayagrahaśca neṣṭe taṃ prati pravṛttatvāt |
kiñca mā hiṃ syātsarvā bhūtānīti vākye vyabhicāraḥ |
kasyāpi sarvahiṃsāprasaktyabhāvāt |
ekadeśe'pūrvatā'stīti cet |
samaṃ mamāpi sarvabhedasya pratyakṣāprāptatvāt |
api ca sauptikasukhānubhavasiddhānuvāde ānandaṃ brahmaṇa iti vākye vyabhicāraḥ |
tasyaiva traikālikatvavidhānārthaṃ taditi cet |
samaṃ prakṛte'pi |
uktarītyā bhedapratyakṣasya śrutyupajīvyatvāditi bhedasya satyatve'sandigdhaśrutimapi paṭhati || paramamityādinā |
nanvatra sāmyādirevocyate na ca bhedaḥ |
na ca tadvyāpakoktyā tadanumātuṃ śakyata iti vācyam |
tarhyāgamatayodāharaṇānupapattiriti cenna |
tadbhinnatve sati tadgatabhūyodharmavattvaṃ sādṛśyamiti bhedagarbhatvāt |
na ca tanniṣṭhatvameva lakṣaṇamastviti vācyam |
svasyāpi svasādṛśyavyavahārāpatteśca |
ata eva gaganaṃ gaganākāramityādau sadṛśāntaraniṣedha eva tātparyamiti vadanti |
nanu tathā'pi bhavatāṃ brahmasādṛśyaṃ siddhāntaviruddhamiti cenna |
`jagadvyāpāravarja'miti nirṇāyakānusaraṇasaṅkocakaraṇāt |
evañca sahabhāvasyāpi tadbhinnatve sati tatkriyāyogitvamiti || bhedagarbha ityādyā iti || parañjyotirupasampadya svena rūpeṇābhiniṣpadyate |
na yatra māyā kimutāpare hareranuvrata ityādirūhyam |
na ceyaṃ saguṇamuktiriti vācyam |
muktidvaividhye mānābhāvāt |
svakriyānyathā'nupapattyā kalpane tvanyonyāśrayaḥ |
astu vā tadvaividhyaṃ sāmyavākye puṇyapāpe vidhūyeti nirañjanatvokteḥ |
upasattivākye sneharūpeṇeti mithyākāre prahāṇokteḥ |
antyena yatra māyeti māyābhāvokteśca na saguṇamuktiparatvamiti |
evamātmabhedaṃ prasādhya jaḍabhedaṃ sādhayati -- ghaṭapaṭeti || nāyaṃ ghaṭa iti pratyakṣamityarthaḥ |
|| śrutyantareṇeti || iṣṭāpūrtaṃ manyamānā variṣṭhaṃ nānyacchreyo vedayante pramūḍhā ityādinetyarthaḥ || muktyanantaramiti || āvidyakasya muktāvanuvṛttyayogādityarthaḥ || sulabhameveti || dharmapratiyoginoryogyatvādityarthaḥ |
|| iṣṭāpūrtayoriti || `iṣṭāpūrtaṃ manyamānā variṣṭhaṃ nānyacchreyo vedayante pramūḍhāḥ |
nākasya pṛṣṭhe sukṛte te'nubhūtvā imaṃ lokaṃ hīnataraṃ vā viśantītyātharvaṇaśruterayamarthaḥ |
iṣṭaṃ yajanaṃ pūrtamannadānādi |
iṣṭapadottara`manyebhyo'pi dṛśyata' iti dīrgha iti viṣṇutattvanirṇayaṭīkāyāmuktam |
anyacchreyaḥ paramātmaprāptilakṣaṇamokṣarūpaśreyo brahma vā vedayante svārthe ṇic vindanti |
nākasya pṛṣṭhe svargasya sthāne sukṛte sukṛtasādhye'nubhūtvā sukhamanubhūya |
imaṃ lokamityādi |
vāśabdo vyavasthitavikalpe |
viṣṇau rājādivatsarvottamatvajñānapūrvakaṃ tadviśeṣāparijñānapūrvakaṃ ca kāmyakarma kṛtvā'nyadevatoddeśyakakarmāpyantato viṣṇāveva samarpayanti |
te svargaṃ bhuṅktvemaṃ lokaṃ mānuṣalokaṃ āviśanti prāpnuvanti |
anyeṣāṃ bhagavatsāmyamanyebhyo bhagavato hīnatvamityādijñānino duryodhanapramukhā ayogyā dakṣiṇā tadarpitānāmācāryāṇāṃ droṇādīnāṃ tejasā svargaṃ bhuktvā tadanantaraṃ hīnataraṃ tamādirūpaṃ punarutthānaśūnyaṃ kramādāviśantīti |
uktaṃ hyetadātharvaṇabhāṣye |
sarvebhyo'pi paraṃ viṣṇuṃ rājavadyastu manyate |
yājī sa mānuṣaṃ yāti sāmyahīnatvavittama iti || atra viśeṣo gītātātpartanirṇayato'vagantavyaḥ |
tathā ceṣṭāpūrtādeḥ paramapuruṣārthatvajñānināṃ nindādvārā śrutiḥ paramapuruṣārthatvaṃ niṣedhati |
arthavādasya vidhiniṣedhāvantarāparyavasānāyogāt |
niṣedhaśca prāptipūrvakaḥ |
prāptirvaktavyā sā ca pratyakṣānumānābhyām |
svargādestanniṣṭhapuruṣārthatāyāstajjanakatvādīnāmatīndriyatve pratyakṣāprasakteḥ |
pakṣasādhyahetvādīnāṃ grahītumaśakyatvenānumānasyāpyapravṛtteḥ |
tathā ca `yanna duḥkhena sambhinna'mityādivākyaiḥ svarganirūpaṇaparairjyotiṣṭomena svargakāmo yajetetyādivākyaiśca tātparyamaviduṣāṃ yāgādisādhyasya svargādeḥ paramapuruṣārthatvabhramarūpā prāptirvartate |
tadviṣayībhūtaṃ yathā śrutyā niṣidhyate tathehāpyastviti bhāvaḥ || pravāhato'nādirūpayeti || idaṃ cāgantukaṃ kathamanādirūpayā śrutyā'nūdyata iti śaṅkānirāsāya |
pravāhato'nāditvaṃ ca yādṛśarūpāśrayādhikaraṇa[kāla]sāmānyaṃ yādṛśarūpāśraya pratiyogikaprāgabhāvadhvaṃsavattādṛśarūpāśrayatvaṃ tena rūpeṇa pravāhato'nāditvamiti rūpaghaṭitaṃ nirvācyam |
mandāramañjaryuktasyaikapravāhetyādilakṣaṇavākyasyoktārthe paryavasānamanyatra pradarśitamasmābhirgranthagauravabhayānneha pradarśitaṃ sudhībhiḥ svayamūhyam || taddhaika āhuriti || chāndogyopaniṣadi ṣaṣṭhe'dhyāye sadeva saumyedamagra āsīdeka evādvitīyamiti svamatamuktvā taddhaika āhurityādinā bauddhasiddhanto'nūdyate |
`kutastatkhalu saumyaivaṃ syādi'tyādinā tanniṣedhārtham |
tathehāpi pravāhato'nādyadvaitamaprāptajīveśvaraikyādikam |
tādātmyaniṣedharūpa(bheda)bodhakaśrutyā nirākriyata ityarthaḥ |
atra yadyapi sarveṣāṃ prāṇināṃ dehagehādikaṃ pratyaparāyattasvāmitvabhramo vartate |
tasya ca bhāvābhāvasādhāraṇadharmatvādbhagavattādātmyabhramarūpa eveti caturthe'pi prāptiḥ |
pratyakṣarūpā viṣṇutattvanirṇaye ācāryairevopapāditā |
tathā'pi sāti sphuṭatayā tādātmyabhramarūpatayā jñātuṃ śakyā bahūpapādanasāpekṣetyāśayena tisṛṇāmevopapādanaṃ kṛtamiti jñātavyam || āśaṅkitumapi śakyā iti || muktau vidyamānānāṃ bādhyatvāyogāditi bhāvaḥ |
|| ghaṭa ityādi || advaitināṃ madhye māyāvādimate bhedasya bhrāntigocaratayā'ṅgīkṛtatvādbhedamātrasādhane siddhasādhanatā |
bhāskaramate sadrūpatvena ghaṭapaṭādīnāmapyabheda eva ghaṭatvādirūpeṇa tu bheda ityaṅgīkṛtatvena tanmātrasādhane siddhasādhanatā |
ubhābhyāṃ tādātmyāsāmānādhikaraṇyasya
bhedasya kutrāpyanaṅgīkṛtatvādatyantābhedasādhane cāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā ghaṭapaṭādīnāṃ tvatāttvikasvarūpaṃ brahmaiveti māyibhiraṅgīkṛtatvādbhede pāramārthikaviśeṣaṇaṃ māyāvādino[nā]'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatāsampādakaṃ bhāskarasya siddhasādhakasampādakamiti bhedasādhanamupekṣya tāttvikatādātmyābhāva eva sādhitaḥ |
sa cādvaitavādināmasammatameveti na siddhasādhanam |
yadyapi tādātmyatvāvacchinnābhāvarūpasāmānyābhāvasādhanenaiva nirvāhe tāttvikatvaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ tādātmyenārthavaditi bhāti |
tathā'pi setsyamānasya tadabhāvaḥ pāramārthika eva saṃsidhyati |
nyūnasattākasya tadabhāvasya tadvirodhitvābhāvenānumitestatsaṃśayanivartakatvānupapatteriti dyotanāya |
yadvā prātibhāsikatādātmyamādāya pareṇa sādhyābhāva(rūpa)tādātmyaṃ prati hetvabhāvasya vyāpakatābhaṅgādvyatirekavyāptyanupapattiśaṅkane āropitasyāsattvena tadadhikaraṇāprasiddhyoktaśaṅkanamayuktamityādyupapādanaprayāsavidveṣeṇa sādhyībhūtābhāvapratiyogikoṭau tanniveśa iti jñeyam |
yadyapi vāstavapadavattādātmyaṃ brahmasvarūpameva parasiddhaṃ tacca ghaṭādāvapi parairaṅgīkṛtam |
anyathaitadgranthasamāptibhāgasthaparānumānadvayāpatti(pravṛtti)prasaṅgāt |
tathā ca kathaṃ vyatireke vyāptigraha iti bhāti |
tathā'pi ghaṭe vyatirekasahacārasya niścayādanyatra ghaṭe tatsandehe'pi ghaṭo yadi paṭātmā syāttatkāryakārī syādityādyanukūlatarkabalāttamavadhūya vyāptiniścayo bhavatīti draṣṭavyam |
abhāvasādhyakakevalavyatirekitvānnātrāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇateti || anupadamaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇaṃ tviti mūla eva vyaktam |
atastacchaṅkānavakāśaḥ |
evamanumānādadvaitaprāptimabhidhāyāgamādapi tāmāha -- yadvetyādi || śrutiprāptādvaitasya śrutyantareṇa pratiṣedhe śruteraprāmāṇyāpattirataḥ || śrutyarthāparijñānāditi || śrutitātparyaviṣayārthāparijñānāt |
tatsādṛśyādirūpārthe śrutitātparyāparijñānāditi yāvat || yatheṣṭāpūrtayoriti || svargakāmo yajetetyādi -- śrutitātparyaviṣayasvārgapadārthaparamātmajñānasādhanatvāparijñānaprāptasya anityasukhasvarūpasvargasādhanatvasya `iṣṭāpūrtaṃ manyamānā variṣṭaṃ nānyacchreyo vedayante pramūḍhā' ityādau pratiṣedha ityarthaḥ |
iṣṭaṃ yajanaṃ pūrtamannadānādi |
sayuktikaśabdābhāsādapyadvaitaprāptimāha -- athaveti || duḥśāstraparamparayā pūrvapūrvopajīvyuttarottaramāyiśāstreṇa |
