nanu ādhunikaśāstraprāptādvaitaṃ kathamanādivedena niṣiddhyata ityata āha -- pravāhato'nādirūpayeti || pravāhato'nāditvaṃ caikapravāhamadhyapatitānekapravāhipratiyogikaprāgabhāvāvacchinnakālāvṛttitvānadhikaraṇadhvaṃsapratiyogipravāhitvamiti mandāramañjaryāmuktam |
tatra ca sādipravāhaghaṭakaghaṭādikriyāyāmativyāptivāraṇāya kālāvṛttitvānadhikaraṇeti kālatvavyāpakārthakam |
sādipravāhaghaṭakavastudhvaṃsasya ca na kālatvavyāpakatvam |
tasya tatpravāhottarayāvatkālavṛttitve'pi tatpravāhaghaṭakānekavastuprāgabhāvāvacchinnayāvatkālavṛttitvābhāvādityetallābhārthamavacchinnāntopādānam |
na tvasya lakṣaṇaghaṭakatvamanarthakatvāt |
kālatvavyāpakapravāhitvamityevoktau anityānādipravāhaghaṭakamanovṛttīnāmasaṅgrahāttādṛśadhvaṃsapratiyogitvaparyantaniveśaḥ |
yadyapyanādipravāhaghaṭakakāmakarmādidhvaṃsānāmapi na pratyekaṃ kālatvavyāpakatvasambhavaḥ |
tathāpi tatpravāhaghaṭakavastudhvaṃsatvena vyāpakatvaṃ vivakṣaṇīyamiti jñāpanāya pravāhipadam |
yadyapyevaṃ na kāmakarmādidhvaṃsapravāhasaṅgrahaḥ |
tadghaṭakavastudhvaṃsāprasiddheḥ |
tathāpyatra bhāvānāmeva lakṣyatvānna doṣaḥ |
abhāvānāmapi lakṣyatve tatpravāhaghaṭakapravāhiprāgabhāvāvacchinnakālatvavyāpakapravāhitvaṃ pravāhato'nāditvaṃ bodhyam |
etena yathāśrutalakṣaṇasyānādipravāhaghaṭakacaramavyaktāvavyāptiḥ |
taddhvaṃsasya tatpravāhaghaṭakavastuprāgabhāvāvacchinnakālāvṛttitvāt |
evaṃ tādṛśakālāvṛttitvānadhikaraṇatvasya tādṛśakālavṛttitvarūpatayā tasya ca sādipravāhaghaṭakavastusādhāraṇatvādativyāptiḥ |
tādṛśayatkiñcitkālāvṛttitvānadhikaraṇatvasya tādṛśayāvatkālavṛttitvaparyavasitasya vivakṣāyāmasambhavaḥ |
kasyāpi dhvaṃsasya tādṛśayāvatkālāvṛttitvāt avacchinnāntavaiyarthyaṃ cetyādikamapāstam |
kecittu ekapravāhetyādinaikapravāhaghaṭakāḥ yāvantaḥ pravāhiṇastattatprāgabhāvāvacchinnakālavṛttidhvaṃsapratiyogitvaṃ vivakṣitam |
tatra ca sādipravāhavāraṇāyāvacchinnāntopādānam |
tadghaṭakavastudhvaṃsānāṃ ca tadghaṭakaprathamavyaktiprāgabhāvāvacchinnakālavṛttitvamityadoṣaḥ |
yadyapi sāmānyato yāvatprāgabhāvāvacchinnakālavṛttitvaniveśe'pi taddoṣavāraṇasambhavaḥ |
tathāpi avyāvartakānantaprāgabhāvaniveśavaiyarthyāt tatpravāhaghaṭakapravāhiprāgabhāvānāmeva niveśaḥ |
na cānādipravāhaghaṭakavastudhvaṃsānāmapi pratyekaṃ tādṛśayāvatprāgabhāvāvacchinnakālavṛttitvābhāvādasambhava iti vācyam |
tattatprāgabhāvāvacchinnakālavṛttivṛttitatpravāhaghaṭakavastudhvaṃsatvāśrayībhūtadhvaṃsapratiyogitvasya vivakṣitatvāt |
na caivaṃ tattatprāgabhāvāvacchinnakālavṛttivṛttitatpravāhaghaṭakatvasyaiva samyaktvāt dhvaṃsapratiyogitvaparyantaniveśo niṣphala iti vācyam |
yathā sanniveśena vaiyarthyānavakāśādityāhuḥ |
evaṃ ca duḥśāstrāṇāmapyuktarūpānāditvādanādita evādvaitasya prāptisambhavādvedaniṣedhyatvopapattiriti bhāvaḥ |
|| niṣedha iti || `asadeve'tyādinā paramatamanūdya `kutastvi'tyādinā niṣedha ityarthaḥ |
kasya matena prāptaṃ kimatra niṣidhyata ityākāṅkṣāyāmāha -- atra hīti || dvā suparṇetyādiśruteḥ pāramārthikabhedaparatvamupasaṃharati -- tasmāditi || advaitaśrutīnāmuktarītyā bādhopapatterityarthaḥ |
vyāvahārikabhedaparatvaśaṅkānirāsasaukaryāt śrutyantarāṇyapyāha -- paramamiti || `yadā paśyaḥ paśyate rugmavarṇaṃ kartāramīśaṃ puruṣaṃ brahmayonim |
tadā vidvān puṇyapāpe vidhūya nirañjanaḥ paramaṃ sāmyamupaiti |
' `yo veda nihitaṃ guhāyāṃ parame vyoman so'śnute sarvān kāmān saha brahmaṇā vipaścitā' |
`idaṃ jñānamupāśritya mama sādharmyamāgatā' ityādyā ityarthaḥ || muktyanantaramiti || tadbhinnatve sati taddharmajātīyadharmavattvasyaiva sāmyādiśabdārthatvāttadbhinnatatsamānadeśatvasyaiva tatsahabhāvarūpatvāttasya ca brahmadarśanaphalatvenoktasyāvidyānivṛttyuttarabhāvitvenāvidyakatvāsambhavāditi bhāvaḥ |
evamātmabhedaṃ prasādhyānātmabhedamapi vyavasthāpayati -- ghaṭapaṭādīti || sulabhameveti || dharmipratiyoginoḥ pratyakṣatvenoktaśaṅkānavakāśāditi bhāvaḥ |
atīndriyabhede tu pūrvavadīśvarapratyakṣamudāhartavyam |
na ca pratyakṣasya vyāvahārikaviṣayakatvam |
mānābhāvāt |
asyaivārthasya vyāvahārikatvāvyāvahārikatvayorvyāghātācca |

sattvenaiva bhedasya pratīyamānatvāt |
tasya ca bādhābhāvena paramārthatvādityanyatra vistaraḥ |
prakārāntareṇāpi tatprāptiṃ darśayati -- yadveti || tattvamasītiśrutestātparyaviṣayībhūto'rthaḥ `tadguṇasāratvattādvyapadeśa' iti brahmasūtroktarītyā siṃho devadatta ityādivattatsadṛśo'sīti tatvanirṇayādyuktarītyā atatvamasīti padacchedena tadbhinno'sīti ca |
tadaparijñānadaśāyāmaikyamarthatayā pratīyate tadanuvadati neha nāneti vākyamityarthaḥ |
śrutyarthāparijñānena prāptaṃ śrutyantareṇa niṣidhyata ityetadadṛṣṭacaramityata āha -- astīti || `darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajete'ti śrutitātparyārthaḥ svargapadavācyeśvaraviṣayakaṃ niṣkāmaṃ karma kāryamiti |
tadaparijñānadaśāyāṃ prāptamanīśvaraviṣayaṃ kāmyakarma `iṣṭāpūrtaṃ manyamānā variṣṭhaṃ nānyacchreyo vedayante pramūḍhā' ityanūdya `nākasya pṛṣṭhe sukṛte te'nubhūtvā imaṃ lokaṃ hīnataraṃ vāviśantī'ti nirākriyate |
nindāyāḥ pratiṣedhe tātparyāt |
nacaivaṃvidhasya karmaṇo'nyataḥ prāptirastīti bhāvaḥ |
iṣṭaṃ yajanam |
pūrtam annadānādikam |
prakārāntareṇādvaitaprāptiṃ darśayati -- athaveti || nanu anādinā vedenā''dhunikavādikṛtaśāstraparamparayā prāptādvaitaniṣedhaḥ kathaṃ kriyata ityata uktamanādirūpayeti || svarūpānāditvāyogāduktaṃ pravāhata iti || yathoktam |
`anādikālato vṛttāssamayā hi pravāhata' iti |
duḥśāstraparamparayā prāptasya śrutyā niṣedho na dṛṣṭa ityata āha -- asticeti || tadeva darśayati -- taddheti || tat tatra jagatkāraṇatve nirūpaṇīye |
eke śūnyavādinaḥ |
āhuḥ ha prasiddham |
kimāhuḥ |
`asadevedamagra āsīt' atrāsacchabdena śūnyamucyate |
svakāryaṃ prati anyannāstītyeke manyante |
śūnyātirekeṇa śūnyasya sahakāriṇaṃ dvitīyaṃ pratiṣedhati || advitīyamiti || vipratipattyantaramāha -- tasmāditi || tasmādasataḥ sajjagadutpadyata iti || evamanūditaṃ pakṣaṃ dūṣayanti || kuta iti || tuśabdaḥ pūrvapakṣavyāvṛttyarthaḥ |
he somya kathamevamasadāsīditi kuta evaṃ syāt na kuto'pi |
vyāhateḥ pramāṇābhāvācceti hovāca |

adṛṣṭatvādasataḥ kāraṇatvānupapatterityarthaḥ |
ityādau niṣedho'stīti sambandhaḥ |
atra keṣāṃ matena prāptaṃ kiṃ niṣidhyata iti sphuṭamapratīterāha -- atreti || bauddheti || bauddhaikadeśimādhyamiketyarthaḥ |
upasaṃharati -- tasmāditi || uktarītyā vyāvahārikatvābhāvādanuvādakatvābhāvāccetyarthaḥ |
udāhṛtabhedaśrutervyāvahārikabhedaparatvaśaṅkānirāsaprayāsagauravamiti manvāno vyāvahārikabhedaparatvaśaṅkānāspadabhedaśrutīrudāharati -- paramamityāditā || yadā paśya iti śruterayamarthaḥ |
paśyatīti paśyo jīvaḥ |
suvarṇavarṇaṃ jagatkartāraṃ tanniyāmakaṃ caturmukhotpādakaṃ paramapuruṣaṃ yadā sākṣātkaroti tadā'parokṣajñānī sa jīvo'niṣṭaṃ puṇyaṃ pāpaṃ ca vināśya nirlepassan svayogyajñānānandādinā paramapuruṣasya sāmyaṃ yātīti |
`yo vede'ti śruteśca yo'dhikārī hṛdayaguhāyāṃ parame vyomni nihitaṃ brahma veda sa vipaścitā brahmaṇā sākaṃ svayogyānsarvānkāmānaśnuta ityarthaḥ |
idaṃ jñānamiti gītāvākyasya ca idaṃ pūrvoktajñānaṃ samāśritya jñānānandādinā mama samānadharmatvaṃ prāptāḥ sṛṣṭau na jāyante pralaye na vyathantītyarthaḥ |
atroktasāmyasahabhāvasādharmyāṇāṃ bhedena vinā'yogāttatsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ || muktyanantaramiti || puṇyapāpavidhūnanasya sarvaviṣayabhogasya sṛṣṭāvajananādeśca saṃsāre'bhāvānmuktyanantaraṃ bhedapratipādakatvam |
avidyānivṛttirūpamuktāvanuvṛttasya cāvidyātatkāryānyataratvavyāptavyāvahārikatvaṃ tvayā nāṅgīkṛtamiti bhāvaḥ |
anumānaṃ ca ghaṭaḥ paṭapratiyogikatāttvikatādātmyādhikaraṇaṃ na bhavati |
pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinnapaṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogidharmādhikaraṇatvāt vyatirekeṇa paṭavat |
aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ cātmabhedasādhakānumāna iva parihartavyam |
vyāvahārikabhedamātreṇa pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāvaśca ghaṭapaṭayorudāharaṇīyaḥ (upapādanīyaḥ)|
|| anumānaṃ ceti || pramāṇamiti śeṣaḥ || pratiyogīti || pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakenānavacchinno yaḥ paṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvastatpratiyogibhūtaghaṭatvarūpadharmādhikaraṇatvādityarthaḥ |
atra pratiyogipadaṃ tatsamānapradhānaparam |
atyantābhāvapratiyogino'sattvasya vyavasthāpitatvena ghaṭasya tadadhikaraṇatvāyogādityarthaḥ || vyatirekeṇa paṭavaditi || paṭe paṭatādātmyasya ghaṭatvānadhikaraṇatvasya ca sattvena sādhyasādhanayorabhāvāditi bhāvaḥ || aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ ceti || yadyapi māyāvādinā prapañcamithyātvaṃ vadatā ghaṭapaṭayoratāttvikatādātmyaṃ nāṅgīkriyata ityaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaśaṅkāprasaro nāstyeva |
tathā'pi jīvabrahmaṇoraikyaṃ sarvapramāṇaviruddhamaṅgīkurvata idamapi sambhāvitamiti tanmatasyātīvaheyatājñāpanāyeyaṃśaṅketi driṣyavyam || ātmabhedasādhakānumāna iveti || ghaṭaḥ paṭapratiyogikatāttvikatādātmyāpannaṃ na veti saṃśayāsambhave'pi paṭapratiyogikatādātmyaṃ ghaṭe'sti na veti sandehamātreṇa ghaṭasya pakṣatvopapattyā hetoḥ pakṣadharmatopapatteriti vā kevalavyatirekiṇi sādhyābhāvasya sādhanābhāvena vyāptigrahādatra sādhyābhāvasya paṭatādātmyasya paṭe prasiddhatvena vyāptivighaṭakatvābhāvāditi vā pariharaṇīyamityarthaḥ |
nanu ghaṭapaṭayorbhedasiddheḥ prāk kathaṃ pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāva ityata āha -- vyāvahārikabhedeti || anyathā tava kutrāpi pāramārthikabhedābhāvena pakṣadṛṣṭāntaghaṭitānumānaprayoga eva kvacidapi na syāditi bhāvaḥ |
atra vyāvahāriketi parāṅgīkārasūcanārtham |
vastutastu bhedamātreṇaivetyetāvadevopayuktam |
tena vyāvahārikabhedasya bhavadbhiranaṅgīkārātkathaṃ taduktiriti śaṅkānavakāśa iti dhyeyam |
|| ghaṭa iti || vyāvahārikasattātādātmyānaṅgīkāre siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya tāttviketi || pratiyogīti |
pratiyoginā sāmānādhikaraṇyasyāvacchedako yo dharmo niṣprakārakacaitanyāparokṣotpattiparyantaṃ svādhikaraṇe svapratiyogipratītyavirodhitvarūpo dharmastadanavacchinnastadrahito yaḥ paṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvastatpratiyogikambugrīvādidharmādhikaraṇatvādityarthaḥ |
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ |
pareṇa hi sarvamithyātvavādinā ghaṭatvādīnāṃ paṭādāviva svādhikaraṇaghaṭādāvapyabhāvo'ṅgīkriyata eva |
parantu ghaṭatvasya paṭe yo'tyantābhāvaḥ sa tatpratiyogikasamānādhikaraṇaḥ |
ataḥ svādhikaraṇe pratiyogipratītiṃ niruṇaddhi |
paṭe tu pratiyogisamānādhikaraṇaḥ |
ata eva ghaṭe ghaṭatvābhāvasattve'pi ghaṭatvapratītyavirodhaḥ |
iyaṃ pratiyogipratītirniṣprakārakabrahmāparokṣaparyantameva |
utpanne tu tasminnayamapi pratiyogivirodhyeva bhavati |
evañca pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedako yo dharmaḥ svapratiyogipratītyavirodhitvarūpastadrahito yo'tyantābhāvastatpratiyogighaṭatvādidharmādhikaraṇatvaṃ heturiti || paṭaniṣṭhetyevokte paṭa eva vyabhicāraḥ |
pareṇa svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvaṃ mithyātvamityaṅgīkurvatā paṭatvāderapi paṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvāṅgīkārāt |
ataḥ pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinnetyuktam |
paṭe paṭatvātyantābhāvastu svādhikaraṇapratītyavirodhitvarūpoktadharmāvacchinna eva |
ghaṭatvātyantābhāvastu na tathā |
na ca ghaṭe paṭatvādipratītirapi bhramarūpā sambhavatīti tasyāpi pratiyogisāmānadhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinnatvaṃ nāstyeva |
na ca pratītiḥ pramākārā vivakṣiteti ghaṭe ghaṭatvapratītiḥ pramātvānaṅgīkārāditi vācyam |
pratīterbrajñānetarajñānābādhyāyā eva vivakṣitatvāt |
pratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇetyevokte paṭa eva vyabhicāraḥ |
paṭatvātyantābhāvasya paṭa eva pratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇatvasya vaktuṃ śakyatvāt |
tathā hi |
pratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇatvaṃ hi pratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatvābhāvaḥ |
pratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatvaṃ pratyabhāvo'nya eva |
evañca pratiyogisamānādhikaraṇasyāpyabhāvasya pratiyogisamānādhikaraṇāntaragatapratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatvarūpadharmarāhityaṃ yujyata eva |
ataḥ pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinnetyuktam |
evañcābhāvāntataragatapratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatvasyābhāve'pi pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakatvaṃ nāstīti na vyabhicāraḥ |
tatra pratiyogipadaṃ pratiyogitāvacchedakānavacchinnaparam |
tena pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyasyāpi pratiyogiviśeṣasāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinnatvasambhavādvyabhicāra iti nirastam |
yadvā paṭaniṣṭhasaṃyogasya svātyantābhāvasāmānādhikaraṇatvena tadvati paṭe vyabhicāravāraṇāya pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinneti viśeṣaṇam |
nanu ghaṭapaṭordharmisattāsamānasattākabhedaḥ pareṇāṅgīkriyata eveti kiṃ tatsādhanena |
na ca pāramārthyasādhanārthaṃ yatnaḥ |
dharmiṇāmeva tasya sādhyatvāt |
ekādhiṣṭhānādhyastatvameva jaḍānāmaikyamiti tanmatam |
na ca bhedasādhanaprasaṅgācchiṣyaṃ pratīdamucyata ityuttaraṃ māyāvādirītyaiva granthacalanācchiṣyaṃ prati siddhasādhanatvānneti cenna |
ghaṭaḥ paṭatādātmyādhikaraṇaṃ na bhavatītyasya paṭāropādhiṣṭhānādhyasto na bhavatītyarthakatvāt |
hetuvākyasyāpi paṭadharmātyantavilakṣaṇadharmādhikaraṇatvāditi niṣkṛṣṭārthaḥ |
atyantavilakṣaṇayorekādhiṣṭhāne āropāsambhavāditi bhāvaḥ |
yadvā vācārambhaṇamityādyarthakatvena yajjaḍaikyaṃ tenāṅgīkṛtaṃ tadabhāva evātra sādhyata ityadoṣaḥ |
nanu sarvapadārthānāmaikyāṅgīkārādaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatetyata āha -- aprasiddheti || vyāptyādyavighaṭakatveneti śeṣaḥ |
etadanumānātpūrvaṃ jaḍabhedāsiddheḥ pakṣadṛṣṭāntānupapattirityata āha -- vyāvahāriketi || asatpratiyogikavailakṣaṇyena bhedasya sādhanasādhanāṅgatvam |
anyathā'yaṃ paṭa etattantuniṣṭha ityādāvapi samānamiti bhāvaḥ |
|| anumānaṃ ceti || ghaṭapaṭabhede pramāṇamiti śeṣaḥ |
abhāvasya sādhyatvānna sādhyāprasiddhirdoṣa ityāha -- aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ ceti || pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakenāvacchinnaḥ paṭaniṣṭho yo'tyantābhāvastatpratiyogī yo dharmo ghaṭatvādistadadhikaraṇatvaṃ ghaṭatvasya tadā yadi ghaṭatvādirghaṭavṛttidharmaḥ paṭe na syāt |
tattadapi tadā yadi tayoḥ pāramārthiko bhedastvadyāpyasiddhaḥ |
|| pratiyogītyādi |
pratiyogipadaṃ svapratiyogiparam |
anyathā tasyāvyāvartakatāpatteḥ |
anavacchinnāntamatyantābhāvaviśeṣaṇam |
sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ kiñcinniṣṭhaṃ grāhyam |
na tu hetuśarīraniviṣṭātyantābhāvaniṣṭham |
svamate sarvatra nyāyamate ca vyāpyavṛttyabhāve prasiddheḥ |
tasya sāmānādhikaraṇyasya yo'avacchedaka ubhayādhikaraṇyaṃ, ghaṭe gaganābhāve ghaṭatvasāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ na paṭa iti pratīteḥ |
gaganābhāvaniṣṭhaghaṭatvasāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ ghaṭāvacchedyaṃ na paṭāvacchedyamiti tadabhilāpavākyārthaḥ |
paṭe gaganābhāve na ghaṭatvasāmānādhikaraṇyamityatra tu gaganābhāvaniṣṭhaghaṭatvasāmānādhikaraṇyābhāvaḥ paṭāvacchedya iti idaṃ sārvabhaumenāṅgīkṛtam |
pratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇayatsamānādhikaraṇetyādisiddhāntalakṣaṇe maṇikṛtāṅgīkṛtatvāt |
ayaṃ cātra granthastadanusāreṇa pravṛttaḥ |
ghaṭatvātyantābhāvapratiyogighaṭatvasāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ yadgagābhāvaniṣṭhaṃ tadavacchedakaḥ paṭo na, kintu ghaṭa eva |
tadanavacchinnapaṭaniṣṭhetyatra paṭavṛttitvaviśiṣṭe'bhāve'navacchinnatvasya viśeṣaṇatve phalato paṭa[ghaṭa]nirūpitādheyatāyāṃ ghaṭatvā[paṭatvā]bhāvaniṣṭhāyāṃ paṭatvasāmānādhikaraṇyarūpāyāṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ paryavasyati |
ghaṭatvābhāve tasyāvyāvartakatvāt |
tathā ca svapratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinnapaṭanirūpitādheyatāvadabhāva ityarthaḥ |
tādṛśaśca ghaṭatvātyantābhāvastatpratiyogighaṭatvarūpadharmavattvaṃ ghaṭe [hetau] vartata ityarthaḥ |
asya prayojanamuttaragranthe sphuṭībhaviṣyati |
ghaṭapaṭasaṃyogādikamavyāpyavṛttyabhāvapratiyoginamādāya vyabhicāravāraṇāya cedam |
ghaṭapaṭasaṃyogātyantābhāvasya ghaṭa[paṭa]niṣṭhatve'pi tanniṣṭhasaṃyogarūpapratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ prati paṭasyāvacchedakatvāttanniṣṭhatadatyantābhāvamupādāya paṭādau vyabhicāraḥ |
sāmānādhikaraṇyasyāvyāpyavṛttitvānaṅgīkartṛdīdhitikṛdrītyā tu pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyetyādeḥ pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyānirūpakanirūpitapaṭanirūpitādheyatetyarthaḥ |
kiñcinniṣṭhapratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyanirūpakatvaṃ pratiyogyadhikaraṇasyaivetyuktaviśeṣaṇena svapratiyogyanadhikaraṇapaṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogidharmavattvāditi hetuḥ paryavasyati |
pratiyogivaiyadhikaraṇye pratiyogyadhikaraṇatā hetutāvacchedakasambandhāvacchinnā bodhyā |
prayojanaṃ svayamūhyam |
kecittu pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyarūpaṃ yadavacchedakadharmastadanavacchinnastadaviśiṣṭaḥ sanpaṭaniṣṭho yo'bhāva ityarthaḥ |
pratiyogisamānādhikaraṇānyasyābhāvaviśeṣaṇatve vakṣyamāṇanyāyena paṭatvābhāvāderatathātvādevamuktam |
uktavākyasyāpi pratiyoyogyanadhikaraṇapaṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvetyādyuktahetau paryavasānamata idamapi kathamiti śaṅkānavakāśa ityāhuḥ |
atraivānumānānyāhaḥ || anumānaṃ ceti || paṭetyādeḥ paṭapratiyogikatādātmyādhikaraṇatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedavānityarthaḥ |
tāttvikatādātmyeti pāṭhe paṭatvaviśiṣṭatādātmyāvacchinnabhedamādāya siddhasādhanavāraṇāya tāttvikapadaṃ tadupalakṣitatādātmyaniveśalābhārtham |
tadavacchinnavyāvahārikabhedasiddhyā'rthāntaratvaṃ tvātmabhedasādhakānumānarītyā pariharaṇīyam || pratiyogīti || avyāpyavṛttisaṃyogādikaṃ vyāpyavṛttipaṭatvādikaṃ cādāya paṭe vyabhicāravāraṇāyānavacchinnāntaniṣṭhāntayorabhāvaviśeṣaṇatvena niveśaḥ |
na ca pratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇatvaviśeṣaṇenaiva saṃyogābhāvādivyāvṛtteranavacchinnāntavyāvṛttyabhāva iti vācyam |
guṇādau pratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇasyaiva saṃyogābhāvasya paṭaniṣṭhatvena tadavyāvṛtteḥ |
anavacchinnāntaniveśe ca pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakaṃ yatsaṃyogābhāvatvaṃ tadanavacchinnatvābhāvena tadvyāvṛtteḥ |
na caivaṃ ghaṭatvābhāvāderapi ghaṭatvādirūpapratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakaprameyatvādyavacchinnatvādasiddhiriti vācyam |
svanirūpakapratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakaṃ yadyattadanavacchinnatvasya vivakṣitatvāt, prameyatvādinirūpakapratiyogyaprasiddhyā svapadena tadupādānāyogāt |
saṃyogabhāvatvādereva ca tathātvāt |
nanu sarvasyaiva paṭatādātmyādhikaraṇatvāt tadavacchinnabhedarūpasādhyāprasiddhirata āha -- aprasiddheti || parihartavyamiti || prakṛtānumityavirodhitvena jñeyamityarthaḥ |
abhāvasādhyakavyatirekyanumāne tadadoṣatvasyopapāditatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
evamātmabhede pramāṇatrayamabhidhāyānātmabhede'pi tadupapādayan pratyakṣaṃ tāvadāha -- ghaṭapaṭādīti || pratyakṣaṃ ghaṭaḥ paṭo na bhavatītyādyaṃ sulabhameva nānumānavatprayāsenopapādanīyam |
nacāsya pratyakṣasya neha nāneti śrutibādhitatvādaprāmāṇyamiti vācyam |
tasyāḥ śruteśca brahmaṇi guṇakarmādibhedaniṣedhakatvena ghaṭapaṭabhedabādhakatvābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ || pratiyogīti || ghaṭe ghaṭatvātyantābhāvasya ghaṭatvarūpapratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatvābhāvena pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāprasiddhyā tadavacchedakasyāprasiddhatvena tadanavacchinnaḥ paṭaniṣṭho yo'tyantābhāvaḥ ghaṭatvātyantābhāvaḥ tatpratiyogidharmo ghaṭatvaṃ tadadhikaraṇatvādityarthaḥ |
pratiyogītyādiviśeṣaṇakṛtyaṃ svayameva vakṣyati -- paṭavaditi || tatra hi paṭapratiyogikatādātmyādhikaraṇatvābhāvābhāvaḥ paṭapratiyogikatādātmyādhikaraṇatve vartate |
tādṛśaghaṭatvarūpadharmādhikaraṇatvābhāvaśca vartata ityarthaḥ |
nanu mayā paṭapratiyogikatādātmyādhikaraṇatvasyaivāṅgīkārāttadabhāvarūpasādhyasyāprasiddhatvenāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvamityata āha -- aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ ceti || yathā jīvo brahmapratiyogikatādātmyādhikaraṇaṃ na bhavatītyatrābhāvasya sādhyatvānnāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvasya doṣatvaṃ tathehāpyabhāvasya sādhyatvādvyāptyādivighaṭakatvābhāvānnāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ dūṣaṇamityarthaḥ |
nanu ghaṭapaṭayorbhedasiddheḥ pūrvaṃ kathaṃ tayoḥ pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāva ityata āha -- vyāvahārikabhedamātreṇeti || ātmabhedasādhakānumānavaditi vartate |
tathāca pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāve pāramārthikabhedasya prayojakatve tvayā tasya kutrāpyanaṅgīkāreṇānumānamātrocchedāpattyā bhedamātrasya prayojakatve vācye tasya ghaṭapaṭayorapi vidyamānatvāttayoḥ pakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāvopapattirityarthaḥ |
nanu tathā'pi ghaṭapaṭayorapāramārthikabhedaṃ vadatā mayā ghaṭatvāderapi paṭaniṣṭhatvāṅgīkāreṇa paṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogikadharmādhikaraṇatvaṃ ghaṭasya nāstītyasiddhiriti cenna |
(kiṃ) paṭe ghaṭatvaṃ tathyamatathyaṃ (mithyā) vā |
nādyaḥ |
apasiddhāntāt |
dvitīye tu kathaṃ paṭe ghaṭatvātyantābhāvānaṅgīkaraṇam |
`sarveṣāmeva bhāvānāṃ svāśrayatvena sammate |
pratiyogitvamatyantābhāvaṃ prati mṛṣātmate'ti vacanāt tvayā svasamānādhikaraṇātyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ mithyātvamityaṅgīkārāt |
paṭasya ghaṭatvaṃ pratyāśrayatvameva nāstīti tatra tadatyantābhāvābhāve'pi tatra tasya mithyātvaṃ na virūdhyata iti cet tarhi paṭe ghaṭatvaṃ pratyanāśraye (pratyanāśrayatve) ghaṭatvātyantābhāvādhikaraṇatvaṃ suprasiddhamiti kathamasiddhiḥ |
nanu ghaṭapaṭayoraikyavāde paṭatvasyāpi ghaṭe sattvena paṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvābhāvāddhaṭatvasya tadanadhikaraṇatvaṃ ghaṭe'siddhamiti śaṅkate -- nanviti || athā'pyaprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvābhāve'pi kathañcitpakṣadṛṣṭāntabhāvopapattāvapītyarthaḥ |
paṭe ghaṭatvātyatābhāvamaṅgīkārayituṃ vikalpena pṛcchati -- paṭa iti || sarveṣāmiti || sarveṣāmapi bhāvānāṃ ghaṭādipadārthānāṃ svāśrayatvena sammate bhūtalādāvatyantābhāvaṃ prati pratiyogitvameva mṛṣātmatā mithyātvamiti vacanārthaḥ |
tathā ca ghaṭe paṭatvamatathyaṃ cettanniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ ghaṭatvasya durvāramiti kathamasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
nanvata eva svāśrayatvena sammata ityuktam |
tathā ca paṭasya ghaṭatvāśrayatvābhāvāttadatyantābhāvasya paṭe'naṅgīkāre'pi na ghaṭatvasya tathyatvāpattiḥ |
svāśrayatvena sammate ghaṭe svātyantābhāvasāmānādhikaraṇyasya sattvāttāvanmātreṇaiva mithyātvopapatteriti bhāvena śaṅkate -- paṭasyeti || evaṃ tarhi na sutarāmasiddheravakāśa iti bhāvena pariharati -- tarhīti |
tasyaivārthaḥ paṭasya ghaṭatvaṃ pratyanāśrayatva iti || suprasiddhamitīti |
ghaṭānāśrayasya bhūtalasya ghaṭātyantābhāvādhikaraṇatānaiyatyasya sarvasammatatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
viśeṣaṇāsiddhimāśaṅkya samādhatte || nanviti || apāramārthikabhedāṅgīkārādityupalakṣaṇam |
vāstavatādātmyāṅgīkārādityapi draṣṭavyam |
ghaṭatvāderityādipadena pṛthubudhnodarākāratvādergrahaṇam |
ghaṭapaṭayoraikye ghaṭasyaiva paṭatvena ghaṭatvādeḥ paṭāvṛttitvaṃ nāstītyarthaḥ |
uktarītyā ghaṭatvādeḥ paṭatve paṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ vācyam |
yāvatsvādhikaraṇaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvarūpamithyātvāṅgīkārāt |
evañca tanmātramevāstu hetuviśeṣaṇamiti bhāvena pariharati -- paṭe ghaṭatvamiti || kathamiti || tasyaiva mithyāśabdārthatvāṅgīkārāt |
tatra parasammatimāha -- pareṣāmiti || bhāvānāṃ svāśrayatvena sammate vastunyevādhikaraṇe'tyantābhāvaṃ prati pratiyogitvaṃ mṛṣātmeti sambandhaḥ |
syādetat |
yadi ghaṭo'paṭatvaṃ pratyāśrayaḥ syāttadā mithyātvaghaṭanāya tadatyantābhāvo'pi tatrāṅgīkāryaḥ syāt |
na caivam |
pūrvamasiddhicodanaṃ tu paṭasya paṭatvaviśeṣāghaṭatvaṃ pratyāśrayatvābhāve'pi ghaṭapaṭayostādātmyamāśrityetyata āha -- paṭasya ghaṭatvaṃ pratīti || suprasiddhamitīti |
tadatyantābhāvasyaiva tadanyāśrayatvaśabdārthatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
yaduktaṃ paṭatvaviśeṣāśrayatvaṃ nāstīti |
tanna |
paṭasyaiva svabhāvatvena tadvinā paṭasya vāstavaveṣāntarābhāvāt |
na hi cetaneṣviva jaḍeṣu bhāgatyāgenaikyamucyata iti |
yastvapāramārthiko bhedo mayā'pyaṅgīkṛtaḥ sa na parasparadharmasaṅkrāntinivartaka ityato'siddhirityata āha -- nanvathā'pīti |
aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvasya doṣatvābhāve'pītyarthaḥ |
parasparadharmasaṅkrāntau pakṣadṛṣṭāntābhāve'pi durupapādanīya eveti na tadaṅgīkāro'thā'pītyarthaḥ || asiddhiriti || hetvasiddhirityarthaḥ |
anayaiva rītyā sarveṣāṃ sarvadharmavattvena paṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogidharmādhikaraṇatvasyāprasiddheḥ |
dvitīye tvityādi |
paṭe ghaṭatvasya mithyātve tvayā svasamānādhikaraṇātyantābhāvapratiyogikatvaṃ mithyātvamityaṅgīkārāt |
paṭe ghaṭātyantābhāvo'vaśyamaṅgīkāryaḥ |
tathā ca nāsiddhiḥ |
yadi ca ghaṭatvasyādhikaraṇaṃ ghaṭa eva tanniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvenaiva tasya mithyātvamityucyate |
tadā sutarāṃ ghaṭatvātyantābhāvaḥ paṭa iti samudāyārthaḥ |
|| tathyamiti || atredaṃ pāramārthikamiti prāmāṇikavyavahāragocarībhūtaṃ na vetyarthaḥ || mṛṣātmatetīti |
mṛṣātvaṃ matamityarthaḥ |
mṛṣātmeti pāṭhaḥ sādhuḥ || suprasiddhamiti || ghaṭatvaṃ nāstīti tvadīyavyavahārabalādāgatasya kathamanaṅgīkāra ityarthaḥ || asiddhiriti || svapadena ghaṭatvātyantābhāvasya grahaṇe svapratiyogibhūtaghaṭatvāśrayāprasiddhyā taddhaṭitasādhanāprasiddhiḥ |
atyantābhāvasyāsatpratiyogikatvāduktāvacchinnatvābhāvaghaṭitasādhanaprasiddhyaṅgīkāre tādṛśadharmavattvaṃ paṭe nāstīti svarūpāsiddhirityarthaḥ ||
paṭaniṣṭhatvāṅgīkāreṇeti || ghaṭapaṭayoḥ svarūpaikyāditi bhāvaḥ |
yadyapyevaṃ paṭe ghaṭavyavahārāpattiriti vakṣyamāṇadūṣaṇamatra śakyate vaktum |
tathāpi paṭe ghaṭatvātyantābhāvasyaiva prakṛtopayuktatvāttameva tāvadaṅgīkārayati -- paṭa iti || aṅgīkriyamāṇamiti 4śeṣaḥ |
nanu paṭe ghaṭatvasya mithyātve'pi tatra tadatyantābhāvāṅgīkāraḥ kuta ityata āha -- sarveṣāmiti || ghaṭādīnāṃ tattvataḥ svādhikaraṇāprasiddheḥ svādhikaraṇatvena sammata ityuktam |
svaprakārakadhīviśeṣye yāvatītyarthaḥ |
iti vadatā tvayeti sambandhaḥ |
tathāca ghaṭatvāderetādṛśamithyātvānurodhādeva paṭādau tadatyantābhāvāṅgīkāra iti bhāvaḥ |
nanu svāśrayaniṣṭhābhāvapratiyogitvameva mithyātvam |
svāśrayatvaṃ ca na vāstavam |
nāpi prātibhāsikasādhāraṇam |
yena virodhaḥ siddhārthatā vā syāt |
api tu vyāvahārikamevetyāśayena śaṅkate -- paṭasyeti || suprasiddhameveti || parasparaviruddhayoranyataraniṣedhasyānyataravidhivyāptatvaniyamāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu ghaṭapaṭayorekatvena ghaṭatvaṃ yathā ghaṭe vartate tathā paṭe'pyasti |
evaṃ ca paṭe ghaṭatvātyantābhāvo nāstīti ghaṭatvasya paṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitvābhāvena tadāśrayasya paṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogidharmādhikaraṇatvābhāvādasiddhiriti śaṅkate -- nanviti || aprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvābhāve'pi svarūpāsiddhirastītyarthaḥ |
tenaiva paṭe ghaṭatvātyantābhāvamaṅgīkārayituṃ vikalpena pṛcchati -- paṭa iti || apasiddhāntāditi || ghaṭatvasyāpi satyatvāṅgīkāre brahmaṇa ekasyaiva satyatvamiti siddhāntavirodhādityarthaḥ |
kuta ityata āha -- svasamānādhikaraṇeti || svaṃ mithyātvenābhimatam |
tatsamānādhikaraṇo yo'tyantābhāvaḥ tatpratiyogitvamityarthaḥ |
yathā śuktirūpyaṃ mithyā |
tatredaṃ lakṣaṇamasti |
svaṃ śuktirūpyam |
tatsamānādhikaraṇaḥ tena sahaikādhikaraṇe śuktiśakale vidyamāno yo'tyantābhāvaḥ rūpyātyantābhāvaḥ |
tatpratiyogitvamasti rūpyasya |
evaṃ paṭaniṣṭhaghaṭatvasyāpi mithyātvam |
svaṃ ghaṭatvam |
tatsamānādhikaraṇaḥ tena sahaikādhikaraṇe vidyamāno yo'tyantābhāvaḥ ghaṭatvātyantābhāvaḥ tatpratiyogitvaṃ ghaṭatvasyāṅgīkāryamiti kathaṃ ghaṭatvātyantābhāvaḥ paṭe na syādityarthaḥ |
aṅgīkāraśca tadvākyādeva jñāyate ityāśayenoktam || sarveṣāmeveti || asyārthaḥ sarveṣāṃ bhāvānāṃ padārthānāṃ svāśrayatvena sammate vidyamānātyantābhāvapratiyogitvarūpaṃ mithyātvaṃ tadā virudhyeta yadi ghaṭatvaṃ pratyāśrayatvaṃ paṭasya sammataṃ syāt |
nacaivamiti śaṅkate -- paṭasyeti || tatra paṭe |
tat ghaṭatvam |
tatra paṭe |
tasya ghaṭatvasya || suprasiddhamiti || paṭasya ghaṭatvaṃ pratyāśrayatvaṃ netyukte tadabhāvaṃ pratyāśrayatvasya parasparaviruddhayoriti nyāyaprāptatvāditi bhāvaḥ || kathamasiddhiriti || paṭasya ghaṭatvātyantābhāvādhikaraṇatve ghaṭasya paṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogidharmādhikaraṇatvasadbhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu tarhi ghaṭe ghaṭatvasya mithyātvena ghaṭasyāpi ghaṭatvaṃ pratyāśrayatvaṃ nāstītyasiddhiriti cenna |
ghaṭasya ghaṭatvātyantābhāvaṃ pratyāśrayasyāpi ghaṭatvāśrayatvasyāpyaṅgīkāryatvāt |
anyathā ghaṭatvasya niradhikaraṇatvena svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvarūpaṃ mithyātvaṃ ghaṭatvāderna syāt |
nanu tathā'pi paṭe vyabhicāraḥ |
paṭe sādhyābhāvasadbhāve'pi sādhanābhāvābhāvāt |
paṭe paṭatvasya (paṭatvasyāpi) mithyātvena paṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogipaṭatvaṃ prati paṭasyāśrayatvāditi cedata eva heturviśeṣitaḥ pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinneti || paṭe paṭatvātyantābhāve satyapi tasya paṭatvarūpapratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatvena pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakāvacchinnatayā sādhanābhāvasadbhāvāt |
nanu tarhyasiddhiḥ |
ghaṭapaṭayorabhedaṃ vadatā paṭe ghaṭatvasyāṅgīkāreṇa paṭe'pi ghaṭatvātyantābhāvasya pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāditi cenna |
tvayā'pi ghaṭapaṭayorapāramārthikabhedaṃ vadatā paṭe ghaṭavyavahāraprasaṅgavirodhaparihārāya vyāvahārikabhedamātreṇa bimbapratibimbasattvāsattvavyavasthāvatpaṭe ghaṭatvātyantābhāvasyāṅgīkāryatvāt |
ghaṭatvasya sārvatrikatve'pyavidyāvaśātpratiniyato vyavahāra iti cen (na) |
na hyavidyā''ropitarajatābhāve'pi śuktyādau rajatamiti vyavahāraṃ janayati |
tathātve ghaṭe'pi taṃ janayet |
ghaṭapaṭayostādātmyasya pāramārthikatvāṅgīkāre tu jībrahmaṇoriva ghaṭapaṭayoḥ svarūpasyāpi pāramārthikatvaṃ syāditi prapañcamithyātvavācoyuktirayuktā syādityanukūlatarko draṣṭavyaḥ |
nanvevaṃ sati panurasiddhirdurvāraiva |
ghaṭasyāpi ghaṭatvādhikaraṇatvābhāvāt |
ghaṭatvasya ghaṭe'pi manmate mithyātvāṅgīkārāt |
atyantābhāvapratiyogitvasyaiva mithyātvarūpatvāt |
tathā ca yadā ghaṭe ghaṭatvasyātyantābhāvastadā dhaṭatvaṃ pratyanāśrayatvameva tasya prāptamiti pratibandīmāśritya śaṅkate -- nanu tarhīti |
atyantābhāvādhikaraṇasya pratiyogyanāśrayatvāṅgīkāra ityarthaḥ |
ghaṭatvātyantābhāvavati paṭe ghaṭatvaṃ pratyanāśrayatvavadghaṭatvātyantābhāvavati ghaṭe'pi tadanāśrayatvaṃ tvayā nāṅgīkartuṃ śakyam |
tathā sati ghaṭatvasya svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvarūpamithyātvābhāvāpātāt |
tathā ca ghaṭasya ghaṭatvāśrayatāyā avaśyāṅgīkāryatvena kathamaprasiddhiriti bhāvena pratibandīṃ pariharati -- neti || ghaṭatvasya niradhikaraṇatveneti || paṭasya tu ghaṭatvaṃ pratyāśrayatvaṃ nāstyeva |
tadvaddhaṭe'pi ghaṭatvānaṅgīkāre ghaṭatvāderniradhikaraṇatvameva prasajyata ityarthaḥ |
pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinneti viśeṣaṇakṛtyaṃ vaktumākṣipati -- nanviti || tathā'pīti |
asiddhiparihāre'pītyarthaḥ || paṭe vyabhicāra iti || vyatirekā dṛṣṭāntabhūte paṭe vyatirekavyāptervyabhicāra ityarthaḥ |
tamevopapādayati -- paṭa iti || sādhyābhāvasadbhāve'pīti |
paṭapratiyogikatāttvikatādātmyasadbhāve'pītyarthaḥ || sādhanābhāvābhāvāditi || sādhanasyaiva sattvādityarthaḥ |
tamevopapādayati -- paṭa iti || nanvatyantābhāvādhikaraṇe kathaṃ pratiyogino'vakāśa ityata uktaṃ paṭe paṭasya mithyātveneti || svasamānāśrayātyantābhāvapratiyogitvasyaiva mithyātvatayā manmate'ṅgīkārādityarthaḥ || ata eveti || uktavyabhicāraparihārārthamevetyarthaḥ || heturiti || hetvantargato'tyantābhāva ityarthaḥ || pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyeti || pratiyoginā paṭatvena yatsāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ tadavacchedakaṃ yatpaṭīyapaṭatvātyantābhāvatvaṃ tadavacchinnatayā'navacchinnatā'bhāvastadgarbhitasādhanābhāvādityarthaḥ |
yadyapyatra pratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇetyeva paṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvo viśeṣṭuṃ śakyastāvataiva paṭatvātyantābhāvavyāvṛttisiddheḥ |
tathā'pi ghaṭatvātyantābhāvasyāpi ghaṭe pratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatvātpratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇatvasyābhāvāttatpratiyogidharmo'prasiddha eveti punarasiddhiśaṅkā syāttannirāsāya pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinnetyuktiḥ |
evamukte tu nāsiddhiḥ |
ghaṭatvātyantābhāvasya sarvatraikatve'pi prakṛte paṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapadena na sāmānyarūpo'bhāvo vivakṣitaḥ |
kintu paṭaniṣṭhatvāvavacchinnātyantābhāvavān |
etaccātyantābhāvatvaṃ na ghaṭatvasāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakaṃ tadavacchedakaṃ tu ghaṭaniṣṭhatvāvacchinnameva māyimate |
ato na ghaṭāntarbhāvena paṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvasya ghaṭatvarūpapratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyamādāya pratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇasyābhāvasyāprasiddhiḥ śaṅkyā |
tataśca taddhaṭitahetoḥ kathamasiddhiriti || nanvidamekaṃ sandhitsatā'nyaccayavata ityāpannam |
yato vyabhicāravāraṇāya viśeṣaṇopādāne punarasiddhiḥ prāptā |
ghaṭapaṭayorabhedavāde paṭe'pi ghaṭatvasattvāt |
tasya ca mithyātvena tadatyantābhāvasya pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyasadbhāvena tadavacchedakāvacchinnatayā'navacchinnatvābhāvāditi bhāvena śaṅkate -- nanu tarhīti || tvayā'pīti |
ghaṭapaṭayorabhedaṃ vadatā'pi tvayā paṭe ghaṭatvātyantābhāvasyāṅgīkāryatvāditi sambandhaḥ |
atra pratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇasyeti pūraṇīyam |
tathā ca paṭe ghaṭatvasyānaṅgīkāryatvāditi tātparyaṃ bodhyam |
anyathā ghaṭatvātyantābhāvasya tenāpyaṅgīkṛtatvāddoṣānivṛtteḥ |
kuto ghaṭatvaṃ paṭe nāṅgīkārya mayetyata uktaṃ paṭe ghaṭavyavahāraprasaṅganirodhāyeti |
yadi paṭe'pi ghaṭatvaṃ syāt tarhi ghaṭa iva paṭe'pi ghaṭavyavahāraḥ syāditi bhiyā nāṅgīkāryamiti bhāvaḥ |
ghaṭapaṭayoḥ pāramārthikabhedāṅgīkāre kathaṃ paṭe ghaṭatvasyānaṅgīkāraḥ sambhavet |
na hi prayojanādhīnā prameyasiddhiryena paṭe ghaṭavyavahāraprasaṅganirodhāya tadanaṅgīkāra iti syādityata uktaṃ ghaṭapaṭayorapāramārthikabhedaṃ vadateti |
ata eva tvayā'pāramārthiko bhedo'pyaṅgīkṛto'stīti bhāvaḥ |
bhedasyāpāramārthikatve'sattvasyaivāpattyā punaruktadoṣānivṛttirityata uktaṃ vyāvahārikabhedamātreṇeti |
apāramārthikaśabdena vyāvahārika eva bhedo vivakṣito nāsannityāśayaḥ |
nanvidamapyasambhavatyeva |
svābhāvikaikye vyāvahārikabhedamātreṇa kathaṃ ghaṭa eva ghaṭatvaṃ na paṭe iti vyavasthopapattirityata uktaṃ bimbapratibimbetyādi |
yathā tvanmate bimbapratibimbayorabhede'pi vyāvahārikabhedamātreṇa bimbe mukhe bimbatvameva na pratibimbatvam |
pratimukhe ca pratibimbatvameva na bimbatvam |
tathā bimbe satyatvameva nāsatyatvaṃ pratibimbe'satyatvameva na satyatvamiti vyavasthā svīkṛtaivamihāpītyarthaḥ |
nanu ghaṭapaṭatādātmyavāde ghaṭatvaṃ ghaṭa iva paṭe'pyastyeva |
na ca tathātve paṭe'pi ghaṭavyavahārāpattiḥ |
avidyāvaśātpratiniyatavyavahāropapatterityāśaṅkate -- ghaṭatvasyeti || sarvatra paṭādau |
aviśeṣe'pi sattve'pīti yāvat |
yadyavidyāvaśātpaṭe ghaṭatvasattve'pi ghaṭavyavahāranirodhastadā tvanmate ghaṭapaṭayoriva śuktirajatayorapyabhedācchuktau rajatatvasadbhāve'pi kutastatra sarvadā na rajatavyavahāra iti pṛṣṭe'vidyāvaśādeveti vaktavyam |
tataśca rajatāropakāle'pi rajatavyavahāro na syāt |
vyavahāravighaṭikāyā avidyāyā jāgarūkatvāt |
athaivaṃ brūṣe |
na kevalāvidyā vyavahārasya sādhikā bādhikā vā kintvāropitasāpekṣaiva |
tathā ca rajatāropasadbhāve rajatamidamiti vyavahāraṃ janayati tadabhāve na janayatīti |
tarhyāropitaviṣayalakṣaṇanimittabhāvābhāvayoreva vyavahāratadabhāvaprayojakatvaṃ na kevalāvidyāyā ityāyātam |
tathā ca paṭe ghaṭatvarūpanimittasadbhāve tatra ghaṭavyavahāro durvāra eva |
avidyāmātrasyākiñcitkaratvaditi bhāvena pariharati -- na hīti |
kuto na janayatītyata āha -- tathātva iti || taṃ rajatamiti vyavahāram |
atrānumāne hetoraprayojakatāśaṅkāṃ pariharati -- ghaṭapaṭayoriti || sādhye tāttvikatādātmyetyuktatvāttadanurodhena tādātmyasya pāramārthikatvāṅgīkāra ityuktam |
kecittu kiṃ tadaikyaṃ pāramārthikamutāpāramārthikamiti vikalpyādyapakṣe dūṣaṇamidamityāhuḥ || ghaṭapaṭayoḥ svarūpasyeti || na hi dharmipratiyoginorapāramārthikatve tattādātmyasya pāramārthikatvaṃ sambhavatīti bhāvaḥ |
iṣṭāpattiṃ parihartumāha -- prapañceti || vāco yuktirityaluk |
vākprayoga ityarthaḥ |
evaṃ hetuviśeṣaṇāsiddhimuddhṛtyedānīṃ viśeṣyāsiddhimāśaṅkyoddharati || ghaṭe ghaṭatvasyeti || hetau paṭāvṛttirghaṭatvādidharmādhikaraṇatvaṃ khalu vivakṣitam |
ghaṭe paṭatvādermithyātvena tadatyantābhāvasattvāt |
pakṣe hetvabhāvasyaivāsiddhiśabdārthatvāditi śaṅkārthaḥ |
na ca hetvabhāvamātramasiddhiḥ |
kintu pratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇa eva hetvabhāvaḥ |
prakṛte ghaṭatvābhāvasattve'pi ghaṭatvamapyaṅgīkaraṇīyam |
anyathā paṭāvṛttino ghaṭe'pyasattvenānirāśrayatvaṃ syāt |
svātyantābhāvasamānāśrayatvarūpamithyātvahāniśca syāditi bhāvaḥ |
pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinneti padakṛtyaṃ spaṣṭīkartumāha -- nanviti || paṭe tādātmyasattvena sādhyābhāvasattvāt |
paṭatvātyantābhāvādermithyātvena paṭāvṛttidharmādhikaraṇatvarūpahetośca sattvāditi bhāvaḥ || heturviśeṣita iti || hetvantargatābhāvo viśeṣita ityarthaḥ |
tena vyabhicāraprakāramāha -- paṭe paṭatvātyantābhāva iti || ghaṭapaṭayorekatvena ghaṭatvasyāpi paṭe sattvātpaṭe'pi ghaṭatvātyantābhāvasya pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyasattvātpunarviśeṣaṇāsiddhimāśaṅkate -- nanu tarhīti |
evañca sarvapadārthānāmabhedamaṅgīkṛtya sarvatra sarvadharmāṅgīkāre'pi pratiniyatavyavahārasiddhyarthaṃ vyavasthāviśeṣo'ṅgīkartavya eveti naivāsmābhirasiddhiḥ supariharetyāha -- neti || sarvatra sarvadharmāṅgīkāre'pi ghaṭatvātyantābhāvo ghaṭa eva pratiyogisamānādhikaraṇaḥ |
paṭe tu neti vyavasthā'ṅgīkāryaiva |
evañca tādṛśapratiyogyasāmānādhikaraṇyameva hetuviśeṣaṇamiti nāsiddhiriti phalito'rthaḥ || avidyāvaśāditi || tasyā aghaṭitaghaṭanāpaṭīyastvāditi bhāvaḥ |
avidyā'pi tadvati tadvyavahāraṃ janayettadrahite tadabhāvavyavahāraṃ janayennānyathā darśanādityāha -- neti || āropitarajātābhāve'pyadhiṣṭhānabhūtaṃśuktyādi tadeva rajatamiti vyavahāraṃ na hi janayatītyarthaḥ |
avidyāyā aghaṭitaghaṭakatve'rajate'pi rajatamiti vyavahāraṃ janayet |
tadarthaṃ prātibhāsikarajatajanmāṅgīkāraḥ kuta iti bhāvaḥ |
vyāvahārikabhedamātreṇa ghaṭe paṭāvṛttidharmopapattau pāramārthikaprayojako heturityatastarkamāha -- ghaṭapaṭayośceti || jīvabrahmatādātmyasya pāramārthikatvena yathā tatsambandhinorjīvabrahmaṇoḥ pāramārthikatvaṃ tatheti bhāvaḥ || vāco yuktiriti || vācaḥ prayogaḥ |
nanu tarhīti |
yathā paṭe ghaṭatvasya mithyātvena tatra tadatyantābhāvastathā ghaṭe'pi ghaṭatvasya mithyātvena tatrāpi tadatyantābhāvaḥ |
tathā ca tādṛśaghaṭatvādhikaraṇatvaṃ ghaṭe'pi nāstī'ti nāsiddhirityarthaḥ |
ghaṭasya ghaṭatvapratītiryadi ghaṭatvaṃ kutrāpi na syāttadā svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvarūpaṃ mithyātvaṃ tasya na syāt |
ataḥ kutracidaṅgīkaraṇīyam |
tacca pratītibalāddhaṭa evāṅgīkāryamiti nāsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ || nanu tathā'pīti |
paṭatvasyāpi mithyātvena paṭe'pi tadatyantābhāvasattvātpaṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogidharmasāmānyābhāvena paṭa iti vyatirekevyāptau vyabhicāra ityarthaḥ |
avacchinneti hetuviśeṣaṇabalādeva na vyabhicāra ityāha -- ata eveti || hetuviśeṣito hetvantargato'tyantābhāva ityarthaḥ || paṭe paṭatvātyantābhāve sattve'pīti |
paṭe paṭatvātyantābhāvasattve'pi sa pratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇaḥ |
uktarītyā paṭatvasyāpi tatra sattvāt |
tādṛśaśca paṭatvāderabhāvatadadhikaraṇatvasāmānyābhāvaḥ paṭa iti na vyabhicāra ityarthaḥ |
atra pratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇatvamātroktau paṭatvātyantābhāvasyāpi ghaṭe pratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatvātpratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇapaṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogidharmo'prasiddha eva syāditi hetvaprasiddhiḥ syāt |
ataḥ pratiyogyasāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinneti || nanu tathāpyuktadoṣānivṛttiḥ |
paṭavṛttighaṭatvātyantābhāvasya ghaṭarūpapratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakāvacchinnatvena tādṛśābhāvāprasiddheriti cenna |
pratiyogyadhikaraṇatvena paṭasyaiva viśeṣaṇāt || nanu tarhīti || vyabhicāravāraṇārthaṃ hetuviśeṣaṇa ityarthaḥ || tvayā'pīti || ghaṭapaṭayoḥ pāramārthikabhedamanaṅgīkurvatā'pītyarthaḥ || paṭe ghaṭatvātyantābhāvasyeti || paṭe ghaṭatvasyānaṅgīkāryatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
anyathā bhāvasya tenāpyaṅgīkṛtatvāddoṣānivṛtteḥ |
yadyapyetāvanmātraṃ prāgeva vaktavyam |
tāvataivāsiddhiparihāro bhaviṣyati kṛtaṃ paṭe ghaṭatvamithyātvavicāreṇa |
tathā'pi na vicāracāturīśikṣāyai prāgeva noktamiti mantavyam || ghaṭatvasyeti || mayā paṭe ghaṭatvamaṅgīkriyata eva |
na ca ghaṭe paṭatvavyavahāraprasaṅgaḥ |
aghaṭitaghaṭanāpaṭīyasyāvidyayaiva tannirodhopapattirityarthaḥ || na hyavidyāropiteti || avidyā āvidyakaviṣayasāpekṣe vyavahāranirvāhikā |
anyathā''vidyakarajatābhāve'pi śuktyādau rajatamiti vyavahāraṃ janayet |
tataśca tatrāvidyakarajatāṅgīkāro vyarthaḥ syāt |
paṭe'pi rajatavyavahāraprasaṅgaśca syāt |
evañca ghaṭe āvidyakaghaṭatvasattve'vidyāyā api vyavahāranirodho na sambhavatīti bhāvaḥ |
yadyapi śuktau śuktitvasattve'pyavidyāyāstadvyavahāranirodhakatvavaddhaṭatvasattve'pyavidyāyā vyavahāranirodhaḥ sambhāvitastathā'pi tatra bādhottaraṃśuktivyavahārasya sattvātsato'pi śuktitvasya vyavahāranirodho'vidyākṛta ityaṅgīkriyate |
na caivam |
prakṛte ghaṭapaṭavyavahārasya kadā'pyabhāvāditi nātrāvidyākṛtastannirodha iti bhāvaḥ |
mithyātvaṃ na syāditi || ghaṭatvādīnāmavidyāśaktitvamiti matenedam |
tena teṣāmapi brahmasvarūpatvādiṣṭāpattiriti codyānavakāśaḥ |
tanmataṃ tvanyatrāsmābhirnirastam |
pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyetyādiviśeṣaṇaprayojanaṃśaṅkāsamādhānābhyāṃ vyanakti || nanvityādinā || bimbeti || ekasyaiva mukhyasya vyāvahārikaupādhikamātreṇāsya bimbatvamevāsya pratibimbatvameva bimbasya satyatvameva pratibimbasya mithyātvameveti vyavasthāyāḥ sattvāt |
tādṛśavyavasthāyā nyāyaprayogakāle'pi sattvenoktarītyā tvadudbhāvitasādhanāprasiddhyaprasakteḥ |
avidyaiva vyavahāraniyāmikā sā yathā janayati tathā vyavahāro bhavati |
naitāvatā paṭe ghaṭatvānadhikaraṇatvaṃ sidhyatītyukta[kti]sādhanāprasiddhirdurvāreti śaṅkate -- ghaṭatvasyeti || avidyā'pi svakalpitaviṣayānapekṣaiva vyavahāraṃ janayati |
anyathā'tiprasaṅgādityato vyavahāraviṣayavyavasthā'ṅgīkartavyetyāha |
|| na hīti || avidyā śuktāvāropitarajatābhāve'pīdaṃ rajatamiti vyavahāraṃ te tava mate'pi janayati kiṃ ? na hi janayatītyarthaḥ |
iṣṭāpattau bādhakamāha -- tathātva iti || nanvārope sati nimittānusaraṇam |
na hi nimittamastītyāropa iti nyāyena vyavahārasattve tasyāvidyāmūlakatvaṃ kalpyate |
na tvavidyā'stītyetāvatā tadvyavahārāpādanaṃ yuktamiti cenna |
prāmāṇikārthaviṣayakatvāttannyāyasya |
na hi paṭe ghaṭatvasatve kimapi pramāṇamasti |
sarvavastvabhāve'pyavidyā vyavahāraṃ janayatītyapi vaktuṃ śakyatvena brahmasvarūpasyānirvacanīyāmbarādiprapañcasyāpi vyavahārakāla evāsattvaprasaṅgāt || ghaṭapaṭayoriti || jīvabrahmaṇoriti || tatra bimbatvapratibimbatvarūpaviruddhākāratyāgenobhayānugatacaitanyamātrasvarūpamivātrāpi ghaṭatvapaṭatva[sva]rūpaviruddhākāraparityagenobhayasvarūpabhūtamekaṃ jaḍapāramārthikaṃ syādityarthaḥ |
na ca brahmaivobhayasvarūpabhūtaṃ tattvam |
`śuktikājñāne rajataṃ tattvato jñātaṃ bhavati sā hi tasya tattvami'ti vācaspatyukteriti vācyam |
tathā sati ghaṭapaṭayorjīvacidātmakatvena jaḍatvānupapatteḥ |
prapañcitaṃ cedamanyatra |
hetughaṭakacaramādhikaraṇatvaṃ vāstavameva niviṣṭamityāśayena śaṅkate -- nanviti || vyāvahārikasādhāraṇamadhikaraṇatvameva hetughaṭakamityāśayena samādhatte || ghaṭasyeti || prātibhāsikānaikāntyaṃ tu na dūṣaṇamiti bhāvaḥ |
anyathā ghaṭasya ghaṭatvaṃ prati vyāvahārikādhikaraṇatvānaṅgīkāre || niradhikaraṇatveneti || ghaṭātirikte tādṛśādhikaraṇatvasyāsambhāvitatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
svasamānādhikaraṇātyantābhāvapratiyogitvasvātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvayoraviśeṣādāha -- svātyantābhāveti || anavacchinnāntaviśeṣaṇamavyāpyavṛttyabhāvamātravārakamiti manvāno darśayati -- nanviti || na ca paṭe paṭatvādhikaraṇatvasyāpi mithyātvena tadabhāvasattvānna vyatirekavyabhicāra iti vācyam |
vyatirekiṇi pratiyogivyadhikaraṇasādhanābhāvanirūpitavyāptereva prayojakatvāt |
paṭe paṭatvādhikaraṇatvābhāvasya ca pratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatvāt |
anyathā pṛthivītvābhāvavyāpakābhāvapratiyogisaṃyogavattvena jalādau pṛthivītvasiddhiprasaṅgāt |
anavacchinnāntadānādeva nāyaṃ doṣa iti samādhatte || ata eveti || etādṛśadoṣaprasaṅgādapītyarthaḥ |
heturhetughaṭakābhāvaḥ || pratiyogīti ||
athaivaṃ ghaṭatvādyabhāvasyāpi ghaṭādau pratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatvena pratiyogisāmānādhiraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinnābhāvāprasiddhiriti cedatrāhuḥ |
pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyasya nirūpakatayā yadavacchedakaṃ tadanavacchinnapaṭavṛttikātyantābhāvapratiyogidharmavattvaṃ vivakṣitam |
ghaṭatvādyabhāvaśca paṭādau tādṛśa iti nāprasiddhiriti |
tadasat |
pratiyogyadhikaraṇasya pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakatvena niveśane bījābhāvāt |
yattu pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinnetyasya pratiyogyanadhiraṇatvena paṭaviśeṣaṇe tātparyānnāprasiddhiriti || taccintyam |
tathāsati pratiyogyanadhikaraṇetyanuktvā pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinnetyuktau bījābhāvaprasaṅgat |
avyāpyavṛttyadhikaraṇatvasyāvyāpyavṛttitve saṃyogamādāyānaikāntyācca |
vastutastu paramate abhāvānāmadhikaraṇātmakatvāt ghaṭaniṣṭhaghaṭatvābhāvasya pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakāvacchinnatve'pi paṭaniṣṭhābhāvasya tadanavacchinnatvamavyāhatameva |
na ca pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakībhūtaghaṭatvābhāvatvasyaikyānna paṭaniṣṭhābhāvasya tadanavacchinnatvasambhava iti vācyam |
anuyogitāviśeṣarūpābhāvatvasyābhāvabhedena bhinnatvāt |
athavā pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyarūpaṃ yadavacchedakaṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ tadanavacchinnatvaṃ tadviśiṣṭabhinnatvamiti yāvat |
tadupalakṣitabhinnatvasya paramate paṭavṛttitvaviśiṣṭaghaṭatvābhāvādāvabhāve'pi tadviśiṣṭabhinnatvamakṣatameva |
paṭavṛttitvaviśiṣṭasaṃyogādyabhāve ca na pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyaviśiṣṭabhinnatvamiti na kaściddoṣaḥ |
paramate pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinnābhāvāprasiddhirityāśaṅkate -- nanu tarhīti || ghaṭapaṭayorabhedaṃ vadatā'pi tvayā paṭe ghaṭatvātyantābhāvasya pratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇaghaṭatvābhāvasyāṅgīkāryatvāditi sambandhaḥ |
nanu ghaṭapaṭayorabhede ghaṭatvābhāvasya paṭe pratiyogyasāmānādhikaraṇyameva kathamupapadyata ityāśaṅkyoktam || ghaṭapaṭayorvyāvahārikabhedamātreṇeti || atra dṛṣṭāntaḥ || bimbeti || yathā bimbapratibimbayoḥ svarūpaikye'pi vyāvahārikabhedabalād bimbasyaiva bimbatvaṃ sattvaṃ ca, pratibimbasyaiva pratibimbatvamasattvaṃ ceti vyavasthā tathā ghaṭasyaiva ghaṭatvaṃ na tu paṭasyetyaṅgīkāryamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanu ghaṭavat paṭe'pi ghaṭatvamaṅgīkriyata eva |
na caivaṃ ghaṭa eva ghaṭavyavahāro na paṭa iti vyavasthānupapattiriti vācyam |
avidyāsāmarthyena tadupapatterityāśaṅkate -- ghaṭatvasyeti || tathā coktahetvaprasiddhitādavasthyamiti bhāvaḥ |
viṣayanirapekṣāvidyāyāḥ sākṣāttādṛśavyavahāraniyāmakatvamasiddhamityāha -- na hīti || anyathā''vidyakaviṣayavaiyarthyaprasaṅgāt |
viṣayasampādanadvāraiva tasyāstādṛśavyavahāraniyāmakatvamaṅgīkāryamiti bhāvaḥ |
tataḥ prakṛte kimityata āha -- tathātva iti || viṣayasampādanadvārā vyavahāranirvāhakatve cetyarthaḥ || ghaṭe'pi taṃ janayediti || paṭe ghaṭatvarūpaviṣayasattvādavidyā tadvyavahāraṃ janayedityarthaḥ |
yattu tathātve āropitarajatābhāve'pyavidyāyāḥ rajatavyavahārajanakatve paṭe'pi rajatavyavahāraṃ janayediti vyākhyānaṃ tadasat |
avidyāsāmarthyāt paṭe ghaṭavyavahāraṃ pariharantaṃ prati paṭe rajatavyavahārāpādanāyogāt |
ghaṭarajatayoraviśeṣāt |
atha śuktau rajatāropadaśāyāṃ śuktitvarūpaviṣayasattve'pi tadavyavahāravat paṭe ghaṭatvasattve'pi tadavyavahāra upapadyatāmiti cenna |
śuktau virodhyāropeṇa śuktitvavyavahārapratibandhasambhavāt |
paṭe ca virodhyāropābhāvadaśāyāṃ ghaṭatvavyavahārasyāpādanīyatvāt |
na ca avidyāsāmarthyādeva tatra tatpratibandho'stviti vācyam |
paṭe ghaṭatvakalpakāvidyāyāstadvyavahāravirodhitvāyogāt |
paṭe ghaṭatvaṃ parikalpyāvidyāyāstadvvavahāravirodhitvakalpanasya prakṣālanāddhīti nyāyenānucitatvācca |
syādetat |
uktānumāne'siddhyādivāraṇe'pi vipakṣe bādhakābhāvādaprayojakatvaṃ durvāramata āha -- ghaṭapaṭayoriti || yadi ghaṭapaṭayorbhedaḥ pāramārthiko na syāttadā tadabhedaḥ pāramārthikaḥ syāt |
tataśca tayorapi pāramārthikatvaprasaṅgaḥ |
anyathā ghaṭapaṭanivṛttau tadabhedasyāpi nivṛttyāpattiriti bhāvaḥ |
pratibandyā prakārāntareṇa svarūpāsiddhiṃ śaṅkate -- nanviti || tarhīti || yadi ghaṭe ghaṭatvasya mithyātvāttadatyantābhāvādhikaraṇatvamaṅgīkriyate tarhītyarthaḥ || mithyātveneti || tathātve ghaṭe ghaṭatvātyantābhāvaprāpteriti bhāvaḥ || ghaṭasyeti || uktātyantābhāvapratiyogighaṭatvarūpadharmādhikaraṇatvasya ghaṭe satvānna svarūpāsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ || anyatheti || yadi ghaṭatvaṃ prati ghaṭasyāśrayatvaṃ nāṅgīkriyate tarhyanyasyāśrayasyābhāvāt ghaṭatvasyāśraya eva nāstīti syāt |
tathāca svāśrayatvena sammate vidyamānātyantābhāvapratiyogitvarūpaṃ mithyātvaṃ ghaṭatvasya na syādityarthaḥ |
pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinneti viśeṣaṇakṛtyaṃ vaktumākṣipati -- nanviti || uktarītyā'siddhiparihāre'pi paṭe vyabhicāraḥ syādityarthaḥ |
sādhanābhāvamupapādayati -- paṭe paṭatvasya mithyātveneti || idaṃ paṭe paṭatvātyantābhāvopapādanāyoktam |
tathāca paṭaniṣṭho yo'tyantābhāvaḥ paṭatvātyantābhāvaḥ |
tatpratiyogipaṭatvaṃ tadāśrayatvena paṭe paṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogidharmādhikaraṇatvarūpasādhanasadbhāvādityarthaḥ || ata eveti || yata evaṃ paṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogidharmādhikaraṇatvarūpo hetuḥ paṭe vyabhicarito'ta eva pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinnatvena viśeṣita ityarthaḥ |
yadyapīdamatyantābhāvaviśeṣaṇam |
tathāpi tadviśeṣaṇatve tadviśiṣṭahetuviśeṣaṇatvamapyastītyabhipretyaivamuktamiti dhyeyam |
datte'pi viśeṣaṇe kathaṃ vyabhicāraparihāra ityata āha -- paṭa iti || pratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatveneti || paṭatvātyantābhāvavati paṭe paṭatvarūpapratiyogino'pi sattvāditi bhāvaḥ || pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyeti || paṭatvarūpapratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ paṭatvātyantābhāve'sti |
tadavacchedakaṃ paṭatvātyantābhāvavattvam |
tadavacchinnatayetyarthaḥ || sādhanābhāveti || pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinnatvagarbhitasādhanasyābhāvānna vyabhicāra ityarthaḥ |
yadi vyabhicāraparihārāya viśeṣaṇopādānaṃ tarhi svarūpāsiddhiriti śaṅkate -- nanvityādinā || pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāditi || paṭe ghaṭatvātyantābhāvasya ghaṭatvarūpapratiyogisamānādhikaraṇatve pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakaghaṭatvātyantābhāvavattvāvacchinnatvena pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinnatvagarbhitahetorabhāvādityarthaḥ |
ghaṭapaṭayorabhedaṃ vadatā tvayā'pi paṭe ghaṭatvātyantābhāvasyāṅgīkāryatvādityarthaḥ |
kuta ityata uktaṃ paṭe ghaṭavyavahāraprasaṅgeti |
yadi paṭe ghaṭatvaṃ tarhi ghaṭapadavyavahāraprayojakasadbhāvātpaṭe ghaṭavyavahāraḥ syāt |
atastatparihārāya paṭe ghaṭatvaṃ nāṅgīkāryamityarthaḥ |
tayorabhinnatvena ghaṭo ghaṭa eva na paṭa iti, ghaṭa eva ghaṭatvaṃ na paṭa iti vyavasthaiva kathamityata uktam || ghaṭapaṭayorapāramārthikabhedaṃ vadateti || abhedamaṅgīkurvatā'pi tayorvyāvahārikabhedāṅgīkārāduktavyavasthopapadyata ityarthaḥ |
vastuto'bhinnatve kathaṃ vyāvahārikabhedamātreṇeyaṃ vyavasthetyuktaṃ vyāvahāriketi |
yathā hi tvanmate bimbe bimbatvameva na pratibimbatvaṃ tathā bimbe satyatvameva na tvasatyatvaṃ pratibimbe'satyatvameva na satyatvamiti vyavasthā tathehāpītyarthaḥ |
nanu mayā paṭe ghaṭatvamaṅgīkriyata eva |
na ca tarhi nimittasadbhāvātpaṭe ghaṭavyavahārāpattiriti vācyam |
ghaṭavyavahāranimittasya ghaṭatvasya ghaṭapaṭādisarvapadārtheṣvaviśeṣe'pyavidyāsāmarthyena ghaṭa eva ghaṭavyavahāro na paṭa itivyavasthopapatteriti śaṅkate -- ghaṭatvasyeti || pratiniyataḥ prativastuniyataḥ |
ghaṭe ghaṭa ityeva na paṭa ityādirūpaḥ |
avidyāyā etādṛśasāmarthyamevāsiddhamityāha -- nahīti || yadi vyavahāratadabhāvau prati nimittatadabhāvayoraprayojakatvamaṅgīkṛtyāvidyāyā eva tannirvāhakatvamaṅgīkriyate |
tarhyavidyā śuktikāyāṃ rajatāropābhāvadaśāyāṃ tatra rajatavyavahāraṃ janayet |
āropitarajatatvarūpanimittasya tvayaivāprayojakatvābhyupagamāt |
nacaivaṃ tathātve ghaṭe'pi rajatatvavyavahāraṃ janayedaviśeṣāt |
ataḥ śuktikāyāṃ rajatavyavahāranimittāropitarajatatvasadbhāvājjanayati |
paṭe tadabhāvānna janayatīti nimittatadabhāvayoreva vyavahāratadabhāvaprayojakatvamiti paṭe ghaṭatvasattve ghaṭa iti vyavahāraprasaṅgo durvāra ityarthaḥ |
nanūktānumāne heturastu ghaṭapaṭayoraikyamevāstu natu tadabhāvarūpaṃ sādhyamityaprayojakatvamiti cet atra vaktavyam |
kimaikyaṃ pāramārthikamutāpāramārthikam |
nāntyaḥ parasparaviruddhayoriti nyāyenaikyasyāpāramārthikatve tadviruddhabhedasya pāramārthikatvāpatteḥ |
ādya āha -- ghaṭapaṭayoriti || pāramārthikaṃ na nivartate apāramārthikaṃ tu nivartata iti khalu vastugatiḥ |
tatra ghaṭapaṭayorapāramārthikatvāṅgīkṛtāvaikyamapi nivarteta |
tataśca kutaḥ pāramārthikatvam |
ata aikyasyāpāramārthikatve ghaṭapaṭayoḥ pāramārthikatvamaparihāryamiti bhāvaḥ || jīvabrahmaṇoriti || yathā tayoḥ pāramārthikābhedāśrayatvena pāramārthikatvaṃ tathehāpītyarthaḥ |
iṣṭāpattiṃ pariharati --
kiñca ghaṭaḥ ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākapaṭādidharmikatraikālikābhāvapratiyogī dravyatvātpaṭavat |
ghaṭātyantābhāvo vā ghaṭapratiyogikapaṭādidharmikatraikālikābhāvānyaḥ abhāvatvātpaṭābhāvavat |
ghaṭasaṃsargo vā paṭādidharmikatraikālikābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakaghaṭaniṣṭhadharmānyaḥ saṃsargatvātpaṭaniṣṭhasaṃsargavat |
ghaṭaprāgabhāvo vā ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākapaṭādidharmikānādyabhāvānyaḥ abhāvatvāt paṭapradhvaṃsavat |
ghaṭapradhvaṃso vā ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākapaṭādidharmikanityābhāvānyaḥ abhāvatvāt paṭaprāgabhāvavat |
ghaṭapaṭayorabhedavādinā'pi (paṭe ghaṭapratiyogikānyonyābhāvābhāve'pi) paṭapratiyogikātyantābhāvāderghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogikatvāderaṅgīkāryatvānna sādhyavaikalyādi dṛṣṭāntasya |
tayoḥ pāramārthikābhede'pi vyāvahārikabhedamātreṇa paṭapratiyogikātyantābhāvāderghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogikatvamityaṅgīkāryatvāt |
anyathā ghaṭārthinaḥ paṭe pravṛttiprasaṅgaḥ |
siddhaghaṭasya puṃsaḥ paṭasāmāgryanupādānaprasaṅgaśca |
na ca vyāvahārikānyonyābhāvasiddhyā'rthāntaratvam |
traikālikatvādinā'nyonyābhāvasya viśeṣitatvāt |
vyāvahārikatve ca tadayogāt |
anukūlatarkasyātraivo(nyatro)ktatvānnopādhyābhāsasamānayogakṣematvāprayojakatvāni |
uktānumāne'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvādidoṣaparihārakleśamālocya yathā tadaprasaktiḥ syāttathā'numānāntaramāha -- kiñceti || paṭo na ghaṭa iti sākṣikamanyonyābhāvamādāya pakṣe sādhyaparyavasānaṃ bodhyam |
atrābhāvapratiyogītyuktau prāgabhāvapradhvaṃsādāvādāyārthāntaratā || atastraikāliketi || yadyāpyanityagato bhedo'nitya eva tathā'pi `naṣṭānāmapi vastūnāṃ bhedo naiva vinaśyatī'ti mataṃ naiyānikamataṃ cāvalambya traikālikatvoktiḥ |
ghaṭe ghaṭo nāstīti pratīyamānaṃ ghaṭadharmikaghaṭātyantābhāvamādāyārthāntaratānirāsāya paṭādidharmiketi |
tāvatyukte paṭe ghaṭo nāstīti pratīyamānaṃ ghaṭātyantābhāvamādāyārthāntaratā |
tannivṛttyai saṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitāketi || atyantābhāvasya saṃsargābhāvaprabhedatvena saṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitākatayā tadanavacchinnapratiyogitākatvābhāvāt |
idamapi nyāyamatarītyaiva |
svamate'tyantābhāvasya saṃsargābhāvatvānaṅgīkārāt |
atyantābhāvatvasya sākṣādevābhāvavibhājakatvena saṃsargābhāvaprabhedatvasya tattvasaṅkhayānaṭikāyāṃ dūṣitatvāt |
saṃsargapratiyogibhāvasya yathāsambhavaṃ prāgabhāvādiṣvevāntarbhāvakathanācceti mantavyam |
paṭe paṭo nāstīti pratīyamāne paṭadharmikatraikālikātyantābhāve paṭasaṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitāke paṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvābhāvena dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyamiti śaṅkānirāsāya ghaṭeti |
tathā ca paṭe paṭātyantābhāvasya paṭasaṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitākatve'pi ghaṭasaṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvābhāvānna sādhyavaikalyamiti padakṛtyaṃ bodhyam |
asminnapyanumāne'tyantābhāvavyāvṛttyarthaṃ saṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitāketi viśeṣaṇapraveśādgauravaṃ manyamāno'numānāntarāṇyāha -- ghaṭātyantābhāva ityādinā |
atrānyonyābhāvamādāyaiva pakṣe tadanyatvaparyavasānaṃ bodhyam |
dṛṣṭānte paṭātyantābhāvamādāya tadanyatvaparyavasānam |
atrābhāvānya ityukte prāgabhāvapradhvaṃsāvādāya tadanyatvasiddhyā'rthāntaratvam |
atastraikāliketi |
tāvatyukte ghaṭaniṣṭhapaṭātyantābhāvanyatvasiddhyā'rthāntaratā |
tadvyāvṛttyarthaṃ ghaṭapratiyogiketi |
tādṛśaścābhāvo'nyo nāstītyanyonyābhāvamādāyaiva tadanyatvevā'nye ghaṭapaṭayorbhedasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
ghaṭasaṃsargo veti |
atra paṭe ghaṭo neti pratītisākṣikamanyonyābhāvamādāyaiva tatpratiyogitāvacchedako ghaṭaniṣṭho yastādātmyarūpo dharmastadanyatvarūpasādhyaparyavasānaṃ pakṣe bodhyam |
atra ghaṭaniṣṭhadharmānya ityukte rūpādyanyatvasiddhyā'rthāntaratā |
ato'bhāvapratiyogitāvacchedaketi |
tāvatyukte prāgabhāvapradhvaṃsābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakottarapūrvakālīnatvarūpaghaṭaniṣṭhadharmamādāya tadanyatve'siddhyā'rthāntaratā |
tannivṛttyai traikāliketi |
ghaṭe ghaṭo nāstīti pratītisiddhaghaṭadharmikaghaṭapratiyogikātyantābhāvamādāya tatpratiyogitāvacchedakaghaṭaniṣṭhasaṃsargarūpadharmānyatvasya ghaṭasaṃsarge bādhitatvāttadvāraṇāya paṭādidharmiketi |
tādṛśaścābhāvo'nyonyābhāvaṃ vinā nānyo bhavitumarhatīti ghaṭapaṭayorbhedasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte ca paṭe ghaṭo nāstīti pratīyamānaghaṭātyantābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakaghaṭaniṣṭhasaṃsargānyatvamādāya sādhyaparyavasānam || ghaṭaprāgabhāvo veti || atra paṭo na ghaṭa iti pratītisākṣikamanādibhūtamanyonyābhāvamādāyaiva tadanyatvarūpaṃ sādhyaṃ pakṣe bodhyam |
atrānādyabhāvānya ityukte ghaṭaniṣṭhapaṭātyantābhāvamādāyārthāntaratā |
tadvāraṇāya paṭādidharmiketi |
tāvatyukte paṭaniṣṭhaghaṭātyantābhāvamādāyārthāntaratā |
tannirāsāya ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitāketi |
tādṛśaścābhāvo ghaṭapratiyokapaṭadharmikānyonyābhāva eva bhavitumarhatīti ghaṭapaṭayorbhedasiddhiḥ |
dṛṣṭānte ca paṭepaṭo nāstīti pratītisākṣikapaṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvamādāya tadanyatvarūpaṃ sādhyaṃ bodhyam || ghaṭadhvaṃso veti || atrāpi ghaṭapratiyogikapaṭadharmikamanyonyābhāvamādāyaiva tadanyatvaṃ pakṣe paryavaseyam |
atra nityābhāvānya ityukte ghaṭe paṭātyantābhāvamādāyārthāntaratā |
ataḥ paṭādidharmiketi || tāvatyukte paṭe paṭātyantābhāvamādāyārthāntāratā |
ataḥ saṃsargānavacchannapratiyogitāketi || tathāpi paṭe paṭātyantābhāvamādāyārthāntaratā |
tasya paṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvādato ghaṭeti || tādṛśaścābhāvaḥ paṭo na ghaṭa iti pratīyamāno'nyonyābhāva eva bhavatīti vivakṣitasiddhiḥ |
dṛṣṭānte paṭaprāgabhāve ca paṭe paṭātyantābhāvamādāya tadanyatvarūpasādhyaparyavasānam |
nanu pūrvānumāne etadanumāne ca ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākapaṭāntarādidharmikapaṭātyantābhāvamādāya tadanyatvaṃ pakṣe'stveti siddhasādhanam |
tannivṛttyarthaṃ ghaṭapratiyogikatvenābhāvaviśeṣaṇe dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyam |
ghaṭadhvaṃsatatprāgabhāvayorghaṭapratiyokatvābhāvāditi cenna |
paṭādidharmiketyatra paṭādipadena paṭādisāmānyasyaiva vivakṣaṇīyatvena pratiyogibhinnaghaṭādergrahītumaśakyatvāt |
paṭasya ghaṭādi paṭāntareṇāpi bhinnatvasyādyāpyasiddhatvāt |
siddhau vā ghaṭenāpi kimaparāddham |
kecittvetacchaṅkānirāsārthaṃ svapratiyogipratiyogikatvenābhāvaṃ viśiṃṣanti |
taccintyam |
uktarītyā paṭādipadena paṭādisāmānyavivakṣāyāmuktaśaṅkāyā evānavakāśena viśeṣaṇadānavaiyarthyāt |
tādṛśābhāvasyānyonyābhāvamatyantābhāvaṃ ca vinā'nyasyābhāvādanādyabhāvapadayornityābhāvapadayorvā vaiyarthyāpātācceti || nanvatra ghaṭātyantābhāvādipakṣakānumānacatuṣṭaye sādhye'nyapadenānyonyābhāva eva grāhyaḥ |
tathā ca jaḍamātre tadanaṅgīkāriṇaṃ prati tasya kutrāpyaprasiddhatvātkathaṃ tatsādhane nāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā |
anyathā paramparāṃ vinā sākṣādeva ghaṭaḥ paṭādbhinna ityeva prayogaprasaṅgāditi cenna |
atra sarvatrānyapadena bhedamātrasya vivakṣitatvāt |
tasya ca vyāvahārikabhedaṅgīkāriṇā'pyaṅgīkārāditi |
nanvatra ghaṭapakṣakādyānumāne dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavaikalyam |
ghaṭapaṭayorabhedavāde paṭābhāvasyāpi ghaṭabhāvarūpatvena paṭapratiyogikapaṭadharmikātyantābhāvasya ghaṭasaṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvāvaśyambhāvāttadanavacchinnapratiyogitākatvābhāvena tādṛśābhāvaṃ prati paṭasya pratiyogitvābhāvāt |
evaṃ dvitīyānumāne'pi |
dṛṣṭāntīkṛtasya paṭadharmikapaṭābhāvasya ghaṭapaṭayoraikyavāde ghaṭapratiyogitayā'pi sattvena ghaṭapratiyogikābhāvarūpatāsambhavena sādhyavaikalyam |
dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāntikabhāvasya bhedavyāptatvena ghaṭādipakṣakānumāne paṭasya vā paṭābhāvādervā dṛṣṭāntatvānupapattiścetyata āha -- ghaṭapaṭayoriti || atra prathamādipadena paṭaniṣṭhasaṃsargagrahaṇam |
dvitīyādipadena dvitīyatṛtīyānumānayorghaṭapratiyogikapaṭadharmikatraikālikābhāvānyatve paṭādidharmikatraikālikābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakaghaṭaniṣṭhadharmānyatvayorgrahaṇam |
sādhyavaikalyādītyadipadena tu dṛṣṭāntatvānupapattiḥ |
nanvabhedavāde kathamidamaṅgīkartumarhatītyata āha -- tayoriti || ghaṭapaṭayorityarthaḥ |
atrāpyādipadadvayena pūrvoktānāmeva grahaṇam |
tataścāyamārthaḥ |
ādyānumāne tāvanna dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyam |
paṭādidharmikapaṭapratiyogikātyantābhāvasya ghaṭapaṭayorvyāvahārikabhedāṅgīkāreṇa ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogikatvasattve paṭasya tādṛśābhāvapratiyogitāsadbhāvāt |
nāpi dvitīyānumāne |
ghaṭapaṭayorvyāvahārikabhedamātreṇa paṭātyantābhāvasya ghaṭapratiyogikapaṭadharmikātyantābhāvānyatvāsambhavāt |
na cāpi tṛtīyānumāne |
paṭaniṣṭhasaṃsargasya ghaṭapaṭayorvyāvahārikabhedāṅgīkārādeva paṭaniṣṭhaghaṭātyantābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakaghaṭaniṣṭhasaṃsargarūpadharmānyatvasambhavāt |
ata eva ghaṭaghaṭātyantābhāvaghaṭasaṃsargapakṣakānumāneṣu paṭapaṭābhāvapaṭaniṣṭhasaṃsargānāṃ dṛṣṭāntatvenopādānaṃ copapadyata iti || vipakṣe bādhakadvayamāha -- anyatheti || vyāvahārikabhedamātreṇoktavyavasthānaṅgīkāra ityarthaḥ || siddhaghaṭasyeti || bahuvrīhiḥ |
padārthina iti śeṣaḥ |
ghaṭapaṭayoḥ pāramārthikābhedasya jāgarūkatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
paṭasāmānyādītyādipadena paṭaparigrahaḥ |
eṣā ca sādhyavaikalyaśaṅkātatparihāraścādyānumānatrayaviṣaya eva |
na caturthapañcamaviṣayaḥ |
tatra dṛṣṭāntīkṛtayoḥ paṭadhvaṃsatatprāgabhāvayoranādyabhāvanityābhāvānyatvasyaiva sattvāt |
tarhi tadanumānatrayāntaramevaiṣa carcaḥ kartavya iti cet |
satyam |
tathā'pi ghaṭapaṭabhedasādhanāyaitadanumānapañcakasyāpi prakrāntatvena madhye carcāntarasyānucitatvāttadeva sādhana evāyaṃ carca ucita iti mantavyam |
eke tu ghaṭapakṣakānumānaviṣaya evāyaṃ carca ityācakṣate |
tatrādipadānāṃ kṛtyaṃ kimiti cintyam |
anye tu ghaṭātyantābhāvapakṣakadvitīyānumānaviṣaya evāyaṃ carca ityabhipretya dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavaikalyamupapādyam |
evaṃ sisādhayiṣitānyonyābhāve'pi na ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvam |
ghaṭātyantābhāvatadanyonyābhāvayorabhedāt |
tathā ca bādha ityabhipretya sādhyavaikalyādītyādipadena bādho grāhyaḥ |
dṛṣṭāntasyeti pakṣasyāpyupalakṣaṇamityāhuḥ |
tatrāpyādyadvitīyayorādipadayoḥ kṛtyaṃ cintyameveti || vyāvahārikatve ceti || vyāvahārikasyāvidyakatvenāvidyānivṛttau mokṣe tannivṛtterapyāvaśyakatvena traikālikatvāyogādityarthaḥ |
nanu jaḍabhedasādhakānumāneṣu sarvatra pakṣe taratvamupādhiḥ |
evameva sādhyābhāvasyāpi sādhayituṃ śakyatvenābhāsasamānayogakṣematvam |
hetūnāmaprayojakatā cetyata āha -- anukūlatarkasyeti || anyatra nyāyāmṛta uktatvādityarthaḥ |
tatra hi prathamaparicchede dvaitapratyakṣasyādvaitaśrutibādhakatvakhaṇḍanabhaṅge idaṃ rūpyaṃ gauro'hamityādibhramāṇāṃ pramātvaṃ syāt |
ātmani dehādbhedasyānṛtādivyāvṛtteśca bodhakānāṃ vedāntānāmaprāmāṇyaṃ ca syāt |
ghaṭajñānenaiva tadabhinnasya brahmatadabhedādeḥ sarvasyāpi vastuno jñātatvena sārvajñaṃ vedāntānāṃ vaiyarthyaṃ sadyo mokṣaśca syāt |
sukhaduḥkhabandhamokṣabhedābhedadūṣaṇabhūṣaṇajayaparājayabhrāntipramāderapi vastuto'bhinnatvena sarvasaṅkarāpattyā svakriyāsvanyāyasvavacanavirodhāśca syurityādyuktam |
yadyapyatraiva ghaṭapaṭayostādātmyasya pāramārthikatvāṅgīkāre tu jīvabrahmaṇoriva ghaṭapaṭasvarūpasya pāramārthikatvaṃ syādityādinā'nukūlatarkaḥ prāguktaḥ |
dūrasthavanaspatyorabhedagrāhi ca pratyakṣaṃ tattvāvedakaṃ syāditi vakṣyate ca |
tathā'pi bahūnāmanukūlatarkāṇāṃ jñāpanārthamanyatretyuktamiti jñātavyam |
kecittvanyatra nyāyamṛta uktatvāduktaprāyatvāditi vyākhyāya yadi ghaṭapaṭayostāttvikamaikyaṃ syāt tarhi ghaṭanāśe paṭanāśaḥ syāt |
ghaṭajñāne paṭajñānaṃ syādityādestarkasya sattvādityāhuḥ |
nanvevaṃ tādātmyasya mithyātvamastu |
kathaṃ tāvatā bhedapāramārthikatvam |
na hīdānīṃ tādātmyanirākaraṇamātreṇa parapakṣadūṣaṇamupakrāntam |
kintu bhedasatyatvasādhanena svapakṣasamarthanameva |
na ca parasparaviruddhyoranyatarastvata eva setsyatīti vācyam |
bhedasiddhāvapi tatpāmārthyasādhanopāyābhāvāditi cenna |
yatra yatsattāvacchinnasya niṣedhastatra tatsattāvacchinnasyaiva tadviruddhasyaiva dharmasya vidhiḥ |
evañca pāramārthikatādātmyaniṣedhe tādṛśasyaiva bhedaprāptiḥ prayogaśca |
tādātmyaṃ svaniṣedhapratiyogitāvacchedakasattāvatsvaviruddhavanniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogi mithyātvājjīveśvarabhedavat |
na ca dharmiṇo'pi tādṛśasattākatvamupādhiḥ |
anyathā śaśaviṣāṇe nityatvasya mithyātve'nityatvaṃ syāditi vācyam |
tādātmyamuktadharmavatyadhikaraṇe'bhāvapratiyogīti sādhane upādherapi pakṣe lābhāt |
na caivamukte śaśaviṣāṇanityatve vyabhicāraḥ |
mithyātvādityasya sattāvatyadhikaraṇe niṣedhapratiyogitvādityarthakatvāditi || nanu tathā'pi tādātmyaniṣedhamukhena bhedopalambho na sākṣāt |
tathā'pi dharmisattvasādhanāyāsagrastam |
na caivaṃ sākṣādeva ghaṭaḥ paṭādbhinna iti sādhayāmīti vācyam |
bhedākhyapadārthasyaivābhāvenāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāt |
bhedavyavahārastvanyonyadharmātyantābhāvenaivopapannaḥ |
na caivaṃ sarvādvaitavāde'nyonyadharmātyantābhāvo'pyayukta iti vācyam |
dharmāṇāṃ svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvāṅgīkārāt |
kiñca bhedamātrasādhane vyāvahārikabhede siddhasādhanam |
pāramārthiketi viśeṣaṇe tvaprasiddhiḥ |
na cātmabhedasādhana iva dharmisattāsamānasattāketi viśeṣaṇasambhavaḥ |
dharmiṇāmapi vyāvahārikatvādityasvarasātprakārāntareṇa prayuṅkte |
ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākaścāsau traikālikābhāvaśca sa ca ghaṭānyonyābhāva ityabhipreto'rthastatpratiyogītyarthaḥ |
ghaṭapratiyogikapaṭādidharmikātyantābhāvenānyathāsiddhivāraṇāya ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitāketi || atyantābhāvasyoktasambandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvena tadvyudāsaḥ |
atra saṃsargānyonyābhāvarūpasthaladvaye'pi saṃsargatādātmyayoḥ pratiyogitāvacchedakatvena lokaprasiddhyanusārādevamuktiriti jñeyam |
vastutastu tayorāropyasambandhamātraṃ nāvacchedakamityuktaṃ purastāt |
evañcātra ghaṭasaṃsargānāropyaketi tadartho jñeyaḥ |
evamuttaratrāpi |
ghaṭatādātmyāvacchinnetyevoktāvaprasiddhasādhyavaikalye syātāmityevamuktiḥ |
saṃsargānavacchinnetyādyevoktāvapi tāveva doṣāvityata ādau saṃsargaviśeṣatayā ghaṭagrahaṇam |
aprastutacetanabhedenārthāntaratāvāraṇāya ghaṭādidharmiketi || viyadādipadena jaḍamātraṃ vivakṣitamiti jñeyam |
ghaṭaprāgabhāvānyonyābhāvairanyathāsiddhivāraṇāya traikāliketi || asminmate prāgabhāvadhvaṃsayoḥ samavāyideśavṛttitvaniyamābhāvena paṭādidharmiketyanenaiva tadvyudāsopapatteḥ |
kutastaditi śaṅkānavakāśaḥ |
nanu prāgabhāvadhvaṃsayoḥ saṃsargābhāvatvena saṃsargānavacchinnetyanenaiva vyudāsānna tadarthaṃ traikālikapadamiti cenna |
atrātyantābhāvasyaiva saṃsargābhāvatvarītyā'numānapravṛtteḥ |
yathoktaṃ jayatīrthaśrīmaccaraṇaiḥ |
yaccānyairatyantābhāvasvarūpamuktvā saṃsargapratiyogikābhāvo'tyantābhāva iti || yadvā saṃsargānavacchinnetyasya viśeṣaṇāntarāghaṭitasaṃsargamātrāvacchinnetyarthaḥ |
evañcātrātyantābhāve tanmātrāvacchedakatvena tadvyudāsa iti || vyāvahārikānyonyābhāvamātravyudāsāya traikāliketi || paṭavaditi || paṭātyantābhāvasya saṃsargānavacchinnatve'pi ghaṭasaṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvābhāvāttatpratiyogini paṭe viśiṣṭābhāvena sādhyamupapādanīyam |
ghaṭātyantābhāvo veti || ghaṭapratiyogikaḥ paṭādidharmiko yastraikāliko'bhāvastadanya ityarthaḥ |
sa cānyonyābhāva evaṃ vaktavya iti bhāvaḥ |
ghaṭādyatyantābhāvenārthāntaratāvāraṇāya ghaṭapratiyogiketi paṭādidharmiketi traikāliketi pūrvavat || paṭābhāvavaditi || tasyoktarūpaghaṭātyantābhāvānyatvena sādhyavattvaṃ jñeyam |
nanu tathā'pyekasyātyantābhāvasya ghaṭādipratiyogikātyantābhāvānyaparatvena siddhasādhanateti cenna |
ghaṭatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākātyantābhāvasya bhede'pyavacchedakabhedābhāvenaikatvāt |
na ca ghaṭātyantābhāvasya nīlaghaṭātyantābhāvānyatvena sa eva doṣa iti vācyam |
pratijñāyāṃ ghaṭapratiyogiketyasya ghaṭatvāvacchinnapratiyogitāketyarthakatvāt |
nanu tathā'pi saṃyogāropyaghaṭātyantābhāvasya sambandhāntarāropyakaghaṭātyantābhāvatvenānyathāsiddhireva |
na cātra svajñānakāraṇībhūtapratiyogyāropaniṣṭhasambandhānyasambandhānāropyaketi || abhāvo viśeṣayituṃ śakyaḥ |
anyonyabhāvasyātyantābhāvādvilakṣaṇasambandhāropyakatvena bādhāt |
na cātyantābhāvamātrasyāpi pakṣatvānnoktadoṣa iti vācyam |
sarvātyantābhāvānāmanyatvenānyathāsiddheḥ |
na cātyantābhāvo'nya ityasyātyantābhāvāvacchinnādhikaraṇatākānyatvavānityartho vivakṣita iti vācyam |
dṛṣṭāntasādhyavaikalyāpatteriti || maivam |
ghaṭātyantābhāvo'nya ityasya yāvatsvādhikaraṇakamekābhāvanirūpitaṃ yadanyatvaṃ tadvānityarthakatvāt |
svādhikaraṇapadaṃ svasamabhivyāhṛtaparamiti na sādhyavaikalyādidoṣaḥ |
nanvatrānyonyābhāvamātrāsiddhidaśāyāmanya ityukte sādhyāprasiddhyā'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇateti cenna |
anyatrānyapadasyānyonyābhāvamaṅgīkurvatā'pyanyādivyavahāraviṣayatayā yadvivakṣitaṃ tanmātrārthakatvāt || ghaṭasaṃsargo veti || ghaṭādidharmiko yastraikālikābhāvāstatpratiyogitāvacchedakayorghaṭaniṣṭhastadanya ityarthaḥ |
saṃsargastādātmyeti dharmadvayaṃ hyabhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakatayā vivakṣitam |
atra saṃsargasya tādṛśadharmānyatvaṃ tādṛśatādātmyarūpadharmānyatvena paryavasyati |
tadavacchedyapratiyogitāko yo'bhāvo'nyonyābhāva eveti bhāvaḥ |
atra ghaṭaniṣṭhadharmānya ityevokte rūpādyanyatvenārthāntaram |
ato'vacchedakāntaṃ viśeṣaṇam |
ghaṭaniṣṭhatādṛśadharmānyatvasiddhivāraṇāya ghaṭaniṣṭheti || paṭaniṣṭhasaṃsargavaditi || tasya pakṣabhūtasaṃsargānyatvena sādhyatvaṃ jñeyam |
śeṣaṃ pūrvavat |
ghaṭaprāgabhāvo veti || ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnaḥ paṭadharmiko yo'nādyabhāvaḥ sa ghaṭādyanyonyābhāva eveti bhāvaḥ |
atra paṭaniṣṭhakuḍyātyantābhāvānyatvasiddhivāraṇāya ghaṭapratiyogiketyapi viśeṣaṇaṃ grāhyam |
ghaṭātyantābhāvānyatvāsiddhivāraṇāya ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitāketyuktam |
vyāvahārikānyonyābhāvasiddhivāraṇāyādīti |
idaṃ tu vyāvahārikabhedasyāgantukatvābhiprāyeṇeti jñeyam |
yadvā'nādītyanaupādhiketyarthaḥ |
athavā dhvaṃsānyadvāraṇāyānādīti |
atra saṃsargānavacchinnetyanenaiva pūrvavaddraṣṭavyaḥ || ghaṭapradhvaṃsavaditi || tasyoktarūpapakṣīkṛtya taddhaṭaprāgabhāvānyatvena sādhyatvaṃ jñeyam || ghaṭapradhvaṃso veti || atrāpi ghaṭapratiyogiketyapi grāhyam |
viyatpadaṃ pūrvavadvyāvahārikavāraṇāya vā prāgabhāvavyudāsārthaṃ veti jñeyam || ghaṭaprāgabhāvavaditi || tasyoktarūpadhvaṃsānyatvena sādhyavattavaṃ jñeyam |
nanveteṣvanumāneṣu dṛṣṭāntīkṛtapaṭādau ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnatvetyantābhāvapratiyogitvādinā sādhyamupapādanīyam |
tanna yujyate |
sarvābhedavādino mama ghaṭasyaiva paṭatve paṭātyantābhāvāderapi ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvāt |
evañca sādhyavaikalyāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvādītyata āha -- ghaṭapaṭayoriti || ghaṭātyantābhāvapadena kuḍyātyantābhāvādergrahaṇam |
na tu ghaṭapaṭaprāgabhāvādeḥ |
pratiyogitvādirityādipadena dvitīyādyanumānābhipretaghaṭādipratiyogikatvādergrahaṇam |
tadupapādayati -- tayoriti || tvayā ghaṭapaṭādivyavahārasāṅkaryāya vyāvahārikabhedo'ṅgīkṛtaḥ |
tāmeva vyavasthāmāśritya vayaṃ sādhayāmaḥ |
vaiyātyādvyavasthāmanaṅgīkurvato'tiprasaṅgamāha -- siddhapaṭasyeti || ghaṭapaṭayorekatvenoditotpādanīyatvābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ || traikālikatvādineti || atrādipadenānāditvādergrahaṇam |
nanu kimidaṃ traikālikatvam |
trikālasambandhitvaṃ vā trikālābādhyatvaṃ vā |
nādyaḥ |
vyāvahārike'pyākāśādivattadavirodhāt |
na dvitīyaḥ |
sādhyavaikalyāprasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvāpatteḥ |
evamanāditvamapi prāgabhāvāpratiyogitvaṃ vā'vidyāprayojyatvābhāvo vā |
tathā nityatvamapi dhvaṃsāpratiyogitvaṃ vā bādhāgocaratvaṃ veti vikalpāsahamiti || maivam |
traikālikābādhyatvameva traikālikatvam |
na coktadoṣaḥ |
abhāvapāramārthikavādinaṃ pratyupapatteḥ |
na caivamanyonyābhāvamātrasādhane tasya pāramārthyaṃ svataḥ setsyatīti traikāliketi viśeṣaṇaṃ vyarthamiti vācyam |
saṃsargābhāvamātrasya pāramārthikatvamaṅgīkṛtyānyonyābhāvameva yo nāṅgīkaroti |
kathañcitsambhave'pi bādhya eva bhavediti manyate taṃ pratyupapatteḥ |
yadvā trikālasambandhitvaṃ traikālikatvam |
na coktadoṣaḥ |
bhedasya satyopādhijaḍānyatvaṃ tadvināśanāśyatvaṃ sarvasyāpi satyatvaṃ yo bhāskaro'ṅgīkaroti taṃ pratyupapatteḥ |
asminpakṣe mūle vyāvahārikapadamaupādhikaparamiti jñeyam |
evamanāditvamapi nirvacanīyamiti || nanveteṣvapyanumāneṣu ghaṭānyatvamupādhiḥ |
na ca pakṣetaratvānnopādhitvam |
pakṣamātrānabhyupagatasādhyakānumāne tādṛśasyaiva vaktavyatvāt |
dūṣakatābījasadbhāve pakṣetaratvarūpamātrasyāprayojakatvāt |
kiñcaite ābhāsasamānayogakṣamāḥ |
ādyaprayoge saṃsargānavacchinnetivattādatmyānavacchinneti prayoge saṃsargābhāvānyonyābhāvavilakṣaṇābhāvāntarasiddhyāpatteḥ |
ghaṭādidharmiketi sthāne ghaṭadharmiketyukte svasyaiva svabhedāpatteśca |
evaṃ dvitīyaprayoge'pīti |
api ca neti prayojakāḥ |
hetusādhyayoranubandhānupalambhe ityata āha -- anukūleti || anyatreti || nyāyāmṛte |
tathā hi |
yadi jaḍānāṃ parasparabhedo na syāt |
yadi caikyaṃ pāramārthikaṃ syāt tarhi dūrasthavanaspatyaikyaṃ pratyakṣaṃ na bhrāntistadbādha eva pramā syāt |
svaparapakṣayoraikyena paradūṣaṇe svakriyāvirodhaḥ syāt |
dūṣaṇasādhanayorekatvena paradūṣaṇameva tatsādhanaṃ syāt |
upādhyāropakatvena jīvayoraupādhikabhedo na syāt |
ghaṭārthinaḥ paṭe pravṛttiḥ syāt |
parasya nityaduḥkhameva bhūṣaṇaṃ syādityādi |
nanu vanaspatyaikyapratyakṣaṃ vyāvahārikyaṃ gṛhṇāti |
tacca nāstīti bhrāntariti cet |
tasyaikyamātragrāhakatvena vyāvahārikatvollekhasyānānubhāvikatvāt |
anullekhipratyakṣasyāpāramārthikārthasvābhāvyādgṛhyamāṇamaikyamāropitameveti cet tarhi eko vanaspatiriti vākyajanyaṃ jñānaṃ pramā syāt |
vākyasya pāramārthikaviṣayatvopapatteḥ |
tadapyāropitāviṣayamiti cet tarhi jīvabrahmaikyamapi tathā syāt |
kiñcāropitasya bādhakonneyatvena nirbādhasya vāyuvanaspatyaikyasya nāropitatvam |
dvau vṛkṣāvityuttarapratyakṣasya bhrāntitvena viṣayābhāvarūpapramātvena rūpabādhatvāyogāt |
tacca vyāvahārikabhedasvakriyāvirodhādiparihāraḥ sambhavatīti tannāparamārthatastadāpattiriti |
|| kiñca ghaṭa iti || ghaṭasaṃsargeṇānavacchinnā pratiyogitā yasya paṭādidharmikatraikālikābhāvasya tasya pratiyogītyarthaḥ |
paṭe paṭātyantābhāvamādāya sādhyaṃ bodhyam |
pakṣe tu ghaṭātyantābhāvasya ghaṭasaṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvena tamādāya sādhyaparyavasānamityanyonyābhāvasiddhiḥ |
tasya tādātmyāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvena ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
traikālikatvaviśeṣaṇaṃ tvanyonyābhāvasya traikālikatvarūpoddeśyasiddhyarthaṃ sā'pi vyāvahārikamanyonyābhāvamādāya siddhasādhanatānivṛttyarthamityagre sphuṭam |
na tu prāgabhāvadhvaṃsābhāvamādāya siddhasādhanatānivṛttyartham |
tayorghaṭasaṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvena siddhasādhanatānavakāśāt |
evaṃ paṭādidharmiketi || yadyapi paṭadharmikasya ghaṭānyonyābhāvasya na siddhānte traikālikatvaṃ paṭādyātmakatvāttasya tathā'pi nyāyarītyedam |
atadguṇasaṃvijñānabahuvrīhiṇā nityadharmikānyonyābhāvābhiprāyako veti bodhyam || ghaṭātyantābhāvo veti || ghaṭapratiyogikapaṭadharmiko yastraikālikābhāvastadanya ityarthaḥ |
paṭātyantābhāvamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya ghaṭapratiyogiketi || dṛṣṭānte ghaṭātyantābhāvamādāya sādhyaṃ bodhyam || paṭasaṃsargo veti || paṭadharmiko yastraikālikābhāvastatpratiyogitāvacchedako ghaṭaniṣṭhadharmastadanya ityarthaḥ |
paṭādisaṃsargānyatvamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya ghaṭaniṣṭheti || dharmapadārthaṃ tādātmyasaṃsargayorviśeṣaṇam |
tena ghaṭatadavacchinnapratiyogitākastraikālikapaṭadharmiko'bhāvaḥ sidhyan anyonyābhāva eva sidhyati |
saṃsargānyatvasya pakṣe bādhāditi bhāvaḥ |
ghaṭasaṃsargānyatvamādāya dṛṣṭānte sādhyamiti bhāvaḥ || ghaṭaprāgabhāvo veti || ghaṭasaṃsargeṇānavacchinnā pratiyogitā yasya tādṛśo yaḥ paṭadharmiko'nādirabhāvastadanya ityarthaḥ |
ghaṭātyantābhāvānyatvamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya saṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitāketi || dṛṣṭāntīkṛte paṭadhvaṃse paṭādidharmikapaṭātyantābhāvānyatvamādāya sādhyasattvāttadarthaṃ ghaṭeti || dhaṭātyantābhāvo yadyapi saṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākastathā'pi na ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitāka iti paṭaghaṭabhedasiddhyarthaṃ paṭādidharmiketi || paṭadharmikapaṭīyarūpādidhvaṃsānyatvamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāyānādīti |
ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākabhinnapaṭādidharmikapaṭatvānyatvamādāya tadvāraṇāyābhāveti || nanu dṛṣṭāntīkṛtapaṭadhvaṃse yathā paṭātyantābhāvanyatvamādāya sādhyam |
tathā pakṣe'pi tadanyatvamevādāya sādhyamastyeveti siddhasādhanam |
na ca tannivṛttyartha ghaṭapratiyogikatvaviśeṣaṇaṃ deyamiti vācyam |
dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyāpatteḥ |
na ca dṛṣṭānte ghaṭaprāgabhāvānyatvamādāya sādhyaṃ bhaviṣyatīti vācyam |
tasya ghaṭasaṃsargānavacichannapratiyogitākatvaghaṭādidharmikatvayorabhāvāt |
agrimagranthavirodhācca |
seyamubhayataḥ pāśārajjuriti cenna |
svapratiyogipratiyogikatvenābhāvaviśeṣaṇāt |
svaśabdaḥ samabhivyāhṛtaparaḥ pakṣe sādhyayojanadaśāyāṃ ghaṭaprāgabhāvaṃ bodhayati |
dṛṣṭānte tadyojane tu paṭadhvaṃsam |
evañca na siddhasādhanaṃ na vā dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyamiti || anādyabhāvapadaṃ tūparañjake bhaviṣyatīti iti na kiñcidanupapannam || ghaṭapradhvaṃso veti || pūrvavadevātra sarvaṃ bodhyam |
nanu jaḍānāṃ parasparaṃ tattvato bhedasyābhāva iti mate ghaṭapaṭayorabhedeti ghaṭātyantābhāvapaṭātyantābhāvayorabhedācca na paṭātyantābhāvasya ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākabhinnatvamiti dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyam |
evaṃ siṣādhayiṣitā'nyonyābhāve'pi na ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvam |
ghaṭātyantābhāvatadanyonyābhāvayorabhedāt |
tathā ca bādha ityata āha -- ghaṭapaṭayorabhedavādineti || idamupalakṣaṇam |
tadubhayātyantābhāvayorabhedavādinā ghaṭātyantābhāvaghaṭānyonyābhāvayorabhedavādinetyapi bodhyam || sādhyavaikalyādīti |
ādipadena bādhaḥ |
dṛṣṭāntetyupalakṣaṇaṃ pakṣasyāpi || anyatheti || vyāvahārikabhedānaṅgīkāra ityarthaḥ |
nanu sarvatrāpi pakṣetaratvasya tatsamīpaśīlasyaivopādheḥ sambhavāt |
sopādhikatvamanyonyābhāvatvasyāpi sādhyaśarīrapraveśena sampannasyābhāsasamānayogakṣematā'prayojakatvaṃ cetyata āha -- anukūlatarkasyeti || anyatra nyāyāmṛte |
uktatvāt uktaprāyatvāt |
yadi ghaṭapaṭayostāttvikamaikyaṃ syāt tarhi ghaṭanāśe paṭanāśaḥ syāt |
ghaṭajñāne paṭajñānaṃ syāt |
siddhaghaṭasya puṃsaḥ paṭasāmagar_yupādānaṃ na syādityādestarkasya sattvāt |
hetoḥ sādhyavyāpyatvena sādhyavyāpyahetvavyāpakatvenopādheḥ sādhyavyāpakatvāt |
na sopādhikatvam |
anyonyābhāvānyatvasyāpi sādhyaśarīre praveśena sampannasyābhāvasyābhāsasamānātmakasyābhāsānāmanukūlatarkābhāvena na samānayogakṣematā |
ata eva nāprayojakatvamapīti bhāvaḥ |
|| kiñceti || ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinneti || paṭaniṣṭhaghaṭātyantābhāvamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinneti |
saṃsargānavacchinnatvamātroktau bhedasiddheḥ prāgghaṭaniṣṭhatādṛśatraikālikābhāvāprasiddhyā sādhyāprasiddhiḥ syādato ghaṭasaṃsargeti |
dṛṣṭānte tādṛśaṃ paṭa[ghaṭa]niṣṭhaṃ paṭātyantābhāvamādāya sādhyopapattiḥ |
tasya ghaṭa[paṭa]saṃsargāvacchinnatve'pi paṭasaṃsargānavacchinnatvāt |
pakṣe coktasādhyasiddhiranyonyābhāvaṃ vinā na paryavasyati |
ghaṭīyaprāgabhāvapradhvaṃsayoḥ pratiyogisamavāyakāraṇavṛttitvena paṭadharmikatvābhāvāt |
prācīnamate pūrvāparakālāvacchinnasamavāyayoreva tatpratiyogitāvacchedakatayā ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnatvā[nnā]bhāvāt |
traikālikātvābhāvācca |
tādṛśābhāvānyonyābhāva eva vaktavya iti tatsiddhiḥ |
na ca paṭadharmiketi viśeṣaṇaṃ vyartham |
paṭaniṣṭhaghaṭabhedarūpoddeśyasiddhyarthatvāt |
traikāliketyapi tadarthameva |
tasya pāramārthikatvasiddhyarthatvamapyagre grantha eva vyaktam |
na ca vyadhikaraṇasambandhāvacchinnaghaṭātyantābhāvaṃ paṭaniṣṭhamādāya siddhasādhanamiti vācyam |
saṃsargāvacchinnapaṭa[ghaṭa]niṣṭhapratiyogitvānirūpakatvasya tadarthatvāt || ghaṭātyantābhāva iti || ghaṭapratiyogikatvaṃ paṭātyantābhāvabhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāyānyatra bhedamaṅgīkṛtya ghaṭapaṭasthala eva vyabhicāravelāyāmetatsādhyasya pravṛttatvānna bhedaghaṭitasādhyāprasiddhiḥ śaṅkyā |
paṭadharmikatvatraikālikatvayoḥ kṛtyaṃ tu pūrvavadeva |
dṛṣṭānte pakṣībhūtaghaṭātyantābhāvabhedamādāya sādhyamupapādanīyam |
pakṣe tatsiddheḥ svabhedamādāya paryavasānāyogāt |
tādṛśānyonyābhāvasiddhiḥ |
na ca paṭaniṣṭhaghaṭapratiyogikanānāsambandhāvacchinnātyantābhāvānāṃ madhye ekasminparabhedamādāya sādhyopapādanasambhavena siddhasādhanatā |
ghaṭātyantābhāvatvāvacchedena sādhyasādhane'pyekasminparabhedamādāya sarvapakṣeṣu sādhyasattvātsādhyatāvacchedakarūpeṇa pakṣatāvacchedakavyāpakatvamabādhitameva |
bhedaghaṭitavyāpakatābhānamanumitau pūrvameva nirākṛtamiti na tadbhānabalādbhedasiddhiḥ syāt |
ghaṭapratiyogikapaṭadharmikatraikālikābhāvatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedasādhane pakṣe'pyuktapratiyogitāvacchedakākrānte sādhyasyābhāvadbādhāpattiriti cenna |
anyonyābhāvaghaṭitaiva yāpakatā sarvatrānumitāvutsargato bhāsate |
laghuśarīratvāt |
tadbādhagrahasahakṛtasāmagryadhīnānumitāvatyantābhāvaghaṭitā bhāsata ityabhyupagamena prakṛte bādhaniścayābhāvenānyonyābhāvaghaṭitavyāpakatayā eva bhāvenābhimatasiddhiḥ |
lāghavajñānabalādvā prakṛte tadbhānāṅgīkārādabhimatasiddheḥ |
athavā''śrayaniṣṭhapratiyogitākatvasvasāmānādhikaraṇyobhayasambandhena ghaṭātyantābhāvatvavadanyatvena sādhyībhūtabhedasya viśeṣaṇīyatayoktabhedānāṃ tādṛśatvena tadvāraṇeneṣṭasiddheḥ || ghaṭasaṃsarga iti || ghaṭasambandhisaṃsarga ityarthaḥ |
ghaṭaniṣṭhatraikālikābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedako yo ghaṭaniṣṭhadharmastadanya ityarthaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte paṭaniṣṭhābhāvāntarīyapratiyogitāvacchedakasaṃsarge etādṛśaghaṭasaṃsargabhedamādāya sādhyamupapādanīnayam |
tatsiddheḥ svānyatvamādāya paryavasānāyogāt |
tādṛśābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakībhūtaṃ ghaṭasaṃsargabhinnatādātmyamupādāya tadbhedo vaktavyaḥ |
prācīnaistādātmyasya saṃsargarūpatvānaṅgīkārāt |
ādhārādheyabhāvaniyāmakasaṃyogasamavāyasvarūpādereva tathātvābhyupagamāt |
sa eva bheda ityuddeśyasiddhiḥ |
prācīnaistādātmyāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvasyaiva bhedatvābhyupagamāt |
na ca tarhi viṣaya[yi]tāsambandhasya bhavaduktasaṃsargapadārthatvābhāvāttatsādhāraṇarūpeṇa pakṣatve'siddhiprasaṅgāttasya pakṣabahirbhāvāvaśyakatve paṭaniṣṭhaviṣayatā(sambanā)vacchinnābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakībhūtaviṣayatābhedamādāya siddhasādhanatā viṣayatvādīnām |
vṛttyaniyāmakatayā tādātmyātiriktavṛttyaniyāmakasambandhasyābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakatvānaṅgīkartṛprācīnanaiyāyikamate tasya tādṛśāvacchedakatvameva nāstītyucyate |
tathā'pyatyantābhāvīyapratiyogitāvacchedaghaṭatvabhedasya ghaṭasaṃsargeṣu sattvātsiddhasādhanameveti vācyam |
saṃsargavidhayā yā vilakṣaṇāvacchedakatā tadvattvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedasyaiva sādhyatvāt |
anyonyābhāvanirūpitapratiyogitānirūpitatādṛśāvacchedakatāyāstādātmya evāṅgīkārāttadbhedasyaiva sādhyatvaprāpteḥ |
ghaṭatvasya vā tathātvānna tadbhedamādāya siddhasādhanatā |
na caikasaṃsarge aparasaṃsargabhedamādāya siddhasādhanam |
tādṛśābhāvīyapratiyogitānirūpitavilakṣaṇāvacchedakatākatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedasya sādhane bādhaḥ |
pakṣasyāpi tathāvidhapratiyogitāvacchedakadharmākrāntatvāditi cenna |
pūrvoktarītyā ghaṭaniṣṭhasaṃsargatvāvacchedena tādṛśapratiyogitāvacchedakabhedasya sādhyatā vyāpakatā ca bhedaghaṭitaiva bhāsata iti vā svasamānādhikaraṇapratiyogitākatvasvasamānādhikaraṇānuyogikatvobhayasambandhena ghaṭasaṃsargatvaviśiṣṭānyatvasya sādhyībhūtabhede niveśanīyatayā vāraṇasambhavāt |
na ca pratiyogitāvacchedake ghaṭaniṣṭhatvaviśeṣaṇaṃ dṛṣṭāntībhūtapaṭasaṃsarge paṭaniṣṭhatādṛśābhāvīyapratiyogitāvacchedakaghaṭasaṃsargabhedābhāvādvyabhicāravāṇāyeti vaktavyam [vācyam] |
tacca na ghaṭate |
tādṛśastambhasaṃsargabhedamādāya sādhyasattvāditi vācyam |
pakṣe'pi tamādāyaiva sādhyasattvopapattyā bhedāsiddhiprasaṅgavāraṇāya tasya sārthakatvāt || ghaṭaprāgabhāvo veti || ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākapaṭādidharmiko yo'nādyabhāvastadanya ityarthaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyoyogitākapaṭādidharmiko'nādyabhāvaḥ paṭarūpaprāgabhāvastadanyatvaṃ vartate |
pakṣe caitādṛśasādhyaṃ sidhyatpaṭabhedamādāyaiva paryavasyati |
