ghaṭātyantābhāvasya ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvavirahāt |
ata eva tadviśeṣaṇaṃ sārthakam |
anyathā'tyantābhavābhedamādāyārthāntaratāpātāt |
saṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvamātraniveśe paṭe paṭabhedasyevānyabhedasyāpi vicāradaśāyāṃ tulyanyāyena sandigdhatayā paṭaniṣṭharūpaprāgabhāvātyantābhāvānāṃ saṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvena saṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākapakṣaniṣṭhānādyabhāvaprasiddhirato ghaṭasaṃsargeti |
anāditvānupādāne paṭaniṣṭharūpadhvaṃsabhedamādāya siddhasādhanatā syādato'nādīti |
nanu paṭaniṣṭharūpaprāgabhāva eva hetorvyabhicāraḥ |
na ca tādṛśarasādiprāgabhāvādibhedasattvānna vyabhicāra iti vācyam |
tathāpīti pakṣībhūtapaṭapragabhāve'pi tamādāya sādhyasattvādeva siddhasādhanatā |
tādṛśābhāvatvāvacchinnabhedasādhane ghaṭaprāgabhāvasya paṭadharmikatvābhāvenoktābhāvatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedasattvātsiddhasādhanam |
paṭe vidyamānānādyabhāveṣu vyabhicāraścādhikaḥ |
paṭadharmikānādyabhāve ghaṭapratiyogitvopādāne bhedasiddheḥ prāgghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnaghaṭapratiyogikapaṭadharmikābhāvāprasiddhyā'prasiddheḥ |
ghaṭa[paṭa]pratiyogikaprāgabhāvasya ghaṭa[paṭa]saṃsarganācchinnatvavirahāt |
paṭadharmikatvācca |
atyantābhāvasya ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvavirahāt |
vyadhikaraṇasambandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākaghaṭapratiyogikapaṭadharmikānādyabhāvabhedamupādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya ghaṭasaṃsargetyasya saṃsargānavacchinnaghaṭavṛttipratiyogitākānyatvaparyantamavaśyaṃ vivaraṇīyatayā tasyāpyaghaṭakatvāt |
anādipadavaiyarthyaṃ ca |
paṭaniṣṭhasaṃyogadhvaṃsāderghaṭapratiyogikatvenaiva vāritatvena tadbhedamādāya siddhasādhanatvābhāvāt |
maivam |
paṭadharmikoktābhāvasyaiva svapratiyogipratiyogikatvasvabhinnatvobhayasambandhena sādhyatvāṅgīkārāt |
paṭadhvaṃsarūpe dṛṣṭānte paṭadharmikapaṭātyantābhāva evoktasambandhena vartate |
pakṣe (ca) ghaṭapratiyogikānādye bhāvāntarabhedamādāya siddhasādhanatvāderanavakāśāt |
na caivaṃ gaganābhāve prameyatvabhede ca paṭaniṣṭhe vyabhicāraḥ |
tatpratiyogipratiyogikābhāvāntarasya tatrābhāvāditi vācyam |
paṭe prameyatvasya svarūpasambandhena sattve'pi vyadhikaraṇasambandhānavacchinnānāṃ bahūnāmabhāvānāṃ sattvenaikasminnaparābhāvamādāya sādhyatvopapatteḥ |
evaṃ gananasya samavāyasvarūpasambandhādyanavacchinnanānābhāvānāṃ ghaṭe sattvenaikābhāvamādāyāparasminsādhyatvānna kutrāpi vyabhicāraḥ |
na ca paṭadharmikatvānāditvaviśeṣaṇayorvaiyarthyam |
ghaṭapratiyogikābhāvānāṃ ghaṭaprāgabhāva uktasambandhenāsattvāt |
ghaṭīya[paṭīya]saṃsargābhāvānāṃ ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnatvenaiva vāraṇāditi vācyam |
paṭe ghaṭabhedasiddheruddeśyatvena paṭadharmikatvasya, bhede jñānakalpitatvābhāvarūpamithyātvābhāvasiddhyarthamanāditvasya sārthakyāt |
na coktābhāvasya svapratiyogipratiyogikatvasambandhamātreṇa sādhyatve'pyuktasādhyasyānyonyābhāvaṃ vinā paryavasānābhāvādbhedaghaṭitobhayasambandhena sādhyatā vyartheti vācyam |
uktavaiyarthyaparihārāya pratiyogipratiyogikatvasya paṭadharmikābhāvāṃśābhāva eva sādhyakoṭau viśeṣaṇīyatvāt |
na ca tarhi svapratiyogipratiyogitādṛśābhāvakatvamātraṃ sādhyamastu tādṛśā[śa]bhāvānyatvaṃ vyarthamiti vācyam |
bahuvrīhyarthasambandhasyāvaśyaṃ niveśanīyatayā tadbhinnatvasyaiva tatkatvapadārthatvāt |
pakṣadṛṣṭāntayoḥ svatvā[sattvā]nanugamāsahiṣṇutāyāṃ svapratiyogipratiyogitvasambandhena svaviśiṣṭo yaḥ saṃsargāvacchinnaghaṭavṛttipratiyogitākānyapaṭadharmikābhāvastanniṣṭhapratiyogitākabhedavattvatādātmyobhayasambandhena kiñcidviśiṣṭatvaṃ pakṣe sādhyam |
saṃsargakoṭau svatvaṃ paricāyakamiti nānanugamaḥ |
sambandhadvayaprayojanaṃ sphuṭameva |
atredamavadheyam |
uktasiddhasādhanatāparihārāya svapratiyogipratiyogikatvasya sādhyatāvacchedakaśarīrāntaḥ pātitayā vā pūrvoktobhayasambandhatvena sādhyatāvacchedakaśarīrāntaḥpātitve vā prakṛtānumānasiddhānyonyābhāva eva vyabhicārāddhetorvastuto duṣṭatvāpattirato bhedāṃśo'nupādeya eva vyarthatvāddoṣasampādakatvācca[tvāt] |
na ca mūlavirodhaḥ |
asyā goranyo'nveṣṭavya ityādāvanyapadasya bhedaghaṭitasājātyāvacchinnārthakatāyā dṛṣṭatvāt |
prakṛte sājātyaghaṭakapratiyogipratiyogikatvamupādāya bhedāṃśaparityāge'pi mūlārthasyāparityaktatayā tadvirodhavirahāt |
pratiyogimātroktāvapi svapratiyogyapratiyogikatvasvabhinnatvobhayasambandhena tādṛśābhāva eva sādhyamiti vastutaḥ pūrvoktasādhye na ko'pi doṣaḥ |
(vastutaḥ) ghaṭasāmānyabhedataddhaṭabhedānu[vu]pādāya parasparasādhyasattvānna vyabhicāraprasaktiḥ |
tathā'pi mūloktānyatvavaiyarthyaparihārāya yathākathañcitsārthakīkartumuktaprayāsa ityalamityāhuḥ |
pare tu ghaṭarūpaprāgabhāvādikamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya ghaṭapratiyogikatvamevābhāve viśeṣaṇaṃ deyam |
ghaṭapratiyogikapaṭadharmikabhedasiddheḥ prāguktaviśeṣaṇākrāntābhāvāprasiddhyā sādhyāprasiddhiriti na śaṅkyam |
paṭādidharmika ityasya paṭāvṛttitvaprāgabhāvābhinnatvaitadubhayābhāvavadarthakatvāt |
ādipadaṃ tu stambhādikamapi niveśyameva prayoktavyamityabhiprāyeṇa |
tādṛśaśca pakṣībhūta eva ghaṭaprāgabhāva iti tadanyatvarūpasādhyaprasiddhyā dṛṣṭānte tamādāyaivopapādanasambhavāt |
pakṣībhūte svasminsvabhedamādāya sādhyāparyavasānāt |
ghaṭapratiyogikapaṭadharmikabhedasiddhiḥ |
ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnaghaṭapratiyogikoktābhāvakatvamātroktau ghaṭaprāgabhāvarūpapakṣa eva tādṛśa iti svavaiśiṣṭyasāmānyaṃ svasminvartata iti siddhasādhanamarthāntaratā ca syādatastadanyatvaparyantānudhāvanam |
dhvaṃsaprāgabhāvapratiyogitayoḥ saṃsargānavacchinnatvamiti matāśrayaṇānna punaḥ sādhyāprasiddhiśaṅkāvakāśaḥ |
ghaṭapaṭobhayavṛttidvitvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedasya paṭādyavṛttitvābhāvenobhayābhāvavattvāt |
ghaṭapratiyogikatvācca tadanyatvamādāya siddhasādhanatānavakāśaḥ |
vyāsajyavṛttidharmāvacchinnabhedasya paṭādirūpapratiyoginyavṛttitvāṅgīkāreṇoktobhayābhāvavirahāt |
aṅgīkāre vā vyāsajyavṛttidharmāvacchinnapratiyogitākānyatvenāpyabhāvasya viśeṣaṇīyatvāt |
ghaṭamātrapratiyogikatvaṃ vā pratiyogyavṛttitvaṃ vā'bhāve niveśyoktadoṣo vāraṇīya iti tu na sat |
prathamaviśeṣaṇena ghaṭadvaya[dvaye]bhedasyāvāraṇena tadanyatvamādāya siddhasādhanatvāvāraṇāt |
dvitīyaviśeṣaṇapakṣe ghaṭātyantābhāvasyāpyanenaiva vāraṇena ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnetyasya vaiyarthyāpatteriti prāhuḥ |
vastutastu ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnetyasya ghaṭasaṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitāko'bhāva iti vigrahaḥ |
ghaṭapadottaraṣaṣṭhyarthasya pratiyogitvasyābhāva evānvayaḥ |
saṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitāketyetadatyantābhāvabhinnatvatātparyakam |
atyantābhāvapratiyogitāyā avaśyaṃ saṃsargāvacchinnatvaniyamāt |
prāgabhāvadhvaṃsapratiyogitvayordharmāvacchinnatvasaṃsargāvacchinnatvāṅgīkārāt |
paṭā[ghaṭā]dītyatrādipadagrāhyatāvacchedakarūpaṃ ghaṭatvābhāvavattvam |
tathā ca ghaṭapratiyogiko'tyantābhāvabhinno ghaṭatvābhāvavaddharmiko yo'nādyabhāvastadanyatvaṃ sādhyaṃ paryavasitam |
atra ghaṭatvānadhikaraṇakapālavṛttighaṭaprāgabhāvabhedamādāya dṛṣṭāntādiṣu sarvatra sādhyasattvanirvāhaḥ |
pakṣe svabhedamādāya paryavasānāyogāddhaṭabhedasya paṭādiniṣṭhasya siddhiḥ |
kapālavṛttirūpaprāgabhāvabhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya ghaṭapratiyokiti || ghaṭatvānadhikaraṇavṛttighaṭātyantābhāvabhedamādāya tadvāraṇāyātyantābhāvabhinneti || dhaṭadhvaṃsabhedamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāyānādīti |
ghaṭatvetaradharmānavacchinnaghaṭavṛttipratiyogikatvaṃ vaktavyam |
tena ghaṭāsamavetabhedasyāpi ghaṭapratiyogi(tā)katayā prāgabhāvaniṣṭhatadbhedamādāya nārthāntaratā |
ghaṭaprāgabhāvapratiyogitāyā dharmānavacchinnatvāttadbhedamādāya dṛṣṭānte sādhyatvanirvāhaḥ |
ghaṭānyasminsarvatra ghaṭabhedasiddheruddeśyāyāḥ sampattaye [ca] gha[pa]ṭatvābhāvavaddharmiketi viśeṣaṇamiti sāram || ghaṭadhvaṃso veti || pūrvānumānavadeva sarvam |
jñānocchedyasvarūpaṃ mithyātvādityādimatanirāsapūrvakaṃ bhedasya pāramārthikatvad siddhaye nityatvaviśeṣaṇamanusandheyam |
ghaṭābhedasya (paṭe) svasiddhyabhāvavelāyāṃ paṭātyantābhāvasyāpi ghaṭasaṃsargā(na)vacchinnapratiyogitākatvena paṭātyantābhāvapratiyogitātvamupādāya kathaṃ paṭasvarūpe dṛṣṭānte sādhyasattvamityāśaṅkāṃ vyāvahārikabhedasya sarvavyavahāranirvāhakṣamatve (na uktadūṣaṇa)parihārakṣamatvamapi vartata ityāśayena pariharati -- ghaṭapaṭayorityādinā na doṣaprasaṅgaścetyantena || anyonyābhāvo'pi ghaṭasaṃsargāvacchinno na bhavatīti krameṇa |
bādhaḥ sādhyavaikalyādītyatrādipadārthaḥ |
(trai)kālikatvādineti padārthāya kālikatvādineti || ādinā'nāditvanityatvayorgrahaḥ || vyāvahārikatve ceti || ajñānakalpitatve jñānocchedyatve cetyarthaḥ || anukūlatarkāṇāmiti || nyāyāmṛtādāvityarthaḥ |
bhedasya pāramārthikatve 
ghaṭanāśe sati paṭānāśaḥ syāt |
bandhamokṣayoraikyāpattyedānīṃ muktatvāpattyā tadarthaṃ śravaṇādyanupapattirūpastarka iti jñeyam || nopādhīti |
pakṣetaratvamupādhiḥ |
gha(pa)ṭatādātmyānavacchinnaghaṭapratiyogikatvamapyabhāve niveśya prayoge pañcamābhāvasiddhyāpattyā''bhāsasāmyaprayojakatvaṃ (ce)vetyarthaḥ |
|| kiñcetyādi || ghaṭasaṃsargeṇānavacchinnā pratiyogitā yasya tādṛśo yaḥ paṭādidharmikaḥ traikāliko'bhāvastatpratiyogītyarthaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte paṭātyantābhāvamādāya sādhyasattvaṃ, pakṣe ca tatpratiyogitvabādhāddhaṭapaṭabhedamādāyaiva sādhyaparyavasānāduddeśyasiddhiḥ |
ghaṭātyantābhāvapratiyogitāmādāya siddhasādhanavāraṇāya ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinneti |
tatra ghaṭapadaṃ dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyavāraṇāya |
na ca saṃyogena paṭo nāstīti pratītyā saṃyogasāmānyasyaiva pratiyogitāvacchedakatvāvagāhanāt kathaṃ paṭābhāvasya ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvamiti vācyam |
lāghavena pratiyogisaṃsargasyaiva pratiyogitāvacchedakatvāt |
ghaṭatvasahitasaṃsargānavacchinnatvavivakṣaṇādvā doṣābhāvāt |
ātmānātmabhedasiddhyā'rthāntaravāraṇāya paṭādidharmiketi |
paṭe ghaṭabhedasiddheruddeśyatve paṭadharmikatvamabhāvaviśeṣaṇam |
daṇḍādau tatsiddheruddeśyatve ca taddharmikatvameva tathetyāvedayitumādipadam |
ghaṭadhvaṃsaprāgabhāvapratiyogitāmādāya siddhasādhanavāraṇāya traikāliketi |
na ca tayoḥ pratiyogisamavāyimātravṛttitvaniyamena paṭādidharmikatvābhāvādeva vāraṇasambhava iti vācyam |
tādṛśaniyame mānābhāvāt |
dhvaṃsaprāgabhāvayoḥ pratītibalena pratiyogisambandhimātravṛttitvaucityāt |
paṭāderapi saṃyogādinā pratiyogisambandhitvāt |
yattu ghaṭadhvaṃsaprāgabhāvayoḥ saṃsargābhāvatvena ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvābhāvādeva vāraṇaṃ sambhavatīti |
taccintyam |
dhvaṃsaprāgabhāvayoḥ saṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvānaṅgīkartṛtārkikaikadeśimatenaitatprayogāt |
ata eva siddhānte paṭādidharmikabhedasya dharmisvarūpatvena traikālikatvānupapattāvapi na bādhaprasaṅgaḥ |
yattvatadguṇasaṃvijñānabahuvrīhiṇā paṭādipadasya nityamātraparatvānna doṣa iti |
tadasaṅgatam |
tathā sati prakāntaghaṭapaṭabhedāsiddhiprasaṅgāt |
paṭādidharmika samavāyādipratiyogitvasiddhyā'rthāntaravāraṇāyābhāvapadam |
evamagre'pi |
|| ghaṭātyantābhāvo veti || atra ghaṭātyantābhāvānyatvamādāya dṛṣṭānte sādhyasattvam |
pakṣe tadbādhādanyonyābhāvānyatvamādāyaiva sādhyaparyavasānāduddheśyasiddhiḥ |
paṭātyantābhāvādanyatvamādāya siddhasādhanavāraṇāya ghaṭapratiyogiketi viśeṣaṇaṃ pūrvavat |
yadyapi ghaṭātyantābhāvasyāpi ghaṭapratiyogikapaṭādidharmikatraikālikābhāvatvena tadanyatvā sambhavādbādhaḥ |
tathāpi yādṛśābhāvādanya ityekavacanāntena samāsāt |
tādṛśayatkiñcidabhāvānyatvasyaiva sādhyatvalābhānna doṣaḥ |
na caivaṃ tādṛśaghaṭapaṭobhayābhāvānyatvamādāya siddhasādhanam |
ghaṭātyantābhāvatvāvacchedena tadanyatvasādhanāt |
|| ghaṭasaṃsargo veti || atra ghaṭātyantābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedako yo ghaṭasaṃsargastadanyatvamādāya dṛṣṭānte sādhyaṃ bodhyam |
pakṣe ca tadayogāt paṭadharmikabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakībhūtatādātmyānyatvamādāya sādhyaparyavasānāduddeśyasiddhiḥ |
na ca ghaṭātyantābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakaghaṭatvānyatvamādāya siddhasādhanamiti vācyam |
paṭadharmikatraikālikayatkiñcidabhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakatvāvacchinnabhedasya sādhyatvāt |
yatkiñcidabhāvapadena ghaṭātyantābhāvaparigrahe tatpratiyogitācchedakatvāvacchinnabhedasya pakṣe bādhitatvena bhedasiddherāvaśyakatvāt |
na ca ghaṭasyaikasaṃsarge aparasaṃsargāvacchinnātyantābhāvapratiyogitāvacachedakatvāvacchinnabhedasya siddhatvāt siddhasādhanamiti vācyam |
ghaṭasaṃsargatvāvacchedena sādhyasiddheruddeśyatvena doṣābhāvāt |
paṭasaṃsargāvacchinnapaṭātyantābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakabhedamādāya siddhasādhanabhaṅgāya ghaṭaniṣṭheti |
anyat pūrvavat |
saṃsargatvaṃ ca mitho bhinnapratiyogyanuyogikasambandhatvaṃ, tādātmyātiriktasambandhatvaṃ veti dhyeyam ||
|| ghaṭaprāgabhāvo veti || ghaṭasya yaḥ saṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākapaṭādidharmikānādyabhāvastadanya ityarthaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte ghaṭaprāgabhāvānyatvamādāya sādhyasattvam |
pakṣe ca tadanupapattyā paṭadharmikabhedabhinnatyamādāyaiva sādhyaparyavasānāduddeśyasiddhiḥ |
ghaṭadhvaṃsānyatvena siddhasādhanavāraṇāyānādīti |
anyadharmikabhedasiddhyā'rthāntaravāraṇāya paṭādidharmiketi |
ghaṭātyantābhāvānyatvena siddhārthatābhaṅgāya saṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitāketi |
paṭādidharmikarūpādiprāgabhāvānyatvamādāya taddoṣavāraṇāya ghaṭeti |
tasya ca ghaṭapratiyogikatvābhāvānna doṣaḥ |
atrābhāvāntarasya dṛṣṭāntatvasambhave'pi paṭadharmikānādyabhāvānyatvasya paṭadhvaṃse sphuṭatvāttasyaiva dṛṣṭāntīkaraṇamiti jñātavyam |
kecittu ghaṭasaṃsargeṇānavacchinnapratiyogitāko yaḥ paṭādharmikānādhyabhāvastadanyatvaṃ sādhyārthaḥ |
ghaṭātyantābhāvānyatvamādāya siddhasādhanavāraṇāya ghaṭasaṃsargetyādi |
tatra ghaṭapadaṃ tu dṛṣṭānte paṭātyantābhāvānyatvamādāya sādhyasattvanirvāhārtham |
ghaṭapaṭabherūpoddeśyasiddhyarthaṃ paṭādidharmiketi |
paṭīyarūpādidhvaṃsānyatvamādāya siddhārthatvanirāsāyānādīti |
prathamadalasya ghaṭasaṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitākānyaparatayā tathāvighapaṭatvādyanyatvamādāya taddoṣavāraṇāyābhāvapadam |
nanvevaṃ dṛṣṭāntavat pakṣe'pi paṭātyantābhāvānyatvamādāya siddhasādhanam |
na ca ghaṭapratiyogikatvenābhāvo viśeṣaṇīyaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyāpatteḥ |
na ca ghaṭaprāgabhāvānyatvamādāya dṛṣṭānte sādhyasattvanirvāha iti vācyam |
tasya ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvapaṭādidharmikatvaśūnyatvāt |
ghaṭapaṭayorityagrimagranthavirodhācca |
tasya paṭātyantābhāvānyatvamādāya dṛṣṭānte sādhyasattvopapādanaparatvāditi cenna |
svapratiyogipratiyogikatvenābhāvaviśeṣaṇāt |
svapadasya pratijñāyāṃ pakṣaparatvādudāharaṇe ca dṛṣṭāntaparatvāt na ko'pi doṣa ityāhuḥ |
taccintyam |
anādipadavaiyarthyāpatteḥ |
anumānapañcake'pyatyantābhāvamādāyaiva dṛṣṭānte sādhyasattvopapādane traikālikatvānāditvanityatvarūpavibhinnaviśeṣaṇopādānasya nirbījatvaprasaṅgācca |
etena vyāvahārikabhedasiddhyā'rthāntaravāraṇamanādipradaprayojanamityapāstam |
tāvanmātrasya tatprayojanatve pūrvānumāne'pyanāditvasyaiva lāghavena niveśanaucityāt |
agrimagranthastvanukūlatayaiva vyākhyāsyate |
tasmāddhaṭadhvaṃsaprāgabhāvayoḥ saṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvapaṭādidharmikatvābhyupagamābhiprāyaka evāyaṃ grantha iti yuktamutpaśyāmaḥ |
|| ghaṭadhvaṃso veti || ghaṭasya yaḥ saṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākapaṭādidharmikanityābhāvastadanya ityarthaḥ |
dṛṣṭānte ghaṭadhvaṃsānyatvamādāya pakṣe ca ghaṭabhedānyatvamādāya sādhyamupapādanīyam |
ghaṭaprāgabhāvanyatvamādāya siddhasādhanavāraṇāya nityeti |
uddeśyasiddhyarthaṃ paṭādidharmiketi |
ghaṭātyantābhāvānyatvamādāya siddhārthatābhaṅgāya saṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitāketi |
paṭīyarūpadhvaṃsānyatvamādāya taddoṣanirāsārthaṃ ghaṭeti |
atrāpi paṭadhvaṃsāderdṛṣṭāntatvasambhave'pi pūrvavat sphuṭapratipattaye prāgabhāvagrahaṇam |
nanvatra ghaṭapakṣakānumāne dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavaikalyam |
ghaṭapaṭayorabhedamate paṭātyantābhāvasyāpi ghaṭasaṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvena tadanyābhāvapratiyāgitvasya paṭādāvabhāvāt |
evaṃ ghaṭātyantābhāvādipakṣakānumāneṣu siddhasādhanam |
paṭātyantābhāvasyāpi ghaṭapratiyogikatayā paṭasaṃsargasyāpi ghaṭaniṣṭadharmatayā paṭaprāgabhāvadhvaṃsayorapi ghaṭapratiyogikatvapaṭadharmikatvasambhavena ca tattadanyatvarūpasādhyasya ghaṭātyantābhāvādirūpapakṣe siddhatvādityata āha -- ghaṭapaṭayoriti || paṭātyantābhāvāderityādipadena paṭasaṃsargapaṭaprāgabhāvapaṭadhvaṃsānāṃ parigrahaḥ |
dhaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvāderityādipadena ghaṭapratiyogikatvadhaṭaniṣṭhadharmatvapaṭadharmikatvānāṃ rāhityaparigrahaḥ |
dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavaikalyādītyādipadena siddhasādhanaparigrahaḥ |
yattvatyantānyonyābhāvayoraikyamate ghaṭānyonyābhāvasya ghaṭasaṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitākānyatvāyogād bādhasyādipadena saṅgraha iti tanna |
ubhayasiddhapramāṇavirodhābhāvena bādhānavakāśāt |
atyantābhāvasya satyatvena mithyābhūtānyonyābhāvaikyāyogācca |
anyathā paṭātyantābhāvasya traikālikatvāyogena dṛṣṭāntavaikalyasya duruddharatvaprasaṅgāt |
tasmādbhāvādvaitanirāsaparo'sau grantha ityavadeyam |
|| anyatheti || uktavyavasthānirvāhakavyāvahārikabhedānaṅgīkāra ityarthaḥ |
siddhaghaṭasya labdhaghaṭasya paṭasāmagryāṃ pravṛttirna syādityarthaḥ |
kāraṇībhūtapaṭecchānupapatteriti bhāvaḥ |
traikālikatvādinetyādinā'nāditvanityatvaparigrahaḥ || tadayogāditi || traikālikatvādyayogādityarthaḥ |
vyāvahārikasyāvidyākāryatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvetāvatā'pyarthāntaratvaṃ durvārameva |
yāvatkālavṛttitvarūpatraikālikatvasya vyāvahārikabhede'pi sambhavāt |
kālasyāpyavidyākāryatvena tadabhāve tadabhāvāvaśyambhāvāt |
evaṃ pūrvottarakālarāhityarūpānāditvanityatvayorapi sambhavo draṣṭavyaḥ |
maivam |
prāgabhāvāpratiyogitvamanāditvam |
dhvaṃsāpratiyogitvaṃ nityatvam |
tadubhayaśālitvaṃ ca traikālikatvam |
na caitad vyāvahārike sambhavati |
na ca vyāvahārike'pyavidyāvadanāditvamiṣyata iti vācyam |
svarūpato'nāditve'vidyākāryatvānupapatteḥ |
anyathā nityasya nivṛttirityapi syāt |
pratiyogitādātmyāvidyānadhīnapratītikatvarūpānāditvavivakṣāyāmarthāntaraśaṅkānavakāśācceti dik |
nanūktānumāneṣu pakṣetaratvādikamupādhiḥ |
anukūlatarkābhāve pakṣetaratvasyāpyupādhitvasambhavāt |
ata eva tādātmyānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvenābhāvaviśeṣaṇādābhāsasamānayogakṣematvamaprayojakatvaṃ cetyata āha -- anukūlatarkasyeti || ghaṭapaṭādyabhedasya pāramārthikatve ghaṭapaṭādisvarūpaṃ pāramārthikaṃ syādityatiprasaṅgarūpasyātraivoktatvāt |
anyatreti pāṭhe jaḍabhedābhāve muktisāṅkaryādiprasaṅgarūpasya nyāyāmṛtādau coktatvādityarthaḥ |

agnerāpaḥ |
adbhyaḥ pṛthivī |
pṛthivyā oṣadhayaḥ' ityādayaḥ śrutayo'pi jaḍabheda udāhartavyāḥ |
prapañceti || uktānumāne'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvasvarūpāsiddhivyabhicāraparihāre prayāsagauravamityaparitoṣādāha -- kiñceti || ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinneti || ghaṭasaṃsargeṇānavacchinnā avyāvṛttā pratiyogitā yasyāsau ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākaḥ |
yatpratiyogitā ghaṭasaṃsargeṇa nāvacchidyate ghaṭasaṃsargalakṣaṇapratiyogitāvacchedakarahita iti yāvat |
sa cāsau paṭādidharmikaḥ paṭādivṛttiḥ |
traikālikaḥ trikālavṛttiryo'bhāvaḥ tatpratiyogītyarthaḥ |
atra paṭādidharmikābhāvapratiyogītyukte prāgabhāvadhvaṃsābhāvapratiyogitvamādāyārthāntaratā atastraikāliketi |
tayośca krameṇa pratiyogipūrvottarakālamātravṛttitvāt |
tāvatyukte'tyantābhāvapratiyogitvamādāyārthāntaratā |
ato ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitāketi |
atyantābhāvasya saṃsargarūpapratiyogitāvacchedakopetatvānna tathātvam |
tāvatyukte dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyam |
evaṃvidhasyānyonyābhāvarūpatvena tatpratiyogitvasya paṭe pareṇānabhyupagamāt |
ato ghaṭeti |
tādṛśaśca paṭātyantābhāvaḥ tatpratiyogitvaṃ paṭe |
pareṇābhyupagamāt |
paṭātyantābhāvasya paṭasaṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvena ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvāt |
evamabhāvatrayavyāvṛttābhāvapratiyogitve ghaṭasya siddhe tādṛśābhāvasyānyonyābhāvānatirekaddhaṭapaṭabhedasiddhiḥ |
uktānumāne'tyantābhāvavyāvṛttyarthaṃ ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitāketi viśeṣaṇapraveśādgauravamityaparitoṣādāha -- ghaṭātyantābhāva iti || ghaṭapratiyogiketi || ghaṭapratiyogikaḥ paṭādidharmikaḥ traikālikoyo'bhāvaḥ tadanya ityarthaḥ |
atra paṭapratiyogikapaṭādidharmitraikālikapaṭātyantābhāvānyatvasiddhyā'rthāntaravāraṇāya ghaṭapratiyogiketi || itaratpūrvavaddraṣṭavyam || paṭābhāvavaditi || tatroktavidhaghaṭātyantābhāvānyatvasadbhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
pratiyogitāvacchedakapakṣakānumānamāha -- ghaṭasaṃsarga iti || paṭādidharmikoyoyaṃ traikālikobhāvaḥ tasya pratiyogitāvacchedakībhūtoyoyaṃ ghaṭaniṣṭhadharmaḥ tadanya ityarthaḥ |
ayamarthaḥ |
traikālikābhāvohidvividhaḥ |
atyantābhāvo'nyonyābhāvaśca |
tatrātyantābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedadharmaḥ saṃsargaḥ |
anyonyābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakaṃ tādātmyam |
tatra ghaṭasaṃsargasyapaṭādidharmikatrakālikābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakaghaṭaniṣṭhadharmānyatve siddhe ghaṭe tādṛśābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakadharmāntarasiddhiḥ |
tasya tādṛśābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakatvañca tādṛśābhāvaṃ vinānasidhyati |
tādṛśaścānyonyābhāva eveti ghaṭapaṭayorbhedasiddhiriti atra paṭādidharmikābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakadharmānyatvamātrasādhane paṭātyantābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakapaṭaniṣṭhasaṃsargānyatvasiddhyāthā |
ntaram |
ato ghaṭaniṣṭheti || paṭādidharmikābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakaghaṭaniṣṭhadharmānyatvasādhane pradhvaṃsaprāgabhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakapūrvottarakālīnatvarūpadhamā |
nyatvasiddhyā'rthāntaram |
atastraikāliketi || paṭaniṣṭhasaṃsargeti || tatroktavidhasaṃsargarūpadharmānyatvasadbhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
traikālakatvaviśeṣarāpraveśenānumānadvayamāha -- ghaṭaprāgabhāva ityādinā || ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinneti || ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākaḥ paṭādidharmiko yo'bhāvaḥ tadanya ityarthaḥ |
atra pradhvaṃsānyatvasiddhyā'rthāntaravāraṇāyānādīti |
tasya sāditvāt |
atyantābhāvānyatvasiddhyā'rthāntaravāraṇāya ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitāketi || (ghaṭapradhvaṃsavaditi) ghaṭasaṃsargeti || atra prāgabhāvānyatvasiddhyā'rthāntaravāraṇāya nityeti || tasyānityatvāt |
śeṣaṃ pūrvavat || ghaṭaprāgabhāvavaditi || vyākhyātaprāyametat |
nanvatra dvitīye ghaṭapakṣakānumāne paṭarūpadṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyam |
ghaṭepaṭapratiyogikātyantābhāvevidyamāne'pi tasya ghaṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvaṃ nāsti |
ghaṭapaṭayoraikyena paṭapratiyogikātyantābhāvasyāpi ghaṭasaṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogikatvena ghaṭatvasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākatvābhāvādityata āha -- ghaṭapaṭayoriti || atyantābhāvāderityuddeśyam |
pratiyogikatvāderiti vidheyam |
nanu bhedābhāvātkathamidamaṅgīkāre tayorekatvāditi bhāvaḥ || siddhaghaṭasyeti || siddhoghaṭo yasya sa tathoktaḥ tasyetyarthaḥ || paṭasāmagrīti || ghaṭapaṭayoraikyena ghaṭasya lābhe paṭasyāpi labdhatvena paṭalābhāyatatsāmagrayāṃ pravṛttirna syādityarthaḥ || vyāvahāriketi || uktānumāneṣu pāramārthikatvaviśeṣaṇābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ || tadayogāditi || brahmajñānabādhyatvasyaiva vyāvahārikatvenānyonyābhāvasya vyāvahārikatve brahmajñānottarambādhaḥ syāt |
evaṃ ca na trikāle vidyamānatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanu jaḍabhedasādhakānumāneṣu sarvatra pakṣetaratvasyopādhitvam |
ghaṭaḥ paṭasaṃsargānavacchinnapratiyogitākaghaṭādidharmikatraikālikābhāvapratiyogityapisādhanasaulabhyenābhāsasamānayogakṣematvaṃ hetvaṅgīkāreṇa sādhyaparityāgasambhavenāprayojakatvaṃ cetyata āha -- anukūlatarkasyānyatroktatvāditi || jaḍabhedābhāvebhedasiddhyasiddhyordoṣatadabhāvayoścābhedena svakriyāvirodhaḥ syāt |
muktisāṅkaryaṃ ca syādityanukūlatarkasya nyāyāmṛte dvitīyaparicchede uktatvādityarthaḥ |
upalakṣaṇametat |
ghaṭapaṭayostādātmyasya pāramārthikatvāṅgīkāra ityādinā prāguktatvāt dūrasthāvanaspatyoścaśuktirūpyayoścābhedagrāhipratyakṣantatvāvedakaṃ syādityādinā vakṣyamāṇatvāccetyapi draṣṭavyam |
(33) yaduktamabhede pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇamiti tadasat |
ahaṅkārasya cidacitsaṃvalanātmakasya tvayā bhinnatvāṅgīkāreṇāhaṃpratyayasya tadabhede'pramāṇatvāt |
ādyānumāne'vedyatvarūpasvaprakāśatvasyāsmābhiranaṅgīkārādasiddhiḥ |
abādhyatvaṃ tarhyātmatvamiti cettarhyasmābhirdehādeḥ svarūpeṇābādhyatvāṅgīkārādvyabhicāraḥ |
na ca tadapi pakṣatulyamiti vācyam |
tvayā(api) dehātmābhedasyādhyastatvāṅgīkāreṇa tadabhedasyāpāramārthikatayā paramārthato na bhidyanta iti sādhyābhāvāt |
bhedābhāvasyaivābhedatvāt |
jñānatvaṃ cedātmatvaṃ (tadā) naiyāyikaṃ pratyasiddheḥ |
jñānādhāratvaṃ cedātmatvaṃ tavaivāsiddheḥ |
upādherbādhavyabhicārānyatarodbhāvanadvārā dūṣaṇatvapakṣe paramātmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānanusandhātṛtvamupādhiśca |
evaṃ bhede pramāṇānyabhidhāya yaduktamabhede pramāṇatritayamastīti tatra tāvatpratyakṣaṃ nirākartumāha -- paduktamityādinā || ahaṅkārasyeti || yadyapyahampratyaya ātmana ekatvaṃ gṛhṇāti |
tathā'pi tvanmate tasya nātmaviṣayaḥ kintvahaṅkāra eva |
sa ca cidacitsaṃvalanātmakaḥ |
cetanācetanamiśrāntaḥkaraṇarūpa iti yāvat |
ato'sāvātmanā bhinna evāṅgīkṛtaḥ |
tathā cātmabhinnāhaṅkāraviṣayasyāhampratyayasyātmābhede pramāṇatvenābhidhātumaśakyatvādityarthaḥ |
upalakṣaṇametat |
astu vā'hampratyayasyātmaikyaviṣayatvaṃ tathā'pi na tena jīveśvarābhedasiddhiḥ |
ayaṃ stambha ityekākāreṇānubhūyāmānasyāpi stambhasya piśācābhedāsiddherityapidraṣṭavyam |
athābhedaviṣayāṇyanumānāni krameṇa nirācaṣṭe || ādyānumāna ityādinā |
vimatā ātmānaḥ paramātmanaḥ paramārthato na bhidyante ātmatvatparamātmavadityanumāna ityarthaḥ |
tatrātmatvaṃ nāma svaprakāśatvamiti pareṇaivoktam |
svaprakāśatvaṃ ca na svaviṣayaprakakāśarūpatvam |
kartṛtvakarmatvayorekatra tvanmate'naṅgīkārāt |
kintvavedyatve satyaparokṣavyavahāraviṣayatvamevetyaṅgīkṛtam |
tacca pramaṇavedyatvāṅgīkāriṇāmasmākamasiddhamityarthaḥ |
dehādernityatvādyāropitadharmarūpeṇa bādhyatve'pi śaktirajatādivatsvarūpeṇa bādhyatvābhāvāddhetumati dehādau sādhyābhāvādvyabhicāra ityāha -- tarhīti |
māyimate dehādeḥ svarūpeṇaiva bādhyatvāṅgīkārādasmābhirityuktam |
yadyapi siddhānte dehāderetmani svarūpeṇa vā''tmatvādidharmarūpeṇa vā''ropitatvaṃ nāstyeva |
mama deha iti hyeva na deho'hamiti pramā |
upacāraśca kṛṣṇo'hamitītyādivacanāt |
yairvastramālyābharaṇānulepanaiḥ śvabhojanaṃ svātmatayopalālitamityādipurāṇavacanāni tu dehātmanoḥ spaṣṭavivekābhāvaparāṇītyuktatvācca |
tathā'pyatra nityatvādidharmarūpeṇāropamaṅgīkṛtya svarūpeṇābādhyatvoktiriti mantavyam |
na ca sarvātmanā'bādhyatvameva hetutvena vivakṣitamiti vācyam |
pakṣasapakṣayorapi dharmarūpeṇa bādhyatāsadbhāvādasiddhisādhanavaikalyayorāropāpattiriti || tadapi dehādikam || pakṣatulyatvamiti || sandigadhasādhyamamityarthaḥ |
tathā ca tatra sādhyābhāvaniścayābhāvānna vyabhicāracodanāvakāśa iti bhāvaḥ |
dehādau sādhyābhāvamupapādayati -- tvā'pīti |
ātmābhedavādinā'pītyarthaḥ |
tadabhedasya dehātmābhedasya |
nanu dehātmābhedasyādhyastatvenāpāramārthikatve'pi prakṛtasādhyasya bhedābhāvatvena tasya satyatvopapattyā kathaṃ dehādau sādhyābhāva ityata āha -- bhedābhāvasyaiveti || tathā cābhedo bhedābhāva iti paryāyatvānna dehādau sādhyasattvamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanvevaṃ cetsiddhānte kathaṃ kāryakāraṇādīnāṃ bhedābhedobhayāṅgīkāra iti cet |
satyam |
yadyapyabhedo na bhedābhāvarūpaḥ kintu tadvirodhī |
virodhaśca vadhyaghātukabhāvarūpaḥ |
sa ca virodhaḥ kvacidviśeṣabalena pratibaddho bhavati |
yatrātyantābheda eva tatra bhedasāmānyaghaṭako viśeṣo nāstyeva |
tathā ca na siddhānte kā'pyanupapattiḥ |
tathā'pi paramate naivamaṅgīkartuṃ śakyate |
bhedābhedayoḥ kvacitsāmānādhikaraṇyasya viśeṣānaṅgīkāriṇā nirvoḍhumaśakyatvāt |
atastanmate bhedābhāvasyaivābhedarūpatvenāvaśyāṅgīkāryatvāttanmatarītyaiveyamuktiriti || nanu siddhānte'pi kathamabhedasya bhedābhāvādbahirbhāvaḥ |
bhedasya tādātmyapratiṣedharūpatvāt |
abhāvābhāvasya pratiyogisvarūpatvaniyamena tādātmyasya bhedābhāvarūpatvāvaśyambhāvāditi cenna |
tathā satyatyantābhāvapratiyoginorbhedābhedayorubhayorapyaprāmāṇikatvaprasaṅgāt |
prāmāṇikasyātyantābhāvapratiyogitvā sambhavasyopapāditatvāt |
ato bhedo nāma tādātmyapratiṣedharūpo'pyanyonyebhāva eva |
ata eva tasya dharmisvarūpatvamaṅgīkṛtam |
bhāvābhāvasvarūpatvānnānyonyābhāvatā pṛthagityādau |
abhedastu bhāvasvarūpa eva na bhedātyantābhāvarūpaḥ |
ata eva tasyāpi dharmisvarūpatvamaṅgīkṛtam |
atyantābhāvarūpatve dharmisvarūpatvāyogāt |
tathā ca siddho'bhedabhedābhāvayorbheda ityalam || jñānatvaṃ cediti || yadyapi siddhānte'pi na jñānatvamātmanaḥ |
jñāturjñānatvāyogāt |
ata eva pramāṇalakṣaṇaṭīkāyāṃ svatantrajñānamīśajñānamitīśajñānasya lakṣaṇamabhidhāya tatra jñānamitīśavyudāsa ityuktam |
tathā'pi saviśeṣatādātmyasattvānnaiyāyikaṃ pratītyuktam |
tena jñānātmanoratyantabhedasyaivāṅgīkārāt || tavaivāsiddheriti || jñānarūpasyātmano jñānādhāratvānaṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ || paramātmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānanusandhātṛtvamiti || atra duḥkhānanusandhātṛtvamityuktau parasparaduḥkhānanusandhātṛtvasya jīveṣvapi sattvādupādheḥ sādhanavyāpakatā syāt |
ataḥ paramātmānanusaṃhiteti duḥkhaviśeṣaṇam |
tataśca paramātmānanusaṃhitaṃ yadduḥkhaṃ tadananusandhātṛtvamityarthaḥ |
tataśca jīveṣu parasparaduḥkhānanusandhātṛtvasattve'pi na paramātmānanusaṃhitātvāvacchedena duḥkhānanusandhātṛtvam |
svasvaduḥkhānusandhānasattvāt |
ato na sādhanavyāpakateti bhāvaḥ |
yadi ca duḥkhaśabdena duḥkhamātraṃ vivakṣyate tadopādhi vyatirekeṇa bādhādyudbhāvane'prayojakatāśaṅkāyāmanukūlatarkasphoraṇārthamidaṃ viśeṣaṇamiti bodhyam |
nanūpādheḥ pratipakṣonnāyakatvenaiva dūṣaṇatvaṃ na svataḥ |
prakṛte ca na pratipakṣaḥ sambhavati |
vimatā ātmānaḥ paramātmanaḥ paramārthato bhidyante paramātmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvād vyatirekeṇa paramātmavaditi pratipakṣānumānaṃ kāryam |
etacca prāk jīvo brahmapratiyogikadharmisattāsamānasattākabhedādhikaraṇamityatraivoktam |
ato vyarthamidamupādhyudbhāvanamityata uktamupādherityādi |
mā'stvasyopādheḥ pratipakṣonnāyakatvena dūṣaṇatvam |
tathā'pi sādhyavyāpakatvādupādheḥ pakṣādvyāvṛtau pakṣe sādhyābhāvasyāvaśyakatvādbādhaḥ |
pakṣa eva vyabhicāro vā bhaviṣyatīti bhāvaḥ |
sa evopādhidoṣo'pīti pramāṇalakṣaṇādau pratipakṣonnāyakatvenaivopādherdūṣaṇatāyā uktatvādbādhavyabhicārānyatarodbhāvanadvārā dūṣaṇatvapakṣa ityuktam |
ādhikyākhyanigrahasthānaparihārāyānyatareti |
evaṃ bhede pramāṇaṃ pratiṣṭhāpyābhede pramāṇaṃ parācaṣṭe || yaduktamityādinā || cidacitsaṃvalanātmakasyeti || ahamiti pratyayasyāhaṅkāraviśiṣṭacaitanyaviṣayakatvenāhamityekākāreṇānubhavena na śuddhaikyasiddhiriti || vastutastvahampratyayasya śuddhaviṣayatve'pi nābhedaviṣayatā |
caitreṇa caitra evāhaṃ maitraśca bhaviṣyāmītyananubhavāt |
kintu sarvātmanā pratyuktarūpaikākārākrāntatvamātraṃ gocarayati |
anyathā ghaṭeṣu ghaṭatvaikākārapratyayātteṣāmaikyaṃ syāt |
tatra sthūlatvāṇutvādibhedapratyayo'stīti cet |
atrāpi sukhaduḥkhādibhedapratyayo'stītyuktam |
etenāhamanubhavasya viśiṣṭaikyasiddhiriti nirastam || ādya iti || vimatā ātmāna ityādyanumāna ityarthaḥ |
hetūkṛtamātmatvaṃ nāma svaprakāśatvamityuktam |
tadeva vedyatvaṃ svaprakāśatvaṃ vā |
nādya ityāha -- avedyatve satīti |
na dvitīyaḥ |
tasyāsiddheḥ |
śiṣyaśaṅkayā'styaprayojakatvaṃ brahmānandādyananusandhānamupādhiścetyuttaraṃ bādhyam |
etena svavyavahāre svātiriktasaṃvidanapekṣatvaṃ svaprakāśatvamiti parāstam || svarūpeṇeti || anyathā vijñātasya samyagviditatvarūpaṃ bādhyatvāṅgīkāre'pi svarūpeṇābādhyatvāṅgīkārādityarthaḥ |
sarvathā bādhyatvavivakṣāyāmasiddhiścetyapi jñeyam || adhyastatvāṅgīkāreṇeti || anyathā dehātmavādicārvākanirākaraṇānupapattiriti dhyeyam || iti sādhyābhāvāditi || paramārthata ityuktaviśeṣaṇasya bādhitatvādityarthaḥ |
nanu tarhi cidācidaikyābhāve tasya sarvādvaitavādinaṃ kathamityata āha -- bhedābhāvasyaiveti || bhedāpāramārthikatvābhāvasyetyarthaḥ |
nādvaitavāditvavādivirodha iti vākyaśeṣaḥ |
yadvā paramātmano na bhidyanta ityatra paramātmapratiyogikabhedābhāvamātrasyaiva vivakṣitatvena dehāderapi pakṣatve dehātmabhedāpattirityata āha -- bhedābhāvasyaiveti || naiyāyikaṃ pratīti bhedavādināṃ sarveṣāṃ prativaditvāditi bhāvaḥ |
vastutastu jñeyabhāvājjñānatvaṃ durnivāham |
savyabhicāraścetyūhyam || upādheśceti || yatra paramātmabhedastatropādhiḥ |
śiṣṭaṃ tu prayojakatāsphuraṇārtham |
nanūpādheḥ satpratipakṣonnāyakatvenātra brahmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvānumānasya sāmyameva prāptamiti nāsya svapakṣanirṇāyakatvaṃ syāttathā cāsyaivānumānasya pṛthak svapakṣanirṇāyakaprayogo vyartha ityata uktamupādherbādhavyabhicārodbhāvanadvārā dūṣakatāpakṣa iti || paroktātmatvasya prayojakatvādasya ca vyāhatisattvena tadbādhakatvenopapattiriti bhāvaḥ |
|| ahaṅkārasyeti || cidacitsaṃvalanātmakasyetyātmabhinnatve hetuḥ |
tadabhede ātmābhede'pramāṇatvāt |
pramāṇatvābhāvādityarthaḥ |
athavā pramāṇatvāditi padacchedaḥ |
tadā tadabhede'haṅkārabhede |
ahaṅkārābhede eva pramāṇatvānnātmabheda iti yojanā || ādyānumāna iti || vimatā ātmānaḥ paramātmanastattvato na bhidyante ātmatvātparamātmavadityatretyarthaḥ || asiddhiriti || ātmatvaṃ na hi jātiḥ kintu svaprakāśatvamiti tvayaivoktaṃ taccāvedyatve satyaparokṣavyavahārayogyatvamiti tvayaiva nirucyate svagrantheṣu |
tatrāvedyatvasyāsmanmate ātmatvasyaivābhāvādasiddhirityarthaḥ || tarhīti |
manmate dehāderāropitaṃ dharmato bādho'pi svarūpeṇābādhyatvāt |
paramātmapratiyogitāttvikabhedābhāvādvyabhicāra ityarthaḥ |
sarvātmanā bādhyatvaṃ na hetūkartuṃ śakyate pakṣasapakṣayorapi dharmato bādhāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu dehātmābhedasyādhyastatve'pi dehātmabhedābhāvaḥ satyaḥ kinna syādityata āha -- bhedābhāvasyaiveti || lāghavāditi bhāvaḥ || upādheriti || sādhyavyāpakasya pakṣāvṛttivṛttitve bādhodbhāvanaṃ tadvṛttitve vyabhicārodbhāvanaṃ dvāramityarthaḥ || paramātmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvamiti || atra paramātmānanusaṃhiteti nopādhighaṭakam |
avyāvṛttakatvāt |
kintūpādhi vyatirekeṇa bādhonnayanadaśāyāmanukūlatarkasphoraṇārtham |
satpratipakṣonnāyakatvena dūṣakatve tu satpratipakṣatve'pi pakṣamātravyāvarttakaviśeṣaṇavattvādayamevopādhiḥ syāditi nānena satpratipakṣonnayanaṃ kartuṃ śakyamiti sa pakṣastyakta iti vā bodhyam |
suptamūrchitetyādivyabhicāritvaṃ tu prāgeva vivecitam |
evaṃ svapakṣe pramāṇāni vyavasthāpya bhedābhāve paroktapratyakṣādipramāṇāni kramānnirākaroti || yaduktamityādinā || cidacitsaṃvalanātmakasyeti || antaḥkaraṇāvacchinnacitta evāhaṃ padārthatayā'ntaḥkaraṇānāṃ ca bhinnatvena tadbhedāvaśyakatvāt |
sā pratītiḥ sarvacidaikye na pramāṇaṃ kintu antaḥkaraṇa(tvarūpa)jātyaikya eva pramāṇaṃ syādityarthaḥ |
tadabhede cidabhede'pramāṇatvāt pramāṇatvābhāvādityarthaḥ || ādyānumāna iti || vimatā ātmāna ityanumāne ātmatvaṃ svaprakāśatvamiti pūrvapakṣāvasare uktatvāttannirākaroti || avedyatve'ti || aparokṣavyavahāragocaratve sati avedyatvamiti paraiḥ svagranthe niṣkarṣasya kṛtatvāt tatāsiddhimāha -- asmābhiriti || svātiriktasaṃvidanapekṣatvamapyasiddhaṃ suṣuptikāle (sva)vyavahārasyaivābhāvāt |
suṣuptyuttarakālīnasyābhilapanarūpasya vyavahārasya dehāditādātmyādhyāsamālādijñāneṣṭasādhanatājñānādirūpasvabhinnajñānasāpekṣatāyā
api niyamena sattvāt |
svātiriktasaṃvidadhīnasvavyavahārasyaivābhāvādityapi draṣṭavyam |
upalakṣaṇavidhayā diṅmātrapradarśanaṃ kṛtam |
ata (eva) etādṛśa(sva)prakāśatvasya hetutvasambhavātkathamanumānamasiddhamiti śaṅkānavakāśaḥ || dehāderiti || svarūpeṇeti || ātmatādātmyarūpeṇa vā parānāyattasvasvātmikatvarūpeṇa vā bādhyatvātsvarūpeṇetyuktam |
sarvātmanā bādhyatvābhāvādātmanyapi nāsti, dehatādātmyādirūpeṇa bādhyatvāditi bhāvaḥ || pakṣatulyatvamiti || paramātmapratiyogikapāramārthikabhedābhāvasya dehādāvapi mayā'ṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ || tvayā'pīti || apirnaiyāyikādisamuccaye || dehātmābhedasyeti || sādhyābhāvāditi || bhedābhedarūpaviruddhadharmayormadhye'bhedasya pāramārthikatāyāstvayaivoktatvena bhedasya pāramārthikatvaṃ balādvā''gatamiti vyabhicāro duruddhara iti bhāvaḥ || naiyāyikaṃ pratīti |
tasyāpi bhedasatyatvavāditvādidānīṃ tatprakriyāmavalambyaiva vicārasya pravṛttatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
tavaiveti || vṛttijñānopādāne tadādhāratvaṃ manasa eva na pakṣībhūtātmani |
svarūpajñānagrahaṇe na tadādhāratvamātmani svasya svādhāratvāyogādityasiddhirityarthaḥ || upādheriti || sādhyavyāpakatvena gṛhītopādhyabhāvātsādhyābhāvasyaiva niścayena bādha eva tasyāntarbhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
satpratipakṣotthāpakatve samabalatvaṃ syāditi samabalapramāṇavirodhasyaiva satpratipakṣatvāt |
tacca na ghaṭate |
uktopādhyabhāvarūpaparamātmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvasyānukūlatarkasācivyena bhedanirṇāyakatāyā eva sattvātpakṣa ityuktam |
satpratipakṣotthāpakatve pakṣetaratvaparyavasāyyetadupādhyabhāvarūpasatpratipakṣahetāvapi 
pakṣetaratvasyopādhitvaṃ vaktuṃ śakyata iti tatpakṣaparityāga iti kecit |
viśiṣṭopādhyabhāvarūpahetau pūrvoktarītyā vyāptigrahaupayikatvādviśiṣṭasyopādhitvaṃ duḥkhānanusandhātṛtvamātrasyaivopādhitvamitarāṃśasya sādhyābhāvānumāne'nukūlatarkotthāpakatvena sārthakyamevetyavadheyam |
evaṃ bhede pramāṇamupapādya paroktamabhedapramāṇaṃ khaṇḍayati -- yaduktamiti || ahaṅkārasyeti || ahampratyayaviṣayasyāntaḥkaraṇāvacchinnacidrūpasya śuddhacidrūpatvābhāvānna tatpratyayasya śuddhacidaikyapramāṇatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
yadapyuktamanumānāni cetyādi tadapyasadityāha -- ādyānumāna iti || vimatā ātmanaḥ paramātmanaḥ paramārthato na bhidyante |
ātmatvāt |
paramātmavadityanumāna ityarthaḥ |
atra hetubhūtamātmatvaṃ svaprakāśatvamityuktam |
taddūṣayati -- avedyatveti || prakāśāviṣayatvarūpasya svetaraprakāśāviṣayatvarūpasya vetyarthaḥ |
asmābhiranaṅgīkārādityanena svātmakaprakāśaviṣayatvarūpasvaprakāśatvasya kriyākarmavirodhena parairanaṅgīkārādasiddhiriti sūcitam |
ito'tiriktasvaprakāśatvaṃ tu durvacameveti bhāvaḥ |
tarhi na svaprakāśatvamātmatvam |
api tarhyanyadevetyāśaṅkate -- abādhyatvamiti || nanu dehāderāropitakartṛtvādyākāreṇa bādhyatvāt kathamabādhyatvamityāśaṅkāparihārāya svarūpeṇetyuktam |
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ |
sarvathā'bādhyatvasya hetutve'siddhiḥ |
ātmano'pyāropitākāreṇa bādhyatvāt |
ataḥ sattvenābādhyatvaṃ vācyam |
tacca sattvaprakārakapramitiviṣayatvaparyavasitaṃ dehendriyādau sādhyaśūnye'pyastītyanaikāntyamiti |
nanu dehādāvapyātmābhedasādhanānna doṣa ityāśaṅkāṃ nirāha -- na ceti || pakṣatulyaṃ prakṛtadharmitulyaṃ sandigdhasādhyakamiti yāvat |
niścitasādhyābhāvavati vyabhicārasyaiva doṣatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
dehādeḥ pakṣatulyatvamasiddham |
tatrātmābhedāṅgīkāre'pasiddhāntāpatterityāha -- tvayā'pīti || adhyastatveti || anyathā dehātmanoritaretarādhyāsasamarthanānupapatteriti bhāvaḥ |
tadabhedasya dehaparamātmābhedasyāpāramārthikatayā pāramārthikabhedābhāvarūpasādhyābhāvādityarthaḥ |
nanvabhedasyāpāramārthikatve'pi kutastādṛśasādhyābhāva ityata āha -- bhedābhāvasyeti || na cābhedastādātmyam |
tacca svarūpameva na bhedābhāva iti vācyam |
ātmasvarūpasyāpyadhyastatvena mithyātvāpātāt |
na cādhyastamapi dehādisvarūpameva mithyā, nātmasvarūpamiti vācyam |
itaretarādhyāse vaicitryakalpanasyāyuktatvāt |
dehātmanoḥ pāramārthikabhedābhāve tata eva tadubhayasvarūpaikyāpatteśca |
idamupalakṣaṇam |
dehādeḥ pakṣatulyatve'pi sandigdhānaikāntyaṃ durvārameva |
anukūlatarkābhāve tasyāpi doṣatvādityapi bodhyam |
|
|| asiddheriti || naiyāyikenātmano jñānādhāratvasyaivopagamāt pakṣadṛṣṭāntayoḥ sādhanasya vyāpteścāsiddherityarthaḥ |
yadyapi vṛttijñāne vyabhicāraḥ siddhānte'pi śakyate vaktum |
tathāpi nityajñānatvasya hetutvena tathetyāśayaḥ || tavaivāsiddheriti || ātmano jñānamātratvāṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ |
na cājaḍatvamātmatvamiti vācyam |
jaḍatvasyānātmatvarūpatayā parasparāśrayāt |
na ca pṛthivyādidravyānyatve sati guṇādyanyatvamātmatvamiti vācyam |
māṃ prati guṇādyanyatvāsiddheḥ |
tadanuktau tārkikamate guṇādau vyabhicārāt |
kathañcit hetunirvacane'pyanukūlatarkābhāvādupādhyādigrastatvaṃ durvāramityāha -- upādheriti || atra paramātmānanusaṃhitānanusandhātṛtvamātraṃ duḥkhānanusandhātṛtvamātraṃ vopādhiḥ |
prathame duḥkheti svarūpakīrtanam |
dvitīye paramātmānanusaṃhitetyupādheḥ sādhyavyāpakatvopapādanamityavagantavyam |
nanūpādherbādhavyabhicārādyanātmakatvādadūṣaṇatvamityata uktam || dūṣaṇatvapakṣa iti || sākṣādanumityavirodhitvena dūṣaṇatvāyogādbādhavyabhicārodbhāvanadvāreti || parārthaprasaṅgādudbhāvanetyuktam |
bādhonnayanadvārā vyabhicāronnayanadvārā vetyarthaḥ |
prakṛte copādhyabhāvena pakṣe sādhyābhāvānumānasyopādhi vyabhicāreṇa hetau sādhyavyabhicārānumānasya ca sambhavāditi bhāvaḥ |
yadvā nanu pakṣe sādhyasandehena vipakṣe ca hetvabhāvena kvacidupādhevyabhicāronnāyakatvāsambhavātkathaṃ dūṣaṇatvamityata uktam || bādhavyabhicārodbhāvanadvāreti || bādhonnītavyabhicāronnayanadvāretyarthaḥ |
tathā copādheḥ pakṣe sādhyābhāvaniścayasampādanadvārā vyabhicāronnāyakatvaṃ sambhavatītyāśayaḥ |
atra ca bādhonnāyakatvenāpi dūṣaṇatvaṃ bodhyam |
evaṃ bhedepramāṇatritayamupapādya yaduktabhedepratyakṣaṃ tāvadityādinā pramāṇatrayaṃ tannirākurvanpratyakṣaṃ tāvannirākaroti || yaduktamityādinā || ahaṃ kārasyeti || ahamiti pratyakṣaṃ khalvātmābhede mānamuktam |
tattadā syādyadi tasyātmaviṣayakatvaṃ syāt |
naca tadasti |
tasya tvanmatechetanācetanācetanamiśritāntaḥkaraṇaviṣayatvāṅgīkārāt |
tasya cātmano bhinnatvāt |
ato natadviṣayakāhampratyaya ātmābhedepramāṇamityarthaḥ |
athātmābhedasādhakānumānāni nirācaṣṭe || ādya ityādinā || vimatā ātmānaḥ paramārthataḥ paramātmano na bhidyante ātmatvāt paramātmavadityatra pareṇaiva khalvātmatvaṃ svaprakāśatvaṃ najātiviśeṣa ityuktam |
tatra svaprakāśatvaṃ svaviṣayaprakāśatvarūpaṃ kartṛkarmavirodhena pareṇa nāṅgīkriyata iti pramāṇāntarāvedyatvarūpameva tadaṅgīkāryam |
tacca māṃ pratyasiddham |
mayā''tmanaḥ pramāṇāntaravedyatvāṅgīkārādityarthaḥ |
tarhyanyadevātmatvamastviticet tatkimabādhyatvarūpamutajñānatvarūpamathajñānādhāratvarūpamiti vikalpānhṛdinidhāyādhyantāvaddūṣayituṃ śaṅkate -- abādhyatvamiti || idaṃ cātmanastvayāpyaṅgīgṛtamiti na pūrvoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ |
dūṣayati -- tarhi ti || vyabhicāra iti || abādhyatvarūpahetumatidehādāvātmabhedābhāvarūpasādhyābhāvādvyabhicāra ityarthaḥ |
vyabhicāramāśaṅkyanirākaroti || naceti || tat dehādi |
pakṣatulyaṃ pakṣe yathā''tmabhedābhāvaḥ sādhyate tathā dehādāvapi atastatra sādhyābhāvasya sandigdhatvena sādhyātyantābhāvaniścayavati hetuvṛttyabhāvānna vyabhicāra ityarthaḥ |
nanūktarītyā niścitavyabhicārābhāve'pi sandigdhavyabhicāraḥ syādeva |
sādhyābhāvasandehavatihetuniścayasyaiva sandigdhavyabhicāratvāditi cenna |
tathātve sarveṣāṃ hetūnāṃ sādhyābhāvasandehavati pakṣe vidyamānatvenānumānamātrocchedāpatteḥ |
kintu niścitasādhyābhāvavatihetusandeha evasandigdhavyabhicāraḥ |
nacāyaṃ prakṛte'stīti bhāvaḥ |
pakṣatulyatvamevāsiddhamityāha -- tvayeti || paramārthata iti || pāramārthikaṃ paramātmapratiyogikabhedābhāvarūpaṃ yatsādhyaṃ tadabhāvādityarthaḥ |
kuta ityata uktaṃ radabhedasyeti || dehātmābhedasyetyarthaḥ |
tadapikuta ityata uktam |
tvayeti || adhyastatvamāropitatvam |
anyonyasminnanyonyātmakatāmadhyasyeti tadīyabhāṣyāditi bhāvaḥ nanvatrabhedābhāva eva sādhyate nābhedaḥ ato mayā dehābhedasyādhyastatvāṅgīkāreṇa tasyāpāramārthikatve'pi dehātmabhedābhāvasya pāramārthikatvenāstyeva dehādau sādhyamityata āha -- bhedābhāvasyeti || tathācābhedasyādhyastatvāṅgīkāre bhedābhāvasyāpi tadaṅgīkṛtamiti bhāvaḥ |
dvitīyayamāśaṅkya nirācaṣṭe || jñānatvaṃ cediti || dehasyacajñānarūpatvābhāvānnoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ || naiyāyikaṃ pratīti || jñānasamavāyikāraṇatvamātmatvamiti vadatā tenātmano jñānarūpatvānaṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ |
tṛtīyamāśaṅkate -- jñānādhāratvamiti || na ayārikenātmano jñānādhāratvāṅgīkārānnoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ || tavaivāsiddheriti || tvanmate ātmano nirguṇatvena jñānādhāratvānaṅgīkārāduktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ || paramātmānanusaṃhiteti || paramātmānanusaṃhitaṃ paramātmānanubhūtaṃ yadduḥkhaṃ tadananusandhātṛtvaṃ tadananubhavitṛtvamityarthaḥ |
yatrātmābhinnatvaṃ tatra paramātmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānanusandhātṛtvaṃ yathā paramātmani |
yatrātmatvaṃ na tatra paramātmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānanusandhātṛtvam |
jīveṣvātmatvavatsuparamātmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvasyaiva satvena sādhanā vyāpakatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanūpādheḥ sākṣāddhetudoṣatvābhāvena pratipakṣodbhāvakatvena hetudoṣatvaṃ vācyam |
evaṃ cātmānaḥ paramātmanaḥ paramārthato bhidyante |
paramātmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvādvyatirekeṇa paramātmavadityupādhi vyatirekeṇa pratipakṣānumānaṃ kāryam |
idaṃ cavyartham |
ātmabhedasādhanāvasara evāsyoktatvādityata uktam upādherbādhavyabhicārodbhāvanadvārā dūṣaṇatvapakṣa iti || upādheḥ sādhanāvyāpakatve sādhanavataḥ pakṣadvayāvartamāna upādhiḥ sādhyavyāpakatvena sādhyamapi vyāvartayati |
tathāca pakṣe sādhyābhāvaniścayādbādhaḥ |
pakṣe sādhyābhāvapamāyā evabādhatvāt |
tathoktarītyā pakṣe sādhyābhāvaniścaye tasyaiva vipakṣatvāttatra hetoḥ satvānna vyabhicāraḥ |
hetorvipakṣavṛttitvasyaiva vyabhicāratvāditi yanmataṃ tadanusāreṇedamupādhyudbhāvanam |
natu tasya svavyatirekeṇa pratipakṣotthāpakatvamiti matānusāreṇa |
ato navaiyarthyamiti bhāvaḥ |
vimatā ātmānaḥ paramātmano'paramārthato (aparamārthato'pi) na bhidyante ātmatvāt paramātmavadityābhāsasamānayogakṣematvaṃ ca |
anukūlatarkābhāvādaprayojakatā ca |
dvitīye devadattaśabdena taccharīrāvacchinnacaitanyavivakṣāyāṃ yajñadattādiśarīrāṇāṃ devadattaśarīrāvacchinnacaitanyādhīnatvāsambhavena tadbhogāyatanatve sādhye bādhaḥ |
anavacchinna(caitanya)vivakṣāyāṃ tasyābhoktṛtvena bādhaḥ |
suptamūrcchitaśarīreṣu vyabhicāraḥ |
teṣāmapi pakṣasamatve (pakṣatve) bādhaḥ |
na ca vācyaṃ bhogāyatanatvātyantābhāvānadhikaraṇatvaṃ sādhyamiti |
tvayā bhogāyatanatvasya mithyātvāṅgīkāreṇa tadadhikaraṇasya śarīrasya tadatyantābhāvādhikaraṇatvāṅgīkārāt |
tṛtīye sādhyāniruktiḥ |
na cādhiṣṭhānayāthātmyajñānanivartyatvaṃ tat |
nivṛttiśabdena dhvaṃsavivakṣāyāṃ mithyātvāsambhavāt atyantābhāvavivakṣāyāṃ ca bādhaḥ |
tvayā'pi vyavahāradaśāyāṃ bhedāṅgīkāreṇa (bhedāṅgīkāre) tadayogāt |
pāramārthikatvākāreṇa tathātvamiti cenna |
abādhyatvarūpasya pāramārthikatvasya tvayocyamānabādhyatvajñānādhīnajñānatvenānyonyāśrayāt |
|| vimata ityādi |
vimatātmanāṃ paramātmapratiyogikāpāramārthikabhedasya tenāṅgīkārāttadabhāvasādhanamaniṣṭam |
dṛṣṭānte pāramārthikabhedasya tenāṅgīkārāt sādhyavaikalyaprāptayā'numānasyābhāsatvaṃ ca jñātavyam || dvitīya iti || vimatāni śarīrāṇi devadattasyaiva bhogāyatanāni śarīratvāddevadattaśarīravadityarthaḥ |
atra bhogāyatanatvātyantābhāvānadhikaraṇatvaṃ sādhyam |
tena suṣuptamūcchritamṛtaśarīreṣu na bādho nāpi vyabhicāra iti yatprāk pūrvapakṣiṇoktaṃ tadidānīṃ dūṣayiṣyan prastāvanāya pūrvapakṣiṇā''śaṅkya parihṛtameva dūṣaṇaṃ svayamāha -- suptamūcchriteti || yadarthamidaṃ dūṣaṇamabhihitaṃ tadidānīmāśaṅkya nirākaroti || na ca vācyamiti || mithyātvāṅgīkāreṇeti || tanmate svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvarūpatvānmithyātvasyeti bhāvaḥ || tṛtīya iti || bhedo mithyā bhedatvāccandrabhedavadityatretyarthaḥ |
prāṅniruktameva mithyātvamāśaṅkya nirākaroti || na ceti || atyantābhāvavivakṣāyāṃ veti || atra nivṛttiśabdena yadyapyatyantābhāvavivakṣā kartuṃ na śakyate |
tasyājanyatvāt |
jñānanivartyatvasya ca jñānajanyanivṛttipratiyogitvarūpatvāt |
tathā'pi nivṛttapratiyogitayā jñāyamānatvameva nivartyatvamiha vivakṣaṇīyam |
ubhayatrāpi bādhamupapādayati -- tvayā'pīti |
bhedamithyātvavādinā'pi tvayā vyavahāradaśāyāṃ jñānottarajīvanmuktidaśāyāṃ bhedānuvṛttyaṅgīkāreṇa jñānena bhedadhvaṃsasyaivābhāvāt |
atyantābhāvasya tu vyavahāradaśāyāṃ bhedāṅgīkāreṇa sutarāmayogāttadayogānniruktamithyātvāyogādityarthaḥ |
kecittu pradhvaṃsārthakatvapakṣe mithyābhūtasya vināśāyogāditi bhāva iti bādhopāpādanaṃ bahireva kṛtvā tvayā'pīti vākyamatyantābhāva evopapādanārthaṃ pravṛttamityāhuḥ |
atyantābhāvapakṣamaṅgīkṛtya bādhaṃ parihartuṃ śaṅkate -- pāramārthikatvākāreṇeti || tathātvamiti || atyantābhāvapratiyogitvamityarthaḥ || abādhyatveti || pāramārthikatvaṃ nāmābādhyatvam |
tacca bādhyatvajñānādhīnajñānam |
abhāvajñānasya pratiyogijñānādhīnatvāt |
bādhyatvaṃ ca pāramārthikatvākāreṇātyantābhāvapratiyogitvam |
tathācābādhyatvasiddhau tadrūpeṇātyantābhāvapratiyogitvarūpabādhyatvasiddhiḥ |
tatsiddhau ca tadabhāvarūpābādhyatvasiddhirityanyonyāśrayādityarthaḥ |
|| aparamārthato'pi na bhidyanta iti || vyāvahārikabhedenāpi rahitā iti yāvat || paramātmavaditi || tasya parapratiyogikabhedābhāvena vyāvahāribhedasyābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ || anukūlatarkābhāvāditi || yadi paramātmābhedo na sttarhyātmatvaṃ na syāt |
nānyato'stidraṣṭetyādinā'nyasya jñātṛtvaniṣedhāditi tarko'stīti cenna |
yadi paramātmābhedaḥ syāt |
kena kaṃ paśyediti tasya jñātṛtvaniṣedhāditi vaiparītyena tarkasya parānukūlatvāditi dik || dvitīya iti || vimatāni śarīrāṇītyanumāna iti śeṣaḥ || adhiṣṭhānatvāsambhaveneti || tasya paricchinnatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
adhīnatvāsambhaveneti pāṭhe yajñadattādiśarīrāṇāṃ yajñadattaprayatnānuguṇavyāpāravikalatvena tadbhogāyatanatvāditi bhāvaḥ || bādha iti || apasiddhāntaścetyapi grāhyam |
nanu devadattānyaviśiṣṭapraviṣṭaviśeṣyabhinnaviśeyakaviśiṣṭāntaramiti tadbhogāyatanatvameva devadattabhogāyatanatvamiti cenna |
ekasya viśeṣyasya viruddhāviśiṣṭadvayaniveśaḥ kiṃ svarūpeṇota viśeṣaṇāntaropodbalitena |
ekasyaiva śuklatvapītatvayordarśanāt |
daṇḍitvakuṇḍalitvayoraviruddhatvopapattiḥ |
na dvitīyaḥ |
anavasthādyapatteriti || tadatyantābhāvānadhikaraṇatvāṅgīkārāditi || svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvaṃ mithyātvamityaṅgīkāreṇātyantābhāvādhikaraṇatvaṃ sādhyamiti cenna |
tvanmate sarvasya sarvātmakatve sarvadharmāṇāṃ sarvatra sattvena pratiyogyasamānādhikaraṇātyantābhāvasyaivāprasiddhatvāt |
vastutastu hetoraprayojakatvaṃ devadattakarmārjitatvamupādhiśceti mūrdhābhiṣiktaṃ dūṣaṇadvayam |
na ca vimatāni śarīrāṇi devadattakarmārjitāni śarīratvādityupādherapi pakṣe sādhayituṃ śakyatvāditi bhāvyam |
tatra devadattabhogāyatanatvasyopādhitvāt |
dvayoḥ sandigdhatvena śaṅkitopādhitvopapatteḥ |
dvayoryugapatsādhane caikena vā bhedalābhādādhikyena nigraha iti || tṛtīya iti || bhedo mithyā bhedatvādityanumāna ityarthaḥ || sādhyāniruktiriti || vyāvahārikatvaprātibhāsikatvayoḥ pratyekaṃ pakṣadṛṣṭāntānanugamādanugatasyānirvacanāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu niruktamevetyatastadāśaṅkya nirāha -- na ceti || nivṛttiśabdasya dvedhāsambhāvito'rtho nobhayamapītyāha -- tadayogāditi || atyantābhāvapratiyogibhedasya svātyantāsattvaprāpterdharmāṅgīkāre caikasyādhiṣṭhānajñānotpattau bhedasyāpi na kasyāpi tatpratītiḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvatyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ na svarūpeṇa yenāsattvaṃ syātkintu dharmāntareṇeti śaṅkate -- pāramārthikatveti || abādhyatveti || prakārāntareṇa pāramārthikatvasya durvacatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
abādhyatvaṃ pāramārthikatvam |
tava bādhyatvaṃ nāma svarūpeṇāniṣedhyatve sati pāramārthikatvākāreṇātyantābhāvapratiyogitvamiti punaḥ pāramārthikatvapraveśādanyonyāśrayatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
|| tṛtīye tviti || bhedo mithyā bhedātvāccandrabhedavadityatretyarthaḥ |
tatsādhyam || nivṛttiśabdeneti || adhiṣṭhānayāthātmyajñānajanyadhvaṃsapratiyogitvaṃ tajjanyātyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ vā na sādhyam |
tvayā brahmajñānottaradaśāyāṃ jīvanmuktidaśāyāṃ bhedānuvṛttyaṅgīkāreṇa tadayogāt |
atyantābhāvasya ca janyatvāyogādityapi bodhyam || caramavṛtteḥ pūrvavṛttyanadhikaviṣayatvena tadanivṛtyanivartakatvāyogāścetyapi bodhyam || pāramārthikatvākāreṇeti || jīvanmuktidaśāyāṃ pāramārthikatvākāro nivartate |
ata eva dagdhapaṭāyamānatvāditi bhāvaḥ || abādhyatvarūpasyeti || bādhyatvaniruktau pāramārthikatvapraveśitasya nābādhyatvagarbhatvādanyonyāśraya iti bhāvaḥ |
|| vimatā ātmāna iti || aparamārthata iti || tathā ca vyāvahārikaparamātmabhedābhāvasādhanasyāpyāpāta iti bhāvaḥ || dvitīya iti || 
sarvāṇi śarīrāṇītyanumāna ityarthaḥ || tadadhikaraṇatvāṅgīkārāditi ||
tathā ca tadadhikaraṇatvasādhane bādha iti bhāvaḥ |
pūrvapakṣabhāgasthaitadvākyasyānye tviti rītyā yathāśrutasādhyaparatayā vyākhyānapakṣe'pi bhogāyatanatvāderanubhavabādha eva draṣṭavyaḥ || tṛtīya iti || bhedo mithyā bhedatvādityanumāna ityarthaḥ || sādhyāniruktiriti || asattvasādhane'pasiddhānto bādhaśca sadasadvilakṣaṇatvasādhane'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatā'taḥ sādhyāniruktirityarthaḥ || bādha iti || tadupapādayati -- tvayā'pīti |
janyajanakabhāvādivyasthāyā bhedādhīnatayā''tmajñānena tannivṛttau tajjanyatvādeḥ sādhyaghaṭakasyaivāyogādbādhaḥ |
atyantābhāve tajjanyatvasyaivābhāvāttaddhaṭitasādhyasattvānirvāhādbādha ityarthaḥ || pāramārthikatvākāreṇeti || jñānajanyapāramārthikatvāvacchinnapratiyogikadhvaṃsapratiyogitvaṃ tadartha ityarthaḥ || abādhyatvarūpasyeti || pāramārthikatvasya bādhyatvābhāvārūpatayā bādhyatvaśarīre'bādhyatvaṃ niviṣṭamityātmāśraya ityapi draṣṭavyam |
nanu jñānajanyadhvaṃsapratiyogitvameva bādhyatvam |
na pāramārthikatvasya taccharīre niveśaḥ |
kintu jñānena pāramārthikatvena tasminnadhyastena viśiṣṭasya jagato nāśo bhavati |
ata eva dagdhapaṭāyamānatayā jagadavasthānaṃ jīvanmuktau |
tathā ca vastutastādṛśadhvaṃsamādāya bādhyatvamupapādanīyamiti vācyam |
vastutaḥ tādṛśadhvaṃsasya tādṛśadhvaṃsatvenaiva niveśa idānīṃ jñānaviṣayatāviśiṣṭaghaṭasya nāśāttasya bādhyatvavyavahārasyedānīmapyāpattiḥ |
adhiṣṭhāne yathā''tmajñāneti niveśe'dhiṣṭhānatvasyāropaviśeṣyatvādanyasyānatiprasaktasya nirvaktumaśakyatvāt bhedapratītestayā tasya ca svamithyātve'siddhiḥ prāgasiddhatayā'prasiddhaviśeṣaṇatvādikaṃ bodhyam |
|| vimatā ātmāna iti || yadyapyatra vyāvahārikabhedasya paraṃ pratyasiddhyā sādhyāprasiddhiḥ |
tathāpi vyavahāragocarabhedaśūnyatvasya sādhyatvānna doṣaḥ |
paramate pāramārthikasyaiva vyavahāragocaratvāt |
yadvā brahmajñānetarābādhyabhedaśūnyatvaṃ sādhyam |
na ca pratyakṣaviruddhatvānnaivamanumānasambhava iti vācyam |
paramārthato na bhidyanta ityasya pramāṇatrayaviruddhatvāt |
tacca vyutpāditam || anukūlatarkābhāvāditi || na caikye lāghavamevānukūlatarka iti vācyam || lāghavasya pramāṇabādhitatvāt |
anyathā paramalāghavāt śūnyavādamevāṅgīkuryāḥ |
brahmātiriktaviyadādyaśeṣaprāmāṇikaprapañcasyātyantānupapannāvidyākalpitatvakalpana eva gauravācca |
|| tadbhogāyatanatva iti || devadattaśarīrāvacchinnacaitanyabhogāvacchedakatve sādhye bādha ityarthaḥ |
anyathā yajñadattaśarīrasukhādau devadattasya mamedamityādyanusandhānāpatteriti bhāvaḥ || bādha iti || sādhyāprasiddhyā pakṣe sādhyasattvānirvāha ityarthaḥ |
atha devadattapratiyogikapāramārthikabhedābhāvavadbhogāvacchedakatvasya sādhyaghaṭakatvānnāyaṃ bādhaḥ |
yajñadattādestādṛśabhedābhāvavattvasambhavāt |
athavā devadattabhinnabhogānavacchedakatve sati bhogāvacchedakatvamātraṃ sādhyamastu |
bhokturdevadattatvamarthāt setsyatīti cedevamapi devadattayajñadattādīnāṃ pāramārthikabhedagrāhakoktavidhapratyakṣādipramāṇabādho durvāraḥ |
na ca teṣāmaprāmāṇyam |
prāmāṇye bādhakābhāvāt |
balavadvirodhipramāṇānupalambhāditi |
evamatra devadattakarmanirmitatvamupādhirityādikaṃ ca draṣṭavyam |
atra prāguktadoṣe paroktasamādhānaṃ dūṣayitumāha -- suṣuptamūcchriteti || vivṛtametadadhastāt || tvayetyādi || tathā ca punarbādha eveti bhāvaḥ |
atha devadattabhogāyatanatvasya pāramārthikatvenābhāve'pi vyāvahārikatvena tatsattvābhyupagamāttena rūpeṇa tadabhāvānadhikaraṇatvasya sādhyatvānna bādhāvakāśaḥ |
na ca paraṃ prati vyāvahārikatvāprasiddhiḥ |
saprakārakajñānābādhyatvena bhogāyatanatvasya yo'bhāvastadanadhikaraṇatvasyaiva sādhyatvāt |
saprakārakajñānābādhyatvasya ca paramate pāramārthika eva prasiddhereti cenna |
saprakārakajñānābādhyatvena bhogāyatanatvābhāvasyaivāprasiddheḥ |
vyadhikaraṇadharmāvacchinnābhāvānaṅgīkāribhiḥ sāmānyarūpeṇa viśeṣābhāvānabhyupagamāt |
na ca saprakārakajñānābādhyatvaviśiṣṭabhogāyatanatvatvena bhogāyatanatvābhāvasya vivakṣitatvānna doṣa iti vācyam |
tasya kevalabhogāyatanatvatvāpekṣayā gurutvenābhāvapratiyogitānavacchedakatvāt |
etena bhogāyatanatvasya mithyātve tadatyantābhāvavattadadhikaraṇatvasyāpi mithyātvena tadanadhikaraṇatvamapyavyāhatameveti noktabādhāvakāśa ityapāstam |
vyāvahārikatvādinā tadadhikaraṇatvābhāvasyāprasiddheḥ |
tadadhikaraṇatvābhāvamātrasya sādhyatve tu prātibhāsikatvādinā tadabhāvamādāyārthāntaratādavasthyāditi dik |
|| tṛtīya iti || bhedo mithyā bhedatvāt candrabhedavat ityanumāne nirduṣṭamithyātvanirvacanāsambhava ityarthaḥ |
sambhavannirvacanasya duṣṭatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
tadevāha -- na ceti || bādhyatvarūpaṃ mithyātvamiti śeṣaḥ || mithyātvāsambhavāditi || satyatvapratikṣepāsambhavādityarthaḥ |
pradhvaṃsapratiyogitvasya manmate satyatvavyāpyatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
atra dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyamapi bodhyam |
na cādhiṣṭhānayāthātmyajñānajanyadhvaṃsapratiyogijñānaviṣayatvavivakṣaṇāt na doṣa iti vācyam |
taddhi bhramāviṣayatvaparyavasitam |
tathā ca mayā sato'pi bhramaviṣayatvāṅgīkāreṇārthāntaratādavasthyāt || tadayogāditi || atyantābhāvapratiyogitvāsambhavādityarthaḥ |
anyathā'tyantāsattvāpattiriti bhāvaḥ |
kecittu adhiṣṭhānayāthātmyajñānajanyātyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ mithyātvamityaṅgīkāre jīvanmuktikālīnavyavahāradaśāyāṃ tvayā bhedānuvṛttyaṅgīkāreṇāsambhavaprasaṅgāditi vyācakṣate |
vyavahārasato'pi bhedasya pāramārthikatvenātyantābhāvapratiyogitvopagamānna doṣa ityāśaṅkate -- pāramārthikatveti || tathātvamatyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ mithyātvamiti śeṣaḥ || abādhyatveti || pāramārthikatvenātyantābhāvapratiyogitvarūpabādhyatvajñānasya pāramārthikatvajñānādhīnatvāttasya caitādṛśabādhyatvābhāvarūpasya bādhyatvajñānādhīnatvenānyonyāśrayādityarthaḥ |
upalakṣaṇametat |
dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyaṃ ca |
pāramārthikatvena candrabhedābhāvāprasiddheḥ |
asataḥ sadrūpeṇa pratiyogitvānupagamāt |
na cātyantābhāvapratiyogitvamātraṃ mithyātvam |
tacca sadrūpeṇa asadrūpeṇa vā matadvayasiddhameveti vācyam |
bhedasya sattve'pyasattvenātyantābhāvapratiyogitvasambhavādarthāntaraprasaṅgāt |
vimatā ityādi || ātmanāṃ paramātmanaḥ sakāśādapāramārthikabhedasya tenāṅgīkārattadabhāvasādhanasyāniṣṭavatvaṃ paramātmani paramātmapratiyogikāpāramārthikabhedasya tenānaṅgīkārānnasādhyavaikalyaṃ dṛṣṭāntasyeti dhyeyam || dvitīya iti || vimatāni śarīrāṇi devadattasyaiva bhogāyatanāni śarīratvaddevadattaśarīravadityarthaḥ |
atra kiṃ devadattaśabdena devadattaśarīrāvacchinnacaitanyaṃ vivakṣitamutatadanavacchinnacaitanyamiti vikalpauhṛdikṛtvādyamanūdyadūṣayati -- devadattaśabdeneti || tadbhogāyatanatva iti || devadattaśarīrāvacchinnacetanyabhogasādhanatvesādhye tasya vimateṣu yajñadattādiśarīreṣvabhāvādbādha ityarthaḥ |
yajñadattādiśarīreṣu devadattaśarīrāvacchinnacaitanyabhogasādhanatvesādhye tasya vimateṣu yajñadattādiśarīreṣvabhāvādbādha ityarthaḥ |
yajñādattādiśarīreṣu devadattaśarīrāvacchinnacaitanyasya bhogasādhanatvaṃ kuto nāstītya uktaṃ yajñadattādiśarīrāṇāmiti || tadadhīnameva hi tadbhogasādhanaṃ svarūpamātrābhede'pyavacchinnasya bhinnatvena devadattaśarīrāvacchinnacaitanyādhīnatvasya pratyakṣabādhitatvena tvayāpyanaṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ |
kiṃ śarīrāvacchinne ātmanyahaṃkāragatamaveva bhoktṛtvādikamāropitam |
tatrānavacchinnasyātmano'haṃkārasānnidhyasyaivābhāvānna tatra bhoktṛtvādyāropa iti tvayā'ṅgīkāreṇānavacchinnacaitanyabhogasādhanātvasya sādhane bādha iti bhāvaḥ || teṣāmapīti || ato na tatra sādhyābhāvaniścaya iti na vyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ || bādha iti || tatra bhogasādhanatvarūpasādhyasyaivābhāvādaṃśe bādha ityarthaḥ |
tanucaitaddoṣaparihārāya bhogāyatanatvaśabdebhogāyatanatvātyantābhāvānadhikaraṇatvaṃ prāgevavivakṣitamityata āha -- naceti || tvayeti || tvayāhisarveṣu śarīreṣu vidyamānaṃ bhoktṛtvaṃ mithyetyaṅgīkriyate |
mithyātvaṃ casvātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvam |
evaṃ ca śarīreśubhogāyatanatvaṃ tadatyantābhāvaścāṅgīkāryaḥ |
tathāca bhogāyatanatvātyantābhāvānadhikaraṇatvarūpasādhyasyābhāvādbādha iti bhāvaḥ || tadadhikaraṇasyeti || bhogāyatanatvādhikaraṇasyetyarthaḥ |
tadatyantābhāvaḥ bhogāyatanatvātyantābhāvaḥ || tṛtīya iti || bhedo mithyā bhedatvāt candrabhedavadityatra mithyātvarūpasādhyanirvacanaṃ durghaṭamityarthaḥ || adhiṣṭhāneti || vyākhyātametadadhastāt |
tatkiṃ mithyātvaṃ nivartyatvaṃ nivartyatvaṃ nivṛttipratiyogitvam |
tatra nivṛttirnāmadhvaṃsovātyantābhāvovā |
ādhyamanūdyadūṣayati -- nivṛttiśabdeneti || mithyābhūtasya vināśāyogāditi bhāvaḥ |
dvitīyamanūdyadūṣayati -- atyantābhāveti || bādhamevopapādayati -- tvayeti || advaitavādinā'pi jīveśabhedasya vyāvahārikasatyatvamaṅgīkurvatā vyavahāradaśāyāṃ bhedo'stīti aṅgīkriyate |
anyathā tasya vyāvahārikatvāyogāt |
vyavahārakālevidyamānasya caitrakālikaniṣedharūpātyantābhāvapratiyogitvamanupapannamiti bhāvaḥ |
tadayogāt atyantābhāvapratiyogitvādayogāt |
na vayaṃ svarūpeṇātyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ brūmo yena vyavahārakāle sato bhedasya tanna syāt_ |
apitu pāramārthikatvākāreṇaiva |
vyāvahārikatvena rūpeṇa sato'pi pāramārthikatvarūpeṇa atyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ sambhavatyeva |
yathā śyāmatvena rūpeṇa sato'pi ghaṭasya raktatvarūpeṇātyantābhāvapratiyogitvamityāśayena śaṅkate -- pāramārthikatvākāreṇeti || pāramārthikatvarūpeṇa dharmeṇetyarthaḥ |
tathātvamatyantābhāvaprapratiyogitvamityāśayena śaṅkate -- pāramārthikatvākāreṇeti || pāramārthikatvarūpeṇa dharmeṇetyarthaḥ |
tathātvamatyantābhāvapratiyogitvam || abādhyatvarūpasyeti || pāramārthikatvakāreṇātyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ hi mithyātvam |
pāramārthikatvaṃ cābādhyatvameva |
tacca bādhyatvābhāvaḥ |
bādhyatvaṃ ca mithyātvameva |
tathāca riktamithyātvajñāne'bādhyatvarūpapāramārthikatvajñānasya kāṇatvaṃ pāramārthikatvajñāne mithyātvarūpabādhyatvajñānasya kāraṇatvamityanyonyāśraya ityarthaḥ || tvayocyamāneti || tvayedānīṃ nirucyamāne ityarthaḥ |
bhedatvena pratīyamānatvādityatra kimidaṃ pratīyamānatvam ? (kimidaṃpratīyamānatvam ?) |
pramāviṣayatvamapramāviṣayatvaṃ vā ? pratītitvasāmānyāvacchinnaviṣayatvaṃ vā ? (pramātvamapramātvaṃ vā ? kiṃ vā pratītitvasāmānyam ?) |
nādyaḥ |
tavāsiddheḥ |
na dvitīyaḥ |
mamāsiddheḥ na tṛtīyaḥ |
candrabheda(pratyakṣa)pratīterapi mithyātvenāprāmāṇikatvātprāmāṇikāprāmāṇikayoranugatasāmānyābhāvenāsiddheḥ |
bhedābhedayoḥ bhede'naikāntyaṃ ca |
tasyāpi pakṣatve bādhaḥ |
bhedābhedayorbhedasyāpāramārthikatve'nenaivānumānenābhedasyāpi mithyātvaṃ syāt |
vyāvahārikabhedasadbhāve'pi paramārthato brahmābhinnasya (vyāvahārikabhedasadbhāve'paramārthato brahmaṇo bhinnasya) jīvasya pāramārthikatvavatparamārthataḥ pāramārthikābhedābhinno bhedaḥ (api) pāramārthikaḥ syāditi mithyātvasādhane bādhaḥ |
pāramārthikāpāramārthikabrahmaprapañcabhede vyabhicāraḥ (vyabhicāraśca) |
brahmaṇi(ṇo) dharmisamasattākabhedo nāstīti pāramārthikatvaṃ ghaṭādau so'stītyapāramārthikatvamiti cen (na) |
tarhi jīvabrahmaṇoḥ prapañcavaddharmisattāsamānasattākabhedo nāstīti tayorapāramārthikatvaṃ prapañcaḥ pāramārthikaḥ syāt |
(tarhi brahmaṇaḥ prapañcāt dharmisamānasattāko'bhedo nāstīti tadapāramārthikatvaṃ prapañcavat syāt |
) candrabhede prātibhāsikatvamupādhiśca |
sa ca na pakṣe sādhayituṃ śakyaḥ |
vyāvahātarikatvāṅgīkāravirodhāt |
abhedo mithyā abhedatvāt dehātmabhedavadityādyābhāsasāmyaṃ ca |
na ca dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavaikalyaṃ śaṅkyam |
tathātve'`nyonyasminnanyonyātmakatāmanyonyadharmāṃścādhyasye'tyadhyāsabhāṣyasyonmattapralāpatvaprasaṅgāt |
gauro'haṃ kṛśo'hamityādijñānasya tattvāvedakatvena prāmāṇyaprasaṅgācca (tattvāvedakaprāmāṇyaprasaṅgācca) |
caturthe tu nānāpadārthānāmeva senāvanādivadupādhibhedena jagacchabdavācyatayā padārthāntarābhāvena ghaṭādīnāṃ tadabhedasādhane bādhaḥ |
pṛthivyādīnāṃ jagadākhyapadārthā(ntarā)rambhakatve tadārabdhe jagati pṛthivītvādīnāṃ sāṅkaryaprasaṅgaḥ |
guṇādīnāṃ jagadantarbhūtānāṃ tadārambhakatve(tvena) teṣāṃ dravyatvāpattiśca |
niravayavānāṃ (guṇādīnāṃ) dravyārambhakatvaprasaṅgācca(ṅgaśca) |
ghaṭādīnāmatyantyāvayavitvahāniśca syāt |
ghaṭādipadārthānāmeva pakṣatve tu svāntarbhedaśabdena svapratiyogikabhedābhāve'bhimate (svapratiyogikabhedābhāvasādhane) siddhasādhanam |
parasparaṃ bhedābhāvamātrasādhane tu (bhedamātrābhāvasādhane tu) sādhyavikalo dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
ākāśabhedasādhanaṃ tvasmābhiranyatra kṛtaṃ draṣṭavyam |
(ākāśabhedasādhanaṃ tvasmābhiranyatra kṛtam |
granthagauravabhayānneha tanyate |
) tasmānnātmābhede'numānaṃ pramāṇam |
bhedatvāditi hetorasiddhiparihārārthaṃ bhedatvena pratīyamānatvādityartho vivakṣita ityuktaṃ taddūṣayituṃ vikalpayati -- bhedatveneti || pramātvamapramātvaṃ vetyādi |
kiṃ sā pratītiḥ pramārūpota bhrāntirūpā kiṃ vā pratītisāmānyamiti bhāvaḥ || tavāsiddheriti || bhedapratītestvayā pramātvānaṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ |
mamāsiddheḥ mayā tasyāḥ pramātvāṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ |
tṛtīyapakṣe'pyasiddhimupapādayati -- na tṛtīya iti || yathā candrabhedasya prātibhāsikatvenāprāmāṇikatvātprāmāṇikāpramāṇikayoranugatabhedatvasāmānyābhāvādasiddho heturityuktam |
tathā candrabhedapratīterapi prātibhāsikatvenāprāmāṇikatvādubhayasādhāraṇasāmānyābhāvātpratīyamānatvaheturapyasiddha ityarthaḥ |
kvacitpratyakṣapratīteriti pāṭhaḥ |
tadā bhramarūpacandrabhedapratyakṣapratīterityarthaḥ |
kiñcāstāṃ tāvadanye bhedābhedayostu bhedaḥ pāramārthiko vācyaḥ |
tathā ca tatra heturanaikāntyamityāha -- bhedābhedeti || tasyāpi pakṣatvāṅgīkārātkathamanaikāntyamityata āha -- tasyāpīti |
aṃśe bādha ityarthaḥ |
bhedābhedayorbhedasya pāramārthikatve syādanaikāntyamaṃśe bādho vā |
na caivaṃ kintvasāvapāramārthika evetyata āha -- bhedābhedayoriti || tayorbhedasyāpāramārthikatve'bhedasyaiva pāramārthikatvāpattyā'pāramārthikabhedābhinnasya bhedasyāpi bhedarūpatvena bhedo mithyā bhedātvādityanumānenaiva mithyātvaṃ syāditi bhāvaḥ |
evaṃ bhedābhedayorbhedasyāpāramārthikatve tayorabhedāpattyā bhedābhinnasyābhedasya mithyātvaṃ syādityuktam |
idānīṃ tenaiva nyāyena bhedasyāpyabhedābhinnatāyā aṅgīkāryatve pāramārthikābhedābhinnasya bhedasyāpi pāramārthikatvasthitau bhedasya mithyātvasādhane bādhaśca syāditi bhāvenāha -- vyāvahāriketyādinā |
nanu paramārthataḥ pāramārthikābhedābhinnasyāpi bhedasya na pāramārthikatvaprāptiḥ |
tayorvyāvahārikabhedasyāpyaṅgīkārādityata uktam || vyāvahārikabhedasadbhāve''pīti |
tathā ca pāramārthikābhedasadbhāve vyāvahārikabhedo'kiñcitkaraḥ |
anyathā paramārthato brahmābhinnasya jīvasyāpi vyāvahārikabhedasadbhāvamātreṇāpāramārthikatvaṃ syāditi bhāvaḥ |
bhedatvahetordūṣaṇāntaraṃ cāha -- pāramārthiketi || brahmaprañcayorbhedastāvatpāramārthika evetyaṅgīkāryam |
brahmaṇaḥ pāramārthikatvātprapañcasya tvanmate'pāramārthikatvāt |
viruddhadharmādhikaraṇayośca jalānalavadbhedasya naiyatyāt |
tataśca tayorbhede vyabhicāraśca syādityarthaḥ || bādho veti || tasyāpi pūrvavatpakṣatvāṅgīkṛtāviti bhāvaḥ |
kvacidbādho veti pāṭha eva nāsti |
ata eva kaiścanaitadvākyaṃ na vyākṛtam |
nanu brahmaprapañcayoḥ pāramārthikāpāramārthikatve'pi tayorbhedaḥ pāramārthikaḥ kutaḥ |
na ca tayorbhedasyāpāramārthikatve brahmaṇo vā prapañcavadapāramārthikatvāpattiḥ |
prapañcasya vā brahmavatpāramārthikatvāpattiriti vācyam |
bhedasyāpāramārthikatve'pi brahmaprapañcayoḥ pāramārthikatvāpāramārthikatvāṅgīkāropapatteḥ |
tatra prayojakāntarasadbhāvena bhedāpāramārthikatvasyāprayojakatvāditi bhāvena śaṅkate -- brahmaṇa iti || yadyapi brahmaṇi prapañcādbhedo'sti tathā'pi na sa dharmisamasattākaḥ |
pāramārthiko na bhavatīti yāvat |
ghaṭādau prapañce tu brahmapratiyogiko bhedo dharmisamasattākaḥ |
vyāvahārika iti yāvat |
atastayorbhedasyāpāramārthikatve'pi na pāramārthikatvādikṣatiriti bhāvaḥ |
dūṣayati -- neti || tarhīti |
niyāmakapramāṇaṃ vinaiva pāramārthikatve dharmisattākabhedābhāvasyāpāramārthikatve ca tatsadbhāvasya prayojakatve'ṅgīkriyamāṇa ityarthaḥ || jīvabrahmaṇoriti || dharmisamasattākabhedo yatrāsti tasya pāramārthikatvaṃ yatra sa nāsti tasyāpāramārthikatvamityapi vyāvasthāyāḥ sukaratvājjīvabrahmaṇostadabhāvādapāramārthikatvaṃ prapañcasya tatsadbhāvātpāramārthikatvamiti vaiparītyamapi prasajyetetyarthaḥ |
evamapi tvayā brahmaṇi prapañcavadevāpāramārthikatvaṃ syādeveti parihārābhiprāya ityāhuḥ || candrabheda iti || itthaṃ hi vākyayojanā |
asminnanumāne prātibhāsikatvamupādhiśca |
katham |
dṛṣṭānte candrabhede prātibhāsikatvaṃ vartate |
ataḥ sādhyavyāpakaḥ |
sa copādhiḥ pakṣe vimatabhede sādhayitumaśakyaḥ |
tasya vyāvahārikatvāṅgīkāravirodhāt |
ataḥ sādhanāvyāpaka iti || kutaḥ sādhyavaikalyaṃ na śaṅkyamityata āha -- tathātve iti || dehātmabhedasya satyatvāṅgīkāre dehātmanoranyonyātmakatāyā adhyastatvavarṇanaparasya tvadīyabhāṣyasyonmattapralapitavākyavatsvābhyupagamavirodhaḥ syādityarthaḥ |
dūṣaṇāntaramāha -- gauro'hamiti || dehātmanorabhede gauratvādīnāṃ dehadharmāṇāmevātmadharmatvena tajjñānasya tattvaviṣayakatāpattyā pramātvaprasaṅgāccetyarthaḥ |
tattvāvedakatvenetyasya tattvaviṣayakatetyarthaḥ |
na ceṣṭāpattiriti vācyam |
anyonyadharmāṃścādhyasyeti bhāṣyasyonmattavākyatvaprasaṅgāditi bhāvaḥ |
ata eva pūrvavākye'nyonyadharmāṃścādhyasyetyaṃśasyāpyudāharaṇam || caturthe tviti || vimato jagadākhyaḥ padārthaḥ tattvataḥ svāntarbhedahīno dṛśyatvādgaganādivadityanumāna ityarthaḥ |
tatra tāttvikasvāntarbhedahīnatvaṃ nāma jagadantargatānāṃ ghaṭādipadārthānāmānyonyaṃ vā taiśca ghaṭādibhirjagato vā pāramārthikabhedābhāvaḥ |
tathaiva pūrvapakṣagrantha upapāditatvāt |
tatra ghaṭādīnāṃ tadabhedasādhane |
jagatpratiyogikabhedābhāvasādhane |
ghaṭādīnāṃ sakāśāttasya jagato bhedābhāvasādhana iti vā bādha ityarthaḥ |
kuto bādha ityata uktam || padārthāntarābhāveneti || ghaṭādivyatiriktajagadākhyapadārthāntarābhāvenetyarthaḥ |
sati hi padārthāntare kasyacitpadārthasya tato bhedo vā bhedābhāvo vā sādhayituṃ śakyate na tu tasyaivābhāve |
na hi śaśaviṣaṇāddhaṭāderbhedābhāvaḥ sādhanārhaḥ |
ato'tiriktajagadvyakterevābhāvena tato bhedābhāvasādhane bādho yukta ityarthaḥ |
padārthāntarābhāve'pi kuta ityata uktaṃ nānāpadārthānāṃ hi jagacchabdavācyatayeti || hiśabda evārthe |
tatkathamityata uktam upādhibhedeneti || kālalakṣaṇopādhiviśeṣeṇetyarthaḥ |
tatra dṛṣṭāntakathanaṃ senāvanādivaditi || yathā karituragarathādīnāmevaikasthānasthitilakṣaṇopādhinā senāśabdavācyatvaṃ yathā ca nānāvṛkṣāṇāmekasthānanivāsena vanaśabdavācyatvaṃ yathā vā dhānyādīnāmekasthāne militānāṃ rāśiśabdavācyatvamevaṃ vivakṣitaikakālopādhigrastānāṃ ghaṭādīnāmeva jagacchabdavācyatvamityarthaḥ |
ata evoktaṃ mandāramañjaryām |
pravāha iti gīyante sajātīyāḥ pravāhiṇaḥ |
kālenopādhinā grastā nānāvṛkṣā vanaṃ yatheti || upalakṣaṇametat |
ghaṭādīnāṃ parasparabhedābhāvasādhane'pi pratyakṣādivirodhādbādha ityapi draṣṭavyam |
nanu na ghaṭādaya eva jagacchabdārthaḥ kintu tantubhiḥ paṭa iva ghaṭādinā nāpadārthairjagadākhyaḥ padārtha ārabhyate |
ato noktadoṣa ityata āha -- pṛthivyādīnāmiti || jātisāṅkaryaprasaṅga iti || pṛthivyādyārabdhasyāvayavinaḥ pṛthivītvādiniyamātpṛthivyādisarvapadārthārabdhe jagati jātisāṅkaryaṃ syādityarthaḥ |
idamapi nyāyamatarītyaivoktam |
siddhānte'nekavijātīyārabdhe'pi dravye jātisāṅkaryābhāvāt |
na hi pṛthivyādikamapi kevalapṛthivyādimātrārabdham |
kintvekaikamapi bhūtaṃ bhūtatrayārabdhameva |
tathā hi śrutiḥ |
tāsāṃ trivṛtaṃ trivṛtamekaikāmakaroditi || vyavahārastu tattadbhūtabhāgānāṃ vaiśeṣyāt |
uktaṃ ca sūtreṣu |
vaiśeṣyāttu tadvādastadvādaḥ |
tryātmakatvāttu bhūyastvāditi || sāṅkaryasya jātibādhakatvābhāvasyopapāditatvācceti || dravyatyāpattiriti || dravyasyaiva samavāyikāraṇatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
idamapi nyāyamatālambanenaiva |
siddhānte guṇavadupādānaṃ guṇavadupādeyatayā pariṇata ityaṅgīkārāt || niravayavānāmiti || idamapi nyāyarītyaiva |
siddhānte niravayavavastuna evābhāvāt |
yathoktam |
ataḥ sarvapadārthānāṃ bhāgāḥ santyeva sarvadā |
sarvadikṣvapi sambandhāditi || atiprasaṅgaśceti || niravayavairguṇakriyājātyādibhirapi dravyarambhaprasaṅga ityarthaḥ || antyāvayavitvahāniśceti || dravyāntarānārambhakakāryadravyasyaivāntyāvayavitvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu ghaṭādisamudāyarūpaṃ vā tadārabdhaṃ vā jagatpakṣīkṛtya naivamanumīyate |
kintu ghaṭādīneva pakṣīkṛtya svāntarbhedahīnatvaṃ sādhyate |
ato noktadoṣa ityata āha -- ghaṭādīti || svāntarbhedaśabdeneti || hīnaśabdottareṇeti śeṣaḥ |
athavā svāntarbhedaśabdena svapratiyogikabhedaṃ gṛhītvā tadābhāvasādhana iti vyākhyeyam || bhedābhāvamātrasādhana iti || svāntaḥ padena svapratiyogikatvaṃ na vivakṣyate |
kintu parasparapratiyogikatvameva |
tathā ca kathaṃ siddhasādhanamiti bhāvaḥ || sādhyavikalo dṛṣṭānta iti || ākāśeṃ'śānāṃ mitho bhedasyaiva sattvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvidameva sādhyavaikalyamāśaṅkya prāgeva nirākṛtamityata āha -- ākāśabhedasādhanaṃ tviti || ākāśeṃ'śabhedasādhanaṃ tvityarthaḥ |
anyatra mandāramañjaryām |
nanvathā'pi pratīti balātsaṃyogatadatyantābhāvayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyamastvityādinā tasmādākāśabhedavādinaṃ pratīti sādhūktamityantena granthenākāśabhedavādinaṃ prati pratītyasiddheriti ṭīkāvākyavivaraṇāvasare kṛtamākāśabhedasādhanaṃ draṣṭavyamityarthaḥ |
naitāvatā''kāśāṃśānāṃ mitho bhedaḥ siddhānte'ṅgīkṛta iti bhramitavyam |
yadā''kāśabhedo nāstīti siddhāntastadāntaśabdo viśeṣavacana ityupādhikhaṇḍanaṭīkāyāṃ khe'pi deśāntarasya sa iti mūlavyākhyānāvasara uktatvāt |
siddhānte ākāśāṃśānāṃ deśaviśeṣāṇāṃ yāvaddravyabhāvitvādatyantābhedasyāṅgīkāradbhedavācyantaraśbdaprayogo nopapadyata ityata āheti yadeti vākyasya mandāramañjaryāmevāvatāritatvācca |
kinnāmākāśe aupādhikabhedāṅgīkāriṇaṃ naiyāyikaṃ prati kaścana svābhāvikamevāṃśabhedamaṅgīkṛtya pratyavatiṣṭhate cettaṃ prati naiyāyikasya na kimapyuttaramityabhiprāyeṇaivākāśabhedasādhanaṃ tvasmābhiranyatra kṛtamityuktiriti bodhyam |
ata eva tatra mandāramañjaryāmeva pareṇaupādhikabhedo'ṅgīkriyate |
taṃ prati svābhāvikabhedamaṅgīkārayatītyāheti prakārāntareṇaitadeva vākyamavatāritamiti || kecittvākāśāṃśānāṃ svābhāvikabhedasādhanamanyatra nyāyāmṛtādau kṛtamityartha ityāhuḥ |
paroktānumānanirākaraṇamupasaṃharati -- tasmāditi || upapāditarītyā duṣṭatvādityarthaḥ |
ātmābheda ityupalakṣaṇam |
anātmabheda ityapi grāhyam |
prādhānyādātmābhedamātrasya saṅkīrtanam |
anyathā caturthānumāne'nātmabhedamātranirāsādātmābhedasādhanābhāvena sāmānyenātmābhedānumānanirākaraṇopasaṃhārāyogāt |
kecittu tatra vimatamiti sarvaṃ cetanācetanajātaṃ vivakṣitamato nātmābhede'numanāmityupasaṃhāropapattirityāhuḥ |
tatra cetanasyāpi vimatapadena saṅgrahe tatra dṛśyatvahetorbhāgāsiddherdurvāratvātkathaṃ pūrvapakṣiṇastadabhiprāyakalpanamiti cintyam |
yaccoktaṃ bhedatvena pratīyamānatvena hetūkṛtaṃ bhedatvamiti tadvikalpya nirāha -- pratīterapi mithyātveneti || pareṇāropasthale jñānārthayorubhayorapi prātibhāsikatvāṅgīkārādityarthaḥ || anugatasāmānyābhāveneti || ayaṃ bhāvaḥ |
bhedatvādityatra mithyāsatyayoranugata sāmānyābhāvadasiddhiśaṅkāyāṃ tadanugamāya bhedasyānanugame'pi tadubhayapratītyorbhedapratītatvasyānugatatvena bhedatvaṃ nāma bhedatvena pratīyamānatvādityuktam |
tattu bhramasthale'rthamithyātve'pi pratīteḥ satyatvamiti mate sambhavati na tu tattvata iti || tasyāpi pakṣatve iti || bhedābhedayorbhedastasyāpi pakṣatve vayādhātena bādha ityarthaḥ |
vyāvahārikabhedena vyāhatiparihāra ityaṅgīkāre bādhakamāha -- bhedeti || abhedasyāpi mithyātvaṃ syāditi || bhedābhedayoḥ paramārthata ekatvāpattyā bhedamithyātvasādhanasyaivābhedamithyātvasādhanatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
yadi caivamabhedasyāpāramārthikatvaṃ tadā sakalabhedasyāpi mithyātvasādhane bādhaḥ syāt |
yo yatsākaṃ tadabhinnastatsattāka iti vyāpteḥ |
yadvā bhedaḥ satyaḥ satyābhedabhinnatvāt yo yatsattākabhinnaḥ sa satyo tathā jīvo brahmabhinna ityanumānamatrābhipretamityūhyam || vyabhicāra iti || brahmaprapañcayorbhedasya pāramārthikatvābhāve tayoḥ pāramārthikatvavyāvahārikatvarūpavailakṣaṇyāyogāditi bhāvaḥ || bādho neti || tasyāpi pakṣatva iti śeṣaḥ |
brahmaṇaḥ pāramārthikatve'pi prapañcapratiyogikabhedasya tathātvābhāvāt |
na vyabhicāra iti śaṅkate -- brahmaṇa iti || prapañcaḥ pāramārthikaḥ syāditi || jīvabrahmaṇoḥ prapañcapratiyogikadharmisattāsamānasattākabhedābhāvātprapañcavadeva mithyātvaṃ syādityarthaḥ |
prayogaśca |
brahma mithyā bhavitumarhati prapañcapratiyogikapāramārthikabhedaśūnyatvāt prapañcavaditi || candrabheda iti || candrabhede vidyamānasya mithyātvasya prātibhāsikatvaṃ prayojakatvaṃ na bhedatvamityarthaḥ |
nanūktahetunaivopādhiṃ pakṣe sādhayāmītyata āha -- sa ceti || na ca ghaṭādau sādhyavyāpakatābhāva iti vācyam |
bhedatvarūpasādhanāvacchinnasādhyavyāpakatāpatteḥ |
vyāvahārikabhedasya pakṣatvānna doṣaḥ || sādhyavaikalyaṃ na śaṅkyamiti || parasya sarvādvaitavāditvāditi bhāvaḥ || unmatteti || asatyatve'nyonyasminnanyonyātmakatāmadhyasyetyāropoktyayogāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu bhāṣye cādhyāsoktirvyāvahārikasyaiva pāramarthikaikyaṃ tvanumatameveti cet tarhi tadāropitaikyameva dṛṣṭāntīkṛtamiti kathaṃ sādhyavaikalyam |
nanvevamapi dehātmanoḥ pāramārthikaikye vyabhicāra iti cenna |
tasyāpi pakṣatvāt |
tathā'pyātmanaḥ svapratiyogikaikye vyabhicāra iti cenna |
bhedasāmānādhikaraṇyaikyatvasyaiva hetutvāt |
na ca viśeṣaṇāsiddhiḥ |
yatra pareṇābhedamaṅgīkṛtya vyāvahārikabhedo'ṅgīkriyate tatsthalīyābhedasyaiva pakṣīkṛtatvāt |
kiñca bhedaḥ pāramārthikobhedatvāccandrabhedavat |
brahma mithyā vastutvāccandrabhedavadityābhāsasāmyaṃ cetyūhyam || gauro'hamiti || asya dehātmābhedapratyakṣatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvidaṃ pratyakṣaṃ vyāvahārikaṃ dehātmābhedaṃ gṛhṇāti |
ato bhrāntamiti cenna |
kimatra vyāvahārikaṃ yadaikyaṃ gṛhṇātītyarthaḥ |
utaikyaṃ vyāvahārikaṃ gṛhṇātīti |
nādyaḥ |
tadabhāvāt |
dvitīye aikyāṃśe pramā syāt |
na ceṣṭāpattiḥ |
anyonyātmakatāmadhyasyeti virodhāt || caturtheti || vimataṃ tāttvikasvāntarbhedahīnāmityatretyarthaḥ |
atra vimatapadena jagadākhyaḥ padārthaḥ pakṣīkṛtaḥ |
atra ghaṭādyatiriktastadanugata ucyate |
uta ghaṭādirevetyabhipretyā krameṇa nirāha |
yathā vijātīyagajaturagādyā ekadeśatvopādhinā ekarājādhīnatvopādhinā vā ekadeśenetyucyate na gajādyatiriktāṃśe nāsti |
tathā ghaṭādyā ekabuddhyupārūḍhatvādyupādhinā jagaducyate |
evañcātiriktajagata evābhāvenābhedasādhane bādha ityarthaḥ |
ekaikasya ghaṭāderanekatvāyogāditi bhāvaḥ |
tantvārabdhapaṭavajjagadapi kinna syādityata āha -- pṛthivyādīnāmiti || pṛthivītvaṃ jalatvaṃ vinā pṛthivyāṃ jalatvaṃ ca tadvinā jale taddvayamapi jagati kāraṇagatajāteḥ kārye'pyāvaśyakatvāditi sāṅkaryamiti bhāvaḥ || dravyatvāpattiśceti || samavāyikāraṇatvasyaiva dravyatvapadārthatvāditi jñeyam || dravyārambhakatva iti || niravayavasya mahato dravyārambhakatve siddhāntahāniprasaṅgāccetyarthaḥ |
kiñca ghaṭādipadārthānāṃ jagadārambhakatvaṃ pratyekaṃ militānāṃ vā |
nādyaḥ |
ekasyānārambhakaniyamāt |
anekānugatadhīvirodhācca |
na dvitīyaḥ |
bhūtabhaviṣyatāṃ jagadutpattau melanāsambhavāt |
kiñca jagata ārabhyatve'nādyantaṃ jagadetaditi vacanavirodhaḥ |
ghaṭādīnāṃ jagattve'pyanāditvavirodha iti cenna |
ghaṭādipravāhasya jagattvāt |
ghaṭādyabhāvakāle gaganādau jagattvasya paryāptatvācca |
dvitīyaṃ dūṣayati -- ghaṭādipadārthānāmiti || svapratiyogiketi svāntaśabdasya tadarthatvaucityāditi bhāvaḥ |
astu vaiko vā jagatpadārthaḥ |
ākāśe ākāśāṃśabhedasattvātsādhyavaikalpam |
kathaṃ tatraṃśabheda ityata āha -- ākāśabhedasādhanaṃ tviti || anyatra mandāramañjaryām |
tathā hi |
ākāśaḥ svābhāvikasvāntarbhedavān yugapadanekamūrtasaṃyogitvāt |
na ca kālādau vyabhicāraḥ |
pakṣatulyatvāt |
na ca heturaprayojakaḥ |
ākāśānāṃ yadi bhedo na syāt tarhi mūrtānāṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ syāditi bādhakaraṇḍasattvāt |
na caupādhikabhedādavirodhaḥ |
upādhīnāmapi sāmānādhikaraṇyavirodhāt |
anyathā ghaṭādīnāmapi tatsaṅgapratiyogitvopādhinaupādhikaḥ svāṃśabhedaḥ syāt |
na svābhāvikaḥ |
kiñcāhaṃ deśāntarādāgato deśāntare ca gacchāmi |
iha pakṣī neha pakṣītyādipratītistadbhede mānam |
na cāsyālokādibhedaviṣayatvam |
andhakāre'pi spārśanādineha stambho neha stambha iti pratīteḥ |
kiñca yatra gṛdhrastatraiva rātrāvapītyadhikaraṇaikyaṃ pratīyate |
sauracandrāvalokayoścanyonyatvenālokaviṣayatve tadasambhavāt |
kiñca yatrālokastatrāndhakāra iti dhīrnalokādhikaraṇatvaṃ sahate |
na ca sarvāsāṃ pratītīnāmaupādhikaviṣayatvaṃ vācyam |
iha pakṣī neha pakṣītyādāvupādhyadarśanāt |
na ca pakṣiśarīratadabhāvayorekādhikaraṇyam |
ātmāśrayāt |
upalakṣake cetpradarśitā |
svābhāvikaikadeśābhāve ghaṭasyopādheyārvadākāśasambandhitvam |
sarvo'pi ghaṭākāśaḥ syāt |
yadatroktaṃ ghaṭākāśaḥ syāditi ko'rtha iti |
tatrāyameva pradeśo ghaṭākāśo nānyaḥ |
kintu mahākāśo'yamiti yadvailakṣaṇyaṃ pratīyate tanna syāditi || yaccoktamupādeyavadākāśasambandhitve'pyupādheḥ pratibimbapakṣapātitvena svaparimāṇādikaṃ pratibimbabhūtaghaṭākāśa evoparañjayati |
na mahākāśa iti || tattu prakriyāvilasitam |
ghaṭākāśamahākāśayorbimbapratibimbabhāvasyaivābhāvāt |
pūrvamekamekameva mukhamidānīṃ mukhāntaramutpannamitivadākāśāntaramutpannamityapratīteḥ |
kintu pūrvapradeśo'yaṃ caika evābhūdidānīṃ vicchinnaṃ bhāgadvayamityeva pratītiḥ |
tathā'pyupādhyanusaraṇaniyamādbhedasya na svābhāvikatvamita cenna |
sūkṣmabuddhīnāmupādhyabhāve'pi bhedapratīteḥ |
mūḍhabuddhīnāṃ tu samudramadhyapatitasya pārādyadarśanāvasthāyāṃ jalānāmanyonyasādṛśyātsaṃśleṣaviśeṣācca jalabhedājñānavadupapatteḥ |
kutaścāyaṃ svābhāvikabhede pradveṣaḥ |
sāvayavatve'nityatvaṃ syāditi cenna |
kṛtakatvasya virodhisannipātasya dravyatvāvacchinne sādhye madhyamaparimāṇasyaiva bādhitatvāt |
sāṃśatve'pi sopādānatvarūpasāvayavatvānaṅgīkārāccetyalaṃ prapañcena |
yaccoktaṃ saṃyogaḥ svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇa iti |
tanna |
virodhāt |
agre vṛkṣaḥ kapisaṃyogī mūle netyabādhitapratītibalādaṅgīkriyata iti cenna |
agre mūle cetyadhikaraṇabhedasyaiva pratīteḥ |
na ca vācyaṃ vṛkṣaḥ saṃyogī netyubhayatra vṛkṣasyaiva viśeṣyatayā bhānāttasyaivādhikaraṇatvam |
nāgramūlayoriti || tathātve'gre mūle ceti saptamyanupapatteḥ |
agramūla puraskāraṃ vinā'pi saṃyogatadabhāvapratītyāpatteśca |
agramūlayorapyadhikaraṇatvānubhavavirodhācca |
agre vṛkṣaḥ kapisaṃyogītyādau matupaḥ saṃyogatadabhāvasambandhitāsāmānyaviṣayakatvenādhikaraṇatā'viṣayatvācca |
tasmānneyaṃ pratītirvṛkṣādhikaraṇatvagocarā |
kintu mūlāgrayorevādhikaraṇatvam |
vṛkṣastu tādṛśamūlāgravāniti paramparayā saṃyogatadabhāvayorvṛkṣasambandhitā gocarataiva |
anyathā'gre kapirmūle neti pratītyā kaperapi svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvaṃ syāt |
svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvaṃ nāma tadadhikaraṇasamavetatvameveti cenna |
saṃyogātyantābhāvasya tadabhāvāpatteḥ |
tasmātsvasvocitasambandhena svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇatvaṃ kapyādīnāṃ suvacameva |
kapisaṃyogasyaiva tathātvena tadupapattau kaperapi tatkalpane gauravamiti cet tarhi saṃyogasya yaḥ samavāyastasyaiva tathātvenopapattau saṃyogasyātatkalpyam |
kiñcāgre vṛkṣaśca samavāyī mūle neti pratītyā samavāyo'pi svātyantābhāvasamānādhikaraṇaḥ syāt |
api ca vṛkṣādāvaṃśabhedenopapattāvapyākāśabhede'naṅgīkāreṇa tadanupapatteścetyalam |
prameyatvaṃ pramāviṣayatvam |
aprameyatvamapramāviṣayatvam |
pratītitvaṃ pratītiviṣayatvam |
kvacittu pratīyamānatvaṃ pramātvaṃ pratītitvaṃ sāmānyaṃ veti pāṭhaḥ |
tatra pratīyamānatvaghaṭakaṃ pratītitvamityarthaḥ || pratyakṣapratīterapīti |
bhramarūpacandrabhedapratyakṣarūpapratīterapi tvanmate prātibhāsikatvenobhayasādhāraṇasāmānyāyogādityarthaḥ || bhedābhedeti || bhedābhedayormithyātvābhāvādanaikāntyamityarthaḥ |
nanu tasyāpi pakṣīkaraṇānnānaikāntyamityata āha -- tasyāpīti |
bhedābhedasya pāramārthikatvamavaśyamaṅgīkāryam |
tatrāpi mithyātve sādhye bādha ityarthaḥ |
