kutaḥ pāramārthikatvamaṅgīkāryamityata āha -- bhedābhedayoriti || anena bhedābhedasādhakena |
nanu bhedābhedayorbhedo yadi prātibhāsikaḥ syāttadā bhedamithyātvasādhakenābhedasyāpi mithyātvaṃ syāt |
na caivaṃ kintu vyāvahārikaḥ |
ata eva na bādho'pi |
vyāvahārikasyāpi mithyātvādata āha -- vyāvahārikabhedasadbhāve'pīti |
jīvabrahmaṇoriva vyāvahārikabhedasya pāramārthikabhedavirodhitvābhāvāt |
pāramārthikābhedābhinne bhede pāramārthikatvamāvaśyakamiti duruddharo virodha ityarthaḥ |
pāramārthikāpāramārthiketi brahmaprapañcayorviśeṣaṇaṃ tadbhedapāramārthikatvadyotanāya |
tasyāpi pakṣatve āha -- bādho veti || brahmaprapañcayorbhedasyāpāramārthikatve dvayoḥ pāramārthikatvamapāramārthikatvaṃ vā syāt |
na tvekasya pāramārthikatvamaparasyāpāramārthikatvamatastasya pāramārthikatve vaktavye tatra ca mithyātve sādhye bādha ityarthaḥ |
nanu brahmaprapañcabhedasyāpāramārthikatve'pi tayoḥ pāramārthikatvāpāramārthikatvavyavasthā prakārāntareṇa bhaviṣyatītyāśayena śaṅkate -- brahmaṇa iti || dharmisattāsamānasattākabhedatadabhāvāpāramārthikatvapāramārthikatvavyavasthāpakāvityatra niyāmakābhāvāt |
viparīto niyamyaniyāmakabhāvaḥ kinna syādityata āha -- tarhīti |
tathā ca na sādhanavyāpakateti bhāvaḥ |
anyonyadharmāṃścetyasyopanyāsastu vyavahitenādhasthenānvayapradarśanārthaḥ |
kvacidanyonyātmakamadhyasthetyeva pāṭhaḥ || gauro'hamiti || dehātmābhedasya mithyātvābhāve gauro'hamityādijñānasyāpramātvaprasaṅgādviṣayābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ || caturtha iti || vimataṃ tāttvikasvāntarbhedahīnaṃ matatvata ityatretyarthaḥ |
jagadatra pakṣaḥ |
tacca paṭādivanna ghaṭapaṭārabdhadravyāntaraṃ kintu senāvanādinyāyena kenacidupādhiviśeṣaṇaṃ prameyatvādinā kroḍīkṛtam |
ghaṭapaṭādyeva tatra tāttvikasvāntarbhedahīnatve sādhye bādhaḥ |
brahmaprapañcayostāttvikabhedasya sādhitatvāt ghaṭādirūpajagati tatprayogikābhedasādhane iṣṭāpatteragre vadiṣyati |
nanu pṛthivyādhārajagadākhyaḥ padārthaḥ kinna syādityata āha -- pṛthivyādīnāmiti || prathivyādyārabdhasyāvayavinaḥ prathivītvādiniyamājjagati pṛthivītvādeḥ sāṅkaryaṃ syādityarthaḥ || dravyatvāpattiśceti || samavāyikāraṇaṃ dravyamiti tallakṣaṇāditi bhāvaḥ || ghaṭādīnāmiti || dravyārambhakatveti |
dravyānārambhakaṃ kāryadravyamantyāvayavīti tallakṣaṇatvāditi bhāvaḥ || ākāśabhedasādhanamiti || ākāśāṃśanāṃ svābhāvikabhedasādhanamanyatra nyāyamṛtādau kṛtamityarthaḥ |
tathā ca parābhipretasādhyasyāpi tatra vaikalyamityarthaḥ |
|| kimidaṃ pratīyamānatvamiti || pratīyamānatvaghaṭakapratītitvamityarthaḥ |
kvacitpratītatvamityeva pāṭhaḥ || taveti || tvanmate (bhedapratīteḥ) bhramatvāditi bhāvaḥ || mameti || tatpratīteḥ pramātvasyaiva mayā'ṅgīkṛtatvāditi bhāvaḥ || tvatpakṣa iti || pratīteriti || tvatpakṣe bhramasyāvidyāvṛttirūpatayā viṣayatvānmithyātvena tatra pratītitvasyaivābhāvena pratītiviṣaya[tvasya ]candrabhede'sattvāddṛṣṭānte sādhanavaikalyamityarthaḥ |
prāmāṇikāprāmāṇikayorekajātistvanyatra nirākṛteti bhāvaḥ || bhedābhedabheda iti || bhedābhedayorbhedastatretyarthaḥ || bādha iti || tasya pāramārthikatvāvaśyambhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
kuta ityatastadupapādayati -- bhedābhedayoriti || anenaiveti || bhedamithyātvasādhaka[bādha]syaivābhedamithyātvasādhakatvāvaśyambhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
paramārthatastayorabhede'pi vyāvahārikasarvavyavasthāsambhava ityata āha -- vyāvahārikabhedasadbhāva iti || paramārthata iti || aparamārthato brahmaṇo bhinnasyeti cchedaḥ |
tathā ca pāramārthikabrahmaṇā'paramārthato bhedavato jīvasya pāramārthikābhedavat |
pāramārthikabhedena sahāpāramārthikabhedavato jīvasya bhedasya bhedena saha pāramārthikābhedasya sattvena pāramārthikatvaprāptyā mithyātvasādhane bādha eveti bhāvaḥ || paramārthato'bhedāditi || paramārthato'bhedādityarthaḥ |
na ca svarūpataḥ paramārthasya pāramārthikapratiyogikapāramārthikabhedābhāvastasya pāramārthikatvāpādakaḥ |
jīvabrahmaṇordṛṣṭatvāt |
na ceha tathā bhedasyāpāramārthikatvāditi vācyam |
vyāpyatāvacchedakakoṭau pāramārthikatvaniveśe gauravāt sādhyasyāpi hetujñānakāle evasādhyatvaprāptyā tena sādhanābhāvaprasaṅgācca |
ataḥ yatpratiyogikapāramārthikabhedābhāvo yatra tatra paramārthatastadātmakatvamityeva vyāptiḥ |
asatyapi satpratiyogikabhedaḥ pāramārthika evāto na kutrāpi vyabhicāra ityāśayenoktāpādanāt |
keṣucit pustakeṣu vyāvahārikabhedasadbhāve'pi paramārthato brahmaṇo bhinnasya jīvasya pāramārthikatvavatparamārthataḥ pāramārthikābhedābhinne bhede pāramārthikatvaṃ syāditi mithyātvasādhane bādha iti pāṭhastatra na ko'pi kleśaḥ || pāramārthikāpāramārthiketi || idaṃ ca bhedasya mithyātve brahmaṇo'pāramārthatvaṃ (prapañcasya paramārthatvaṃ) syādityaniṣṭasūcanāya || vyabhicāraśceti || pakṣabahirbhāva ityādiḥ || bādho veti || pakṣāntarbhāva ityādiḥ |
prapañcapratiyogikabrahmadharmikabhedasya mithyātvānna tatra vyabhicārabādhau |
na ca tarhi brahmaṇo'pāramārthikajagatā sahābhedāpattiḥ |
iṣṭāpatteḥ |
na ca tarhi brahmaṇo'pāramārthikatvāpattiḥ |
āpādakābhāvāt |
apāramārthapratiyogikābheda evāpādaka iti cet (na) |
svasamānasattākatādṛśābhedasyaivāpāramārthikatvaṃ prati vyāpyatvābhyupagamāt |
brahmaṇi tadabhāvenāpādanāyogāt |
nāpi brahmapratiyogikaprapañcaniṣṭhabhede bādhavyabhicārau tasya mithyātvābhyupagamāt |
na ca tarhi brahmaṇo'pi mithyātvaṃ syāt paramārthaniṣṭhābhedapratiyogitvāditi cenna |
apāramārthikaniṣṭhapratiyogisamānasattākābhede pratiyogitvasyaivāpāramārthikatvavyāpyatvena jaganniṣṭhabrahmābhedasya brahmasamānasattākatvābhāvenāpādanāsambhavāt |
anyathā''ropitarajatānuyogikābhedavacchukterapyapāramārthikatvaṃ syādityabhisandhipūrvikāmāśaṅkāmudghāṭya dūṣayati -- brahmaṇa iti || dharmisamānasattāko'bheda iti || abheda iti chedaḥ |
apāramārthikapratiyogikābheda ityarthaḥ |
tasya vyāptigrahāya sahacāraṃ darśayati -- ghaṭādāviti || so'stītyapāramārthikapratiyogikadharmisamānasattākabheda ityarthaḥ |
etena prapañce brahmabhedasya mithyātvamāśritya kṛtā śaṅkā'pi tulyanyāyenopapāditaprāyā bhavatīti na nyūnatā |
tatpakṣasya śaṅkāyāmevānabhipretatve pūrvaṃ sāmānyoktyā brahmapratiyogikaprapañcabhede prapañcapratiyogikabrahmabhede ca vyabhicārabādhayoruktatvenaikatra taddoṣamuddhṛtyānyatra tadakaraṇe śaṅkāyā asāmāñjasyaprasaṅgāt |
na ca bhedadvaye vyabhicārādiśaṅkāyā abhipretatve brahmaprapañcabheda ityekavacanāsaṅgatiriti vācyam |
prapañcaikasyābhāvena brahmaghaṭabhedaghaṭabrahmabhedapaṭabhedādisaṅgrahāya jātyaikavacanatvāt |
apāramārthikatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākasvasamānasattākabhedaśūnyatvaṃ yatra tatrāpārarthikatvamiti vyāpterghaṭādiprapañcāntarbhāvena tavāpi siddhatvāt |
apāramārthika[tva]prapañcapratiyogi[tā]kasvasamānasattākabhede śūnyatve brahmaṇo'pāramārthikatvaṃ durvāraṃ syāt |
atastasya satyatve'bhyupagantavye bādhakavyabhicārau durvārāvevetyāśayena samādhatte || tarhīti |
svasamānasattākabheda iti || atra svasamānasattākabheda iti chedaḥ |
anye tu || bādho veti || brahmaprapañcabhedasyāpāramārthikatve dvayoḥ pāramārthikatvamapāramārthikatvaṃ vā syāt |
na tvekasyaiva pāramārthikatvamaparasyāpāramārthikatvamityavaśyaṃ bhedasyāpāramārthikatve vaktavye tatra mithyātvasādhane bādha ityarthaḥ |
nanu brahmaprapañcabhedasyāpāramārthikatve'pi tayoḥ pāramārthikatvāpāramārthikatvavyavasthā prakārāntareṇa bhaviṣyatītyāśayena śaṅkate -- brahmaṇa iti || dharmisattāsamānasattākabhedatadabhāvāpāramārthikatvapāramārthikatvavyavasthāpakāvityatra viparīta eva niyamyaniyāmakabhāvaḥ kiṃ na syādityāha tarhītyāhuḥ |
tanmate ubhayatrāpi bheda ityeva chedaḥ |
prapañcavaditi dṛṣṭāntastu apāramārthikatvāśaya eva |
taduktirapi svarūpato mithyātvāpādanāya |
anyathopahitarūpeṇa mithyātvāṅgīkārādiṣṭāpattiḥ syādityavadheyam |
kvacitpustake vākyadvaye'pi dharmisamānasattākabheda iti pāṭho dṛśyate |
tatacaiṣa (tatraiṣa) evārtha iti dhyeyam || prātibhāsikatvamiti || brahmajñānetarabādhyatvādirūpamityarthaḥ || na ceti || tatprātibhāsikatvam |
anyonyasminniti yuṣmadasmatpratyayagocarayorityanena prakṛtayorātmānātmanoranyonyasmin ātmanyanātmano'nātmanyātmano'dhyasya sarvo'pi vyavahāra iti bhāṣyārthaḥ || gauro'hamiti || gauravarṇaśarīrasyāhaṃ padārthātmani bhāsamānābhedasyādhyastatvādityarthaḥ |
nanvanyataracandrasyāropitatvādāropitānāropitabhedasya satya(sādhya)tvātsādhyavikalo dṛṣṭānta iti sarvagatatvādhikaraṇatattvaprakāśikāyāmuktam |
na ca candrāntarāropasya pūrvapakṣadūṣitasya siddhānte vyavasthāpanapūrvaṃ sādhyavaikalyasya dṛḍhīkṛtatvāt (tat)parityāgena dūṣaṇāntarasyaiva kathanācca tadvirodha iti cenna |
yataścandrāntarāropa eva yuktisiddho bhavati |
tathā hi |
candrāntarārope'ṅgulicalanādinaikacandracalanasyāparacandracalanābhāvasya ca pratyayānupapattiḥ |
kriyāmātrasya candra evāropasvīkārāt |
evaṃ vakratvāderapyubhayatrāpi tulyatayā pratyayaprasaṅgaśca |
ato bhīṣaṇatvādiviśiṣṭasarpasya rajjāviva vakratvacalanādiviśiṣṭacandrasyaivāropo vācyaḥ |
na ca pūrvapakṣoktarītyā'dhiṣṭhānānupapattiḥ |
purovṛtticandrasyaivādhiṣṭhānatvasambhavāt |
na ca purovṛtticandrabhinnatayā pratīyamānasya kathaṃ (tat)tādātmyena bhānamiti vācyam |
tattādātmyabhānaṃ vinaiva candrasya bhramagocaratāsambhavāt |
na ca bhramaviṣayasya niyamenādhiṣṭhānatādātmyenaiva bhānamiti niyamaḥ |
paramate sphaṭikādau lauhityarūpapratibimbādhyāsasthale pratibimbasya, sarvapakṣe'traiva bhrame bhāsamānakriyādeḥ, sthireṣveva parvatādiṣu calanabhramasthale calanādeḥ, keśoṇḍrakādibhramasthale ca dīrdhatayā bhāsamānaraśmyādīnāṃ ca tādātmyena bhāsamānavastvantarānirūpaṇāt |
parvatādivṛttirūpādīnāmāropyamāṇādatiriktatvenaiva bhānāt |
keśoṇḍrakādisthale teṣāṃ dīpasambandhitvenaiva bhānena tadatiriktatvasyaiva darśanāt |
antarālabhāge tattādātmyāpannatayā bhāsamānasyaindriyakasyānupalambhāt |
sphaṭikarūpe lauhitābhedādhyāsa ityuktāvapi mukhādhyāsasthale'gatireva darpaṇāntargatatayaiva bhānāt |
na ca māyāvādinā bauddhena vāniradhiṣṭhānabhramaḥ svīkartuṃ śakyo'pasiddhāntaprasaṅgāt |
na ca bhramaviṣayībhūtatādātmyena bhāsamānatvasyaiva tadadhiṣṭhānarūpatvāccandrādīnāṃ cātathātvātkathamadhiṣṭhānatvamiti vācyam |
uktabhrame'dhiṣṭhānanirūpaṇānyathā'nupapattyā śuktirajatādibhrame rajatādau bhāsamānasattvasyādhiṣṭhānabhūtasatpadārthanirūpaṇānyathā'nupapattyā ca tadbhrama[tadupa]janakakaraṇasannikarṣāśrayatvasyaivādhiṣṭhānatvapadārthatvābhyupagamāt śuktikāsannikṛṣṭacakṣuṣā doṣavaśādrajatākārajñānamivoktadoṣasahitacakṣuṣā saha candradīpādīnāṃ sannikarṣavaśādeva candradīpādiṣu pratīyamānavakratvādimaccandrāntarabhedadīrghākāraraśmyādyākārakajñānotpatyaṅgīkārāt |
tatra bhāsamānāropitacandrasyāsattvena satyacandre bhāsamānatadbhedasya satyatvāt |
na caika evāyaṃ candra iti bhedabādhānubhavavirodhaḥ |
anayoranyatara eka eva cūto'paraḥ panasa iti cūtapanasayoḥ svarūpasvatvāvagāhitvapūrvakaṃ tayostādātmyamātrasya bādhāvagāhyanubhavavadanayorabheda eva na tu bheda ityanubhavābhāvāt |
pratyuta bhāsamānayoranayormadhye etatpradeśastha eka eva candro'sti na tvapara iti bhedāvaśeṣapūrvakaṃ candrasyaiva bādhānubhavāt |
na cāropaprayojakasmaraṇānupapattirūpapūrvapakṣoktadoṣaḥ |
tasya pradhānajñānamudrayā apekṣitatvena paridṛśyamānacandrasyādhiṣṭhānasyāpi pradhānatve bādhakābhāvena tajjñānamātreṇāropopapatteḥ |
tajjñānaṃ cānubhavarūpaṃ yathākathañcitsmaraṇarūpaṃ vā |
tvanmate bhedabhramahetubhedajñānavadityubhayathā'pi na doṣaḥ |
candrāntarāropo'pi bauddhasammataḥ |
yatsvarūpata eva kalpitaṃ tanmithyā yathā'ṅgulyavaṣṭambhanimitto'paraścandramā iti viṣṇuttattvanirṇayaṭīkāyāṃ tanmatopanyāsāt |
itthaṃ ca candrāntarasyāropitatvena tadbhedasya satyatvena ṭīkoktaṃ dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyaṃ sudṛḍhameva |
etadgranthe tadvyavasthāpanābhāvastu bhedatvahetorasiddherapyuktatvena tādṛśāsiddherbhedāropapakṣāśrayatātparyeṇāpyupapādayituṃ śakyatvenaikasminneva pakṣe nirbharasyāpradarśitatvāttadvyavasthāpanānāvaśyakateti sahṛdayairākalanīyam || caturthe tviti || vimataṃ tattvata ityanumāna ityarthaḥ || upādhibhedeneti || pṛthivyādisamudāyatvenetyarthaḥ || padārthāntarābhāveneti || na cāpekṣābuddhyā samudāyākhyaṃ nānāsamudāyaṃ śaṅkyam |
ekaṃ viśiṣṭamutpadyate |
tatra na vakṣyamāṇadūṣaṇānāṃ prasaktiḥ |
tatrāṃśavṛttidharmāṇāmanaṅgīkārāt |
anyathā daṇḍasambandhāddaṇḍaviśiṣṭapuruṣotpattau daṇḍatvapuruṣatvajātyoḥ sāṅkaryāpattiriti vācyam |
vartamānakālamātravṛttisamudāyasya tathā vaktuṃ śakyatve'pyatītānāgatānāṃ jñānānāṃ melanasya kadā'pyayogena prakṛte tādṛśasamudāyotpattervaktumaśakyatvādityabhiprāyāt |
ghaṭāditulyatayā pṛthivyādyārabdhatvāṅgīkāre vakṣyamāṇadūṣaṇānāmapi saṅgateḥ || bādha iti || bhedasya pratyakṣataḥ satyatvenaiva niścitatvāditi bhāvaḥ || pṛthivītvādīnāmiti || dārupāṣāṇādinirmitagṛhādāvapyeṣa(pyevaṃ) doṣaḥ syāditi cenna |
tasya sarvapratyakṣādisiddhatvena yathākathañciddoṣaparihārasambhave'pi prakṛtasyāprāmāṇikatvādevaite doṣā abhyuccayatvenoktā ityabhiprāyāt || guṇādīnāmiti || dravyatvāpattiśceti || samavāyikāraṇatvasya dravyalakṣaṇatvādityarthaḥ || niravayavānāmiti || na ca niravayavaparamāṇūnāṃ dravyārambhakatvadarśanātkathametaditi vācyam |
gaganasyaikasya dravyāntarārambhakatve loke gaganavyavahārabhāgipadārthasyaivābhāvaprasaṅgaḥ |
ghaṭārambhakaparamāṇūnāmiva tasya pṛthagapratīteḥ |
aṃśata ārambhakatvaṃ tu niravayavasyānupapannamiti tātparyeṇa niravayavatvaviśeṣaṇopādānāt || ākaśabhedasādhanamiti || ākāśāṃśānāṃ svābhāvikabhedasādhanamityarthaḥ |
anyatra nyāyāmṛtādau |
tathā ca parābhimatasvāntarbhedaśūnya(rūpa)tvasādhyasyāpi tatra vaikalyamityāśayaḥ |
paroktahetumapi dūṣayati -- bhedatveneti || idaṃpratīyamānatvamityekapadam |
idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaviśeṣasāmānyākārabodhaupayikaṃ pratīyamānatvapadaṃ yatparaṃ tatpratītiviśeṣaṇaṃ kimityarthaḥ |
tena na vakṣyamāṇavikalpānupapattyavakāśaḥ || tavāsiddheriti || bhedamātrasya mithyātvena tatpratīteḥ pramātvāyogāt pakṣadṛṣṭāntayorhetvasiddherityarthaḥ || mamāsiddheriti || ghaṭapaṭādibhedāsyāropitānāropitacandrabhedasya ca satyatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
na ca candrāntarasyāropitatve gauravam |
candrasamīpadeśe candrāntarāropaṃ vinā tatpratītyanupapatteḥ |
mithyaiva candrāntaraṃ pratyabhādityanusmṛteśca |
etena dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalpaṃ ca sūcitam |
|| candrabhedapratyakṣapratīteriti || tanmate candrabhedapratyakṣasya bhramatvena prātibhāsikatvāt vyāvahārikaprātibhāsikayoranugatasāmānyābhāvena hetvasiddherityarthaḥ |
anyathā bāṣpāropitadhūmādimati vahnyādyabhāvādanumānocchedaprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ |
abhyupetyāpi doṣamāha -- bhedābhedeti || bhedābhedayoryo bhedastatretyarthaḥ || tasyāpi pakṣatva iti || tatsādhāraṇavimateḥ pakṣatāvacchedakatvāt pakṣe vyabhicārasya cādoṣatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu bhedābhedabhedasyāpi mithyātvāṅgīkārāt na bādha ityata āha -- bhedābhedayoriti || mithyātvaṃ syāditi || abhedasyāpi bhedatvena pakṣatvāt hetumattvācceti bhāvaḥ |
atraiva doṣāntaramāha -- vyāvahāriketi || pāramārthikaḥ syāditi || abhedasya pārāmarthikatve tadabhinnabhedasya mithyātvāyogāditi bhāvaḥ |
bhedābhedayoḥ vyāvahārikabhedasattvāt kathamabheda ityata uktam || paramārthato'bhinna iti || vyāvahārikabhedaśca na pāramārthikābhedavirodhītyāśayaḥ |
atra dṛṣṭānto vyāvahāriketyādiḥ |
sthalāntare vyabhicāramāha -- pāramārthiketi || pāramārthikaṃ brahma apāramārthikaśca prapañca ityanayorbhedasya pāramārthikatvāt tatrāpi vyabhicāra ityarthaḥ || tasyāpīti || brahmaprapañcabhedasyāpītyarthaḥ |
tasyāpāramārthikatve ca brahmavat prapañcasya pāramārthikatvāpattyā tadantargatabhede punarbādha evetyarthaḥ |
syādevaṃ yadi brahmapratiyogikapāramārthikabhedaśūnyatvaṃ brahmadharmāpādakaṃ syāt |
na caivam |
api tu brahmapratiyogikadharmisattāsamasattākabhedaśūnyatvamevāto noktadoṣa ityāśaṅkate -- brahmaṇīti || bhedo brahmabhedaḥ || prapañcavat syāditi || tulyanyāyena prapañcapratiyogikadharmisattāsamānasattākabhedaśūnyatvasya prapañcadharmāpādakatvaprāpteriti bhāvaḥ |
candrabheda iti sādhyavyāpakatvapradarśanārtham |
yadyapi mithyātvavati ghaṭadau na prātibhāsikatvam |
tathāpi bhedatvāvacchinnasādhyavyāpakatvādupādhitvamiti || nanvasya sādhanavyāpakatvāt nopādhitvam |
bhedatvena pakṣe'pyupādhyanumānādityāśaṅkāṃ vārayati -- na ceti || tathā cāpasiddhānta iti bhāvaḥ |
śaṅkarabhāṣyavirodhameva vivaṇoti -- gauro'hamiti || dehātmābhedasya mithyātvānaṅgīkāre tadviṣayasya gauro'hamityādijñānasyābādhitaviṣayatvena pramātvāpattyā tadbhramatvavarṇanaparabhāṣyavirodha iti bhāvaḥ |
prāmāṇyaprasaṅgācceti pāṭhe tu dūṣaṇāntarābhidhānametaditi jñātavyam |
yattu jīveśābhedasyāpi mithyātvamiṣṭameveti noktābhāsasāmyāpattiḥ |
na caivaṃ bhedasya satyatvāpattiḥ |
śrutyā dṛśyatvāvacchedenaiva mithyātvāvadhāraṇāditi tat tuccham |
bhedābhedobhayamithyātvasya vyāhatatvena śrutyāpi pratipādayitumaśakyatvāt |
upapannasyaiva śrutyarthatvāt |
anyathā'bhimānyadhikaraṇavirodhāditi |
caturthānumāne vimataṃ jagat tattvataḥ svāntarbhedahīnaṃ meyatvāt gaganavat ityatra gaganādivat jagataḥ svatantrapadārthatve tadavayavarūpatadekadeśaprasiddhyā tadabhedarūpasādhyasiddhiḥ syāt |
na caivam |
nānāvastūnāmeva senāvanādipadārthatvavat samūhatvaviśeṣopādhinā jagatpadārthatvāt |
tadavacchinne ca sādhyasya viśeṣaṇāprasiddhyā bādha ityāha -- caturthe tviti || nanvastu tarhi gaganādivat jagat padārthāntarameva |
na caitāvatā pṛthivyādīnāṃ kathamabhedasiddhiriti vācyam |
pṛthivyādīnāṃ jagadupādānatvena tadekadeśatvādityāśaṅkyā'ha || pṛthivyādīnāmiti || jātisāṅkaryaprasaṅga iti || pṛthivyādyārabdhasya pṛthivyāditvanimāditi bhāvaḥ || jagadantarbhūtānāmiti || jagadārambhakatve hetuḥ || dravyatvāpattiriti || upādānatvarūpārambhakatvasya dravyadharmatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
niravayavānāṃ vibhudravyāṇāṃ jagadākhyadravyārambhakatve tato'pakṛṣṭaparimāṇatvaprāptyā vibhutvavyāghāta iti draṣṭavyam || antyāvayavitvahāniśceti || antyāvavitvasya dravyānārambhakadravyatvarūpatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
idamupalakṣaṇam |
pṛthivyārabdhasya jagato'pi jagadantargatatvena jagadārambhakatvāvaśyambhāvena jagadantarasvīkārāpattyā'navasthā ca bodhyā |
nanu viśiṣṭaghaṭādiḥ svāntarbhedahīna iti sādhanānnoktadoṣa ityāśaṅkya sādhyavikalpena dūṣayati -- ghaṭādipadārthānāmiti || sādhyavikala iti || mayā gagane ghaṭādipratiyogikapāramārthikabhedābhyupagamāditi bhāvaḥ |
svaikadeśabhedābhāvasādhane'pyāha -- ākāśeti || ākāśaikadeśānāmākāśena mithaśca tāttvikabhedasādhanamanyatra nyāyāmṛtādau kṛtamato dṛṣṭānte sādhyavaikalyamiti bhāvaḥ |
evaṃ vimatau paramārthataḥ parasparābhinnau prameyatvāt parasparavat ityādyaśeṣābhilaṣitabhedasādhakānumāneṣu pratyakṣādipramāṇabādhaḥ |
sarvatra sākṣiṇaḥ pārameśvarasya ca bhedapratyakṣasya pramāṇasiddhatvāt |
vimatau na paramārthataḥ parasparābhinnau parasparaviruddhadharmavattvāt vyatirekeṇa parasparavat ityanumānāt, dvā suparṇetyādyāgamācca |
parasparaviruddhadharmānadhikaraṇatvamantataḥ pakṣetaratvamapyupādhiḥ |
vimatau vyavahārato parasparābhinnau meyatvāt parasparavat ityābhāsasāmyam |
anukūlatarkābhāvādevāprayojakatvaṃ cetyevamādyantadoṣaduṣṭatvāt nādvaitamupādeyam |
yadatra kenacitpralapitam |
so'yaṃ bhedaḥ kiṃ dhārmisvarūpastaddharmo vā |
nādyaḥ |
bhinnamiti pratītau prakāratvānupapatteḥ |
ghaṭabhedapratītyoraviśeṣaprasaṅgācca |
viṣayavailakṣaṇyābhāvāt |
grāhakasāmagrīvaicitryānupapatteśca |
ghaṭapratītau bhedasandehānupapatteśca |
ajñātāṃśābhāvāt |
bhedasya sapratiyogitvānupapatteśca |
dharmiṇo ghaṭāderniṣpratiyogikatvāt |
rūpāderapi dharmisvarūpatvāpatteśca |
bhedavadabhedasyāpi dharmisvarūpatvena bhedābhedayorabhedāpatteśca |
tatpratītyoraviśeṣaprasaṅgācca |
tatpadayoḥ paryāyatvāpatteśca |
etena kāraṇatvādīnāṃ dharmisvarūpatvamapyapāstaṃ veditavyam |
dvitīye bhāvo'bhāvo vā ? nādyaḥ |
jātyādirūpatve sapratiyogikatvādyanupapatteḥ |
pṛthaktvādirūpatve ca guṇādau bhedapratyayānupapatteḥ |
anyonyābhāvenaivopapattau pṛthaktvarūpaguṇāntarānaṅgīkārācca |
nāntyaḥ |
bhedasyānyonyābhāvarūpatve kapisaṃyogavati vṛkṣe tadbhedasattvaprasaṅgāt |
mūle vṛkṣo na kapisaṃyogīti pratīteḥ |
atyantābhāvarūpatve ca nāsti na bhavatīti pratītyoraviśeṣaprasaṅgāt |
svasminnapi svātyantābhāvasattvena svabhedabuddhyāpatteśca |
tadbhedasya taddharmātyantābhāvarūpatve ca prathamadoṣānistārāt |
kiñca pratiyogipramāṇayorabhāvānna bhedasiddhiḥ |
tathāhi na tāvat ghaṭabhedasya ghaṭaḥ pratiyogī |
ghaṭavati ghaṭabhedānupapatteḥ |
laghavādabhāvatvāvacchedenaiva pratiyogivirodhāvadhāraṇāt |
kiñca bhedasyābhedābhāvatvenābhedasyaiva tatpratiyogitvaṃ syāt |
na cābhedo ghaṭādisvarūpa eveti vaktuṃ śakyam |
ghaṭavati ghaṭabhedānupapatteḥ |
na cābhedasyāpi bhedapratiyogitvaṃ sambhavati |
tajjñānasya bhedajñānādhīnatvāt bhedajñānasya ca pratiyogijñānādhīnatvenānyonyāśrayāt |
ata eva na tatra pratyakṣaṃ pramāṇam |
anyonyāśrayāt |
evaṃ ghaṭe paṭabhedābhedayoḥ saṃśayaniścayadaśāyāṃ paṭe ghaṭabhedaniścayādarśanāt ghaṭabhedaniścaye paṭabhedaniścayasya hetutvaṃ vācyam |
`yatsaṃśayavyatirekaniścayau yatra pratibandhakau tanniścayastaddhetu'riti niyamāt |
evaṃ paṭabhedaniścaye ghaṭabhedaniścayasya hetutvamityanyonyāśrayaḥ |
kiñca pratiyogitvagrāhakasannikarṣābhāvānna pratiyogitvaviśeṣitabhedapratyakṣasambhavaḥ |
jñānameva sannikarṣa iti cet hanta tarhi tajjñānaṃ pratiyogitvaviṣayaṃ bhedajñānāpekṣamityanyonyāśrayaḥ |
nāpyanumānam |
devadatto yajñadattabhedavān tadduḥkhānusandhātṛtvādityāderaupādhikabhedenāpyupapatteḥ |
ata eva nāgamaḥ |
pāramārthikabhedāsādhakatvāt |
tasmāt sarvā'pi bhedapratītiranirvacanīyāvidyāvilāsa eveti yuktam |
tadukta`masambhavanīyāvabhāsacaturā hi se'ti |
tadetat sarvamapi viśeṣāṅgīkāraparāhatam |
tathāhi -- bhedastāvat dharmisvarūpastaddharmaśca bhavati |
abhede'pi dharmadharmibhāvo viśeṣaprabhāvāt |
ata eva bhinnamiti pratītau prakāratvamapi |
ghaṭabhedrapratītyorvailakṣaṇyamapi viśeṣādeva |
na ca viśeṣasya taṭasthasya pratītivaicitryaniyāmakatve viṣayavaiyarthyāt vijñānavādaprasaṅgaḥ |
viṣayavidhayā tathātve cānubhavavirodha iti vācyam |
ghaṭatvabhedatvarūpaprakāravaicitryādeva pratītivaicitryam |
prakārayostu viśeṣakṛtaṃ vaicitryamityatra doṣābhāvāt |
ata eva grāhakasāmagrīvaicitryamapyupapadyate |
grāhyavaicitryasyopapāditatvāt |
etena ghaṭapratītau bhedasandehānupapattirityapi nirastam |
ghaṭatvena tatpratītāvapi bhedatvena tadapratīteḥ |
ghaṭatvabhedatvayorabhede'pi viśeṣasadbhāvādeva tadupapatteḥ |
bhedasya sapratiyogikatvamapi viśeṣabalādeva |
na caivaṃ ghaṭo na sapratiyogika iti vyavahārānupapattiḥ, sapratiyogikabhedābhinnatvāditi vācyam |
tadabhinnatve'pi tadviśeṣasyaiva tadvyavahāraviṣayatvāt |
abhede bhedakāryanirvāhako viśeṣaḥ |
rūpāderdharmisvarūpatvaṃ tviṣyata eva |
na caivaṃ rūpapratītau rasādipratītiprasaṅgaḥ |
rasatvādinā tadapratīterviśeṣata evopapatteḥ |
bhedābhedayorabhedaśceṣṭa eva |
parāpekṣayā bhedasya svāpekṣayā'bhedatve bādhakābhāvāt |
na caivaṃ ghaṭe svāpekṣayeva parāpekṣayā'pi abhedavyavahāraprasaṅgaḥ |
svarūpe viśeṣabhāvāditi vācyam |
viśeṣābhāve svāpekṣayetyasyaivānupapatteḥ |
tadbhāve tu tata eva svābhedo na parābhedaḥ |
ata eva na bhedābhedapratītyoraviśeṣaḥ |
na vā tatpadayoḥ paryāyatvam |
pratipādyavaicitryasyoktatvāt |
etena kāraṇatvādikamapi vyākhyātam |
yaccoktaṃ bhāvo'bhāvo veti, tatra brūmo yo bhedo yatra pratīyate sa tatsvarūpa iti |
na caivaṃ ghaṭātyantābhāve pratīyamānaghaṭabhedasya ghaṭātyantābhāvarūpatayā tasya ca ghaṭe'pi sattvāt ghaṭabhedapratītiprasaṅga iti vācyam |
ghaṭe ghaṭabhedatvena tadasattvāt |
na ca svarūpapratiyogipratiyogitāvacchedakāviśeṣe bhedatvātyantābhāvatvayorapyaviśeṣa iti vācyam |
ghaṭaniṣedho'tyantābhāvo ghaṭatādātmyaniṣedho'nyonyābhāva iti viśeṣāt |
ghaṭo na bhavatīti sākṣāddhaṭasya pratiyogitvaṃ bhāsata iti cet ghaṭo'pi pratiyogyeva |
tādātmyaṃ bhedapratiyogitāvacchedakamityapyāhuḥ |
etena saṃyogena saṃyogyatyantābhāvasya saṃyogarūpasaṃyogitādātmyāvacchinnapratiyogitākatayā tadbhedatvāpattiriti nirastam |
saṃyogasya saṃyogyatyantābhāvāpratiyogitvāt |
tadavacchedakatve'pi saṃyogatvenaiva |
na tu tādātmyatveneti viśeṣāt |
vastuto'nyonyātyantābhāvayoḥ sadasatpratiyogikatvābhyāmasti mahān viśeṣaḥ |
etena nāsti, na bhavatīti pratītyoraviśeṣaprasaṅgo'pi nirastaḥ |
yadapi mūle vṛkṣaḥ kapisaṃyogī neti pratītermūlāvacchinnavṛkṣe kapisaṃyogibhedaḥ siddhyediti tadiṣṭameva viśiṣṭabhedavādinām |
na caivaṃ pratyabhijñāvirodhaḥ |
śākhāmūlādyavacchinnayorabhedasyāpi sattvāt |
na ca bhedābhedayorvirodhaḥ |
viśeṣabalādavirodhopapatteḥ |
na caivaṃ viśeṣa evāstu, kiṃ bhedeneti vācyam |
bhedasya pramitatvāt |
yattvabhāvatvāvacchedena pratiyogivirodhāvadhāraṇāt ghaṭavati ghaṭabhedānupapattiriti |
tadasat |
ghaṭavati ghaṭaprāgabhāvāderanupapatteḥ |
prāgabhāvādeḥ pratiyogideśavṛttitvasya pramitatvāt |
pratiyogikālāvṛttitvarūpo virodhastatrāvadhāryata iti cet tarhi bhedasya pratiyogideśakālavṛttitvasya pramitatvāt pratiyogivastvavṛttitvarūpo virodho'trāvadhāryatām |
pratiyoginaikadaikatrānavasthānarūpavirodhaḥ prāgabhāvādyanugata iti cet svābhāvenaikadaikatrānavasthānarūpavirodho bhedādyanugata ityastu |
vastutaḥ pratiyogitāvacchedakasambandhena pratiyogivirodhaḥ sarvābhāvasādhāraṇaḥ |
bhedasyāpi tādātmyena pratiyogiviruddhatvāt |
yadapi bhedasyābhedābhāvatvānna ghaṭādestatpratiyogitvamiti, tadapi na |
prāgabhāvāderiva bhedasya svābhāvābhāvatve'pi ghaṭādipratiyogikatvāvirodhāt |
abhedasya ghaṭādisvarūpatve'pi na doṣaḥ |
abhedatvenābhedasya pratiyoginyeva sattvāt |
ghaṭatvaghaṭābhedatvayorviśeṣasadbhāvādeva ghaṭatvena ghaṭavati ghaṭābhedatvena tadasattvasambhavāt |
yadapi bhedābhedayoḥ parasparajñānādhīna jñānatvādanyonyāśraya iti |
tattuccham |
ghaṭābhedajñānahetorghaṭabhedājñānasyāghaṭatvena ghaṭajñānamātrādhīnatvāt |
ata eva bhedapratyakṣamapyupapadyate |
yadapi ghaṭapaṭabhedayoranyonyajñānādhīnajñānatvādanyonyāśraya iti |
tadapi na sādhu |
ghaṭapaṭabhedayoranyonyasaṃśayasya grāhyasaṃśayavadanyonyajñānāpratibandhakatvenānyonyajñānādhīnajñānatvāsiddheḥ |
anyathā grāhyasaṃśayasyāpi pratibandhakatvaprasaṅgena vastumātrasyaiva svajñānādhīnajñānatvāpatyā jñānamātrāpalāpāpatteḥ |
vipakṣe bādhakābhāvena yatsaṃśayetyādiniyamasyāprayojakatvācca |
yadapi pratiyogitvāṃśe sannikarṣābhāvāt pratiyogiviśeṣitabhedapratyakṣānupapattiriti |
tadatimandam |
nirvikalpakopasthitereva sannikarṣatvasambhavāt |
tasyāśca bhedajñānānapekṣatvenānyonyāśrayānavakāśarat |
pratiyogitvasya pratiyogisvarūpatvena tadupasthitestatsannikarṣatvasambhavācca |
evamanumāne dharmisattāsamānasattākatvena bhedaviśeṣaṇāduddeśyasiddhiḥ |
na cāprayojakatvam |
vipakṣe caitrādermaitrādiduḥkhānusandhānāpatterbādhakatvāt |
caitramaitrayoraikyāccaitracaitanyasya maitrīyaduḥkhādyanusandhānamiṣṭameveti vācyam |
maitraśarīrotpannaduḥkhādeścaitraśarīre'nusandhānasyāniṣṭatvāt |
na ca tattaccharīragatāntaḥkaraṇabhedādanusandhānābhāva iti vācyam |
yoginyanupapatteruktatvāt |
ata evā'gamo'pi svābhāvikabhedameva grāhayati |
attyanaśnan ityuktāśanānaśanayoḥ svābhāvikabhedena vinā'nupapatteḥ |
muktau bhedaśrutīnāmudāhṛtatvācca |
na ca bhedasya vastusvarūpatvāṅgīkārāt svarūpasya ca svābhāvikatve vivādābhāvādarthāntaramiti vācyam |
yato na bhedo vastusvarūpamātram |
dharmadharmibhāvādyanupapatteḥ |
kintu tadviśeṣa ityuktam |
tathā ca dharmatvaparapratiyogikatvādiviśeṣitavastusvarūpaviśeṣasya sādhyatvānna doṣaḥ |
atha vastusvarūpa eva viśeṣasadbhāve kiṃ mānamiti cedidaṃ tāvat svarūpasya vastvabhinnatve'pi vastunaḥ svarūpamiti sambandhānubhavādeva svarūpasya vastuto viśeṣasiddhiḥ |
evaṃ -- `yathodakaṃ durge vṛṣṭaṃ parvateṣu vidhāvati |
evaṃ dharmān pṛthak paśyaṃstānevānuvidhāvati', `ekadhaivānudraṣṭavyaṃ', `neha nānāsti' ityādiśrutibhiḥ brahmaṇastaddharmāṇāṃ cābhedaḥ siddhaḥ |
dṛśyate ca `satyaṃ jñānamanantaṃ brahma', `ānandaṃ brahmaṇo vidvān' ityādau viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvādinā vyavahāraḥ |
tadubhayavirodhādarthādākṣipyate viśeṣaḥ |
evaṃ ghaṭataddharmāṇāmapi pṛthaganupalambhenaikatve siddhe dharmadharmibhāvāderviśeṣa eva śaraṇamiti ||
yattvanirvācyāvidyāsāmarthyādeva sarvalaukikavaidikavyavahāropapattau kiṃ viśeṣeṇeti |
tattuccham |
avidyāyā viṣayanirapekṣatve vijñānavādaprasaṅgāt |
tatsāpekṣatve ca tatsvabhāvabhūtayorabhedadharmatvayorvirodhadhrauvyāt |
abheda eva svābhāviko dharmatvaṃ tu mithyaiveti cet tarhi tatpratipādakaśrutīnām atattvāvedakatvādaprāmāṇyaprasaṅgaḥ |
paramārthato nirviśeṣacaitanyasvarūpamātre sarvaśrutīnāṃ tātparyānnāyaṃ doṣa iti cenna |
dṛṣṭādṛṣṭatyāgakalpanāprasaṅgāt |
pratītaviśeṣaparityāgenāpratītanirviśeṣasvīkārāt |
śabdato nirviśeṣānubhavasyādṛṣṭatvācca |
na ca so'yamityādau tattedantāviśiṣṭayorabhedānvayabādhena svarūpamātrabodho dṛṣṭa eveti vācyam |
tatrāpyatītasambandharūpatattopalakṣitasya tatkālasambandhadhvaṃsarūpatattāviśiṣṭasya vā vartamānakālasambandharūpedantvaviśiṣṭenābhedānvayāt |
kiñcādvaitavāde tāvadavidyaiva nopapadyate |
vikalpadoṣāt |
satyatve dvaitaprasaṅgāt |
mithyātve kalpakābhāvaḥ |
svakalpitatve ātmāśrayāt |
avidyāntarakalpitatve cānavasthā |
tathā ca kuto dharmatvādermithyātvaparikalpanāvakāśaḥ |
nanvevamanupapannatvaṃ mithyātve sādhakameva |
upapannatve hi satyatvaṃ syāditi cet tarhi lāghavādātmaivāvidyeti kalpyatām |
ātmano'vidyātvamavidyānivṛttāvātmanivṛttyāpattyā'nupapannamiti cedata eva mithyetyasya suvacatvāt |
ātmano'vidyātvamaprāmāṇikamiti cettaddharmasyāvidyātve kiṃ pramāṇamupalabdhamāyuṣmatā pratītapramāṇānāṃ nirastatvāditi dik |
nanvevaṃ viśeṣo'pi nopapadyate |
vikalpadoṣāt |
tathāhi |
dharmadharmiṇorviśeṣo yadi dharmadharmibhyāṃ bhinnastarhi bheda evāstu kimantargaḍunā viśeṣeṇa |
yadyabhinnastarhi dharmadharmiṇāveva sto na viśeṣaḥ |
tathā ca dharmadharmibhāvo'pi na syāditi cenmaivam |
abhinnatve'pi viśeṣasya tanmātratvābhāvena doṣābhāvāt |
viśeṣasya viśeṣitve viśeṣānavasthā syāditi cet vastvantaratvābhāvenādoṣatvāditi |
syādetat |
bhedaviśeṣayornityatvenānityaghaṭādisvarūpatvam |
anityatve cottarakālaṃ nirviśeṣābhedāpattiriti cenmaivam |
anityagatasyānityatve'pyuttarakālaṃ vastuna evābhāvena nirviśeṣābhinnavastvavaśeṣaprasaṅgābhāvāt |
nityagatasya nityatvena sarvābhedaprasaṅgasyātyantamayuktatvācca |
tasmājjaḍajīveśvarāṇāṃ mithaḥ svābhāvika eva pañcavidho bhedaḥ |
tathā hi śrutiḥ --
`jīveśvarabhidā caiva jaḍeśvarabhidā tathā |
jīvabhedo mithaścaiva jaḍajīvabhidā tathā |
mithaśca jaḍabhedo'yaṃ prapañco bhedapañcakaḥ ||
so'yaṃ satyo hyanādiśca sādiścennāśamāpnuyāt |
na ca nāśaṃ prayātyeṣa na cāsau bhrāntikalpitaḥ ||
kalpitaścennivarteta na cāsau vinivartate |
dvaitaṃ na vidyata iti tasmādajñānināṃ matam' || ityādiḥ ||
atra jīveśvarabhedatvaṃ nāma na jīveśvarobhayapratiyogikabhedatvam |
parasparabhedasyobhayapratiyogikatvābhāvenāsaṅgrahāt |
ata eva nobhayānuyogikabhedatvam |
ekasyobhayānuyogikatvābhāvāt |
nāpi jīveśvarānyatarapratiyogikabhedatvamanyatarānuyogikabhedatvaṃ vā |
jaḍeśvarabhedādeḥ pṛthagupādānavaiyarthyāt |
nāpi jīveśvarānyatarapratiyogyanuyogikabhedatvam |
mitho jīvabhedasya pṛthagupādānānupapatteḥ |
kintu jīveśvaradvitvasamānādhikaraṇapratiyogitānuyogitākabhedatvam |
jīveśvaragatapratiyogitvānuyogitvayorjīveśvaradvitvasamānādhikaraṇatvājjīveśvarabhedasaṅgrahaḥ |
jaḍeśvaragatapratiyogitvānuyogitvayoratathātvānna tatsaṅgrahaḥ |
na caivaṃ mithojīvabhedasaṅgrahāpattiḥ |
tatpratiyogitvānuyogitvayoḥ pratyekaṃ jīveśvaradvitvasamānādhikaraṇatvāditi vācyam |
svānuyogivṛttijīvaveśvaradvitvasamānādhikaraṇapratiyogitākabhedatvasya vivakṣitatvāt |
mitho jīvabhedasya ca svānuyogivṛtti yajjīveśvaradvitvaṃ tatsamānādhikaraṇapratiyogitākatvābhāvenāsaṅgrahāt |
etena jaḍeśvarabhedatvaṃ jaḍajīvabhedatvaṃ ca vyākhyātaṃ veditavyam |
mitho jīvabhedatvādikaṃ ca jīvapratiyogikajīvānuyogikabhedatvādirūpam |
jīvatvaṃ cātra bhagavatpratibimbatvameveti saṅkṣepaḥ |
nanvevamīśvarasya parabrahmatvānupapattiḥ |
tasya trividhaparicchedarāhityarūpatvāt |
deśakālaparicchedātiriktavastuparicchedasya tadbhedarūpatvāditi cenmaivam aprāmāṇikaṃ bhāṣiṣṭāḥ |
`atha kasmāducyate brahmeti bṛhantohyasmin guṇā' ityādinigamaniruktaniravadhikaniḥśeṣanirdoṣakalyāṇaguṇapūrṇatvasyaiva parabrahmatvarūpatvāt |
tasmāt jaḍajīveśvarāṇāṃ parasparabhedaprapañcasya pāramārthikatvāt parameśvaraparamaprasādasiddhaye sakalajagajjanmādikāraṇatvādyanantakalyāṇaguṇākaratayā sarvottamatvena sarvadaiva samupāsyaḥ śrīnārāyaṇa eveti siddham |
evaṃ sādhyaṃ nirākṛtya hetumapi nirākaroti || bhedatvenetyāninā || candrabhedasya mithyātvenāprāmāṇikatvātprāmāṇikāpra āmāṇikānugatasāmānyābhāvena bhedatvasāmānyaṃ naheturityatobhedatvena pratīyamānatvaṃ heturityuktam || tatra pratīyamānatvaṃ pratītigocaratvam |
tacca pratītisvarūpaṃ kīdṛśamityarthaḥ || pratītitvasāmānyamiti || pramā'pramānugataṃ pratītitvasāmānyamityarthaḥ || taveti || tvayābhedapratīteḥ pramātvānaṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ || pratīterapīti || yathā candrabhedasya mithyātvenāprāmāṇikatvātprāmāṇikāprāmāṇikānugatabhedatvasāmānyābhāvaḥ tathābhedapratīterapi mithyātvenāprāmāṇikatvātprāmāṇikāprāmāṇikānugatapratītatvarūpasāmānyābhāvena svarūpāsiddho heturityarthaḥ || bhedābhedeti || bhedaścābhedaśca bhedābhedau tayorbhedevyabhicāra ityarthaḥ |
tatra bhedatvaṃ vartatemithyātvaṃ nāstīti bhāvaḥ || bādha iti || mithyātvarūpasādhyābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu bhedo mithyaivāto na bādha ityata āha -- bhedābhedayoriti || apāramārthikatve mithyātve |
anena bhedo mithyetyādinā || abhedasyāpi mithyātvaṃ syāditi || yadi bhedābhedasayorbhedasya mithyātvaṃ tarhi mithyātvasyāsatvaparyāyatvena bhedābhedayorbheda eva nāstīti syāt |
bhedābhāvecatayorabheda eva syāt |
evaṃ cānenānumānena bhedasya mithyātve sādhite tadabhinnābhedasyāpi tatsyāt |
ata eva taddoṣaparihārāya bhedābhedasya sattveṅgīkārye bādho duṣparihara iti bhāvaḥ |
prakārāntareṇa bādhamupapādayati -- vyāvahāriketi || abhedasya khalu pareṇa pāramārthikatvamaṅgīkriyate |
yadyuktarītyā bhedābhedayorabhedaḥ syāt tarhi pāramārthikābhedābhinno bhedo'pi pāramārthikaḥ syāditi punastasya mithyātvasādhane bādhaḥ syāt |
nanu bhedābhedayoḥ pāramārthikābhede'pi vyāvahārikabhedasya pyaṅgīkārādabhesyapāramārthikatve pinabhedasya pāramārthikatvamityata uktaṃ vyāvahāriketyādi |
jīveśayovyovahārikabhedasadbhāve'pi tasyāprayojakatvena paramārthataḥ pāramārthika brahmābhinnatvena jīvasya yathā pāramārthikatvamaṅgīkriyate tathehāpītyarthaḥ |
sthalāntarevyabhicāramāha -- pāramārthikā apāramārthiketi || pāramārthikaṃ brahma |
apāramārthikaḥ prapañcaḥ |
anayorbhedatvena pratīyamānatvaṃ vartate |
mithyātvaṃ nāstīti vyubhicāra ityarthaḥ |
tasyāpi mithyātvānna vyabhicāra ityatobrahma prapañcayoḥ pāramārthika apāramārthikatvaviśeṣaṇaṃ tasyāpi mithyātvānna vyabhicāra ityatobrahmaṇo pāramārthikatvaṃ prapañcasyapāramārthikatvaṃ syāt |
ata eva taddoṣaparihārāya tayorbhedasya sattve vācye syādeva vyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ |
nanu brahmaprapañcayorbhedasya mithyātve'pi noktadoṣaḥ |
tathāhipāramārthikatve dharmisamasattākabhedarahitatvaṃ prayojakam |
āramārthikatve tudharmisamattākabhedavattvam |
evaṃ ca brahmaṇi dharmisattāsamānasavaśakaḥ pāramārthiko bhedo nāstīti tasya pāramārthikatvam |
prapañce dharmisamasattākavyāvahāriko bhedo'stīti tasyāpāramārthikatvam |
ato nabrahmaṇopāramārthikatvaṃ prapañcasya pāramārthikaṃ syādityāpādanaṃ yuktamityā śaṅkate -- brahmaṇo dharmīti || evamapi tvayā brahmaṇi prapañcapratiyogikadharmisamasattākabhedānaṅgīkārādevaprapañcābhinne brahmaṇi prapañcavadevāpāramārthikatvaṃ syādeveti pariharati -- neti || candrabheda ityupādheḥ sādhyavyāpakatvopapādanāyoktam |
tathāca yatra mithyātvaṃ tatra prātibhāsikatvaṃ yathā candrabhede |
ataḥ sādhyavyāpakatvam |
yatra bhedatvaṃ tatra prātibhāsikatvamiti naniyamaḥ |
hetumatyapivimatabhedeprātibhāsikatvābhāvena sādhanāvyāpakatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanu vimato bhedaḥ prātibhāsikobhedatvāccandrabhedavavaditi pakṣe piprātibhāsikatvaṃ sādhyate |
ataḥ sādhanavyāpakatvānnāyamupādhirityata āha -- sa ceti || vyāvahārikatveti || tvayāvimatabhedasya vyāvahārikatvāṅgīkāreṇedānīṃ prātibhāsikatvasādhane svavākyavirodha ityarthaḥ || sādhyavaikalyamiti || dehātmābhedasyāsatvatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
kuta ityāta āha -- tathātva iti || yadi dehātmabhedasya mithyātvaṃ na syāt tarhi tasya mithyātvapratipādakabhāṣyasyāvidyamānapratipādakonmattavākyatulyatvaṃ syādityarthaḥ |
anyonyasminnanyonyātmakatāmadhyasyeti śāṅkarabhāṣyasyātmani dehatādātmyaṃ dehe cātmatādātmyamāropyetyarthaḥ |
atrādhyasyetyanena mithyātvaṃ pratipādyata iti dhyeyam |
dūṣaṇāntaramāha -- gaurohamiti || yadi dehātmābhedasya satyatvaṃ tarhyātmana evadehatvena dehadharmāṇāṅgauratvādīnāmātmani satyatvena tadavagāhijñānaṃ tatvāvedakaṃ syāt |
naceṣṭāpattiḥ |
anyonyadharmāścadhyasyetyuttarabhāṣye ātmani dehadharmāṇāṃ mithyātvapratipādanavirodhāditi bhāvaḥ || caturtha iti || vimataṃ jagattattvataḥ svāntarbhedaśūnyaṃ dṛśyatvādbhagaganavadityatretyarthaḥ || nānetyādi || uktānumānena hi jagadākhyapadārthasya svaikadeśaiḥ paṭādidabhirbhedābhāvassādhyate |
tattadā syāt yadi ghaṭādīnāṃ jagadākhyapadārthaikadeśatvaṃ syāt |
na caitadasti |
ghaṭādinānāpadārthānāmeva kālādyupādhiviśeṣeṇa jagacchabdavācyatayā kālaghaṭādivyatirekeṇa jagadākhyapadārthāntarasyābhāvāt |
atra dṛṣṭāntassenāvanādivaditi |
yathā gajaturagādīnāmevaikasthānanivāsena senāśabdavācyatvaṃ yathā vā nānāvṛkṣāṇamekasthānanivāsena vanaśabdavācyatvaṃ tathehāpītyarthaḥ |
nanu nānāpadārthānāmeva jagacchabdavācyatvaṃ kintu taṃ tu bhinna paṭa ivanānāpadārthairjagadākhyapadārtha ārabhyate |
ato ghaṭādīnāntadekadeśatvasambhavānnoktadoṣa ityata āha -- pṛthivyādīnāmiti || sāṅkaryaprasaṅga iti || parasparaparihāreṇa vidyamānayorekatra samāveśaḥ sāṅkaryam |
pṛthivītvaparihāreṇa jalatvaṃ jale jalatvaparihāreṇa pṛthivītvaṃ pṛthivyām ubhayostadārabdhe jagati samāveśaḥ |
evamanyatrāpi |
ato jātimātrasya sāṅkaryaprasaṅga ityarthaḥ || dravyatvāpattiśceti || samavāyikāraṇasya dravyatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
niravayavānāṃ guṇakarmādīnām |
sāvayavapadārthasyaiva dravyārambhakatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu niravayavānāma piparamāṇūnāṃ dvyaṇukākhyadravyārambhakatvaṃ dṛṣṭamiti kathamidamaniṣṭamiti cenna |
dravyārambhakaśabdena mahaddravyārambhakatvasyābhipretatvāt || antyāvayavitvahāniśceti || kāryadravyatve sāti dravyāntarānārambhakatvaṃ hyantyāvayavitvam |
yadi ghaṭādīnāmapi jagadākhyadravyārambhakatvantarhyatyāvayavitvahāniḥ syādityarthaḥ |
nanu na ghaṭādipadārthairārabdhaṃ jagadākhyaṃ padārthaṃ pakṣīkṛtya tasya svāntarbhedahīnatvaṃ sādhyate |
yenoktadoṣaḥ |
kintu ghaṭādikameva pakṣīkṛtya tasya svāntarbhedahīnatvaṃ sādhyate |
ato noktadoṣa ityata āha -- ghaṭādīti || svāntarbhedaśabdena hīnaśabdasāhiteneti śeṣaḥ |
ataḥ svānatarbhedaśabdamātrasya bhedābhāvāsādhakatvātkathamidamiti codyānavakāśaḥ || siddhasādhanamiti || ghaṭādīnāṃ svāpratiyogikabhedābhāvasyāsmābhirapyaṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ |
sādhyavikala iti || ghaṭa maṭhākāśādīnāmanyonyabhedasyaiva sadbhāvāditi bhāvaḥ |
tatroktabādhakaparihārāyāha -- ākāśabhedeti || anyatra mandāramañjaryām |
ātmābhedasādhakānumānanirākaraṇamupasaṃharati -- tasmāditi |
yaccedamucyate (ghaṭapaṭādyabhede'numānaṃ pramāṇamiti) |
vimataṃ ghaṭādi paṭapratiyogika(tātvika)tādātmyādhikaraṇam abhidheyatvāt prameyatvādvā paṭavat |
paṭo vā na ghaṭādvastuto bhidyate dravyatvāt pṛthivītvādvā ghaṭavaditi |
atredaṃ vaktavyam |
mudgaraprahārādinā ghaṭaḥ kiṃ naśyatyuta neti |
na cedanityatvapratipādakaśrutivirodho'pasiddhāntaśca |
ghaṭārthaṃ kriyamāṇavyāpāravaiyarthyaṃ ca syāt |
(ghaṭādyarthakriyākaraṇādidoṣāpattiśca |) ādye tadaiva gaganādiravidyāprapañcaḥ (gaganāvidyādiprapañcaḥ) kiṃ naśyatyuta neti |
ādye (ghaṭādyartha)kriyābhāvaḥ prasajyeta |
gaganāvidyayornāśāyogācca |
mahāpralayamokṣau ca syātām |
dvitīye kathaṃ paṭasya ghaṭādinā pāramārthikābhedaḥ |
na hi naṣṭe rajate'nuvṛttāyāḥ śukteḥ pāramārthikābhedaḥ |
dūrasthavanaspatyorabhedagrāhi ca pratyakṣaṃ tattvāvedakapramāṇaṃ syāt |
vastutastayostādātmyāditi pratikūlatarkā ūhyāḥ |
abhedasādhakānumānamātrasya pratyakṣānumānāgamabādhakatarkaparāhatisopādhikatvābhāsasamānayogakṣematvādīni sādhāraṇadūṣaṇānīti |
advaitaśrutayastu bhagavatpādairevānyatrānyathā vyākhyātāḥ || cha ||
ramānāthāya haraye pūrṇānandaśarīriṇe |
nirdoṣaguṇapūrṇāya sarvayajñabhuje namaḥ ||
(ramānāthāya rāmāya sarvayajñabhuje namaḥ ||
nirdoṣaguṇapūrṇāya pūrṇānandaśarīriṇe || 1 ||)
|| iti śrīmatpadavākyapramāṇapārāvārapāraṅgataśrīmadbrahmaṇyatīrthapūjyapādaśiṣyeṇa vyāsayatinā kṛtaṃ bhedojjīvanaṃ samāptam ||
(|| iti śrībrahmaṇyapūjyapādānāṃ śiṣyeṇa vyāsayatinā racitaṃ bhedojjīvanaṃ sampūrṇam || cha ||)
evaṃ kenacicchaṅkitamanumānacatuṣṭayaṃ nirākṛtyāpareṇa śaṅkitamanātmabhedasādhakamanumānadvayamapi nirākartumanuvadati || yaccedamucyata iti || atra ghaṭavadityanantaramiti śabdo'dhyāhāryaḥ || apasiddhāntaśceti || pareṇāpi ghaṭādermithyātvasiddhyai paricchinnatvāṅgīkāreṇa vyāvahārikavināśāṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ || ghaṭādyarthakriyeti || mudgarādiprahārānantaramapi tena ghaṭena jalāharaṇādyarthakriyākaraṇarūpadoṣaścetyarthaḥ |
āpattiścāsau doṣaśceti doṣāpattiriti viparītasamāsaḥ || tadeveti || ghaṭanāśakāla evetyarthaḥ |
na gaganāvidyādītyādipadena stambhādisaṅgrahaḥ |
jagadabhedavāde ghaṭasya paṭenaiva gaganādinā'pyabhedāttadaiva vināśaḥ sambhāvita iti vikalpopapattiḥ || uta netīti |
vaktavyamityanuvartate || ghaṭādyarthakriyābhāva iti || teṣāṃ gaganādīnāṃ yā'rthakriyā'vakāśadānādirūpā tadabhāvaḥ prasajyetetyarthaḥ || gaganāvidyayornāśāyogācceti || gaganasya vyāvahārikanityatvenāvidyāyā jñānaikanivartyatvena mudgaraprahārādinā ghaṭanāśakāle tayorvināśo na yujyata iti bhāvaḥ |
tayorvyāpakatvena nāśāyogādityapyāhuḥ |
tādṛśayorapi gaganāvidyayoritareṣāṃ cedānīmeva nāśāṅgīkāre kiṃ bādhakamityata āha -- mahāpralayamokṣau syātāmiti || tadaivetyanuvartate |
tathā ca yadi ghaṭanāśakāle sarvavināśaḥ syāttarhīdānīmeva mahāpralayaḥ syāt |
avidyāvināśe cedānīmeva mokṣo'pi syādityarthaḥ || dvitīya iti || ghaṭanāśe'pi gaganādīnāmanāśaścedityarthaḥ || kathamiti || naṣṭānaṣṭayoḥ pāramārthikābhedāyogāditi bhāvaḥ |
tadevopapādayati -- na hīti || pāramārthikābheda iti || naṣṭarajateneti śeṣaḥ |
atra sarvasyāpi vyāvahārikaprapañcasya vipratipattiviṣayatayā naṣṭānaṣṭaghaṭapaṭayornidarśayitumaśakyatvātprātibhāsikavyāvahārike rajataśuktī eva nidarśayati |
na hi vyāvahārikayorghaṭapaṭayoriva prātibhāsikavyāvahārikayoḥ rajataśuktyoḥ pāramārthikābhedo'ṅgīkartuṃ śakya iti || jaḍābhedasādhakānumāne tarkaparāhatiṃ cāha || dūrastheti || yadi jaḍānāṃ pāramārthiko'bhedastarhīti pūraṇīyam |
evaṃ pratyekamanumānāni dūṣayitvā sādhāraṇadūṣaṇairapi dūṣayati -- abhedasādhakānumānamātrasyeti || vimatā ātmāna ityādyanumānaṣaṭkasyāpītyarthaḥ |
yogakṣematvādītyādipadenāprayojakatvaparigrahaḥ |
tathā hi |
ādyānumānasya tāvannāhaṃ sarvajño na nirduḥkha ityādipratyakṣaparāhatiḥ |
dvā suparṇetyāgamaparāhatiḥ |
jīvo brahmapratiyogikadharmisamānasattākabhedādhikaraṇam |
brahmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvādityādyanumānaparāhatiḥ |
paramātmatvena sopādhikatvam |
vimatā ātmānaḥ paramātmano'paramārthato'pi na bhidyante ātmatvātparamātmavadityābhāsasamānayogakṣematvam |
astvātmatvaṃ tathā'pi sādhyaṃ mā'stvityaprayojakatā ca |
vimatāni śarīrāṇi devadattasyaiva bhogāyatanānītyanumānasya na mama yajñadattaśarīraṃ bhogāyatanamiti devadattīyapratyakṣaparāhatiḥ |
bhinnāśca bhinnadharmāśca padārthā ityādyāgamaparāhatiḥ |
vimatāni śarīrāṇi na devadattasyaiva bhogāyatanāni devadattānadhiṣṭhitatvāt vyatirekeṇa devadattaśarīravat |
na cāsiddhiḥ |
devatatto na yajñadattādiśarīrādhiṣṭhātā'vyāpakatvād vyatirekeṇeśvaravadityanumānena tatsiddheḥ |
tathā cānumānaparāhatiḥ |
devadattādhiṣṭhitatvamupādhiḥ |
vimatāni kalatrāṇi devadattasyaiva bhogāyatanāni kalatratvāddevadattakalatravadityabhāsasāmyam |
anukūlatarkābhāvena hetoraprayojakatā ca |
bhedo mithyeti tṛtīyānumāne'pi nāhaṃ sarvajño nāpi yajñadatto nāpi ca ghaṭa ityādidevadattapratyakṣavirodhaḥ |
satyaṃ bhidetyādyāgamavirodhaḥ |
bhedaḥ satyaḥ pramāṇadraṣdṛtvādātmavadityanumānavirodhaḥ |
āropitatvamupādhiḥ |
abhedo mithyā'bhedatvāddehātmābhedavadityābhāsasāmyaṃ coktameva |
bhedābhedabhede vā brahmaprapañcabhede vā bhedatvasadbhāve'pi mithyātvādarśanādaprayojakatā ca |
caturthānumāne ghaṭādīnāmanyonyabhedasyābādhitapratyakṣasiddhatvātsulabhaiva pratyakṣaparāhatiḥ |
bhinnāśca bhinnadharmāśca padārthā nikhilā apītyāgamavirodhaḥ |
ghaṭo na paṭādibhyastāttvikabhedaśūnyaḥ |
ghaṭatvād vyatirekeṇa paṭavadityādyanumānavirodhaḥ |
īśvaratvamupādhiḥ |
vimataṃ tāttvikasvāntarbhedavaddṛśyatvādityapi prayoktuṃ śakyatvādābhāsasāmyamaprayojakatā ca |
evameva pañcamaṣaṣṭhayorapi pratyakṣādiparāhatiḥ sopādhikatvaṃ copapādanīyam |
atha śrutayaśca neha nānetyādikā abhedabodhiketyuktaṃ nirākaroti || advaitaśrutayastviti |
anyatra bṛhadāraṇyabhāṣyādāvanyathā iha brahmaṇi kiñcana nānā bhinnaṃ guṇakarmādikaṃ nānātvaṃ vā nāstītyevaṃ bhagavati bhedābhāvaparatayā neha nānā'sti kiñcaneti śrutirvyākhyātā |
tattvamasyādikāstu sādṛśyaparatayā vyākhyātā ityarthaḥ |
yathoktamanuvyākhyāne svātantrye ca viśiṣṭatve sthānamatyaikyayorapi |
sādṛśye caikyavāk samyak sāvakāśā yatheṣṭata iti || iti śabdasyetyayaṃ parihāra iti pūrvapratijñāvākyena sambandhaḥ |
samāptidyotakatvaṃ vā |
hetvarthako vā'yamiti śabdaḥ |
tasmādviṣṇvākhyasya brahmaṇaścetanācetanātmakaviśvabhinnatvaṃ siddhamityupasaṃhāravākyaśeṣeṇānvayaḥ |
evaṃ samāpitaprakaraṇaḥ śrīmadvyāsatīrthamunirāṭ yadarthamanena prakaraṇena viṣṇorviśvato bhedaṃ samarthitavān tallakṣaṇaviśiṣṭatayā viṣṇuṃstuvannamati || ramānāthāyeti || nirdoṣaścāsau guṇapūrṇaśceti vigrahaḥ |
yathā guṇapūrṇatvasiddhyarthaṃ viśvabhedasamarthanamevaṃ nirdoṣatvasiddhyarthamapi bhavatīti bhāvena nirdoṣetyuktiḥ |
etenādimaśloke'pi nirdoṣatvena cetyupalakṣaṇayā grāhyamiti sūcayati |
yathoktamanuvyākhyāne |
tasya bhedaśca sarvataḥ |
adoṣatvasiddhyarthaṃ yadabhede tadanvaya iti || bhagavannamanamiva bhagavatīnamanamapi bhagavatprītiheturiti jñāpanāyoktaṃ ramānāthāyeti || svakṛtagranthasya vāgyajñarūpatvāttena sarvayajñabhoktā bhagavānprīṇātviti bhāvenoktaṃ sarvayajñabhuja iti || yathoktaṃ nyāyāmṛtānte |
matprerakeṇa hariṇā yā pūjā svasya kāritā |
vāgyajñarūpā lakṣmīśastayā prīṇātu keśava iti || nanvaśarīraścedviṣṇuḥ kathaṃ tasya sarvayajñabhoktṛtvam |
śarīrī cedyajñadattavadantavān syāt |
kiñca yajñabhoktṛtvāṅgīkāre tasyāpūrṇatāprasaṅgaḥ |
apūrṇasyaiva loke bhogadarśanādityata uktaṃ pūrṇānandaśarīriṇa iti || astyeva bhagavataḥ śarīram |
taccāndādiguṇātmakameva |
ato na bhoktṛtvānupapattirnāpi mṛtyādidoṣānuṣaṅgaḥ |
na ca tarhi bhokturapūrṇatāpattiḥ |
pūrṇānandasyāpi krīḍābhogopapatteriti bhāva ityaśeṣamatimaṅgalam || cha ||
nirdoṣoruguṇārṇavāya nikhilāmnāyaikavedyāya te satyāśeṣajagajjanisthitilayajñānādisaddhetave ||
śrīmanmadhvahṛdabjasadmanivasaddhaṃsāya kaṃsadviṣe saṃsevatsu manaḥ svabhīṣṭatanave śrīkṛṣṇavṛṣṇe namaḥ || 1 ||
bhedojjīvanabhāvadīpanamidaṃ vāgbuddhiśuddhyai kṛtaṃ pūrvācāryavaco vicārya sarasaṃ saṅgṛhya sāraṃ tataḥ ||
bāliśyādbata śaiśavaṃ khalu mayā prādarśi tatkṣamyatāṃ vatsānāmiva māturaudhasapayaḥ pāne prasaktaṃ budhaiḥ || 2 ||
kroḍāryapādapātho'jabhājā bhagavatā kṛtā ||
bhūyādbhagavataḥ prītyai bhedojjīvanapañcikā || 3 || cha ||
|| iti śrīmadbhāskarīveṅkaṭavarāhācāryapūjyacaraṇacaraṇārādhakena bhagavannamnā viracito bhedojjīvanabhāvapradīpaḥ samāptaḥ || cha || cha ||
vimataṃ ghaṭādīti |
yena bhāskarādinā |
jagadādīnāmapi satyatvaṃ svābhāvikabhedābhāvaścāṅgīkriyate tasyedamanumānamiti jñeyam |
atra tāttvikapadaṃ svābhāvikaparam |
yadvā māyāvādina evedamanumānam |
atra tāttvikatādātmyādhikaraṇamityasya tāttvikabhedādhikaraṇaṃ na bhavatītyarthaḥ || śrutīti |
śvobhāvo martyasyetyādirudāhartavyā || ghaṭādyarthakriyeti || yadi mudgaraprahārādinā ghaṭo naśyati tarhi tadanantaramapi jalāharaṇādikriyāpattirityarthaḥ || tadaiveti || ghaṭanāśakāla evetyarthaḥ || ghaṭādyarthakriyābhāva iti || ghaṭanāśe paṭādīnāmapi nāśena tadarthakriyā'pi na syādityarthaḥ || nāśāyogācceti || nityatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
sarvasyāpi pralayo mokṣaśca syādityāha -- mahāpralayeti || kathāmiti || ghaṭanāśe'pyanaṣṭasya tadabhedāyogāditi bhāvaḥ |
tatra dṛṣṭāntamāha -- na hīti |
bhramotpannarajatasya bādhe'pyabādhitāyāḥ śukterajatābhedādarśanādityarthaḥ |
nanu mudgaraprahāre'pi na ghaṭanāśaḥ |
śrutistu sarveṣāṃ pralaye yugapannāśenāpi caritārthā |
na ca mudgaraprahārānantaraṃ ghaṭānupalambhavirodhaḥ |
vyañjakābhāvenopapatteḥ |
tātkālikavacanaṃ tvadarśanamātraviṣayam |
naṣṭo ghaṭa iti pratyakṣaṃ tvavyaktatvaṃ gocarayati |
vyaktasyaivārthakriyākāritvānnārthakriyāpatteścet |
maivam |
nityasyākāśādeḥ kadā'pi nāśābhāvena tadātmakasyāpyanāśena śrutivyākopāt |
kiñca vyañjakābhāvaḥ kimatyantābhāvo dhvaṃso vā |
ādye na kadā'pi ghaṭo palabdhiḥ syāt |
dvitīye sarveṣāṃ yugapannāśa ityasya bhaṅgāpattiḥ |
na ca vyañjakasyāpyanabhivyaktireva na nāśa iti vācyam |
anavasthāpatteḥ |
nanu tathā'pi vyāvahārikabhedabalenaikasya nāśe'pyanyasyānāśopapattiriti vācyam |
tasya vyavahārato nāśābhāve'pi paramārthatastadavaśyambhāvāt |
na ceṣṭāpattiḥ |
ghaṭanāśātpūrvamuttaratra ca gagane viśeṣānulambhādityalamatiparihasanīyaprakriyānirāsāyāsaleśena |
jaḍānāṃ tāttvikābhede bādhakatarkaṃ cāha -- dūrastheti || advaitaśrutaya iti || advaitaparatayā parābhimatā ityarthaḥ || anyatheti || īśvarīyasvāntarbhedābhāvasvātantryaviśiṣṭatvakārakaśaktidhārakatvāntarniyāmakatvasarvavyāptatvasādṛśyapriyatvasthānaikyamatyaikyasāyujyādiviṣayatayetyarthaḥ |
evaṃ svātantryādyaśeṣabhinno hariḥ sarvottama ityaśeṣaguṇākaratvādanyonyabhinnajīvajaḍātmakātprapañcādatyantamatimaṅgalam || cha || cha ||
pratyakṣaiḥ sakalātmasākṣibhiralaṃ niḥśeṣadoṣojjhitaiḥ pratyakṣāgamasammatāṅganicayaurnirbādhatarkairapi ||
sāṅgāmnāyapurāṇasasmṛtikalāsūtreti hāsāgamairbhinno'smatpramitaḥ parātparatamaḥ śrīśo'stu me preyase || 1 ||
murāriṃ kalpāṅgaṃ balayati ramākalpalatikā varā kalpānalpaprasavaphalapatrālimahitā ||
karālambāsmākaṃ muraharanagārohaṇavidhau surāmāvāsā janayatu manīṣārathaphalam || 2 ||
prāripsorjagadīśvarasya jagatīṃ nāṭyādiraṅgāyate svairaṃ viśvadhuraṃ bibharti yuvarāḍyaḥ sthāpanecchorapi ||
nāsīraḥ kila vīrabhūṣaṇamaṇiryaḥ sañjihīrṣorasau vāyurvyāsadivākarasya taraṇī bhūtosti madhvātmanā || 3 ||
mūḍhaḥ prauḍhāgragaṇyo bhavati hi badhiro vāgdharāṇāmudāro mūkaḥ syādvāvadūko kukṛtirapagatiḥ paṅgurākrāntiguḥ syāt ||
vyaṅgo bhūyādanaṅgaḥ kumatirapi nṛpaḥ paṇḍakaḥ puṃpravīṇo yaddṛṣṭisvṛṣṭimātrātsakalamavikalaṃ sā'vatādbhāratī naḥ || 4 ||
jayāryavacanāṅgatāgahanabhāvanānārasā cakāsti caturālayācaritacāranānāpadi ||
gurūktamaṇisantaterdṛḍhasuvarṇasūtrairalaṃ vikīrṇavividhatrayīkacanibandhasairandhrikā || 5 ||
vāṇī yasya satāṃ sudhārasakarī mādhuryasāropamā svātantryāsikaṭhoratejitamahāprāsāgradhārā satām ||
dṛṣṭāduṣṭajanāghapuṇyaphalasamprākārito vyāsarāṭ so'yaṃ me diśatāddṛśaṅkaviśiraḥ paṭṭāgrabhūṣāmaṇiḥ || 6 ||
yajjihvā siṃhapīṭhe vilasati vacasāṃ rājyasāmrājyalakṣmīryatsvāntaḥ śrīrniśānte viharati sa hariḥ kāntayā klāntihīnaḥ ||
ye śrīmadhvāryavāṇīvivaraṇaviduṣāmagragaṇyā vareṇyāste tīrthācāryavaryā mama kila guravo viṭhṭhalāryaprasūtāḥ || 7 ||
mudgalāryatanūjena janārdanapuraukasā ||
śarmaṇā''nandatīrthena yathā prajñamabhāṣi vāk || 8 ||
bhedacintāratnamidaṃ madhvaśāstrābdhisambhavam ||
buddhiśāṇasamullīḍhaṃ dhriyatāṃ hariṇā hṛdi || 9 ||
iti śrīpadavākyapāraṅgatavyāsatīrthapūjyacaraṇakṛtabhedojjīvanavivaraṇaṃ bhedacintāratnaṃ samāptam || cha || cha ||
|| apasiddhāntaśceti || brahmajñānenāvidyakanivṛtteraṅgīkārāt pratyakṣavirodhaśca bodhyaḥ || ghaṭādyarthakriyākaraṇadoṣāpattiriti || mudgarapraharottaramapi ghaṭasattve ghaṭādyarthakriyā syādityarthaḥ |
kvacittu ghaṭārthaṃ kriyamāṇavyāpāravaiyarthyaṃ syāditi pāṭhaḥ || gaganāvidyayoriti || vyāpakasya nāśādarśanādityarthaḥ || mahāpralaya iti || ghaṭapaṭādivatprapañcāntargatasya sarvasya nāśe mahāpralayasya ca mokṣasya ca nāśaḥ syādityarthaḥ |
dvitīye ghaṭādīnāṃ nāśe'pi gagananāśe kathaṃ ghaṭapaṭādinā gaganādeḥ pāramārthiko bheda ityarthaḥ |
śukteḥ pāramārthikabhedo rajate na yaditi jaḍānāṃ pāramārthikabhedaḥ syāditi tarke āpādakaṃ pūraṇīyam |
evaṃ prātisvikānanumānadoṣānabhidhāya sādhāraṇānapi tānāha -- abhedasādhakānumānamātrasyeti || ādipadenāprayojakatvasatpratipakṣau |
etāni tu tatra pradarśitānyeva kānicitpunarūhyānīti bhāvaḥ |
evamanumānāntare'pīti || advaitaśrutaya iti || tā vivṛtāśca vyākhyātā asmābhiranyatra bodhyam |
tathā ca yujyate viṣṇvākhyasya parabrahmaṇo viśvabhinnatvamityupasaṃhāro'pi bodhyaḥ || cha || cha ||
vyāsahaṃsagatiḥ kutra mādṛk kvāpi gatiḥ kva vā ||
anayorihasaṃyogaṃ manye nṛharisādhitam || 1 ||
tarkakarkaśakaśāvaśataścedgauriyaṃ vicalitā gurūmāgati ||
gṛṣṭirityapi vicintya dayātaḥ sādhubhiḥ sapadi tatra vidheyā || 2 ||
vidvaccakorāstṛpyantu bhedacandrikayā'nayā ||
bhedojjīvanaṭippaṇyānirūḍhārthavilokanāt || 3 ||
iti śrīkumbhārīnārāyaṇācāryasūnuvāsudevācāryaviracitā bhedojjīvanaṭippaṇī bhedacandrikā samāptā || cha || cha ||
|| tāttviketi || prātibhāsikatādātmyamādāya siddhasādhanatāvāraṇāya brahmasvarūpeṇa tādātmyaṃ tayorvāstavamityabhimānaḥ || vastuta iti || tādṛśabhedamupādāya bādhavāraṇāyānityatveti || `tato'nyadārta'miti śrutītyarthaḥ || apasiddhāntaśceti || mahāpralaye sarveṣāṃ kāraṇāvasthāpannarūpalayāṅgīkārādityarthaḥ || ghaṭādyarthamiti || punarghaṭotpattyarthamityarthaḥ |
anyathā ghaṭasya siddhatvāttadvaiyarthyāpāta iti bhāvaḥ |
ghaṭārthakriyākaraṇadoṣāpattiriti pāṭhe ghaṭasya jalāharaṇādirūpā yā kriyā tatkaraṇaprasaṅgādityarthaḥ || kriyābhāva iti || ākāśābhāvena nityaṃ tatkriyotpatteradarśanādityarthaḥ || nāśāyogāditi || niravayavatvena samavāyikāraṇanāśarūpakāraṇābhāvāt |
nityatvena kāraṇaśūnyayoḥ kāraṇātmanā sthitirūpalayāyoga ityarthaḥ || mahāpralayeti || sarvaprapañcasya nāśāṅgīkārapakṣe tannāśāvaśyakatvāditi bhāvaḥ || dvitīye iti || gaganānīnāṃ nityatva ityarthaḥ || kathamiti || prameyatvābhidheyatvādihetumatsu gaganādiṣu paṭapratiyogikatāttvikatādātmyādhikaraṇatvābhāvena vyabhicārāduktahetavaḥ kathaṃ tatsādhayerannityarthaḥ |
nāśe'pi gaganatādātmyaṃ vāstavamaṅgīkriyata eva paṭasyetyata āha -- na hīti |
tathātve rajatārthinaḥ śuktau pravṛttiḥ syādeveti bhāvaḥ || ābhāsasamānayogakṣematvādīnīti |
ādinā'prayojakatvasatpratipakṣau grāhyau || śrutaya iti || vyākhyātā iti || asmābhirapi tā vivṛtā iti śeṣaḥ || samāsa iti || saṅkṣepaḥ kṛta ityarthaḥ |
tasmātsvātantryādiguṇaviśiṣṭo viṣṇurityupasaṃhāravākyaṃ pūraṇīyamityaśeṣamatimaṅgalam || cha ||
naijasvātantryādiguṇaiḥ siddhabhedasvarūpavān ||
cidacidbhyaḥ sa me vyāsaḥ prīto'stu jagadīśvaraḥ || 1 ||
durvādibhujagaḥ paridarśanena naṣṭābhidhāmamṛtavāgbalato'bhirakṣan ||
yo vyāsarājayatirāṭ tadanuvrateyaṃ vāṇī mameha viduṣāṃ vitanotu modam || 2 ||
śrīmadvyāghrapurīvāsivāsudevāryasūnunā ||
śiṣyeṇa teṣāṃ racitā bhedojjīvanaṭippaṇī || 3 ||
anayā granthakṛtyā yatprāptaṃ puṇyaṃ samastaśaḥ ||
asmadguruhṛdabjasthaprāṇeśāyārpayāmyaham || 4 ||
iti śrīvyāghrapuryupanāmaka śrīnāthaviracitā bhedojjīvanaṭippaṇī bhedasañjīvanī sampūrṇā ||
|| śrīkṛṣṇārpaṇamastu ||
durantadurmatadhvāntadhvaṃsakṛnmadhvabhāskaraḥ |
balitthetyādivedoktaguṇaḥ prīto'stu me sadā ||
|| iti śrīmadvyāsarājatīrthaśrīpādakṛtabhedojjīvanayākhyānaṃ śrīmahāmahopādhyāyakāśītirumalācāryakṛtaṃ samāptam ||
anātmabhedasādhakānumānānāṃ pareṇānuktatvādidānītānyaśaṅkate -- yaccedamityādinā || atra paṭābhedasādhanamupalakṣam |
evameva ghaṭasyānātmaprapañcamātrābhedaḥ sādhanīyaḥ |
yathā rajatanāśe'pyanuvṛttāyāḥ na śukte rajatābhedaḥ evaṃ ghaṭanāśe'pyanuvṛttapaṭādiprapañcasyanaghaṭābheda ityāśayenāha -- atredamityādinā śukteḥ pāramārthikābheda ityantena || ānityatvapratipādakaśrutīti || yamapyeti bhuvanamityādiśrutivirodha ityarthaḥ |
|| apasiddhānta iti || mudgaraprahārādinā ghaṭanāśasyapūrvācāryairaṅgīkārāttvayedānīṃ tadanaṅgīkāre siddhāntavirodha ityarthaḥ || ghaṭādyarthakriyeti || yadi mudgaraprahārādinā ghaṭo na naśyati tarhi tadanantaraṃ jalāharaṇādyarthakriyākāritvaprasaṅga ityarthaḥ || tadaiva yadā ghaṭo naśyati tadaivetyarthaḥ |
arthakriyābhāva iti || ghaṭanāśakāla evāvidyānāśe ghaṭāntarādyutpattirna syāt |
evaṃ ca jalāharaṇādikriyālopaḥ syādityarthaḥ || nāśāyogācceti || aikyāparokṣajñānena mahāpralaya eva tannāśābhyupagamenedānīṃ tannāśāyogāt nāśe cedānīmeva mahāpralayamokṣau syātāmityarthaḥ |
tadevopapādayati -- nahīti || yathā hi nedaṃ rajatamiti bādhakajñānena rajate naṣṭe'pi śukternāśabhāvānna tayoḥ pāramārthikābheda evaṃ ghaṭanāśe'pyanaṣṭasyāvidyāgaganādiprapañcasya na ghaṭābheda ityarthaḥ |
anātmā bhedasādhakānumānasya tarkaparāhatimāha -- dūrasthavanaspatyoriti || yadi jaḍeparasparaṃ pāramārthikābhedo'ṅgīkriyate tarhi dūre vidyamānayordvayoḥ cūtapanasavṛkṣayoreka evāyaṃ vṛkṣa iti yobhramojāyate tasyāpi prāmāṇyaṃ stāt |
tayorvāstavābhedena tadavagāhinastasya tatvāvedakatvādityarthaḥ |
evamātmānātmabhedasādhakānumānānāṃ pratyekaṃ dūṣaṇamabhidhāyedānīmabhedamātrasādhakānumānasādhāraṇaṃ tadāha -- abhedasādhaketi || pratyakṣetyādi || vimatā ātmānaḥ paramātmanaḥ paramārthato na bhidyanta ityādyātmābhedasādhakānumānasya nāhaṃ sarvajño na nirduḥkha ityādipratyakṣavirodhaḥ |
dvāsuparṇetyādyāgamavirodhaḥ |
jīvaḥ brahmapratiyogikadharmisattāsamānasattākabhedādhikaraṇaṃ brahmānanusaṃhitaduḥkhānusandhātṛtvāt |
vyatirekeṇa brahmavadityadyanumānavirodhaḥ |
anukūlatarkābhāvātpakṣetaratvasyopādhitvam |
vimatā ātmānaḥ paramātmanaḥ paramārthato'pi na bhidyante ātmatvāt paramātmavadityapisādhayituṃ śakyatvenābhāsasamānayogakṣematvam |
vimataṃ ghaṭādi paṭapratiyogikatādātmyādhikaraṇam abhidheyatvādityādyanātmābhedasādhakānumānasya ghaṭaḥ paṭo netyādipratyakṣavirodhaḥ |
ghaṭaḥ paṭapratiyogikatādātmyādhikaraṇaṃ na bhavati pratiyogisāmānādhikaraṇyāvacchedakānavacchinnapaṭaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogidharmādhikaraṇatvāt vyatirekeṇa paṭavadityādyanumānavirādhaḥ |
mithaśca jaḍabhedo'yamityāgamavirodhaḥ |
anukūlatarkābhāvena pakṣetaratvasyopādhitvam |
vimataṃ ghaṭādi vyavahāro'pi paṭapratiyokitādātmyānadhikaraṇaṃ na bhavati abhidheyatvāt paṭavaditi sādhayituṃ śakyatvenābhāvasasamānayogakṣematvamiti vivekaḥ |
idānīmabhedasādhakaśrutīrnirākaroti || advaitaśrutaya iti || nehanānetyādyā ityarthaḥ || anyatreti || kāṭhakopaniṣadbhāṣyādau bhagavato guṇakarmādibhedaniṣedhakatvena vyākhyātā ityarthaḥ || evaṃ samāpitaprakāraṇobhagavān vyāsatīrthamunirmaṅgalamadhyānimaṅgalāṃ tāni śāstrāṇyāyuṣmatpuruṣakāṇibhavantīmismaraṇādaṃ te'pi tadācarati || ramānāthāyeti || haraye nama iti sambandhaḥ |
bhedanirūpaṇasya prayojanasūcanāyaramānāthāyeti viśeṣaṇam |
aramāpadaṃ cetanācetanātmakasarvajagadupalakṣaṇam |
tathāca cetanācetanātmakajagannāthāyetyarthaḥ |
anena bhedasamarthanasaydyapadyoktajagadīśvaratvasamarthanārthatvaṃ sūcimiti dhyeyam |
bhedakadharmakathanāya sarvayajñabhuja iti |
tarhyapūrṇatvaṃ syādityata uktaṃ guṇapūrṇāyeti |
svasyānandādiguṇapūrṇatve'pi bhaktānugrahāya bhuṅkte natu tṛptyarthamiti bhāvaḥ |
yatra guṇāstatra doṣā iti niyamāddoṣavānapikimityato nirdoṣeti |
nirdoṣaścāsau guṇapūrṇaśceti vigrahaḥ |
yadvā etadapibhedakadharmakathanam |
nanu hareḥ śarīramasti navā |
ādye maraṇādiprasaṅgaḥ |
dvitīye tasya bhoktṛtvāyoga ityata uktaṃ pūrṇānandaśarīriṇa iti |
astyevaśarīraṃ tacca sukhātmakaṃ natu vināśāvinābhāviprākṛtam |
ato noktadoṣa ityaśeṣamatimaṅgalam || 6 ||
padmāpadmājavandyāyapadmapatranibhekṣaṇa ||
padmanā bhāyarāmāyanamaḥ satpadmamāline ||
(śarkarāśrīnivāsena bālabodhāya nirmitā ||
bhedojjīvanavyākhyeyaṃ bhūyātprītyai ramāpateḥ ||
bhedojjīvanavyākhyānalabdhaṃ puṇyamanuttamam ||
prāpyāsmadguravaḥ prītāḥ kriyāsuḥ karuṇāṃ mayi) ||
